śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ |

śriyai namaḥ | śrīdharāya namaḥ ||

śrī mādhyam 3.1.1]

atikrāntādhyāyadvayena – 'nikhilajagadekakāraṇaṃ nirasta nikhila dopagandham apari- mitodāraguṇasāgaram, sakaletaravilakṣaṇaṃ brahma, mumukṣubhirupāsyatayā vedāntāḥ pratipāda- yanti' – ityayamarthaḥ, smṛtinyāya virodhaparihāra- parapakṣapratikṣepa-vedāntavākya paraspara- virodhaparihārarūpakāryasvarūpasaṃśodhanaiḥ taddurdharṣaṇatva hetubhiḥ saha sthāpitaḥ | ataḥ adhyāyadvayena 'brahmasvarūpaṃ pratipāditam ||
uttareṇedānīm – tatprāpyupāyaiḥ saha prāptiprakāraḥ cintayitumiṣyate ||

tatra 'tṛtīye'dhyāye - upāyabhūtopāsanaviṣayā cintā - vartate | upāsanārambhā- syarhito pāyaśca - prāpya vastuvyatiriktavaitṛṣṇyaṃ prāpya tṛṣṇā ceti, tatsiddhyarthaṃ jīvasya lokāntareṣu saṃcarataḥ, jāgrataḥ, svapataḥ, suṣuptasya, mūcrchataśca doṣāḥ ; 'parasya' brahmaṇaḥ tadrahitatā, kalyāṇa' guṇakaratvaṃ ca prathama dvitīyayoḥ pādayoḥ pratipādyante' ||

tatra - dehād dehāntaraṃ gacchan ayaṃ jīvaḥ, 'dehāntarā 'rambha hetubhiḥ bhūtasūkṣmaiḥ sampariṣvakta eva gacchati 1 uta na 1 - iti cintāyām ;
' ityayamarthaḥ ' ityasmātpūrvaṃ prathamādhyāyārthaḥ saṅkṣipta: | nikhiletyādiviśeṣaṇānāṃ pūrvavadarthaḥ | smṛtinyāyeti, dvitīyādhyāyasya prathama dvitīyapādārthasaṅkṣepaḥ | vedāntavākye - tyādinā, tṛtīya- turīyapādārthaḥ saṅkṣiptaḥ | evaṃ kiñcidvistareṇa adhyāyadvayārthaḥ uktaḥ | ataḥ iti, prathamadvikasya anugatamarthaṃ saṅgraheṇā''ha ||
uttareṇeti uttaradvikārthasaṅkṣepaḥ | idānīm - viṣaye siddhe, ityarthaḥ | prathamadvike siddhasvarūpaviṣaya cintā kṛtā | dvitīye tu sādhyasvarūpaviṣayacintā kriyate ityarthaḥ ||

tatreti, tṛtīya- caturthayoḥ sādhana - phalaviṣayakatayā sakramaḥ tṛtīyādhyāyārthaḥ | abhyarhi - tam — antaraṅgam | atra 'paramātmani yo rakto virakto'paramātmani ' ityasyārtho'bhipretaḥ | lokāntareṣu sañcarato doṣā ścintyante | tatra jāgarādyavasthasya doṣāḥ phalitāḥ ||
tatreti | kiṃ dehāntaraṃ gacchan jīvaḥ bhūtasūkṣmaiḥ sampariṣvakto raṃhati ? uta asampariṣvaktaḥ ?–iti | ' vettha yathā pañcamyāmāhutau āpaḥ puruṣavacaso bhavanti '

' iti tu

yatra yatra jīvo yāti, tatra tatra bhūtasūkṣmāṇāṃ sulabhatvāt, asampariṣvato yātīti - prāptam | paścādapi pūrvapakṣabījāni upanyasya nirasi' ṣyati' ||

pāñcālaḥ pravāhaṇaḥ, karmiṇāṃ gantavyadeśa, punarāvṛttiprakāraṃ, devayāna- pitṛyāṇapathavyā- vartane, amuṣya lokasya 'aprāptāraṃ ' ca vettha ? - iti pṛṣṭvā, idamapi papraccha ; 'vettha yathā pañcamyāmāhutāvāpaḥ puruṣavacaso bhavanti ?' iti | tataḥ imaṃ paścimaṃ praśna pratibuvaṃśca', dyulokam agnitvena rūpayitvā, tasminnetasminnannau devāḥ śraddhāṃ juhvati | tasyā āhuteḥ |

somo rājā ' sambhavati'

ityādinā, – ' devākhyāḥ jīvasya prāṇāḥ, agnitvena' rūpite' dyuloke, śraddhākhyaṃ vastu prakṣipanti sā ca śraddhā, somarājākhyāmṛtamaya deha' rūpeṇa pariṇamate | taṃ ca amṛtamayaṃ deha, ta eva prāṇāḥ, parjanye agnitvena 'rūpite' prakṣipanti | sa ca dehaḥ tatra "prakṣiptaḥ " varṣa bhavati | tacca varṣa ta eva prāṇāḥ pṛthivyām agnitvarūpitāyāṃ prakṣipanti | tacca tatra ' prakṣiptam' annaṃ bhavati | tacca annaṃ, ta eva, puruṣe agnitva- rūpite prakṣipanti | tacca tatra reto bhavati | tacca ta eva, yoṣāyām agnitvarūpitāyāṃ prakṣipanti | tacca tatra " prakṣiptaṃ " garbho bhavati' – ityuktvā āha-

' iti tu pañcamyāmāhutau āpaḥ puruṣavacaso bhavanti ' iti | pañcamyāmāhutau hutāyām, āpaḥ 'puruṣa' śabdābhilapyā bhavantītyarthaḥ |
pañcāgnītyādinā, prakaraṇasya anekapraśna- tatprativacanakramarūpatāṃ darśayati, arthāntaravyava- dhānaśaṅkānivṛttyartham | 'praśnānantaraṃ bhūtasūkṣmapariṣvaṅgaparaṃ prativacanaṃ na dṛśyate' iti na śaṅkā | vyāvartane—vyāvartakākārau | ' ityādinā ' ityasya ' uktavāha' ityuttaratrā'nvayaḥ | śraddhāśabdena bhūtasūkṣmāvasthāviśeṣa ucyate | 'amṛtamaya deha' śabdaḥ, 'somarāja 'śabda- vyākhyānam | prāṇāḥ - mukhyaprāṇāḥ indriyāṇi ca | prakṣipantīti | prāṇādhīnaḥ prakṣepaḥ homatvenopacaritaḥ ityabhiprāyaḥ | purucavacasaḥ iti bahuvrīhisamāsena sambandhamātraṃ pratītam | tatra puruṣa 'śabdaḥ svarūpaparaḥ | ' vacaḥ ' śabdo vācakaparaḥ | ataḥ 'samāsapratipannaḥ sambandhaḥ, vācyavācakabhāvākhyaviśeṣarūpa:' iti darśayituṃ 'puruṣaśabdābhilaṇyāḥ' ityuktam ||

'evamukte'–pūrvāsvapyāhutiṣu anuvartamānānāmeva apāṃ sūkṣmarūpāṇām, idānīṃ puruṣākāratvaṃ bhavati ityuktaṃ bhavati | ataḥ evaṃ praśnaprativacanābhyāṃ 'dehahetubhūtaiḥ bhūtasūkṣmaiḥ saha tatra tatra yati ' iti gamyate || 1 ||

nanu – ' āpaḥ puruṣa vacasaḥ ityukte, apāṃ puruṣākāra- pariṇāmapratīteḥ, gacchatā jīvena tāsāmeva pariṣvaṅgaḥ pratīyate | 'ataḥ kathaṃ sarveṣāṃ bhṛtasūkṣmāṇāṃ pariṣvaṅgaḥ iti 1 ; 'tatrāssha'
289. vyātmakatvāttu bhūyastvāt || 3-1-2 || (si)
' tu' śabda: codyaṃ vyāvartayati | " dehārambha' kāṇām apāṃ kevalānām, na

evamiti | praśna- prativacanavātrayayorubhayorapi 'pañcamyāmāhutau āpaḥ purucavacasaḥ ityukte, hotavyadravyākāṅkṣāyāṃ svavākyasthatayā saṃnihitāḥ āpa eva, homyatvena svarasataḥ pratītāḥ | vākyāntarasthā śraddhā, somarājarūpeṇa pariṇāmasya ayogyā | ataḥ sā'pi apāmeva jñāpikā | evaṃ praśna prativacanavākyasvārasyāt, prasiddhaśraddhāyāḥ ayogyatvācca pañcasvāhutiṣu apāṃ homyatvenā'nuvṛttirastīti tāsāmeva puruṣavacastvamityarthaḥ || ādyaṃ sūtraṃ pañcāgnividyāprativacanaviṣayameva ; ( na pañcāgnividyā- pitṛyāṇaviṣayam ) pitṛyāṇaviṣayavākyasya, ' aśrutatvā diti cenna' ityatrodāharaṇīyatvāt iti bhāṣyakārābhiprāyaḥ || 1 | jyātmaketi | prathamasya pañcamyantapadasya, saṃpariṣvakto raṃhatītyanvayo darśitaḥ śaṅkāgranthastha 'pariṣvaṅga' śabdena | dvitīyapañcamyantapadasya pratijñāvākyaṃ sāmarthyādadhyāhṛtam ;

dehārambhasaṃbhavaḥ; dehādyārambhā yahi ! -

' tāsāṃ trivṛtaṃ trivṛtamekaikāmakarot ' '
iti trivṛtkaraṇam | kevalānām apāṃ śravaṇaṃ tu, tāsāṃ bhūyastvāt | dehe ca lohitādibhūyastvena, ārambhakeṣu apāṃ bhūyastvaṃ gamyate || 2 ||
290. prāṇagateśca || 3-1-3 || (si)
itaśca – 'bhūtasūkṣmapariṣvaktavya gananam ' - iti gamyate | utkrāmati jīve,

'prāṇānāṃ tadanugatiḥ śrūyate-
'tamutkrāmantaṃ prāṇo'nūtkrāmati, prāṇamanūtkrāmantaṃ
sarve prāṇā anūtkrāmanti

iti | smaryate ca-

' manaḥṣaṣṭhānīndriyāṇi prakṛtisthāni karṣati | śarīraṃ yadavāpnoti yaccāpyutkrāmatīśvaraḥ |
gṛhītvaitāni saṃyāti vāyurgandhānivā''śayāt || '

iti | na ca nirāśrayāṇāṃ gatirupapadyate iti, tadāśrayabhūtānāṃ bhūtasūkṣmāṇāmapi gatiḥ, abhyupagantavyā || 3 ||
kevalānāmiti | vaiśepyāditi sūtre, trivṛtkṛteṣvapi ekaikasyādhikyāt, tannibandhanaḥ tadvyavahāraḥ ityuktam | atra tu tadādhikyamapāmastīti darśayituṃ sūtrārambhaḥ || 2 ||

itaścati' avāntarasādhyānvayavyāvṛttyartham || 3 ||

291. agnyādigatiśrutoriti cenna bhāktatvāt || 3-1-4 || (si)

'yatrāsya puruṣasya mṛtasya is'gniṃ vāgadhyeti,
vātaṃ prāṇaścakṣurādityam'
ityādinā prāṇānāṃ jīvamaraṇakāle agnyādiṣu apyayaśravaṇāt, na teṣāṃ jīvena saha gamanamiti, 'gatiśrutiḥ' anyathā neyā - iti cet !
na; bhāktatvāt agnyādidhu adhyayaśravaṇasya |

kathaṃ bhāktatvam ?

'oṣadhīrlomāni vanaspatīn keśāḥ '
iti, anapiyadbhiḥ lomādibhiḥ saha śravaṇāt | ataḥ ' cakṣurādyapyayaśrutiḥ, adhiṣṭhātṛ- devatāpakramaṇaparā || 4 ||

292 prathame'śravaṇāditi cenna tā eva hayupapatteḥ ||3-1-5|| (si)
yaduktam– 'adbhiḥ sūkṣmābhiḥ 'bhūtāntara 'saṃsṛṣṭābhiḥ pariṣvakto jīvo gacchati' iti praśna - prativacanābhyām avagamyate - iti ;
tannopapadyate ; dyulokāgniviṣaye prathame home, apāṃ ' homyatvā śravaṇāt |

' tasminnetasminnanau devāḥ śraddhāṃ juhvati'
iti, śraddhaiva hi ! homyatvena śrutā | śraddhā nāma jīvasya manovṛttiviśeṣatvena śrutā | ataḥ na āpaḥ tatra homyāḥ - iti cet !
na; yataḥ tāḥ – āpa eva 'śraddhā' śabdena tatrā'bhidhīyante | kutaḥ 1 praśna - prativacanopapatteḥ |

3.

' vettha yathā pañcamyāmāhutāvāpaḥ puruṣavacaso bhavanti ' iti praśnasya, prativacanopakrame hi ! śraddhā dyulokānau homyatvena śrutā | tatra yadi śraddhā' śabdena āpo nocyeran ! tataḥ anyathā praśnaḥ, anyathā prativacanamiti asaṅgataṃ syāt |
' iti tu pañcamyāmāhutāvāpaḥ puruṣavacasaḥ'
iti prativacana' nigamanaṃ ca śraddhāyāḥ adhvameva 'sūcayati' | 'vettha yathā ' iti hi ! praśnagataḥ prakāraḥ, ' iti tu pañcamyām' iti 'iti' śabdena, parihāre nigamyate |
nigamyata iti | praśnagatasya prakārasya prativacanānte nigamanāt madhye'pi tatprakāra

śraddhā- 'somarāja' - varṣā 'nna- reto garbharūpeṇa apāṃ pariṇāmamuktvā hi ! 'evamāpaḥ puruṣa- vacasaḥ' iti nigamyate |

śraddhā ' śabdasya ca apsu 'vaidika' prayogo dṛśyate-
apaḥ praṇayati | śraddhā vā āpaḥ ' ?

iti |

' śraddhāṃ juhvati | tasyā āhuteḥ somo rājā 'sambhavati' ' iti somākāreṇa pariṇāmaśca, apāmeva upapadyate | ataḥ – bhūtāntara saṃsṛṣṭābhiradbhiḥ sampa' riṣvakto jīvo' raṃhati - ityupapannam || 5 ||
293. aśrutatvāditi ceneṣṭādikāriṇāṃ pratīteḥ || 3-1-6|| (si) yatpunaḥ uktam–' adbhiḥ sampariṣvakto jīvaḥ yāti' ityayamarthaḥ, 'etasmā- dvākyāt avagamyate - iti ; tat nopapadyate ; 'asmin vākye jīvasya aśravaṇāt | atra hi ! śraddhādaya eva 'ambyavasthā 'viśeṣāḥ homyatvena śrutāḥ ; na tu jīvaḥ-

tatpariṣvaktaḥ - iti cet !

tanna; iṣṭādikāriṇāṃ pratīteḥ | asminneva' vākye hi ! uttaratra - ' brahmajñāna- vidhureṣṭāpūrta- dattakāriṇaḥ, dyulokaṃ prāpya somarājāno 'bhavanti' | puṇyakarmāvasāne ca, punarāgatya garbhaṃ prāpnuvanti' – ityucyate ;
'atha ya ime grāme iṣṭāpūrte dattamityupāsate, te dhūmamabhi' sambhavanti "

ityārabhya,

'pitṛlokādākāśam, ākāśācandramasam | eṣa somo rājā 'taddevānā 'mannam | taṃ devā bhakṣayanti |
'tasmin yāvatsampātamuṣitvā athaitameva | 'dhvānaṃ punarnivartante' ' yo yo hyannamatti yo retaḥ 'siñcati' tadbhūya eva bhavati' iti | atrāpi dyulokānau,
'śraddhāṃ juhvati | tasyā āhuteḥ somo rājā saṃbhavati' iti, tadekārthatvāt śraddhāvastha dehaviśiṣṭaḥ somarūpadehaviśiṣṭo bhavatītyuktam - iti gamyate | dehasya jīvaviśeṣaṇataikasvarūpasya vācakaḥ śabdaḥ, viśeṣye jīve eva paryavasyati | ataḥ 'saṃpariṣvakto jīvo yāti' ityupapadyate || 6 ||

na nu ca – ' taṃ devā bhakṣayanti' iti devairbhakṣyamāṇatva'-

asminneva vākye—mahāvākye ityarthaḥ | somarājānaḥ - divyadehāḥ | pūrtam — khātādikarma | ityupāsate – uktaprakāreṇa anutiṣṭhanti | sambhavantīti | bhavatiḥ prāptivācī | parasmaipaditvaṃ tu chāndasam | yadvā-sattārtho bhavatireva upasargavaśāt ( sam ) prāpyarthaḥ | yāvatsampātamiti 'sampāta' śabdena, ' saṃpatati anena svargam' iti vyutpattyā karmā'bhidhīyate | tadbhūya eva bhavati — tadyogī bhavatītyarthaḥ | ' atrāpīti' | ubhayatra ' somarājaśabdena pratyabhijñānāt aikārthyam | asphuṭajīvasambandhasya pūrvavākyasya, sphuṭajīvasambandhena uttaravākyena aikārthyāt ukta evārthaḥ ityarthaḥ | dehasyeti | śraddhā- somādiśabdaiḥ tattadavastha dehaviśiṣṭa - vacanaṃ lākṣaṇikaṃ ityatra || 6 ||

294. bhāktaṃ vā'nātmavittvāt tathā hi darśayati || 3-1-7 ||
' vā ' śabda: codyaṃ vyāvartayati | iṣṭādikāriṇaḥ anātmavictvāt, saḥ devānāṃ bhogopakaraṇatvena, iha amutra ca vartate | iha 'iṣṭādinā tadārādhanaṃ kurvan upakaroti | ārādhanaprītaiḥ devaiḥ dattam amuṃ lokaṃ prāpya, tatra tatsamānabhogaḥ tadupakaraṇaṃ bhavati | yathā 'paśuḥ evaṃ sa devānām '
iti, anātmavidaḥ devānāmupakaraṇatvaṃ darśayati śrutiḥ |

smṛtirapi – ātmavidāṃ brahmaprāptim, anātmavidāṃ ca devabhogyatvaṃ darśayati- ' devān devayajo yānti madbhaktā yānti māmapi '
iti | ataḥ jīvasya devānāṃ bhogopakaraṇatvābhiprāyam, annatvena bhakṣyatvavacanam | ataḥ tad bhāktam | tena tṛptireva ca devānāṃ bhakṣaṇamiti śrūyate -

'na vai devā aśnanti na pibanti etadevāmṛtaṃ dṛṣṭvā tṛpyanti' iti | tasmāt — bhūtasūkṣmaiḥ sampariṣvakto jīvo raṃhati iti siddham || 7 | iti tadantarapratipacyadhikaraṇam || 1 ||

kṛtātyayādhikaraṇam || 3-1-2 ||
295. kṛtātyaye'nuśayavān dṛṣṭa-smṛtibhyāṃ yathetamanevaṃ ca || 3-1-8 ||

kevaleṣṭāpūrtadattakāriṇāṃ dhūmādinā 'pitṛyāṇena pathā gamanam, karmaphalāvasāne punarāvartanaṃ ca ānātam -

yāvatsampātamuṣitvā, athaitamevā'dhvānaṃ punarnivartante'

iti |

tatra pratyavarohan jīvaḥ, kim anuśayavān pratyavarohati ? uta na ? – iti saṃśayyate |

kiṃ yuktam ? karmaṇaḥ kṛtsnasya upabhuktatvāt, na anuśayavān - iti prāptam | anuśayo hi ! upabhuktaśiṣṭaṃ karma | tacca kṛtsa' phala bhoge sati na avaśiṣyate | yāvatsampātamuṣitvā'

iti vacanāt kṛtsnopabhogaśca 'jñāyate' | 'saṃpatanti anena svarga lokam' iti, sampātaḥ karmocyate | śrutyantaraṃ ca-

' prāpyantaṃ karmaṇastasya yatkiceha karotyayam |
tasmāllokāt punaretyasmai lokāya karmaṇe || '

iti |

evaṃ prāpte, abhidhīyate - ' anuśayavān pratyavarohati ' - iti | kutaḥ ? dṛṣṭa smṛtibhyām | śruti smṛtibhyāmityarthaḥ | śrutistāvat-
' tadya iha ramaṇīyacaraṇāḥ abhyāśī ha yat te ramaṇīyāṃ yonimāpadyeran; brāhmaṇayoniṃ kṣatriyayoniṃ vaiśyayoniṃ vā'
'atha ya iha kapūyacaraṇāḥ abhyāśī ha yat te kapūyāṃ yonimāpadyeran ; śvayoniṃ vā sūkarayoniṃ vā caṇḍālayoniṃ vā'
iti pratyavarūḍhān prati śrayate | 'amuṣmāllokāt pratyavarūḍheṣu ramaṇīyakarmāṇaḥ, ramaṇīyacaraṇā:' ityādi vākyaṃ kiṃ saṃpātādiśabdasya phalapradānapravṛtta karmaparatā mavagamayati ? na ? iti | kim atra ' caraṇa' śabda : ācāraparaḥ ? uta sukṛta - duṣkṛtaparaḥ ? iti |
yadā sa ācāraparaḥ tadā, tena vākyena sampātādiśabdasya phalapradānapravṛtta karmaparatvānava- gamāt 'yāvatsampāta' mityādivākyasya sānuśayanivartana virodhitayā niranuśayo nivartate - iti - syāt ! yadā' caraṇa 'śabdaḥ sukṛta- duṣkṛtaparaḥ ; tadā ' tadya iha ramaṇīyacaraṇā:' ityādivākyai - kārthyena saṃpātādiśabdasya phaladānapravṛttakarmaparatvāvagamāt, ' yāvatsampātam' ityādivākyasya - sānuśaya nivartanā virodhitayā, ' sānuśayo nivartate' iti sidhyati |
kṛtātyaye kṛtasya karmaṇaḥ anubhavena vināśe | tacceti, tacchabdaḥ karmamātraparaḥ | sampatantīti | ' yāvatsampāta 'mityasya ' yāvatpatanam' ityarthaścet ! vākyavaiyarthyamityabhiprāyaḥ || 'dṛṣṭa' śabdena prasyakṣa śrutirvivakṣitā | abhyāśaḥ iti padaṃ 'di (vi) śa:' itivat prathamābahuvacanam | abhi āṅ pūrvāt 'aśū vyāptau iti dhātoḥ evaṃ rūpaṃ bhavati | ye iha abhyāśaḥ – abhyāgantāraḥ, te ramaṇīyacaraṇāḥ yat – sādhukarma -

ramaṇīyāṃ brāhmaṇādiyoniṃ pratipadyante kapūpacaraṇāḥ kutsitakarmāṇaḥ kutsitāṃ · – śva-sūkara- caṇḍālādiyoniṃ pratipadyante' iti pratyavarūḍhānāṃ puṇya pāpa karmayogaṃ *

darśayati |

smṛtirapi

'varṇā āśramāśca svakarmaniṣṭhāḥ pretyakarmaphalamanubhūpa, 'tataḥ śepega' viśiṣṭa. deśa- jāti kula- rūpā''yuḥ - śruta - vitta vṛtta - sukha - 'medhasaḥ, janma pratipadyante | viṣvañcaḥ, viparītāḥ naśyanti' –

iti | tathā,

' tataḥ parivṛttau karmaphalaśeṣeṇa jātiṃ, rūpaṃ, varṇa, balaṃ, medhāṃ, prajñāṃ dravyāṇi dharmānuṣṭhānamiti pratipadyante | tat cakravat ubhayorlokayoḥ sukha eva ' vartate '

iti | ' yāvatsampāta' miti, phaladānapravṛtta karma viśeṣaviṣayam | 'yatkiceha karotyayam " itīdamapi tadviṣayameva | abhuktaphalānām prakṛtaprāyaścittānāṃ ca karmaṇām, karmāntara- phalānubhavānnāśopi anupapannaḥ | ataḥ amuṃ lokaṃ gatāḥ, sānuśayā eva,

yathetam anevaṃca punarnivartante - ārohaṇaprakāreṇa prakārāntareṇa ca punarnivartante ityarthaḥ |

ārohaṇaṃ 'hi' ! dhūma - rājyaparapakṣa-dakṣiṇāyana paṇmāsa- pitṛlokākāśa- candrakrameṇa | avarohaṇaṃ tu, candramasaḥ sthānāt ākāśa - vāyu- dhūmātra 'meghakrameṇa | tatra ākāśāva- rohaṇāt yathetam | vāyvādiprāpteḥ pitṛlokādyaprāpteśca anevam || 8 ||
296. caraṇāditicenna tadupalakṣaṇārtheti kārṣṇājiniḥ ||3-1-9|| 'ramaṇīyacaraṇāḥ, kapūyacaraṇāḥ' iti na 'caraṇa' śabdena puṇya-pāparūpaṃ karma -abhidhīyate ; 'caraṇa' śabdasya loka- vedayoḥ ācāre prasiddheḥ |
laukikāḥ khalu ! 'caraṇam, ācāraḥ, śīlam, vṛttam' iti paryāyāna- 'bhimanyante' | vede ca
'yānyanavadyāni karmāṇi | tāni sevitavyāni '
yānyasmākaṃ sucaritāni | tāni tvayopāsyāni'
iti caraṇa- karmaṇī bhedena vyapadiśyete | ataḥ caraṇāt - śīlāt yoniviśeṣaprāptiḥ; na anuśayāt – iti cet !

'tanna; ' ' caraṇaśrutiḥ karmopalakṣaṇārthī' iti (hi) kāṣrṇājinirarācāryo manyate "kevalādācārāt ' sukha-duḥkhaprātyasambhavāt | sukha-duḥkhe hi ! puṇya-pāparūpakarmaphale || 9||

298. sukṛta - duṣkṛte eveti tu bādariḥ || 3-1-11|| 'puṇyaṃ karma ācarati, pāpa karma ācarati' iti karmaṇi carateḥ prayogāt,

300. saṃyamane tvanubhūyetareṣāmārohāvaroho tadgatidarśanāt ||3-1-13 || (pū)

syāt 1.

netyāha-

'sarve' iti śabdasya pretamātraviṣayatayā, 'ye vai ke cetyādi vākyena sarveṣāṃ candraprātyavagamāt, aniṣṭādikāriṇo'pi yātanānubhava (nantaraṃ candramasaṃ gatvā nivartante iti |

yadā paralokā saṃpūraṇavacanaṃ tṛtīyasthānasya dehaniṣpatteḥ pañcamā hutyanapekṣatāmavagamayati, tadā puruṣatvasampatteḥ paṃcamāhutyanapekṣatayā 'ya ime' ityupakramasyāpi pitṛyāṇasyeṣṭādikāri- viṣayatvāvagamakatayā tasya aniṣṭādikāriṇāmaprāptatvāt 'sarva' śabdasya iṣṭādikā ripreta viṣaya- tayā, 'ye vai ke ca' ityādivākyena sarveṣāṃ candranātyavagamāt aniṣṭādikāriṇaḥ na candrama-- saṃ gacchanti iti sidhyati ||

'iṣṭādi' śabdena iṣṭāpūrte ucyete | atra dehāraṃbhasya pañcamāhutyapekṣārūpaśrutyarthāpatiśca abhipretā || 12 ||
aviśiṣṭaiveti | pāpasya niṣphalatvaṃ syādityarthaḥ ||

candrārohāvarohau tu yātanāṃ yamaśāsane | anubhūyaivetareṣāmiti tadgatidarśanāt || '

'tu' śabda: śaṅkāṃ vyāvartayati | itareṣām – aniṣṭādikāriṇām, candrārohā- barohau, saṃyamane - yamaśāsane, tatprayuktayātanāḥ anubhūyaiva nānyathā |

303. tatrā'pi tadvyāpārādavirodhaḥ || 3-1-16|| (pū) teṣvapi saptasu yamājñayaiva gamanāt, avirodhaḥ || 16 ||

304. vidyā - karmaṇoriti tu prakṛtatvāt ||3-1-17|| (si) 'tu' śabda: pakṣavyāvṛttyarthaḥ | 'aniṣṭādikāriṇāmapi candraprāptirasti ' ityetat nopapadyate| kutaḥ ? – vidyā karmaṇoriti | vidyā karmaṇoḥ phalabhogārthatvāt, devayāna- piṭhyāṇayoḥ ||

etaduktaṃ bhavati – aniṣṭādikāriṇāṃ yathā vidyāvidhuratvāt devayānena pathā

lokeṣu sthāneṣu | yamasadanaprāptivacanāni purāṇādiṣu paṭhyante ; tannibandhanā iyaṃ śaṅkā | yamājñayeti | vyāpāraḥ praśāsanarūpaḥ | yamasadana - narakaprātyoḥ kramāt avirodhaḥ ityarthaḥ || 16 ||
bhogārthatvāditi | sambandhasāmānyaviṣayā ṣaṣṭhī | tādarthyaṃ ca tatphalabhogārthatvam |

311. anyādhiṣṭhite pūrvavadabhilāpāt || 3-1-24 || (si)

'avarohanto jīvāḥ vrīhyādibhāvena jāyante ' iti śrūyate- 'megho bhūtvā pravarṣati | ta iha vrīhiyavā oṣadhivanaspatayaḥ tilamāṣā 'iti jāyante''

iti |

te kim, anyairbhoktabhiḥ vrīhyādiśarīraiḥ adhiṣṭhitān brīhmādīn āśliṣyanti 1 uta te bhoktāraḥ vrīhmādiśarīrāḥ jāyante ? – iti vijaye,

'jāyante' iti vacanāt, 'deśe jāyate, manuṣyo jāyate ' itivat, vrīhyādi-

śarīrava-

iti prāpte, ucyate—anya viṣṭhite iti | jīvantareṇādhiṣṭhite vrīhyādiśarīre teṣāṃ saṃśleṣamātrameva |

kutaḥ 1 pūrvavadabhilāpāt – ākāśādimeghaparyantavat kevala tadbhāvābhi 'lāpāt | yatra hi ! bhoktvamabhipratam ; tatra tatsādhanabhūtaṃ karma abhilapyate ;
'ramaṇīyacaraṇāḥ....kapūyacaraṇāḥ'

312. aśuddhamiti cenna śabdāt | 3 - 1 - 25 || (si)
naitadasti yat anyādhiṣṭhite vrīhyādiśarīre saṃśleṣamātram | bhoktṛtva hetvabhāvāt na vrīhyādibhāvena janma - iti ; bhoktṛtva hetu sadbhāvāt | svargopabhogyaphalaṃ 'iṣṭādi * karmaiva, aśuddham – pāpamiśram, 'agnīṣomīyādi' hiṃsāyuktatvāt | hiṃsā ca,

'na hiṃsyāt sarvā bhūtāni '
iti niṣiddhatvāt pāpameva ||

?

saṃśleṣamātram iti || samāptatvāt – 'yāvatsampātamuṣitva|' iti vacanāt | madhye- prakaraṇamadhye | karmāntarābhāvāt karmāntaśravaṇābhāvāt | manuṣyādijanmanimittaṃ karmaiva, tadupayogitvāt vrīhmādiprāptihetuḥ ; na tu karmāntaramityabhiprāya: || vrīhyādiśarīrakāśca jīvāḥ, tallavanadaśāyāmevā'pakrāmanti; lavana-śoṣaṇa-peṣaṇa- pacanābhyavahārādyavasthāsu anuvartamānatvaṃ, na taccharīrāṇāṃ jīvānām ; kintu tatsaṃśliṣṭānāmeveti || 24 ||

na cātra, padāhavanīyādivat utsargāpavādabhāvaḥ sambhavati ; bhinnaviṣayatvāt | agnīṣomīya' hiṃsāvidhiḥ', hiṃsāyāḥ ṛtūpakārakatvaṃ bodhayati | 'na hiṃsyāt' iti tu, hiṃsāyāḥ pratyavāyaphalatvam |

athocyeta agnīṣomīyādiṣu vidhitaḥ

pravṛtteḥ, na tadviṣayaṃ niṣedhavidhiḥ

āskandati ; rāgaprāptaviṣayatvāt tasya iti |

naivam ; ihā'pi rāgaprāpteḥ aviśiṣṭatvāt |
' svargakāmo yajeta'

ityevamādau hi ! kāminaḥ kartavyatayā yāgādyupadeśāt yāgāde: svargasādhanatvamavagamya, phalarāgata eva yāgādau pravartate agnīṣomīyādiṣvapi teṣāṃ phalasādhanabhūtasya yāgāde- rupakārakatvaṃ śāstrādavagamya, rāgādeva pravartate |
laukikyāmapi hiṃsāyāṃ, kenacit pramāṇena hiṃsāyāḥ 'svasamīhita' sādhanatva -

mavagamya, rāgāt pravartate iti na kaścana viśeṣaḥ |

tathā nityeṣvapi karmasu

'sarvavarṇānāṃ svadharmānuṣṭhāne param aparimitaṃ sukham ' ityādivacanāt phalasādhanatvamavagamya, rāgādeva pravṛttiriti teṣāmapi aśuddhiyuktatvam |
ataḥ 'iṣṭādīnā' pāpamiśratvena aśuddhiyuktānāṃ svarge'nubhāvyaṃ phalaṃ svarge'nubhūya, hiṃsāṃśasya phalaṃ, vrīhyādisthāvara bhāvenā'nubhūyate | sthāvarabhāvaṃ ca pāpaphalaṃ smaranti- 'śarīrajaiḥ karmadoṣairyāti sthāvaratāṃ naraḥ'
iti | ataḥ vrīhyādibhāvena, bhogāya 'anuśayino' jāyante – iti cet !

tanna | kutaḥ 1 - śabdāt — agnīṣomīyādeḥ saṃjñapanasya, svargalokaprāptihetutayā hiṃsātvābhāvaśabdāt | paśohiṃ ! saṃjñapananimittāṃ svargalokaprāptiṃ vadantaṃ śabdamāmananti ' hiraṇyaśarīraḥ 'ūrdhvaḥ' svarga lokameti'
ityādikam | atiśayitābhyudaya sādhanabhūto vyāpāraḥ, alpaduḥkhado'pi na hiṃsā ; pratyuta rakṣaṇameva | tathā ca mantravarṇaḥ -
'na vā u vetantriya se na riṣyasi devān 'ideṣi' pathibhiḥ sugebhiḥ |
yatra yanti sukṛto nā'pi duṣkṛtastatra tvā devaḥ savitā dadhātu || ' iti | cikitsakaṃ ca tādātvikālpaduḥkhakāriṇamapi, rakṣakameva vadanti pūjayanti ca tajjñāḥ || 25 |
sarvavarṇānāmityādi āpastambasūtram | śrīviṣṇupurāṇe ca tattadāśramadharmānuṣṭhāyināṃ phalaviśeṣā uktāḥ | teṣāmiti | nityakarmādīnāmapi saṃvatsarapaśvālambhanādibhiḥ aśuddhi- yuktatvamityarthaḥ | atiśayiteti | duḥkhaheturapi paśusaṃjñapanaṃ na hiṃsetyāśayaḥ | tathā ceti | na kevalaṃ svargaloka gamanena ahiṃsātvakalpanam | kintu ahiṃsātvaṃ kaṇṭhoktaṃ ceti bhāvaḥ ||
cikitsakaṃ ceti | hisārakhe sati hi ! uktadoṣasambhavaḥ | hiṃsatvābhāvādeva dūrotsārito doṣaḥ | ayameva samīcīno duṣpradharṣaṇaḥ parihāraḥ | ataḥ śyenāmīṣomīya vaiṣamyaṃ cedameva ; alpaduḥkhado'pi atiśayitābhyudayasādhako vyāpāraḥ- rakṣaṇam | anarthodaka vyāpāraḥ - hiṃsetyarthaḥ || 25 ||

313. retaḥ sigyogo'tha ||3-1-26 || (si) ||
itaśca aupacārikaṃ vrīhyādijanmavacanam | vrīhyādibhāvavacanānantaram,
'yo yo dyannamatti ' yo retaḥ siścati, 'tadbhūya eva bhavati' iti retaḥsigbhāvaḥ anuśayināṃ śrūyamāṇaḥ, yathā tadyogamātraṃ pratipādayati tadvat vrīhyādibhāvo'pītyarthaḥ || 26 ||

314. yoneḥ śarīram || 3-1-27 || (si)
yoniprāpteḥ paścādeva, "anuśayināṃ śarīraprāptiḥ, tatraiva sukhaduḥkhopabhoga- sadbhāvāt | tataḥ prāk, ākāśādiprāptiprabhṛti tadyoga' mātramevetyarthaḥ || 27 || iti anyādhiṣṭhitādhikaraṇam || 6 ||
iti śrībhagavadrāmānujaviracite śrīśārīrakamīmāsābhāpye
tṛtīyasyā'dhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ || 1 ||

anantaram ityantena ' atha ' śabdo vyākhyātaḥ | tadbhūya eva bhavati | ityādhyāhāraḥ | bhūyaḥ — paścāt tadbhavati – retaḥ sig bhavati – retaḥ sigyogī bhavatītyarthaḥ | tattadbhāvasya aupacārikatvaprakaraṇam avicchinnamiti bhāvaḥ || 26 ||

315. sandhye sṛṣṭirāha hi ! || 3-2-1 || (pū)

evaṃ-karmānurūpagamanāgamanajanmādiyogena jāgrato jīvasya duḥkhitvaṃ khyāpitam | idānīm–asya svapnāvasthā parīkṣyate | svamamadhikṛtya śrayate-

'na tatra rathā na rathayogā na panthāno bhavanti | atha rathān rathayogān pathaḥ sṛjate | na tatrā''nandā mudaḥ pramudo bhavanti | athānandān mudaḥ pramudaḥ sṛjate |
na tatra veśāntāḥ puṣkariṇyaḥ sravantyo bhavanti | atha 'veśāntān' puṣkariṇyaḥ | sravantyaḥ sṛjate | sahi kartā'

iti ||

tatra saṃśayaḥ – kimiyaṃ rathādisṛṣṭi: 'jīvenaiva' kriyate ? āhokhita īśvareṇa 1 -

iti ||

kiṃ yuktam ? – ' sandhye sṛṣṭiḥ jīvena ' - iti |
kutaḥ 1 sandhyaṃ – svamasthānamucyate ;
' sandhyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ svamasthānam
iti vacanāt | sā tu 'jīvenaiva' kriyate ; 'sṛjate ', ' sa hi kartā' ityāha hi | svamaka jīva eva tatra pratīyate || 1 ||

316. nirmātāraṃ caike putrādayaśca || 3-2-2 || (pū) kiṃ ca- enaṃ jīvaṃ, svame kāmānāṃ nirmātāram, eke śākhinaḥ adhīyate—

ya eṣa supteṣu jāgarti kāmaṃ kāmaṃ puruṣo nirmimāṇaḥ ' iti | putrādayaśca tatra, kāmyamānatayā 'kāma' śabdena nirdiśyante na icchāmātram' | pūrvatra hi ! -
'sarvān kāmāṃśchandataḥ prārthayasva '

śatāyuṣaḥ putra-pautrān vṛṇīṣva '

iti putrādaya eva kāmāḥ prakṛtāḥ " ||

ataḥ rathādīn jīvaḥ svapne 'sṛjati' | jīvasya ca satyasaṅkalpatvaṃ prajāpativākye śrutam | ataḥ upakaraṇādyabhāve'pi sṛṣṭirupapadyate || 2 ||

iti prapte, abhidhīyate-

317- māyāmātraṃ tu kātsnyenā'nabhivyaktasvarūpatvāt ||3-2-3|| (si)

nirmātāramiti paramātmaka śrutyantare śrūyate' ityāśaṅkāyāmidaṃ sūtram | putrādayaśceti jīvaliṅgaṃ darśayati |
jīvasya ceti | aparikarasya jīvasya kathaṃ sṛṣṭiḥ ? ityatra | ' sandhye sṛṣṭiḥ nirmātaram' iti sūtradvayaṃ pūrvapakṣaviṣayam || 2 ||

.

māyāmātram — āścaryarūpameva | anāścaryarūpaṃ na kiṃcidapītyarthaḥ ||
ca - svaśākhāyāṃ paṭhanti ' puruṣo nirmimāṇaḥ ' iti || ]
'kāmānāṃ svapnakāle syānnirmātā jīva eva tu | vadantyeke śākhinaśca putrādīn 'kāma' śabdataḥ ||'
' svapne yat sṛṣṭamāmnātaṃ tadidaṃ brahmaṇā kṛtam | āścaryabhūtaṃ jīve na satyasaṅkalpatā tadā || '

vyāvartayati | svame ratha- puṣkariṇyādyarthajātaṃ, māyāmātraṃ- māyā 'śabdo hi ! āścaryavācī ;
janakasya kule jātā devamāyeva nirmitā' ityādiṣu ' tathā darśanāt ' atrāpi,
na tatra rathā na rathayogā na panthānaḥ '
sakaletara puruṣānu bhāvyatayā na bhavantītyarthaḥ |
atha rathān rathayogān pathaḥ sṛjate '

' tu' śabda: pakṣaṃ

parama puruṣasṛṣṭamityarthaḥ |

strapradṛganubhāvyatayā tatkāla mātrāvasānān sṛjate iti, āścaryarūpatvamevāsse |

evaṃvidhāścarya' rūpā' sṛṣṭiḥ, satyasaṅkalpasya paramapuruṣasyaivopapadyate ; na jīvasya | "tasya satya' saṅkalpatvādiyuktasyā'pi, saṃsāradaśāyāṃ kātsnrtsnyena anabhivyaktasvarūpatvāt, na jīvasya ' tathā vidhāścarya' sṛṣṭirupapadyate |

' kāmaṃ kāmaṃ puruṣo nirmimāṇaḥ '
iti ca, paramapuruṣameva nirmātāramāha |

' ya eṣu supteṣu jāgarti'
' tadeva śukraṃ tad brahma tadevāmṛtamucyate |

tasmi~llokāḥ śritāḥ sarve tadu nātyeti kaścana || ' iti upakramopasaṃhārayoḥ paramapuruṣa sādhāraṇasvabhāvapratīteḥ |
atha veśāntān puṣkariṇyaḥ sravantyaḥ sṛjate | sa hi kartā' iti ca tayā śrutyā aikārthyāt paramapuruṣameva kartāramāha || 3 ||
svābhāvikaṃ cet jīvasya apahatapāpmatvādikaṃ kutaḥ tat na abhivyajyate ?

-ityata āha-

318. parābhidhyānāttu tirohitaṃ tato hyasya
bandhaviparyayau || 3-1-4|| (si)

'tu' śabda: śaṅkāvyāvṛtyarthaḥ | parābhidhyānāt - parama puruṣasaṅkalpāt, asya jīvasya, svābhāvikaṃ rūpaṃ tirohitam | anādi karmaparamparayā kṛtāparādhasya hi ! asya, svābhāvikaṃ kalyāṇarūpaṃ paramapuruṣaḥ tirodhāpayati | tataḥ tatsaṅkalpādeva hi ! asya jīvasya, bandha mokṣau śrutau-
'yadā ḍeyeṣa etasminnaśye'nātmye'nirukte'nilayane'bhayaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ vindate | atha nomayaṃ gato bhavati'
'yadā hayevaiṣa etasminnudaramantaraṃ kurute | atha tasya bhayaṃ bhavati' 'eṣa hayevā''nandayāti'
'bhīṣā'smādvātaḥ pavate'

319. dehayogādvā so'pi || 3-2-5 || (si)
so'pi –'tirobhāvo'pi', dehayogadvāreṇa vā bhavati ; sūkṣmācicchaktiyogadvāreṇa vā sṛṣṭikāle, dehāvasthena acidvastunā saṃyogādbhavati | pralayakāle nāmarūpa- vibhāgānarhātisūkṣmācidvastuyogāt | ataḥ anabhivyaktasvarūpatvāt, svapne jīvaḥ— na rathādīn saṅkalpamātreṇa sraṣṭuṃ śaknoti
tasmillokāḥ śritāḥ sarve tadu nātyeti kaścana'
iti 'sarveṣu supteṣu jāgaraṇaṃ, sarvalokāśrayatvam' ityādayo hi ! " paramapuruṣa ' syaiva' sambhavanti | ataḥ jīvānām alpālpakarmānuguṇaphalānubhavārthaṃ tāvanmātrakālāvasānān tadekānubhāvyānarthān utpādayati || 5 ||

320. sūcakaśca hi śruterācakṣate ca tadvidaḥ || 3-2-6||
itaśca 'svāpnā arthā: ' na jīvasaṅkalpapūrvakāḥ

yataḥ svapnaḥ, 'abhyudayā-

'nabhyudayayoḥ sūcakaḥ śruteravagamyate-

yogādveti | bhagavatsaṅkalpasya dvārabhūtaṃ prakṛtyādikamityarthaḥ | sṛṣṭīti | 'vā' śabdaḥ na tulyavikalpavācī ; api tu vyavasthita vikalpavā cītyabhiprāyaḥ | acicchaktīti śakti- śabdena bhagavadapṛthaksaddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ vivakṣitam | ata iti | alpālpakarmānubhavārtham asthira- tattadvastusṛṣṭiḥ ityarthaḥ || 5 ||
sūcakaśceti | jīvasya svāpnasṛṣṭimātropayogisatyasaṅkalpatvādyāvirbhāvaḥ tadānīṃ syāditi

'tirobhāvo guṇānāṃ tu sṛṣṭau dehasya yogataḥ | pralaye prakṛteryogamātreṇaiva tathā bhavet || [sūtrārthaḥ | hiyataḥ, sūcakaśca svapnaḥ śubhāśubhayoḥ jīvasambandhinoḥ bhaviṣya- darthayoḥ sūcanakāryamapi bhavati | ataḥ svāpnortha īśvarasṛṣṭa eva | sūcakatvaṃ ca śruteḥ śrutivākyā- davagamyate | tadvidaḥ - svapnādhyāyavidaḥ | ācakṣate ca svapnaṃ śubhāśubhasūcakaṃ varṇayanti ca || śubhādi sūcakaḥ svapna iti śrutyā hi dṛśyate | tadvidāṃ vacanāccaiva na jīvasyā

1 tirobhāvaḥ • svāpnāthaḥ - na.

'yadā karmasu kāmyepu striyaṃ svapneṣu paśyati |

samṛddhiṃ tatra jānīyāt tasmin svapnanidarśane ||'

iti ;

'atha svapne (māḥ) puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇadantaṃ paśyati | sa enaṃ hanti' 'ityādeśca' | svapnādhyāyavidaśca svapnaṃ śubhāśubhayoḥ sūcakamācakṣate | sūcakatvaṃ ca svasaṅka- lpāyattasya nopapadyate | tathā ca aśubhasya aniṣṭatvāt ' śubhasya sūcakameva sṛṣṭvā paśyet | ataḥ ' svapne sṛṣṭiḥ īśvareṇaiva kṛtā || 6 ||

iti sandhyādhikaraṇam || 1 ||

tadabhāvaḥ dhikaraṇam || 3-2-2 ||
321. tadabhāvo nāḍīṣu tacchruterātmani ca || 3-2-7|| (si)

idānīṃ suṣuptisthānaṃ parīkṣyate | idamāmnāyate—
atha 'yatraita' tsuptaḥ samastaḥ 'samprasannaḥ' svapnaṃ na vijānāti ; āsu tadā nāḍīṣu supto bhavati'

.

iti | tathā,

atha yadā suṣupto bhavati yadā na kasyacana' veda, hitā nāma nāḍyo dvāsaptatisahasrāṇi hṛdayāt purītatamabhipratiṣṭhante, tābhiḥ pratyavasṛpya purītati śete'

iti | tathā,

'yatraitatpuruṣaḥ svapiti nāma, satā somya tadā sampanno bhavati ' iti | evaṃ, nāḍyaḥ purītat brahma ca suṣuptisthānatvena śrūyate ||

kim eṣāṃ vikalpaḥ 1 samuccayo vā 1 - iti viśaye,

iti prāpte, ucyate - ' tadabhāvaḥ' - iti | tadabhāvaḥ – svapnābhāvaḥ, suṣuptiḥ, nāḍīṣu purītati ātmani ca bhavati | 'epa' sthānānāṃ samuccayaḥ - ityarthaḥ |
kutaḥ 1 tacchruteḥ– trayāṇāṃ sthānatva' śruteḥ | na ca kāryabhedena samuccaye saṃbhavati, pākṣikabādhagarbhavikalpo nyāyyaḥ | 'sambhavati ca' prāsāda - khaṭvā - paryaṅkavat nāḍyādīnāṃ kāryabhedaḥ | tatra nāḍī-purītatau prāsāda - khaṭvāsthānīyau ; 'brahma tu' paryaṅkasthānīyam | ataḥ brahmaiva sākṣāt suṣuptisthānam || 7 ||

321. ataḥ prabodho'smāt || 3-2-8 || (si)
yataḥ brahmaiva sākṣātsuṣuptisthānam, ataḥ asmād brahmaṇaḥ, eṣāṃ jīvānāṃ prabodhaḥ śrayamāṇaḥ, upapadyate ;
'sata: ' āgamya' na viduḥ sata āgacchāmahe'

ityādiṣu || 8 ||

karmānusmṛtiśabdavidhyadhikaraṇam || 3-2-3 ||
323. sa eva tu karmānusmṛtiśabdavidhibhyaḥ || 3-2-9 || (si)

kiṃ suṣupta eva prabodha' samaye' uttiṣṭhati ? uta anyaḥ - iti saṃśaye,
asya sakalopādhivinirmuktasya, brahmaṇi sampannasya,
śrācīnaśarīrendriyādisambandhābhāvāt anyaḥ - iti prāpte,

muktā'davilakṣaṇatvena, '

anusmṛtirapi - 'ya evā'haṃ suptaḥ, sa eva 'prabuddho 'smi ' - iti | śabdo'pi - 'suṣuptaprabuddhaḥ sa eva ' - iti darśayati ;

|

'ta iha vyāghro vā siṃho vā vṛko vā 'varāho vā kīṭo vā pataṅgo vā deśo vā maśako vā yadyadbhavanti tadābhavanti" iti | vidhayaśca mokṣārthāḥ, suṣuptasya muktatve anarthakāḥ syuḥ | na ca - asau sarvopādhivinirmuktaḥ āvirbhūtasvarūpaḥ ; 'tadyatraitat suṣuptaḥ'

iti 'suṣuptaṃ prakṛtya,

'nāha khalvayamevaṃ sampratyātmānaṃ jānātyayamahasmīti, no evemāni bhūtāni, vināśamevā'pīto bhavati nā'hamatra bhogyaṃ paśyāmi'

iti vacanāt | 'muktasya ca',
'paraṃ jyotirūpasampadya svena rūpeṇā'bhiniṣpadyate '

'sa tatra paryeti jakṣat krīḍan ramamāṇaḥ'

' sa svarāḍ bhavati | tasya sarveṣu lokeṣu kāmacāro bhavati'
'sarva ha paśyaḥ paśyati sarvamāpnoti sarvaśaḥ '
iti sarvajñatvādi śrūyate |

ataḥ, subutaḥ saṃsaranneva 'āyasta' sarvakaraṇaḥ, jñāna- bhogādya śaktaḥ, 'viśrama' sthānaṃ paramātmānam upasampadya āśvastaḥ, punarbhogāya uttiṣṭhati || 9 ||
iti karmānusmṛtiśabda vidhyadhikaraṇam || 3 ||

324. mugdhe'rdhasampatiḥ pariśeṣāt || 3-2 - 10 || (si)

nimittavairūpyāt ākāravairūpyācca, na suṣupti-maraṇe | nimittaṃ hi | mūrcchāyāḥ, abhighātādiḥ | 'pāriśeṣyāt', maraṇāyārddhasampattiḥ - mūrcchā | maraṇaṃ hi sarvaprāṇa- dehasambandhoparatiḥ | sūkṣma prāṇadehasambandhāvasthitiḥ - mūcchā || 10 ||

iti mugdhādhikaraṇam || 4 ||

ubhayaliṅgādhikaraṇam || 3-2-5 ||

325. na sthānato'pi parasyobhayaliṅgaṃ sarvatra hi ||3-2 - 11|| (si)

doṣadarśanāt vairāgyodayāya, jīvasya avasthāviśeṣāḥ nirūpitāḥ |
idānīṃ brahmaprāptitṛṣṇa jananāya, 'prāpyasya brahmaṇaḥ' nirdoṣatva kalyāṇaguṇātma- katvapratipādanāya, ārabhate |

tatra 'jāgara'svapna suṣupti-mugdhyutkrāntiṣu sthāneṣu tattatsthānaprayuktāḥ jīvasya ye

doṣeti | dvitīyapādapratipādye, paramātmano nirdoṣatva-kalyāṇaguṇākaratve svapna suṣuptinirūpaṇena, paramātmanaḥ svāpnavicitrārthasraṣṭratvaṃ suṣuptisthānatvaṃ ca ākāradvayaṃ pratipāditaṃ bhavatīti, paramātmaguṇasiddheḥ, asmin pāde tannirūpaṇam | mūrcchādi- nirūpaṇaṃ tu pūrvapāde niravakāśatvāt, suṣuptinirūpaṇa sāpekṣatvācca, atra kṛtam | ārabhata iti | sūtrakāraḥ iti śeṣaḥ |
sthāneṣu avasthāsu ityarthaḥ | subutyādisthāneṣu sthitasya jīvaśarīrakasya parasya, jīvagatāḥ apuruṣārthaḥ kiṃ prasajyante ? na ? iti cintyate | dehādisambandhasya apuruṣārthahetutvaṃ, kiṃ tadvastusvabhāvaprayuktam ? uta karmaviśeṣakṛtam ? iti | kiṃ paramātmaviṣayā ubhayaliṅgaśrutiḥ, apuruṣārthasya vastusvabhāvaprayuktatāṃ vyudasya, karmanibandhanatām avagamayituṃ prabhavati ? na ? iti |

'parasya brahmaṇo doṣaḥ sthānato'pi na vidyate | yato nirdoṣa kalyāṇaguṇatobhayaliṅgatā ||

doṣāḥ te tadantaryāmiṇaḥ parasya brahmaṇo'pi tatra tatrā'vasthitasya santi ? na ? iti vicāryate |

kiṃ yuktam ? santi - iti |

śrīmāṇyam 3.2.5]

kutaḥ 1 tattadavasthaśarīre avasthānāt ||

nanu -

' sambhogaprāptiriti cenna vaiśeṣyāt'
sthityadanābhyāṃ ca '

ityādiṣu, parasya akarmavaśyatvena doṣābhāva uktaḥ | tat katham akarmavaśyasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ, tattatsthānasambandhāt doṣa ucyate ?

itthamucyate— 'karmāṇyapi dehasambandhamāpādayanti, apuruṣārthajananāni bhavanti'

iti,

'dehayogādvā'

ityatroktam | tacca dehasambandhasya apuruṣārthatvena bhavati | itarathā, karmāṇyeva duḥkhaṃ janayi vyanti kiṃ dehasambandhena 1 | ataḥ akarmavaśyatve satyapi, nānāvidhāśucideha- sambandhaḥ apuruṣārtha eva ||
ataḥ, 'tanniyamanārtha' svecchayā 'tatpraveśe'pi, apuruṣārthasambandhaḥ avarja- nīyaḥ | pūya - śoṇitādimajjanaṃ hi ! svecchākāritamapi apuruṣārtha eva |

ataḥ yadyapi, jagadekakāraṇa 'sarvajñatvādi' kalyāṇaguṇākaraṃ ca brahma; tathāpi,

yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan '
' ya ātmani tiṣṭhan '

tattaditi | tacchabdaḥ jāgarādiparaḥ | jāgarādihetutā śarīrasyā'vasthā | taccetyādi | yathā rājakopasya vadhādihetutve'pi dvārabhūtakhaṅgaprahārasya vadhaṃ prati sākṣād hetutvaṃ ; tathehāpi dvārabhūtadeha sambandha: apuruṣārthaḥ ityabhiprāya: ||
atastaditi | karmavaśyatvādṛte svecchayā praveśe'pi, apuruṣārtha sambandhaḥ vastusvabhāvā- yattatvāt, avarjanīyaḥ | yathā ananyādhīnasya svayameva asinā svacchandato'pi śiraśchedaḥ apuruṣārtha eva tadvat - [ ityāśayaḥ | |

'yaścakṣuṣi tiṣṭhan ' yo retasi tiṣṭhan '

ityādivacanāt tatra tatrā'vasthitasya tattatmambandharūpāḥ apuruṣārthāḥ santi - iti ||

evaṃ prāpte, pracakṣmahe – 'na sthānato'pi parasya ' iti | na pṛthivyātmādi- sthānato'pi parasya brahmaṇaḥ, apuruṣārthagandhaḥ sambhavati |

kutaḥ ? – ubhayaliṅgaṃ sarvatra hi | yataḥ sarvatra śruti smṛtiṣu paraṃ brahma, ubhaya- liṅgam – ubhayalakṣaṇamabhidhīyate | nirasta nikhila doṣatva-kalyāṇaguṇākaratvalakṣaṇopeta- mityarthaḥ |

'apahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyurviśoko vijighatso'pipāsaḥ satyakāmaḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ'
'samasta kalyāṇa guṇātmako'sau svaśakti' leśoddhṛtabhūtasargaḥ ' ' tejobalaiva mahāvabodhasūtrīvaiśacyā diguṇaikarāśiḥ |' piraḥ parāṇāṃ sakalā na yatra kuśādayaḥ santi parāvareśe' ? 'samayarahitaṃ viyākhyaṃ paramaṃ padam |'
ityādiśrutismṛtisthaya ubhayalakṣaṇaṃ hi ! brahma avagatam || 11 ||
326. bhedāditi cenna pratyekamatadvacanāt || 3-2-12 || (si) ..

...

yathā jīvasya prajāpativākyātragatāpahatapāpmatvādyubhayaliṅgasyā'pi, devādi- dehayogarūpāvasthābhedāt apuruṣārthayogaḥ ; tathā antaryāmiṇaḥ parasyā'pi svato'pahata- pāpmatvādyubhayaliṅgasya, tattaddevādiśarīrayogarūpāvasthābhedāt apuruṣārthayogaḥ avarjanīyaḥ- - iti cet !,

' yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan'

sata ātmā'ntaryāmyamṛtaḥ'

ityatroktam |

nanu - svecchayā kurvato'pi tattadvastu' svabhāvāyattā' puruṣārthasambandho'varjanīyaḥ ityuktam !

yatheti | ubhayaliṅgatvaśrutiḥ jīvasyāpyasti | tatra avasthābhedena hi tannirvāha: | tadvat paramātmano'pītyarthaḥ | pratiparyāyamiti | 'amṛta' śabdasya sakṛduktiścet, nityatva - paratvaṃ syāt ; asakṛdāvṛttatvāt pratiparyāya muktatattadva mtugata doṣa viśeṣa sambandharā hityameva, tadabhipretamityarthaḥ | jīvasya tviti | tirodhānādeḥ śrutisiddhatvāt jīvaviṣayā ubhayaliṅga- tvaśrutiḥ saṅkocitā | paramātmanastu anyādhīnatirodhānādiśrutyabhāvāt tadviṣayā ubhayaliṅgatva-- śrutiḥ, asaṅkucittavṛttirityarthaḥ |

na hīti | vastusvabhāvāyattatve sahi hi | apuruṣārthatvaprasaṅgaḥ | tadeva nāstītyarthaḥ | acihnastvapīti | na kevalaṃ kṣetrajñasyaiva apuruṣārthasambandhaḥ aupādhikaḥ | acetanasyā'pi

karmavaśyānāṃ tu karmasvabhāvānuguṇyena, paramapuruṣasaṅkalpāt ekameva vastu, kālabhedena puruṣa bhedena ca ' sukhāya duḥkhāya ca bhavati | vastusvarūpa prayukta' tu tādrūpye, sarva sarvadā sarvasya sukhāyaiva duḥkhāyaiva vā syāt ! na caiva dṛśyate | tathā coktam-

|

327. api caivameke | 3-2 - 13 || (si)

328. arūpavedava hi ! tat pradhānatvāt || 3-1-14 || (si) devādiśarīrānupraveśe 'tena rūpeṇa yuktamapi, arūpavadeva tat - brahma, rūparahita- tulyameva | jīvavat śarīritvanibandhanaṃ karmavaśyatvam, asya na vidyate ityarthaḥ | kutaḥ ? 'nirvāhakatvena pradhānatvāt |
'ākāśo ha vai nāma-rūpayornirvahitā, te yadantarā tad brahma' iti sarvānupraveśe'pi nāma-rūpakāryāsparśena nāma-rūpayorniyameva brahmaṇaḥ pratipāda- yati |

ittham ; yathā jīvasya tattajanyasukha- 'duḥkha bhāvena' tattadrūpasambandhaḥ ; tathā tadabhāvāt parasya' 'arūpavatvam | vidhi - niṣedhaśāstrāṇyapi 'karmavaśyameva adhikurvanti |

nāma-rūpakāryāsparśeneti taṃ yadantararā' ityasyāthamāha |

.

'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma'

ityādibhiḥ, nirviśeṣaprakāśaikasvarūpa brahma avagamyate | anyattu sarvajñatva-satyasaṅkalpatva- jagatkāraṇatva-sarvāntarātmatva- satyakāmatvādikam

3. neti neti

'yathā ca, ' 6

'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma'

330. āha ca tanmātram || 3-2-16 || (si)
kiṃ ca- satyaṃ jñānamanantam' ityādivākyaṃ brahmaṇaḥ prakāśasvarūpatāmātraṃ prati- pādayati; nā'nyat satyasaṅkalpatvādikaṃ vākyāntarāvagataṃ 'niṣedhati' | 'neti neti' iti ca 'niṣedhasya viṣayaḥ' anantarameva vakṣyate || 16 ||

331. darśayati cā'tho api smaryate ||3-2-17|| (si) darśayati ca vedāntavākyagaṇaḥ' kalyāṇaguṇākaratvaṃ nirastanikhiladoṣatvaṃ ca- na guṇabhūtam ; prādhānyena śrutipratipannatvāt tata eva sarvajñatvādayo'pi svīkriyantām | vyāptivirodho'pi nā'sti; guṇabhūtajñānasyaiva jñānādyanāśrayatvadarśanāt | ataḥ sarvamupapannamiti bhāvaḥ || 15 ||
nā'nyaditi | itaraniṣedhavyavacchedaparo mātraśabda ityarthaḥ || 16 ||
athaśabdaḥ kārthaparaḥ | vedāntavākyagaṇa iti | "niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ ,"

brahma hyubhayaliṅgaṃ tu vadataśca viśeṣataḥ ||

' vākyārthāvaiyarthyāṃta.
4 niṣedha - ca. u.

'tamīśvarāṇāṃ paramaṃ maheśvaraṃ taṃ daivatānāṃ paramaṃ ca daivatam |' 'sakāraṇa karaṇādhipādhipo na cā'sya kaśvijanitā na cādhipaḥ | ' 'na tasya kārya karaṇaṃ ca vidyate na tatsamaścābhyadhikaśca dṛśyate |
parāsya śaktirvividhaiva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñāna bala-kriyā ca || ' 'yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvavid yasya jñānamayaṃ tapaḥ ' 'bhīṣā'smād vātaḥ pavate bhīpodeti sūryaḥ | ' ' sa eko brahmaṇa ānanda:'
'yato vāco nivartante aprāpya manasā saha |
ānandaṃ brahmaṇo vidvān na vibheti kutaścaneti || ' 'niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ śāntaṃ niravadyaṃ nirañjanam ' ityādi | smaryate ca -
'yo māmajamanādiṃ ca vetti lokamaheśvaram |' 'viṣṭabhyā'hamidaṃ kṛtsnamekāṃśena sthito jagat | ' 'mayā'dhyakṣeṇa prakṛtiḥ sūyate sacarācaram |
hetunā'nena kaunteya jagaddhi parivartate ||'
'uttamaḥ puruṣastvanyaḥ paramātmetyudāhṛtaḥ |
yo lokatrayamāviśya bibhartyavyaya īśvaraḥ ||' 'sarvajñaḥ sarvakṛt sarvaśaktijñānabalarddhimān |
anyūnacāpyavṛddhiśca svādhīno'nādimān vaśī ||' 'kumatandrībhayakrodhakāmādibhirasaṃyutaḥ |
niravadyaḥ paraḥ prāpte 'niradhiṣṭho'kṣaraḥ kramaḥ ||' ityādi | ataḥ sarvatrāvasthitasyāpi brahmaṇaḥ ubhayaliṅgatvāt tattatsthānaprayuktā doṣāḥ na paraṃ brahma spṛśanti || 17 ||

332. ata eva copamā sūryakādivat || 3-2-18 || (siṃ)
yato nānāvidheṣu sthāneṣu ' sthitasyā'pi parasya brahmaṇaḥ, na tatprayuktadoṣabhāktvam ; ata eva jaladarpaṇādiprativimbitasūryādivat 'paramātmā tatra tatrāvasthito'pi nirdoṣa iti, śāstraṣu upamā kriyate-

tathāsssmaiko hyanekastha jalādhāreṣvivāṃśumān |' eka eva hi | bhūtātmā bhūte bhūte vyavasthitaḥ |
ekadhā bahudhā caiva dṛśyate jalacandravat ||'

ityādiṣu || 18 ||

333. ambuvadagrahaṇāt tu na tathātvam || 3-2-19 || (pū)

' tu ' śabda : codyaṃ dyotayati | 'ambuvat' iti saptamyantād vatiḥ | ambu- darpaṇādiṣu yathā sūryamukhādayo gṛhyante ; na tathā pṛthivyādiṣu sthāneṣu paramātmā gṛhyate |

tu' |

334. vṛddhi hāsabhāktvamantarbhāvādubhayasāmañjasyādevaṃ darśanācca || 3-2-20 || (si)
pṛthivyādisthānāntarbhāvāt sthāninaḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ svarūpato guṇataśca pṛthivyādisthānagatavṛddhi hrāsādidoṣabhāktvamātraṃ, sūryādidṛṣṭāntena nivartyate | kathamidamavagamyate 1

ubhayasāmañjasyādevam – ubhayadṛṣṭāntasāmañjasyāt evamiti niścīyate |

" a. kāśamekaṃ hi ! yathā ghaṭādiṣu pṛthagbhavet '
' jalādhāreṣvivāṃśumān'

iti doṣavatsu anekeṣu vastuṣu vastutaḥ avasthitasya ākāśasya, vastutaḥ anavasthitasya aṃśumataśca 'ubhayasya dṛṣṭānta 'syopādānaṃ' hi !, paramātmanaḥ pṛthivyādigatadoṣabhāktatva- nivartanamātre pratipādye, samañjasaṃ bhavati | ghaṭa- karakādiṣu yathā vṛddhi-hāsabhākṣu pṛthaka pṛthak saṃyujyamānamapi ākāśa, vṛddhi-hāsādidoṣaiḥ na 'spṛśyate'; yathā ca jalādhāreṣu viṣameṣu dṛśyamānaḥ ' aṃśumān, tadgatavṛddhi-hāsādibhiḥ na spṛśyate ; tathā ayaṃ paramātmā, pṛthivyādiṣu nānākāreṣu' acetaneṣu 'cetaneṣu ca ' sthitaḥ, tattadgatavṛddhi- hrāsādidoṣaiḥ aspṛṣṭaḥ | sarvatra vartamāno'pi eka eva aspṛṣṭadopagandhaḥ 'kalyāṇa- guṇā' kara eva ||

" etaduktaṃ bhavati - yathā - jalādiṣu vastuto'navasthitasya aṃśumataḥ hetvabhāvāt jalādidoṣānabhiṣvaṅgaḥ; tathā - pṛthivyādiṣvavasthitasyā'pi paramātmanaḥ, doṣapratyanīkā- kāratayā doṣahetvabhāvāt na doṣasambandhaḥ - iti ||

darśanācca - 1 dṛśyate caiva sarvātmanā sādharmyābhāve'pi vivakṣitāṃśa sādharmyāt dṛṣṭāntopādānam - " siṃha iva māṇavakaḥ' ityādau | ataḥ, svabhāvato nirasta- nikhilājñānādidoṣagandhasya samasta kalyāṇaguṇākarasya, pṛthivyādisthānato'pi na doṣasambhavaḥ || 20 ||
pāramādhye tu jalasūryā dadṛśanto na ghaṭate | tasmāt ubhayadṛṣṭāntānuyāyī ākāraḥ vṛddhi- hrāsādidoṣabhāvaḥ svīkārya : ityarthaḥ ||
etaditi | dṛntaye'pi, deśa-kāla-svabhāva saṃnikarṣatraya samudāyābhāvaḥ doṣāsparśe anuyāyī hetuḥ | saṃnikarṣatraya samudāya: dopasaṃsparśahetuḥ | deśaviprakarṣasyodāharaṇaṃ jalasūryādi | svabhāvaviprakarṣasya ākāśaḥ | deśaviprakarṣavat svabhāvaviprakarṣo'pi doṣāsparśa heturiti darśayituṃ dṛṣṭāntayopādānamityarthaḥ || 20 ||

svāpādanaṃ - tha. 5 nānāprakāreṣu - da.

atha syāt-
'dve vāva brahmaṇo rūpe mṛrta caivāmūrta ca ' iti prakṛtya, samastaṃ sthūla sūkṣmarūpaṃ prapañcaṃ brahmaṇo rūpatvena parāmṛśya,
'tasya ha vaitasya puruṣasya rūpaṃ yathā 'māhārajanaṃ' vāsaḥ' ityādinā ākāraviśeṣaṃ cā'bhidhāya,
'athāta ādeśo neti neti | na hyetasmāditi netyanyat paramasti' iti, sarva prakṛtaṃ brahmaṇaḥ prakāram 'iti' śabdena parāmṛśya, tat sarvaṃ pratiṣidhya, 'sarvaviśeṣādhiṣṭhānaṃ sanmātrameva brahma viśeṣāstu evaṃvidhaṃ svasvarūpamajānatā brahmaṇā kalpitāḥ' iti darśayati | ataḥ, katham ubhayaliṅgatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ 1 - iti; ' atrāha '

335. prakṛtaitāvattvaṃ hi ! pratiṣedhati tato bravīti ca bhūyaḥ || 3-2-21 || (si)

naitadupapadyate - yat brahmaṇaḥ prakṛtaviśeṣavattvaṃ, 'neti neti' iti pratiṣidhyate iti | tathā sati bhrānti' jalpitāyamānatvāt | na hi ! brahmaṇo 'viśeṣaṇatayā pramāṇāntarā- prajñātaṃ sarva tadviśeṣaṇatvena upadiśya, punaḥ tadeva anunmattaḥ 'pratiṣedhati' |
yadyapi, nirdiśyamāneṣu 'kecana padārthāḥ " pramāṇāntaraprasiddhāḥ; tathāpi, teṣāṃ brahmaṇaḥ prakāratvam, 'aprajñātameva ' | itareṣāṃ tu svarūpaṃ brahmaṇaḥ prakāratvaṃ ca 'ajñātam " | ataḥ teṣāmanuvādāsambhavāt atraivopadiśyante | ataḥ tanniṣedho nopapadyate | yasmādevaṃ,
mahārajanam – kusumbhaḥ | 'ataḥ nopapadyate' ityantena, sūtrastha 'hi' śabdābhi

tasmāditi netyanyat paramasti | atha nāmadheyaṃ satyasya satyamiti |

vai

teṣāmeṣa satyam'

prāṇā va satyam,

iti |

ayamarthaḥ – " iti na' iti yad brahma pratipāditaṃ, tasmādetasmāt anyadvastu paraṃ nāsti | brahmaṇo'nyat svarūpato guṇataśca utkṛṣṭaṃ nāstītyarthaḥ | tasya ca brahmaṇaḥ, 'satyasya satyam' iti nāmadheyam | tasya ca nirvacanam " prāṇācai' satyaṃ, 'teṣāmeṣa' satyam' iti | 'prāṇa' śabdena prāṇasāhacaryāt jīvāḥ parāmṛśyante | "te tāvat satyam ; viyadādivat svarūpānyathābhāvarūpapariṇāmābhāvāt | ' teṣāmeṣa 'satyam - tebhyo'pi eṣa paramapuruṣaḥ satyam; jīvānāṃ karmānuguṇyena jñānasaṅkoca - "vikāsa" vidyete; paramapuruṣasya tu apahatapāpmanaḥ, tau na vidyete | ataḥ tebhyo'pi eṣaḥ satyam ||
pretārtha uktaḥ | naiva naivamiti | uktaprakāramātra viśiṣṭatayā brahmaṇa iyattā kṛtā | sā'pi prakāra eveti sā 'iti' śabdena parāmṛśyate ityarthaḥ ||
anantara śabdena 'tataḥ ' śabdo vyākhyātaḥ | 'bhūyaḥ' śabdena bhūyiṣṭhatvamucyate | ayamarthaḥ ityārambhaḥ, 'na hyetasmāt ityatra niṣedhaparatvaṃ pratīyate ; na guṇaparatvam, svarūpasya satyatvamātraṃ ceti śaṅkāvyāvṛtyartham | svarūpato guṇataścotkṛṣṭamityanena,

'ataścaiva' vākyaśeṣodita 'guṇajāta 'yogāt, 'neti neti' iti, brahmaṇaḥ saviśeṣatvaṃ na pratiṣidhyate ; api tu pūrvaprakṛteyattā mātram |

ataḥ ubhayaliṅgameva

paraṃ brahma || 21 ||

336. tadavyaktamāha hi ! || 3-2-22|| (si)

337. api saṃrādhane pratyakṣānumānābhyām || 3-2-23 || (si)

9. puruṣavidyādhikaraṇam –

10. vedādyadhikaraṇam -
śukraṃ pravidhyetyādimantrāṇāṃ na vidyāṅgatvam ; kintvadhyayanāṅgatvam ||
11. hānyadhikaraṇam –
viduṣa utkrāmataḥ śrutayoḥ sukṛtaduṣkṛtahānopāyanayoḥ sarvavidyāsu cintanam ||
12. sāmparāyādhikaraṇam –
viduṣo dehādutkramaṇakāle eva puṇya-pāpayorniravaśeṣeṇa hā niścintyā ||
13. aniyamādhi karaṇam – sū. 32]

viduṣo'rcirādigatiḥ sarvavidyā sādhāraṇī ||
14. akṣaradhyadhikaraṇam –

akṣara vidyoktānāmasthūlatvā dibrahmaguṇānāṃ sarvaparavidyāsūpasaṃhāraḥ ||
15. antaratvādhikaraṇam –
uṣastapraśnaprativacanayoḥ kaholapraśnaprativacanayośca pratipannā vidyā ekaiva ||
16. kāmādyadhikaraṇam –
chāndogya-vājasaneyīyayordahara vidyayorabhedaḥ, rūpābhedāt ||
17. tannirdhāraṇāniyamādhikaraṇam –
karmāṅgāśritodgīthopāsanādeḥ kratuṣu upādānanaiyatyābhāvaḥ ||
18. pradānādhikaraṇam –
havistyāge indrādīnāmiva, pratitadguṇacintanaṃ paramātmacintanā vṛttiḥ ||
19. liṅgabhūyastvādhikaraṇam –
sahasraśīrṣānubākasya sarvaparavidyopāsyaviśeṣanirdhāraṇaparatvam ||

20. pūrvavikalpādhikaraṇam –
agnirahasyoktamanaścidādīnāṃ vidyāmayakratvanupraveśa eva ||

pratyagātmana upāsanaṃ āvirbhūyamānaguṇāṣṭakavatvenaiva ||

sūtrāṇi 1-54]

prathamaṃ tāvat ekasyā vaiśvānaravidyā dikāyāḥ anekaśākhāsu śrayamāṇāyāḥ, kim ekavidyātvam ? uta vidyābheda: ? iti cintyate |

aviśeṣapunaḥ śravaṇasya prakaraṇāntarasya ca bhedakatvāt śākhāntare ca ubhayora- varjanīyatvāt, vidyābhedaḥ - iti prāptam | ata eva,

|

'teṣāmetāṃ brahmavidyāṃ vadeta śirovrataṃ vidhivadyaistu cīrṇam ' iti, śirotratavatāṃ ātharvaṇikānāmeva vidyopadeśaniyamaḥ upapadyate | vidyaikye hi ! 'vidyāṅgasya śirovratasya, ' anyeṣāmapi śākhināṃ prāpteḥ niyamo nopapadyate ||

aneka.... māṇāyāḥ ekasaṃjñatvena ekaphalatvena śrayamāṇāyāḥ | pūrvamīmāṃsā yāṃ śākhāntarādhikaraṇe, 'śākhāntare karmabheda:' iti sutreṇa pūrvapakṣamuktvā, ' ekaṃ vā saṃyogarupacodanākhyā viśeṣāt ' iti siddhāntitam | atra tu sa nyāya: vedāntavākyeṣu kiṃ pravartate ? utana ? iti saṃśayya, pravartata iti nirṇīyate | nānāśākhāmnātāyāḥ vaiśvānara vidyā dikāyāḥ, kiṃ pratiśākhaṃ bhedaḥ ! uta ekatvam ? iti | kiṃ śākhāntare pratyabhijñānam aikyamavagamayati ? na ? iti | śirovratavatā mātharvaṇikānāṃ vidyopadeśaniyamaḥ, vedāntavihitavidyāsu śākhāntarā dhikaraṇanyāyāvatāraṃ niruṇaddhi ? na ? iti | kiṃ śirovrataṃ vidyāṅgam ? uta adhyayanāṅgam ? iti | vidyāṅgatve, śākhāntaranayāvatārapratibandhakatvāt | pratyabhijñānasya aikyānavagamakatayā vidyābheda: | adhyaya- nāṅgatve, śirovratasya vidyāsu śākhāntaranayā pratibandhakatayā, pratyabhijñānasya aikyāvagamakatvāt vidyekatvam — iti ||

aviśeṣeti | 'agnihotraṃ juhoti ' ' daghnā juhoti' ityādiṣu 'juhoti' padasya punaḥ śravaṇe'pi, ‘daghne’ti viśeṣaḥ śrūyate | tadvyāvṛtyarthamidamuktam punariti | 'iḍo yajati' 'bahiryajati ' tanūnapātaṃ yajati' ityādiṣu hi ! 'yajati 'śabdādeḥ punaḥ śravaṇaṃ bhedakaṃ dṛśyate ekasyāmapi śākhāyām | ata eveti adhikāṃ pūrvapakṣayuktimāha | śākhāntararādhikaraṇasiddhānta- yuktirevā'trā'pi syāditi | vidyābhedādevetyarthaḥ | | vidyakya iti | brahmavidyāṅgasya śirovratasya anyeṣāmapi śākhināṃ | prāptiḥ aniṣṭā | vidyaikye, tadaṅgasya śirovratasya, anyeṣāmapi prāpteḥ na niyamopapattiḥ | tasmāt na vidyaikyamityarthaḥ ' ||

evaṃ prāpte, ucyate - sarvavedāntapratyayam ekamupāsanam - iti |
kutaḥ 1 | codanādyaviśeṣāt | codanā tāvat - ' upāsīta, vidyāt' ityevaṃjātīyakaḥ, dhātvarthaviśeṣavidhiḥ | 'ādi' śabdena,
'ekaṃ vā saṃyoga-rūpa-codanā-sskhyā'viśeṣāt '
iti karmakāṇḍaśākhāntarādhikaraṇa sūtroktāḥ, saṃyogarūpākhyāḥ gṛhyante | eṣāṃ codanādīnām aviśeṣāt, 'seveyaṃ vidyā' iti śākhāntare pratyabhijñāyate | tathā hi ; chāndogya vājasaneyakayoḥ,

'vaiśvānara 'mupāste''

iti codanā tāvat, ekarūpā | 'beka' nirūpaṇīya svarūpasya vidiparyāyasya upāservedya- bhūtavaiśvānaraikyāt rūpamapi aviśiṣṭam | ākhyā ca 'vaiśvānaravidye 'ti, aviśiṣṭā | phalasaṃyogo'pi ubhayatrāpi brahmaprāpti rūpaḥ aviśiṣṭaḥ | ataḥ ebhiḥ pratyabhijñānāt, śākhāntare'pi vidyaikyam || 1 ||

356. bhedānneti cedekasyāmapi || 3-3-2 || (si) aviśeṣapunaśśrutyā prakaraṇāntarācca 'vidheyabhedāt na' vidyaikyam – iti cet ! ekasyāmapi vidyāyāṃ pratipattṛbhedāt punaḥśrutiḥ, prakaraṇāntaraṃ ca upapadyate | yatra hi ! ekasmin pratipattari, punaḥ śrutiḥ prakaraṇāntaraṃ 'ca vidyate'; tatra anyathānupapatyā vidheyabhedāt vidyābhedaḥ | pratipattṛbhede tu tatpratipatyarthatayā punaḥśrutyādyapapatteḥ, tatra na vidheyāntarasaṃbhavaḥ || 2||

darśayati ca śrutiḥ, upāsanasya sarvavedāntapratyayatvam | tathā hi ! chāndogye-

śrīmādhyam 3-3.2]

anyathātvādhikaraṇam || 3 - 3-2 ||

ityārabhya,

"te ha devā ūcuḥ ; hantā'surān yajña udgīthenā'tyayāma' iti, udgīthena asura' vidhvaṃsanaṃ pratijñāya, udgīthe vāgādimanaḥ paryantadṛṣṭau asurairabhibhava-

muktvā,

'atha hemamāsanyaṃ prāṇamūcuḥ'
ityādinā udgīthe prāṇadṛṣṭyā asuraparābhavamuktyā,
'bhavatyātmanā, parā'sya dviṣan pāpmā bhrātṛvyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda'
iti, śatruparājayaphalāya, udgīthe prāṇadṛṣṭivihitā |

evaṃ chandogānāmapi,

'devāsurā ha vai yatra saṃyetire'

ityārabhya,

'taddha devā udgīthamājahaḥ ; 'anenainānabhi' haniṣyāmaḥ' iti, udgīthena asuraparābhavaṃ pratijñāya, tadvadeva, udgīthe vāgādidṛṣṭau doṣamabhidhāya, " atha ha ya evāyaṃ mukhyaḥ prāṇastamudgīthamupāsāṃcakrire ityādinā, udgīthe prāṇadṛṣṭyā asuraparābhavamuktvā,
'yathā'śmānamākhaṇamṛtvā vidhvasate, 'evaṃ haiva' sa vidhvaṃsate; ya evaṃvidi pāpaṃ kāmayate'
iti, śatru parābhavāya udgīthe prāṇadṛṣṭirvihitā vedanaviṣaya vidhipratyayāśravaṇe'pi phalasādhanatvaśravaṇāt, vedanaviṣayo vidhiḥ kalpyate | udgīthavidyāyāḥ 'kratvarthatvena' ṛtusādguṇyaphalatve'pi, ārthavādikamapi phalaṃ tadaviruddha grāhayameveti devatādhi-

karaṇe pratipāditam |

tatra saṃśayyate -kimatravidhaikyam ? uta na 1 -- iti |

kiṃ yuktam ? vidyaikyamiti

kutaḥ ? ubhayatra, udgīthasyaiva adhyasta prāṇabhāvasya upāsyatvaśravaṇāt, codanādya- viśeṣāt | phalasaṃyogastāvat śatruparibhavarūpaḥ, na viśiṣyate | rūpamapi, adhyasta - prāṇabhāvodgīthākhyopāsyaikyāt aviśiṣṭam | codanā ca vididhātvardhagatā aviśiṣṭā | ākhyā ca ' udgīthavidye 'ti aviśiṣṭā || atra 'rāddhānti' cchāyayā paricodya pariharati- ' anyathātvaṃ śabdāditi cennā'viśeṣāt ' -- iti ||

yaduktaṃ -- vidhaikyamiti, tannopapadyate ; rūpabhedāt | rūpānyathātvaṃ hi ! śabdādeva pratīyate | vājasaneyake hi
phalasādhanatvameva nāsti ; udgīthavidyāyāḥ ṛtusādguṇyameva phalamiti, arthavādoktaśatru parābhavādau tātparyābhāvāt - | pratipāditamiti | pratipāditamiti | arthavādo hi ! stāvakatvena rucijanakaḥ | asatā guṇena rucirna jāyata iti, tatrā'pi tātparyamastyeveti, 'taduparyapi bādarāyaṇaḥ' ityatra pratipāditamityarthaḥ | evaṃ viṣayaśuddhiḥ kṛtā ||
tatreti | kim ubhayatra vidyaikyam ? utaḥ vidyābhedaḥ | kim ubhayatra vidyā pratyabhijñāyate ? na ? iti | kimubhayatra udgīthamyaiva upāsyatvam ? uta kvacit udgīthāvayavaḥ praṇavaḥ; kvacit udgātā ca upāsyaḥ ? | 'omityetadakṣaramudgīthamupāsīta' 'tebhya eṣa prāṇa udagāyat' iti vākyadrayaṃ kim ubhayatra vihitasyopāsanasya udgīthaviṣayatāmudgātṛviṣayatāṃ ca avagamayati ? na ? iti | yā nā'vagamayati ; tadā ubhayatra udgīthasyaiva upāsyatvena vidyāpratyabhijñānāt, vidyaikatvaṃ syāt | yadā tu vākyadvayam udgīthāvayavasya praṇavasya udgātuśca upāsyatāmavagamayatiḥ tadā rūpabhedena, chāndogye vājasaneyake ca ekavidyāpratyabhi- jñānābhāvāt vidyābhedaḥ syāt iti ||

'atha hemamāsanyaṃ prāṇamūcustvaṃ na ugāyeti | tatheti tebhya eṣa prāṇa

udagāyat'

iti, 'udgānasya' kartari prāṇadṛṣṭyā asuraparābhavamuktvā,
' ya evaṃ veda'

iti, kartaryeva prāṇadṛṣṭiḥ, ' evaṃ ' śabdādavagamyate | chāndogye-
"atha ha ya evāyaṃ mukhyaḥ prāṇaḥ, tamudgīthamupāsāṃcakrire ' iti, udgānasya karmaṇi udgīthe prāṇadṛṣṭyā asuraparābhavamuktvā,
ya evaṃvidi pāpaṃ kāmayate'

iti ' evaṃ ' śabdāt karmaṇyevodgīthe prāṇadṛṣṭirvihitā | ataḥ, ekatra kartari prāṇadṛṣṭi- śabdāt, anyatra karmaṇi prāṇadṛṣṭiśabdācca, rūpānyathātvaṃ spaṣṭam | rūpānyathātve ca, vidheyabhede sati, kevalacodanādyaviśeṣaḥ akiṃcitkara iti, vidyābhedaḥ - iti cet !

"tanna ; aviśeṣāt ' | aviśeṣeṇa hi ! ubhayatra udgītha' sādhanaka' para ' paribhavaḥ', upakrame pratīyate | vājasaneya ke
' te ha devā ūcuḥ ; hantāsurān yajña udgīthenā'tyayāma' iti, upakrame śrūyate | " chāndogye'pi',
' taddha devā udgīthamājahuḥ ; 'anenainā' nabhihaniṣyāmaḥ ' iti | ataḥ upakramāvirodhāya,
tebhya epa prāṇa udagāyat'

iti adhyastaprāṇabhāvaḥ udgīthaḥ udgānakarmabhūta eva, pākādiṣu odanādivat, saukaryāti- śayavivakṣayā kartṛtvenocyate | anyathā, upakramagataḥ
lākṣaṇikaḥ syāt | ataḥ, vidyaikyam || 6 ||

udgītha' śabdaḥ, kartari

iti prāpte, pracakṣmahe

361. na vā prakaraṇabhedāt parovarīyastvādivat || 3-3-7|| (siṃ)
'na ve'ti, pakṣaṃ vyāvartayati | na caitadasti yat vidyaikyamiti |

kutaḥ 1 prakaraṇabhedāt |

'omityetadakṣaramudgīthamupāsīta '
iti, prakṛtam udgīthā 'vayavabhūtaṃ' praṇavaṃ prastutya,
' etasyaivākṣarasyopavyākhyānaṃ bhavati' devāsurā ha vai yatra saṃyetire'

ityārabhya,

atha ha ya evā'yaṃ mukhyaḥ prāṇastamudgīthamupāsāṃcakrire ' iti, udgīthāvayavabhūtapraṇavaviṣayamupāsanaṃ chandogā adhīyate | vājinastu tādṛśa- prācīnaprakaraṇābhāvāt,
hantā'surān yajña udgīthenā'tyayāma '

iti kṛtsnamudgīthaṃ prastutya,

atha hemamāsanyaṃ prāṇamūcuḥ, tvaṃ na udgāya' ityādi kṛtsnodgīthaviṣayamadhīyate | ataḥ prakaraṇabhedena vidheyabhedaḥ; 'vidheyabhede # ca rūpabheda iti, na vidyakyam |

kiṃ ca-

'atha ha ya evāyaṃ mukhyaḥ prāṇaḥ, tamudgīthamupāsāṃcakrire'

iti pūrvaprakṛtaḥ udgīthāvayavabhūtaḥ praṇava vājināṃ tu kṛtsnasya udgīthasya kartā

eva adhyasta prāṇabhāvaḥ chandogānāmupāsyaḥ ;

udgātā, prāṇadṛṣṭayā upāsyaḥ - iti |
na udgāyeti | tatheti tebhya eṣa prāṇa

'atha hemamāsanyaṃ prāṇamūcuḥ, tvaṃ udagāyat'
iti udgātari prāṇābhyāsaṃ nirdiśya,
' ya evaṃ veda'

|

iti, udgātaiva adhyasta prāṇabhāvaḥ, upāsyo vidhīyate | ataśca rūpabhedaḥ |

na ca udgātaryupāsye vihite,
'udgīthenā'tyayāma'

364. sarvābhedādanyatreme || 3-3-10| (si)

iti vedane vidhipratyayābhāvāt, anagnatāmaṅkīrtanasya stutyarthatayā 'anvayo 'papatteśca, bhojanāṅgasyāsscamanasya smṛtyācāraprāptatvena vidhipratyayabalāt prāṇavidyāṅgam ācamanāntaraṃ vidhīyate,

iti prāpte, brūmaḥ - ācamanīyānāmapāṃ prāṇasya vāsastvānusandhānameva, iha apūrvam - aprāptaṃ vidhīyate; kāryākhyānāt – aprāptākhyānāt | aprāptākhyāne' śabdasya ' arthavaccāt ityarthaḥ |
na cet ! ācamanāntaravidhiḥ syāt | pratīyate cet ! ācamanīyāsvapsu vāso- dṛṣṭividhiḥ syāt |

stutyarthatayeti | na anusandhānārthatayetyarthaḥ | aprāptapadam apūrvapadavyākhyānam | iha.... vidhīyate iti ' iha ' śabda: prakaraṇaparaḥ | dṛṣṭividhyantaropetatvaṃ vivakṣitam | 'yadidaṃ kiṃ ca āśvabhyaḥ ākīṭapataṅgebhyaḥ tatte'nnam ' iti tattatprāṇyadanīye vastuni prāṇāpyāyakabhūte prāṇānnatvadṛṣṭirvidhitsiteti, tatsāhacaryādapi vāsastvadṛṣṭividhiyukta iti bhāvaḥ | aprāptākhyāne iti aprāptaṃ vidhīyate, aprāptākhyānādityukte sādhyāviśiṣṭatā syāditi tannivṛtyartham | na hi jñātajñāpane prayojanam |
etaduktamiti | evaṃvidācāmet ' iti, 'snātvā bhuñjīta' iti pratipannasyaivāprāptatvāt, apratipannārthāntara kalpanāyogāt, upakramopasaṃhāratātparya liṅgo petatvāt śākhādvayānuyāyitvācca,

etaduktaṃ bhavati-

'kiṃ me vāsaḥ ? āpo vāsaḥ'
'adbhiḥ paridadhati'
" etameva ' tadanamananaṃ kurute'

ityupakramopasaṃhārayoḥ vākyasya, apāṃ prāṇavāsodṛṣṭiparatvapratīteḥ, ācamanasya smṛtyācāra - prāptatvāt, ācamanamanūdya ācamanīyāsvapsu prāṇavāsastvānusandhānaṃ vidhīyate iti | ata eva chāndogye,

' tasmādvā etadaśiṣyantaḥ purastāccopariṣṭāccādbhiḥ paridadhati' iti, adbhiḥ paridhānamevoktam ; na ācamanam ||18||

iti kāryākhyānādhikaraṇam || 5 ||

samānādhikaraṇam || 3-3-6 ||
373. samāna evaṃ cā'bhedāt || 3-3-19 || (si)

vājasaneyake agnirahasye, śāṇḍilyavidyā āmnātā-
'satyaṃ 'brahmetyupāsīta' | atha khalu kratumayo'yaṃ puruṣa:'

ityārabhya,

sa ātmānamupāsīta; manomayaṃ prāṇaśarīraṃ 'bhārūpamākāśātmānaṃ .... satya-.. saṅkalpam

iti | tathā tasminneva bṛhadāraṇyake punarapi śāṇḍilyavidyā āmnāyate—

'manomayo'yaṃ puruṣo bhāḥ satyam | tasminnantarhṛdaye yathā vrīhiva ' yavo vā | ' sa eṣa sarvasya vaśī sarvasyeśānaḥ sarvasyādhipatiḥ sarvamidaṃ praśāsti; yadidaṃ kiṃ ca'

iti |

tatra saṃśayaḥ – kimatra vidyā bhidyate ? uta na 1 - iti |

saṃyoga codanā khyānām saṃyogacodana

aviśeṣe'pi, vaśitvādyupāsyaguṇabhedena rūpabhedāt,

vidyābhedaḥ,

iti prāpte, ucyate - 'samāna evam ' - iti | yathā agnirahasye, manomaya - prāṇaśarīra- bhārūpa- satyasaṅkalpatva' - guṇagaṇaḥ śrutaḥ; evaṃ bṛhadāraṇyake'pi, manomayatvādi ke samāne sati, adhikasya vaśitvādeśva satyasaṅkalpatvaguṇābhedāt na rūpabhedaḥ | ataḥ,

vidyaikyam || 19||

iti samānādhikaraṇam || 6||

śrīmāṣyam 3-3.7]

sambandhādhikaraṇam || 3-3-7||
374. sambandhādevamanyatrāpi || 3-3-20 ||

bṛhadāraṇyake śrūyate-
satyaṃ brahma

ityupakramya |

' tadyat satyam asau sa ādityo ya eṣa etasmin maṇḍale puruṣa: ; yaścāsyaṃ 'dakṣiṇe'kṣan ' '
ityupakramya, ādityamaṇḍale 'akṣaṇi ca satyasya brahmaṇaḥ vyāhṛtiśarīra (ka) tveno-- pāsyatvamuktvā,

' tasyopaniṣadahariti '

adhidaivatam,

' tasyopaniṣada hamiti '

adhyātmam,

iti dve upaniṣadau - rahasyanāmanī, upāsanaśeṣatayā āmnāyete |

kiṃ yathāśrutasthānaviśeṣaniyatatvena vyavasthite ?
aniyamena ? - iti saṃśaye,

uta ubhayatra ubhe

te,

satyasya vyāhṛtiśarīrasyaivopāsyasya brahmaṇaḥ, dvayoḥ sthānayoḥ sambandhāt upāsyaikyena, rūpābhedāt saṃyogādyabhedācca, vidyaikyāt aniyamena – iti prāptam ; tadidamucyate--' sambandhādevamanya ' trāpi' – iti' |

yathā manomayatvādiguṇaviśiṣṭa' syaikatvāt', upāsyaikyena rūpābhedāt vidyakyāt", guṇopasaṃhāraḥ evam anyatra akṣyādityasambandhino brahmaṇaḥ satyasya ekatvena vidhaikyāt, ubhayoḥ ubhayatropasaṃhāraḥ - iti || 20||

'na caitadasti-yat, vidhaikyādupasaṃhāraḥ - iti |
kuta: 1 ; viśeṣāt – upāsyarūpaviśeṣāt | brahmaṇa ekatve'pi, ekatra āditya- maṇḍalasthatayā upāsyatvam; itaratra akṣyādhāratayā upāsyatvamiti, sthānasambandhitva- bhedena rūpabhedāt vidyābhedaḥ | naivaṃ śāṇḍilyavidyāyāḥ upāsyasthānaṃ bhidyate ; ubhayatra hṛdayādhāratvena upāsyatvāt | ataḥ vyavasthite - iti || 21 ||

377. sambhṛti - vyāptyapi cātaḥ || 3-3-23 || (si)

samāhāradvandvāt ekavadbhāvaḥ | sambhṛtyādikam anāramyādhītamapi, ata eva sthānabhedāt vyavasthāpyam ; na sarvatropasaṃhartavyam |
katham anāramyādhītānāṃ sthānaviśeṣaniyatatvam ?
svasāmarthyāt - iti brūmaḥ |

dyavyāptistāvat, hṛdayādyalpasthānagocarāsu vidyāsu, na upasaṃhartuṃ śakyā | sambhṛtyādayo'pi tatsahacāriṇaḥ- tattulyadeśā iti, alpasthānaviṣayāsu vidyāsu anupasaṃhāryāḥ |

śāṇḍilya - daha dividyāsu alpasthāna viṣayāsu,
'jyāyān pṛthivyāḥ'

'yāvān vā ayamākāśaḥ, tāvāneṣo'ntarhṛdaya ākāśaḥ ' ityādayaḥ tatra tatra aśakyopasaṃhārāḥ, manomayatvāpahatapāpmatvādiviśiṣṭasyopāsyasya, māhātmya pratipādanaparāḥ || 23 ||

puruṣavidyādhikaraṇam || 3-3-9 ||

378. puruṣavidyāyāmapi cetareṣāmanāmnānāt || 3-3-24 || (si)

taittirīyake puruṣavidyā''mnāyate ;

' tasyaivaṃ viduṣo yajñasyā''tmā yajamānaḥ śraddhā patnī śarīra midhmamuro

vedirlomāni barhiḥ'

ityādikā |

chāndogye'pi puruṣavidyā''mnāyate ;

iti brahmavidyāmabhidhāya tatphalatvena,

'brahmaṇo mahimānamāpnoti'

ityuktatvā,

'tasyaivaṃ viduṣaḥ'

ataḥ brahmavidyāṅgatvāt brahmaprāptirevā'tra phalam |

śrīmāṣyam 3-3-10]

iti | vājasaneyinastu, 'pravargya' brāhmaṇam,

yuktam ? ; vidyāṅgamiti |

kiṃ

kuta: 1 ; sannidhisamāmnānāt vidyāṅgatvapratīteḥ |

śaṃ no mitraḥ'
sahanāvavatu '

380. hānau tūpāyanaśabdaśeṣatvātkuśācchandaḥ stutyupagānavat taduktam ||3-3-26 || (si)

chandogā āmananti

aśva iva romāṇi vidhūya pāpaṃ candra iva rāhormukhāt pramucya |
dhūtvā śarīramakṛtaṃ 'kṛtātmā brahmalokamabhisambha' vāni' |' - iti | ātharvaṇikāśca,
tadā vidvān puṇya-pāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti | ' iti | śāṭyāyaninastu,
tasya putrā dāyamupayanti; suhṛdaḥ sādhukṛtyām dviṣantaḥ pāpakṛtyām' " ityādi | kauṣītakinastu,

tadubhayam ekaikavidyāyāṃ śrutamapi, sarvavidyāṅgamāstheyam; sarvabrahmavidyā niṣṭhasyāpi brahma prāpnuvataḥ puṇya-pāpagrahāṇasya avaśyambhāvitvāt, prahīṇavipayatvācca upāyanasya | taccintanaṃ ca vidhīyamānaṃ, sarvavidyāṅga bhavitumarhati ||

tatredaṃ vicāryate—hānicintanam upāyanacintanam ubhayacintanaṃ ca, vikalpe (lpye) ran ? upasaṃhiyeran vā ? |

kiṃ yuktam 1 ; 'vikalpe (lapye) ran - iti' |

kuta: 1 ; pṛthagāmnānasāmarthyāt | samuccaye hi ! 'sarvatra' ubhayānusandhānaṃ syāt | tacca 'kauṣītaki' vākyenaiva siddhamiti, anyatra mnānam anarthakameva syāt | ataḥ anekatrā''mnānasya vikalpa eva prayojanam |
tathā saṃnidhisamāmnātahānopāyanacintā anantaraṃ prāpteti saṅgatiḥ | sarva....māstheyamiti 'aṅga' śabdena upekṣitamucyate | 'prahīṇa viṣaya' śabdena prahīṇasambandha ucyate | upāyanam - anyatra praveśaḥ | 'sukṛtamupayantī 'ti śrutyanusārāt ' upāyana' mityuktam | prahīṇasambandhitvā- dupāsanasya, prahīṇasya karmaṇaḥ kvacit praveśo hyavaśyaṃbhāvītyarthaḥ | taccintanaṃ ca vidhīya- mānamiti | tatkratunyāyāt, ' etamitaḥ pretyā'bhisambhavitāsmīti yasya smādaddhā' iti itikaraṇaśravaṇāt, ‘abhisambhavāmī' tyanusandhānasya śrutyā vidhīyamānatvaṃ siddham | sarvavidyāṅga- miti | hānopāyanacintanasya tatsiddhyarthatvāt, taccintanaṃ sarvavidyāṅgamityarthaḥ | evaṃ viṣayaḥ śodhitaḥ ||

vikalpena - vikalpena tiṣṭheyuḥ | 'vikalpyeran' iti ca pāṭho'sti | hāneḥ upāyanasya ubhayasya ca cintanāni vihitāni kiṃ vikalperan ? uta samuccīyeran ?| kim upāyanaṃ hāni- śeṣatayā vidyāṅgam ? uta svatantratayā ? iti | kim upāyanavākyaṃ śākhāntarādhītamapi hā nivākyaśeṣam ? uta svatantram ? | kiṃ kauṣītakinām ubhayāmnānam, upāyanavākyasya hā nivākyaśeṣatā virodhi ? na ? iti | virodhitve, upāyanavākyasya svātantryāt, upāyanaṃ svātantryeṇa vidyāṅgamiti taccintanānāṃ vikalpaḥ | ubhayānnānaṃ śeṣatvā virodhi cet !,

na ca – vidyābhedena vyavasthāpayituṃ śakyam ; 'sarvaśeṣabhūtam idamanusandhānam ' ityuktatvāt ||

tūpāyana śabdaśeṣatvāt ' - iti | ' tu' śabda: pakṣa ' hānau' iti pradarśanārtham | kevalāyāṃ hānau kevale ca upāyane śrūyamāṇe, tayoḥ itaretarasamuccayaḥ avaśyambhāvī |

atredamucyate - ' hānau

vyāvartayati |

kutaḥ 1 upāyanaśabdaśeṣatvāt – upāyanaśabdasya hānivākyaśeṣatvāt | upāyana- vākyasya hi ! hānivākyaśeṣatvameva ucitam ; viduṣā tyaktayoḥ 'puṇya-pāpayoḥ praveśa'- sthānavā citvāt – upāyanavākyasya |

pradeśāntarāmnātasya vākyasya pradeśāntara manātavākyaśeṣatve dṛṣṭāntā upanyasyante— kuśācchandaḥ stutyupagānavat ' - iti | 'kālāpinaḥ 1,

kuśā vānaspatyā: ' ( ? )
ityāmananti | śāṭyāyanināṃ tu,
'audumbaryaḥ kuśā:'

iti vākyaṃ, 'sāmānyena 'vānaspatyatvenā'vagatāḥ ' kuśāḥ, audumbaryaḥ' iti 'viśiṣat', tadvākyaśeṣatāmāpadyate | tathā,

devāsurāṇāṃ chandobhiḥ '
ityādinā aviśeṣeṇa devāsurāṇāṃ chandasāṃ prasaṅge,
devachandāṃsi pūrvam ' ( ? )

iti vacanaṃ, kramaviśeṣaṃ pratipādayat, tadvākyaśeṣa tāṃ gacchati | tathā, ' hiraṇyena ṣoḍaśinaḥ stotramupākaroti '

iti aviśeṣeṇa 'prāpte',

'samayāviṣite sūrye ṣoḍaśinaḥ stotramupākaroti ' iti 'viśeṣaviṣaya' vākyaṃ, tadvākya' śeṣatāṃ bhajate | tathā,

' ṛtvija upagāyanti '

iti aviśeṣaprāptasya,
' nā'dhvaryurupagāyet '
iti vākyam, anadhvaryudhiṣayatām avagamayat, tadvākyaśeṣatvamṛcchati |
evaṃ sāmānyenā'vagatamarthaṃ viśeṣe vyavasthāpayituṃ kṣamasya vākyasya taccheṣatva- manabhyupagacchadbhiḥ, tayorarthayoḥ vikalpaḥ samāśrayitavyaḥ | sa, ca, sambhavantyāṃ gatau na yujyate ||

taduktaṃ, pūrvasmin kāṇḍe

'api tu vākya 'śeṣasyā' danyāyyatvād vikalpasya vidhīnāmekadeśaḥ syāt iti | tadevaṃ kevalahānopāyanavākyayoḥ ekavākyatvāt, kevalasya hānasya kevalasya ca upāyanasya abhāvāt vikalpo nopapadyate | kauṣītakinām ubhayāmnānam, aviśeṣapunaḥśravaṇatvena pratipattabhedāt aviruddham || 26 ||

iti hānyadhikaraṇam || 11 ||

sāmparāyādhikaraṇam || 3-3-12 ||

381. sāmparāye tartavyābhāvāt tathāhyanye || 3-3-27 || (si)

sukṛta-duṣkṛtayoḥ hānam upāyanaṃ ca, sarvāsu vidyāsu cintanīyam - ityuktam |

|

taddhānaṃ, kiṃ dehaviyogakāle, dehādutkrāntasya adhvani ca 1 uta dehaviyogakā le evaṃ 1 - iti viśaye,

ubhayatra – iti yuktam ; ubhayathā śrutatvāt | evaṃ hi ' ! kauṣītakinaḥ samāmananti -
' sa etaṃ devayānaṃ panthānamāpadya, 'agnilokama | gacchati'' ityupakramya,
' sa āgacchati virajāṃ nadīm | tāṃ manasā'tyeti | tatsukṛta- duṣkṛte dhūnute '
iti | atra vākye, adhvani sukṛta- 'duṣkṛta hāniḥ pratīyate | tāṇḍinastu-

aśva iva romāṇi vidhūya pāpaṃ candra iva rāhormukhāt pramucya |
dhūtvā śarīramakṛtaṃ 'kṛtātmā brahmalokamabhisambhavāmi || ' iti | atra tu dehaviyoga' kāle' iti pratīyate | śāṭyāyana ke'pi,
' tasya putrā dāyamupayanti, suhṛdaḥ sādhukṛtyāṃ dviṣantaḥ pāpakṛtyām ' iti, putreṣu dāyasaṅkrāntisamakālaṃ sukṛta - duṣkṛtasaṅkramaṇaṃ śrūyamāṇaṃ dehaviyoga' kāle- iti 'gamyate| ataḥ sukṛta- duṣkṛtayorekadeśaḥ, dehaviyogakāle hīyate ; śeṣastu adhvani||
iti prāpte, ucyate - ' sāmparāye' – iti | sāmparāye – 'dehādapakramaṇa' kāle eva, viduṣaḥ sukṛta- duṣkṛte niravaśeṣaṃ hīyete |
kuta: ? ; ' tartavyābhāvāt ' - viduṣaḥ dehaviyogātpaścāt sukṛta- duṣkṛtābhyāṃ taritavya bhogābhāvāt | vidyāphalabhūtabrahmaprāptivyatirekeṇa hi ! sukṛta- duṣkṛtābhyāṃ | "bhoktavye sukha-duḥkhe' na vidyate |
' tathā hyanye ' dehaviyogādūrdhvaṃ brahmaprāptivyatirikta sukhaduḥkhopabhogābhāvamadhīyate- aśarīraṃ vā vasantaṃ na priyāpriye spṛśataḥ '

eṣa samprasādo'smāccharīrāt samutthāya paraṃjyotirūpasampadya
svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyate '

' tasya tāvadeva ciraṃ yāvanna vimokṣye atha sampatsye ' 'iti' || 27 ||
'sukṛtaḥ duṣkṛte dhūnute' iti vākyāvayavasya ' sa etaṃ devayāna' mityasmāt prāk anvayaḥ : sukṛtaduṣkṛte uta yathā pāṭhamanvayaḥ | kim aśarīrasya sukha-duḥkhānubhava śravaṇānurodhena, prāganvayavarṇanaṃ nyāyyam ? uta devayānagatyai sūkṣmadeha sthityarthaṃ yathā pāṭhamanvayavarṇanaṃ nyāyyam | kiṃ sūkṣmadeha- sthitiḥ, karmaṇā vinā upapadyate ? na ? iti || na cet ! sūkṣmadeha sthinaye yathā pāṭhamanvayasya nyāyyatvāt tathaivā'nvaya iti ubhayatra hāniśravaṇāt, dehaviyogakāle adhvani ca hānaṃ syāt | sūkṣmadehasthitiḥ karmaṇā vinā upapadyate cet ! prāganvayavarṇanasya nyāyyatvāt tathaivā'nvaya iti, ubhayatra śravaṇābhāvāt dehaviyogakāle eva sukṛta- duṣkṛtahānaṃ syāt ||

vartavyābhāvāditi | śrutivākye satyapi arthasvābhāvyamevātra heturukta: ; sūtroktatvāt ||27||

382. chandata ubhayāvirodhāt || 3-3-28 || (si)
evam arthasvābhāvyāt sukṛta- duṣkṛta hā nikāle avadhṛte sati, ubhayāvirodhena- śruteḥ arthasvabhāvasya cā'virodhena, chandataḥ - yatheṣṭaṃ, padānāmanvayaḥ varṇanīyaḥ | 'kauṣītaki ' vākye,

tatsukṛta- duṣkṛte dhūnute'
iti caramaśruto vākyāvayavaḥ,

' etaṃ devayānaṃ panthānamāpadya' iti prathamaśrutāvayavāt prāk, anugamayitavyaḥ ityarthaḥ || 28 ||

383. gaterarthavattva' mubhayathā'nyathā' hi virodhaḥ || 3-3-29 || (pū)

sukṛta- duṣkṛtayorekadeśasya, dehaviyogakāle hāniḥ śeṣasya ca paścāt - iti, ubhayathā karmakṣaye satyeva, gaterarthavattvam – devayānagatiśruteḥ arthavaccam ityarthaḥ | 'anyathā hi virodhaḥ' – dehaviyogakāle 'eva' sarvakarmakṣaye, sūkṣmaśarīrasyā'pi vināśaḥ " syāt | tathā sati, kevalasya ātmanaḥ gamanaṃ nopapadyate | ataḥ utkrāntisamaye, viduṣo niśśeṣakarmakṣayaḥ 'nopapannaḥ || 29 ||

evaṃ prāpte, pracakṣmahe - ' akṣaradhiyāṃ tvavarodhaḥ ' - iti | akṣarabrahma' sambandhinī- nām' asthūlatvādidhiyām, sarvabrahmavidyāsu avarodhaḥ – saṅgrahaṇam ityarthaḥ |

guṇānām pradhānānuvartitve, dṛṣṭāntamāha – aupasadavat ' – iti | yathā jāmadagnya- 'catūrātra' puroḍāśādyupasadguṇabhūtaḥ sāmaveda paṭhitaḥ,

389 antarā bhūtagrāmavatsvātmano'nyathā bhedānupapattiriti cennopadeśavat || 3-3-35||

]

'yo śanāyā - pipāse śokaṃ moha jarāṃ mṛtyumatyeti, etaṃ vai ( evaṃ hai) tamātmānaṃ : viditvā brāhmaṇaḥ putraiṣaṇāyāśca vittaiṣaṇāyāśca'

'aton padārtam'

ityantam ||

kiṃ pūrvatra jīva upāsyaḥ, anyatra para: ? uta ubhayatra para eva ? iti | kimatra sarvāntaratva - prāṇitṛtve dehādijāmradādyavasthāpekṣe ? uta anavacchinne ? | 'na dṛṣṭerdraṣṭāram' iti, dṛṣṭivyati- riktatvaniṣedha: ? uta śravaṇādikartuḥ mantavyatvaniṣedha: ? iti || yadā dṛṣṭirūpatāvyatireka- niṣedhaḥ, tadā tu asya jīve sambhavāt sarvāntaratva - prāṇitṛtvayorapi āpekṣikatvena, pūrvatra jīva upāsya iti, vidyā bhidyate | yadā śravaṇādikartuḥ upāsyatvaniṣedhaḥ, tadā sarvāntaranva- prāṇitṛtvayorapi anavacchinnatayā, pūrvatrā'pi para evopāsya iti vidyaikyamiti || prativacana- bhedāditi | prativacanaprakāreṇa hi | praśnārtho jñāyate iti bhāvaḥ |

.

bhūtagrāmavataśca pratyagātmanaḥ, tasya bhūtagrāmasya sarvasya antarātmatvena', sarvāntara- tvamapi upapannam | yadyapi, pratyagātmanaḥ sarvāntaratvaṃ bhūtagrāmamātrāpekṣatvena āpekṣikam ; tathā'pi tadeva grāhyam | agyathā mukhyantarātmaparigraha lobhāt paramātmasvīkāre, prativacanabhedo nopapadyate |

prativacanaṃ hi ! pūrvatra pratyagātmaviṣayam paramātmanaḥ prāṇitṛtvā-pāni- tṛtvādya saṃbhavāt | paraṃ ca paramātmaviṣayam ; aśanāyā- 'pipāsādyatītatvāt | tadida- māśaṅkate – 'antarā bhūtagrāmavatsvātmano'nyathā bhedānupapattiriti cet ' – iti | antarā - 'sarvāntaratvena' prathamaprativacanaṃ bhūtagrāmavatsvātmanaḥ - bhūtagrāmavān tadantaraḥ

svātmā pratyagātmā, sarvāntara ityucyate - ityarthaḥ | anyathā,
'yaḥ prāṇena prāṇiti' ' yo'śanāyāpipāsādyatītaḥ'
iti prativacanabhedānupapattiḥ - iti cet !,

atrottaram, neti | na vidyābhedaḥ ityarthaḥ | ubhayatra paraviṣayatvāt praśna- prativacanayoḥ | tathā hi ;
yat sākṣādaparokṣād brahma ya ātmā sarvāntara: ' iti praśnastāvat paramātmaviṣaya eva | brahma 'śabdasya paramātmā sādhāraṇatve'pi, pratyagātmanyapi kadācit upacaritaprayogadarśanāt, tadvadyāvṛdhyā 'paramātma pratipatyarthaṃ, yat sākṣādbrahma' iti viśeṣaṇaṃ kriyate | aparokṣatvamapi sarvadeśa- 'sarvakāla'- sambandhitvam,

satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma'

ityārabhya,

ya ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmano'ntara: ' iti, sarvāntaryāmiṇaḥ paramātmana eva sambhavati |
prativacananapi tathaiva paramātmaviṣayam |

yaḥ prāṇena prāṇiti' iti nirupādhikaṃ prāṇanasya kartṛtvaṃ paramātmana eva' pratyagātmanaḥ suṣuptau prāṇanaṃ prati kartṛtvābhāvāt | evamajānatā upastena, prāṇane kartutvamātramuktaṃ ' mantrānena, pratyagātmano'pi sādhāraṇatvaṃ prativacanasya matvā atuṣṭena, punaḥ pṛṣṭaḥ taṃ prati pratyagātmano vyāvṛttaṃ nirupādhikatvena 'prāṇanasya' kartāraṃ

'indriyādhīnānāṃ' darśana - śravaṇa-manana-vijñānānāṃ katāraṃ pratyagātmānaṃ, prāṇanasya kartṛtvenokta iti na mantrīthāḥ ; tasya suṣupti-mūrchādau prāṇanāderakartṛtvāt |
' ko vāsnyāt kaḥ prāṇyāt, yadeṣa ākāśa ānando na syāt '

ato'nyadārtam

uta tamādeśamaprākṣyaḥ 1'

vivakṣitam | tena jīvavyāvṛttiḥ | sahi karmānuguṇadehendriyādīnāmadhiṣṭhātṛtvena kadācit prāṇayitā | evamiti, punaḥ praśnasya arthāntaraviṣayatvaśaṅkāṃ pariharati | indriyeti |

3 prāṇane - ta ' hetutvāyā - tha.

atha syāt - yadyapi, ubhe praśna - prativacane parabrahmaviṣaye ; tathā'pi, vidyābhedaḥ avarjanīyaḥ ; ekatra sarvaprāṇiprāṇana hetutvena 'upāsyam' ; itaratra aśanāyādyatītatveneti, upāsyaguṇabhedena rūpabhedāt praṣṭṛbhedācca | 'pūrvatra hi' upastaḥ praṣṭā ; uttaratra kaholaḥ - iti ; tatrā'ha -
390. vyatihāro viśiṣanti hītaravat || 3-3-36|| (si)
nāstra vidyābhedaḥ praśna- prativacanābhyām ekarūpārthaviṣayābhyām ekena ca vidhipadena ekavākyatvapratīteḥ | praśnadvayaṃ tāvat sarvāntarātmatvaviśiṣṭabrahmaviṣayam | dvitīye praśna,

' yadeva sākṣādaparokṣād brahma, ya ātmā sarvāntara: ' iti evakāraśca pūrvatra upastena pṛṣṭaguṇaviśiṣṭabrahmaviṣayatvaṃ kaholapraśnasyāvadhārayati ||

iti, sarvāntarātmatvaviśiṣṭabrahmaviṣayam ekarūpameva | vidhipratyayaśca, uttaratraiva dṛśyate- 'tasmādbrāhmaṇaḥ pāṇḍityaṃ nirvidya bālyena tiṣṭhāset iti | evaṃ sarvāntarātmatvaviśiṣṭabrahmaka viṣayatve dvayoravadhṛte sati ekasminneva sarvāntarātmatva' viśiṣṭa brahmaṇyupāsye, upasta- kaholayoḥ itaretarabuddhivyatihāraḥ kartavyaḥ | uṣastasya yā sarvāntarātmano brahmaṇaḥ sarvaprāṇiprāṇana hetutvaviṣayā buddhi:, sā kahole- nā'pi praṣṭrā kāryā | yā ca kaholasya tasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ aśanāyādyatīta tvaviṣayā buddhiḥ, sā uṣastenāpi kāryā |
evaṃ vyatihāre kṛte, ubhābhyāṃ sarvāntarasya brahmaṇaḥ, jīvavyāvṛttiḥ 'avagatā ' bhavati | ' enaṃ sarvāntarātmānaṃ pratyagātmano vyāvṛttamavagamayituṃ, 'sarvaprāṇi' prāṇana- hetutvāśanāyādyatītatvapratipādanena viśiṣanti hi ! yājñavalkyasya prativacanāni | ataḥ, brahmaṇaḥ sarvāntarātmatvameva upāsyaguṇaḥ | prāṇana hetutvādayastu, 'tasya' upapādakāḥ; na upāsyāḥ |

taducyate-- 'sarvaprāṇi 'prāṇana hetutvena sarvāntarātmani jīvāvyāvṛtte brahmaṇi upastena " avagate' sati, kaholena jīvasya sarvātmanā asambhāvitena svabhāvaviśeṣeṇa sarvāntarātmā vyāvṛtto'nusandheyaḥ - iti kṛtvā punaḥ praśnaḥ kṛtaḥ | yājñavalkyo'pi tadabhiprāyamabhijñāya, pratyagātmano'sambhāvitam aśanāyādipratyanīkatvamuktavān | ataśca, upāsyasya vyāvṛttipratīti siddhayartham, ubhābhyāṃ parasparabuddhivyatihāraḥ kartavyaḥ ||

| sūtrasthadṛṣṭāntāṃśavivaraṇam ]

itaravat - yathā itaratra sadvidyāyāṃ bhūyo bhūyaḥ praśnaiḥ prativacanaiśca tadeva sat brahma vyavacchidyate ; na punaḥ pūrvapratipannāt guṇāt guṇāntaraviśiṣṭatayā upāsya pratipādyate, tadvat || 36 ||

393. ādarādalopaḥ || 3-3-39 || (si)
brahmaguṇatvena pramāṇāntarāprāptānāṃ guṇānāmeṣāṃ satyakāmatvādīnām, tasmin yadantastadanveṣṭavyam '
eṣa ātmā'pahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyurviśoko vijighatso'pipāsaḥ satyakāmaḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ '
'sarvasya vaśī sarvasyeśānaḥ '
'eṣa sarveśvara eṣa bhūtādhipatireṣa bhūtapāla eṣa seturvidharaṇa
eṣāṃ lokānāmasambhedāya'

tadya ihā''tmānamanuvidya vrajantyetaści satyān kāmān teṣāṃ sarveṣu lokeṣu kāmacāro bhavati'

na ca - mātāpitṛsahasrebhyo'pi vatsalataraṃ śāstraṃ, pratārakavat apāramārthikān nirasanīyān guṇān pramāṇāntarāpratipannān – ādareṇopadiśya, saṃsāracakra parivartanena pūrvameva bamnamyamāṇān mumukṣūn, bhūyo'pi bhramayitumalam |

'neha nānāssti kiścana' 'ekadhaivā'nudraṣṭavyam

bhūyo bhūya iti abhyāsoṃ darśitaḥ | avedaneti | guṇaviśiṣṭavedana muktatvā tadviṣayāvedana nindā kriyamāṇetyarthaḥ | evaṃ satyakāmatvādīnāmapūrvatvam, vedane phalaśravaṇam, vedanaphalakathanā'- vedananindārthamāvṛttiḥ, ' eṣa sarveśvaraḥ' ityādyāvṛttiśca tasmin yadantastadanveṣṭavyam' ityupa- kramopasaṃhārau, arthavādaḥ, apūrvatvena vihitasya niṣedhāyogarūpā upapattiḥ, ityādibhiḥ ādaro dṛśyata ityarthaḥ | tatheti | vājasaneyake'pi evamādikam ādaraliṅgaṃ draṣṭavyamityabhi- prāya: ||
na cetyādi | guṇoktiḥ na vipralipsā mūlā ; nairguṇyoktāvapi prasaṅgāt | na cā'rvācīna- phalopāsanārthā ; mokṣaprakaraṇasthatvāt satyaśruteśca - iti bhāvaḥ | neheti | gobalīvardanayāt brahmaprakāratayā bhedo vihita iti, sāmānyaniṣedhasya vihitaviśeṣavyatiriktaviṣayatvāt 'neha nānā'stī'tyādeḥ abrahmātmakanānātvaviṣayo niṣedhaḥ (śru. dī.

ekadhaivā'nudraṣṭavyam – keṣāṃcit kadācit svaniṣṭhatvam anya paratantratvaṃ vā na mantavyam |
api tu paramātmaparatantratayā aikarūpyameva draṣṭavyamityarthaḥ |

tvena pūrvasiddhanānātvadarśanaṃ niṣedhatī' tyayamarthaḥ, prāgeva prapaścitaḥ |
' sa eṣa neti netyātmā'
ityatra ca 'iti' śabdena pramāṇāntarapratipanna 'prapañcākāraṃ 'parāmṛśya, 'na tathāvidhaṃ brahma' iti, sarvātmabhūtasya brahmaṇaḥ prapañcavilakṣaṇatvaṃ pratipādyate | tadeva ca anantara- 'mupapādayati'

a (gṛ) grāhyo na hi gṛhyate, aśīryo na hi śīryate, asaṅgo na hi 'sajyate,
avyathito na vyathate na riṣyati'
