padmakośapratīkāśaṃ hṛdayaṃ cā'pyadhomukham '
ityādinā hṛdayapuṇḍarīkā bhidhānam, asya nārāyaṇānuvākasya dahara' vidyopāsya' nirdhāra- ṇārthatvamupoddhayatīti ||

evaṃ prāpte, pracakṣma he - ' liṅgabhūyastvāt ' iti | asya nikhilaparavidyopāsya- viśeṣanirdhāraṇārthatve bhūyāṃsi liṅgāni dṛśyante | tathā hi ! paravidyāsu akṣara - śiva- śambhu parabrahma-parajyotiḥ- paratatva- paramātmādiśabda nirdiṣṭam upāsyaṃ vastu, iha taireva śabdaḥ anūdya, tasya nārāyaṇatvaṃ vidhīyate | bhūyasīṣu vidyāsu śrutān anūdya, nārāyaṇatvavidhāna' bhūyastvaṃ, 'nārāyaṇa evaṃ sarvavidyā' sūpāsyam' asthūlatvādi- viśeṣitānandādiguṇakaṃ paraṃ brahma' iti viśeṣa nirṇaye, bhūyaḥ - bahutaraṃ liṅgaṃ bhavati |

atra 'liṅga ' śabdaḥ, cihnaparyāya: | cihnabhūtaṃ vākyaṃ bahutara mastītyarthaḥ ||

taddhi ! prakaraṇād balīyaḥ | tadapyuktaṃ, prathame kāṇḍe

śruti-liṅga vākya-prakaraṇa-sthāna- samākhyānāṃ samavāye pa (pā) radaurbalyamarthavipra- karṣāt '

iti |

yattaktam- 'padmakośapratīkāśam' ityādivacanaṃ, daharaśeṣatvam asya 'upodbala- yati — iti' | tanna ; balīyasā pramāṇena sarvavidyopāsyanirdhāraṇārthatve avadhṛte sati, daharavidyāyāmapi tasyaiva nārāyaṇasyopāsyatvena, tadvacanopapatteḥ |
na ca - ' sahasraśīrṣam' ityādidvitīyānirdeśena, pūrvānuvā ko ditopāsinā sambandhaḥ śaṅkanīyaḥ ;
' tasmin yadantastadupāsitavyam '
iti upāsigatena kṛtpratyayena, upāsyasya karmaṇaḥ abhihitatvāt, tadupāsye dvitīyā- nupapatteḥ ||

viśvamevedaṃ puruṣaḥ '
' tavaṃ nārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ '

ityādiprathama nirdeśācca, prathamārthe dvitīyā veditavyā |
'antarbahiśca tat sarva vyāpya nārāyagaḥ sthitaḥ '
tasyāḥ śikhāyā madhye paramātmā vyavasthitaḥ '
'brahmā' sa śivaḥ sendraḥ so'kṣaraḥ paramaḥ svarāi'
iti nirdeśaiḥ, 'sarvasmātparo nārāyaṇa evaṃ sarvatropāsyaḥ' iti nirṇīyamānatvācca, prathamārthe dvitīyeti 'niścīyate || 43 ||

iti liṅgabhūyastvādhikaraṇam || 19 ||

pūrvavikalpādhikaraṇam || 3-3-20 ||
398. pūrvavikalpaḥ prakaraṇāt syāt kriyā mānasavat || 3-3-44 || (pū)

vājasaneyake agnirahasye manaścitā (dā) dayo'gnayaḥ śrūyante -
' manaścitaḥ vācitaḥ prāṇacitaḥ cakṣucitaḥ śrotracitaḥ karmacitaḥ agnicitaḥ

iti |

tatra saṃśayaḥ kimete manacidādayaḥ sāmpādikatvena vidyārūpā agnayaḥ, kriyāmayakratvanupraveśena kriyārūpāḥ ? āhosvit vidyāmayakatvanupraveśena vidyārūpā eva 1 - iti viśaye ;

kriyārūpatvaṃ tāvadāha – ' pūrvavikalpa: ' – ityādinā | cityāgnitvena sampā- ditānāmeṣāṃ manavidādīnāṃ kratvanupraveśa sākāṅkṣāṇāṃ svadeśe ṛtuvidhyabhāvāt pūrvatra,

asadvā idamagra āsīt '

ityādinā iṣṭakacitāḥ prakṛtatvāt tasya ca kriyāmayatravavyabhicāritvena tatra ṛtusaṃnidhānāt tatprakaraṇagṛhītāḥ manacidādayaḥ, tena iṣṭakacitenā'gninā 'vikalpya - mānā:', kriyārūpā eva syuḥ | vidyārūpāṇāmapi kriyāmayakratvanupraveśena kriyārūpatvaṃ, mānasagrahavat upapadyate | yathā dvādaśāhe avivākye daśame'hani, mānasagrahasya mano - niṣpādyagrahaṇāsādana stotra śastrapratyāharaṇabhakṣaṇatvena vidyārūpasyā'pi kriyāmayakatvaṅgatayā kriyārūpatvam ; tathā ihāpi || 44 ||

399. atideśācca || 3-3-45|| (pū)
itaśca, iṣṭakacitenāgninā manavidādīnāṃ vikalpaḥ, kriyārūpatvaṃ ca avagamyate | teṣāmekaika eva tāvān yāvānasau pūrvaḥ '
iti pūrvasya iṣṭakacitasyāgneḥ vīrya, manacidādiṣu atidiśyate | tena tulyakāryatvāt vikalpaḥ | tataśca iṣṭakacitavat tatkratunirvartanena tadaṅgabhūtāḥ 'manacidādayaḥ, kriyāmaya- ṛtvanupraveśena' kriyārūpā eveti' || 45 ||

* evaṃ prāpte, pracakṣmahe -

400 vidyaiva tu nirdhāraṇāt darśanācca || 3-3-46 || (si)
tu śabdaḥ pakṣa vyāvartayati | yaduktam - manacidādayaḥ kriyāmaya' kratvanupraveśena kriyā' rūpā eva – iti ; naitadasti | vidyārūpā evaite | vidyārūpakatvanvayinaḥ ityarthaḥ |
kutaḥ 1 – nirdhāraṇāt darśanācca | nirdhāraṇaṃ tāvat,
' te te vidyācita eva | vidyayā haivaita evaṃvidaścitā bhavanti '
iti | vāṅmanaścakṣurādivyāpārāṇām iṣṭakādivat cayanānupapatteḥ, manasā sampādi- tāgnitvena vidyārūpatve siddhe'pi, 'vidyācita eva ' vidyayā haivete' iti ca avadhāraṇaṃ,

vidyāmayakratvanvayena vidyārūpatvajñāpanārtham - iti niścīyate |

dṛśyate ca atraiva, 'eṣāṃ' śeṣī vidyārūpaḥ kratuḥ ;
' te manasaivā'dhīyanta manasaivā'cīyanta manasaiṣu grahā agṛhyanta manasā'stuvanta manasā'śaṃsan | yatkiñca yajñe karma kriyate yatkiṃca 'yajñīyaṃ' karma manasaiva teṣu manomayeṣu manaścitsu manomayamakriyata'
iti | ' iṣṭakaciteṣvagniṣu, yat kriyāmayaṃ yajñīyaṃ karma kriyate, tat manonirvatryeṣu manaścādyagniṣu manomayamevā'kriyata iti vacanāt ṛturapi vidyāmayo'tra pratīyate || 46 ||

nanu – atra vidhipadāśravaṇāt phalasambandhāpratīteśca iṣṭakacitānyupasthāpita- kriyāmayaṛtuprakaraṇāt, vidyāmayakatvanvayena vidyārūpatā eṣāṃ 'bādhyeta' ! netyāha-
401. śrutyādibalīyastvācca na bādhaḥ || 3-3-47 || (si)

śruti - liṅga vākyānāṃ prakaraṇād balīyastvena tadanvayaśca durbalena prakaraṇena bādhituṃ na śakyate | śrutistāvat-
' te haite vidyācita eva
iti | 'tāṃ' vivṛṇoti-

śrutyādyavagataḥ ṛtuḥ, eṣāṃ

vidyayā hai evaṃvidaścitā bhavanti '
iti | vidyayā - vidyāmayena ṛtunā sambaddhā: 'manacidādayaḥ citā' bhavantītyarthaḥ | tān hai tānevaṃvide 'sarvadā' sarvāṇi bhūtāni 'cinvantyapi khapate '

iti liṅgam | vākyaṃ ca ' evaṃvide cinvanti ' iti | samabhivyāhāro vākyam | evaṃvide - vidyāmakratumate, sarvadā sarvāṇi bhūtāni 'cinvantī' tyarthaḥ | sarvabhūta kartṛka sarvakālavyāpi cayanaṃ manasā 'sampāditaṃ', parimitakartṛkāla kriyāmayeṣṭakacitakārya dvāreṇa kratvanu' praveśasambhava' malabhamānaṃ, vidyāmayakratvanupraveśe liṅgaṃ bhavati || 47 |

402 anubandhādibhyaḥ prajñāntarapṛthaktvavaddṛṣṭaśca
taduktam || 3-3-48 || (si)

iṣṭakacitānvayinaḥ kriyāmayāt kratoḥ, vidyāmayaḥ ayaṃ kratuḥ pṛthaktvena, anubandhādibhyaḥ pṛthaktvahetubhyaḥ avagamyate | anubandhāḥ – yajñānubandhino graha - stotra - śastrādayaḥ ;

' manasaiṣu grahā na gṛhyanta manasā'stuvanta manasā'śaṃsan ityādinā pratipāditā: | 'ādi' śabdena śrutyādayaḥ pūrvoktāḥ gṛhyante | 'śrutyādibhiḥ ' sānubandhaiḥ, vidyāmayaḥ kratuḥ pṛthagavagamyate ityarthaḥ | prajñāntara pṛthaktvavat - yathā prajñāntaraṃ – daharavidyādi, kriyāmayāt kratoḥ pṛthagbhūtaṃ śrutyādibhiravagamyate; evam ayamapi | evaṃ ca anubandhādibhiḥ pṛthagbhūte vidyāmaye yajñe avagate sati, vidhiḥ parikalpyate | dṛṣṭaśca anuvāda' sarūpeṣu' kalyyamāno vidhiḥ | taduktam-
'vacanāni tvapūrvatvāt '

iti | phalaṃ ca,

'' teṣāmekaika eva' tāvān yāvānasau pūrva:' ityatideśāt, svakratudvāreṇa iṣṭakacitasyāgneḥ yat phalaṃ tadeva manaśvidādīnāmapi svaṛtudvāreṇa phalamityavagamyate || 48 ||
dṛṣṭaśceti | sānubandhaiḥ śrutyādibhiḥ vidyāmayakratoravagatatvāt śrutānaprasaṅgaparihārārtham | vidhiḥ kalpyata iti bhāvaḥ | yat phalamiti | phalakāmanayā kṛtaṃ cet ! tatphalāvāptiḥ | anyathā cet muktiphalamiti bhāvaḥ || 48 ||

kriyāmayāt pṛthagayaṃ pṛthakstotrāditaḥ śruteḥ | dahādibhedavad dṛṣṭo'nuvāde'pi vidhirnanu || ' ' svarūpeṣu - da. * teṣāmevaikaika; – ṅa.

yat punaḥ - atideśena tulyakāryatvāvagamāt kriyāmayakatvanupraveśo'vagamyate-

ityuktam ; tatrāha -

403. na sāmānyādapyupalabdhermṛtyuvannahi lokāpattiḥ || 3-3-49 || (si)

na avaśyam, atideśāt avāntaravyāpārasyāpi tulyatayā bhavitavyam ; yena kriyāmayakratvanupraveśaḥ eṣāṃ syāt ! ; yasmātkasmāccit sāmānyamātrāt atideśopa- labdheḥ | upalabhyate hi !
sa eṣa eva mṛtyurya epa etasmin maṇḍale puruṣa: ' ityādiṣu saṃhartṛtvādisāmānyamātrāt atideśaḥ | na hi tatra maṇḍalapuruṣasya mṛtyuvat tallokāpattiḥ – taddeśaprāptirapi bhavati |

evam ihāpi manavidādīnām iṣṭakacitāgnivadbhāvātideśamātreṇa iṣṭakacitāgni- deśarūpakriyāmayatratvanupraveśenā'pi na bhavitavyam | ataḥ iṣṭakacitāgneḥ svakratudvā reṇa yat phalaṃ tadeva manaścidādīnāmapi vidyāmayakratudvāreṇa phalamiti, atideśādava- gamyate || 49 ||

404. pareṇa ca śabdasya tādvidhyaṃ
bhūyastvāttvanubandhaḥ || 3-3-50 || (si)
pareṇa ca brāhmaṇena, asyā'pi manaśvidādyabhidhāyinaḥ śabdasya, tādvidhyam- tadvidhatvam, 'vidyāmayapratipāditvam' avagamyate | pareṇa hi brāhmaṇena, 'ayaṃ vāva loka eṣo'gniḥ citaḥ | tasyā''pa eva pariśritaḥ | '

ityādinā,

sayo haitadevaṃ veda lokampṛṇām .... evaṃbhūtametatsarvamabhisampadyate '

iti pṛthakphalā vidhaiva vidhīyate | tathā vaiśvānara' vidyādau ca vidhaiva vidhīyate | ataḥ agnirahasyasya kriyaikaviṣayatvaṃ nā'sti |

evaṃ tarhi ! vidyāmayāḥ manaścidādayaḥ bṛhadāraṇyake 'anubaddhavyāḥ', kimartham iha | 'nubadhyante ? tatrocyate – 'bhūyastvā svanubandhaḥ ' - iti | manaścidādiṣu sampāda - nīyānām agnyaṅgānāṃ bhūyastvāt, tatsannidhau iha anubandhaḥ kṛtaḥ || 50 ||

iti pūrvavikalpādhikaraṇam || 20 ||

śarīre bhāvādhikaraṇam || 3-3-21 ||
405. eka ātmanaḥ śarīre bhāvāt || 3-3-51 || (pū)

sarvāsu paravidyāsu, upāsyopāsanasvarūpavat upāsakasvarūpasyā'pi jñātavyatva-

muktam ;

'trayāṇāmeva caivamupanyāsaḥ praśnaśca'
iti | vakṣyati ca asya pratyagātmanaḥ paramātmātmakatvenā'nusandhānam ;

'ātmeti tūpagacchanti grāhayanti ca'

iti ||

kimayaṃ pratyagātmā, jñātā kartā bhoktā ihāmutra sañcārakṣamaḥ anusandheyaḥ ? uta prajāpativāyoditāpahatapāpmatvādisvarūpaḥ 1 ||

-

kiṃ yuktam ? ' jñātṛtvādyākāramātra ḥ 1 – iti eke manyante ||

kutaḥ ? asya upāsakasyā''tmanaḥ śarīre bhāvāt ātmanaḥ śarīre vartamānasya, tā (yā) dṛśameva rūpam ; tāvataivā'nusandhānena tatphalasiddhayupapatteśca ||

evaṃ prāpte, pracakṣmahe -

406. vyatirekastadbhāvabhāvitvānnatūpalabdhivat || 3-3-52 || (si)
natvetadasti ; yat jñātṛtvādyākāra evā'nusandheyaḥ - iti | asyātmanaḥ saṃsāra- 'daśāyāḥ ' ' mokṣadaśāyāṃ yo' vyatirekaḥ, saḥ apahatapāpmatvādikaḥ anusandheyaḥ | asya mokṣadaśāyāṃ yādṛśaṃ rūpam ; tādṛgrūpa eva upāsanavelāyām ātmā anusandheyaḥ ityarthaḥ |

kutaḥ 1 tadbhāvabhāvitvāt – 'tadrūpāpatteḥ' |
yathākraturasmi~lloke puruṣo bhavati, tathetaḥ pretya bhavati' ' taṃ yathā yathopāsate 'tathaiva bhavati '
iti yathopāsanameva hi ! prāptiḥ śrūyate |

na ca – 'parasva' rūpamātra viṣayamevedam - iti vaktuṃ śakyate ; pratyagātmano'pi upāsyabhūta parabrahma'śarīratayā upāsya koṭinikṣiptatvāt | ataḥ - prajāpativākyoditāpa- hatapāpmatvādiguṇakapratyagātma'śarīra' paramātmopāsanasya, tathārūpameva ' prāpyam' – ityuktaṃ

bhavati ||

ata eva,

' evaṃ kraturha amuṃ lokaṃ pretyā'bhisambha (vitāsmi ) vati'
ityucyate | tasmāt pratyagātmā prāpyākāra eva anusandheyaḥ |

? 'upalabdhivat - brahmopa-

labdhivat' | yathā brahmopalabdhiḥ vihitā yathāvasthitabrahmasvarūpaviṣayā; tathā ātmopa- labdhirapi yathāvasthitātmasvarūpaviṣayetyarthaḥ |

aṅgāvabaddhādhikaraṇam || 3-3-22 ||
407. aṅgāvabaddhāstu na śākhāsu hi prativedam || 3-3-53 || (si)

'omityetadakṣaramudgīthamupāsīta '
lokeṣu pañcavidhaṃ sāmopāsīta '
ukthamukthamiti vai prajā vadanti | tadidamevoktham iyameva pṛthivī '

"ayaṃ vā va loka' eṣo'gnicitaḥ
-ityevamādyāḥ ṛtvaṅgāśrayā upāsanāḥ bhavanti |

tāḥ kiṃ yāsu śākhāsu śrayante tāsveva niyatāḥ ? uta sarvāsu śākhāsu udgīthādiṣu sambadhyante 1

iti vicāraḥ |

sarvavedāntapratyayatve sthite'pi prativedaṃ svarabhedāt, udgīthādayo bhidyanta iti, tatra tatra vyavatiṣṭheranniti 'yuktā śaṅkā' |

kiṃ yuktam 1 vyavatiṣṭheran - iti |

kutaḥ ? ' udgīthamupāsīta ' iti sāmānyenodgīthasambandhitayā śrutāyāḥ, tasyāmeva śākhāyāṃ svara'viśeṣa' yuktasyo' gītha viśeṣasya ' saṃnidhānāt tasminneva viśeṣe paryavasānaṃ yuktamiti evamādyāḥ tāsveva śākhāsu vyavatiṣṭheran - iti ||

evaṃ prāpte, pracakṣmahe - ' aṅgāvabaddhāstu' iti | 'tu' śabda: pakṣa vyāvartayati | na hi ! udgīthādyaṅgāvabaddhāḥ upāsanāḥ, tāsveva śākhāsu vyavatiṣṭheran; api tu prativedaṃ sambadhyeran, sarvāsu śākhāvityarthaḥ | 'hi' śabdoṃ hetau | yasmāt śrutyaiva udgīthādyaṅga- mātrāvabaddhāḥ; tasmāt yatra udgīthādayaḥ tatra sarvatra sambadhyeran |

yadyapi, svarabhedena udgīthavyaktayo bhidyante ; tathā'pi, 'sāmānyenodgītha 'śrutyā sarvā vyaktayaḥ saṃnihitā iti, na kvacit vyavasthāyāṃ pramāṇamasti | sarvaśākhāpratyaya-

vyaktiviśeṣasaṃnidhiścet ! tasyaiva 'udgītha 'śabdavācyatvāt tattacchākhā viśeṣeṣu upāsanavyavasthā | sarvavyaktisaṃnidhiścet | 'udgītha 'śabdasya udgīthamātravācitvāt tadviṣayo- pāsanāni na vyavatiṣṭhante iti |

sarvavedānteti | yathā karma ca upāsanaṃ ca sarvaśākhāsu ekam ; tathā udgīthasyāpi ekatvāt sarvatra udgīthānusāreṇa prāpnuvanti upāsanāni - iti adhikaraṇārambhākṣepaḥ | uhīthādīnāṃ svarabhedena vyaktibhedāt, tattatsvara viśeṣa viśiṣṭavyaktiviṣayāḥ upāsanā: ? uta sarvodgīthavyaktiviṣayāḥ ? iti saṃśayaḥ syāditi ākṣepasamādhirityarthaḥ |
tasminneveti | loke' gāmānaye' tyādiṣu sāmānyaśabdānāṃ saṃnihita viśeṣaparyantatva- darśanāditi bhāvaḥ |
aṅgamātrāvabaddhāḥ – aṅgamātraviṣayāḥ pratīyante | na kvaciditi | 'gāmānaye' tyādiṣu

gībhasya - ṅa. sāmānyādudgītha - bhā.

'nyāyena ca, ' sarvāsu śākhāsu ṛturekaḥ | ataḥ sarvāsu śākhāsu ekasya ṛtoḥ saṃnidhānāt, ṛtvaṅgabhūtodgīthādayo'pi saṃnihitā iti, na ekasya saṃnidhiviśeṣo'stīti, na vyavasthā || 53 ||
408. mantrādivadvā'virodhaḥ || 3-3-54 || (si)
' vā 'śabdaḥ cārthe | 'ādi' śabdena jāti-guṇa-saṅkhyā- sādṛśya-krama- dravya- karmāṇi gṛhyante | yathā mantrādīnām ekaikaśākhākhānātānāmapi śeṣiṇaḥ kratoḥ sarvaśākhāsu ekatvena yathāyathaṃ śrutyādibhiḥ sarvāsu śākhāsu viniyogo na virudhyate ; tadvat ihāpyavirodhaḥ || 54 ||

iti aṅgāvabaddhādhikaraṇam || 22 ||

bhūmajyāyastvādhikaraṇam || 3-3-23 ||
409. bhūmnaḥ kratuvajjyāyastvaṃ tathā hi darśayati || 3-3-55|| (si)

'prācīnaṃśāla aupamanyavaḥ'
ityārabhya vaiśvānara vidyā āmnātā | tatra vaiśvānaraḥ paramātmā trailokyaśarīra upāsyaḥ śrutaḥ, svarlokādityavāyvākāśātpṛthivya 'vayavaḥ | tatra ca - dyauḥ mūrdhā, ādityaḥ cakṣuḥ, vāyuḥ prāṇaḥ, ākāśaḥ sandehaḥ - madhyakāya ityarthaḥ | āpo vastiḥ | pṛthivī pādau - ityavayava viśeṣāḥ ||

tatra saṃśayaḥ - kimasya trailokyaśarīrasya vyastasya upāsanaṃ kartavyam 9 uta vyastasya samastasya ca ? atha samastasyaiva 1 - iti ||

kutaḥ ? upakrame vyastopāsanopadeśāt | tathā hi ! upadiśyate ; aupamanya- vādayaḥ kila uddālakaṣaṣṭhāḥ, kekayam aśvapatim upasadya,
ātmānamevemaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ sampratyadhyeṣi, tameva no brūhi' iti papracchuḥ | sa ca tebhyaḥ pratyekaṃ svopāsyān dyuprabhṛtīn uktavadbhayaḥ, mūrdhādiṣu 'vyasteṣūpāsanaṃ, tatra tatra' phalaṃ ca uktavān |

atyannaṃ paśyati priyaṃ bhavatyasya brahmavarcasaṃ kule, ya etamevamātmānaṃ vaiśvānara- mupāste, mūrdhā tveṣa ātmanaḥ '

iti |

eṣa vai sutejā ātmā vaiśvānaraḥ '
ityādinā, teṣu teṣūpāsaneṣu upāsyasya vaiśvānaratvaṃ cāssha | ataḥ 'vyastasyaivo 'pāsanaṃ kartavyam | paratra,
' yastvetamevaṃ prādeśamātramabhivimānamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramupāste ' iti, dyuprabhṛtipradeśāvacchinnamātre vaiśvānare uktasya mūrdhādyupāsanasya samāsenopasaṃhāraḥ-

ityavagantavyam |

apara āha—evameva samastasyā'pyupāsanaṃ kāryam - iti | pṛthakphalanirdeśāt,

' yastvetamevaṃ prādeśamātramabhi' vimānamātmānaṃ ' vaiśvānaramupāste ; sa sarveṣu lokeṣu
sarveṣu bhūteṣu sarveṣvātmasvannamatti '
iti | na ca - etāvatā vākya bhedaḥ ; yathā bhūmavidyopakrame, nāmādyupāsanaṃ 'tatphalaṃ ' cā'bhidhāya,
eṣa tu vā ativadati, yaḥ satyenā'tivadati '

ityādinā bhūmavidyāmupadiśya,

' sa svarāibhavati, tasya sarveṣu lokeṣu kāmacāro bhavati' iti tatphalaṃ ca vyapadiśati ; tatra bhūmavidyā' paratve'pi ' vākyasya, nāmādyavāntaropāsanaṃ 'tatphalaṃ ' cā'ṅgīkriyate ; tathā ihāpi - iti ||

vaiśvānarātmavedanam alabhamānāḥ tena ca saha aśvapatiṃ kekayaṃ vaiśvānarātmavedinam 'upasaṅgamya',
ātmānamevemaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ sampratyadhyepi ; tameva no brūhi' iti pṛṣṭvā, tatsakāśāt, - paramātmānaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ svarlokādi pṛthivyantaśarīram, upāsya- mavagamya, tatphalaṃ ca sarvaloka-sarvabhūta- 'sarvātmāna' bhūta brahmānubhavam avagatavantaḥ - ityupa- " saṃhārataḥ ", vākyasya ekatvamavagamyate |

3.

evam ekavākyatve avagate sati, 'avayavaviśeṣeṣūpāstivacanaṃ phalanirdeśatha, 'samastopāsanaikadeśānuvāda' mātram ' iti niścīyate ||

ṛtuvat - yathā,

vaiśvānaraṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ nirvapet putre jāte '
iti 'vihitasyaiva' kratoḥ ekadeśāḥ,
' yadaṣṭākapālo bhavati'
' ityādibhiranūdyante' ; tathā samastopāsanameva nyāyyam ; na vyastopāsanam | tathā hi darśayati- 'iyaṃ śrutiḥ' vyastopāsane anartha bruvatī ;

mūrdhā te vyapatiṣyat ; yanmāṃ nā''gamiṣyaḥ'

iti,

'andho bhaviṣyaḥ, yanmāṃ nā''gamiṣyaḥ'

ityādikā |

ataḥ, idamapyapāstam - yat nāmādyupāsanasāmyamuktam | tatra hi ! nāmādyupāsa- neṣu anartho na śrutaḥ | nāmādyupāsanebhyaḥ bhūmopāsanasya atiśayitaphalatvaṃ śrutam ;
eṣa tu vā ativadati, yaḥ satyenā'tivadati'
iti | tata eva tatra bhūmavidyā paratve'pi vākyasya nāmādyupāsanānāṃ saphalānāṃ vivakṣitatvam | anyathā atiśayitaphalatvanimittātivādena bhūmavidyāstutyanupapatteḥ | ataḥ samastopāsanameva nyāyyam || 55 ||

|

iti bhūmajyāyastvādhikaraṇam || 23 ||

śabdādibhedādhikaraṇam || 3-3-24 ||
410. nānā śabdādibhedāt || 3-3-56 ||

eka-

kimatra vidyakyam 1 uta vidyābhedaḥ 1 - iti saṃśayyate ||

atraiva āsa parasparabhede samarthite sati, ekasyāḥ daharavidyā dikāyāḥ sarvavedānta- pratyayanyāyaḥ ||

kiṃ yuktam ? vidyaikyamiti |

śrīmāṣyam 3.3.24]

411. vikalpo'viśiṣṭaphalatvāt || 3-3-57 || (si)

brahmaprāptiphalānāṃ sadvidyā - dahara vidyādīnāṃ nānātvamuktam 1
idānīm - āsāṃ vidyānām 'ekasmin puruṣe prayojanavatvena samuccayo'pi saṃbhavati ? uta prayojanābhāvādvikalpa eva 1 iti 'viśaye'

kutaḥ 1–‘aviśiṣṭaphalatvāt ' - | sarvāsāṃ hi brahmavidyānām, '-
śayānandabrahmānubhavaḥ phalaṃ aviśiṣṭaṃ śrūyate

anavadhi kāti-

'brahmavidāpnoti param '

' sa eko brahmaṇa ānandaḥ | śrotriyasya cākāmahatasya '

yadā paśyaḥ paśyate rukmavarṇa kartāramīśaṃ puruṣaṃ brahmayonim |
tadā vidvān puṇyapāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti' ityādibhyaḥ ||

brahma hi ! svasya parasya ca 'svaya' manubhūyamānam, anavadhikātiśayānandaṃ bhavati | sa ca tādṛśo brahmānubhavaḥ, ekayā vidyayā avāpyate cet ! kimanyayeti, "na samuccaya' sambhavaḥ | svargādehiṃ deśataḥ kālataḥ svarūpataśca parimitatvena, tatra deśādyapekṣayā bhūyastvasambhavāt tadarthinaḥ samuccayaḥ 'sambhavati' | iha tu tadviparītasvarūpe brahmaṇi, tanna sambhavati | sarvāśca vidyāḥ brahmānubhavavirodhyanādikarmā- vidyānirasanamukhena brahmaprāptiphalā iti, aviśiṣṭaphalatvāt, sarvāsāṃ vikalpa eva || 57 ||
brahmaprāptivyatiriktaphalāstu vidyāḥ, svargādiphalakarmavat yatheṣṭaṃ 'vikalperan', samuccīyeran vā ; tāsāṃ parimita' phalatvena bhūyastvāpekṣā sambhavāt | tadāha-

kutaḥ ? -- tatsahabhāvāśruteḥ - udgīthāṅgabhāvāśruteḥ ityarthaḥ | aṅgabhāve hi ! sahabhāvaniyamo bhavati |

yadyapi - 'udgīthamupāsīte' tyasmin padasamudāye, adhikārāntaraṃ na pratīyate ; tathāpi tadanantarameva,
'yadeva vidyayā karoti śraddhayopaniṣadā tadeva vīryavattaraṃ bhavati '

iti, 'vidyāyāḥ' ṛtuvīryavattaratvaṃ prati sādhanabhāvaḥ pratipādyate | tena ṛtuphalāt pṛthagbhūtaphalasādhanabhūtā vidyā, 'udgīthamupāsīta' iti kartavyatayā vidhīyate | ṛtuphalāt pṛthagbhūtaphalasādhanatayā'vagatasya upāsanasya, kratvaṅgabhūtodgīthāṅgatayā vini- yogo nopapadyate ||

'atha'—upāsanasya āśrayāpekṣāyāṃ saṃnihita udgīthaḥ āśrayamātraṃ bhavati ; udgīthaśca ṛtvaṅgabhūta iti, ṛtuprayuktodgīthā' dyāśraye' upāsane, kratvadhikāriṇa eva kratorvīryavattaratvecchā nimittamidam' adhikārāntaramiti, na ṛtuṣu tadupādānaniyamaḥ | vīryavattaratvaṃ ca ṛtuphalasya prabalakarmāntaraphalena apratibandhaḥ - ityuktam | -ityuktam | ṛtoḥ

avilambitaphalatvamityarthaḥ |

parṇatādīnāṃ tu, 'yadeva vidyayā karoti....nadeva vīryavattaraṃ bhavati' iti, vidyāyāḥ phalasādhana' tvavat', apāpaślokaśravaṇādiphalaṃ prati sākṣātsādhanabhāvo na śruta iti, kratvaṅgabhūtajuhvādyaṅgatayā viniyogāvirodhāt, tadaṅgabhūtānāṃ phalāntarasādhanabhāva- kalpanānupapatteḥ, 'tatra phalaśrutiḥ " arthavādamātraṃ syāt || 63 ||

419. puruṣārtho'taḥ śabdāditi bādarāyaṇaḥ || 3-4-1 || (si)
guṇopasaṃhārānusaṃhāraphalā vikatva - nānātvacintā kṛtā | idānīṃ - 'vidyātaḥ ' puruṣārthaḥ ? uta 'vidyāṅgakāt karmaṇaḥ 1 - iti cintyate |

kiṃ yuktam 1 ataḥ - 'vidyātaḥ, puruṣārtha:' iti, bhagavān bādarāyaṇo

manyate | kutaḥ ? śabdāt |

yate hi ! aupaniṣadaḥ śabdaḥ, vidyātaḥ 'puruṣārtha '

bruvan :

'brahmavidāpnoti param '

'vedā'hametaṃ puruṣaṃ mahāntamādityavarṇaṃ tamasaḥ parastāt |
tamevaṃ vidvānamṛta iha bhavati nānyaḥ panthā vidyate'yanāya ||' 'yathā nadyaḥ syandamānāḥ samudre'staṃ gacchanti nāmarūpe vihāya ||
tathā vidvān nāmarūpa dvimuktaḥ parāt paraṃ puruṣamupaiti divyam ||' ityādiḥ || 1 ||
kiṃ vidyātaḥ puruṣārtha: : utaḥ ? vidyāṅgakāt karmaṇaḥ ? iti | kiṃ vidyā kratukartṛbhūtātmasaṃskāradvāreṇa ṛtvartha : uta apunarāvṛttiphalasādhanatayā puruṣārthā | kiṃ 'brahmavidāpnoti' ityādayaḥ phalavidhayaḥ ? uta arthavādā : : kiṃ paravidyāsu karmībhūtaḥ jīvaḥ ? uta tadanyaḥ paramātmā ? | kim ācāradarśanādiliṅgānugṛhītaṃ tattvamasyā disāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ, upāsyasya jīvabhāvamavagamayati ? | na ? iti | kim idaṃ liṅgam anaikāntikam ? na ?- iti ||

anaikāntikatvābhāve, tadanugṛhītasāmānādhikaraṇyena upāsyasya jīvabhāvāvagamāt paravidyānāṃ svakarmabhūtajīvasaṃskāratvāt ātmajñānaphalaśruteḥ arthavādatayā, pṛthakphalasādhana- tvābhāvāt, vidyā kratvartheti, tadaṅgakāt karmaṇaḥ puruṣārthaḥ syāt | liṅgasya anaikāntikatve, tadanugrahābhāvena sāmānādhikaraṇyasya jīvānatirekānavagamakatvāt paravidyānāṃ jīvakarmakatvā- bhāvena tatsaṃskāratvābhāvāt, ātmavidyāphalaśruteḥ arthavādatvābhāvena pṛthakphalasādhanatayā puruṣārthatvāt vidyātaḥ puruṣārthasiddhiḥ syāt ||
idaṃ ca siddhāntopakramam | vidyāyāḥ pūrvapāde prastutatvāt 'ataḥ ' śabdena sotā ||
'brahmaviditi vākye vidyayā prāpyāvāptiruktā | 'vedā'ha 'mityatra vidyāyā * brahmaprāptihetutvaṃ pratibandhanivṛtti ra kamityarthaḥ | tatra brahmaṇaḥ vigrahādiviśiṣṭatvam upāyāntara- niṣevaśva siddhaḥ | 'yathā nadya' iti vākye ubhayamapyabhihitam || 1 ||

'atra' pūrvapakṣī pratyavatiṣṭhate-
420. śeṣatvāt puruṣārthavādo yathā'nyaṣviti jaiminiḥ || 3-4-2 || (pū)
naitadevam - yat vidyātaḥ puruṣārthāvāptiḥ śabdādavagamyate iti | na hyeṣaḥ 'brahmavidānoti param ' ityādiśabdaḥ, vedanāt puruṣārthāvāptim avagamayati ; karmasu kartṛbhūtasyā''tmanaḥ, yāthātmyavedanapratipādanaparatvāt | ataḥ kartuḥ saṃskāra- dvāreṇa vidyāyāḥ ṛtuśeṣatvāt, tatra phalaśrutiḥ' arthavādamātram ; yathā anyeṣu 'dravyādiṣu' – iti jaiminirācāryo manyate | taduktam-

'dravya' - saṃskāra- karmasu pararārthatvāt phalaśrutirarthavādaḥ syāt '

iti |

nanu ca ' karmasu karturjīvādanyaḥ, mumukṣubhiḥ prāpyatayā vedānteṣu vedya upadiśyate ' iti prāgevopapāditam ;

bhedavyapadeśācca '

421. ācāradarśanāt || 3-4-3 || (pū)
brahmavidāṃ prādhānyena karmasva ācāro dṛśyate - aśvapatiḥ kekayaḥ kila ! ātmavittamaḥ, tadvijñānāyopagatān tānṛṣīn pratyāha-
yakṣyamāṇo ha vai bhagavanto'hamasmi '
iti | " tathā " janakādayo brahmavidagresarāḥ, karmaniṣṭhāḥ 'smṛtiṣu dṛśyante ; 'karmaṇaiva hi ! saṃsiddhimāsthitā janakādayaḥ'
iyāja so'pi subahūn yajñān jñānavyapāśrayaḥ'
iti | ataḥ brahmavidāṃ karmapradhānatvadarśanāt vidyāyāḥ kartṛ svarūpavedanarūpatvena karmāṅgatvameveti, na vidyātaḥ puruṣārthaḥ || 3 ||

427. tulyaṃ tu darśanam || 3-4-9 || (si)

430. adhyayanamātravataḥ || 3-4 -12 || (si) yaduktam - vidyāvataḥ karmavidhānāt vidyā karmāṅgam - iti; naitadyuktam ; 'vedamadhītya' iti adhyayanamātravato vidhānāt ||

na ca adhyayanavidhireva arthāvabodhe pravartayati ; ādhānavat adhyayanasya akṣararāśigrahaṇamātre paryavasānāt | gṛhītasya ca svādhyāyasya phalavatkarmāvibodhitva- darśanāt, tannirṇayale tadarthavicāre puruṣaḥ svayameva pravartate | tataḥ karmārthī karmajñāne pravartate ; mokṣārthī ca brahmajñāne - iti, na vidyā karmāṅgam |

taṃ vidyā....iti samanvārambhaṇaśrutiḥ sāṃsārika phala vidyā- karmaviṣayā | brahmavidyā- tadaṅgakarmaviṣayā vā syādiyaṃ śrutiḥ | 'nehābhikramanāśo'sti' iti nyāyāt, prārabdhāvasāne muktidānāyā'nveti | karma ca janmāntare'pi vidyotpādanāyā'nvetī- tyarthaḥ || 11 ||

vidyā

435. ūrdhvaretassu ca śabde hi ! || 3-4-17 || (si) ūrdhvaretassu āśrameṣu brahmavidyādarśanāt teṣu agnihotra-darśapūrṇamāsādikarmā- bhāvācca na vidyā karmāṅgam |

436. parāmarśa jaiminiracodanā cā'pavadati hi || 3-4-18 || (si)

naitadupapadyate yataḥ 'trayo dharmaskandhāḥ ' ' ityādiṣu' vākyeṣu teṣāmāśramāṇāṃ parāmarśamātraṃ kriyate – anuvādamātramityarthaḥ |

gṛhasthāśramasāmyāccā'pyanuṣṭheyaṃ budhairiha | anyāśramaṃ śrutiścāha dharmaskandhāstrayastviti || ' ' ityevamādau - da. ' aurdhva - da. 3 ityevamādiṣu - vā.

'trayo dharmaskandhāḥ '
iti tritvena 'sagṛhya',

....

'brahmasaṃstho'mṛtatvameti'

iti paratra śrayamāṇo 'brahmasaṃstha' śabdaḥ, yaugikaḥ sarvāśramasādhāraṇaḥ sarveṣāmāśramiṇāṃ brahmasaṃsthāsambhavāt | brahmaṇi saṃsthā - saṃsthiti: brahmasaṃsthatvam | tacca sarveṣāṃ sambhava- tyeva | brahmaniṣṭhāvi 'kalāḥ kevalāśramiṇaḥ puṇyalokabhājaḥ | teṣveva brahmaniṣṭhaḥ amṛtatva bhāy bhavati | tadetat vispaṣṭamuktaṃ bhagavatā parāśareṇa
' prājāpatyaṃ 'brāhmaṇānām'

ityārabhya,

brāhma sannyāsināṃ smṛtam'

ityantena, varṇānām āśramāṇāṃ ca kevalānāṃ brahmalokaprātyantaṃ phalamabhidhāya, ekāntinaḥ sadā brahmadhyāyino yogino hi ye |
teṣāṃ tat paramaṃ sthānaṃ yadvai paśyanti sūrayaḥ || '
iti, teṣveva brahmaniṣṭhānāṃ brahmaprāptimabhidadhatā | ataḥ, gṛhasthāśramatulyāḥ ūrdhvaretasa āśramā api dṛśyanta iti, te'pi anuṣṭheyāḥ |

' ye ce me'raṇye śraddhā tapa ityupa sate'

438. vidhirvā dhāraṇavat || 3-4-20 || (si)
' vā ' śabda: avadhāraṇārthaḥ | vidhirevā'yam āśramāṇām dhāraṇavat - yathā diṣṭāgnihotre
'adhastāt samidhaṃ dhārayannanudravet; upari hi devebhyo dhārayati' ityatra anuvādasarūpādapi vākyāt upari dhāraṇasya aprāptatvādvidhirāśrīyate | taduktaṃ śeṣalakṣaṇe-

iti ; tathā atrāpi aprāptatvāt, vidhirevā'śrayaṇīyaḥ ||

yadi vidyā yajñādyanapekṣaiva amṛtatvaṃ sādhayati | tarhi gṛhastheṣvapi tadanapekṣaiva sādhayitumarhati | yajñādiśrutirapi 'vividiṣanti' iti śabdāt karmaṇo vedanāṅgata na pratipādayati- iti : ata āha— 'sarvāpekṣā' – iti | 'yajñādi sarvakarmāpekṣaiva vidyā, 'karmavatsu gṛhastheṣu' |

ityādinā yajñādayo hi | vidyāṅgatvena śrūyante | yajñādinā vividiṣanti - veditu- micchanti, yajñādimiḥ vedanaṃ prāptumicchantītyarthaḥ | yajñādīnāṃ jñānasādhanatve satyeva, 'yajñādibhiḥ jñānaṃ prāptumicchanti' iti vyapadeśa upapadyate | yathā ase: hananasādhanatve sati, 'asinā jighāṃsati' iti vyapadeśaḥ | ataḥ yajñādīnāṃ jñānasādhanatvamavagamyate ||

jñānaṃ ca - 'vākyārthajñānādarthāntarabhūtaṃ dhyānopāsanādiśabdavācyaṃ viśadatama- 'pratyakṣatāpanna' smṛtirūpaṃ niratiśayapriyam aharaharabhyāsādheyātiśayam āprayāṇādanu- vartamānaṃ mokṣasādhanam ' – ityuktamasmābhiḥ pūrvameva | vakṣyati ca

aśvavat — yathā 'gamana' sādhanabhūto'śvaḥ svaparikarabandhaparikarmāpekṣaḥ evaṃ mokṣasādhana bhūtā'pi vidyā, nitya naimittika karmaparikarāpekṣā' | tadidamāha svayameva bhagavān -

svakarmaṇā tamabhyacya siddhiṃ vindati mānavaḥ ||'

473. na pratīke na hi saḥ || 4-1-4 || (si)

'mano brahmetyupāsīta '
''sa yo nāma ' brahmetyupāste ityādipratīkopāsaneṣvapi ātmatvānusandhānaṃ kāryam ? uta na 1 - iti cintāyām,

mano brahmetyupāsīta '

iti brahmopāsanatvasāmyāt, brahmaṇaśca upāsiturātmatvāt, ātmetyevopāsīta - iti ||

'evaṃ prāpte,' abhidhīyate—'na pratīke' – iti | pratīke na ātmatvānusandhānaṃ kāryam na hi saḥ na hi ! upāsituḥ ātmā pratīkaḥ | pratīkopāsaneṣu pratīka evopāsyaḥ ; 'na brahma' | brahma tu tatra dṛṣṭiviśeṣaṇamātram | pratīkopāsanaṃ hi nāma- abrahmaṇi brahmadṛṣṭā'nusandhānam | tatropāsyasya pratīkasya upāsiturātmatvābhāvāt, na tathā'nusandheyam || 4 ||

nanu - atrāpi brahmaivopāsyam; brahmaṇa upāsya tvasambhave, mana ādīnāma- cetanānām alpaśaktīnāṃ copāsyatvāśrayaṇasya anyāyyatvāt | ataḥ mana ādidṛṣṭayā brahmaivopāsyam vopāsyam - iti; 'tatrāha "

475. ādityādimatayaścāṃga upapatteḥ || 4-1-6 || (si)

'ya evāsau tapati tamudgīthamupāsīta'

ityādiṣu karmāṅgāśrayeṣūpāsaneṣu, saṃśayaḥ – kim udgīthādau karmā ādityādidṛṣṭiḥ kartavyā ? uta ādityādiṣu udgīthādidṛṣṭiḥ 1 - iti ||
'utkṛṣṭe nikṛṣṭadṛṣṭiḥ kartavyā' iti nyāyāt, udgīthādīnāṃ ca phalasādhana- bhūtakarmāṅgatvena aphalebhyaḥ ādityādibhyaḥ utkṛṣṭatvāt, ādityādiṣu udgīthādi- 'dṛṣṭi' ||
ādityeti | devatāyā utkarṣāt dṛṣṭiviśeṣaṇatvamuktam | tadutkarṣākṣepeṇa karmaṇa- evotkarṣaśaṅkayā avāntarasaṅgatiḥ | kratvaṅgāśrayopāsaneṣu - kim ādityādāvudgīthā didṛṣṭiḥ ? | phalasādhanatayā utkarṣaḥ, kim ādityādeḥ ? uta udgīthādeḥ ? | udgīthādeścat ! ādityādāvudgīthādidṛṣṭiḥ | ādityādeścat ! udgīthādau ādityā didṛṣṭiḥ kāryā syāt ||

pradhānasya karmaṇaḥ phalasādhanatayā vihitatvāt devatāprīti: avāntaravyāpāramātraṃ ; kāṣṭhānāṃ jvālāvat iti, aprādhānyamāśaṅkayate | devatāyāḥ phalasādhanatvaṃ pūrvameva siddhamiti, kathaṃ pūrvapakṣotthānam ? || brahmamativat a | dityādimatayo'pi iti 'ca' śabdārthaḥ || 'mano brahma' ityatra upāsyaṃ prathamaṃ dṛṣṭiviśeṣaṇaṃ ca caramaṃ nirdiṣṭam | evam 'ya evāsau tapati tamudgītham' ityatrāpi, udgīthasya caramanirdeśāt, saḥ dṛṣṭiviśeṣaṇam | devatāyāṃ yadvṛttayogāt prāthamyācca uddeśyatvamityapi pūrvapakṣayuktirabhipretā | aphalebhyaḥ- aphalasādhanebhyaḥ ||

iti prāpte, abhidhīyate - 'ādityādimatayaścāṅge' iti | 'ca' śabdaḥ avadhāraṇe | kravaṅge udgīthādau ādityādidṛṣṭaya eva kāryāḥ |
kutaḥ 1 upapatteḥ- ādityādīnāmeva utkṛṣṭatvopapatteḥ | ādityādi devatā- rādhanadvāreṇa hi ! karmaṇāmapi phalasādhanatvam | ataḥ taddaṣṭiḥ udgīthādyaṅge ||6||

iti ādityādimatyadhikaraṇam || 4 ||

tiṣṭhet ||

kutaḥ 1 ; sambhavāt | āsīnasyaiva hi ! ekāgracittatāsambhavaḥ; sthiti gatyoḥ prayatnasāpekṣatvāt, 'śayāne' ca nidrāsambhavāt | ' paścārdha' dhāraṇa prayatna 'nivṛttaye, ' " sāpāśraye' āsīnaḥ kuryāt || 7 ||
mokṣetyādi | anantarādhikaraṇayoḥ prāsaṅgikatvāt, paramaprakṛtena pradhānabhūtena brahmopāsanena saṅgatiruktā | yadvā dṛṣṭayupāsanānāmapi āsana sāpekṣatve'pi, āsanasamarthana hetoraikāgryasya ādyādhikaraṇasamarthitatvāt tena saṅgatiruktā | kim āsīnaścintayet ? uta āsīnaḥ, śayānaḥ, tiṣṭhan gacchan vā ? iti | kiṃ gamanādiṣu dhyānaṃ sambhavati ? na vā ? iti | sambhavati cet ! āsanāniyamaḥ | na sambhavati cet ! tanniyamaḥ |

sambhavāditi | upāsanasambhavādityatopi, ekāgratāsambhavāditi ucitam | upāsanasambhavāt ityukte'pi katham ? ityapi śaṅkāyāḥ ekāgratā miprāyeṇa śamayitavyatvāt ||7||

489. vāṅmanasi darśanācchabdācca || 4-2 -1 || (si)

|

500. pratiṣedhāditi cenna śārīrātū spaṣṭo hyekeṣām ||4-2-12 || (si)

iti pṛṣṭaḥ,

'neti hovāca yājñavalkyaḥ ; atraiva samava (nī ) līyante, sa ucchvayatyādhmāyati, ādhmāto mṛtaḥ śete '
iti | ataḥ, vidvān ihaiva amṛtatvaṃ prāpnoti - iti cet !

tanna ; śārīrāt pratyagātmanaḥ prāṇānāmutkrāntirhi ! atra pratiṣidhyate, na śarīrāt |

' na tasya prāṇā utkrāmanti '

' ityatra tacchabdena, ' 'athā'kāmayamānaḥ' iti prakṛtaḥ śārīra eva

parāmṛśyate ; na aśrutaṃ śarīram ||

' tasye 'ti ṣaṣṭhayā, prāṇānāṃ sambandhitvena śārīro nirdiṣṭaḥ ; na tūtkrāntya - pādānatvena | utkrāntyapādānaṃ tu śarīrameva – iti cet !

na; apādānāpekṣāyām, aśrutāt śarīrāt, sambandhitayā śrutasya ātmana eva saṃnihitatvena, apādānatayā'pi grāhyatvāt |
kiṃ ca prāṇānāṃ jīvasambandhitayaiva prajñātānāṃ tatsambandha' kathane prayojanā- bhāvāt, sambandhamātravācinyā ṣaṣṭhayā, 'apādānameva viśeṣa iti niścīyate ; yathā naṭasya śṛṇoti' iti ||

na cātra vivaditavyam ; - spaṣṭo hyekeṣām – mādhyandinānām āmnāye śārīro jīva eva apādānamiti,

yo'kāmo niṣkāma āptakāma ātmakāmaḥ ; na tasmāt prāṇā utkrāmanti '

iti ||

śārīrāt prāṇānāmutkrāntiprasaṅgābhāvāt, 'tatpratiṣedho' nopapadyate - iti cet !

na;

tasya tāvadeva ciram '

iti, viduṣaḥ śarīraviyogakāle brahmasampattivacanena, 'prāṇānāmapi tasmin kāle śarīrāt viduṣaḥ viyoga: prasajyate | tataśca devayānena pathā brahmasampattiḥ nopapadyata iti,
' na tasya prāṇā utkrāmanti '
devayānena pathā brahmaprāpteḥ prāk, 'jīvādviduṣo'pi prāṇā: na 'viśliṣyantī' tyucyate' ||

praśna' pūrvaka' pratyuktāḥ || tatra ca

'apapunarmṛtyuṃ jayati'

iti, apām agnyannatvajñānāt agnijaya eva 'mṛtyujaya ucyate | ato nātra viduṣaḥ prasaṅgaḥ | aviduṣastu prāṇānutkrāntivacanaṃ, ' sthūladehavat prāṇā na muñcanti ; api tu bhūtasūkṣmavat jīvaṃ pariṣvajya gacchanti ' ' iti pratipādayatīti niravadyam || 12 ||

501. smaryate ca || 4-2-13 || (si)

‘ūrdhvamekaḥ sthitasteṣāṃ yo bhittvā sūryamaṇḍalam |
brahmalokamatikramya tena yāti parāṃ gatim ||'

' tejaḥ parasyāṃ devatāyām '

504. tadoko'grajvalanaṃ tatprakāśitadvāro vidyāsāmarthyāt taccheṣagatyanusmṛtiyogācca hārdānugṛhītaḥ śatādhikyā || 4-2 - 16 || (si)

tatredamāmnāyate

505. raśmyanusārī || 4-2 - 17|| (si)

viduṣaḥ hṛdayāt śatādhikayā mūrdhanyanāḍyā 'nirgatasya, ' ādityaraśmīnanusṛtya ādityamaṇḍalagatiḥ śrūyate-

ityavadhāraṇāt | pākṣikatve hi evakāraḥ anarthakaḥ syāt |

tadyathā mahāpatha ātata ubhau grāmau gacchatīmaṃ cāmuṃ ca evamevaita ādityasya raśmaya ubhau lokau gacchantīmaṃ cāmuṃ ca | amuṣmādādityāt pratāyante | 'ta' āsu nāḍīṣu sṛptāḥ | " ābhyo nāḍībhyaḥ pratāyante | te'muṣminnāditye sṛptāḥ'
iti | tasmāt niśyapi sūryaraśmisambhavāt niśi mṛtānāmapi viduṣāṃ raśmyanu- 'sāreṇaiva brahmaprāptirastyeva || 17 ||

509. arcirādinā tatprathiteḥ || 4-3 - 1 || (si)

viduṣa utkrāntasya nāḍīviśeṣeṇa hārdānugrahāt gatyupakramaḥ uktaḥ | tasya gacchataḥ, mārga idānīṃ nirṇīyate ||

tatra śrutiṣu mārgaprakārāḥ 'bahudhā' āmnāyante | chāndogye tāvat yathā puṣkarapalāśa āpo na śliṣyante, evamevaṃvidi pāpaṃ karma na śliṣyate '

ityupakramya brahmavidyāmupadiśya, āmnāyate-

atha 'yaducaivāssmin śavyaṃ kurvanti, 'yadu ca na', acivamevāmisambhavanti, arciṣo'harahna āpūryamāṇapakṣamāpūryamāṇapakṣād yān paṭṭadaṅ ṅeti māsāṃstān, māsebhyaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ sarvatsarādā dityama | dityāccandramasaṃ candramaso vidyutam | tatpuruṣo'mānavaḥ |
sa enān brahma gamayati | eṣa devapatho brahmapathaḥ | etena pratipadyamānā imaṃ mānavamāvata ' nāvartante''

iti | tatraivā'ṣṭame

athaitaireva raśmibhirūrdhvamākramate '
iti | kauṣītakinaśca devayānamārgam anyathā'cīyate—
'sa etaṃ devayānaṃ panthānamāpadyāgnilokamāgacchati, sa vāyulokaṃ sa varuṇalokaṃ ( samādityalokaṃ ) sa indralokaṃ sa prajāpatilokaṃ sa brahmalokam '

iti | tathā bṛhadāraṇyake

'ya evametadvi' duryeceme'raṇye' śraddhāṃ 'satyamupāsate', te'rciṣamabhisambhavanti acipo'harahna āpūryamāṇavakṣamāpūryamāṇādyān paṇmā- sānudaṅṅāditya eti, māsebhyo devalokaṃ devalokādādityamādityādvaidyutaṃ vaidyutāt puruṣo mānasa etya brahmalokān gamayati'

iti | tatraiva punaranyathā-

'yadā vai puruṣo'smāllokāt prati sa vāyumāgacchati, tasmai sa tatra 'vijihīte', yathā rathacakrasya kham | tena sa ūrdhvamākramate | sa ādityamāgacchati | tasmai sa tatra 'vijihīte' | yathā āḍambarasya kham | tena sa ūrdhvamākramate | sa candramasa- māgacchati | tasmai sa tatra 'vijihīte' | yathā dundubheḥ kham ityādi ||

tatra saṃśayaḥ - kim arcirādiḥ eka eva mārgaḥ abhiḥ śrutibhiḥ pratipādyata iti, tenaiva brahma gacchati vidvān ? uta tasmādanye 'anyatra' mārgā iti, tairvā anena- veti aniyamaḥ 1 - iti |

| yauti kaḥ pūrva: pakṣa: ]

iti |

kiṃ yuktam 1 | aniyamaḥ

kutaḥ 1 | anekarūpatvāt, nairapekṣyācca - iti |

evaṃ prāpte, abhidhīyate - arcirādinā - iti | arcirādiḥ eka eva mārgaḥ sarvatra pratipādyate | ataḥ arcirādinaiva gacchati |

kutaḥ 1 | tatprathiteḥ tasyaiva sarvatra prathiteḥ | prathitiḥ prasiddhiḥ | tasyaiva sarvatra pratyabhijñānāt ityarthaḥ ||

pratyabhijñānāt sa eva' mārgaḥ sarvatra nyūnādhikabhāvena pratipādyata iti, vidyā- guṇopasaṃhāravat, anyatroktānām anyatropasaṃhāraḥ kriyate |

chāndogye tāvat, upakosalavidyāyāṃ paścāgnividyāyāṃ ca ekarūpa evā''mnāyate | vājasaneyake ca pañcāgnividyāyāṃ tathaiva arcirādiḥ 'alpāntara' āmnāyate | ataḥ tatrā'pi sa eveti pratīyate | anyatrā'pi sarvatra agnyādityādayaḥ pratyabhijñāyante ||1||

iti acirādyadhikaraṇam || 1 ||

vāyvadhikaraṇam ||| 4- 3-2 ||

510. vāyumabdādaviśeṣa - viśeṣābhyām || 4-3-2 || (si)

hitayoḥ saṃvatsara - devalokayoḥ pañcamyā'bhihitasya śrautakramasya tulyatve'pi, 'arciṣo'harahna āpūryamāṇapakṣamāpūryamāṇapakṣa t yān ' paḍadaḍūṅeti māsāṃstān'
iti, adhikakālānāṃ nyūnakālebhyaḥ uttarottaratvena niveśadarśanāt saṃvatsarasyaiva 'māsādanantaraṃ' buddhau viparivṛtteḥ saṃvatsara eva māsādūrdhvaṃ niveśayitavya iti, tata ūrdhvaṃ devaloka iti niścīyate | anyatra vājasaneyinaḥ-
'yadā vai puruṣo'smāllokāt praiti, sa vāyumāgacchati tasmai sa tatra "vijihīte, " yathā rathacakrasya kham tena 'sa' ūrdhvamākramate sa ādityamā gacchati'

iti, ādityātpūrvaṃ vāyumadhīyate | kauṣītakinastu -

" sa etaṃ devayāna panthānamāpadyā'gnilokamāgacchati sa vāyulokam '
iti, 'agniloka' śabdanirdiṣṭāt arciṣaḥ paraṃ 'vāyumadhīyate' | tatra kauṣītakināṃ- pāṭhakrameṇa, arciṣaḥ paratvena prāptasya vāyoḥ, vājasaneyinām-
'tena sa ūrdhvamākramate sa ādityamāgacchati'
iti, 'ūrdhva' śabda nirdiṣṭa śrautakrameṇa pāṭhakramādbalīyasā, ādityāt pūrva praveśo niścīyate | ataḥ ādityāt pūrva saṃvatsarādū, devaloko vāyuśca prāptau ||

abhigacchet ? uta anarthāntaratvena saṃvatsarādūrdhvaṃ devalokaṃ santaṃ vāyumabhigacchet 1 - iti| |

kiṃ yuktam 1 | bhinnārthatvam ; prasiddheḥ | bhinnārthatve ca 'ūrdhva' śabdena pañcamyā ca ubhayoḥ saṃvatsārādityāntarāle śrutikrameṇa prāptatvāt viśeṣābhāvācca yatheṣṭam | iti prāpte, ucyate - vāyumabdāt - iti | vāyuṃ saṃvatsarādūrdhva 'mabhigacchet ' |
kutaḥ 1 | aviśeṣa - viśeṣābhyāṃ vāyoreva nirdiṣṭatvāt | ' devaloka ' ' śabdo hi' ! aviśeṣeṇa – sāmānyena 'devānāṃ lokaḥ' ityanena rūpeṇa, vāyumabhidhatte |
'sa vāyumāgacchati', tasmai sa tatra'
iti 'vāyu' śabdaḥ, viśeṣeṇa vāyumabhidhatte | ataḥ 'devaloka - vāyu' śabdābhyām aviśeṣa- viśeṣābhyāṃ vāyurevā'bhidhīyata iti, saṃvatsarādūrdhva vāyu' mevā'bhigacchet |

kauṣītakināṃ 'vāyuloka' śabdaśca, 'agniloka' śabdavat 'vāyuścā'sau lokaśca' ti- vyutpattyā vāyumevā'bhidhatte | vāyuśra devānāmāvāsabhūta iti anyatra śrūyate- 'yo'yaṃ pavate 'epa' (eva) devānāṃ 'gṛhāḥ " 1 iti || 2 ||

iti vāyvadhikaraṇam || 2 ||

511. taṭito'dhi varuṇaḥ sambandhāt || 4-3-3 || (si)

kaupīta kinām-

sa etaṃ devayānaṃ panthānamāpadyā'gnilokamāgacchati sa vāyulokaṃ sa varuṇalokaṃ 'sa ādityalokaṃ sa indralokaṃ ' sa prajāpatilokaṃ sa brahmalokam ' ityatra " agniloka' śabda 'sya 'arciṣparyāyatvena' prāthamyam avigītam | vāyośca saṃvatsarārdhvaṃ niveśaḥ, uktaḥ | ādityasyāpyatra prāptapāṭhakramabādhena,
' devalokādādityam 'ādityādvaidyutam ' '
iti vājasaneyako taśrutikramāt ; 'devaloka ' śabdābhi' hitāt vāyorupari niveśaḥ siddhaḥ | idānīṃ varuṇendrādiṣu cintā ||

kim ete varuṇādayaḥ yathāpāṭhaṃ vāyoru niveśayitavyāḥ ? āhosvit

vidyuto'dhi :-
iti viśaye

aciḥ prabhṛtiṣu sarveṣu, 'arciṣoha: ' ityādi śruti kramo'- parodhāt, vidyutaḥ parastācca,
tatpuruṣo'mānavaḥ | sa enān brahma gamayati'
iti vidyutpuruṣasya brahmagamayitṛtvaśravaṇācca, sarvatrā'vakāśābhāvena aprāptau ca, upadeśā- vaiyarthyāya avaśyaṃ kasyacidvādhyatve, pāṭhakramānurodhena vāyoranantaraṃ varuṇo niveśayitavyaḥ | bāyvādityayoḥ kramasya bādhitatvena, indra-prajāpatī api hi ! atraiva niveśayitavyau ||

iti prāpte, ucyate- 'taṭito'dhi varuṇaḥ ' – iti | upariṣṭānniveśayitavyaḥ |

varuṇastāvat vidyuta

kutaḥ 1 - 'sambandhāt ' - meghodaravartitvādvidyutaḥ, varuṇena sambandho loka- vedayoḥ prasiddhaḥ |

etaduktaṃ bhavati - varuṇādīnāmupadeśāvaiyarthyāya, kvacinniveśayitavyatve sati, pāṭhakramādarthakramasya balīyastvāt " vidyuto'dhivaruṇo niveśayitavyaḥ | tataśca amānavasya gamayitṛtvaṃ vyavadhānasaham' ityavagamyate | tasya ca vyavadhāna sahatvāt indrādeśva upadiṣṭasya avaśyaniveśayitavyasya varuṇāduparyupadiṣṭatvāt ', āgantūnām ante niveśayitavyatvācca, varuṇādupari 'indrādiniṃ veśayitavyaḥ - iti || 3 ||

iti varuṇādhikaraṇam || 3||

ātivāhikādhikaraṇam ||4-3-4 ||
512. ātivāhikāsta liṅgāt || 4-3 -4 || (si)

athavā, bhogabhūmaya etāḥ syuḥ ; kālaviśeṣatayā prasiddhānām aharādīnāṃ mārga- cihnatvānupapatteḥ | anyasya ca mārgacihnabhūtasya eteṣāmanabhidhāyakatvāt | bhoga- bhūmitvaṃ ca -
' eta eva lokāḥ yadahorātrāṇyardhamāsā māsā ṛtavaḥ saṃvatsarāḥ ' iti aharādīnāṃ lokatvavacanādupapadyate | ata eva ca kaupītakinaḥ- ' agnilokamāgacchati '
ityādinā lokaśabdānuvidhānena arcirādīn paṭhanti - iti ||

524. sampadyā''virbhāvaḥ svena śabdāt || 4-4-1|| (si)
paraṃ brahmopāsīnānām 'ātmānaṃ ca prakṛtiviyuktaṃ brahmātmakamupāsīnānām, arcirādinā mārgeṇa apunarāvṛttilakṣaṇā 'gati' ruktā | idānīṃ muktānāmaiśvarya- prakāraṃ cintayitumārabhate ||

idamāmnāyate-

'evamevaiṣa samprasādo'smāccharīrāt samutthāya paraṃ jyotirūpasampadya svena rūpeṇās bhiniṣpadyate '
iti | kim asmā charīrāt samutthāya paraṃjyotirupasampannasya devādirūpavat sādhyena rūpeṇa sambandhaḥ anena vākyena pratipādyate ? uta svābhāvikasya svarūpasyā'virbhāvaḥ 1-

|

iti saṃśaye,

sādhyena rūpeṇa sambandhaḥ - iti yuktam | anyathā hi, apuruṣārthāvabodhitvaṃ mokṣaśāstrasya syāt ; svarūpasya svato'puruṣārthatvadarśanāt | na hi ! suṣuptau dehendriya- vyāpāre parateṣu, kevalasyātmasvarūpasya puruṣārthasambandho dṛśyate ||

na ca duḥkhanivṛttimātraṃ paraṃ jyotirupasampannasya puruṣārthaḥ ; yena ' svarūpāvirbhāva eva mokṣa: ' 'ityucyeta'
' sa eko brahmaṇa ānandaḥ | śrotriyasya cākāmahatasya ' 1 ' rasaṃ hyevāyaṃ labdhvā''nandī bhavati' 1
ityādibhyaḥ, muktasya sukhānantyaśravaṇāt ||

na ? iti | kiṃ svarūpasya svato'paricchinnānandatvaṃ sambhavati ? na ? iti | kim ānanda- svarūpasya tirodhānaṃ saṃbhavati ? na ? iti | vaiyarthyamasti na iti ca || yadā vaiyarthya, nandāyogāt śāstrasya na puruṣārthaparyavasānam tadā sādhyena rūpeṇa sambandhaḥ pratipādyaḥ syāt, yadā na abhiniṣpattiśabdavaiyarthya, yadā ca tirodhānasambhavena svato'paricchinnānandatvopapatteḥ - mokṣaśāstrasya puruṣārthaparyavasānam, tadā svābhāvikarūpāvirbhāvaḥ pratipādyaḥ syāt || rūpeṇa- ākāreṇa |
na ca duḥkheti | duḥkhanivṛttimātraṃ mokṣaścet ! saṃsāranirviśeṣaḥ syāt puruṣārthā- puruṣārthamiśratvāt | duḥkhanivṛttiḥ sukhaprāptiśca iṣṭe | sukhanivṛttiḥ duḥkhaprāptiśva aniṣṭe | tatra duḥkhanivṛtteriṣṭatvāt sukhanivṛtteraniṣṭatvāt iṣṭa| niṣṭamiśro mokṣaḥ saṃsāratulya iti ||

kim āvirbhāvaparatve 'abhiniṣpatti' śabdasya yadā ca tirodhānāsambhavena svato'paricchinnā-

na ca - aparicchinnānandarūpa caitanyameva asya svarūpam | tacca saṃsāradaśāyām 'avidyā'tirohitaṃ, paraṃ jyotirūpasampannasya āvirbhavati - iti śakyaṃ vaktum ; jñānasvarūpasya tirodhānāsaṃbhavāt prakāśaparyāyasya jñānasya tirodhānaṃ tadvināśa eve 'ti hi pūrvametroktam ||

na ca prakāśamātrasya ānandatā sambhavati | sukhasvarūpatā hi ānandarūpatā | sukhasvarūpatvaṃ ca 'ātmano'nukūlatvam | prakāśamātrātmavādinaḥ kasya prakāśaḥ- anukūlavedanīyo bhavet 1 iti, prakāśa mātrātmavādinaḥ kathañcidapi ānandasvarūpatā durupapādā ||

svarūpāpattimātre ca ' sādhye, svarūpasya nityaniṣpannatvāt upasampannasya 'svena rūpeṇā'bhiniṣpadyate '
iti vacanam anarthakaṃ syāt | ataḥ apūrveṇa sādhyena rūpeṇa sambadhyate | evaṃ ca, ' abhiniṣpadyate ' iti vacanaṃ mukhyārthameva bhavati |

" " svena rūpeṇe " tyapi.

na bhūtasūkṣmaiḥ saha yāti dehī tatraiva teṣāṃ sulabhāvahetoḥ |
naivaivamāpaḥ puruṣā bhavantītyevaṃ śrutatvāt saha taiḥ prayāti || 1 ||

dehī - jīvātmā, bhūtasūkṣmaiḥ saha – dehanirmāṇādyupayogibhiḥ ākāśādibhūtānāṃ sūkṣmairbhāgaiḥ saṃgataḥ san na yāti — dehāt dehāntaraprāptikāle na gacchati, tatraiva—dehāntara- sampantyarthaṃ yaṃ deśaṃ gacchati tasmin deśe eva teṣām — tadupayogināṃ bhūtasūkṣmāṇām, sulabhatva- hetoḥ - ( nityavibhūteradhaḥ pradeśe vyāpitvena sarvatra satāṃ ) sukhena labdhumaītvāt, ( iti pūrvapakṣe ) evaṃ naiva-uktā rītirnaivocitā, 'āpaḥ puruṣā bhavanti ' ityevaṃ śrutatvāt – 'abupalakṣitā ni bhūtāni dehiśabdavācyāni bhavanti' ityevaṃ śrutau spaṣṭamuktatvāt taiḥ saha prayāti bhūtasūkṣmaiḥ sahaiva jīvaḥ gacchati ; na kevalaḥ || 1 ||

1.

śrīmadbhiḥ vedāntācāryaiḥ viracitā adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||
sādhyā muktirna cet syāt | prasajati viphalā sādhanādhyāyaklṛptiḥ
sādhyā cennaśvarī syāt ! kathamiha punarāvṛttiśūnyo'pavargaḥ ? | maivaṃ brahmānubhūtiḥ parabhajanavatā prāgasiddhaiva sādhyā
dhīsaṅkocapraṇāśa stviyamiti ca bhavatyuttarāvadhyatītā || 1 ||

mukti:- muktipadena śāstreṣu pratipāditā jīvasya avasthitiḥ, sādhyā na syāccet- yatnato niṣpādyā, janyā na bhavati yadi tadā, sādhanādhyāyaklaptiḥ sādhananāmnaḥ śārīraka- tṛtīyādhyāyasya racanā, viphalā prasajati-nirarthima āpatati ; ( sādhyetagaṃ mukti prati sādhanacintayāḥ śaśaviṣāṇacintā taulyāt) muktiḥ sādhyā cet — jīvasya muktirūpā avasthitiḥ

yadi yatnaniṣpādyā bhavati tarhi, naśvarī syāt - cyavanasvabhāvaiva bhavet, ( nāśaṃ prati janya- bhāvatāyāḥ prayojakatvāt ) evaṃ sthite, iha – vedānteṣu (vihitaḥ) apavargaḥ - apavargākhyaḥ ātmana: sthitiviśeṣa:, (iha apavargaḥ - ātmani jāyamānaḥ apavargākhyo'vasthā viśeṣa : iti vā ) (kathaṃ punarāvṛttiśūnyaḥ - ātmagatapunarjanmāsamānādhikaraṇa iti kathaṃ vā sidhyati ? (asiddhau 'na ca (sa) punarāvartate' iti śrutervaighaṭyam ), maivam - īdṛśaṃ śaṅkanaṃ māstu, brahmānubhūtiḥ mukti:- paripūrṇa parabrahmānubhavarūpā yā śāstroktā muktiḥ sā khalu, prāk asiddhaiva paramātmopāsanātpūrvam aniṣpannasvarūpaiva jīve, parabhajanavatā — paramapuruṣopāsanarūpa vedāntoditasādhana viśeṣa sampannena cetanaviśeṣeṇaiva, sādhyā – sampādyasvarūpaiva bhavati, iyam – paramapuruṣopāsanasādhyā muktiḥ, dhīsaṃkocapraṇāśastu svabhāvataḥ siddhāyāḥ asaṃkucitāyāḥ dhiyaḥ (dharmabhūtajñānasya ) yaḥ anādi- karmāyattaḥ saṃkoca: ( bhagavatsvarūpa-guṇa- vibhūtirūpārthānāmagrāhitā) tasya virāmasvarūpaḥ, iti ca - asmādeva ca hetoḥ, uttarāvadhyatītā bhavati — paścātcana kālaparicchedarahitā apracyuta- - svabhāvā satī jāgarti || (ato na ko'pi doṣaleśaḥ ) | ( dhvaṃsasya dhvaṃsābhāvāditi yāvat ) || 1 ||

pādābhyāmatra pūrva janayati bhavināṃ brahmavidyādhikāraṃ

paścāt teṣāmubhābhyāṃ vadati bahuvidhāṃ tāmaśeṣaiḥ sahā'ṅgaiḥ | aiśvaryādau virakti niravadhivibhave pūruṣe cābhilāṣaṃ
vidyābhedāvalambaṃ tadupakaraṇamapyāha pādaiḥ krameṇa || 2||

atra – sādhanākhye tṛtīye adhyāye, pūrva pādābhyām – ādyābhyāṃ vairāgyobhayaliṅgā- skhyābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ caraṇābhyām bhavinām – (anādibhagavacchāsanātivṛttikṛta) saṃsārasambandhava cetanānām, (cetanān prati) brahmavidyādhikāram – ( aihikāmuṣmika phalabhogeṣu vairābhyo- tpādanataḥ) śamādisampattirūpa brahmavidyāītām, pūrva janayati - ādau (adhyāya prathamakṛtyatayā) sampādayati, paścāt ubhābhyām – anantarābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ guṇopasaṃhārā- ṅgākhyābhyāṃ pādābhyām, teṣām — prāptabrahmavidyādhikārāṇāṃ saṃsāricetanānām, (cetanānuddiśya ) bahuvidhāṃ tām- nānāprakārāṃ brahmavidyām, aśeṣaiḥ aṅgaiḥ saha samastaiḥ vidyāniṣpatyupakārakaiḥ sākam, paścāt vadati – ( etadadhyāyīyānantarakṛtyatayā) anantaraṃ spaṣṭaṃ bodhayati, pādaiḥ etadadhyāyīyaiḥ caturbhiḥ pādaiḥ krameṇa - yathāyogyapūrvāparabhāvamanurudhya, aiśvaryādau viraktim – (ādyapādena ) aiśvaryādiviṣayiṇīṃ vitṛṣṇatām, niravadhivibhave pūruṣe ca abhilāṣam – aparicchinnānantāścarya- vaibhavasvabhāve paramapuruṣe eva jīvasya satṛṣṇatām (dvitīyapādena ), vidyābhedāvalambam -

mokṣārthaṃ (svānuṣṭhānāī) vidyāviśeṣasyaikasyāśrayaṇam, (tṛtīyena caraṇena), tadupakaraṇamapi - vidyā niṣpatyādhāyakaṃ tadatiśayādhāyakamapi ca upakārakajātam (turyeṇa caraṇena ca ), āha- - samyagvivṛtavān (cetanānujighṛkṣāvān ) bādarāyaṇaḥ || 2 ||
saṃsārodvimacetāstanubhṛdavikarotyatra śārīrakāṃśe

vairāgyārthastu pādaḥ kimiti punarasau sūtrakārairnibaddha: ? |
satyaṃ prāpyāntarāṇāṃ nirayagaṇatulāropaṇaṃ muktayupāya-
prārambhe'bhyarhitaṃ syāt tvarata iha khalu spaṣṭadṛṣṭa' svadoṣaḥ ' || 3 ||

saṃsārodvignacetāḥ – anādeḥ kālāt santanyamānasya janmajarāmaraṇapravāhasya svasmin -itaḥ paścādapyanuvṛtticintanenā''hitodvega (tīvrabhaya) vadantaḥkaraṇaḥ, tanubhṛt – dehī (śāstrārtha - cintanaupayika viśiṣṭadehādisampannaḥ ) cetanaḥ, atra - dharma vicāraśāstrātmaka mīmāṃsāntaḥpātini, śārīrakāṃśe paramadharmabhūtaparabrahma vicārātmakaśārīra kā khyottaramīmāṃsādhyayane, adhikaroti- pravṛtto bhavati. (evaṃ sthite ) vairāgyārtha :- paramātmetaravaitṛṣṇyaprayojanakaḥ, asau pādaḥ – tṛtīya- syādimaḥ vairāgyābhidhaḥ pādaḥ, sūtrakāraiḥ śārīrakasūtrakṛdbhiḥ bādarāyaṇaiḥ, kimiti nibaddha: - kuto vā śārīrake punaryojita ? (sādhanīyāṃśābhāvena viphalatvāpātāt ) iti śaṅkāyām, satyam – uktametadabhyupagamanīyameva, yat siddhasyaiva vairāgyasya sampādanārthaṃ pravṛttau "vaiphalyamiti, śārīrakārambhāt pūrva vairāgyaṃ siddhamiti ca tathā'pi prāpyāntarāṇām - lambhanīyatvena sambhāvyamānānāṃ parabrahmetareṣāṃ sarveṣāmapi nirayagaṇatulāropaṇam – atyanta dūrī - kārya nara kastomasamatvajñāpanam (paramavairāgya jananam ), mutyupāyaprārambhe– apavargasādhanībhūta- vidyācintanārambhakāle, abhyarhitaṃ syāt – atyantamāvaśyakameva bhavet naivā'tra saṃśayaḥ, spaṣṭa dṛṣṭasvadoṣaḥ (bodhaḥ ) khalu – sphuṭapratipanna sopādhika- nirupādhika svasvarūpavāneva khalu cetanaḥ, iha – vidyārūyasādhanāvalambane, tvarate - avilambena pravartate (ataḥ sādhanādhyāyādau vairāgyacintā saphalata maivetyāśayaḥ) || 3 ||

}--

punaḥ

gatau jīvasya dehā dehāntaraprāptau sambhavantyām divi bhuvi ca svarge bhūloke vā, bhogyanītyā – bhogārhāṇi vastūnīva, dehādyam – śarīrendriyādibhogāvacchedakaṃ bhoga-- sādhanamapi ca tatra tatraiva — niyativaśato yatra yatra gacchati tasmiṃstasmin sthāna eva labhyam – susampādam ( evaṃ sthite) pūrvadehāt - tyajyamānāt prāktanāddehāt mudhā- niṣphalameva, gṛhītaiḥ – prayāṇakāle svīkṛtaiḥ, prāṇādyaiḥ prāṇendriyādibhiḥ jīvanajñānādi - sādhanaiḥ, bhūtasūkṣmairapi pañcabhūtānāṃ sūkṣmairaṃśairapi ca dehāntara niṣpādanasādhanaiḥ, iha kim- gatāgatāśraye jīve ko lābhaḥ ? aṇoḥ paricchinnaparimāṇasya jīvasya cetanasya, gatiṃ ca - dehā dehāntaraprāptirūpaṃ gamanamapi, prāṇanītyā - prāṇasya garti yathā tathā tadvadeva, svayam — bhūtasūkṣmādīnāṃ sahakāra manavalambyaiva, īśvaraḥ – sarvaniyantṛbhūtaḥ paramātmā, upajanayet- nirvahedeva; na tu tatrāsamartho bhavet, (bhataḥ jīvasya prayāṇakāle bhūtasūkṣmādibhiḥ saha gamanaṃ nābhyupagantavyam ; gauravāt iti pūrvapakṣe ) maivam - īdṛśyā svakapolakalpitayā yuktyā śrutiviruddhārthacintanaṃ māstu, svacchandakṛtye – īśvarasya svasaṅkalpamātrādhīne kārye, śrutimita- niyatau - śrutimātra nirṇaya suvyavasthāvati sati, (tadviṣaye) gauravoktaḥ kalpye'rthe sambhavataḥ lāghava-gauravacintāprayuktasya vyavahārasya, ayukteḥ ayogāt asaṅgatatvāt || 4 ||

nānājātīya rāśi vyapadiśati jano bhūyasoṃ'śasya nāmnā prācuryādeva 'māpaḥ puruṣavacasa' ityucyate bhūtavarga: |

vyaṣṭi pañcīkṛtaistaiḥ sṛjati hi | sa vibhustāratamyaṃ punaśca
'śraddhā' śabdastvihā'paḥ kathayati nigame tatsamākhyāvadukteḥ || 5 ||

paratayā prayuktaḥ 'śraddhā' ityayaṃ śabdaḥ param, apaḥ kathayati – jalārūyamarthameva tadupalakṣitabhūta- sūkṣmāṇyeva abhidhatte ; ( na tu ātmasambadhdhinaṃ manovṛttiviśeṣam ), nigame śraddhā vā āpa:' ityasmin śrutivākye, tatsamākhyāvadukteḥ - abvācakaśabdataulyena śraddhāśabda- prayogadarśanāt || 5 ||
dyauḥ parjanyo'tha pṛthvī tadanu ca puruṣo yoṣidityevametān
pañcāgnīn kalpayitvā parikarasahitāṃsteṣu pañcādmividyā |
śraddhākhyaṃ bhūtasūkṣmaṃ kramapariṇatitaḥ somavarṣānnareto.
rūpaṃ havyaṃ sajīvaṃ tanudharamaruto juhvatīti bravīti || 6 ||

dyauḥ- dhunāmā sukṛticetanaprāpya: lokaḥ sukhānubhavaupayikaḥ, parjanyaḥ puṇyoparamānantaram avarohaṇasamaye prāpyaḥ sthānaviśeṣa: (meghāparaparyāyaḥ), atha - anantaraṃ prāpyā, pṛthvī - pṛthivī - padavācyaḥ sthānaviśeṣaḥ, tadanu ca tadanantaraprāpyaḥ puruṣaḥ - pṛthibī sañjātasasyaprabhavadhānyādi- bhoktā dehī cetanaḥ, yoṣit - ( puruṣe retobhāvātyanantaraṃ tadrupeṇa prāpyaḥ) strīrūpo'rthaḥ, (avarohato jīvasya garbhe dhārayitā sthānaviśeṣaḥ), ityevametān paśca – uktākārānarthān pañcasaṃkhyān parikarasahitān svasvaparikaraiḥ yathākramam ādityādibhiḥ vāyvādibhiḥ saṃvatsarādibhiḥ vāgādibhiḥ upasthādibhiśva agniparikaratvena adhyasyamānaiḥ yuktān, agnīn paśca kalpayitvā — agnibhāvāpannāḥ pañcati agnidharmasādṛśyataḥ adhyasya teṣu dhu. parjanya- pṛthivī - puruṣa yoṣitsu tanudharamarutaḥ – dehadhārakāḥ prāṇāḥ, śraddhākhyam - ādau śraddhetyākhyā prāptumarham, kramapariṇatitaḥ - kramaprāptāt pariṇāma ( avasthā) viśeṣāt, somavarṣānnareto- rūpam – somarāja iti varṣa iti annamiti reta iti ca rūpaviśeṣaṃ prāptam, sajīvam – avarohatā jīvena sahitam, bhūtasūkṣmaṃ havyam - sūkṣmabhūtapañcakarūpam abhau prakṣepya sadṛśam, juhvatīti- prakṣipantīti imamartham pañcāgnividyā paṃcāmividyākhyaḥ chāndogyasambandhī aupaniṣadaḥ bhāgaviśeṣaḥ, bravīti — pratipādayati || 6 ||

viṣayidvikābhikhyena anantarādhyāyadvayena 'brahmaprāyupāyaiḥ saha tatprāptiprakāraḥ

cintyate |

tatra tṛtīye upāsanādhyāye – upāyabhūtam upāsanaṃ cintyate | tatra jīvadoṣapāde prathame – jāgradādyavasthasya jīvasya doṣāḥ | ubhayaliṅgapāde dvitīye – paramātmanaḥ nirdoṣatvaṃ kalyāṇaguṇākaravaṃ ca, upāsanārambhāya prāpyavyatiriktavaitṛṣṇyajananārthaṃ prāpyatṛṣṇājananārthaṃ ca - pratipādyate ||
tatra tadantarapratipattyadhikaraṇe - dehā dehāntaraṃ gacchan jīvaḥ, tatra tatra bhūtasūkṣmāṇa -sulabhatvāt taiḥ asampariṣvakta eva gacchati ityāśaṅkaya,
'vettha yathā' ityādi praśna prativacanābhyāṃ bhūtasūkṣmasampariṣvakta eva gacchati ityuktam ||

phalaṃ karmanibandhanaṃ tu –avarohānantarakālikaṃ uttamādhama kula janmādirūpaṃ phalaṃ tatprayojakakarma- hetukameva, (na nirhetukam ; ākasmikatvāpatteḥ) kapūyacārāḥ iti śrutatvāt ca - ' kapūyacaraṇā: kapūyāṃ, ramaṇīyacaraṇāḥ ramaṇīyāṃ yonim' iti śrutau spaṣṭīkṛtatvādapi karmaśeṣavāneva jīva:- pratyavarohatītyaṅgīkāryam || 2 ||

-0-

karmī - iha kāmyabuddhayā kṛtaśrautasmārtādikarmā san, mṛtaḥ - dehaviyoga prāptaścetanaḥ ( krameṇa ) dhūmaṃ rātrim - dhūmarūpaṃ niśā khyaṃ ca sthānaviśeṣam, timarakaluṣitaṃ pakṣam todūṣitaṃ kṛṣṇākhyaṃ pakṣam, dakṣiṇāvṛttimāsān sūryasya dakṣiṇāyana parivṛttihetūn ṣaṭ māsān, (dakṣiṇāyanākhyaṃ sthānam ) paścāt tadanantaram pitṝṇāṃ lokam - pitṛlokākhyaṃ -sthānam, gaganamapi — paścāt ākāśapadābhidheyaṃ sthānam, candram - tataḥ candrapadābhidheyaṃ sthānaviśeṣaṃ ca, abhyeti— ( ārohaṇakāle prāpya prāpya ) yāti | pratyāvṛttau tu - puṇyakarmaṇaḥ phalānyanubhūya punaḥ bhuvaṃ prati avarohaṇavelāyāṃ tu, candrāt - niśākarāt (candrākhyāt sthānāt ) gagana-satatagau—ākāśa-vāyvākhyau sthānaviśeṣau ( labhate ), dhūtram abbhraṃ ca megham - dhūmākhyaṃ sthānaṃ, jaladharākhyaṃ sthānam, meghapadavācyaṃ jalavarṣaṇāvasthaṃ sthānam, (tataḥ) hyādīn -hi-bhṛtīn cetanaiḥ bhakṣaṇādyarhān (tataḥ) retaḥ sicam - yoṣiti retasaḥ sektāraṃ prāpsyamānapitṛbhāvaṃ puruṣaviśeṣam, atha – tadanantaraṃ ca, jananīm – mātṛbhāvaṃ prāptu- mahā~ striyaṃ ca yātanācakravartī duḥkhamayeṣu cakravat nirantaraparivartanasvabhāveṣu asthireṣu -sthānaviśeṣeṣu bhramanneva vartamānaḥ jīvaḥ yāti - ( sthānāt sthānāntaraṃ ) prāpnoti || 8 ||

prayuktaṃ caraṇapadam karmaṇyapi yāgādau vihitakarmaṇyapi ( ācāre iva) vṛttyā mukhyam - śabdavṛttikṛta puṣkala pravṛttinimittava deva ; na gauṇavṛttikam ataḥ, ekadeśe bhanyataramātre, ; -

na nirodhyam-na niruddhavṛttikatayā varṇayituṃ śakyam jātyādi : - janma, āyuḥ, bhogaśca, karmabhedaprabhavaḥ vilakṣaṇa karmasādhyaḥ iti mite ityevam ārṣavacanaiḥ pramāviṣayīkṛte sati, ācārasādhyam - ācāraiḥ sandhyādibhiḥ sampādyam, cintyam – śāstrārthāvadhāraṇa saṃskṛtena - manasā sāvadhānaṃ vicintya nirṇeyam; na niṣphalatayā nirṇeyam || 9 ||
praptācārātivṛttau pratipadamṛṣayaḥ sasmaruḥ pratyavāyān prācīnāṃhaḥ praṇāśaṃ tadanusaraṇataḥ puṇyakarmāītāṃ ca |
nā'taḥ satkarmamātrāt tridiva iti ghiyā tatparityāgaśaṅkā
na hyācāraprahīṇe śvahatijalasamaḥ śodhako vedavargaḥ || 10 ||

prāptācārātivṛttau pāramparikatayā āgatasya sandhyāderācārasya akaraṇataḥ atilaṅghane kriyamāṇe sati pratyavāyān - patanādirūpān doṣān ṛṣayaḥ dharmādharmasvarūpajñāḥ maharṣayaḥ, pratipadam - pade pade, bhūyo bhūyaḥ, sasmaruḥ – svīyāsu smṛtiṣu pratibodhitavantaḥ, tadanusaraṇataḥ - prāptānāmācārarāṇāmanuṣṭhānāt, prācīnāṃhaḥ praṇāśam – pūrvārjitānāmaghānāṃ kṣayam, puṇyakarmārhatāṃ ca svargādiprada satkarmānuṣṭhānayogyatāmapi ca smṛtiṣuktavantaḥ | ataḥ—ācārollaṅghane pratyavāyāt anuṣṭhāne pāpanāśādisampatteśca sattvāt, 'satkarmamātrāt tridivaḥ' iti ghiyā – 'yāgaḥ divihitakarmānuṣṭhānamātrataḥ svargaḥ' iti buddhayā, tatparityāga- śaṅkā na—sandhyādyāca ratyāgaśaṅkā na kāryā, vedavargaḥ – jagatpāvanabhūtaḥ śrutisamūho'pi, ācāraprahīṇe parityakta sandhyādyācāre jane, śvadyatijalasamaḥ śvacarmanikṣiptodakasadṛśaḥ san na hi śodhaka:- pavitratāpādako na bhavati || 10 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||
athaikabhavikanyāyāt karmaśeṣavilopane |
bhuktaśiṣṭasya sambandhaḥ kṛtātyayanayoditaḥ || 113 ||
iti kṛtātyayādhikaraṇam || 2 ||

sarveṣām - iṣṭādikāriṇām aniṣṭādikāriṇāṃ ceti samastānāmapi dehinām, dehapāte sati prāptasya śarīrasya paढ़ne ( maraṇe) samprāpte sati, (tadanantaram ) niyamavatī - avaśyaṃ bhāvinī (anivāryā) candramaḥprāptiḥ candrarūpasthānasamāgatiḥ, uktā- ' candramasameva te sarve gacchantīti śrutau paṭhitā, tasmāt śrutyuktatvāddhetoḥ, pāpottarāṇām — kevala pāpa- kāriṇām, sā candrasamāgatiḥ, nirayagatipuraskāriṇī narakaprāptisamanantarabhāvinī, iti cet na evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na yuktam ' taiḥ paraḥ lokaḥ na sampūryate ' iti girā - ' pāpamātra- kāribhiḥ (candrākhyaḥ) paralokaḥ pūrito na bhavati' ityarthikayā ' tenā'sau loko na sampūryate' iti śrutivācā, sarvaśabda: candramasameva te sarve gacchanti ' iti vākyasthaḥ 'sarva' śabdaḥ, saṅkucet — iṣṭādikā risarvamātraviṣayakaḥ san asaṅkocena sarvabodhako na bhavet | ataḥ tadvākyastha sarvaśabdasya iṣṭādipuṇyakṛtsarvaviṣayakatvāt, te kevalapāpakāriṇa, : tadyatanānte tadanubhāvya nāraka vedanāvasāne tataḥ nāraka vedanānubhavasthānataḥ sahasā haṭhādeva, āhutipañcakamanapekṣyaiva, iha - bhūloke, kutsitāṃ yonim – svasaṅgatapāpaśeṣaphalānubhavaupayi- kīm pāpayonim yānti pratipadyante || 11 |

janmapraptirjarāyuprabhṛtiṣu bhavināṃ karmapā kairvicitrā
bhūyiṣṭhaiḥ puṇya-pāpai (sva) rapavadanavatī pañcamāhutyapekṣā |
tadvad dhūmādimārgaḥ kati kati ca śubhairutkaṭairdehapāte

1- divyaṃ rūpaṃ vimānādikamapi sapadi prāpya yātāḥ prasiddhāḥ || 12 || bhavinām saṃsaraṇapravṛttānāṃ jīvānām, karmapākaiḥ – tatkarmaṇāṃ pariṇāmaviśeṣaiḥ, jarāyuprabhṛtiṣu gaveṣṭana carmapuṭe aṇḍādau ca janmaprāptiḥ śarīrāgatiḥ, vicitrā- avarṇanīya svarūpā atyadbhutā vartate pañcamāhutyapekṣā - janmalābhe śrutisiddhāyāḥ pañcamyā āhuteḥ āvaśyakatā'piṃ bhūyiṣṭheḥ puṇya-pāpaiḥ prāptātiśayena puṇyakalāpena tādṛśena pāpakalāpena ca, apavadanavatī- autsargikyeva ; na nirapavādā satī sārvatrikī, (viśiṣṭābhyāṃ puṇya-pāpābhyāṃ janmāpi viśiṣṭaṃ bhavitumarham ) tadvat - janmalābhe pañcamyā āhuteḥ sāpavādatva- mitra, dhūmādimārgaḥ – dhūmādrimārgataḥ prayāṇamapi sāpavādameva, kati kati — kiyanto'ne ke cetanāḥ, utkaṭaiḥ śubhaiḥ atiśayitaiḥ puṇyaiḥ sādhanabhūtaiḥ, dehapāte- prāptadehasya patanakāle, divyaṃ rūpaṃ vimānādikamapi svargiprāpyaṃ vilakṣaṇaṃ rūpaviśeṣam, (tathā) prāpyasthānaṃ prati gamanasādhanaṃ divyavimānaprabhṛtikamapi sapadi tatkṣaṇa evaṃ prāpya daivānugrahāt labdhvā, yātāḥ svargādikaṃ lokaṃ gatavantaḥ, prasiddhāḥ – itihāsa - puraṇā dī śrutacarāḥ || 12 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ ||

aniṣṭādikāryadhikaraṇe—' ye vai ke cā'smāllokāt prayanti, candramasameva te sarve gacchanti' iti aviśeṣeṇa sarveṣāṃ gatiśravaṇāt, pāpakāriṇo'pi candramasaṃ gacchanti

ityāśaṅkaya

' atha ya ime grāme iṣṭāpūrte dattamityupāsate ' ityuktvā, te dhūmamabhisambhavanti ityādinā iṣṭādikāriṇāmeva pitṛyāṇaśravaṇāt, 'candramasameva te sarve gacchanti ' iti vacanasyā'pi iṣṭādikāriviṣayatayā neyatvāt pāpakāriṇaḥ candramasaṃ na gacchanti ityuktam ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ

āhutyordehavatvaṃ prathamacaramayornirvivādaṃ tathā syā- jjanmaivā''kāśa vāyuprabhṛtiṣu bhavateranvayādityasāram | retaḥ simbhāvanītyā pṛthagamilapanā naī tāmātra (meva) mantra

dyukvoḥ (dyubhtroḥ) puṇyaprasādhyaṃ phalamiha paṭhitaṃ nā'sti bhogaśca madhye || 13|| prathama-caramayoḥ āhutyoḥ – 'devāḥ śraddhāṃ juhvati | tasyā āhuteḥ somo rājā- sambhavati' ityuktāyāṃ somabhāvāpādikāyām ādhāyāmāhutau (tathā) 'pumān retaḥ siñcati .... pañcamyāma|hutāvāpaḥ puruṣavacaso bhavanti' ityuktāyāṃ puruṣabhāvāpādikāyāṃ caramāyāmāhutau ca, dehavattvaṃ nirvivādam—tacaccharīrakatvātmakaṃ deha sambandhitvaṃ avipratipannaṃ yathā, tathā-tayaiva rītyā, ākāśa-vāyuprabhṛtiṣu ākāśa-vāyvādibhāvasthale'pi, janmaiva syāt - dehaparigraha eva tatadbhāvo- bhavitumarhati bhavateḥ anvayāt — ādyantayoḥ śrutasya 'bhū' dhātoḥ atrāpi sambandhāta ( tasya ca viṣamarūpatāyāṃ hetvabhāvāt ) ityasāram -- evaṃ cintanaṃ adṛḍham (sthāpaka yuktidūram ) retaḥ sigbhāvanītyā- 'ya: retaḥ siñcati tadbhūya eva bhavati' ityuktaretaḥ sikpuruṣabhāvāpatti- nyāyena, atra—ākāśādiviṣaye, pṛthaga ....mātram - pṛthakpratipatti vyavahārādyanaīḥ sambandha- viśeṣa eva ākāśādibhāvaśabdena vivakṣitaḥ ; na tattaccharīrakatvarūpaḥ saḥ iha–asmin prakaraṇe, kvoḥ - divi bhuvi ca, puṇyaprasādhyam - sukṛtasampādanīyam phalam - bhogarūpaṃ prayojanam paṭhitam śrutau ādyantā hutyoruktam, madhyeca- devabhāvādanantaraṃ martyabhāva- prāpterarvāk ( etayorantararā), bhogaḥ nāsti – puṇyaphalānubhavaprasaktirna vartate; teṣu teṣu sthāne- vyavasthitimātramiti || 13 ||

nayasaṃgatimālikā ||
iṣṭādikāriṇāmatra mṛgāṅkā davarohatām |
tatsvābhāvyāpattinītyā'vādibhāvapariṣkriyā || 115 ||

vyoma | disthityavasthā–avarohato jīvasya ākāśādau avasthitirūpā śrutyuktā

( avasthā) daśā, śuklāvasthānayāt – retaḥ saṅgatāvasthāyāṃ vilambā vilambaniyamābhāvo yathā tadvat aniyatiḥ - śaighraya vilambarūpaniścitakālā va dhiśūnyaiva ciram aciramiti vyakta- nirdeśahāne:—– vilambabodhakena cirādiśabdena śaiprayabodhārthakā cirādiśabdena vā śrutau sphuṭa-- nirdeśasyābhāvāt, (iti pūrvapakṣe ) iti na - evaṃ śaṅkā nocitā, svārasikyāḥ pravṛtteḥ- svabhāvasiddhāyāḥ śrutivākyagatāyāḥ bodhanārthavyā pṛtereva anyatarapakṣaniścāyakatvāt | hi— yataḥ, vrīhyādibhyaḥ – jīvāvasthitisthānabhūtebhyaḥ vrīhyādirūpebhyaḥ arthebhya: ( avadhibhūtebhyaḥ) durniṣprapataramiti śrūyate - duḥkhatara niṣkramaṇa sādhyatābodhakaḥ 'durniṣprapatataram' ityayaṃ śabdaḥ luptaitakāraḥ śrutau paṭhyamāno'sti tena trīhmādibhāvāt niṣkramaṇa vilamba bodhakaśrautaśabdākhya- niyāmakasaśvena, pūrvam – ākāśādibhāvadaśā rūpa pūrvatanakā le, śīghram - avilambenaiva, tattaddaśāyāḥ---ākāśa-vāyvabhrameghādyavasthāyāḥ, tyajanamiti – prahāṇamiti, vākyaśaktyā śrutivākyagatayā āvaśyakārthabodhanaśaktyā, parijñāyate—asandigdhaṃ niścīyate || 14 ||

anyādhiṣṭhitādhikaraṇam ||3-1-6 ||
śārīrakanyā yakalāpa saṅgrahaḥ ||

'jāyanna' 'itye |' viśevaśabdād brīhyādidehāstu bhavanti jīvāḥ | naitat tu gauṇaṃ jananābhidhānaṃ taddhetvabhāvāt paratastu bhāvāt || 6 ||
jāyanta' ityeva viśeṣaśabdāt - ' jāyante' ityevaṃ śarīrasambandhātmaka janma- vācakasya viśeṣaśabdasyaiva sattvāt , jīvāḥ svargādirūpāt -sthāna viśeṣādava rohanto jīvāḥ, vrīhyādidehāstu - trīhiyavādiśarīrakā eva santaḥ, bhavanti- janmabhāgino bhavanti ; ( na tu tatsaṃśleṣamātravantaḥ ), iti śaṅkane, etat na tu idaṃ śaṅkanaṃ naiva sādhu, jananābhidhānam – 'jāyante ' ityanena utpattervarṇanam, gauṇaṃ tu–avasthāntara- prāpti mātrataḥ aupacārikameva, ( na tu janmākhyāvasthā viśeṣapratipādanena mukhyam), taddhetvabhāvāt - vrīhibhāvena bhogāpādakasya karmaṇaḥ anavaśeṣāt paratastu bhāvāt — anantarapratipāditapañca- māhutisampattyanantarameva dehāpādakasya taddvārā bhogāpādakasya ca ramaṇīya-kapūyādi karmaṇaḥ sadbhāvataḥ vyāvṛtipratipādanāt || 6 ||

vṛkṣau ṣadhyādīnāmacarāṇām, nairātmyam - ātmānadhiṣṭhitatvam na ca sthāpayanti naiva vyavasthāpayanti, (caturvidhasyā'pi dehasya ātmādhiṣṭhāna naiyatyāt) iti cet — (bhataḥ avarohan jīvaḥ vrīhyādiśarīraka eva) iti śaṅkane, na- idaṃ na sādhu, pravṛtte varṇayitu- mārabdhe, puṇyasyaiva phalapa rigaṇane puṇya mātra phalakramaprasaṅkhyāne, sthāvaratvottyayogāt - sthāvaratvarūpakarmadoṣaphalavarṇanasya anaucityāpatteḥ, (ataḥ ) retaḥ sigvaṇīva - retaḥ- sektṛpitṛśarīre iba (bhaviṣyatputra bhāvasya cetanasya, saṃśleṣamātrarūpaḥ pitṛdehe yoga iva)
sthāvare - ajaṅgame, anyadehe'pi - ātmāntarādhiṣṭhite'pi dehe, janim - janmabodhakaṃ jan dhātum, upacarati gauṇyavṛttyā prayukte śrutiḥ | saṃśleṣamātrameva janmeti upacārata: śruti- vyavaharatīti vārthaḥ || 15 ||
hiṃmāyogādaśuddhaṃ śrutivihitamapi nyāyyamiṣṭādikaṃ tat
pāpāṃśa vrīhibhāvaprabhṛtiṣu sukṛtī bhuṅkta ityapyayuktam |
uktā mantrārthavādaiḥ paśuhitamiti sā taccikitsā' badeṣā'

brūte yajñe vadho'sāvavadha iti manuḥ stauti nindāvidā'nyat || 16 ||

iṣṭādikam – yāgādirūpaṃ karma, śrutivihitam cetaneṣu vatsalatareṇa vedākhyena śastreṇaivopadiṣṭam nyāyyamapi hitapravṛttena śāstraṇaiva vihitatvāt nyāyādanapetamapi ( athāpi ) hiṃsāyogāt - (paśuyāgādau paśusaṃjñapanakṛtā) varjya prāṇihiṃsā sambandhāt aśuddham - pāpasaṃvalita- mevāsti, tat - tasmāddhetoḥ sukṛtī- iṣṭādipuṇyakarmakṛt cetanaḥ, pāpāṃśam - śrutipratipādita- svargasukhaprade karmaṇi sambaddha mekadeśabhūtaṃ pāpam, vrīhibhāvaprabhṛtiṣu - svena prāpteṣu vrīhiyavādi- sthāvarabhāveṣu, bhuṃkte - phaladvārā anubhavati (ataḥ avarohato jīvasya sthāvarabhāvaḥ puṇyaphalānu- bhavānantarakālikaḥ pāpaphalānubhava eveti, tasya madhye kathanamapi nānaucityāya) ityapyayuktam- evaṃ śaṅkanamapi nocitam sā- pāpatvenāśaṃkyamānā paśuprāṇaviyogaupayikavyāpārātmikā hiṃsā, mantrārthavādai: 'na vā uvetantriya se' ityādibhirmantraiḥ, 'hiraṇyaśarīraḥ' ityevaṃ- prabhṛtibhirarthavādaiśva, paśuhitamiti uktā - paśoḥ bhāvisukhahetutvena hitātmiketi parigaṇitā, tat — hitarūpatvāt eṣā - hiṃsātvena śakyamānā paśumāraṇakriyā, cikitsāvat - vaidyasya bhaiṣajyavyāpārasadṛśī na ghātukavyāpārasadṛśī, manuḥ vaidiko mantraviśeṣaḥ, manvākhya- ṛṣirvā, yajñe śraute devayajane, asau vadhaḥ - paśusaṃjñapanādirūpaḥ vadhatvena śaṅkayamānaḥ

vyāpāra viśeṣaḥ, avadha iti - pāpājanakatayā vastuto vadhapadavācya eva na bhavatīti brūte- vakti, iha nindā tu yajñādikarmaviṣaye hiṃsātvakathanato nindā param

anyat — karmaṇa

itarat jñānam, stauti praśaṃsati ( ' naśvare svargādisukhe na yate ; śāśvate mokṣe eva yateta ' ityetadbodhayati || 16 ||
karturdoṣaṃ diśet saṃjñapanamiha paśostat ṛtoścopakuryāt
tasmādasminniṣedhaṃ kṣipati na vidhirityabuvan sāṃkhyabhaktāḥ |
nirdhūte paśvanarthe na khalu taducitaṃ piṣṭapaśvādikalpa-
stattatkāla | dhikāripratiniyata iti kvā'pi na syādvirodhaḥ || 17 ||

iha – paśuyāgādau śraute karmaṇi, paśoḥ saṃjñapanam - paśuviśasanarūpo vyāpāraḥ, kartuḥ - paśunā yāgaṃ kurvāṇasya puruṣasya, doṣaṃ diśet - prāṇihiṃsā nimittaṃ pāpaṃ janayatu, ṛtośca - yāgasyā'pi, upakuryāt — atiśayādhānaṃ karotu nāma, anayoḥ ko virodha: ? tasmāt - puruṣadoṣāvahatva kratūpakārakatvayorvirodhābhāvāt vidhiḥ - 'paśumālabheta' ityayaṃ paśvā- lambhanavidhiḥ, asmin niṣedham- 'na hiṃsyāt ' itīmaṃ hiṃsāsāmānyapratiṣedhaṃ śrutyantasiddham, na kṣipatīti - apavādatāmāśritya bādhate iti, sāṃkhyabhaktāḥ – sāṃkhyānusāriṇaḥ abruvan uktavantaḥ, paśvanarthe—yāgasya paśuhiṃsanātmaka sve, nirdhūte – paśusvargajñāpakaiḥ śrītairvākyaiḥ nivārite sati, tat – ( utsargāpavādabhāvāśrayaṇena vinā ) kartṛdoṣāpādakatva ṛtūpakārakatvayoḥ viṣayayoḥ pṛthakkaraṇena samīkaraṇe yatanam, nociṃta khalu na hi yuktaṃ bhavati, piṣṭapaśvādi- kalpaḥ - piṣṭamayaṃ paśunāśritya yāganirvahaṇaprakārastu tattatkā... niyata iti-kāla viśeṣādhi- kā riviśeṣānuvidhā yivyavasthāvalambīti, kvāpi kasminnapi viṣaye, virodhaḥ na syāt - vaighaṭyaṃ naiva prāpnoti (iti yathāvyavasthaṃ sarva samañjasameva ) || 17 ||

kiṃcotsargāpavādakramamiha jahataḥ kīdṛśī nityahiṃsā
śuddhaṃ na kvāpi sidhyet tava hi vidhipadaṃ spṛṣṭa' tattanniṣedham | yatrā'satyādi vaidhaṃ tadanu ca vihitā niṣkṛtistannimittā
tatrā'gatyā tathā syāditaravadathavā kevalaṃ tannimittam || 18 ||

kiṃ ca - api ca, iha – sāmānya viśeṣa vidhisthale, utsargāpavādakramam— 'viśeṣa - vidhiviṣayavyatiriktaviṣayakatvaṃ sāmānyavidheḥ' ityevaṃrūpaṃ utsargāpavādanyāyam jahataḥ-

“anabhyupagacchataḥ sāṃkhyādeḥ, nityahiṃsā – akaraṇe pratyavāyaṃ nivivārayiṣoḥ svargādike phale nākāṅkṣasya jyotiṣṭomādinityakarmānuṣṭhānanāntarīyakā paśvālambhanādijanyā hiṃsā, kīdṛśī- -rāgato janyeti vā vidhitaḥ prāpteti vā kīdṛśīti niścetuṃ śakyā ? tava utsargāpavādanyāya- tyāginastava, śuddham - pāpamiśratārahitaṃ (vidhiviṣayatāvat ) kvāpi yatra kutrāpi, na sidhyet — naiva labdhasvarūpaṃ bhavet, hi yataḥ, vidhipadam - vighigocarībhūtaṃ sarvamapi, spṛṣṭatattanniṣedham – tattatsthalīyena tena tena niṣedhaviśeṣeṇa ākāntasvasvarūpameva dṛśyate sarvatra, yatra vivāhādau viṣayaviśeṣe, asatyādi - ( niṣiddhasvarūpameva ) anṛtavacanādikaṃ vaidham- śāstrīyāyā abhyanujñāyāḥ āspadabhūtam ('pañcānṛtānyāhurapātakāni' iti ) tadanu ca- tadanantarameva ca tannimittā - anṛtottyādihetukā, niṣkṛtiḥ - tajjanya doṣa nivā rikā prāyaścittiśva vihitāśāstrata eva jñāpitā ca tatra tādṛśe sthalaviśeṣe, agatyā- utsargāpavādabhāvato vidhiniṣedhayorviṣaya bhedākhya gatyabhāvāt tathā syāt — vidhānaṃ tatprāya- ścittaṃ cetyubhayamapi śāstrata eva siddha bhavatu nāma, athavā yadvā, itaravat – anṛtoktividhānavat kevalaṃ tannimittam - anṛtokti nimittatayā kevalamāśritya ; ( na tu doṣatvena vibhāvya ) -akṣāralavaṇaṃ bhojanādikaṃ vidhīyate || 18 ||

pādopasaṃhāraḥ ||

śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṃṅgrahaḥ ||
bhūtasūkṣmaiḥ pariṣvaṅgaḥ karmaśeṣasahāyatā | apuṇyānāmanāroho viyadādisahakṣatā || 7 ||
tatrā'cirādavasthānaṃ vrīhmāde: 'śleṣaṇaṃ tathā |
ityarthāḥ ṣaḍamī samyak pādenā'nena cintitāḥ || 8 ||
bhūta.... vaṅgaḥ - (ādye'dhikaraṇe) deha | dudehāntaraprāptisamaye jīvasya dehārambhaupayikaiḥ sūkṣmaiḥ pañcabhirbhūtaiḥ saṅgatasyaiva gamanam karma... yatā - (dvitīya) svargādyanubhavapaścātkā la- mavarohaṇasamaye svīyabhuktakarmaśeṣa sahakṛtatayaiva sthitiḥ, apuroha: (tṛtīye) kevala pāpināṃ candrānāroha: ; viya...kṣatā - (caturthe) avarohakāle ātmana ākāśādibhāvasya ākāśādi- sādṛśyarūpatvam, tatrā... sthānam - (pañcame ) ākāśādiṣu sthāneṣu sthitau avilambaḥ, bīghā .... ṇaṃ tathā - ( ṣaṣṭhe ) śrutyuktasya śrātmano vrīhyādibhāvasya tatsaṃśleṣamātrarūpatvam iti amī ṣaṭ arthāḥ – evaṃ paṭsaṃkhyāḥ arthāḥ anena pādena tṛtīyādyena pādena, samyak cintitāḥ- nipuṇaṃ vicāritāḥ bhavanti || 7, 8 ||

saṃsārabandhāpagamākhyaṃ mokṣamapi, avyaktasya - avyaktapadābhidheyaprakṛtivikāra bhūtasyaivā''huḥ, bhavabhujamapi – saṃsārabhāvena mānyamānamapi jīvam, anādi.... bhāvam - anādeḥ kālāt svabhāvata eva muktam, (na tūpāyānuṣṭhānaiḥ) āhuḥ teṣām uktānāṃ dvividhasāmayikānāmapi, itthaṃ manīṣām — evaṃprakārāṃ matim ( ātmasvarūpa - tatkarma - tatphala - tanmokṣaviṣaye ayathābhāvā- vagāhinīṃ ghiyam ) nayaiḥ – adhikaraṇairemiḥ, eṣaḥ – tṛtīyādyaḥ, vairāgyapādaḥ – vairāgyapādanāmā pādaḥ, bahirakṛta - bahiṣkṛtavān (śruteranabhimataṃ bodhayāmāsa ) || 19 ||

pādetvarthāḥ ṣaḍasmin vapuriha vijahadbhūtasūkṣmaiḥ saheyād bhuktasvargo'varohedanuśayasahito mātrayā bhinna' mārgaḥ |
candrāvāsyādi na syānnirayapathajuṣā mambarādau sadṛttatvaṃ

tasmācchīghro'varoha: paravapuṣi ciraṃ vrīhipūrve tu yogaḥ || 20 ||

asmin pāde tu - tṛtīyasyādime vairāgyākhye caraṇe tu, arthā: - cintitā: nirṇī- tārthāḥ, ṣaṭ – ṣaṭsaṃkhyā: 1) iha – martyaloke, vapuḥ – svīkṛtaṃ pūrvapanaṃ deham, vijahat- parityajan jīvaḥ, bhūtasūkṣmaiḥ saha – dehāntarārambhopayogibhiḥ sūkṣmaiḥ pañcabhirbhūtaiḥ sahita eva, iyāt – lokāntaraṃ gacchet, 2 ) bhuktavarga:- puṇyaphalabhūtasvargānubhavasampatyā kṣīṇapuṇyaḥ, anuśayasahitaḥ -- bhuktaśiṣṭa karmayukta eva san mātrā bhinnamārgaḥ - ārohaṇamārgāt kiṃcidiva vyatyastavarmā, avarohet — svargasthānādbhuvaṃ pratyavarohaṃ prāpnuyāt 3 ) nirayapatha- juṣām – kevalapāpakarmācaraṇena narakamārgamātrāvalambinām, candrāvātyādi – candrāvāsyādikam, na syāt – naiva bhavet, 4) ambarādau - ākāśabhāvādiprāptivelāyām, sahattvam- ākāśādisāmyameva ; na ākāśādiśarīra katvam, 5) tasmāt – ākāśa | deḥ sthānāt, śīghraḥ avarohaḥ - avilambita eva pratyāgamanakramaḥ, 6 ) trīhipū trīhiyavādyātma ke, paravapuṣi - jīvāntaraśarīre, ciram - vilambitakālaṃ yāvat, yogastu –avarohaṇakāle jīvasya saṃśleṣamātram ; na śarīritā - iti || 20 ||

|| T: ||

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ | śriyai namaḥ | śrīdharāya namaḥ ||

tṛtīyasyāsdhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ || 32 ||
sandhyādhikaraṇam || 3-2-1||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ ||
' gamanāgamana' prakāracinnā vihitopa sisiṣā' prasiddhi' hetoḥ |
na ( sa ) tu kaścana doṣamātmasaṃsthaṃ yadi nekṣeta tadā paraṃ chupāte || 1 ||

gamanāgamanaprakāra cintā —– jīvātmanaḥ deha | dehāntaraprāptiḥ, tadarthaṃ lokāntaragamanam, lokāntarāt bhuvaṃ prati āgamanaṃ ca kena prakāreṇetyaṃśasya yo vicāra:, ( pūrvapāde kṛtaḥ ) saḥ vihitopa sisiṣāprasiddhi hetoḥ– brahmaprāsyuddeśena vedāntairvihitaṃ yadupāsanaṃ tadviṣayiṇyā utkaṭecchāyāḥ sampattaye, kaścana – yaḥ kaścit jīvaḥ, ātmasaṃstham - jīvātmani vidyamānam, doṣam - apuruṣārthabhūtamaṃśam, yadi na nekṣeta tu tadā na paśyatītyaṃśaḥ kadā'pi yadi naiva syāt tadā ( saṃsarataḥ svasya apuruṣārthamayatvaṃ, jāgradādyavastha svasvarūpa sārvadika cintanataḥ khacitaṃ yadā bhavati tadā), paraṃ hi - ( tādṛśa puruṣārtha gandhavidūraṃ) paramātmānameva khalu, upāste- (nirdoṣanirupādhikasva svarūpāvirbhāvaprāptaye ) dhyānarūpeṇa karmaṇā upāste || 1 ||

svāsādhāraṇaiḥ dharmaiḥ, bahumukham - bahubhiḥ prakāraiḥ avadat - bādarāyaṇo nyarūpayat tasya - nirūpitasyaiva parabrahmaṇaḥ, iha sādhanādhyāyamadhye – prastute brahmaprāptisādhanabhūtopāyapratipādanapara- tṛtīyādhyāyāntarbhūte dvitīye pāde, asau ākṛṣya punaḥ cintā – carvitacarvaṇasadṛśī ākarṣaṇa-- pūrvikā bhūyo vicāraṇā, kimiti –— kasmai phalāya kriyate ? (nūtanaṃ na kimapi phalaṃ sidhyatītyarthaḥ) maivam — anayā rītyā śaṅkanaṃ nocitam vidyāḥ brahmaprāptau upāyabhūtāḥ sadvidyā''nandamayavidyādirūpā : vidyāḥ, prabhettum - vivicya pratipādayitum, param - brahma siddhopāyabhūtam, viśadayati – nirdoṣatva - nikhilakalyāṇaguṇā karatvābhyāṃ spaṣṭaṃ nirūpayati sūtra- kāraḥ, tat hi – viśadatayā parabrahmanirūpaṇaṃ hi tadrūpabhedāt - brahmasambandhināṃ guṇa-vibhūtyā - dīnāṃ rūpāṇāṃ viśeṣādeva sidhyati, prāpya tṛṣṇāprathigne – sarvairapyātmabhiḥ prāpyatvasvābhāvyavati parasmin brahmaṇi viṣayāntaravaitṛṣṇyapūrvakaṃ utkaṭecchā viśeṣasya jananāya vibhoḥ - prāpyatva- prāpakatvobhayavataḥ paramātmanaḥ, siddhopāyādibhāvam – vidyāgatasādhyopāyatvavilakṣaṇaṃ siddhatva- samānādhikaraṇamupāyatvaṃ (tathā) upāyatvasamānādhikaraṇamupeyatvaṃ ( prāpyatvaṃ ca prathayati- vistāreṇa pratipādayati asmin ubhayaliṅgapāde || ( ato nātra sādhanādhyāye siddhabrahmanirūpaṇe'pi asāṅgatya-paunaruktayā dikaṃ padamādadhātīti bhāvaḥ ||) || 1 ||

nairguṇyaṃ brahmaṇaśced vitatha iha guṇairbrahmavidyāvibhāgaḥ

so'stvetaiḥ kalpitaiścecchrutimatavihatirnā'tra dṛṣṭikramo'pi |
nirdoṣatvaṃ ca nityaṃ yadi vadasi mudhā doṣaśāntyartha yatnaḥ
karūpyaṃ cedduṣṭatā syāt prakṛtiriti parakṣiptaye caiṣa pādaḥ || 2 ||

brahmaṇaḥ—upāsyasya prāpyasya parabrahmaṇaḥ, nairguṇyaṃ cet — guṇasāmānyābhāva eva pāramārthiko yadi | tadā, iha — śārīrakagatasādhanādhyāye, guṇaiḥ brahmavidyāvibhāgaḥ— upāsyaviśeṣaṇabhūtaparamātmaguṇanibandhanaḥ brahmaprāptisādhanatayopadiṣṭānāṃ vidyānāṃ pārthakyavyapadeśaḥ, vitathaḥ — niṣprayojanaḥ, saḥ - guṇanibandhano brahmavidyāvibhāgaḥ, kalpitaiḥ etaiḥ astu cet- adhyastaireva guṇairbhavatu iti śaṅkane, śrutimatavihatiḥ - ('svābhāvikī jñānabalakriyā ca ' ) śrutitātparya vikopāpattiḥ, atra – mokṣārthopāsanasthale, dṛṣṭikramopa – phalaviśeṣārtham anyasmin anyadharmādhyāsātmakaḥ ‘mano brahma' ityādau dṛṣṭaḥ prakāro'pi na naiva vaktuṃ śakyate nityaṃ nirdoṣatvaṃ ca yadi vadasi — brahmaṇaḥ sārvakālika doṣavidūratvaṃ svīkuruṣe cet ! doṣa- śāntyarthayatnaḥ–brahmā bhinnasya jīvasya vedāntaśravaṇa śamadamādirūpaḥ doṣaharaṇaupayikaḥ sarvo'pi

prayatnaḥ mudhā — vyartha: syāt, kalpyaṃ cet ! - brahmaṇo nirdoṣatvaṃ na nityaṃ ; kintu kalpanīya - meva' iti yadyucyeta ! duṣṭatā prakṛtiḥ syāt - sadoṣatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ svābhāvikameva syāt, iti parakṣiptaye - evaṃ rītyā māyāvādimatīyān nirākartum eṣa pādaḥ ayaṃ tṛtīyadvitīyaḥ ubhayaliṅgādaḥ || 2 ||

kiṃ cādau cintyabhāvaḥ pramitiviṣayatā svaprabhavaṃ sukhatvaṃ

viśrādhiṣṭhātā ca svacahubhavanadhīrnirviśeṣe kathaṃ syāt ? |
sarvaśrutyarthahānaṃ svavacanavihatiḥ sarvamānaiśca bādho
māyāvaiyātyabhājāmiti saguṇadaśopāstipādaśca duḥsthaḥ || 3 ||
kiṃ ca-api ca ādau – śāstrārambhe, cintyabhāvaḥ brahmaṇo jijñāsyatā, pramiti- viṣayatāstrajanyamā gocaratvam svapramatvam - svayaṃprakāśasvarūpatvam sukhatvam - ānandaikatānatvam viśvādhiṣṭhānatā ca – jagadadhyāsadharmitvam svabahubhavanadhī: – svasyaiva bahurūpatvaprāptisaṅkalpaḥ, (itīdaṃ dharmaja: taṃ) nirviśeṣe nirdharmakadharmimātrasvarūpe brahmaṇi kathaṃ syāt ! – (nirviśeṣatvarakṣaṇāya uktānāmeṣāṃ jijñāsyatvādīnāmeva avāstavikatā svīkāre) śrutipratipannasyaiva sarvasyārthasya parityāga saṅgaḥ sarvāsāmapi śrutīnāṃ nirabhiprāyakatvāpattirvā, svavacanavihatiḥ - svairevoktayoḥ nirviśeṣatva jijñaḥ syatvādyoḥ mitho vyāghātāpattiḥ, sarva- mānaiśca bādhaḥ sarvairapi pratyakṣānumānāgamākhyaiḥ pramāṇaiḥ svoktārthasya bādhitatvaṃ ca māyāvai- yā tyabhājām – māyākhyasyaikasyārthasyāvalambanena sarvaṃ samarthayāmaḥ' ityevaṃ dhāṣṭaryayuktānāṃ mṛṣāvādināmadvaitinām prasajyate iti evaṃ rītyā cintane, saguṇadaśopāstipādaśca- saguṇadaśāyāmeva brahmagaḥ upāsanapratipādanaparaḥ guṇopasaṃhārapādo'pi tṛtīyatṛtīyaścaraṇaḥ, duḥsthaḥ – durghaṭaikasthitirbhavati || ( ataḥ śārīrakaṃ sarvamapi teṣāṃ daurghaṭyamayameveti bhāvaḥ || 3 || (292) tyaktaṃ doṣairguṇa' ḍhyaṃ yadi punariha tad brahma cintyeta pāde

jīvasvapnādyavasthāmananamatha kathaṃ 'saṅghaṭeteti cenna | svapnārthasraṣṭṛbhāvaprabhṛtibahuvidhabrahmamāhātmya siddhayai
jantorasya svamuktāvatiparavaśatājñaptaye caitadatra || 4 ||
iha pāde – asmin prakrānte ubhayaliṅgapāde, doṣaiḥ tyaktam - nirdoṣataikasvarūpam, guṇāḍhyam - anantakalyāṇaguṇa paripūrṇasvarūpaṃ ca tat brahma prāpyaṃ siddhopāyabhūtaṃ yadi punaḥ

cintyeta - vicicārayiṣitaṃ yadi, tadā, jīva mananam - jīvātmanaḥ svapna suṣuptiprabhṛti- daśāviṣayakaṃ nyāyataścintanam kathaṃ saṅghaṭeta 1 – kayā rītyā saṅgataṃ bhavet ! (bhagavadguṇa- nirūpaṇapare jīvadharmanirūpaṇasya asaṅgatatvāt ) iti cet na – ityevaṃ na sādhu, atra asmin paramātmaguṇopasaṃhārapare pāde etat - nīvīyasvapnādidaśā cintanam, svāpnā··· siddhayai- acintitānāmatyāścaryarūpāṇāṃ svapnadaśā sambandhināmarthānāṃ sṛṣṭikartṛtvādirūpaṃ yat nānāprakāraṃ parabrahmaṇo māhātmyaṃ tasya sphuṭapratipattaye, (tathā) asya jantoḥ saṃsarato jīvātmanaḥ, svamuktau- svīyāyāṃ mokṣāvasthāyām atijñaptaye ca- atyantaparamātmapāratantrya jñāpanāya ca ato na kāpyasāṅgatyamiti || 4 ||
