pādasyā'syā''dyamardha katicidadhijaguḥ pūrvapādasya śeṣaṃ
'paścādardhantu (śca)' sākṣādanughaṭitamupāstyarthatattadguṇokteḥ |
etannā'tīva hṛdyaṃ śacalitakathane cāturīvaiparītyād
brahmoktau jīvadoṣagraha iha tu mukhaṃ tatpratidvandvasiddheḥ || 5 ||

katicit — śārīra kārthacintane pravṛttāḥ kecana, asya pādasya – tṛtīyadvitīyasya ubhayaliṅgapādasya (adhikaraṇāṣṭakasampannasya), ādyam ardham – prāthamikam adhikaraṇacatuṣṭayātmakaṃ bhāgam pūrvapadasya – atītasya vairāgyapādasya, śeṣam – tannirupitāvaśiṣṭāṃśabhūta-jīvīyasvapna- -suṣutpādyavasthāgata doṣapratipādakatayā tadupakārakam, (tathā) paścādardhantu tadanantara pravṛttaṃ —tadanantarapravṛttaṃ adhikaraṇacatuṣṭayātmakaṃ etatpādīyottarārdhabhāgaṃ tu, upāstya.... ṇokteḥ - paramātmopāsanaupayikānāṃ bhagavadguṇaviśeṣāṇāṃ tatra pratipādanāddhetoḥ, sākṣādanughaṭitam - pāramparikatāṃ vinā sākṣādevaita- tpādīyapradhānārthasambaddham iti ca adhijaguḥ uktavantaḥ, etat — īdṛśaṃ varṇanam, atīva hṛdyaṃ na atyantaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ na bhavati, śabalitakathane - vilakṣaṇamilitārthadvaya nirūpaṇe ekasmin pāde kṛte sati, cāturīvaiparītyāt — sūtrakṛtaḥ acāturyāpatteḥ, (evamabhiprāyakatve pūrvapādaśeṣaṃ pūrvapāde eva yojayet; uttarārdha ca guṇopasaṃhārapāde eva yojayet | na tvīdṛśaṃ anugataikārtha- rahitaṃ pādamekaṃ racayet sūtrakāraḥ iti | ) brahmoktau – nityanirdoṣasya brahmaṇa: nikhilakalyāṇa- - guṇākarasya pratipādane kriyamāṇe, iha — asmin pāde, jīvadoṣagrahaḥ jīvasambandhināṃ doṣāṇāṃ

grahaṇam, mukhaṃ tu - dvāramātrameva bhavati, tatpratidvandvisiddheḥ - doṣābhāvapratibhaṭasya sadoṣatvasya avagamasiddheḥ (abhāvajñāne pratiyogijñānasya kāraṇatvāt ) || 5 ||

śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṅgrahaḥ ||
guṇitvataḥ saṃnidhitaśca jīvaḥ svāmārthakarteti na śaṅkyametat | asambhavantvādahitakriyāyāstirohitatvena guṇasya 'cā'sya' || 2 ||

jīvaḥ – svāpnādyarthānāmanubhavitā saṃsārī jīvaḥ, guṇitvataḥ svānubhāvyārthasṛṣṭyaupa- yika satyasaṅkalpatvādiguṇāśrayatvāt saṃnidhitaśca – svenānubhūyamānārthena sākaṃ svasya niyata- sāmīpyavasvācca hetoḥ, svāmārthakarteti svanadaśāyāmanubhūyamānānāmarthānāṃ sraṣṭeti pūrvapakṣe, na śaṅkyametat – uktaṃ svāmārthasratvaṃ na śaṅkāīm ahita kriyāyāḥ – svaduḥkhapariṇāminyāḥ svānārtha(du:kha) sṛṣṭikriyāyāḥ, asambhavactvāt svanaiva buddhipūrva sambhāvitatvābhāvāt asya guṇasya - etadīyasya svābhāvikasyā'pi satyasaṅkalpatvādiguṇasya, tirohitatvena ca – anāvi- bhūtatvena ca hetunā uktārthasiddheḥ || 2 ||

bhavādinā satyatvena niścīyamāne'rthe śrutapariharaṇāyogataḥ - śrutāṃśasya parityāgānau cityāt, sṛṣṭimātra svīkṛtya – svapna rthasṛṣṭirūpaṃ viṣayaṃ vāstava svarūpamabhyupagamya, aṃśe tu - itarāṃśe eva kasmiṃścit bādhāt - prāmāṇikāt tadabhāvāvagāhijñānāta, bhramamapi - bhrāntirūpatā mapi, yathā jāgaram – jāgraddaśānubhāvyeṣvartheṣu yathā pramāyāḥ bhramasya cetyubhayorapi yathāyogyaṃ saṃvalana- masti tathā svapne'pyubhayoryathāyogyaṃ melanaṃ vidyamānam, na kṣipāmaḥ naiva pratyākhyāsyāmaḥ || 6 ||

kaścidyogaprabhāvānnijaparabhavana cairasañcāranītyā

niṣkrāntaḥ pūrvadehādviśati paravapuḥ pūrvamāpnoti bhūyaḥ |
itthaṃ svapne'pyudantasthitiriti katicicchvāsavṛtyā ''nyathā'nye ' cittodyaddhīprabhṛtyetaratanubhajane sau marinyāyasiddheḥ || 7 ||

nija... nītyā - ( loke sarveṣāṃ janānāṃ svabhavane anyadīyabhavane ca (svasaṅkalpasaha- -kṛta svīya hastapādādivyāpārataḥ ) yathecchaṃ dehinaḥ gatāgatavyāpāro yathā saṃpravartate tathaiva, (naramahādeva ) yogaprabhāvāt – yogaviśeṣajanyān cittādigatāta sāmarthyaviśeṣāt kaścit - prāptasāmarthyaḥ kaścana yogī, pūrvadehāt - prāktanāt svaśarīrāt niṣkrāntaḥ - utthitaḥ san paravapuḥ - anyadīyaṃ śarīram, viśati - praviśati, bhūyaḥ punaḥ pūrvam – prāktanaṃ svīyaṃ śarīram āpnoti - praveśena sambati ( idaṃ haṭhayogāditaḥ sampadyata iti yogaśāstrataḥ itihāsapurāṇāditaśca avagatam ), ittham - evameva viśeṣe'pi, udantasthitiriti vṛttāntāvasthānamiti, cintakāḥ (svapnavyavahāraṃ nirvahanti ) anye – apare śvāsocchvāsādikaṃ daihika vyāpāraṃ vidyamānamālocya anyathā - svāpnavyavahāraṃ prakārāntareṇa nirvahanti ( kiṃ tat prakārāntaramiti cet ! ) cittodyaddhī prasṛtyā - cittasahakārataḥ udyantī- vikāsaṃ prāpnuvantī yā dharmabhūtajñānākhyā dhīḥ tasyāḥ prasaraṇarūpayā vyāpṛtyā, ( śayāna de he svapnasṛṣṭadehe ca vyāpāra nirvāho bhavatīti) itaratanu bhajane - śarīrāntarāśrayaṇe kriyamāṇe, saubharinyāyasiddheḥ saubharināmnā ṛṣiṇā nānāśarīraparigrahakāle, ekasmin dehe cetanasyā- vasthānaṃ śiṣṭeṣu taddhīvyāyā vyavahāra nirvāhaḥ ityayamaṃśo yathā dṛṣṭaḥ ; tathā svapnadaśā panne jīve'pi śayānadehe śvāsādivyāpāraḥ anyatra gatāgatādivyāpāraśceti sarvavyavahārasāmīcīnya- siddheḥ || 7 ||

svapne'pi – svapnākhye jīvīye daśā- katicit kecana jīvanavyavahāra- kecana, śvāmavṛcyā – pūrvatane śarīre

ukta patyā prajānāṃ bhāvini daharavat satyasaṅkalpatādyaṃ putrādeścaiṣa kartā prakṛta iha sṛjet svāpnamarthaṃ ca mevam || muktau tādṛgguṇokteranabhimatasamutpādanāderayogāt
'svāpnānāṃ' sūcakatvādapi nikhila jagatkartu reṣā (hi) pisṛṣṭiḥ || 8 ||

prajānāṃ patyā – prajāpatinā caturmukhena, daharavat — daharākāśapadā bhidheye paramātmanī, bhāvini - (saṃsārasya bhāgini ) jīvātmanyapi, satyasaṅkalpatādyam – satyasaṅkalpatva- satyakāmatva- prabhṛtiguṇajātam, uktam– 'eṣa ātmā'pahatapāpmā' ityādinā vākyena pratipāditam, (ataḥ ) eṣaḥ - jīvātmā, putrādeśca kartā - putra kalatrādirūpasarvavidhakāmyārthasraṣṭṛtvavānapi iha prakṛta:- asmin prakaraṇe prastutaśca, ('kāmaṃ kāmaṃ puruṣo nirmimāṇaḥ, putrapautrān vṛṇīṣva' ityādau kaṭhavākye) svāṣnamarthaṃ ca sṛjet — svapnāvasthāyāṃ svenā'nubhāvyamarthaviśeṣaṃ ca nirmātuṃ prabhavedeva. (iti śaṅkane), maivam — itthaṃ śaṅkanaṃ nocitam muktau tādṛgguṇokteḥ - jīvātmanaḥ muktikāle eva satyasaṅkalpatvādiguṇajātāvirbhāvasya prajāpativākye pratipādanāt anabhimatasamutpādanādeḥ ayogāt – (svapne yataḥ anukūlā narthāniva pratikūlānapyarthān paśyati) svayaṃ sraṣṭṛtve pratikūlārtha sṛṣṭirūpavyāpārasya aghaṭamānatvāt (nahi kaścit svapratikūlameva svayaṃ nirmātu- micchati yatate vā) svāpnānām – svapnadaśāyāmanubhūyamānānāmarthānām, sūcakatvāt - jāgraddaśābhāvīṣṭāniṣṭārthaviṣayakajñānajanakatvasya śrutyādipratipannatvādapi ca eṣā sṛṣṭirapi- svāpnārthaviṣayiṇī sṛṣṭikriyā'pi nikhilajagatkartuḥ - sakala nuvananirmāturbhāgavata eva ; na jīvasyeti || 8 ||

kāmaṃ kāmaṃ vidhātetyapi ṇamulucito'nūdyate ceśa itthaṃ

so'yaṃ supteṣu jāgapi viśadamidaṃ sampariṣvaṅgavākyāt | tattatkālāvasānāḥ kati kati niyatā jāgare'pyarthabhedā- stattatkarmānurūpaṃ phalavitaraṇamityetadapyuktamāptaiḥ || 9 ||

kāmakāmaṃ vidhāteti — kaṭhavallīgate ' kāmaṃ kāmaṃ puruṣo nirmimāṇaḥ' ityasmin vākye, ('vidhātā' iti nirmimāṇaḥ' ityasya śrautapadasya arthānuvādaḥ) ṇamulapi ucita :- kāmam' iti dviruktaḥ śabdaḥ ktvāpratyayārthaka 'ṇamul ' pratyayāntaḥ ityeva samīcīno'rthaḥ ; na tu dvitīyā vibhaktayantaḥ iti, (tena 'kamu-kāntau' ityasmāt ṇamuli ' saṅkalpya' ityevārthaḥ ;

natu 'kāmyamānaṃ putramitrakalatrā dikaṃ nānāvidhamarthajātam' iti ) ittham - anena prakāreṇa, (jīvakarmānuguṇanānā saṅkalpa kartṛtvena ) īśaśca – sarveśvaraḥ paramātmaiva, anūdyate - śrutyantare tathopadiṣṭa eva punarjñāpyate, so'yam - vividha saṅkalpapūrvakatratvavattayā jñāpita evāyaṃ paramātmā, supteṣu jāgartyapi jīveṣu uparatavāhyavyāpāratayā avasthiteṣu satsu avismṛtasvakartavyaḥ apramatta evāvatiṣṭhate, idam – jīvasya suptatvadaśāyāṃ paramātmano jāgratvam, sampariṣvaṅgavākyāt- prājñenātmanā sampariṣvakta ' iti prājñapadaghaṭitāt suṣuptau jīvasya paramātmapariṣvaṅgabodhakaśruti- vākyāt, viśadam - spaṣṭam jāgare'pi – jāgraddaśāyāṃ dṛśyamāne jagatyapi, kati kati kiyantaḥ asaṅkhyātāḥ, arthabhedāḥ - arthaviśeṣāḥ, niyatāḥ puruṣaviśeṣamā trānubhāvyatva- niyamavantaḥ, tattatkāla(vasānāḥ - anubhava yogya kālaviśeṣamātre gate avasita ( vinaṣṭa) svarūpāḥ, vartanta ityanubhūtacaram (vidyudvṛṣṭyādīnāṃ ākālikatvāt ) āptairapi daivajña - mahāyogi prabhṛtibhiḥ tattvajñairapi tattatkarmānurūpam — tattajjī vīya karmānuguṇam phalavitaraṇamityetat- paramātmanā cirasthāyyāśuvinā śisukhaduḥkhādibhoga pradānamiti naijaṃ tattvam uktam — śāstreṣu viśadīkṛtam || 9 |

māyāmātroktilā bhācchūtimukhasugatā viśvamithyātvamāhuḥ
śāstrārambhe tadebhiḥ kathitamiha tato'saṅgatatvādiduḥstham | māyāśabdo na mithyāvacana upacara 'tvatra tat' kiṃ tataḥ syāt
satye'strādau prayogāducitaniyamane so'yamāścaryatārthaḥ || 10 ||

māyāmātroktilābhāt - ' māyāmātraṃ tu' iti śārīrakasūtre 'māyāmātra 'śabdasya apāramārthyam, śrutimukhasugatāḥ śrutīn mukhe uccārayantaḥ vaidikatvaṃ sveṣāṃ prakhyāpya antato bauddhīye śūnyatve kṛtamatayaḥ advaitisāmayikā:, ('gomukhavyāghraḥ ' itibadvā) āhuḥ - varṇayanti tat- viśvamithyātvam, śāstrārambhe – śārīraka prāthamika sūtravyākhyāna eva, ebhiḥ - advaitisāmayikaiḥ, kathitam---sūtratātparyaviṣayatayā varṇitam, tataḥ – tasmāddhetoḥ, iha — sādhanādhyāyīye'sminnadhi-- karaṇe māyāmātratvasthāpanam, asaṅgatatvādiduḥstham - asāṅgatya - paunaruktatyādidoṣajaṭilaṃ bhavet, māyāśabdaḥ - ' māyā' ityayaṃ śabdaḥ, na mithyāvacanaḥ apāramārthya vācako na bhavati ; ('māyā vayunaṃ jñānam' iti naighaṇṭukoktayā saṅkalpāparaparyāyajñāna viśeṣa vā cakatvā vagateḥ ) atra -

bādarāyaṇāt sūtrakṛtaḥ sakāśāllabdhatvāt viśvamithyātvam – jagataḥ

māyāmātramiti sūtre, tat – māyāpadam – upacaratu – gauṇyā vṛśyā (ābhidhānikārthatyāgena) mithyātvabodhakaṃ bhavatītyabhyupagamyatāṃ nāma, tataḥ kiṃ syāt tena kiṃ vā prayojanaṃ labhyeta ; na kimapi, satye—paramārthabhūte eva, astrādau – astrādirūpe'rthe, prayogāt – ' tena māyā sahasraṃ - - māyāsahasraṃ tat' ityādipaurāṇika prayogadarśanāt ucitaniyamane - ( aupacārikā naupacārika vivekena) yogyārthe māyāśabdaśakteḥ vyutpādane (vidvadbhiḥ) kriyamāṇe sati so'yam - vipratipannārthakatayā prastutaḥ sūtrasthaḥ māyāśabdaḥ, āścaryatārthaḥ āścaryatvaṃ pravṛttinimittatayā''śritya svāpnasya arthaviśeṣasya bodhakaḥ iti niścīyate || 10 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ ||

atha sandhyādhikaraṇe_' na tatra sthāna sthayogā na panthāno bhavanti ityādinā, ' . " sa hi kartā' iti prakṛtasya jīvasya kartṛśvaśravaṇāta ' svāmārthasṛṣṭiḥ tasyaiva ' ityāśaṅkaya, jīvasya, saṃsāgdaśāyāṃ satyasaṅkalpatvādyanāvirbhāvena vicitrārthasṛṣṭyaśaktatvāt ya eṣu suteṣu jāgarti' ityādau svāpnasṛṣṭeḥ paramātmakartṛtvāvadhāraṇāt 'svāsṛṣṭiḥ paramātmakartṛkeva - ityuktam ||

bhūmiḥ – ādhārabhūtaṃ sthānam, nāḍyaḥ – hitākhyā: nāḍyaḥ, purītat - tannāmakanā ḍī viśeṣo vā, athavā – yadvā paramaḥ - paramātmaiva vā ( etadanyatamo vikalpena yaḥ kaścana) syāt - bhavitumarhati iti — evaṃ śaṅkane, idaṃ na- īdṛśaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na sādhu vikalpadoṣāt - kalpita- vikalpasthale aṣṭadoṣāṇāṃ satvāt prāsāda vat syāt - prāsāda lagataparyaṅka-tadbhatatalapavat trayāṇāṃ samuccityaiva sthānanā bhavitumaītīti ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṃgrahaḥ ||

tadabhāvāghikaraṇe – 'atha yatraitat puruṣaḥ suptaḥ' ityādivākyeṣu, 'nāḍīṣu suptaḥ ' 'purītati śete' 'satā somya tadā sampannaḥ' iti nāḍī-purītan- brahmaṇāṃ suṣuptisthānatva śravaṇāt, yugapat jīvasya anekatra pravṛttyasambhavācca teṣāṃ suṣuptisthānatvaṃ vikalpena ityāśaṅkaya,

trayāṇāṃ suṣuptisthānatvaśravaṇāt prāsāda khaṭavā - paryaṅkavat nāḍyādīnāṃ kāryabhedāt | samuccaye sambhavati, pākṣikabādhagarbhavikalpāsambhavāt samuccayenaiva sthānatvam — ityuktam ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||
mutrihmaṇyapītijaihadasukhagaṇā tādṛśīyaṃ suṣuptiḥ
śrutyaiva khyāpyate'tastadanu tanubhṛdudbudhyamānastato'nyaḥ | maivaṃ karmānuvṛtteḥ smaraṇaniyamataḥ pūrva eveti śabdā-
nmokṣopāyādiśiṣeḥ svapadanuvadanāt prācya eva prabuddhaḥ || 12 ||

muktiḥ muktipadābhidheyo'rtho nāma jahada sukhagaṇā – nivṛttaduḥkhastomā, brahmaṇi apītiḥ paramātmani jīvātmano layaḥ, ātyantikasaṃśleṣaḥ, iyaṃ suṣuptiḥ - prastutā (nidrā- paraparyāyā) jīvasya suṣuptināmnī daśā'pi tādṛśī - brahmaṇi gāḍhapariṣvaṅgarūpā svāpyayākhyā ātyantikaduḥkhanivṛttiyogyā muktireveti vaktuṃ śakyate śrutyaiva – ' svamapīto bhavati, prājñe- nā''tmanā sampariṣvaktaḥ' ‘satā somya tadā sampanna:' ityevamādirūpayā śrutyaiva khyāpyate- suṣupteḥ brahmaṇi jīvasya adhyayarūpatā prakaṭīkriyate, ataḥ muktimuṣayoḥ avilakṣaṇatvāddhetoḥ, tadanu – svāpānantaram, udbudhyamānaḥ – prabodhaṃ prāpnuvānaḥ tanubhṛt – jīvaḥ, tato'nyaḥ- suptājjīvādatirikta eva (ityevaṃ śaṅkane) maivam - anayā rītyā śaṅkanaṃ māstu, karmānuvṛtteḥ- jāti jīve yat prāktanaṃ prārabdhaṃ karma vartate tasya svapna yadyavasthāpane'pi tasmin anuvartanāt, suptotthite'pi tadanuvṛtteḥ, smaraṇa niyamataḥ sa evā'ham' iti suṣupta jāgratoḥ abhedasyaiva pratyabhijñānāt (tathā) svāpātpūrva kālamanubhūtānāṃ viṣayāṇāṃ utthitenaiva smaraṇaṃ nānyeneti niyama- darśanāt, pūrva eveti śabdāt - vyāghra rāhādirūpeṇa pūrvamavasthita evāyaṃ suptotthitaḥ ; nānya- ityarthāt 'vyāghro vā varāho vā .... yadyadbhavanti tadābhavanti' iti śrutivākyāt, mokṣo- pāyādiśiṣṭe:- suṣupte ktirūpatve jīvasāmānyasya svāparūpāyā mukternisargasiddhatayā mokṣopāya- tadaṅga jātādyanuṣṭhāna vidhāyina śāstrāṇāmānarthakyaprasaṅgena tadrūpatvā nabhyupagateḥ apunarāvṛtyavasthā- smakatvāsambhavena tasyaiva prabuddhatvasyāvaśyaṃbhāvāt svapadanuvadanāt – indraprajāpatisaṃvāde nā'ha khalvayam' ityādinā suptaṃ puruṣamanūdya tadīyāvasthāyāḥ abhogyatvasya 'nāhamatra bhogyaṃ paśyāmi' ityanena vākyenokteḥ tasyā muktirūpatvāyogādapi, prabuddhaḥ - suṣuptitaḥ jāgraddaśāṃ gataḥ puruṣa:, prācya eva - pūrva jādraddaśāta : ( tasmiṃstasmindehe) suṣupti prāptaḥ puruṣa eva; nā'nyaḥ || 12 || jīvānā ditvamūce dṛṣadanukaraṇaṃ kṣepsyate cā'pavarge
svargādyarthapravṛtti: śrutinayaviditā saugatādyāśca bhagnāḥ | kalpānte'pyekatoktirniyamitaviṣayā nāmarūpa vahāṇād bhūyaścintā suṣupteḥ pralayasamadaśā sajihāsādisiddhyai || 13 ||

jīvānāditvam - cetanānāmutpattirāhityena anāditām, ūce - pūrvameva bādarāyaṇo vyavasthāpitavān na karmā'vibhāgāt' ityādisūtreṇa, apavarge muktidaśāyām, dṛṣadanu- karaṇam – pāṣāṇakalpata| śrayaṇena caitanyocchedavamvam, kṣepsyate ca - bhogamātrasāmye 'tyādi - sūtrataḥ nirākriyata eva, svargādyarthapravṛttiḥ svargādyuddeśena cetanasya dehinaḥ yo yāgādau yatnaḥ saḥ, śrutinayaviditā - karmakāṇḍasambandhinyā śrutyā, tadarthavicāra tmakena pūrvamīmāṃsāśāstreṇa ca avadhṛta svarūpa :, (aihikāmuṣmikaupayika karmavyavasthāyāḥ sthāyi jīva | bhyupagamaika nibandhanatvāt ) saugatādyāśva ātmā nityatvaparyavasitasiddhāntā bauddha cārvākāthā asaddārśanikā api, bhagnāḥ- parapakṣapratikṣepapare dvitīye'dhyāye nirākṛtā eva kalpānte mahāpralayadaśāyām, ekatokti- rapi pare deve jīvasya ekībhāvaśrutirapi nāmarūpaprahāṇāt niyamitaviṣayā-pṛthakpratipatti- vyavahārā īnāmarūpavibhāgātyantavigamamātrābhiprāyā; na svarūpaikyaparā, ( evameteṣāmarthānāṃ sarveṣāṃ anyataḥ siddhau satyām) bhūyaścintā – asmin adhikaraṇe kṛto vicāraśca suṣupteḥ- nidrākhyāyāḥ dehicetanagatāyā avasthāyāḥ, pralaya.... siddhayai pralaya daśā sadṛśatā mātra- sādhanena muktidaśāyāḥ tadvilakṣaṇatvasiddhayā jāmaddaśāvat suṣuptidaśāyā api tyājyatvabuddheḥ sampattaye || 13 ||

śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṃgrahaḥ ||
prāṇasya sarvendriya saṃhṛteśva vyāpārahānermaraṇaṃ tu mūrcchā |
'mubhdhelā bho' maraṇasya sā syādākāravaiṣamya samutthitibhyām || 13 ||
mūrcchā- dehini kadāciddaśyamānā mūrcchākhyā avasthā, maraṇaṃ tu maraṇapadābhidheya — avasthaiva prāṇasya vyāpārahāne : - ( tasyāmavasthāyāṃ) prāṇāyaktavyāpāraviśeṣasya dehe nāśāt, sarvendriya saṃhṛteśca sarveṣāmeva antarbāhyendriyāṇāṃ uparatavyāpāratvadarśanādapi, (iti śaṅkane naitat ) yataḥ, sāmūrcchā, mugdhe—mūrcchite puruṣe vidyamānā ( avasthā ), maraṇasya ardhalābhaḥ syāt- maraṇadaśāyā ardhāvasthā prāptirūpaiva bhavet, ākāravaiṣamya- samutthitibhyām – itarāvasthā gatā- kārāpekṣayā mūrcchāgatākārasya vilakṣaṇatvāta, (tathā) mūrcchāta: puna: jāgraddaśāprāptirūpasya - samutthānasya darśanācca || 13 ||

janārdanapraṇayiṣu - mokṣaprada (mokṣamicchet-janārdanāt ) śrīmannārāyaṇapravaṇeṣu trayyanteṣu- vedāntabhāgeṣu sāmānya viśeṣavartmasu - sāmānya viśeṣanyāya (saṃśayaparihāra ya śabdopātta- viśeṣaviṣaye sāmānyaśabdasya arthasaṅkocaḥ ) māśritya arthabodhanapravṛtteṣu guṇapratyāsa- vidhyātmasu - brahmaṇo guṇavattā niṣedha - guṇavidhisvarūpeṣu (guṇaniṣedhassāmānyaviṣayaka ḥ guṇavidhiḥ viśeṣaviṣayakaḥ ; vihitavyatiriktāḥ guṇāḥ na santīti) katicana_vedāntapadavyavahāryāḥ kecana, (advaitinaḥ) apaccheda nīticchalāt – apacchedāghikaraṇīyanyāyānusaraṇavyājena, prābalya - daurbalyayoḥ prastāvam - nirguṇavākyeṣu prābalyasya saguṇavākyeṣu daurbalyasya ca prasañjanaṃ, pratipedire yat - anucintitavanta iti yadasti tat - tādṛśaṃ cintanam mīmāṃsā ... dūreṣu karmamīmāṃsā mārgābhyāsagandharahiteṣu ( mandaprajñeṣu śobhate bhābhāsate; (na tu vastuto bhīmāṃsakeṣu ;) apacchedanyāyasya atatsvarūpatāyāḥ samyakcintanato'vagamāt || 68 ||

anyonyabhinnaviṣayā na virodhagandha-
maīnti tanna vidhayaḥ pratiṣedhavādhyāḥ | 69 ||

sarvā bhūtāni' ityevamākārayā hiṃsā sāmānyapratiṣedhaparayā śrutigirā, kiṃ niṣiddhā- bādhitaviṣayā bhavati kim ? ; naiva, paśupadaviṣayaḥ tadvidhigataḥ paśupadārthaśva, chāga eva- chāgākhyaḥ paśuviśeṣa eva na paśusāmānyamiti, chāgo vāditi ca ' chāgovā mantravarṇāt' ityevaṃrūpeṇa jaiminīyasūtreṇa ca, nyarūpi pūrvamīmāṃsāyāṃ nyāyanirūpaṇataḥ nirṇītam, tadubhayanyāyataḥ — uktenotsargāpavādanyāyena chāgapaśunyāyena ceti nyāyadvayena, nirmale – nirastanikhiladoṣe, śrutaśubhaguṇake - śrutiprati - pannakalyāṇaguṇajāte, brahmaṇi - paramātmani, heyadharmakṣepārthā apuruṣārthabhūtaguṇānāṃ dūrīkaraṇārthī, nirguṇoktiḥ– guṇaniṣedhavācoyukti: (śrutyādigateti jñeyam ) || 70 ||

heyaṃ vastu svato yat sthitiriha hi bhaveduḥkhakṛt svecchayā'pi tyājyatvaṃ nā'nyathā syāditi na nirupadherheyabhāvasya hāneḥ | nityasvātantryabhājo bhavina iva daśābhedato nā'pyavadyaṃ
śrasyaikatra dehe para- taditarayoḥ śuddhayaśuddhī hyadhīte || 17 ||

yat vastu – yādṛśamarthasvarūpam svataḥ – svīyastrarūpa-svabhāvābhyām, heyam - tyāgāīm, aparigrāhyam, iha tādṛśe'sminvastuni sthitiḥ– vidyamānatā, sveccha- yā'pi - parapreraṇā vinā svasaṅkalpenaiva prāptāpi satī, duḥkhakṛt - prātikūlyāpādikā, bhavet hi - sampadyetaiva ; ( aśucisamparkasya svakṛtatve parakṛtatve'pi vā mālinya prayojakatve avivādāt ) anyathā – tathātvābhāve, tyājyatvaṃ na syāt - ( tasya vastunaḥ) heyataiva na siṃdhyet (na hi kiṃcit kasyacidaheyaṃ kasyacidgrāhyaṃ bhavitumaīti svabhāvanaiyatyābhāvāpatteḥ) iti na - evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na sādhu, nirupayeḥ – upādhisāmānyā saṃspṛṣṭaṃ paramātmānaṃ prati, heyabhāvasya kasminnapi vastuni pratikūlatvakṛtā yāstyājyatāyāḥ, hāneḥ prasakterevābhāvāt nityasvā- tantryabhājaḥ anāhitasvābhāvikasvādhīnasvapravṛtyādimataḥ paramapuruṣasya, bhavina iva - iva- baddhajīvasyeva, daśābhedato'pi avasthābhedāyattamapi, avadyam – doṣabhāgitvam, na naiva āpadyate, hi-yataḥ, ekatra dehe ekasminneva śarīre, parataditarayoḥ paramātma- jīvātmanoḥ, śuddha śuddhī - śarīrāvasthānakṛtadoṣāyogaḥ, tatkṛtadoṣayogaśca śrutyaiva - 'dvā suparṇe 'ti śrutyaiva, adhīte - tayoranya: pippalaṃ svādvasyanannannanyo'bhicākaśīti' iti khaṇḍena paṭhite || 17 || brahma kṣatrādideheṣvaṇuriva vibhurapyātmabhāvena tiṣṭhaṃ-

kasyāpyabhāvāt na jñāpyate jñāpanīyo na bhavati (ataḥ īśvare ajñātajñāpanarūpasya vidhiprayojanasyā'bhāvaḥ) śāsitṛtvāt – sarvaniyantṛtva svābhāvyāt, śāsyaḥ na – anya kartṛkasya praśāsanasya agocaraḥ (ataḥ īśvare apravṛttapravartanarūpasya vidhiprayojanasyābhāvaḥ ) jagati — loke, hitavidā -kṣemasādhanaṃ jānatā puruṣeṇa, kiṃcijjño hi - jñeyaikadeśamātrajñānavān hi puruṣaḥ, bodhyate ajñāteviṣaye jñāpyate, preryate ca – pūrvaṃ pravṛtteraviṣaye'rthe pravartyate ca || 18 ||

utsarge gāpavādaṃ na khalu nayavidaḥ kṣobhaṇīyaṃ kṣamante

tasmād brāhme guṇādau vidhiviṣayamatikramya tiṣṭhenniṣedhaḥ |
evaṃ śānte virodhe na hi samaviṣayāpacchidānyāyasiddhi-

īṣṭo nityaṃ niṣedhaḥ para iha ca tataḥ syādupakāntinītiḥ || 19 ||

nayavidaḥ – mīmāṃsā nipuṇāḥ, utsargeṇa – sāmānyaśāstreṇa, apavādaṃ kṣobhaṇīyam - apavādāparaparyāyasya viśeṣaśāstrasya bādhyatvavādam, na kṣamante khalu naiva sahante iti prasiddhamet tasmāt — viśeṣaśāstrasya sāmānyaśāstrābādhyatvasiddheḥ, brāhme guṇādau – parabrahma - sambandhiguṇavigrahādidharmaviraye niṣedhaḥ - nirguṇavākyādipratipanno guṇābhāvaḥ, guṇābhāvajñāpakaḥ nirguṇamityādiniṣedha iti vā, vidhiviṣayam – guṇajñāpakaśāstra gocaraṃ - satyakā matvā dikam, atikramya – vihāyaiva tiṣṭhet — avasthitasvarūpo bhavet ; ( na tu vidhigocaramākramya varteta ) evam – viṣayabhedacintanata, virodhe – vidhi - niṣedhayormadhye ekatareṇa aparasya apahṛtaviṣayatve, śānte - nirākṛte sati, sama... siddhiḥ samānaviṣaya mātra gocarasya apacchedādhikaraṇanyāyasya saguṇa-nirguṇavākyaviṣayatālābhaḥ, na hina sambhāvitaḥ khalu niṣedhaḥ pratiṣedhavādaḥ, nityaṃ paraḥ dṛṣṭaḥ niyataṃ vidhyānantaryavāneva anubhavasiddhaḥ ; na tvaniyatapaurvāparyayān ; (avacchedanyāyastu aniyana paurvāparya mātra viṣayaḥ; udgātṛpratihartrādyapacchedaviṣaye paurvāparyanaiyatyā- siddheriti ) tataḥ - niṣedhasya vidhyānantaryaniyamāt iha - saguṇa-nirguṇavākya viṣaye (guṇa siddhi- naiyatyasāpekṣe, upakāntinītiḥ - asañjātavirodhopakramādhikaraṇanyāyaḥ, syāt – pravṛttimaīti na tu apacchedanyāyaḥ || 10 ||

savaṃ kāryasya gopāyati 'kathamasataḥ sadbhavedityadhīti-

vyānyatvaṃ hi kārye vyapanayati paraṃ 'mṛttiketyeva' śabdaḥ |
antarbhāvādviśiṣṭe bhagavati jagato 'neha nāne'ti yuktaṃ

kathamasataḥ dhītiḥ - ( 'sadeva somyedamagra āsīt ' iti prakramya pravṛttaḥ ) 'kathamasataḥ- sajjāyeta?' ityayaṃ śrutipāṭhaḥ, kāryatva sattvam – jāyamānasya vastuna: sadrūpatām, gopāyati- avāghitamāpādayati kārya-kāraṇayoraikarūpyāvaśyaṃbhāvena sadrūpasya jagadākhyakāryasya karaṇe - nā'pi sadrūpeṇāvaśyaṃbhavitavyam, na asadātmakena iti) mṛttiketyeva śabdaḥ - 'vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ' ityanantaramukta: 'mṛttiketyeva satyam' ityayaṃ śrautaḥ śabdaḥ, kārye- utpadyamāne vastuni, dravyānyatvaṃ paraṃ vyapanayati hi kāraṇabhūtadravyāpekṣayā ātyantika- bhedameva khalu vyāvartayati ; ( na tu kāryasya asatyatvam - avāstavikatvaṃ bodhayati), viśiṣṭe- bhagavati—cetanācetanātmakasarvajagadviśiṣṭe paramātmani, jagataḥ - prapañcasya, antarbhāvāt - viśiṣṭaikadeśatārūpāṃśatvasattvāt neha nāneti- 'neha nānā'sti kiṃcana' iti brahmānātma ke tara- niṣedhaparaṃ śrautaṃ vākyam, yuktam – abādhitārthakam yuktam — abādhitārthakam ( na tu brahmetara niṣedhaparatayā yuktam ) 'neti neti' ityuktiśca_ 'athā'na ādeśo neti neti' iti vṛhadāraṇyakaśrutirapi pare- parasmin brahmaṇi, nirdiṣṭeyanvaśaṅkām – pūrvoktaguṇasampannatvamātratā bhrāntim praśamayati- dūrīkaroti ; na tu guṇavattāṃ niṣedhati ( tato'pyadhikaguṇānāṃ vacanāt ) ( ataḥ brahmaṇa: ubhaya- liṅgatvāvirodhaḥ) || 20 ||

****

tattadvastu pradeśe sakalaguṇatayā pūrṇadṛśyaḥ parātmā
vṛddhihāsādibhedojjhita iti hi ! jalādhārasūryośmoktiḥ |
asparśodāhṛtiścanna hi ! ghaṭakarakākāśadṛṣṭāntayukti-

stasmādbrahma dviliṅgaṃ dvividhavibhavamityeva vedāntapakṣaḥ || 21 ||

parātmā – paramapuruṣaḥ, tattadvastu pradeśe - pṛthivyādīnāṃ teṣāṃ teṣāmarthānāṃ pradeśe, sakalaguṇatayā – samasta kalyāṇaguṇa paripūrṇatvena, pūrṇa dṛśyaḥ – aparicchinnatayā yogibhiranu- bhāvyaḥ, vṛddhihāsādibhedojjhita iti hi ! – upacayāpacayādeḥ (paricchinneṣu sato ) viśeṣasya anāśrayaḥ, ityāśayena khalu, jalādhārasūryopamoktiḥ– jaladhāragata sūrya sā dṛśya varṇanopapattiḥ (paramātmani) asparśodāhṛtiścet — jalādhārasūryadṛṣṭāntaḥ paramātmani sparśāyogyatāyāḥ udāharaṇa- miti śaṅkanaṃ tu ( na yujyate ) hi yataḥ ghaṭa.... yuktiḥ na-ghaṭa - karakādi jalādhārasthita - vāstavikākāśadṛṣṭāntasāṅgatyam na bhavati, tasmāt — uktāt hetujātāt, brahma-paraṃ brahma, dviliṅgam – ubhayaliṅgam, dvividhavibhavam - tripādvibhūti-līlā vibhūtyākhya vibhūtidvayavat, ityeva - evameva, vedāntapakṣaḥ - aupaniṣada āśayaḥ || 21 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||

ubhayaliṅgādhikaraṇe - jāgrat svapna suṣuyādisthāneṣu, tattatsthānaprayuktāḥ jīvasya ye doṣāḥ, te paramātmano'pi sambhavanti tatrā'vasthānāt - ityāśaṅkaya,

apahatapāpmā 'niravadyaṃ nirañjanam' ityādibhiḥ ubhayaliṅgatva śravaṇāt tatra

tatra sthitasyā'pi na doṣaḥ - ityuktam ||

loke - jagati kvacidapi kasminnapi vyavahāre muṇḍa: - muṇḍākhya: aśṛṅgagojātiviśeṣaḥ, khaṇḍaśabdena – khaṇḍākhyārdhaśṛṅgagojātīyavācakena śabdena naiva kathitaḥ – sarvathā naiva prayuktaḥ khalu tasmāt – uktobhayapakṣayorapi anaucityāt, jagataḥ brahmaṇaśca acetanaprapañcasya paramātmanaśca, aṃśatvam aṃśitvamapi - viśiṣṭakadeśatvarūpatvamaṃśatvam viśiṣṭatvarūpamaṃ śikaṃ cetyubhayamapi, ātmavat syāt — jīva brahmaṇoraṃśāṃ śibhāva iva nyāyyo bhavet || 15 ||

eke – dārśanikāḥ kecana, brahmaṇi- paramātmani, kaścit – kaścanaikaḥ, nityaḥ-- anādisiddhaḥ eva san, acidaṃśa :- acetanabhāgaḥ, vividhavikṛtimān – nānārūpavikāramā k bhavati ityāhu: - iti svasiddhāntaṃ varṇayanti, anye - apare kecana tattvavarṇanaparāḥ, sati- satpadābhidheye parasminbrahmaṇi, phenādinyāyataḥ sāgare phena-budbuda- taraṅgavat, vikṛti - vaśāt – svagatavikāraviśeṣataḥ, jñājña- sarvajñabhāgānū - cetanācetaneśvarākhyān trīn aṃśān āhu: - kathayanti, katicana — itare ca kecana, iha tasvavivecane ( pravṛttāḥ santaḥ), jagad- brahmaṇoḥ prapañca - paramātmanoḥ, candra- jyotsnādinītyā – indu tatprabhādyeoriva tejastvam, aikajātyam - brahmasvarūpasamānajātyanvayamāhuḥ sarve te - trividhā api te, atra asmin - viṣaye, sarvavedasvarasagatihate ḥ – akhila śrutisvārasya bhaṅgā patterhetoḥ, vitrāsanīyāḥ - ( daṇḍanena dūrīkaraṇīyāḥ) khaṇḍanena avācaḥ kāryāḥ || 23 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ ||
ahikuṇḍalādhikaraṇe – acidvastunaḥ brahmarūpatvam,

ahikuṇḍalavat avasthābhedena,

athavā prabhā - prabhāvatoriva ekajātiyogena — ityāśaṅkaya,

pakṣadvayasyā'pi anupapannatvāt, jīvavat aṃśāṃśibhāvena ityuktam ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||
tathā'hi kuṇḍalana ye proktaśuddhiviśuddhaye |
acito brahmarūpatvaṃ tenopoddhā tasaṅgatiḥ ||

iti ahikuṇḍalādhikaraṇam || 6 ||
parādhikaraṇam || 3-2-7|| śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṃgrahaḥ ||
setutvavyapadeśato mitadhiyā saṃśabdanāt prāpaka-
śrutyā cā'pi tato yaduttarataraṃ tvityeva bhedagrahāt | prāpyo'nyaḥ parato'pi kaściditi yannaitattvasambhedanād dhyeyatvādapavargasādhanatayā (se) hetutva' saṃśabdanāt ' || 16 ||

setutvavyapadeśataḥ - ' atha ya ātmā sa setuḥ' iti, jagatkāraṇabhūtasya paramātmano'sya setutvena śrutau vyavahārāt, (setutvamādāyaiva ca 'etaṃ setuṃ tīrkhā' iti taritavyasbo keśva) mitadhiyā saṃśabdanāt - 'catuṣpādbrahma' 'ṣoḍaśakalaṃ brahma' iti pāda- kalākṛtaparimitatvabuddhyā vyavahārāt prāpakaśrutyā cāpi 'amṛtasyaiṣa setu:' iti asya kāraṇasya, prāpakatvena hetunā prāpyasyānyasya sattāyā avagaterapi tato yaduttarataraṃ tvityeva bhedagrahāt - ' tato yaduttarataram ' iti prāpyasyārthasya jagatkāraṇabhūtārthāvadhikaparatvavattayā śrautavyapadeśena, parasminnarthe etadbheda- niśvayāt parato'pi anyaḥ kaścit – jagatkāraṇataḥ parabhūtārthādanyaḥ kaścanārthaḥ parātparaḥ, iti yat – astīti yadasti śaṅkanam, naitat idaṃ na sādhu, asambhedanāt sarvavastūnāṃ svarūpa svabhāvāsāryarakṣakatvāt, apavargasādhanatayā dhyeyatvāt — jagatkāraṇabhūtasya paramātmanaḥ muktiprāpakatvena hetunā upāsyatvāt, se (he ) tutvasaṃśabdanāt śrutau setutvena vyavahṛtatvāt || ( ataḥ paratrahmaṇi taraṇasādhanatvamātrasya taritavyatvasya prāpyaṃ prati kevalaprāpakatā mātrasya, prāpyārthagataparatvā va dhitāmātrasya vā nā''śaṅkā saṅgacchate || 16 ||

5) katham 1 – śrutyādau brahma-viṣṇu-rudrendrādeḥ tatra tatra varṇanīya devatāstu taye utkṛṣṭatvakathanena 'viṣṇureva paraṃ tattvam' iti kathaṃ niścayaḥ ?
6 ) tatve.... rśanāt prāpyopāsya parata svanirṇaye eva pradhānatātparyavanto ye taittirīya- śrutyādayo vedāntā:, ' nārāyaṇaparaṃ brahma tatvaṃ nārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ' ityādyāḥ, teṣāṃ samyagadhyayanato'vabodhāt (iti praśnottare )
7) tarhi anyāḥ giraḥ katham ? – viṣṇoḥ paratantvatānirṇaye sati, śivādīnāṃ - paratva-dhyeyatvādibodhakāḥ śrutyādigatā vācaḥ kena prakāreṇa susaṅgatārthā varṇayituṃ śakyante ? (iti praśna : )
8) guṇavaśāt – viṣṇugata nirupādhikasukhajanakatva - niratiśaya maṅgala karatvādiguṇamādāya viṣṇāveva śiva-śagbhvādiśabdānāṃ prayogāt ( avirodhena sāṅgatyāt )

( ityuttaram )

9 ) atra rudraḥ kathamāha ? – ukte'rthe asandigdhe sati, rudraḥ yadāha – 'aimekaḥ prathamamāsaṃ vartāmi ca bhaviṣyāmi ca' iti (svasyaiva sṛṣṭeḥ pūrva paścācca vidyamānatāyāḥ pratipādanāt ) tat kathamabādhitaṃ bhavati ?
10 ) taddṛṣṭayā svāntarātmani viṣṇau, svasmiṃśca tanniyāmye abhedasyānusandhānena (iti praśnottare )
11) kathamudbhavati ? – jagatkāraṇabhūto viṣṇuḥ 'brahma-viṣṇu-rudrendrāste sarve samprasūyante, śrutipratipanna (samprasūti) jananāśrayaḥ kathaṃ bhavitumarha : ? (sarva kāraṇasyotpattya - yogāt ) ( iti praśna : )
12) avatarati — viṣṇuḥ āvirbhāvākhyāvatāravāne ; na tu karmakṛtajananavān

ya ukto yājñikyāmupaniṣadi niḥsīmamahimā
sa uktaḥ puṃmukte paramapuruṣaḥ kāraṇatayā | punaḥ saucālikyāmupaniṣadi māhopaniṣadī
gatiścaivaṃ tadvatkalaya kaṭhavalīprabhṛtayaḥ || 72 ||

yājñikyām upaniṣadi — taittarīyānārāyaṇopaniṣadi ambhasītyādikāyām, niḥsīma- mahimā - niravadhikamāhātmyaśālitayā, ya uktaḥ – 'yamantaḥ samudre kavayo vayanti yadakṣare parame prajāḥ' ityādivāgbhiḥ yaḥ puruṣaḥ pratipāditaḥ, saḥ paramapuruṣaḥ saḥ sarvotkṛṣṭaḥ puruṣa eva, puṃmūkte - puruṣasūktākhye sarvavedānāṃ sārabhūte śrutibhāge, kāraṇatayā uktaḥ— 'chandāsi jajñire tasmāt ' ityādinā ' tathā lokā5 akalpayan ' ityantena sarvajagatsraṣṭṛtvena pratipāditaḥ (tasyaiva 'vedāhametaṃ puruṣaṃ mahāntam ' iti pratipādanāt ) punaḥ atra pratipāditatve- nokta evārthaḥ saubāliśyāmupaniṣadi – sucālanā maghaṭitāyām upaniṣadyapi, yaḥ pṛthivī - mantare sañcaran yasya pṛthivī śarīram.... divyo deva eko nārāyaṇa:' iti, tathā tataḥ pūrvameva, ' madhye puruṣo divyaḥ sahasraśīrṣā puruṣaḥ... hṛdayāt sarvamidaṃ jāyate ' iti sarvākāratayā uktaḥ, māhopaniṣadī gatiśca -- mahopaniṣadaḥ sambandhinī kāraṇabhūtārthavarṇanarītirapi evam - nārāyaṇākhyaparamapuruṣasyaiva sarvakāraṇatvaparāḥ dṛśyante 'eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt' ityādinā, tadvat — uktayājñikyādyupaniṣada ivaiva, kaṭhavallīprabhṛtayaḥ – kaṭhopaniṣadgatāḥ vallayo'pi tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam 'eko vaśī sarvabhūtāntarātmā ekaṃ bījaṃ bahudhā yaḥ karoti' ityādyā: giraḥ; tathā upaniṣadantarasthāḥ anyānyā vāco'pi sarvāḥ mahāpuruṣasyaiva apāra mahimno viṣṇoḥ kāraṇatvaparā iti, kalayaniścinu || (ataḥ sarvā api vaidikyo vācaḥ viṣṇoreva sarvotkṛṣṭatvasahitakā ra gatvaparā:; nā'tra vipratipattyanyathā pratipattyāderavakāśa iti ) || 72 || vyājahe jagadekakāraṇatayā sākūtameko ha vā ityārambhavatā mahopaniṣaduddhoṣeṇa nārāyaṇaḥ |

brahmeśa vasundharoḍusalilasvā heśa somāṃśumat-
pūrvāśeṣa' jagatpraṇāśavati' yaḥ kāle samunmīlati || 73 ||

nārāyaṇaḥ - bhagavān mahāviṣṇuḥ, sākūtam — abhiprāyaviśeṣasahitaṃ yathā tathā, 'eko ha vā' ityārambhavatā – 'eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt' ityevamārambhayuktena,

mahopaniṣadudghoṣeṇa - mahopaniṣadaḥ asandigdhaspaṣṭa śabdasandarbheṇa, jagadekakāraṇatayā sarvasyā'pi jagataḥ nimittānatiriktopādānākhyā dvitīyakāraṇatāśrayatvena, vyājahe - vyapadiṣṭo vartate | (tathā) yaḥ – jagadekakāraṇabhūto yo nārāyaṇa eva brahmeśadhupraṇāśavati — vidhi-śiva-yo- pṛthivī-nakṣatra - jala- vaiśvānara (agni) vidhu (candra) - prabhṛti samasta viśvāśeṣapraṇāśā- -spade kāle, samunmīlati — avikalaḥ san jāgarti ( sa eva jagajjanma -sthiti-layānāmekaikaḥ - kāraṇabhūtaḥ sarvasmātparaḥ paramapuruṣaḥ) || 73 ||

aprāptatvānmahatyā mupaniṣadi jagatkāraṇa 'syānuvādo'
na syādeko ha vā ityayamupari hi devebhya ityādinītyā | vākyamvārasyato hi vyagaṇi bhagavataḥ kāraṇatvaṃ pareṣāṃ

kāryatvaṃ cā'viruddhaṃ tadapi na paramastyatra kiṃcidvidheyam || 74 ||
mahatyām upaniṣadi mahopaniṣadi vidyamānaḥ, eko ha vā ityayam -' eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt ityayaṃ śrutibhāgaḥ, anuvādo na syāt — vihitārthajñāpanapara vākyarūpo na bhavet ; (kintu aśā tajñāpanaparo vidhireva ) jagatkāraṇasya aprāptatvāt – nārāyaṇākhyasya jagatkāraṇabhūtasyārthasya pramāṇāntareṇa siddhatvābhāvāt, ( ha vai ityavyayayoḥ anuvādatvajñāpakayoḥ satvāt anuvādatvaṃ nyāyyamiti tu ne citam ; ) upari hi devebhya ityādinītyā- ' upari hi devebhyo dhārayati' ityasmin vākye ' hi' śabdasya sanve'pi, upari dhāraṇasya mānāntarā- siddhatvena aprāptārthabodhakatvakṛtaṃ vidhitvaṃ, tasya vākyasya yathā nirṇītam 'vidhintu dhāraṇe'pūrvatvāt ' ityatra tadvat (evam) 'yadāgneyo'ṣṭākapālo bhavati' ityasmin vākye anuvādaliṅgasya yacchabdasya satve'pi, āgneyayāgasya pramāṇāntarato'siddhatvāt anuvādatvaṃ parityajya vidhitvameva yathā nirṇītam ' vacanāni tvapūrvatvāt ' ityatra tadbacca ; ( athavā ), hi-yata:, bhagavataḥ – jñānā diṣaḍguṇaiśvarya pūrṇasya nārāyaṇasya, kāraṇatvam- sarvajagattraṣṭṛtvam, pareṣām – brahmarudrendrādīnām, kāryatvaṃ ca - bhagavatā sṛjyatvamapi vākya- svārasyataḥ akliṣṭānvayo yathā bhavati tathā 'eko ha vai' ityādisampūrṇa vākyasvarūpa- cintanataḥ, vyagaṇi– vicārya niścitam ataḥ, (nirṇītamidaṃ sarvamapi ) aviruddham - apratihatam, tadapi ' ha vai' ityanayoḥ vidhitva mañjakatvābhyupagame'pi siddhārthānuvādakatvābhyupa- game'pi vā bhagavataḥ param - bhagavato nārayaṇasyāpekṣayā paratvāśrayabhūtaṃ kiñcit taccāntaram,

1 tvānuvādo - ku. pā. SB IV-61

vidheyam – sṛṣṭi-pralayayorvidyamānatvena vidhātumaīm, na atrāsti asmin mahopaniṣadvākya- sandarbhe naiva vidyate || ('sadeva somyedamagra āsīdekamevādvitīyam' iti śrutyantara siddhānu- vāditve'pi '6 vai' śabdajñāpyasyānuvādatvasya cāritārthyāt, 'na brahmā neśāna ' ityādi- spaṣṭaniṣedhasamabhivyāhāreṇa nārāyaṇakāratvasya virodhaśaṅkā gandhānākrāntatvācca ) || 74 || yat saṃjñāmūrtikartuḥ paramapuruṣataḥ kiṃcidākāśanāmno
hṛtpadme syādupāsyaṃ śrutiśirasi 'paraṃ ' sāmagānāmagā yi | tatra praśnottarābhyāmaghaviddatimukhān satyasaṅkalpatāntā-
naṣṭau śiṣṭastadīyānanidamudayinastadguṇānudgṛṇanti || 75 ||

sāmagānā - chandoganāmnā sāmaśāsvādhyāyināma, śrutiśirasi – madhyetavyaveda- śākhāyā antabhāge, (upaniṣadi ) saṃjñā - mūrtikartuḥ sarvasṛjyārthasambandhinornāma-rūpayornirvoduḥ, ākāśanāmnaḥ– ākāśapadena tatraiva vyavahṛtāt paramapuruṣataḥ – puruṣottamāt param-

anyat kiścit – tatra anirdiṣṭa viśiṣṭasvarūpam, hṛtpadme upāsya syāt – hṛdayapuṇḍarīkā- bacchedena upāsanīyaṃ bhavet (iti) yat agāyi - yadvastu vihitaṃ ( ' tasmin yadantastadanveṣṭavyam " iti śrutikhaṇḍena), tat — upāsyabhūtaṃ tadvastu, aghavihatimukhān satyasaṅkalpatāntān- apahatapāpmatvādyān satyasaṅkalpatva paryantānnū, tadīyān - ākāśapadavācyārtha sambandhinaḥ, anidamudayinaḥ— mokṣakāle idaṃ prathamāvirbhāvāī jī vīyaguṇavilakṣaṇān

svābhāvikān,

śiṣṭān — tatraivopadiṣṭān, aṣṭau guṇān apahatapāpmatva - vijaratva - vimṛtyutva - viśokasva- vijighatsatva-apipāsatva- satyakāmatva - satyasaṅkalpatvarūpān aṣṭasaṅkhyān guṇān, tatra praśnottarābhyām – chāndogyīya dahara vidyāprakaraṇasyābhyām tadviṣayakābhyām anuyoga - paryanuyogā- bhyām 'kiṃ tadatra vidyate ? asmin kāmāḥ samāhitāḥ' ityevamākārābhyām (tātparyani- śvāyakābhyām ) udgṛṇanti - niścitya kathayanti tadarthacintanapaṭavaḥ (ākāśapadārthāntarvarti upāsyaṃ tadīyaṃ guṇāṣṭakameva nā'nyat iti ) || 75 ||
