prastutyā mṛtahetumādipuruṣaṃ nyakkṛtya mārgāntaraṃ
tasyaitaddhanāya niṣpratibhaṭāmuṣya sārveśvarīm |
uktistatra tato yaduttarataraṃ tanmuktidāyīti yā

ādipuruṣam - jagatkāraṇabhūtaṃ puruṣottamaṃ nārāyaṇam amṛtahetuṃ prastutya - mṛtyumeti' iti) mārgāntaram — mṛtyutaraṇe upāyamanyam, nyakkṛtya — tiraskṛtya ( avidyamāna- tvenopadiśya), ('nānyaḥ panthā:' iti ) etadghaṭanāya - ādipuruṣasya mṛtyvatyayasādhanatāyāḥ, anyatra tadabhāvasya coktārthasya dṛḍhīkaraṇāya, tasya — prastutasyā''dipuruṣasya, niṣpratibhaṭā sārveśvarīm — apratihataṃ sarveśvagbhāvam uddhuṣya – 'yasmātparaṃ nāparam... puruṣeṇa sarvam '

iti śrutyā ghaṇṭāghoṣeṇa sphuṭīkṛtya tatra tasmin sandarbhe tato yaduttarataraṃ tat muktidāyi iti yā uktiḥ - 'tato yaduttarataraṃ tadarūpamanāmayam | ya etadviduramṛtāste bhavanti' iti yadvacanaṃ dṛśyate tat — uttarottarasya vastunaḥ nirvikāratva- nīrūpatva- muktyupāyabhūtajñānaviṣaya- svānāṃ pratipādakaṃ tadvākyam, śvetāśvatarāgame proktasya – samagraśvetāśvataraśrutau varṇitasya viśiṣṭārthasya hetūktimat- ( tataḥ tasmāddhetoḥ iti ) hetu kathanayuktam, nigamanam - upasaṃharaṇameva; na tu nūtanatayā parātparabhūtasyārthasya idamprathamatayā varṇanam || ( ataḥ jagatkāraṇaṃ brahmaiva parātparam ; na tu tato'nyat kiṃcit tatvāntaram ) || 76 ||

| '

yacchavetāśvatarāgamo bhagavati spaṣṭaprayogāntaraṃ

puruṣaḥ iti – puruṣottamaḥ nirupādhikapuruṣatvavān veti punaḥ vyaktaḥ - 'mahān prabhurvai puruṣa: satvasyaiva pravartakaḥ' iti śvetāśvataraśrutyaiva punarapi spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ | ( ato'yaṃ śivaśabdaḥ, nā kevalarūḍhaḥ ; nirupādhikamaṅgalapradatvena nimittena nārāyaṇaparatvāditi ) || 77 ||

yacchatretāśvataraśrutiḥ śivapadaṃ vakti prayukta harā-
bagre jāgrati viśvakāraṇaparaṃ tatraiva yuktaṃ hi ! tat | sādhāraṇya vidūraviṣṇubhagavannārāyaṇādyā iyai-
rāhuḥ kāraṇame tamopaniṣadā bhāgāstadā kāṅkṣiṇaḥ || 78 || bhāgāstadākāṅkṣiṇaḥ ||78
śvetāśvataraśrutiḥ– śvetāśvataropaniṣat, harau prayuktam - viṣṇo bhagava
'śāśvataṃ śivamacyutam ' ' sarvaḥ śarvaḥ śivaḥ sthāṇuḥ

rāla

ityādau prayoge upayuktam, yat

śivapadam - yādṛśaṃ śivetīmaṃ śabdam, vakti' yadā tamastanna divā....na sanna cāsa cchiva eva kevala:' iti vākye prayuṅkte tat - śivapadam hi yataḥ, viśvaka raṇaparam- jagatkāraṇabhūtatatvapratipādanatātparyavat ato hetoḥ, agre jāgrati sṛṣṭipūrvakāle pralayadaśāyāmapi saṃhṛtatvaṃ vihāya apracyutasvarūpa- svabhāvatayā'vasthite, tatraiva – paramakāraṇe harāveva, yuktam- prayoktuṃ nyāyyam, aupaniṣadāḥ bhāgāḥ – upaniṣatsambandhinaḥ pradeśaviśeṣāḥ, tadākāṅkṣiṇaḥ kāraṇatvaupayikadharmaviśeṣaviśiṣṭaveṣeṇa bodhanodyuktāḥ santaḥ, kāraṇam enam – jagatkāraṇa niścitamimaṃ puruṣam, sādhāraṇya....dyā hvayaiḥ - bhagavadasādhāraṇa. 'viṣṇuḥ bhagavān nārāyaṇaḥ puruṣa : ' ityevamādibhirnāmadheyaiḥ, āhuḥ tatra tatra bodhayanti || ( śivādayaḥ sāmānyaśabdāḥ ; sopādhikanirupādhikobhayavidhaśivatvavatyarthe pravṛtteḥ | nārāyaṇādayastu asādhāraṇāḥ| dvividhānā- mapi śabdānāṃ teṣu teṣvartheṣu prayogo na hi bādhā īḥ || 78 ||

yadagre jāgarti śrutaśivapadaṃ brahma paramaṃ
jagaddhetuḥ śvetāśvataranigamānte sa bhagavān |
prayukto yat tasmin yajuṣi śivaśabdaḥ śrutiśira-
svatatsādhāraṇyaṃ na samagaṇi nārāyaṇagira: || 79 ||

śrutaśivapadam - śrutiga- śivapadābhidheyatvena niścitam yat paramaṃ brahmāśaṃ parabrahmabhūtaṃ tatvam, śvetāśvataranigamānte śvaitāśvatarākhye upaniṣadi agre jāgarti' kiṃ kāraṇaṃ brahma .... paramaṃ tu brahma' iti uttaratra spaṣṭapratipādanagocaro vartate, saḥ – śivapadābhidhāna- gocaraḥ brahmarūpo'rthaḥ, jagaddhetuḥ bhagavān – 'eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt' ityādau jagataḥ

prākkālavidyamānatayā śrutipratipanno nārāyaṇa eva, nānya: ; tasmin — jagatkāraṇabhūte nārāyaṇe viṣaye, śivaśabdaḥ – śivetyayaṃ śabdaḥ, yajuṣi śrutiśirasi - yajurvedāntaḥpātini taittirīya- nārāyaṇopaniṣadi yat prayuktaḥ – yasmāddhetoḥ pratipādakatayā upayuktaḥ, 'śāśvataṃ śivamacyutam ' ityādau ; tasmāt nārāyaṇa giraḥ - nārāyaṇetiśabdasya, atatsādhāraṇam - nārāyaṇa-taditaro- bhayapratipādanasamarthaśabdatvam, na samagaṇi pramāṇaiḥ prāmāṇikaiśca na aṅgīkṛtamabhavat || ( śivapadaṃ maṅgalapradatvaṃ nimittīkṛtya nārāyaṇe taditarasmiṃśca rudrādau tadguṇaleśayogavati, prayoktumarham | nārāyaṇapadaṃ tu na kenā'pi prakāreṇa anyatra prayogāīm; śabdavṛtteranitara- sādhāraṇyāt iti ) || 79 ||

yad devairanuyukta uttaramuśan rudro nivṛtyātmani
vyāja mahatīmadhīśvaradhurāmātharvaṇe mūrdhani |
sa prādurbhavadantarātmabhagavadbhūmāSpha (bha) ṇat sonsarā-
dantaḥ prāviśadityadhīna vidhayā tadvāmadevādivat || 80 ||

ātharvaṇe mūrdhani –atharvaśira ākhye upaniṣadbhāge, devaiḥ suraiḥ, anuyuktaḥ – 'ko bhavān' iti pṛṣṭaḥ, rudraḥ – rudrākhyaḥ saṃhārādhipo devaḥ, uttaramuśan – teṣāmuttaraṃ dātukāmaḥ ( uttaradāne pravṛttaḥ san ) ātmani – svasvarūpe, mahatīm - asaṅkucitam adhīśvara dhurām- sarvaiśvarya kāṣṭhām vivṛtya - ' ahamekaḥ prathamamāsam ityārabhya 'dharmeṇa dharma tarpayāmi svena tejasā' ityetadantaṃ vistaraśa uktvā, vyājahe yat - pratyuttaraṃ dattavān iti yadasti, tat - vicārya cintyamānaṃ uttaradānaṃ tata, ( evaṃ niścāyayati) saḥ uttaradānapravṛtto rudraḥ, prādurbhava.... bhūmā - sākṣātkriyamāṇatā pannasvāntaryāmi bhūtabhagavanmahimavān, sontarā.... viśadityadhītavidhayā – tatraivātharvaśirasi 'sontarādantaraṃ prāviśat ' – rudrākhyo jīvaḥ dehādyapekṣayā sūkṣmāt jīvādapi sūkṣmaṃ paramātmānaṃ prāviśat ' - svasya tasminsvāntarātmatvadhī- prāptirūpāṃ sūkṣmāṃ sthitimavāpa iti śrutipaṭhitaprakāreṇa vāmadevādivat abhaṇat - svasmin bhagavadapṛthasthitiṃ dṛṣṭavanto vāmadevādayo yathā svātmānaṃ paramātmābhedena vyājahuḥ, tathā (adhośvarabhūtabhagavadabhedānusandhānena ) svātmasvarūpamuktavān iti || 80 ||

anuyuktaḥ–‘ko

prāgvat paścādupravākyānuvṛtteḥ so'haṃ nityo'nitya ityādibhaṅgyā || 81 || nā'nyaḥ kaścinmattaḥ iti - atharvaśiraḥ sthāt 'nānya: kaścinmatto vyatiriktaḥ ' ityevamādyākārāta, ugravākyāt – ugrapadābhidheyarudroktādvākyāt, ūrdhvam — paścācchrayamāṇaḥ

itiḥ - itiśabdaḥ (' nānyaḥ kaścinmatto vyatirikta iti' iti vākyānupūrvī) tatsamāptim - rudravākyasyāvasānam, na bravīti na pratipādayati, prāśvat - etadvākyāt pūrva ' ahameka ' iti rudravākyasya satvamiva, paścāt - itiśabdāvasitaitadvā kyānantaramapi, ugravākyānuvṛtteḥ- ( itiśabdasya ) anuvartamāna rudravākyakatvāt ( tajjñānasya tadvākyāvasānabuddhi virodhitvāt ) 'so'haṃ nityo'nitya' ityādibhaṅgayā - ( uttaratra rudravākyānuvṛttiḥ kathaṃ niścīyate ! iti cet ! ittham ) ' so'haṃ nityānityo'haṃ vyaktāvyakto'ham' ityevamātmikayā 'ahaṃ 'śabda- ghaṭitayā vākyaśailyā | ( ' ahamekaḥ prathamam ' iti ' ahaṃ 'śabdaghaṭitaṃ vākyaṃ yena prayuktam ; tenaiva khalvidamapyahaṃ śabdaghaṭitaṃ vākyaṃ prayuktaṃ bhavati nānyeneti ) | ( etanmadhyagataṃ ' so'ntarā- dantaraṃ prāviśat' iti vākyaṃ śrautaṃ sat prakṛtatattvārthāvedakam ; na rudravākyāntargatam | saḥ - paramātmā nārāyaṇaḥ, antarāt — dehāntarvartinaḥ prāṇāderapi, antaram — antarvartinaṃ jīvam, prāviśat anupraviṣṭavān | ataḥ paramātmanaḥ rudrākhye jītre antaḥ praviṣṭatvāt,

tadabheda- pratisandhānapravṛttā, 'ahamekaḥ' ityādirūpā rudrasyoktiḥ na tu svasmin paramātmabhrameṇa ' iti śratyuktirmadhye pravṛttā | anuvartate ca punā rudravākyamiti ) || 81 ||

soḍato hetuṃ vakti netiḥ prakāraṃ prahlādo'kto heturantaḥ praveśaḥ |
prahlādoktiḥ sarvagatvādanantasyeti vyaktā vaiṣṇavākhye pugaṇe || 82 ||

ataḥ ' nā'nyaḥ kaścinmatto vyatirikta iti śrūyamāṇasyetiśabdasya samāsyartha katvā- yogāt sa itiḥ - tadvākyastho'yam 'iti' śabdaḥ, hetuṃ vakti - ( ' sontarādantaraṃ prāviśat diśaśvāntaraṃ prāviśat' iti vākyānantaraṃ niviṣṭaḥ san ) paramātmanaḥ antarbahiḥ sarvatra niviṣṭatvāddhetoḥ' ityevamāśayaṃ brūte, na prakāram - samāptiṃ yathā na bravīti, tathā 'na''nyaḥ kaścinmato vyatirikta ityevamatravīt' iti ), prakāramapyartha na bravīti, antaḥ praveśaḥ – paramātmanaḥ sarvāntaranupraveśaḥ, hetuḥ svasmin paramātmābhedapratītivyavahārayoḥ prayojakaḥ iti prahlādoktaḥ prahādena vyaktīkṛta eva, prahlādoktiḥ - uktā bhiprāyā prahlādasya sūktiśca sarvagatvādanantasyeti – 'sarvagatvādanantasya sa evā'hamavasthitaḥ' ityevamākārā, vaiṣṇavākhye purāṇe - aṣṭādaśapurāṇeṣu vaiṣṇavaśvena prasiddhe purāṇaviśeṣe, vyaktā - sphuṭā paridṛśyate | ('sontarā 'diti śrutiśca na rudravākyarūpā ; kintu śrutivākyarūpā | tadarthaśca pūrva śloke uktaḥ) || 82 ||

athaveti padena heturatra vyatireko'kathi nā'nya ityavītaḥ |
ahameka itīritānvayasya pratipattuṃ sukaraḥ prakārapakṣaḥ || 83 ||

athavā -' iti 'śabdasya śrutisthasya sthānāntara niveśaṃ parityajya svasthāne eka niveśe'pi, atra nā'nya ityadhītaḥ - atharvaśirasi ' matto vyatiriktaḥ anyaḥ kaścana na iti śrutipratipannaḥ, vyatireka:- ahameka iti vākyagatāhampadārthātirikta (bhagavatpṛthaksiddha) svaniṣṭhavastvantarasyābhāvaḥ, ahameka itīritānvayasya - ' aham eka eva sṛṣṭeḥ pūrvakāle sṛṣṭikāle pralayakāle ca kālatraye'pi svaniṣṭhavasturūpaḥ varte ' ityevamuktasya bhāvasambandhasya, (bhāvasambandhaṃ prati ) hetuḥ akathi - śrutigatāditi śabdātprayojakatvena pratipāditaḥ, (yataḥ kālatrayepya pracyutasvarūpa svabhāvaḥ san advitīyo'hamevā'vatiṣṭhe ; bhata eva mattonyaḥ svaniṣṭho'rthaḥ kaścidapi nāstyeva iti) prakārapakṣa :- itiśabdasya

prakārārthakatva -
( iti - evaṃ

pakṣo'pi, pratipattum — abhyupagantum, rītyetyarthaḥ) || 83 ||

abhyupagantum, sukaraḥ – sulabho bhavati |

(' yataḥ vayabhāvavati niyamena dhūmasyābhāvaḥ ; ataḥ dhūmavati niyamena vahnirastyeva iti dhūmavayoḥ anvayavyāptisiddhau, bahyabhāva - dhūmābhāvayoḥ vyatirekabhūtayoḥ avyabhicāro yathā sādhakaḥ ; tathā kālatraye'pi svaniṣṭhasya paramātmetarasyābhāvāt, sarvadeśa-sarvakāla sarvāvasthāsu paramātmana ekasyaivāvasthānaṃ saṅgataṃ bhavatīti) ||

rudrasyoktiḥ 'so'ntarā' dityato'sau
tasmādanyaḥ ko'pi tasyāntarātmā |
anyeṣāṃ cetyucyate sontarādi-
tyuktayā raudrayā so'pi viṣṇuḥ prasiddhaḥ || 84 ||

ataḥ—' iti 'śabdasya prakārārthakatvābhyupagamāt, 'so'ntarāt' ityasau uktiḥ - 'saṃntarādantaraṃ prāviśat diśaścāntaraṃ prāviśat' itīyaṃ śrutigatā uktiḥ, rudrasya — śivasyaiva ; na śruteḥ, tasmāt – ' so'ntarā' dityasya rudravākyatvāt, raudrathā sontarādi- tyuktyā – rudra vaktṛkayā sontarādityanayā bācā, tasya anyeṣāṃ ca – rudrasya taditareṣā ca jīvātmanām anyaḥ ko'pi rudrādatiriktaḥ kaścana anirdiṣṭanāmadheyaḥ devatāviśeṣaḥ, antarātmetyucyate - antaḥ praviśya ghārako niyantā ceti pratipādyate, so'pi antarātmabhūto devatāviśeṣo'pi viṣṇuḥ prasiddhaḥ – ' eṣa sarvabhūtāntarātmā'pahatapāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇaḥ ' ityevamādiśrutyā, ' tavāntarātmā mama ca ' ityādismṛtyā ca viṣṇureveti jñāto bhavatīti || 84 |

ityuktirvā samāpti vadatu haragiraḥ so'ntarādityato'sau mābhūdudroktiritthaṃ sati na vighaṭanā kiñcidasmanmatasya | rudraḥ svātmātmabhūtaṃ harimamalaghiyā saṃpraviścikīrṣu-
dhāvī svavaśyaṃ naramaparamiti vyajyate vedavācā || 85 ||

ityuktiḥ - nānyaḥ kaścinmatto vyatirikta iti' ityetadvākyasthaḥ ' iti ' śabdaḥ, hara giraḥ - ' ahameka:' iti prakrāntāyāḥ śivokteḥ samāptiṃ vā vadatu - avasānameva pratipādayatu] nāma, ataḥ -' iti ' śabdasya rudroktisamāptibodhakatvāt drokteḥ paścādananuvṛtteḥ, asau sontarāditi - ' so'ntarādantaram ' itīyaṃ vāk, rudroktiḥ mābhūt – śivavākyarūpā naivāstu, itthaṃ sati evamabhyupagame'pi, asmanmatasya—nārāyaṇasya sarvāntarātmatvābhyupagantṛ- vaiṣṇavamatasya kiṃcit — alpālpamapi, kiṃcit — alpālpamapi na vighaṭanā - śaithilya saṃbhāvanā'pi naiva bhavati, rudraḥ - paramaśiva, svātmātmabhūtam - svātmanaḥ svasvarūpasyāpi niyantṛtayā dhārakaṃ paramaśeṣiṇaṃ ca, harim - bhagavantaṃ nārāyaṇam, apaladhiyā – nirmalayā manīṣayā, svavaśyaṃ cikīrṣuḥ- svasmin prasannāntaḥkaraṇaṃ kartukāmaḥ san : san sampraviṣṭa:- jagrāha śuddhabuddhyā bhagavatsvarūpaṃ gṛhītavān prapadanātmakayā śemuṣyā bhagavati svasya sarvārpaṇaṃ kṛtavāniti vā (tasya ca vaśīkaraṇaṃ taccharaṇāgatireva ); (kathaṃ sampraviṣṭaḥ ? iti jijñāsāyām ) aparaṃ naram - anyaṃ janam, svavaśyaṃ cikīrṣuḥ svānukūlācaraṇaśīlaṃ kartukāmaḥ, medhāvīva – upāyakuśalaḥ puruṣaviśeṣa iva, ( puruṣāntaraṃ svānukūlapravṛttimantamāpādayituṃ pravṛttaḥ, yathā tasmin puruṣāntare sampraviṣṭaḥ san tadiṅgitādiparijñānaikapravaṇo bhavati ; tena ca tasmin puruṣe (yaṃ sampraviṣṭaḥ iti vyavahriyate ca; tadvat paramaḥ śivo'pi paramātmani sampraviṣṭo'bhavadityartha so'ntarā diti śrutirvadati ||) iti vedavācā vyajyate – ayamarthaḥ atharvaśirogirā dyotyate || 85 ||

śrutvā rudravacastadīyama himastotrāya labdhodyamāḥ

sarvaiśvaryamatharvamūrdhni yadapi vyāja hire'syā'marāḥ |
te'ntaryāmisamādhihetava iha vyaktā hi ! rudroktaya-
stadyuktāstadanuktayastu diviṣadvā kā stadekāśayāḥ || 86 ||
atharvamūrdhni – atharvaśironāmnyāmupaniṣadi amarāḥ surāḥ, rudravacaḥ śrutvā -

'aha mekaḥ prathamasāsam' ityādikaṃ śiveritaṃ vākyaṃ niśamya tadīyamahimastotrāya — rudragatasya bhagavadantaryāmikatvā parokṣyakṛtasya vaibhavasya muktakaṇṭhaṃ kathanāya,

asva bhagavadantaryāmikatvasiddhimato rudrasya sarvaiśvaryamapi — sarvaniyantṛtvarūpaṃ sarvāpekṣayā atiśayitaṃ prabhutvamapi vyāja hire yat – 'yo vai rudraḥ sa bhagavān' ityādinā spaṣṭaṃ vyāhṛtavantaḥ iti yadasti, te amaravyāhārāḥ, antaryāmisamādhihetavaḥ rudrākhye -svarūpe tadantaryāmibhūnabhagavadbhāvanānairantaryakṛtā eva (sarvaiśvaryam tadantaryāminiṣṭhameva, rudre tu bheda- bhāvanāvaśāt cintitam antaryāmisamādheḥ tādṛśasvābhāvyāt ) hi yataḥ, iha - etadupaniṣadgate asmin prakaraṇe, tadyuktāḥ - antaryāmisamādhisampannāḥ, rudroktayaḥ – 'ahamekaḥ' ityādyāḥ śivavācaḥ, vyaktāḥ spaṣṭā eva vidyante, tadekāśayāḥ tacātparya prādhānyavantaḥ diviṣa- dvākāḥ devānāṃ vyāhārāḥ api tadanūktayastu - rudrotyanumāyuktirūpā eva || antaryāmibhāvanānairantaryeṇa pravṛttāḥ devānāṃ stotrādivācaḥ, itareṣāmapi antaryāmibhāvanā nairantarṣe tavaśca bhavantīti jñeyam ) | 86 ||

santaḥ,

(ataḥ

oṅkārapūrvaka tathā pratipāditāni nāmāntarāṇi nanu rudrapura : sarāṇi |

ātharvaṇe śirasi tāni tu na prasiddhadeśitṛtvamupapādayituṃ kṣamante || 87 || ātharvaṇe śirasi - atharvaśira ākhyāyāmupaniṣadi, rudrapuraḥsarāṇi nāmāntarāṇi- rudreśānaprabhṛtīnyanyāni abhidhānāni, oṅkārapūrvakatayā pratipāditāni nanu – praṇava pratipādana - pūrvakaṃ kathitāni khalu tāni tu tāni nāmāni param, prasiddharudreśitṛtvam - trimūrti - madhyasthasya kevalasaṃhārakārakatayā śrutyādivikhyātasya rudrasya sarveśvarabhāvam, upapādayitum— yuktaṃ bodhayitum, na kṣamantena samarthāni bhavanti || 87 ||

samākhyāḥ prakrāntapraṇavaviṣayiṇyaḥ pratipadaṃ
niruktā rudrādyā yadapi hi mahādevaśirasaḥ |
śruterātharvaṇyāḥ śirasi tadidaṃ tāravibhavaṃ
vyaktayoṅkāraḥ syāduparitana vidyāparikaraḥ || 88 ||
ātharvaṇyāḥ śruteḥ śirasi - atharvaśira upaniṣadi (pañcame khaṇḍe), prakrāntapraṇava- viṣayiṇyaḥ – bhagavatpratipādakatayā pūrvakhaṇḍaprastutaṅkārasambandhinyaḥ, rudrādyāḥ mahādevaśirasaḥ - rudraśabdaprabhṛti mahādevaśabdaparyantāḥ samākhyāḥ – nāmadheyarūpā vācaḥ, pratipadaṃ niruktāḥ yadapi ekaikasminnapi pade avayavavyutpattipradarśanena kṛtanirvacanāḥ ityetadapi yadasti, tadidam — tadetannirvacanapradarśana śrutigatam, tāravibhavaṃ vyanakti – oṅkārasya māhātmyaṃ spaṣṭīkaroti (vācakaśabdavaibhavasya tadvācya bhūtārthagatavaibhava paryavasāyitvāt ), oṅkāraḥ - vaibhavavattamā

prakaṭīkriyamāṇaḥ praṇavaḥ, uparitana vidyā parikaraḥ syāt ṣaṣṭhakhaṇḍa pratipādyāyāḥ rudrākhya- devatā kañcukita bhagavadupāsanarūpāyā: vidyāyāḥ aṅgabhūtaḥ san āvaśyako bhavedeva || 88 || ' panthā bandhasya śānteḥ ' paśupatibhajanaṃ nanvihātharva' mūrdhni
proktaḥ prācyā taduktyā sphurati haririhopāsanīya stadātmā | yadvanmadhvādividyā svitarasuratanuḥ sraṣṭṛ - sṛjyādibhāvaḥ
śrautastvatrā'pi tulyaḥ śrutamiha bhasitaṃ prastutopāstiśeṣam || 89 ||

iha atharvamūrdhni – asyām atharvaśirabhākhyāyāmupaniṣadi paśupatibhajanam - paśu- patyākhyasya rudrasyopāsanam, bandhasya śānteḥ - paśusaṃjñānāṃ jīvānāṃ pāśākhyasya bandhasyāpagamaṃ prati, panthāḥ proktaḥ- upāyatvena vihitaḥ khalu ! ( evaṃ sthite rudropāsanaparāyā upaniṣadaḥ, kathaṃ vā bhagavadupāsanaparatvasādhanapravṛttiḥ śobhate yuṣmākam ? - iti cet ! ) prācyā taducyā- pūrvakhaṇḍasthayā 'so'ntarādantaraṃ prāviśat' ityādirūpayā śivasya vācā, ' tadetadudracaritam' ityādikayā upaniṣaduktyā vā tadātmā hariḥ – rudrāntaryāmī bhagavān nārāyaṇaḥ, iha upāsanīyaḥ - atharvaśirasi upāsyatayā vihitaḥ, ( ityuttaram ) ( rudraśarīrakasya paramātmanaḥ kimarthamupāsyatvam? iti cet !) madhvādividyāsu madhuvidyā- indraprāṇa vidyādiṣu itarasuratanuḥ yadvat — vasvādityādiśarīrakaḥ paramātmā, indraśarīrakaḥ paramātmā ca yathā upāsanīyaḥ ; tathaiva atra rudraśarīrakaḥ paramātmā upāsyaḥ, (ityuttaram ) śrautaḥ - śrutyuktaḥ, sraṣṭṛ-sṛjyādibhāvaḥ - kāraṇatā kāryatā ca, tulyaḥ – vasvindrādyoḥ bhagavataḥ, rudrasya bhagavato'pi ca samānaḥ, ('lalāṭāt krodhajo rudro jāyata' iti śruteḥ) iha śrutam - asmin prakaraṇe ( dhāryamāṇatayā ) śrutivihitam, bhasitam – bhasma, prastutopāstiśeṣam - prakrāntarudraśarīraka paramātmopāsanā ṅgabhūtam ; na tu pāśupatāgamasiddhabhasmadhāraṇametat || 89 ||
prastutya rudravapuṣaḥ puruṣottamasya vidyāmatharvaśirasīritamaṅgamasyāḥ |
bhasmaprasaktiritaratra na cāsti yadvat sautrāmaṇiprakaraṇoktasurāgrahasya || 90 ||
rudravapuṣaḥ - saṃhāra karmādhikṛta rudrākhya devatā viśeṣaśarīrakasya, puruṣottamasya – bhagavato mahāviṣṇoḥ, vidyām – upāsanām, atharvaśirasi prastutya - atharvaśiropaniṣadi vidhātuṃ prakramya, asyāḥ aṅgam - prastutāyāḥ rudraśarīrakabhagavadupāsanākhyāyāḥ vidyāyā upakārakatayā bhasma — (upāsakaspa) bhasmasnāna - bhasmadhāraṇādikaṃ karma, īritam – 'abhiriti bhasma' 'bhasma

T

gṛhītvā vimṛjyāṅgāni saṃspṛśet' ityādinā vihitamasti prasakti:- tādṛśabhasmadhāraṇa- prasaṅgaḥ, itaratra – upāsanāntare, na cā'sti naiva saṃbhāvanā maīti, sautrāmaṇi... grahasya - sautrāṇināmakayāgaprastāve 'surāgrahān gṛhṇāti' iti śrutivihitasya surāgraha ( pātraviśeṣa) grahaṇasya itaratra – yāgāntare, yadvat na prasaktiḥ– yathā grahaṇaprasaṅgo naiva sambhāvyate ;

tathaiva || 90 ||

ātharvaṇyāṃ śikhāyāmagaṇi bhagavato yā janiḥ kāraṇaika-
dhyeyatvedamparāyā jagadupakṛtaye so'yamicchāvatāraḥ | yadviṣṇo. kāraṇaikapravaṇaphaṇitibhiḥ kāraṇatvopadeśaḥ
spaṣṭo dṛṣṭaprayogaḥ sukhajanakatayā śambhuśabdo'pi tasmin || 91 ||

kāraṇaika.....rāyām —'kāraṇaṃ tu dhyeyaḥ' iti kāraṇasyaikasyaiva dhyeyatve pradhānatātparyeṇa pravṛttāyām, ātharvaṇyāṃ śikhāyām - atharvaśikhākhyāyāmupaniṣadi bhagavataḥ – mahāviṣṇoḥ yā janaḥ agaṇiyā utpattiḥ kathitā vartate 'brahmaviṣṇurudrendrāste sarve samprasūyante' iti, so'yam — utpattirūpeṇa śrutipratipannaḥ aṃśa, jagadupakṛtaye - duṣṭa nigraha - śiṣṭaparitrāṇādi- rūpalokopakāroddeśyakaḥ, icchāvatāraḥ karmādhīnaḥ saṅkalpapūrvakaḥ āvirbhāvaḥ, yat - yena hetunā, viṣṇoḥ - paramātmano nārāyaṇasya, kāraṇatvopadeśaḥ – ajñātasya jagatkāraṇatvasya kāraṇa .... phaṇitimiḥ — jagajjanmādikāraṇabhūtatatvapratipādane pradhānatātparyavatībhiḥ (kāraṇabhūte ekasminnevā'rthe iti vā ) 'eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt ' 'atha puruṣo ha vai nārāyaṇo'. kāmayata prajāḥ sṛjeyeti' 'viṣṇoḥ sakāśādudbhūtaṃ jagattatraiva ca sthitam' ityādiśruti- smṛtyādigīrbhiḥ, viṣṇoḥ kāraṇatvopadeśaḥ – viṣṇoreva jagatkāraṇatva vidhirityaya maṃśaḥ, spaṣṭaḥ asandigdhaḥ, tasmin — jagatkāraṇatayā niścite viṣṇau, śambhuśabdo'pi - śaṃbhurityayaṃ śabdo'pi sukhajanakatayā - sukhotpādakatvena nimittena dṛṣṭaprayogaḥ - anyatra paricitaprayoga eva || 91 ||

jñāpanam,

uditamupāsanadvaya matharvaśiraḥ śikhayoḥ sphuṭamapunarbhavāya puravairikale bariṇaḥ |
kamaladṛśaḥ kacit kvacana kāraṇatasvaparaśrutivihitasya hārdasuṣimadhyajuṣau viduṣaḥ || 92 || atharvaśiraḥ- śikhayoḥ—atharvaśira upanipadi atharva śikhopaniṣadi ca, apunarbhavāya - mokṣārtham (mokṣārthopāyatayā) uditam – vihitam upāsanadvayam – ' tadetadupa sinavya' miti, 'śambhurākāśamadhye dhyeyaḥ' iti copāsanadvayam viviktasvarūpamiti, sphuṭam – suviditaṃ bhavati,

(taca) kacit — uktopaniṣadanyatarasyāṃ atharvaśira upaniṣadi puravairikale bariṇaḥ- rudrākhyena devaviśeṣeṇa śarīriṇaḥ, kamaladṛśaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣasya bhagavata: ( upāsanam ), kvacana - anantara- śrutāyām atharvaśikhāyāmupaniṣadi kāraṇa... hitasya - jagatkāraṇabhūtaparatattvapratipādanapravṛttāsu śrutiṣu upadiṣṭasya, viduṣaḥ hārdasuṣimadhyajuṣaḥ - upāsaka cenanīya hṛdaya kā śamadhyasthasya, kamala- dṛśaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣasya (viṣṇoḥ) upāsanamiti sujñeyamityarthaḥ || ( ataḥ upaniṣadvaya vihita- mapyupāsanam sadvārakādvārakabhedena bhagavadupāsanameveti nātra kiñcit kliṣṭamasti) || 92 || ukta kenacidīśvaraḥ puruṣa ityuktī śiva- śrīśayo
vartete bahuśo mahatparamavāmyukte viyukte api | ityutkarṣa- nikarṣagocarapadavyākhyātayoretayo
rutkarṣo giriśasya vaktumucitaḥ puṃsaḥ pradhānādiva || 93 ||
mahat- paramavāgyukte - ādau 'mahat 'śabdena 'parama'śabdena ca upapadena viśeṣite, (tathā) viyukte api – tābhyāmupapadābhyāṃ rahite kevale api, īśvaraḥ puruṣaḥ ityuktī - īśvara - puruṣa - śabdau (maheśvaraḥ parameśvara : īśvara:) evam ( mahāpuruṣa: paramapuruṣa: puruṣa: ) bahuśa: - prāyaśa:, śiva- śrīśayoḥ vartete iti-- śambho lakṣmīpatau ca bodhakau vidyete iti (ataśca) utkarṣa - nikarṣagocarapadavyākhyātayoḥ - praśastatvabodhakena 'īśvara' padena, tadapekṣayā apakṛṣṭatvabodhakena puruṣapadena (sevakādisādhāraṇavṛttinā) ca vyaktīkṛta svarūpayoḥ etayoḥ – śiva viṣṇvormadhye, pradhānāt puṃsa iva – prakṛtitattvāta puruṣatatvasyeva, giriśasya – śivasya, utkarṣaḥ vakta- mucitaḥ vyavahartu yogya:, ( ' pradhāna - puruṣau ' ityuktayormadhye 'puruṣa utkṛṣṭaḥ, pradhānamapakṛṣṭam ' ; tathā 'śiva viṣṇū' ityuktayormadhye ' śiva utkṛṣṭaḥ viṣṇurapakṛṣṭa:' iti ) iti kenaciduktam- śivapāramyakathanapravṛttena kenacana janenoktam || 93 ||

naitat sādhu samākhyayā hi balabadvākyādimanārthayā

notkarṣo giriśasya vaktumucitaḥ kenā'pi devendravat | indraḥ syāt parameśvarastvitarathā tattatsamākhyāvaśā-

dākhyā hi ! prabala pramāṇavihatā tasmin mahāvṛkṣavat || 94 ||

etat — puruṣaśabdapravṛttita: śrīśe apakarṣakalpanaṃ, īśvaraśabdapravṛttita: śambhau utkarṣa - kalpanaṃ cetyubhayamapi na sādhuna samīcīnaṃ, hi yataḥ, balabadvā.... rthayā - adhika balayuktaiḥ vākya (samabhivyāhāra) liṅga (sāmarthya) śruti (nirapekṣakha) rūpaiḥ pramāṇaiḥ pratiruddha svāmidheya pratipattimatyā, samākhyayā śrutiliṅgādimadhyapātinyā pramāṇabhūtayaugikaśabdākhyayā

(sādhanatvenāśritayā ) giriśasya utkarṣa:- śivasya viṣṇyapekṣayā atiśayaḥ, kenā'pi [ - pramāṇabalābala cintanapaṭunā yena kenā'pi vaktuṃ nocitaḥ kathayituṃ yogyo na bhavitumarhati (īśvara-puruṣādiśabdāḥ kalpyamāna vyutpattiviśeṣavantaḥ santaḥ, samākhyātvaṃ pratipadyante | tadānuguṇyena ca, arthaviśeṣapratipattau pramāṇatāṃ ca labhante | vākya- liṅga - śrutayastu samākhyātaḥ pūrvameva siddhapramāṇabhāvā iti hetoḥ, samākhyayā sampipādayiṣitamarthaniścayaṃ pratirundhantītyarthaḥ) devendravat – 'mahādeva - devendra 'yormadhye kataraḥ śreyān ? iti vicāre pravṛtte, śabdavyutpatticintanayā, mahattvāśraya deva bhūta śivāpekṣayā paramaiśvaryavaddevabhūtasya śakrasya, atiśaya nirṇayo yathā nocitaḥ tadvat itarathā - pramāṇabalābala cintanaṃ vihāya, kevalena ekadeśagrahaṇenā'pi arthanirṇayo yadi sādhuḥ syāt tadā tattatsamākhyāvaśāt - avayavavyutpatti- viśeṣavataḥ (idi paramaiśvarye) indra- mahendrādiśabdānavalambya indraḥ – devarājaḥ śakra eva, parameśvaraḥ syāt – sarvāvadhika niraṅkuśaiśvaryavattayā abhyupagamyo bhavet na giriśa: ; nā'pi śrīśaḥ, hi- yataḥ, ākhyā - abhidhāyakaḥ śabdaḥ, tasmin — śakre, mahāvṛkṣavat— kṣudre vṛkṣaviśepe yathā 'mahāvṛkṣa' śabda: prayujyamānaḥ pratyakṣato bādhitāvayavārthaḥ tadvat, prabalapramāṇa- vihatā - abhidhāyakaśabdāpekṣayā prābalyavatā śruti - smṛtyādipramāṇena bādhitārthakaḥ | tathaiva maheśvarādiśabdo'pi āpekṣikaiśvarya mahatvādipratipādanena niṣpannaprayojanaḥ san bhagavati śrutyādisiddhaṃ niravadhikanirupādhikaniraṅkuśaiśvaryaṃ pratirodhuṃ naiva prabhavediti || 04 ||

samākhyayā

kiṃ jīva: puruṣoktigocara iti śrīśo'pi tadgocaro

jīvaḥ syāddviriśo dhruvo bhavati kiṃ tau sthāṇusaṃjñāviti |
takṣatvāya ghaṭeta kiṃ mukhabhuvāmācārya saṃśabdanaṃ
maṇḍūkatvamupaiti kiṃ haripadasparśena kaṇṭhīravaḥ || 95 ||

jīvaḥ - jīvātmā, puruṣoktigocara iti - puruṣaśabdabodhya iti hetoḥ, tadgocaraḥ- puruṣaśabdābhidheyabhūtaḥ, śrīśo'pi - nārāyaṇo'pi kiṃ jīvaḥ syāt 1 – jīvo bhavitumaīti vā; naiva, tau śaṅkurūpa dāruviśeṣaḥ śambhuścetyubhāvapi sthāṇusaṃjñāviti – sthāṇupadavācyā viti kṛtvā, giriśaḥ – śambhurapi, dhruvo bhavati kim 1 – śaṅkurūpo dārurbhavati vā naiva, mukha bhuvām — brahmajanmanāṃ viprāṇām, ācāryasaṃśabdanam — ācāryapadāmilapyatvam, takṣatvāya – (brāhmaṇānāmapi ) vardhakibhāvamāpādayitum, ghaṭeta kim 9 – samartha bhavet kim ? naiva bhavet khala, kaṇṭhīravaḥ – siṃho'pi, haripadasparśena– vācyatayā hariśabda sambandhamātreṇa,

maṇḍūkatvam upaiti kim — mekatvaṃ prāpnoti kim ? naiva prāpnoti khalu ! || śabdekyamātra, bhinnayorarthayoḥ bhedapratighātakaṃ naiva bhavati ityarthaḥ || 95 ||

prāṇākāśamukhaiḥ padairupaniṣadbhāgeṣu jodhupyate
kiṃ na brahma paraṃ paratvagaṇanā jāgarti kiṃ tāvatā | prāṇādyeṣu śivādiśabdakathi (gadi) taṃ taccat paratvaṃ kuta-
stattatkāraṇavākya siddhabhagavatsṛjyeṣu rudrādiṣu || 96 ||

paraṃ brahmasṛṣṭyādikāraṇatvena sampratipannaṃ paraṃ brahma, upaniṣadbhāgeṣu trayyantabhāgeṣu, prāṇākāśamukhaiḥ padaiḥ prāṇaḥ ākāśa:' ityādibhiḥ śabdaiḥ, na joghuṣyate kim – 'sarvāṇi havā imāni bhūtāni prāṇamevābhisaṃviśanti prāṇamabhyujjihate' sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtānyākāśādeva samutpadyante ākāśaṃ pratyastaṃ yanti' ityādirūpeṇa tatra tatra bahuśaḥ udghaṣya- māṇaṃ na śrutaṃ kim ? ; śrutameva khalu tāvatā tanmātreṇa prāṇādyeṣu prāṇākāśādiṣvartheṣu, paratvagaṇanā sarvotkṛṣṭatvavyavahāraḥ, kiṃ jāgarti pravartate vā naiva pravartate khalu, tat paraṃ brahma, śivādiśabdakathitaṃ cet — śiva rudra prabhṛtibhiḥ śabdaiḥ nirdiśya bodhitamitye- tāvanmātreṇa, tattatkāraṇa……..sṛjyeṣu taistaiḥ eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt ' ' atha puruṣo ha bai nārāyaṇo'kāmayata prajāḥ sṛjeyeti' ityādibhiḥ kāraṇavākyaiḥ kāraṇatvena siddhena bhagavatā maṣu, rudrādiṣu - cetanabhūteṣu rudrādyartheṣu paratvaṃ kutaḥ sarvasmāt paratvaṃ— atiśayitattvaṃ kena hetunā siddhayet || 96 ||

yadupaniṣadbhiraprathi' paribraḍhimā paramaḥ
kamaladṛśo viśuddhanayalabdhapariṣkṛtibhiḥ |
itara giro'nurodhumidameva ghaṭanta iti
sphuṭamupabṛṃhayanti munayo'pi manupramukhāḥ || 97 ||
viśuddhanayalabdhapariṣkṛtibhiḥ - pramāṇa - tarkādipratighātara hitai: śārīrakalabdhaiḥ vedā- tārthanirṇaya samarthe yayai: mitho virodhādiśaṅkāparihārarūpaṃ pariṣkāraṃ prāptavatībhiḥ, upaniṣadbhiḥ- vedāntavāgbhiḥ, kamaladṛśaḥ - puṇḍarīkadalā malāya tekṣaṇasya bhagavato nārāyaṇasya, paramaḥ - nirastasamābhyadhikaḥ, paritraḍhimā utkarṣātiśayaḥ, aprathi yat— samyak prakaṭīkṛta iti yadasti idameva – upaniṣatprakaṭitaṃ bhagavataḥ sarvātiśāyyutkarṣavatvameva, itaragiraḥ —adhītā-

nadhītavedaśākhāntaragatāḥ sarvā api śrutivācaḥ, anuroddhum anusartum, ghaṭanta iti- samarthā bhavantīti, manupramukhāḥ munayo'pi manuparāśaraprabhṛtayaḥ vedārthasmṛtipravaṇā api sphuṭam – asandigdhaṃ yathā tathā upabṛṃhayanti -- ( tamevārthe paripoṣya) svavāgbhirapi varṇayanti ataḥ paraṃ brahma viṣṇureva parātparaḥ || 97 ||

setutvaṃ setutulyād vidharaṇaniyamād bandhanādvā'tra yukta vyāpte'pyasminnupādheḥ parimitivacanaṃ sārthakaṃ sūtrita prāk | cātuṣpādyaṃ ca 'tatacchrutimi 'ranuguṇaṃ kalpyate'nantabhūnnaḥ
svasyetyevā'mṛtasyetyabhihitamathavā muktirevā'mṛtaṃ syāt || 25 ||

atra - prāpye parasmin brahmaṇi, setutvam -' eṣa seturvidharaṇa:' iti śrutipratipannaṃ setutvaṃ tāvat, setutulyāt – lokaparidṛṣṭasya setoḥ tasya ca sādhāraṇadharmabhūtāt vidharaṇa - niyamāt- amṛtasyeti- 'amṛtasyaiṣa setuḥ' iti śrutau amṛtasyeti padena,

svabhyetyeva abhihitam – 'eṣaḥ - paramātmā, amṛtasya–amṛtarūpasya svasya, setuḥ prāpakaḥ' ityevoktam ; na tu svasmādanyasya amṛtasyeti, ( ataḥ prāpakatvoktiḥ na prāpyatvavādhikā bhavatīti) athavā – arthāntare cintyamāne, amṛtam- amṛtapadābhidheyo'rthaḥ, muktireva syāt – saṃsārabandhasya vimoka eva bhavet, 'eṣaḥ - bhagavān nārāyaṇaḥ, amṛtasya saṃsāravimokasya, setuḥ prāpakaḥ, muktidaḥ' ityarthāt, na prāpya- prāpakayoraikyarūpamasāṅgatyam || ( ataḥ kāraṇaṃ brahmatra parātparamiti ) || 25 ||

anyasyādhikyavāde paramavadhitayā kāraṇaṃ yatra dṛṣṭaṃ
tatra vyākṛtādistadavadhiritarāpekṣayā'sau paraśca |
yasmādanyatparaṃ netyabhihitaviṣaye tatparoktegyukte-
revantvādityanūktistata iti yadi vā vyāpyamuktaṃ tadastu || 26 ||
yatra – yādṛśasthale, kāraṇam — tasvaviśeṣasya kāraṇatayā śratisiddham, param – 'para' śabdābhidheyaṃ vastu, atradhitayā dṛṣṭam - arthaviśeṣagataṃ paratvaṃ prati (apakarṣavattayā ) nirūpakaṃ

sat śrutam ( tādṛśe), anyasyādhikyavāde – vastvantarasyotkarṣapratipāda ke vacane ' akṣarāt parataḥ paraḥ' ityādau, tatra – tādṛśe sthale, tadavadhiḥ – dvitīyasya paratvasya nirūpakaḥ apakṛṣṭorthaḥ, avyākṛtādiḥ – sandarbhānuguṇyena 'avyaktatattvam, jīvaḥ, samaṣṭipuruṣo hiraṇyagarbhaḥ, ' ityevaṃ vācyaḥ asau – avyākṛtādirarthaḥ, itarāpekṣayā - svakāryāpekṣayā, acetanāpekṣayā, svavyaṣṭyapekṣayā ca, paraśca hi atiśayavānapi bhavatyeva khalu ! ; nātra vivādaḥ yasmādanya- viṣaye –'yasmāt nāḍaparamasti kiṃcit' iti pañjabhyantayacchabdabodhye'rthe svāvadhikaparāntara- rāhityapratipādanasthale tatparokterayukteḥ- pūrvavākye sarvasmātparamuktatvā uttaravākye tato'pi parasya kathanaṃ vāghitamiti heto:, tata iti 'tato yaduttarataraṃ tadarūpam ' iti vākyasthaṃ 'tataḥ' iti padam ' evantvāditi anūktiḥ - asya uktāt sarvasmāt paratvāddhetoḥ ityarthakaṃ saḥ uktānuvādarūpameva bhavati ; na tu paratvaṃ prati avadhisamarpakaṃ bhavati, (yena parāntara- tātparyabhramaḥ syāt ) yadi vā yadvā, tat - pañcamyanta tacchabdena 'tataḥ' ityanena bodhyam, uktaṃ vyāpyamastu - 'tenedaṃ pūrṇa puruṣeṇa sarvam' iti puruṣeṇa vyāptatvenā'bhihitaṃ sarvameva bhavatu | (tataḥ — svavyāptāt sarvasmāt ityarthaḥ) || 26 ||

-0-

phalādhikaraṇam ||3-2-8||
śārīrakanyā yakalāpasaṃgrahaḥ ||
sākṣāt kramādvā phaladatvadṛṣṭeratrā'pi karmaiva phalaṃ dadātu |

naiveda'manyārthataya|' tu klṛpteḥ śrutismṛtibhyaśca sa eva datte || 17 ||

sākṣāt – (mardanāda) advārakatayā vā kramādvā - (kṛpyā dau) sadvārakatayā paramparayā vā, phaladatvadṛṣṭeḥ– (karmaṇaḥ) phalajanakatvadarśanāt, atrā'pi trivargāpavargasādhanatayā śrutyādipratipannayāgopāsanādāvapi sthaḻe, karmaiva — yāgādikam upāsanādikameva ca phalam - trivargākhyam apavargākhyaṃ ca phalam dadātu prayacchatu ( na tvanyasya phalapradāturarāvaśyakatā sti iti śaṅkane) idaṃ naiva karmārādhyaṃ vinā karmaṇa eva phalapradatvamityetat naiva sādhu, śruti - smṛtibhyaśca – vedebhyaḥ tadupabṛṃhaṇebhyaḥ itihāsa-purāṇādibhyaśca anyārthatayā tu - svetaradevatāviśeṣaprītyāpādanārthameva ca klṛpteḥ – yā gopāsanādeḥ vidhānadarśanāt sa eva- karmopāsanārādhyaḥ paramātmaiva, datte – svargamokṣā dikaṃ phalaṃ prayacchati || 27 ||

kāṇḍepi etāvatā - ādito daśabhiḥ pādaiḥ bhavabhaya... lakṣyaḥ – saṃsāradāvānalasādhvasena ananyagatikatayā digbhrāntāntaḥkaraṇānāṃ cetanānām prātyekalakṣyatvaniścayataḥ upāsanaika lakṣyabhūtaḥ, paramapuruṣa eva tattacchāstrārthayogyam - yāgādirūpasya karmavidhāyiśāstrārthasya, upāsanādi- rūpasya mokṣopāyavidhāyiśāstrārthasya ca yathāyatham anuguṇam, phalam - prayojanabhūtaṃ trivargamapavarge ca tattadīpsitam diśatīti — anugṛhṇātīti pratipādya, atyudāraḥ atha sthāpyate- ( svavibhūtimārabhya svātmaparyanta pradānarūpa ) nirastasamābhyadhika mahaudārya sampanno'yamiti asminnaṣi- karaṇe dṛḍhīkriyate || 27 ||

kṛpyādermardanā derapi bhavati phalaṃ dvārato vā'nyathā vā

dharmāṇāṃ sādhanatvaṃ śrutimiravagataṃ doṣabādhojjhitābhiḥ |
tasmādīśaprasādāt phalamiti tu vacastatpraśaṃseti cenna
śrautārādhyaprasādatyajana kadanato'pūrva klṛpterayogāt || 28||

kṛpyādeḥ mardanāderapi bhūmau bījānāmāvāpapūrvakaṃ karṣaṇādivyāpārataḥ, (tathā) mardanāderapi telalepapūrvakaṃ aṅgamardanādervyāpārato'pi ca, dvārato vā - madhye yat kiṃcidddvārī- kṛtya vā, anyathā vā yat kiṃcit advārīkṛtya vā, phalaṃ bhavati — ārogya sampatti- sasyasampaśyādikaṃ phalaṃ sampadyate, (tadvat ) dharmāṇām - yāga dāna - homopāsanādirūpāṇāṃ dharma- padābhidheyānāṃ karmaṇām sādhanatvam – traivargikāpavargika phalādhāyakatvam, doṣa-vāghojjhi- tābhiḥ nityanirdo svabhāvābhiḥ pramāṇāntaravā ghara hitābhiśca, śrutibhiḥ (vedavāgbhiḥ) avagatam - spaṣṭameva jñātamasti, tasmāt karmaṇāṃ dharmapadābhitreyānāmeva phalapradatvasiddheḥ, īśaprasādāt phalamitivacastu - bhagavadanugrahā deva phalasiddhirityarthakaṃ purāṇādivacanaṃ param tatpraśaṃsā- īśvarastutyarthavādarūpameva iti cenna — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ sādhu na bhavati, śrautārādhya... kadanataḥ - ' sa evainaṃ bhūrti gamayati' ityādiśrutipratipannasya karmārādhyadevatāprasādasya phalapradatva parityāga- rūpadoṣāpatteḥ, apūrva kloḥ karmopāsanādeḥ svargāpavargādeścāntarā apūrvanāmnaḥ aśrutapūrvasya kasyacanārthasya phalapradatvakalpanāyāḥ, ayogāt - aucityāsambhavāt || 28 ||

yadyapyārādhya mūlaṃ phalamiti phalitaṃ devatādhikriyāyā~
karmāpekṣā tathoktā phalajananaparapreraṇādau tathā'pi |
sākṣitvā nādaratvaprabhṛtiparaguṇān prekṣya tatprīṇanādau
śaṅkā tarniruddhāṃ tvarayitumadhunā tādṛśodāroktiḥ || 29 ||

yadyapi - vastutaścintyamāne, phalam – (phaloddeśena kriyamāṇasya) karmaṇaḥ uddeśyabhūtaṃ phalam . ārādhyamūlamiti — karmaṇā prīṇanīyo yaḥ tatkāraṇakameveti, devatādhikriyāyām - ādyādhyāyīye devatā dhikaraṇe, phalitam – devatāsvarūpa - tadvigraha tatsannidhāna- nadupāsanayogyatā di- vicāraphalatayā siddhameva, tathā tadvadeva, phalajanana - parapreraṇādau – ( para devatā bhūtasya bhagavataḥ ) phalapradāne, vihita-niṣiddhākhyeṣu karmasu pravartane, amayādīnāṃ vāgādyanupraveśepi ca, karmāpekṣā - tattajjīvīya pūrva karmasaha kā rāvalambanā, uktā -parāyattādhikaraṇādau nirūpitā, tathā'pi— eteṣāmarthaviśeṣāṇāmadhikaraṇāntarato lābhe'pi sākṣitvā .... guṇān - kevaladraṣṭutve sati nirvyā- pāratvarūpaṃ yat sākṣitvam avāptasamasta kāmatvena nikhilaṃ jagat tṛṇīkṛtya joṣamāsīnatvarūpaṃ yat anādaratvaṃ, tadādikān abhigamyatvapratibandhakatayā bhāsamānān paramātmagatān guṇa- viśeṣān, prekṣya - śāstrato'vagatānā locya tatprīṇanādau - tādṛśaguṇaviśeṣayukta paramātma- prītijananādau vyāpāre, śaṅkātaṅkaṅkerniruddhān – saṃśaya. bhītyādibhiḥ sthagita pravṛttīn janān, tvarayitum — anaudāsīnyena tīvratayā tatprīṇanodyuktān kartum, kartum, adhunā - sādhanādhyāyīye asmin adhikaraṇe, tādṛśodāroktiḥ - vātsalyātiśayena sarvakarmaphalapradāna tatparatvarūpasya mahaudāryasya bhagavati nirūpaṇam || 29 ||

samrājā sānukampāt piturucitavidaḥ sāmyabhājo vadānyāt

sthāne vindanti putrā niyatarucibhidā yantritāstaṃ tamartham |
tatra prāpyaṃ svato yadvihatiriha yatastatpraśāntiśca yasmā-

deyaṃ ' as 'viśeṣāddamanamapi yathālokamantrā'pi tat syāt || 30 ||

sānukampāt dayāloḥ, ucitavidaḥ – ucitānucitayormadhye ucita vivicya jānataḥ, sāmyabhājaḥ putreṣvaviṣamavṛtteḥ, vadānyāt – udārapravṛtteḥ, sāmrājaḥ – maṇḍaleśvarāt, pitu: --- svajanakāt ( sakāśāt ) putrāḥ - tatsutāḥ, niyata..... yantritāḥ svaṃ svaṃ prati nisarga- kṛtavyavasthāvatā ruciviśeṣeṇa niyantrita prārthanāḥ santaḥ, sthāne – yogye samaye, taṃ tamartham - svaprārthitamarthaviśeṣam, vindanti —– labhante, tatra – samrā pitāputrasthale, yat – yādṛśaṃ gṛha- kṣetrārāmādi svataḥ prāpyam – svabhāvata eva labhyam, yataḥ - yādṛśāt ājñolaṅghanāddhetoḥ aniṣṭakarmācaraṇādvā, iha vihatiḥ - svataḥ prāpyārthaviṣaye vighāto bhavati yasmāt – kṣamā-

prārthanāt tatpraśānviśva – prāptasya vighātasya nivṛttirbhavati,

vighātasya nivṛttirbhavati,

aviśeṣāt — sarvaputra-

sādhāraṇyataḥ, yadvā — yadapi ca vastu, deyam - rājñaiva putrebhyaḥ pradeyaṃ vartate, damanamapi aparādhaṃ kṛtvā kṣamāyā apyaprārthane daṇḍanaṃ ca yadasti, tat - tat sarvamapi yathālokam - lokavadeva, atrāpi - dāntika bhūta jīva- paramātmasthale'pi syāt - saṅgatatayā anviyāt ||30||

-0-

bodhanaṃ ca sarvendriyavyāpārāvasthā jāgradākhyā ca (tathā) kāryavasthā rogaviśeṣarūpā ceti (etā: jīvagatāvasthā ),

mūrcchā ca - sukha - duḥkhavyapoha-
parasyā'pi paramātmano'pi,

nirdoṣabhāvaḥ - doṣasāmānyā saṃspṛṣṭatvam, akhila heya pratyanīkatvarūpaṃ samasta kalyāṇaguṇākaratva- sahitam, jagadaṃśibhāvaḥ – adhikuṇḍalanyāyena mūrtīmūrta jagadākārā śrayatvam, sarveśvaratvam- sarvasmāt parasmāt paratvayutaṃ sarvaniyantṛtvam phaladātṛtā ca tasyaiva traivargikā pavargika - sarvaphalasyā'pi pradātṛtā sadvārakādvā rakatayeti (apūrva kalpanānāvaśyakatā) ete aṣṭamiradhikaraṇaiḥ pratipāditā arthāḥ || 18 ||

nyāyaiścaturbhirarādyairvicintitaḥ pūrva' pādaśeṣastu' |
tasmādatra tu pāde susaṅgataṃ pañcamāt prabhṛti || 19 ||
ādyaiḥ caturbhiḥ nyāyaiḥ _ etatpādīyaiḥ prāthamikai : ( sandhya-bhāva- karmānusmṛtiśabda- vidhimugdhā rūyaiḥ) caturadhikaraṇaiḥ, pūrvapādaśeṣastu – jīvadoṣapratipādana paraprathamapādaśeṣabhūtoṃ'śa eva, vicintitaḥ - vicārya nirṇītaḥ | tasmāt – uktāddhetoḥ atra pāde - dvitīye pāde, pañcamāt prabhṛti tu – ubhayaliṅgādhikaraṇamārabhya adhikaraṇacatuṣṭayameva, (ubhayaliṅga adhikuṇḍala- para- phalākhyam ) susaṅgatam — pradhānapādārthānvayena suṣṭhu saṅgatimadbhavati || 19 ||

sthānitvādacidaikyā 'davaratvādaphaladatvācca' |
anupāsyamiti tu śaṅkāṃ śakalayati smottaraiścaturbhiriha || 20 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||
śuddhānande taditthaṃ śubhaguṇajaladhau satyanitya svadehe

devībhūṣāyudhādyairatiśayini kanadbhogalīlāvibhūtau | śeṣitvādhārabhāvaprabhṛti bahuvidhasthāsnusambandhadīpte
dṛṣṭiḥ svargāpavargaprasavitari harau nirnimeṣā śrutiḥ || 31 ||
tat – paramātmanaḥ sarvaphaladatvasādhanāt ittham - atra vakṣyamāṇayā rītyā, śuddhānande- duḥkhalavenāpya saṃspṛṣṭānandai kasvarūpasvabhāve, śubhaguṇajaladhau – apuruṣārthāsambhinnānāṃ svasya pareṣāṃ ca anukūla karūpāṇāṃ anantānāṃ maṅgalānāṃ guṇānāṃ nidhānabhūte, satya - nityakhade he paramārthabhūta- sadātanadivyamaṅgalavigrahaśālini, devībhūṣāyudhādyairatiśayini - lakṣmyādidevīmi ḥ, śrīvatsa-- kaustubhādibhiḥ divyābharaṇaiḥ, sudarśanādibhiḥ divyāyudhaiśca sarvavilakṣaṇotkarṣavati, kanadbhoga- līlāvibhūtau -- sadā dedīpyamānayā paramapadākhyayā bhogavibhūtyā nirantaraṃ pravāharūpeṇānu- vartamānayā līlāvibhūtyā ca virājamāne, śeṣitvā dīpte nirupādhikasarvaśeṣitva-svābhāvika- samasta jagadādhāratva-sarvaniyantṛtva- sarvaprakāritva- sarvā pṛthaksiddhatva- sarvaśarī ritva sarvakāraṇatvamukhaiḥ vividhaiḥ yāvadātmabhāvibhiḥ ( jagataḥ svasya ca ) sambandhairvilakṣaṇaśobhāvati, svargāpavarga- prasavitari—acintyānantamahimni svasminneva, garbhīkāreṇa nihitaṃ traivargikāpavargika phalajā taṃ ucite kāle yathāyogyamanugrahītari harau – śrīmati mukunde bhagavati viṣaye, naḥ—asmākam, śrutiḥ - nityanirdoṣavedara śireva, nirnimeṣā dṛṣṭiḥ pramādādigandhavidūro netrasthānīyaḥ pramāṇa- mūrdhanyaḥ | (ayaṃ pādapradhānatātparyagācaro'rthaḥ || 31 ||

pāde svānārthahetustadayamiha subutyādhṛtiḥ suptagoptā
mugdheo dbodhādikartā tvanaghaśubhaguṇo'cidbhiraṃśī svadehaiḥ |
pāramyasyaikasīmā sakalaphalada ityucyate bhaktibhūgne
'satye tvevaṃ' guṇādāvatha parabhajane rūpabhedādi cintyam || 32 ||

tat – pādapradhānārthasya īdṛśatvāt iha pāde – asmin tṛtīyādhyāyīyadvitīyacaraṇe, (aṣṭasvapyadhikaraṇeṣu) ayam - divyaguṇa vigraha vibhūtyā disampannaḥ parabrahmabhūtaḥ paramātmā nārāyaṇaḥ

svāpnārthahetuḥ – jīvātmanā svapnadaśāyāmanubhūyamānānimarthānāṃ sṛṣṭikartā, suṣutyādhṛti:- suṣuptyākhyāyāmavasthāyāṃ jīvasyāyastakaraṇakalecarasya viśrāmasthānabhūtaḥ, suptagoptā - suṣuptasya yāvatsvāpaṃ pradāyāśvāsaṃ prabodhapradānena rakṣitā, mugdhodbodhādikartā prāptamūrcchānāṃ yathākarma punaḥ prabodhasya maraṇasya vā sampādakaḥ, anaghaśubhaguṇaḥ - nirasta masta heya divya maṅgalaguṇaḥ, acidbhiḥ svadehairaṃśī mūrtīmūrtakhyā cetanajagadātmakaiḥ śarīrai: sāṃśaḥ, pāramyasya ekasīmā- paratvasya paramakāṣṭha| bhūta;, sakalaphaladaḥ - aihikāmuṣmika sarva phalapradātā, iti — evaṃrītyā (puruṣārthabhūtaguṇāśrayatayā) bhaktibhūmne — upāsana vṛddhayai, ucyate-paramātmaiva adhikaraṇa- bhedairvarṇyate | evam – uktarītyā, satye tu — paramārthabhūte eva, guṇādau – guṇavigrahādau sati, atha - paścāttane tṛtīyapāde, parabhajane - paramātmopāsane rūpabhedādi – upāsyaviśeṣaṇabhūtaṃ minnabhinna rūpādikam, cintyam – upāsanaviṣayatā prāptumarham || 32 ||

i

iti tṛtīyasyāsdhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ || 2 ||

|| T: ||

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ | śriyai namaḥ | śrīdharāya namaḥ ||
tṛtīyasyādhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
sarvavedāntapratyayādhikaraṇam || 3-3-1 ||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ ||
aviśeṣataḥ śrutatvān prakaraṇabhedācca bhedinī vidyā | naivaṃ pratipat bhinnatva! 'codanādyabhedācca' || 21 ||
aviśeṣataḥ śrutatvāt - ekatra śākhāviśeṣe yathā śrutam ; tathā śākhāntare'pi avilakṣaṇatayā punaḥ śrutvāt hetoḥ prakaraṇabhedācca – ekasyāmeva śākhāyāmapi śravaṇasthale prastāva rūpasya vidyāprakaraṇasya bhinnatvādapi hetoḥ, vidyā daharavaiśvānarādirūpā vidyā, bhedinī - chāndogyādigatāt vājasaneyādigatā atyantaṃ bhinnaiva iti śaṅkane, naivam- īdṛśarītikaṃ nocitam pratipattaṇāṃ bhinnatvāt tattacchākhādhyetṝṇāṃ vibhinnatvāt codanādya- bhedācca vidhisvarūpa-rūpa-ākhyā phalasaṃyogānām abhinnatvādapi śākhābhedataḥ prakaraṇabhedataśca aviśeṣapuna śrutyādikā vidyābhedapādhikā na bhavati || 21 |

0-

puruṣārtha prāpakatayā abhyarhitopāyabhūtam (upāsanākhyaṃ sādhanam ) vaktum vedāntasiddhaṃ nyāyato nirṇetum, tattve – cetanācetaneśvarākhyatattvatrayaviṣaye, tarkajvarajanitamahāsannipāta- pralāpān — paramārthanirṇayāparyavasāyino ye'pratiṣṭhitāstarkāḥ kevalamupatāpahetutvena jvarapadābhi- dheyā:, teṣāṃ nirantarānuvṛttyā utpanno yaḥ saṃnipātapadābhidheyaḥ viṣamāvasthāviśeṣaḥ tatkālīnān vyavahāraviśeṣān, nirdhūya — dūrīkṛtya tattvabodhe niṣpanne – nirviśeṣacinmātrākhyasya parasya avabodhe siddhe sati, viduṣā prāptatattvāvabodhena sādhyam- mokṣopayogitayā kartavyam, kimapi na – yatkicidapi naiva vidyate iti udgṛṇadbhayaḥ – evaṃ vādino'dvaitadārśanikānuddiśya, muktilābhāya – mokṣāvāptaye, yāvajīvānuvartya muraripubhajanam - dehadhāraṇaṃ yāvat āprayāṇamanuṣṭheyaṃ bhagavadupāsanam, vakti — hitatamatayā bodhayati adhyāyaśeṣeṇa bādarāyaṇaḥ || 1 ||

pratyaktatvasya

brahmaṇa:

bhīmābhyo yātana | bhyaḥ pitṛpathagamanāvartanādeśca bibhyat
tṛṣṇā kṛṣṇāmṛtāndhau pariṇayati parāṃ yāvatā tāvaduktam |
itthaṃ labdhādhikāraḥ paramadhikurute sādhane yatra sāṅge
pādadvandve parasmin tadiha bahubhidā baraṃ nirbravīti || 2 ||

bhīmābhyaḥ—atyantaṃ bhayaṅkarībhyaḥ, yātanābhyaḥ - nārakībhyo vedanābhyaḥ sakāśāt, (tathā) pitṛpathagamanāvartanādeśca – pitṛyāṇākhye vartmani sukṛtāyattāt ārohāt, sukṛtakṣaya- prayuktāt avarohācca paunaḥpunyena garbhapraveśa| dijanakatayā santanyamānādapi ca bibhyat- bhayaṃ prāpnuvan cetanaḥ, yāvatā – jīvadoṣa bhagavadguṇa diyāvadarthajñānena, kṛṣṇāmṛtābdhau — śrī- kṛṣṇākhye amṛtamaye mahodadhau, parāṃ tṛṣṇām — utkaṭā micchā (tatprāptiṃ vinā ātmadhāraṇā- lābhaparyavasitāṃ parāṃ kāṣṭhāmārūḍhāmā śām ) pariṇayati - svāvinābhūtatayā nirantaraṃ svīkuryādeva, tāvat- jñeyaṃ tāvadviṣayajātam uktam – prāktanena pādadvayena pratipāditam | ittham- anena iccha|sampattirūpeṇa prakāreṇa labdhādhikāraḥ param — sādhanānuṣṭhānapravṛttyarhatā prāpta eva cetanaḥ, sāṅge– parikarasampattimati, yatra sādhane — bhagavadupāsanākhye yasmin paramopāye (aṣṭāṅga- ṣaḍaṅgākhye bhakti - prapatyoranyatarasmin paramapuruṣaṣanigraha saṅkalpa nivarta ke) adhikurute pravṛti prāpnuyāt tat - tādṛśaṃ paramapuruṣopāsanam iha – etadadhyāyīye. parasmin pādadvande- paścātane tṛtīya- turyākhyapādadvaye, bahubhidā barbaram – tattadvedāntoktabahubhedajuṣṭam, nirbravīti- vaktavya sarvārtha vivaraṇapūrvakaṃ pratipādayati bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ || 2 ||

ekasminneva pāde nipuṇanayakṛtā na dvayostarkaṇaṃ syāt- medosbhedaśca naiko viṣaya iha bhavedanyahānaprasaṅgāt | tasmādasmin prakīrṇā nayavita tiriti prekṣitagranthacodye
vedyāvacchinna vidyāniyamakṛdayamityaidamarthya' samarthyam || 3 ||

nipuṇanayakṛtā — kuśalena nyāyagranthakāreṇa, ekasmin pāde eva nyāyagranthāntaḥ- pātini abhinne caraṇe eva, dvayoḥ tarkaṇam – ubhayorarthayościntanam na syāt – naiva yuktaṃ bhavet, iha — asmin pāde, bhedo'bhedaśca ekaḥ viṣayo na bhavet -vidyānāṃ bhedābhedānyatara- dekaṃ vicāraṇīyārtharūpaṃ na bhavitumarham, anyahānaprasaṅgāt — bhedatarkaṇe abhedasya, abhedatarkaṇe bhedasyeti aparārthasya parityaktatvāpattyā bhedābhedavicārarūpatvā yogāpatteḥ, tasmāt – uktānupapatti- rūpāddhetoḥ, asmin - tṛtīye dāde, nayavitatiḥ– adhikaraṇagaṇayojanā, prakīrṇā saṅkīrṇa- bahvarthaiva ; na tu samagrapādānugataikārthā, iti - evamākāre, prekṣitagranthacoghe – ācārya mukhāt agṛhītārthasya kevalaṃ bhāṣyagranthāvalokinaḥ puruṣasya śaṅkane pravṛtte sati; ayam - tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ, vedyā-makṛditi – tattadguṇaviśeṣaviśiṣṭabrahmarūpavedyārthayuktavidyāviśeṣavyavasthākārī iti, aidamarthyam – nirdiṣṭekā rthakatvam, samarthyam - spaṣṭaṃ sādhanīyam || 3 ||

ākhyāvantaṃ guṇānāṃ nijagadurupasaṃhārataḥ pādameta

vidyaikyārthe tadasminnapavadanatayā bhedacintā prasaktā |
itthaṃ śuśrūṣuśaṅkāmiha śithilayituṃ bhāṣitaṃ bhāṣyakāre-
stadbhedābheda mīmāṃsanamiha 'viṣayastvatra' cokto'nuvṛttaḥ || 4 ||

etaṃ pādam - tṛtīyādhyāyīyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ pādam guṇānāmupasaṃhārataḥ - ekā bhidhānāsu vidyāsu sarvavedāntavākyairavagatānāṃ brahmaguṇānām ekatra saṅgṛhyopāsanopayogāddhetoḥ, ākhyā- vantam — guṇopasaṃhārapāḥ ityabhidhānavantam, nijagaduḥ - ( etadarthacintakā vṛddhāḥ ) vyavajahuḥ, tat tato hetoḥ, vidhaiśyārthe – vidyānāmabhedanirūpaṇatatpare, asmin - tṛtīyacaraṇe, bhedacintā – vidyānāṃ bhedavicāraḥ, apavadanatayā prasaktā - pādapradhāna bhūtārthā svādāṃśapradarśa- nārthamātroddeśataḥ āpatitaḥ ; na pādasvabhāvāyattaḥ ittham – evaṃ prakāram, iha - pādārthaviṣaye pravṛttām, śuśrūṣuśaṅkām — śiṣyahṛdayagatāmāśaṅkām, śithilayitum – parihatum, bhāṣya- kāraiḥ—bhāṣyakṛdbhiḥ bhagavadrāmānujaiḥ, tadbhedābhedamīmāṃsanam — vidyānāṃ bhedābhedayorubhayorapi

tarkaṇameva, iha bhāṣitam — pādārambhe, kartavyatayā spaṣṭamuktam, atra – pāde'smin, anuvṛtto viṣayastu — anugataḥ eko'rthastu, uktaḥ vedyāvacchinna vidyāniyamakṛsvam iti, pūrvaśloke

uktaḥ || 4 |

nimsīmānandanāḍindhamanirupadhikā nantasampadguṇaudhe
vidyā bhedairvibhajya praṇidhiriha yadi prāptirapyaṃśataḥ syāt |
maivaṃ taireva dharmaistaditarasahitaiḥ pūrṇakāmasya pūrṇa
prāpyaṃ brahmaiva nā'nyat kimapi phalamatastatkratunyāya siddhiḥ || 5 ||
nissīma ....duṇauve - niravadhika mahānandātiśayādhāya kasvā bhāvikā paricchinna samṛddhiyuta- kalyāṇaguṇagaṇabharite parabrahmabhūte paramātmani viṣaye vidyābhedai ! – upāsanāviśeṣaḥ, vibhajya- asyāmasyāṃ vidyāyāṃ evamevaṃ guṇaka bhagavadupāsanamityevaṃ vivicya (asaṅkīrṇarūpeṇa), iha praṇidhiryadi – upāyānuṣṭhānakāle avicchinnadhyānasantatirūpamupāsanaṃ kriyate yadi, prāptirapi— upeyasampattirapi, aṃśataḥ syāt – (anantaguṇagaṇasyāpi bhagavataḥ ) yatkicidguṇavadātmake eva bhāge syāt ( yatprakārakamupāsanam ; tatprakāreṇa bhagavadanubhavarūpā prāptiriti tatkratunyāyāt | ) iti śaṅkane, maivam -- evaṃ prakāraṃ śaṅkanamanucitam pūrṇakāmasya – paripūrṇa brahmānubhavakā masyo- pāsituḥ, taiḥ dharmaireva — upāsanakāle brahmaguṇatayā cintitaireva guṇaiḥ, taditarasahitaiḥ– (anubhava- kāle) upāsitetaraguṇasamānādhikaraṇatāpannaiḥ, pūrṇa brahmaiva nityajuṣṭaṃ paraṃ brahmaiva prāpyam - mokṣadaśāyāmanubhāvyatayā - upeyam, nānyat kimapi phalam - paripūrṇa parabrahmānubhavāditarat phalamupāsakasya nāsti, ataḥ anena hetunā, tatkratunyāyasiddhiḥ – tatkratunyāyasya (upāsanānu- bhavakā layoraikarūpyeṇa) nirvāha: || 5 ||

vidhiṣu — codanāparaparyāyeṣu liṅā dighaṭitaśabdeṣu 'yajeta' ityādiṣu bhedaḥ - bhinna- svarūpatā karmakāṇḍadvitīye - pūrvamīmāṃsīya dvitīyādhyāye, śabdāntarādyaiḥ śabdāntara abhyāsa- saṃkhyā-saṃjñā-guṇa-prakaraṇākhyaiḥ ṣar̤abhi: pramāṇaḥ, (nirṇeya iti ) niyamitaḥ vyavasthāpitā, anya :- taditarā abhinnasvarūpatā, samudaya niyatāt— anyonya sahakāreṇa samuccitāt, saṃyogādyaikyataḥ - saṃyoga-rūpa- codanāsskhyānām aviśeṣāt niyamitaḥ vyavasthāpitā, vidyāsu–dahara-vaiśvānarādividyāsu viṣayabhūtāsvapi, saiva nīti: bhedābhedavyavasthāyāṃ kāryāyā karmakāṇḍokta pramāṇānāma valambanaiva sādhanabhūtā; nā'nyā vidyā āśrayaṇīyā, tenaiva tadrītyava lambanenaiva, codanāderabhedāt — codanādīnāmavailakṣaṇyarūpāt pramāpakāt ādau uditam - sūtrakāreṇa prathamata uktaṃ, śākhāntaranayam - vidyānāṃ sarvavedāntapratyayatvam, śrutyaiva ākṣipya - teṣāmevaitāmiti muṇḍakāntaśrutyavalambanata eva śaṅkitānaucityaṃ kṛtvā, tam– svoktaṃ sarvavedānta- pratyayatvameva, bhedakānyārthatoktathā bhedakatvaśaṅkā gocarasya śrutivākyasya tātparyāntaravarṇanena, bhūyaḥ pratisamadhita – punaḥ supratiṣṭhitaṃ cakāra || 6 ||

śākhāsu prakriyā'nyā śravaṇamapi punarduṣṭamātrā viśeṣaṃ

vidyābhedastataḥ syāditi na tadubhayaṃ yuktamadhyetṛbhedāt |
teṣāmeveti vākyāt kvacidupajanitā bhedaśaṅkā tvayuktā

svādhyāye brahmavidyāpadamiha hi ! bhavet tadvatenā'nvayokteḥ || 7 ||
śākhāsu - vibhinnāsu śākhāsu, prakriyā anyā prakaraṇāntaram, (tathā) puna: śravaṇamapi aviśeṣam — abhyāsāparaparyāyam viśeṣāntaraśravaṇarahitaṃ punaḥ śravaṇamapi ca, dṛṣṭam - upalabdham, tataḥ—prakaraṇāntarābhyāsādeḥ vidheyārthabhedakasya upalambhāt, atra vidyābheda:- syāt – akṣara-dahara vaiśvānarādiṣvapi vibhinnaśā khādṛṣṭopāsaneṣu pṛthigvidyātvaṃ sambhāvyameva, iti na – evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ nocitam, tadubhayam – prakaraṇāntaram abhyāsaścatyetaddvayamapi, adhyetṛ- bhedāt yuktam – tattacchākha| viśeṣādhyetṛparamparā janitānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ bhinnatvena (anyathāsiddhatvāt ) yuktam — vidyā bhedakatvasyābhāve'pi saphalameva, kvacit – muṇḍakīyākṣaravidyākhye viṣaya viśepe, teṣāmeveti vākyādupajanitā- 'teṣāmevaitāṃ brahmavidyāṃ vadeta śirotrataṃ vidhivadyastu cīrṇam ' ityevamākārāt antimaśrutāt vākyāt teṣāmeveti avadhāraṇataḥ śirovratānuṣṭhātṛmātra viśrānta- vāvagamena jātā, bhedaśaṅkā tu – udaniṣadantarīyākṣaravidyātaḥ bhinnatvasambhāvanā ayuktānocitā, hi yataḥ, iha - 'teṣāmeve 'ti vākye, brahmavidyāpadam — brahmavidyeti padam, tadvratena—ātharvaṇi kairanuṣṭheyena vedavratabhūtena śirasi aṅgārapātradhāraṇarūpeṇa vratena,

saha, anvayokteḥ' naitadacīrṇavrato'dhīte' ityevaṃ sambandhaśravaṇāt jñāpakāt svādhyāye bhavet – vedaśākhārūpe'rthe eva vācakatayā bhavitumarham ; na tu akṣaravidyākhye muṇḍakaśā khābodhye upāsanaviśeṣe iti na ko'pi doṣaḥ || 7 ||

rūpaikyādaikyasiddhau kimitaradupasaṃhāryamanyo guṇaśced
bhedo na syād vikalpyaṃ tadiha kimaphalā tena cinteti cenna | vaidya | kārakyamaikyaṃ diśati tadadhikaṃ kiñcidākṛṣyate'ṅga
karmaṇyapyevameva pahṛtiviṣayo bhedakāṃśātiriktaḥ || 8 ||

rūpaikyāt—upāsyabhūtasya vaiśvānarādeḥ abhedāt aikyasiddhau – tadupāsanarūpāyā vidyāyā api abhedaniścaye sati, upasaṃhāryam – upāsyarūpādatiriktaṃ, śākhāntarādāhṛtya samuccitya upāsane cintanīyam kimanyat — kiṃvā avaśiṣṭaṃ sambhavati : anyo guṇaścet- vedyākārādatiriktaḥ vedyagataḥ vidyāviśeṣapratiniyataḥ guṇaviśeṣa evopasaṃhāryo yadi tadā, bhedo na syāt – vidyābhedarūpo'rtha eva na sidhyet (sarvasya sarvatropasaṃhārataḥ vidyāsu pratiniyatasya viśeṣasyaiva kasyacana anavaśeṣāt ), tat tasmāddhetoḥ, iha - vidyāviśeṣe, vikalpyam- vidyāntarādanupasaṃhṛtya tatra tatraivopayojyatvena nirṇeyaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ rūpam kim ? – na kimapyastītyā- padyate, tena prāptene śena prasaṅgena, (tattadvidyā pratiniyatasya asādhāraṇasya rūpaviśeṣasya anupa- saṃhāryatāyā avarjanīyatvāt ) cintā - guṇopasaṃhāra - bikalpa nirṇayāya kriyamāṇā vicārarūpā cintā, aphalā iti cet na saprayojanā iti śaṅkanam nana sādhu, (siddhāntārtha : - ) vedyākāraikyam – upāsyaviśeṣaṇa bhūtaguṇā dyātmakasya vedyākārasyaikyam, aikyaṃ diśati - vidhaikyaṃ niścāyayati, (na tūpāsyaikyamātraṃ vidyaikyaṃ diśati) tadadhikam — vidyaikyaniścayādhāyakāt vedyākārādatiriktam, kiṃcit aṅgam - upāsanaupayikam (svasminnaśrutaṃ sat anyatra śrutam ), ākṛṣyate—upasaṃhiyate, karmaṇyapi agnihotrādikarmaṇyapi viṣaye. evameva hi svaśākhāyā- maśrutasyaivāṅgakalāpasya śākhāntarata māharaṇamiti dṛḍham (ataḥ) upahṛtiviṣayaḥ - upasaṃhārāspadaḥ aṃśaḥ, bhedakāṃśātiriktaḥ– bidyākarmaṇoḥ svarūpabhedakāt aṃśādbhinnaḥ aṅgabhūtaḥ aṃśa eveti sārvatrikatayā jñeyam || 8 ||

tatra sarvavedāntapratyayādhikaraṇe anekāsu śākhāsu śrūyamāṇāyāḥ vaiśvānarādikāyāḥ vidyāyāḥ, kima eka vidyātvam ? uta na ? iti saṃśaye,

aviśeṣapunaḥśravaṇasya prakaraṇāntarasya ca bhedakatvāt śākhāntare tayoḥ avarjanīya vidyābheda:- :- ityāśaṅkaya

tvācca,

'vaiśvānaramupāste' iti codanāyāḥ aviśiṣṭatvāt dhyeyaikyāt, ākhyā rūpāviśeṣāt, aviśeṣapunaḥ śravaṇādeḥ pratipattṛbhedanibandhanatvācca na vidyābhedaḥ - ityuktam ||

evaṃ śaṅkane, etat na – uktaṃ vidyayorudgīthākhyayoḥ abhinnasvarūpatvam nocitam udgīthāṃ- śodgīthayoḥ prāṇadṛṣṭyā - chandogeṣu udgīthāvayavabhūte praṇave prāṇadṛṣṭi ; vājasaneyiṣu samaste udgīthe eva prāṇadṛṣṭiḥ iti ubhayatra vailakṣaṇyāt jñāpakāta tathā ca- tathaiva, karma-kartrīḥ prāṇadṛṣṭyā ca – chandogeṣu udgānakarmaṇi prāṇadṛṣṭiḥ, vājasaneyiṣu udgānakartṛbhūte udgātari prāṇadṛṣṭiriti vailakṣaṇyācca, rūpānaikyāt – vidheyagatarūpayoḥ bhinnatvāvagamāt ||

chandogodgīthaśabdastadavayava paraḥ prakramā diprasiddheḥ
kṛtsnodgīthābhiśrāyī prakaraṇaniyamābhāvato vājināṃ syāt | pla udgīthoktiśca naiṣāmupa' caraṇa 'vatī gātari prakramasthā
tatkartrā sādhanīye dviṣadupaśamane tatphalatvottyabādhāt || 10 ||

-1210

chandogodgīthaśabdaḥ chāndogyagatodgīthavidyāprakaraṇe śruta: ' udgītha ' ityayaṃ śabdaḥ, prakramādiprasiddheḥ – upaniṣada upakrame khaṇḍāvasāne ca ' omityedakṣaramudgīthamupāsīta ' ' akṣara- mudgīthamupāste' ityevaṃ 'om' ityevamākārākṣaraparatvāvagamāt tadavayavaparaḥ – udgīthākhyasya sāmāvayavabhaktiviśeṣasya ekadeśabhūto yaḥ praṇavaḥ tattātparyakaḥ iti niścīyate vājinām- - bṛhadāraṇyakīyaḥ udgīthaśabdastu, prakaraṇa niyamābhāvataḥ – chāndogye iva prakaraṇākhyasya niyāmakasyābhāvena niyamasyāpyasiddhyā, (saṅkocā bhāvāt ) kṛtsnodgīthā bhidhāyī syāt - praṇavākhyaikadeśaghaṭitasya samastasyaiva udgīthasya vācako bhavitumarhati (naikadeśasya), eṣām - vājasaneyinām, prakramasthā upakrame śrutā, udgīthoktiśva – 'udgīthenā'tyayāma iti vākyagata: ' udgītha 'śabdaśca gātari upacaraṇavatī na - udgīthasya gātṛbhūte udgātari, aupacārika: ( amukhyaḥ) prayoga iti śaṃkā na bhavati, tatkartrā sādhanīye – udgīthākhya- sāmabhakteḥ udgānakartrā puruṣeṇa sampādye, dviṣadupaśamane asurākhyaśatru parābhavarūpe phale, - tatphalatvotyabādhāt kartrā vyāpāradvārā sādhye phale vyāpāra phalatvasyābāghitatayā udgītha- karaṇakatvokteḥ anupacaritatvasiddheḥ || 10 ||

yadyapyabrahmavidyā paraparibhavanādyahi kārthaprayuktā

na grāhyā mokṣaśāstre tadapi samatayā tatparīkṣeti kecit |
kāmyā vidyā'pyaniṣṭavyapanayanamukhairbrahmā vidyopayuktai-
statatsādhyaprabhedairbhavati samucitā loca netyāhu' ranye || 11 ||

yadyapi vastutaścintyamāne, parapari... prayuktā - svetaraśatrunyakkaraṇādirūpāṇi aihika- - prayojanānyuddiśya pravṛttā, atrahmavidyā brahme ropāsanarūpā iyamudgīthanāmikā vidyā, mokṣaśāstra — niḥśreyasamātropāyabhūtārthavicārarūpe śārīra ke śāstre, na grāhyā - naivopādeyā ; na vicārārhā, tadapi tathāpi, samatayā śrutivihitopāsanarūpatāyā: brahmavidyānām asyāśca aviśiṣṭatayā, tatparīkṣā – udgīthavidyā cintā'pi yuktā, iti kecit evamau paniṣada koṭi-

praviṣṭā eva kecana abhiprayanti | anye apare ca tādṛśāḥ, kāmyā vidyāspi - phala- saṅginā phalaviśeṣa kāmanayā anuṣṭhānārthā'pīyamudgīthavidyā, aniṣṭavyapanayanamukhaiḥ vidyā- siddhirūpeṣṭavirodhipāpākhyaśatrunirasanaprabhṛtibhiḥ, brahmavidyopayuktaiḥ brahmopāsanaupayikaiḥ, tatta- tsādhyaprabhedaiḥ tattadupāsakāpekṣyamāṇaiḥ phalaviśeṣaiḥ, (aihikaphalaviratairapi anuṣṭheyatāṃ pratipadyata iti hetoḥ) samucitālocanā bhavatīti – vicāraṇaucityavatī sampadyata iti, āhu:- vadanti || 11 ||

ajñāta brahmatatva: kathamiva vimṛśet kutracid brahmadṛṣṭi
tasmāt tattādṛśīnāṃ samucitamagateratra mīmāṃsanaṃ syāt | ādadhyuḥ karmaṇāṃ ca svaphalavitaraṇe vīryavattvātirekaṃ
brahmadhyānārthakarmātiśayajananataḥ prastutāpekṣitaṃ tat || 12 ||
ajñātabrahmatattvaḥ— brahmarūpaparatattvaviṣayaka jñānarahitaḥ puruṣaḥ, kutracit - brahmaṇa itaratra yatra kutrā'pi udgīthāvayavapraṇavādau, brahmadṛṣṭim - brahmabhāvanāma, kathamiva vimṛśet — kena vā upāyena āśrayet ? ( āropadharmiṇo'jñāne iva, āropyamāṇājñāne'pi āropāsiddheḥ; (na hi sarpājñāne rajjau sarpabuddhirjāyeta !) tasmāt — vinā brahmajñānaṃ dṛṣṭayupāsanānāmasiddhatvāt hetoḥ, agateḥ – gatyantaravirahāt, tattādṛśīnām — abrahmaṇi brahmadṛṣṭirūpāṇāmāsā vidyānām atra – mokṣārthacintāpravṛtte śārīra ke eva, mīmāṃsanam - vicāraṇam, samucitaṃ syāt - yuktaṃ bhavet | (etā: udgīthādividyā : ) karmaṇām – upāsanāṅgabhūtānāṃ karmaṇām, svaphala vitaraṇe vīryavatvātirekam – vidyāsiddhipratibandhaka nivṛtti-vidyā samṛddhayā diphalajanane kārye, vīryavattvāti- rekam – apratibaddhaśaktikatvarūpaṃ vīryātiśayam ādadhyuḥ – sampādayeyuḥ, tat – uktāddhetoḥ brahma......... jananataḥ — paramātmopāsanaupayika karmasāguṇyotpādanāt prastutāpekṣitam- prakṛtaśārīrakaśāstralakṣya siddhayupayogyevedaṃ śārīra ke dṛṣṭivivicintanamiti || 12 ||

10-

āmnātayoḥ udgīthavidyayoḥ, adhyasta prāṇabhāvodgīthaviṣayatvāt rūpādyaviśeṣāt, ekavidyātvam-

ityāśaṅkya,

chāndogye, 'omityetadakṣaramudgīthamupāsīta' iti prastutasyaiva praṇavasya prāṇadṛṣṭyā upāsyatvāt ; vājasaneyake tu, kṛtsnodgīthaviṣayatvācca rūpabhedāt — vidyābhedaḥ - ityuktam ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ

jyaiṣṭhya śreṣṭhyādisāmye kvacana samadhikaṃ bhāti vāsiṣyayapūrva tenetthaṃ rūpabhedādū bahunigamagatā bhidyatāṃ prāṇavidyā |
maivaṃ ; bāgāditattadguṇaparavaśatā varṇanasyā'viśeṣād
bāgādyaiḥ svasvadharmopacaraṇamakṛtaṃ 'tāvatā' syānna bhedaḥ || 24 ||
jyeṣṭha-śreṣṭhayādisāmye jyeṣṭhatva śreṣṭhatvaprabhṛtīnāṃ mukhyaprāṇagatānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ chāndogya-

kvacana – kauṣītakī -

vājasaneyaka kauṣītakī śrutiṣu upāsyaguṇatayā pratipatteḥ aviśeṣe'pi śrutivyatiriktaśrutidvaye, vāsiṣṭhyapūrvam – vasiṣṭhatvādikaṃ guṇajātam,

samadhikaṃ bhāti

kauṣota kī śrutipratipannaguṇāpekṣayā atiriktaṃ sat pratipannaṃ bhavati, tena – sarvasyopāsya guṇasya sarvatra pratipattyabhāvakṛtena vaiṣamyeṇa, ittham — uktena prakāreṇa, rūpabhedāt – upāsyaguṇatmake rūpe vailakṣaṇyasatvāt, bahunigamagatā - nānā vedaśākhāsthā, prāṇavidyā prāṇopāsanarūpā vidyā bhidyatām – mitho'tirekaṃ prāpnotu nāma ; ( atireka niścāyakasattvāt ) iti pūrvapakṣe, maivam- itthaṃ śaṅkanaṃ nocitam ; vāgādi.... varṇanasya vākū prabhṛti tattatprāṇagatānāṃ sarveṣāṃ guṇānāṃ mukhyaprāṇāyattatāpratipādanasya aviśeṣāt – sarvāsu śrutiṣu samānatvāt, vāgādyaiḥ-vākprabhṛtibhi- rindriyaiḥ, svasvadharmopa caraṇam – sveṣāṃ sveṣāṃ vasiṣṭhatva - pratiṣṭhātvādidharmāṇāṃ mukhyaprāṇa- sambandhitvavarṇanarūpamupacaraṇaṃ chāndogya vājasaneyayoḥ dṛśyamānama, akṛtam — kauṣītakyāṃna kṛtam, tāvatā – tāvanmātreṇa bhedaḥ na syāt - prāṇavidyāyā mithovyatirekaḥ sambhāvyo na bhavati || 24 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||
sarvābhedādhikaraṇe rūpabhedavidhānataḥ |
prāṇavidyā bhedaśaṅkā'podyate'ntargatatvataḥ || 131 ||

iti sarvābhedādhikaraṇam ||

10-

nirūpayiṣyati bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ, prakaraṇapaṭhitāt anyat – tattadvidyāprakaraṇe āmnātādati- riktam, vidyāntarasya rūpam - vidyāntarasambandhi brahmaguṇātmakaṃ rūpam, anyatra na yojyam- vidyāntare yojanāṃ nāīti tasmāt — vidyāntaraprakaraṇasthasya vidyāntare yojanasya anucitatvāt, satyatvapūrvā satyatva- jñānatvānantatvānandatvādayo brahmaguṇā api taditaraguṇavat - tatadvidyā- pratiniyataśvena sampratipannā anyānye guṇā iva, vyavasthāpanīyāḥ syuḥ – ānandamaya vidyā mātra - viśrāntāśvintanīyāḥ ; na tu avyavasthayā sarvavidyānugatayā jñeyā bhaveyuḥ, (iti śaṅkane), maivam — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ nocitaṃ bhavati hi yataḥ, iha — brahmavidyāprasanne, yataḥ — yāśāddharma- jātāt, brahmasvarūpāvagatiḥ upāsyabrahmasvarūpasyaiva avagamo bhavati ; na ( brahmasambandhitā- mātre paryavasānam ) tat tādṛśaṃ brahmaguṇabhūtaṃ dharmajātam, sarvāsu aviśeṣeṇa brahmavidyāsāmānye, apekṣyam – upāsyatāvaccheda kī bhūta brahmaguṇatayā āśrayaṇīyam || 14 ||

satyatvaṃ viśvahetau bahuvidhacidacidvikriyājā lahāneḥ
jñānatvaṃ jñātṛbhāvāt svarabahulatayā svaprakāśatvataśca | tridvaye kābhistu sarva pramitamiha paricchittimirbrahmaṇo'nyat

tasyā'nantyaṃ viyogāt tisṛbhirapi sadā nirmalānandadhāmnaḥ || 15 ||

viśvahetau — jagannirūpita nimittatvopādānatvākhyadvividhakāraṇatvavati parasmin brahmaṇi, bahu .... jālahāne :- nānāprakārasya cetaneṣvacetaneṣu ca kāraṇatvavatsu paridṛśyamānasya vikārākhya- doṣajātasya asparśāt, satyatvam - ( śrutistha ) satyaśabdābhidheyatvam, jñānatvam - jñānapada- vācyatvam, jñātṛbhāvāt – jñānaṃ prati āśrayatvāt ( jñānam asya astīti jñānam iti arśa ādyacpratyayāntaḥ), svaprakāśatvataśca – svayaṃprakāśatvācca ( jñānapadavācyatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ ), (ubhayamidam ) svarabahulatayā – jñānaśabde antodāttasvarasya vaikalpikatvena hetunā sidhyati, ( antodāntasvaravatā jñānaśabdena nityāsaṃkucitajñānadharmakatva siddhiḥ ; tadrahitena ca tena svayaṃprakāśatvasiddhiriti ) brahmaṇo'nyat sarvam — brahmātiriktaṃ cetanā cetanātmakaṃ sarvamapi, tridvayekābhiḥ paricchittibhiḥ- deśa - kāla-vastukṛtai tribhiḥ paricchedaiḥ, deśa- vastvāyattābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ paricchedābhyām, kālakunāccai kasmāt paricchedāt iha pramitam - asmin vākye paricchinnatvabodhanataḥ vyāvartitam (prakarṣeṇa mitam – pramitam ) sadā nirmalānandadhāmnaḥ tasya—kālatraye'pi heyapratyanīka kalyāṇakatānasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ, tisṛbhirapi viyogāt- triprakārābhirapi paricchittibhiḥ viśleṣāt ānantyam - śrutigatānantapadavācyatvam iti (' satyaṃ jñānamananta' miti śodhakavākyena kāraṇatvaśaṅkitadoṣanivartakena siddham ) || 15 ||

uktaṃ janmā disūtre nanu nikhila jagaddhetunā brahmalakṣma syāt tenaiva svarūpāvagatiriha mudhā satyatādīti cenna | hetorīśasya hetvantaragata vividhāvadya varga prasa

śaṅkārūḍhe krameṇetara vibhajanatastasya sāphalyasiddheḥ || 16 || janmādisūtre śārīra kī ye ' janmādyasya yataḥ' ityasmin sūtre, jagaddhetutā viśvanidānatvam brahmalakṣma— brahmalakṣaṇamiti, ukta nanu - ( ' yato vā imānītyādi śrutitaḥ) varṇitaṃ khalu tenaiva - jagajjanmādikāraṇatvenaiva lakṣaṇabhūtena brahmasvarūpāvagatiḥ syāt—dhyeyasya brahmasvarūpasya jñānaṃ bhavatyeveti, iha - ānandā adhikaraṇe, satyatādi - ( 'satyaṃ - jñānamanantaṃ brahmeti śrutimādāya ) satyatva - jñānatvānantatvādi lakṣaṇasya (brahmāvagatyartha ) varṇanam, mudhā iti cet — vyarthameveti śaṅkanam na-nocitama, hetoḥ īśasya jagatkāraṇa bhūtasya paramātmanaḥ, hetvantara .... prasaṅge– lokaparidṛṣṭakāraṇāntaravartinānāprakāra doṣajātāpādane, śaṅkā- rūḍhe sambhāvanāspade sati krameṇa ānupūrvyeṇa, krameṇa - ānupūryeṇa, itara vibhajanataḥ – acetana saṃsṛṣṭebhyo baddhacetanebhyaḥ, prakṛtisaṃsargamuktebhyaścetanebhyaḥ, aspṛṣṭaprakṛtisambandhebhyo nityebhyaśca sakāśāt brahmaṇi pārthakyabodhanadvārā, tasya - ihodāhṛtasya ( ānandaballī gatavākyapratipannasya ) satyatvādi- guṇajātasya (svarūpanirūpakasya ), sāphalyasiddheḥ sārthakatālābhāt || 16 ||

nanvādhyānaṃ priyādyairapi bhavati śiraḥ - pakṣa - puccha| dirūpaiḥ
bāḍhaṃ tatra priyādyaistadavagatirataste tu sarvānuvṛttāḥ |
pucchādyaśo niraṃśe na bhavati na ca taddṛṣṭirutkṛṣṭatatve
tasmāccityāgnirūpakramavadiha kṛtaṃ rūpaṇaṃ brahmaṇi syāt || 17 ||

ādhyānam — brahmaṇo'nucintanam śiraḥpakṣapucchādirūpaiḥ – śirastva - pakṣasva - pucchatvādinā kālpanikenākāreṇa rūpyamāṇaiḥ, priyādyairapi priya. moda-pramodānanderapi ānandāṃśa- bhūtaiḥ bhavati nanu - saṃpanna bhavatyeva khalu, ( evaṃ ca satyatvādeḥ yathā sarvopāsanānvayitvaṃ tathā priyāderapi sarvopāsanānvaye, ko vā virodha: ? ) ityākṣepe, bāḍham – ukto'rthaḥ aṅgīkārya eva, tatra — ānande ekadeśatayā vartamānaiḥ, priyādyaiḥ– priya moda-pramodānandaiḥ, tadavagatiḥ– ānandamayaparabrahmasvarūpaniścayaḥ sampadyate, ataḥ - asmādeva ca hetoḥ, te-priyādyā arthāḥ, sarvānuvṛttāḥ tu sarvāsu vidyāsu anugāmina eva, (athā'pi ) niraṃśe – niravayave, brahmaṇi,

pucchādyaṃśaḥ – vālādirūpaḥ paricchinno bhāgaviśeṣaḥ,

na bhavati naiva avasthito bhavet,

(tathaiva) utkṛṣṭatattve sarvotkṛṣṭe paratantvabhūte brahmaṇi, tadddaṣṭiśca – nikarṣāśrayapucchapakṣāditvā -

śepataḥ upāsanamapi, nanaiva yujyate (phalaviśeṣāptaye nikṛṣṭe utkṛṣṭadṛṣṭereva dṛṣṭayupāsaneṣu darśanena ukṛṣṭe nikaṭadṛṣṭeḥ śāstrādanavagate) tasmāt uktāddhetoḥ, iha – ānandavallī- gatanandamaya brahmanirūpaṇāvasare, brahmaṇi kṛtaṃ rūpaṇam - paramātmaviṣaye kālpanika tathā pravartitaṃ priya śirastvādivarṇanam cityāgnirūpakramavat – (pūrvavikalpādhikaraṇe) vidyāmaya kratvanupraviṣṭe manaścidvācidādau yena krameṇa kriyāmayakatvanupraviṣṭacayanārhābhitvasya rūpaṇaṃ kriyate, tameva kramamāśritya syāt — sampannaṃ bhavet || 17 ||

ānandasvapradhānaṃ katicidiha viduḥ sautramānandaśabdaṃ
dharmānandābhidhānaṃ tadubhayavacanaṃ ceti paśyanti kecit |
jñānokto 'caitadevaṃ' taditarasamatā yāvatā syānna śaṅkayā

tāvaddharmānuvṛttirvahubhajanapade brahmaṇi sthāpyate'tra || 18 ||

iha ānandādyadhikaraṇe, sautram ' ānandādayaḥ pradhānasye' tisūtrastham ānanda- śabdam -' ānande 'ti prātipadikam ānandatva pradhānam - brahmasvarūpagatānandatvarūpabhāva- prādhānyavat iti, katicit viduḥ kecana vyākhyātāro jānanti kecit — apare ca kecana, dharmānandābhidhānam – bhagavatsvarūpadharmabhūto ya ānandaḥ tadvācakamiti kecidāhuḥ, tadubhayavacanaṃ veti-tantrataḥ svarūpagatasyānandatvasya dharmabhūtasyānandasyeti dvividhasyāpyarthasya vācakamiti, kecit paśyanti — anye ca kecana vyākhyātāro jānanti etat svarūpagatajñānatvarūpadharma- paratvaṃ dharmabhūtajñānaparatvaṃ, tadubhayaparatvaṃ ceti abhiprāyavaividhyam, jñānoktau ca satyaṃ jñānamiti jñānapade'pi sautrādiśabdollekhye, evam - ānandaśabdavadeva vartate, yāvatā - yāvatā svarūpa- nirūpakadharmajātena brahmaṇi pratipāditena taditarasamatā - brahmā tiriktacetanācetanatulyatā, na śaṅkayā syāt — brahmaṇi prasañjanārdā na bhavet, tāvaddharmānuvṛttiḥ tāvatāṃ viśeṣaṇa bhūtānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ upāsyatāvacchedakatayā'nvayaḥ, bahubhajanapade – nānopāsanagocare, brahmaṇi – parasmin hmaṇi, atra sthāpyate— asmin adhikaraṇe dṛḍhīkriyate || 18 ||

-0-

sarvavidyāsu guṇino brahmaṇa ekatvāt guṇānāṃ ca guṇyapṛthagbhāvāt mānandādīnāṃ svarūpanirūpakatvāca ānandādayaḥ sarvavidyānuyāyinaḥ ityuktam ||

ācāmet iti 'tasmādevaṃvit aśiṣyan ācāmet, aśitvā cācāmet ' iti vākyena vidhīyamānam, iha vidheḥ – atra vākye liṅkhapavidhipratyayaśravaṇāt prāṇavidyāvataḥ - prāṇo. pāsanādhikṛtasya puruṣasya vihitam, apūrvācamanaṃ syāt – smṛtisiddhādācamanādbhinnaṃ nūtnameva ācamanākhyaṃ karma bhavet, (iti śaṅkane) maivam – īdṛśaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na sādhu, smṛtyādi siddhaḥ anuvihitā - smṛtyācārābhyāṃ prāptamācamana karmānusṛtya vidheyatāṃ prāpitā, prāṇavāsastvadṛṣṭiḥ param- – ācamanīyāsvapsu prāṇaṃ prati vāsastvānusandhānarūpā dṛṣṭireva (aprāptā), prāptadhātvartha- niṣṭhaḥ - śāstrāntarato labdhe dhātvarthe anvayitayā bhāsamāna: vidhirapi - vidhipratyayo'pi, bhuñjītetyādinītyā- 'snātvā bhuñjīta' ityatra yathā rāgaprāptabhojanānuvādataḥ pūrva snānā- nuṣṭhānasya niyama vidhāveva paryavasyati tadvat, ghaṭate — aviruddhaḥ saṅgato bhavati, ; prāk- ānandādyedhikaraṇataḥ pūrvasminnadhikaraṇe, prāṇavidyā prāṇe jyaiṣṭhaya. śreṣṭhayādyupāsanātmikā vidyā, uktā cintitā, asyāḥ aṅgaṃ tu — prāṇavidyāyā aṅgabhūtamācamana viśiṣṭa vāsa- stvānusandhānaṃ tu, tadidam – pūrvaprasaktameva sat avasare cintitam – balavatyāḥ śaṅkāyāḥ ānandādyadhikaraṇataḥ samādhānālābhena ucite samaye labdhe sati vicāritamasti || 19 ||

ādāvante ca vāsaḥ paridhirabhihito manyatiścā'tra dṛṣṭacai
sā cā''rādhya priyārthā stutiriha na bhaved 'gatyabhavā 'bhinandyā | yuktaścā'pūrva bhāvāt paridadhati girā tadvidhānābhisandhiḥ
prāṇaśvārādhanīyaḥ parihitavasano yujyate sadbhiradbhiḥ || 20 ||

ādau ante ca – upakrame upasaṃhāre ca, vāmaḥ paridhiḥ abhihitaḥ kiṃ me bāsaḥ ' iti vāsaḥ śabdena, 'adbhiḥ paridadhati' iti paridadhatinā ca paridhānarūpo'rtha uktaḥ, atra manyatiśca–asyāṃ prāṇavidyāyāṃ 'etadanamananaṃ kurvanto manyante' iti anusandhānārthako man dhātuśca, dṛṣṭacai—anusandhāna rūpopāsanabodhikā satyapi dṛṣṭirūpatāyā bodhikā'pi vartate, sā ca dṛṣṭiśva, ārādhya priyārthā - upāsyāyāḥ prāṇadevatāyāḥ priyatvāpādanaphalā, gatya- bhāvābhinandyā—gatyantaravirahe evāśrayaṇīyā, stutirapi - vāsastvānusandhānasya praśaṃsāmātre viśrāntirapi iha na bhavet - atra āśrayaṇīyā na bhavitumarhati, ( - atra āśrayaṇīyā na bhavitumarhati paridadhati girā – 'adbhiḥ paridadhati' iti vacasā, tadvidhānābhisandhiḥ - vāsastvānusandhāna vidhitva nirṇayo'pi, apūrva- bhāvāt yuktaḥ — pūrvamaprāptatvāt nyāyya eva, ārādhanīyaḥ - prīṇanīyaḥ, prāṇaśca -- prāṇākhyo

devatā viśeṣo'pi adbhiḥ parihitavasanaḥ anūpaparidhānatā pannavasanāśrayaḥ san sadbhiḥ yujyate satpuruṣa kartṛkayogaviṣayo bhavati || 20 ||

eva iti śaṅkane, naiva na caivedaṃ yuktam, yasmāt yato hetoḥ, baśitā - vṛhadāraṇyako ko vaśitvākhyo guṇaḥ, saṅkalpasatyatā vitatiḥ agmirahasyoktāyāḥ satyasaṅkalpatāyāḥ kāryatvarūpā vistṛtireveti rūpabhedābhāvāt na vidyābhedaḥ iti ||

jñāpanīyaśca anyaḥ kaśvidguṇaḥ na - aparaśca kaścana guṇaviśeṣo neva vartate, (yena jñāpitena dvaitīyīkaḥ pāṭhaḥ sārthakaḥ syāt) tasmāt -- adhyetṛbheda - vidheyabhedayorabhāvāt iha punaḥ uktā- viśeṣaśravaṇam - pūrvamuktasyaiva aviśeṣataḥ punaḥśravaṇarūpaṃ bhedakatvena prakaraṇe'smin jñāyamānam, vidyām - śāṇḍilyavidyām kiṃ na vibhindyāt 1 – kuto vā atiriktasvarūpāṃ na jñāpayet !, (iti pūrvoktopariśaṅkane) maivam – nocitamidaṃ śaṅkanam, yadyapi vastutaḥ asyāḥ anūkti:- uktāyā evāsyā vidyāyāḥ sthalāntare punarvacanarūpo'nuvādaḥ, guṇavidhaye na tu kalpate- guṇaviśeṣasya vidhānāya naiva prabhavati, tathāpi - ajñātaguṇaviśeṣopadeśarūpaprayojanābhāve'pi, vyasana- samasana nyāyataḥ - 'iṣṭaṃ hi viduṣāṃ loke samāsavyāsadhāraṇam iti nyāyāt, vyaktyai saukaryataśca – (vibhajyoktiḥ ) abhivyaktivaiśadya rtham (saṅkṣipyoktiḥ ) grahaṇadhāraṇa- saukaryārtha ceti (anūktiḥ saphaleti eaikyasiddheḥ – ekasyāmeva śākhāyāṃ sthānadvayapaṭhiyāyā asyā vidyāyā abhedanirṇayalābhāditi || 22 ||

chandogairvājibhiśca sphuṭamanupaṭhinā bhāti śāṇḍilya vidyā
bhedābhedāvamastviha kimiti na sandarśito bhāṣyakāraḥ |
tad brūmo yatra yatrā'dhikaparipaṭhanaṃ tatra tatrā'dhikānā-
mantarbhāvādiyuktā 'vanadhikamadhikaṃ' veti sādhāraṇoktaḥ || 23 ||

chandogaiḥ vājibhiśva – sāmaśākhīyaiḥ śuklayajuḥ śākhīyaiśca śāṇḍilyavidyā- śāṇḍilyākhyā vidyā, sphuṭam atirohitā satī, anupaṭhitā bhāti chāndogye vājasaneyake ca adhītā prakāśate, iha - etacchākhāsthayoḥ śāṇḍilyavidyayorviṣaye, bhedābhedāvamarśaḥ bhinnatvā bhinnatvavicāraḥ, bhāṣyakāraiḥ śrībhāṣyakṛdbhiḥ rāmānujāyaiḥ kimiti kiṃ vā nimittamāśritya na sandarśitaḥ– vicāraṇīyatayā na prakaṭitaḥ, tad brūmaḥ - tatrocitaṃ samādhānaṃ varṇayāmaḥ, yatra yatra – yasmin yasmin śākhāviśeṣe, adhikaparipaṭhanam – vaśitvāderadhikasyāṃśasya āmnānaṃ vartate, tatra tatra – tasmiṃstasmisthale, adhikānām itaratrānāmnātānāṃ vaśitvādīnām, antarbhāvādiyuktau – āmnāte satyasaṅkalpatvādo kāryatvakāṃśatvādinā praviṣṭatvādiyoge sambhavati sati, anadhikamadhikaṃ veti sādhāraṇokteḥ paṭhitaṃ rūpamatiriktaṃ vā anatiriktaṃ vā iti vicārapratipādikāyā uktaḥ sarvasthalānvayyuktirūpatvāt, tatrāpyanenaiva vicāreṇa nirṇaya- lābhāt || 23 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṃgrahaḥ ||

samānādhikaraṇe vājasaneyake'mirahasye bṛhadāraṇyake ca śāṇḍilyavidyā āmnāyate | tāmirahasye, satyasaṅkalpatvādiviśiṣṭa upāsyaḥ | itaratra vaśitvādiguṇaka iti, rūpabhedā- dvidyā bhedaḥ - ityāśaṅkaya,
ubhayatrā'pi manomayatvādike samāne sati, vaśitvādeśva satyasaṅkalpatvaguṇādabhinnatvāt rūpabhedābhāvāt na vidyā bhedaḥ -- ityuktam ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||
akṣyādityopalakṣye bhagavati bhajanaṃ codanāderabhedā-
dekaṃ syāttena nāmnoraniyatiriti na sthānato rūpabhedāt | sthānaṃ tatsthatvabuddhayai dhupadiśati na cet syānna rūpātideśa-
stasmādarkākṣiyogādaharai miti tannāmanī sthāpanīye || 24 ||

akṣyādityopalakṣye – akṣi- ādityamaṇḍala rūpasthānābhyāṃ (candrasya śākhāpravat ) upalakṣaṇabhūtābhyāṃ jñāpye, bhagavati – paramātmani viṣaye, bhajanam – upāsanam, codanādeḥ abhedāt — vidheḥ phalayogasya, rūpasya, ākhyāyāśca aviśiṣṭatvāddhetoḥ, ekaṃ syāt - abhinnameva bhavet, tena - upāsanasyaikatvena hetunā, nāmnoḥ– ' ahaḥ ahaṃ' iti nāmnoḥ avyavasthitatvameva, iti na evaṃ śaṅkā na kāryā, sthānataḥ rūpabhedāt – upāsanasthāna (bheda)- kṛtāt vidyāsambandhino rūpasya bhinnatvāt sthānam - bhagavataḥ akṣirūpam ādityamaṇḍala rūpaṃ ca sthānam, tatsthatvabuddhayai hi— akṣyā dityarūpasthānāvasthitatvapratipattaye khalu, upadiśati— śrutirjñāpayati, na cet — vidyābhedaḥ śrutitātparyāviṣayaḥ syādyadi, rūpātideśaḥ na syāt- ' tasyaitasya tadeva rūpaṃ yadamupyarūpam' iti rūpasyātideśaḥ śrutau na kriyamāṇaḥ syāt, tasmāt—uktayā rītyā vidyayorbhedāt, arkākṣiyogāt – ādityarūpasthānayogāt, akṣi- rūpasthānayogācca, ahaḥ aham iti tannāmanī - ' ahaḥ' iti vidyāyā ekaṃ nāma, ' aham iti ca ekaṃ nāma vidyayoḥ yathākramam, sthāpanīye vyavasthayā upasaṃhartavye || 24 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||
atha sambanvādhikṛtau rūpabhedābhimānataḥ |
akṣyarka vidyayā'podyate sthānabhedataḥ || 135 |:

iti sambandhādhikaraṇam || 7 ||

paṭhanābhāvataḥ - arthaviśeṣaṃ pradhānabhūtaṃ prakramya bhāgnātasya anupalabdheḥ sarvavidyāsu anvīyeta— sarvāsvapi brahmavidyāsu brahmaguṇatayā upasaṃhiyeta iti cet ! na evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na yuktam, agatikato liṅgataḥ - anyathāsiddhaṃ liṅgamāśritya kvacit – kvacidevā'nvaya iti, sthāpita - svāt pūrvamīmāṃsāyāmeva asandigdhaṃ samarthitatvāt, alpasthānāsu vidyāsu - manuṣyahṛdayādi- rūpātyantaparimitapradeśavartitayā paramātmopāsanarūpāsu daharādividyāsu, aghaṭitavapuṣaḥ — anvayā- naī svarūpāyāḥ, dhuvyāpteḥ dyulokavyāptirūpāyāḥ sthiteḥ, khocitasthānavṛtteḥ pṛthivyanta- rikṣadyulokātmaka sarvasthānasambandhasvābhāvyapratipateḥ avarjanīyatvāt, ekamantre sahapaṭhanavaśāt- ekasminneva brahmajyeṣṭheti mantre vīryasambhṛti - dyuloka vyāpyoḥ samānādhikaraṇatvabodhakāt āmnānāt jñāpanā, anye vīrya sambharaṇādayaḥ dhutryāptita itare guṇāḥ sarve, tatsamasthāninaḥ— dhanyātirūpaguṇāśrayatayā pratipadyamānatrahmāśrayaguṇapratipādaka vidyānvayā eva || 25 ||

puruṣavidyādhikaraṇam || 3-3-9|| śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṃgrahaḥ ||

vi

vidyābhedaḥ satyapi saṃjñaikye puruṣavidyeti |

YOUR

savanādiyajñasaṃparikalpanabhedakṛta' rūpabhedatvāt' || 29 ||

puruṣavidyeti saṃjñaikye satyapi - ' puruṣavidyā ' ityevaṃrūpāyā: vidyākhyāyāḥ ekākāratve vidyamāne'pi, vidyā medaḥ taittirīyaka - chāndogyagatayoḥ puruṣavidyayoḥ nānātvameva ; na ekatvam, savanādi ... medatvāt – chāndogye tredhā vibhakte puruṣāyuṣādau savanādikalpanam ; taittirīye sāyaṃ- prātarādau tatkarūpanaṃ, yajamāna - patnyā ditaḥ yajñādikalpanamiti kalpanā dibhedakṛtasya rūpabhedasya sattvāt || 29 |

- spaṣṭa rūpādibhede iṭhasamupanato nāmasāmyādimātrāt puṃvidyāpūrvapakṣo mṛduriti viphalā'dhikriyaiṣeti cenna | anyaiva nyāsavidyāprakaraṇapaṭhitā tadvidhānapradhāne-
tyasvātantryādisiddhyai vibhajanamanayorityatīvā'rthavaśvāt || 27 ||

rūpādibhede - (taittirīya chāndogyagatayoḥ puruṣavidyayorubhayorapi ) mitho bhedāvagamake rūpādito vaiṣamye, spaṣṭe – sphuṭapratipanne sati, nāmasāmyādimātrāt – bhavilakṣaṇākhyā karavaṃ kevalamādāya, iṭhamamupanataḥ — āpātapratīti mātrā patitaḥ, puṃvidyāpūrvapakṣa: – puruṣavidyā sambandhī adhikaraṇāṅgabhūto'yaṃ pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ, mṛduriti — kṣodakṣamo na bhavatītyataḥ, eṣā adhikriyā- puruṣavidyā dhikaraṇamidaṃ śārīrakīyam, niṣphalā iti cet — vyartheti śaṅkanam, na na sādhu bhavati, nyāsavidyāprakaraṇapaṭhitā-nyāsākhyāyāḥ vidyāyāḥ (brahmavidyāyāḥ) upakramapūrvaka - māmnātā, tadvidhanapradhānā - prādhānyena nyāsavidyopadeśāya pravṛttā puruṣavidyā, anyaiva- sarvathā bhinnaiva taittirīyagatā, iti asvātantryādisiddhayai – ( chāndogyīya puruṣa vidyāryā svatantratāyāḥ pūrṇāyuravāyā dipṛthakphalādeḥ) evaṃ taittirīyagatāyāṃ para vidyāśeṣatai ka svarūpatva- phala pārthakyābhāvādeḥ arthaviśeṣasya lābhāya, anayoḥ

puruṣavidyayoḥ pārthakyajñāpanamiti hetunā, atīva artha baccāt - asyā'dhikaraṇasya sarvathā sārthakyāt || 27 ||

yadyevaṃ yajñadṛṣṭiḥ paravidi puruṣe codyate sānubandhe
yajñasyā'naṅgabhūtaṃ kathamiha vividhaṃ kalpyate tatra tatra |
tasmāt prakrāntavidyā stutiriyamucitetyāhureke'nyathā'nye

tiṣṭhatvetadvivāpi prakṛta' sughaṭitā' sampradāyastu cintyaḥ || 28 ||
sānubandhe – antarbāhyākhyaparikarasahite, para vidi— paramātmaviṣayaka vedanasampanne niṣpa nopāsane, puruṣe cetane (adhiṣṭhānabhūte), yajñadṛṣṭiḥ– yajña (sva) kalpanā, yadi codyate - śrutyā yadi vidhīyate ; tarhi, yajñasya anaṅgabhūtam – kaṃcana yāgaviśeṣaṃ prati aṅgatāmanāpannaṃ ( svayameva pradhānabhūtaṃ darśapūrṇamāsādikaṃ) vividham nānāprakāraṃ karmajātam, iha – yajñamadhye, tatra tatra- ' te cāturmāsyāni ya ṛtavaste paśubandhāḥ' ityāditattadvākyakhaṇḍe, kathaṃ kalpyate 1 – kena vā prakāreṇa ( kāṃ vā yogyatāmāśritya ) adhyāsataścintā viṣayatāmāpadyate ?,

tasmāt — uktāt

vaighaṭyāt, iyam –'tasyaivaṃ viduṣaḥ' ityādirūpā kalpanātatiḥ, ucitā – vidyāpraroca- naupayikī, prakrāntavidyāstutiḥ - ārabdhāyāḥ nyāsavidyāyāḥ kevalā praśaṃsā, iti eke āhuḥ – evaṃrītyā kecana vyākhyātāro vivṛṇvate, anye anyathā apare ca prakārāntareṇa śruteḥ viṣaya nirūpaṇe aucityāntarānapekṣaṃ svātantryaṃ varṇayanti prakṛtasughaṭitā – prakṛtārthānanurodhitva- rahitā, etadvidhā'pi tiṣṭhatu ādau anantaraṃ coktā ubhayavidhā'pi rītirāstāṃ nāma, sampradāyastu cintyaḥ – nyāsa vidyāprakaraṇārthaviṣayaka : pāramparikaḥ prakāra viśeṣaḥ param atra samyakcintanīyaḥ ; (yaḥ viśiṣṭādvaitina śrīvaiṣṇavānāmātmottāraṇapaṭuriti śāstrāgamayogijanānu- bhavaiḥ sunirṇītaḥ) || 28 ||

pravidhye'tyādimantrā iva, adhyayanāṅgam – adhyayanaśeṣabhūtā evaite mantrāḥ, tathaiva sāmarthyāt - teṣviva atrāpi vidyamānāt mantragatāt liṅgāt || 30 ||
