adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||

yujyeran brahmavidyā parisara paṭhitāḥ śaṃ na ityādimantrā-
stādarthyāt sarvavidyā sviti na taduditāghītiśeṣatvaliṅgāt | cedanye'pi tadvat savidhapaṭhanataḥ santu śukraṃ pravidhye-
tyevaṃprāyāstadarthā na ca ghaṭata idaṃ liṅgato durbalatvāt || 29 ||

E

brahmavidyā parisarapaṭhitāḥ – upāsana pratipāda kopaniṣatsaṃnidhisamāmnātāḥ, ' śaṃna'- ityādimaṃtrāḥ–' śaṃ no mitraḥ ' śukraṃ pravidhye 'syevaṃprabhṛtayo mantrāḥ, tādarthyāt – vidyā- śeṣatvasāmyāt, sarvavidyāsu yujyeran - nikhileṣvapyupāsaneṣu aṅgatayā'nvayino bhavitumarhanti, iti na evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na sādhu, tadu... liṅgāt – tattanmantrapratipannamadhyayanaṃ śeṣatvasādhakāt mantragatātsāmarthyāta, (saṃnidhipāṭhāvagata vidyā śeṣatvasya vighaṭanāt ) no cet — mantravācya- madhyayanaṃ prati aṅgatāṃ parityajya saṃnidhyāyattavidyāśeṣatvasyaivaucityābhyupagame, tadvat-śaṃno mitrādimantravat, anye'pi śukraṃ pravidhyevaṃprāyāḥ - itare'pi ' śukraṃ pravidhya hṛdayaṃ pravidhye '- tyevamādayo'pi mantrāḥ, savidhapaṭhanataḥ kevalasaṃnighipāṭhabalāt, tadarthāḥ santu - vidyāśeṣabhūtā evetyaṅgīkriyantām idaṃ na ca ghaṭate evamaṅgīkaraṇaṃ naiva saṅgacchate, liṅgato durbalatvāt- liṅga|pekṣayā saṃnidhipāṭasya nyūnabalatvāt (prabalasaṃnidhau svakāryakaraṇākṣamatvāt vidyāṅgatvagamakatā- vaighaṭyāta ) sarvavidyāṅgatvakalpanāyāḥ asiddheḥ || 29 ||

hānyadhikaraṇam || 3-3-11||
śārīrakanyāyakala pasaṃgrahaḥ ||
' puṇyādihānyupāyanayorekaṃ cintyaṃ samucitaṃ neha' |
'pṛthagāmnāna' phalatvānnaitat 'sāpekṣatvāt parasparataḥ || 31 ||

puṇyādi.... nayoḥ puṇya-pāpatyāgaḥ anyatra saṃkramaḥ ityanayoḥ brahmaprāpnuvatpuruṣa- sambandhitvena śrutayoḥ arthayormadhye, ekam – anyataradeva, cintyam - upāsanā tathā cintanīyaṃ bhavati, na samuccitam — militamubhayaṃ na cintanīyam, iha – asmin prakaraṇe, pṛthagā ... tvāt - pārthakyenaikaikapāṭhaphalasya vācyatvāt, (iti śaṅkane) naitat idaṃ nocitam parasparataḥ - hānyupāyanayormithaḥ, sāpekṣatvāt — sākāṃkṣatāsatvāt, (anyatreopāyanārthaṃ dānam hānasiddhyartha- - mupāyanam iti parasparāpekṣā ) || 31 ||

prahāṇam manyatropāyanaṃ cetyubhayamapyuktamiti hetoḥ, pṛthagāmnātasamparka siddhiḥ – pratyekaṃ pratyeka- mamihitayordvayorapi melanasya (śrutyā) avagatirbhavati, hi yataḥ, śākhāntarastham - anyasya śākhāyāṃ vidyamānamapi vākyam - śrautaṃ vākyam, vihitākāṅkṣayā – vidhiviṣayayorarthayoḥ mitho'nvayāpekṣayā, vākyaśeṣaṃ bhavati - ( sarvaśākhā pratyayanyāyena ) vākyagatākāṅkṣā pūrakata yā ekavākyatāṃ prāpnuvat nirākāṅkṣabodhādhāyakaṃ bhavati || 30 ||
itthaṃ brahmajñakarmatya jana mitara saṃkrāntisampṛktamastu

syāccintāyāṃ vyavasthā pṛthaganupaṭhanādityasat klaptidauḥ sthyāt | sarveṣāṃ muktibhājāṃ dvitayamapi yathopāstisādhyaṃ samānaṃ
taccintā'sau tathā tanmahimavida iti sthāpanīyobhayatra || 31 ||

ittham - hānopāyanayo: anyonyasāpekṣatvataḥ upāyanavākyasya dānavākyaṃ prati śeṣatvena, brahmajñakarmatyajanam — brahmavidā kriyamāṇaṃ puṇya-pāpayoḥ parityāgarūpaṃ karma, itara.... pṛktamastu-- suhṛd - dviṣato: puṇya pāpayoḥ praveśena saṃvalitaṃ bhavatu nāma, cintāyām – upāsakakartṛkama- vinmahimānusandhānaviṣaye param, vyavasthā syāt - vikalpakṛtā ekaikasya ekatraikatra cintana miti niyama eva paryavasyatu, pṛthaganupaṭhanāt – amilitatayaiva tattacchākhāmnānāt -- (iti śaṅkane), ityasat — evaṃ śaṅkanamasādhu, kluptidauḥsthyāt – cintāyā viṣaye vikalpakalpanā yāḥ nyāyyatvāsiddheḥ, sarveṣāṃ muktibhājām - mokṣaṃ labhamānānāṃ nikhilānāmapi cetanānām, upāsti- sādhyam –brahmavidyāniṣpādyam, dvitayamapi sukṛta- duṣkṛtayoḥ prahāṇam anyatra saṅkramaścetyubhayam, yathā samānam — aviśiṣṭaṃ yathā bhavati, tathā — tatsāmyena, tanmahimavidaḥ - vidyāmāhā- tmyajñasya upāsakasya, asau taccintā – vidyāsādhyārthaviṣayiṇī vidyā māhātmyānusandhānarītiḥ, iti ubhayatra sthāpanīyā evaṃ phale iva sādhane'pi ca hānau pāyanobhayaviṣayakatvena samānatā dṛḍhīkāryā || 31 ||

kartrā tenaiva bhojyaṃ (gyaṃ) śubhamitaraditi sthāpitaṃ karmakāṇḍe
tasmād brahmajñakarma dviṣati suhṛdi vā nāpatedityayuktam | vidyāmāhātmyato yad vigalati viduṣaḥ karma tatsādhya tulyaṃ
vidvatpradveṣa-bhaktyoḥ phalamiti kathane vākyatātparya siddheḥ || 32 ||

tenaiva kartrā – vihitaniṣiddhānuṣṭhātṛtvavatā puruṣeṇaiva śubhamitarat — śubhātmakaṃ
aśubhātmakaṃ vā karma, bhogya miti – phalabhūta sukhādidvārā anubhāvyam anubhāvyameva ceti,

karmakāṇḍe –pūrvamīmāṃsārūpe karmasvarūpādinirṇāyake etacchāstrapūrvabhāge, sthāpitam - vicārya nirṇītam ityatra ), tasmāt — karmaphalabhūtasya puṇyādeḥ katraikānu- bhāvyatā niśvayāt, brahmajñakarma - brahmavitsamavetaṃ puṇya-pāpākhyaṃ karma, suhṛdi dviṣati vā- mitre amitre vā na āpatet naiva saṅkramā bhavet ityayuktam - īdṛśaḥ keṣāñcidāśayaḥ samīcīno na bhavati, vidyāmāhātmyataḥ — upāsanānuṣṭhānamahimnā, viduṣaḥ – upāsituḥ siddhopāyāt puruṣāt, yat karma vigalati - puṇyarūpaṃ pāparūpaṃ vā yat karmapadābhidheyaṃ viśliṣṭaṃ bhavati, tatsādhya tulyam — tādṛśakarma janyatvāī phalasa jātīyam vidvatpradveṣa-bhaktyoḥ phalam - brahmavitpuruṣadveṣasya tadbhakteśca ( puruṣāntara pambandhinoḥ) sādhyaṃ bhavati, iti kathane evaṃ varṇane, vāśyatātparya siddheḥ' suhṛdaḥ sādhukṛtyām dviṣantaḥ pāpakṛtyām' iti vākyavivakṣitārthasya (nirduṣṭasvarūpasya ) lābhāt || 32 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṃgrahaḥ

hānyadhikaraṇe - 'puṇyapāpe vidhūya, suhṛdaḥ sādhukṛtyām ' ityādiṣvāmnātānāṃ hānādi-
cintanānāṃ vikalpenā'nuṣṭhānam — ityāśaṅkaya,
upāyanasya hā niśeṣatvāt, samuccayenaiva sarvatrānuṣṭhānam ityuktam ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||
procyate hānyadhikṛtau prāgyathā karmaśeṣatā |
tathaiva vastusāmarthyaliṅgādanyonyaśeṣatā || 139 || iti hānyadhikaraṇam |

puṇyā.... cintā - sukṛtaduṣkṛtayoḥ chānopāyana viṣayakamanusandhānam, mṛtau ca mārge ca kāryā dehaviyoga kāle tathā tadanantaraṃ pathi yānakāle ca bhāvinoḥ hānopāyanayorviṣaye kartavyam iti yuktam gatyupapatteḥ tathā satyeva karmasādhyāyāḥ bharcirādigateḥ sambhavāt, iti naiva evaṃ śanaṃ naiva sādhu, maraṇe kartavyā – dehaviyogakāle eva sarvakarmakṣayānusandhānaṃ kāryam, tāvadityukteḥ – 'tasya tāvadeva ciram yāvanna vimokṣye' iti dehapātamātrāvaghikatāyāḥ . śrutyā kathanāt || 32 ||

niśśeṣaṃ karma naśyediha yadi viduṣaḥ sthūladehāntamātre viśrāmyettasya tāvaściramiti hi baco nā'rthavatvaṃ gateḥ syāt | gatvā sampadya cā''virbhavanamiti na sambobhavītītyayuktaṃ
syādūṣī saṅkoca mātra sthitikṛdanugataḥ sūkṣma saṃskārayogaḥ || 34 ||
viduṣaḥ - niṣpannopāsanasya cetanasya, sthūla dehāntamātre - sthūlaśarīrāpagame eva, iha — arcirādiyāna pūrvāvadhibhūte sthāne eva, karma niśśeṣaṃ naśyedyadi - puṇya-pāpākhyaṃ karma nirava- śeṣaṃ yathā syāt tathā kṣīyeta yadi, tasya tāvacciramiti vacaḥ' tasya tāvadeva ciraṃ yāvanna vimokṣye' iti mokṣaparasampattivilambāvadhipratipādakaṃ vākyam, sthūladehāntamātre hi ! viśrāmyet — sthūla dehāvasānasyaiva avadhitā pratipādane khalu paryavasyet, ( tadā ca ) gateḥ arthavattvaṃ na syāt — arcirādigatiśruteḥ pratipādyārthavattvaṃ na bhavet, (gateḥ pūrvameva mokṣalā gatyādeḥ abhāvāpatteḥ) ( tathaivā'bhyupagamyatām ; ko doṣa: ? iti tu na śaṅkayam ) gatvā - arcirādinā mārgeṇa prayāya, sampadya ca paraṃ jyotirabhisampadyaiva, āvirbhavanamiti- rūpāvirbhāvaprāptiriti śrutyukto'rthaḥ, na sambobhavīti - asambhāvitaḥ syāt ityayuktam - dehaviyogakāle eva sarvakarmakṣayaṃ prati evaṃ virodhopavarṇanaṃ na yuktimat dhīsaṅkocamātra- sthitikṛt — guṇāṣṭakā virbhāvavirodhinaḥ dharmabhūtajñānasaṅkocasya kevalasyā'vasthāna hetu:, sūkṣma- saṃskārayogaḥ– anādiprāptasūkṣma saṃskārāvaśeṣānvayaḥ, anugataḥ syāt - anuvṛtto bhavitumarhati (iti pūrvameva niḥśeṣakarmakṣaye'pi sarvasāmañjasyāt ) || 34 ||

svasva-

aniyamādhikaraṇam || 3-3-13 ||
śārīrakanyā yakalāpa saṅgrahaḥ ||
tadvidyā niṣṭhānāṃ tadviyoktārcirādigati' cintā syāt ' |

prakaraṇabhedānnaitat tadya iti tu samānatāvacanāt || 33 ||

tadvidyā niṣṭhānām – yāḥ vidyāḥ prakṛtya arcirādikā gatirāmnātā tadvidyā niṣṭhānāmeva kevalam tadvidyontā tattadvidyā prakaraṇasamānmātāyāḥ arcirādigateḥ tadupāsanāṅgabhūtaṃ cintanam syāt – yuktaṃ bhavet na vidyāntaraniṣṭhānām, prakaraṇabhedāt- vidyāprakaraṇānāṃ ekaikatra pārthakyena prakaraṇāntaragatasya prakaraṇāntare'nvaye, sarvatra sarvānvayaprasaṅgena prakaraṇasya aṅgatvagrāhakapramāṇatāyā vilopapatte:, (iti śaṅkane) naitat - idaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na sādhu, 'tadye' iti tu samānatāvacanāt –' tadya itthaṃ viduḥ, ye ceme'raṇye śraddhā tapa ityupāsate' iti arcirādi- gateḥ sarveṣāmupāsakānāṃ sādhāraṇyasyoktatvāt || 33 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
panthāḥ syādarcirādiḥ phalamiha sakala brahmavidyāsu mā vā prārabhyā'ghītiyogāt prakaraṇaniyatā tasya cinteti cenna |
sarvāsāṃ tadya itthaṃ viduriti vacasā ( vā'tha ) thā ye ceti coktayā
mārge sādhāraṇe'smiṃstadanusaraṇatastadvadevā'sya cintā || 35 ||

iha – (mokṣopāyatayā ) vedānte pratipāditāsu, sakala brahmavidyāsu – upakosalākṣyanta- rādityadaharapaṃcāgnivaiśvānarādyāsu brahmopāsanātmikāsu sakalāsu vidyāsu, arcirādiḥ pantha : – arcirdina pūrva pakṣaṣaḍudaṅmā sābda vā tāṃśumadglau vidyu varuṇendradhātṛvirajā rūpaḥ prayātavyo mārgaḥ phalaṃ syāt mā 1 vā ? – ( brahmaprāptivat ) upāsana phalatayā cintanīyatayā ca sidhyet ? uta na sidhyet ? (iti saṃśaye), prārabhyā'dhītiyogāt – upakosalapañcāgnyākhyavidyā viśeṣaṃ prakṛtya āgnātatvāt jñāpakāt tasya cintā - arcirādeḥ pathaḥ ( upāsanāṅgatayā ) cintanam, prakaraṇaniyatāyāṃ vidyāṃ prakramyokto'rcirādirmārgaḥ tadvidyāmātre (phalatayā) pratiniyatameva ; na sarvavidyānvayi iti cet ! na evaṃ śaṅkanamanucitam tadya itthaṃ viduriti vacasā -

'taya itthaṃ viduriti pañcāgnividyā niṣṭhoddeśyakavākyena, atha- paścāt, atra-masminneva vākye, ye ceti cocyā 'ye ceme'raṇye śraddhā tapa ityupāsate' iti brahmopāsaka sāmānyoddeśyaka- vākyena ca, asmin mārge - arcirādau pathi, sarvāsā sādhāraṇe vedāntoditabrahmavidyā-

nāmakhilānāṃ samāne sati, tadanusaraṇataḥ tadanurodhena tadvadeva sādhāraṇaṃ phalaṃ yathā tathaiva, asya cintāpathaḥ cintanamapi sādhāraṇameveti || 35 ||

heyopādeyamaḥ rgadvitayamupadiśan muktidātā mumukṣo.

yogī yaḥ kaścanaitatsaraṇiyugalavinmuhyate netyagāyat | tasmādasmādṛśāghītyaviśadaviśadīkartṛvākyāvamarśād

brahmaprāptyaī kṛtsna' praṇihita vihitaṃ' mārgacintāvidhānam || 36 ||

muktidātā mokṣapradānakuśalo bhagavān gītācārya:, mumukṣoḥ mokṣā bhilāṣiṇo brahmaniṣṭhasya, heyo.... dvitayam - punarāvṛttiduṣṭatayā dūrī kāryam anāvṛttidāna svīkārya ca vartmadvayam upadiśan – hitaiṣitayā jñāpayan etatsaraṇiyugalavit - yā dhūma - rātri- aparapakṣa-dakṣiṇāyanaṣaṇmāsa- pitṛloka ākāśa- candramasām (āvṛttiyuktā jarāmaraṇāvartāpādana- jaṭila ) saraṇiḥ pitṛyāṇākhyā, yā ca arcir- ahaḥ- śuklākṣa-uttarayaṇa ṣaṇmāsa saṃvatsara āditya- candramas-vaidyuta-varuṇa-indra-prajāpati- candramas-vaidyata varuṇa-indra- prajāpati - virajākhyā (anāvṛttigambhīra ) saraṇiḥ devayānākhyā, īdṛśamārgadvayavettā, yaḥ kaścana yogī – yādṛśo'pi vā kaścidupāsako brahmaniṣṭhaḥ, na muhyate- spaṣṭamārgatayā na bhrānto bhavati, iti agāyat – bhagavadgītāyāmevaṃ jagau ' naite sṛtī pārtha jānan yogī muhyati kaścana' iti, tasmāt bhagavatā evaṃ gītatvāt, asmā... marśāt- mitaprajñānāmasmādṛśānāṃ vedāntādhyayanataḥ spaṣṭamajñāto yo'rthaḥ tatsphuṭīkaraṇa hetorgītā vākyasya sāmiprāya paricintanāt brahmaprāptyarha .... vihitam – parabrahmasampattau nirantarāyamarhatāpādakāni yāni praṇidhānāni tadaṅgatayā tadrūpatayā vā vidhigocarībhūtam idaṃ dehādutkrāntena viduṣā gantavyasyā'dhvanaḥ upāyānuṣṭhānadaśāyāmapi paricintanaprakārātmaka miti avagantavyam || 36 || hānāderacirāderapi kimabhihitaṃ cintanaṃ sūtrakārai-

vidyāṅgatvādisiddhyai yadi bhavatu tadā'nantare pāda etat |
maivaṃ vidyāttāyāmapi bhajanamivedaṃ ca dhītvāviśeṣāt
karmādibhyo vibhaktaṃ kathayitumiha tatsūtraṇaṃ sthānapāti || 37 ||

sūtrakāraiḥ —-śārīrakasūtrakṛdbhiḥ bādarāyaṇācāryaiḥ, hānādeḥ acirāderapi cintanam - viduṣaḥ sukṛta- duṣkṛtayoha nopāyanarūpārthasya (tathā) viduṣā gantavyasya arcirāderadhvano devayānasya ca paricintanaṃ nāma yadasti tat kimabhihitam – kasmai prayojanāya pratipāditam ? vidyāṅga- tvādisiddhyai yadi - cintanasya upāsanaṃ pratyupakārakatāyāḥ upāsanavadeva aharaharāvartanīya- tāyāśca siddhipratipattaye yadi, tadā tarhi, etat - īdṛśārthanirūpaṇam, anantare pāde - paścāttane caturthe aṅgapāde ( vidyāṅganirūpaṇapare ), bhavatu kriyatāṃ nāma na tu vidyāprabheda- nirūpaṇamātroddeśena pravṛtte guṇopasaṃhārapāde'smin, (iti śaṅkane) maivam anayā rītyā na śakyam, vidyāṅgatāyāmapi - upāsanāṅgatvasatve'pi ( cintanasya), idaṃ ca – cintanamapi, bhajanamitra ---upāsanavadeva, dhītvāviśeṣāt - jñānarūpatvāṃśe ( upāsanacintanayoḥ ) abai. lakṣaṇyāddhetoḥ karmādibhyo vibhaktaṃ kathayitum - paścāttānapāda nirūpaṇīyebhyaḥ upāsanāṅga- karma trāta-rāma-damādyantaraṅga bahiraṅga taha kāryākhyebhyaḥ pṛthagbhūtamātredayitum, ii sādhanapare'smin tṛtīyapāde, tatsūtraṇa - iānā decintanasya sautraṃ nirūpaṇam, sthānapāti — yogyasthāna- prāptameva || 37 ||

akṣaradhyadhikaraṇam || 3-3-14||
śārīrakanyāya kalā pasaṃgrahaḥ ||
vidyā viśeṣa kathitāstvamthūlādyā bhavanti tatraiva |

'nā'nyatra' mānahānairnaivā''nandādiva 'nnirūpakataḥ ' || 34 || vidyāviśeṣakathitāḥ - muṇḍaka - vājasaneyakagatayoḥ akṣarabrahmavidyayoreva kevalamupāttāḥ, asthūlādyāḥ – asthūlādipadopasthitāḥ asthūlatvānaṇutvādayo brahmaguṇāḥ param, tatraiva tasmin vidyāviśeṣe eva anusandheyāḥ, nā'nyatra - itarāsu vidyāsu aviśeṣato nā'nusandheyāḥ, māna- hāne: - (tatra tatra anupāttatayā ) tadvidyāpraveśe pramāṇarāhityāt, vyavasthābhaṅgāpatteśca (iti śaṅkane), naiva idaṃ naiva yuktam, ānandādivat – ānandatva satyatva- jñānatvānantatvā mala- svādivat nirūpakataḥ - te'pi brahmasvarūpanirūpakā eva yataḥ || 34 ||

ānandā.... yamitamiti - ānandā dhikaraṇapratipāditaprakriyayā vyavasthāpitamiti heto:, asya – vidyāviśeṣoktasyāpya sthūlatvādeḥ, sārvatrikatvāt – sarvavidyānva yi--

tvamyaiva nyāyyatvāt || 38 ||

satyatvādyaiḥ svarūpāvagatirabhihitā sarvavidyānuvṛttyā bhūyastattulyadharmeṣvadhikaraṇamidaṃ syād vṛthaiveti cenna |

kaiścijjñātasvarūpe kvaciditaragataṃ kiñcidanyanniṣedhyaṃ

vyāvṛtryā na svarūpāvagatirata iti ' prekṣaṇasyā'tra' rodhāt || 39 ||

svāmalatvaiḥ,

satyatvādyaiḥ – ānandatva - satyatva - jñānatvānantatvāma - brahmasvarūpanirūpake dhamaḥ svarūpāvagatiḥ–brahmasvarūpasya upāsyasya pratipattiḥ, sarvavidyānuvṛcyā - teṣāṃ guṇānāṃ sarvavidyā-- sādhāraṇyapratipādanadvārā abhihitā – ānandādyadhikaraṇe spaṣṭaṃ samarthitaiva tattulyadharmeṣu — svarūpanirūpakatvena satyatvā disadṛśeṣu dharmeṣvasthūlatvādiṣu viṣaye, (sarvavidyānvayitva samarthana pravṛttam ) bhūyaḥ punaḥ, idamadhikaraṇam — akṣaradhyadhikaraṇam, vṛthaiva syāt – gatārthaviṣayakatayā na apūrvaprayojanavadbhavitumarhati iti cet na evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na sādhu, kaiścit jñātasvarūpe- katipayairdharmaiḥ adhigatasvasvarūpe, kvacit – kasmiṃściddharmiṇi, itaragataṃ kiñcit — dharmyantaravṛtti prakṛte dharmiṇi niṣedhārha yatkiñcit, anyat - itarat ( dharmiṇyavidyamānam ), niṣedhyam- niṣedhataḥ vyāvartayitumarha bhavati, ( na tu kenā'pi dharmeṇā'vagatasvarūpe dharmiṇi) vyāvṛttyā - itarasmāddharmiṇaḥ prakṛtadharmiṇo vyāvṛttipratipādanamātreṇa na svarūpāvagatiḥ - prakṛtadharmiṇaḥ pratipattirna bhavatīti, iti prekṣaṇasya – evaṃrūpasya mandamateścintanasya, atra rodhāt - asthūlatvādeḥ niṣedhātmaka dharmasyā'pi svarūpanirūpakatva tatkṛtasarva vidyānvayitvābhyāṃ etadadhikaraṇa- nirṇītābhyāṃ nivāraṇāt || 39 ||
vyāvartyānantyatastadvayudasanamapi hi syādanantaṃ tatasta-
'ccintā'kiñcijjña' sādhyā jaladhitaraṇavannopadiśyeta maivam | tattatsāmānyadharmānugatika valitāśeṣa bhedopadeśe
tādṛkcintopapatteranavamamiti vā gṛhyatāṃ saṃgṛhītiḥ || 40 || vyāvarttyānantyataḥ—vyāvartanīyānāṃ prākṛtānāṃ sthūlādīnāmarthānāmasaṅkhyātatvāt

tadvyudasanamapi — anantapratiyogikaṃ vyāvartanamapi, anantaṃ syāt - asaṅkhyātameva bhavet,

(prekṣaṇasyopa) - sā.

' cintā kiñcijjña - sā.

tataḥ tasmāt, akiñcijjñasādhyā-sarvajñaireva kevalaṃ prāpyā, taccintā anantaguṇa- vyāvartana cintā, jaladhitaraṇavat — bāhubhyāṃ sāgaralaṅghanamiva na upadiśyeta – upadeṣṭuma na bhavet, (iti ākṣepe) maitram — evamākṣepo māstu, tattatsāmā ...padeśe – (śukla nīla taddhaṭai- taddhādipratiyogi kānantābhāvavati bhūtale ghaṭatvarūpavyāpakadharmāvacchinnaikā bhāvavasvamiva aparicchinnam ' ityevamādirūpeṇa vyāpyadharmāvacchinnayāvaddharmipratiyogi niṣṭhavyā pakadharmāvacchinna- pratiyogitā kabhedajñāpane, tākcintopapatteḥ vyāvṛttimukhena dharmisvarūpāvagatisiddheḥ, ana- vamamiti vā – akhila heya pratyanīkamityupadeśe vā tatsiddheḥ, saṃgṛhītiḥ - vyāvartya sarva kroḍīkāraḥ, gṛhyatām — sulabha ityavagamyatām || 40 ||

praṣṭroḥ praśnakartrīḥ, bhede'pi upasta - kaholarūpeṇa dvitve satyapi praśne prativacano- sthāpake jijñāsā vākye, yat sākṣāditi samānataḥ yat sākṣādaparokṣāt... tanme vyācakṣva ' iti, prakārataḥ paramātmaviṣayakatvataśca samānākāratāsacvāt prāṇanāśanāyādyoḥ prativacana- gamyayoḥ brahmasambandhinoḥ dharmayoḥ sarvaprāṇiprāṇanahetutvā śanāyā| tītvaprabhṛtikayoḥ, tathā vyati- hāraḥ–buddhyā ubhayatra ubhayarūpayorapi paraspara vinimayataḥ āharaṇam, (kāryam ) vyatihṛtyaiva ca paścāt upāsanaṃ kartavyam vākyam – prativacanadvayarūpaṃ vākyaṃ tu sadvidyāvat — chāndogyagata- sadvidyāprakaraṇe ekasminneva ' bhagavāṃstveva ....bravītu ' ' bhūya eva jñāpayatu' itivat punaḥ punarupadeśaucityavadbhavati || 35 ||

yat sākṣādityamuṣmin śrutiśirasi paraṃ brahma vedyaṃ yadeve- tyetasmiṃścā'stvathā'pi prativacanabhidā tatra rūpaṃ bhinatti | vidyaḥ bhit praṣṭṛbhedo'pyayamiti yadi nāḍanūdya bhūyo'nuyogāt
paścāduktaśca doṣātyaya iha na bhidāṃ sauti sādhāraṇatvāt || 42 ||

'yat sākṣā' dityamuṣmin – upastapraśnagate 'yat sākṣādaparokṣād brahma ya ātmā sarvāntara:' ityevaṃrūpe, 'yadeve' tyetasmiṃśca - kaholapraśnagate 'yadeva sākṣādaparokṣād brahma ya ātmā sarvāntara:' ityevaṃrūpe ca śrutiśirasi – upaniṣadvākye, paraṃ brahma vedyamastu paraṃ brahmaikameva vedyatvena tātparyaviṣaya ityabhyupagamyatām, athā'pi - praśnavākye avaiṣamyeṇa paramātmana eva vedyatve'pi tatra prativacanabhidātayoḥ praśnayoḥ yājñavalkyadatte uttaravākye pratyeka lakṣyamāṇaṃ vailakṣaṇyam, tatra rūpaṃ bhinatti - vidyayoḥ vedyamākāraṃ bhinnaṃ jñāpayati, ayam- asmin sandarbhe dṛśyamānaḥ, praṣṭṛbhedo'pi praśnakartrīḥ upasta - kaholayoranyatā'pi vidyābhit- vidyāsvarūpagataṃ mithobhedaṃ lakṣayati, iti yadi evaṃ śaṅkayate cet !, na nedamucitam, anūdya bhūyo'nuyogāt – prāk kṛtapraśnasyaiva ānupūrvyā anuvādaṃ kṛtvā punaḥ praśnakaraṇamiti avagamāt paścāduktaḥ – dvitīyavāraṃ pratipāditaḥ, doṣātyayaśca – aśanāyādidoṣottīrṇatvaṃ tu, na bhida sautinaiva vidyāyā bhedaṃ jñāpayati, sādhāraṇatvāt — doṣottīrṇatvasya amalatvātma- katayā, svarūpanirūpakadharmatvāt sarvāsu vidyāsvanugatatvādeva bhedakatvābhāvāt || 42 ||

dvidyāyāṃ yathā hi prativacanamidā praśnabhedānusārād
vidyekatve 'viśeṣyaṃ' prakaṭayati parāṃ devatā 'meva tatra |
tenoṣastaḥ kaholaśrutamapi sa ca tatsaṃśrutaṃ saṅkalayya
dhyāyetāṃ brahma sarvāntaramiti phalavat tatra sabrahmacaryam || 43 ||

sadvidyāyām – chāndogyaṣaṣṭhaprapāṭhakagatasadvidyāsthale, vidyaikatve — vidyāyā ekatve āditaḥ āntaṃ prakaraṇapariśīlanataḥ siddhe sati, praśnabhedānusārāt — ekenaiva śvetaketunā kriyamāṇaṃ praśnavailakṣaṇyamanusṛtya, prativacanabhidā — uddālakena dīyamāneṣu pratyuttaravākyeṣu mithovailakṣaṇyaṃ, tatra - sampūrṇā yā meva sadvidyāyām, parāṃ devatāmeva sacchandābhihitāṃ parāṃ devatāmeva, yathā hi. viśeṣyaṃ prakaṭayati — upāsanaviśeṣyatayā spaṣṭaṃ pratipādayati ; tathaivā'trā'pi ekarūpameva brahmavidyāvedyamiti, prativacanaṃ spaṣṭīkaroti, tena – vidyāyā ekatvasya spaṣṭa siddhatvena, upastaḥ- tadākhya upāsakaḥ, kaholaśrutamapi – kaholena gurumukhācchrutvā'dhigatamapi upāsyasvarūpavaiśa - ( viśiṣya) - sā. 2 (mevamantra ) - sā.

pradamaṃśam, saca kaholo'pi tatsaṃśrutam — uṣastena śravaṇato gṛhītamapi vaiśadyāvaddamaṃśam, saṅkalayya — itaratretaratra mitho vyatihṛtya, sarvāntaramiti — sarvāntarātmatvena rūpeṇa, brahma dhyāye- tām upāsyaṃ paraṃ brahma dhyānāspadamāpādayetām iti — evaṃ tayoradhigame sampanne, tatra satrahmacaryam – tadvidyāviṣaye tayordvayorapi eka pravṛttimaśvam, phalavat — upāsya vaiśadyasiddhi- rūpaka phalavadbhavati || 43 ||

śaṅkyate, na tat śaṅkanaṃ naiva sādhu, guṇānām – vidyāṃ prati rūpabhūnānāṃ hṛdayāyatanatva - satyakāmatva--satyasaṅkalpatvādīnām upāsyatāvacchedakānāṃ brahmaguṇānām, sāmānyāttu - samānatva- kṛtāt rūpābhedādeva, daharavidyā ekā śākhādvayapratipannā'pi daharākhyā vidyā abhinava, kvacit — vājasaneyaka gatadahara vidyāprakaraṇe, ākāśasya - ' ākāśastasmin śete' iti vākyasthasya śayanasthānavācinaḥ 'ākāśa' śabdasya, hṛdayavācinvāt - hṛdayākhyasthānabodhakatvāt (tasya cārthasya chāndogye'pi tulyatvāt || 36 ||

0-

chandogānāmupāyaṃ prathitamiha guṇairaṣṭabhirbrahma juṣṭaṃ
taccā'nyeṣāṃ vaśitvaprabhṛtighaṭitamityastu 'rūpasya' bhedaḥ | maivaṃ yat tadvaśitvādyapi tadiha bhidā satyasaṅkalpatāyā
ityaikārthyaṃ nirūḍhaṃ paramapi daharopāsanaṃ tadvadūyam || 45 ||

chandogānām – tāṇḍināmnāṃ sāmaśākhinām, iha – daharavidyāyām, aṣṭabhiḥ guṇaiḥ juṣṭam—apahatapāpmatvaprabhṛtibhiḥ aṣṭasaṅkhyai: guṇairyuktam, brahma-paraṃ brahma, upāsya prathitam - upāsanapradhānadharmīti spaṣṭameva, tacca tadeva brahma, anyeṣām – vājasaneyinām, vaśitvaprabhṛti- ghaṭitamitivaśitva sarveśānatvādiguṇagaṇajuṣṭaṃ sat upāsyamiti, astu rūpasya bheda:- aṅgīkriyatāṃ nāma rūpabhedaḥ, tataśca vidyābhedo'pi - iti śaṅkane, maivam — evaṃ prakāreṇa cintanaṃ naiva sādhu, iha – vājasaneyināṃ dahara vidyāyām, tadvaśitvādyapi yat - upāsyabhūta brahmagataṃ vaśitvaprabhṛtikaṃ guṇajātaṃ śrūyamāṇaṃ yadvartate, tat - vaśitvādikaṃ brahmaguṇabhūtam, satyasaṅkalpa- tāyāḥ midā - satyasaṅkalpatvanāmnā prakhyātasya brahmaguṇasyāvāntarabheda eva; nā'nyaḥ, iti aikārthyaṃ nirūḍham – anena prakāreṇa, dahara vidyāprakaraṇadvayasyā'pi upāsyaikadharmiviṣayakatvanirvahaṇaṃ sampannaṃ bhavati, paraṃ daharopāsanamapi - ito'nyadiva bhāsamānaṃ taittirīyagataṃ ' dahaṃ vipāpmaṃ ' ityādikaṃ daharavidyāprakaraṇamapi tadvat ūhyam – chāndogyādigataprakaraṇavadeva yathāyogyaṃ vicintya abhinnavidyāparatvena nirvācamiti || 45 ||

.

nanvākāśo guṇādyaiḥ para iti dahara dhikriyāyāṃ puroktaṃ tasmānnā'nyārthaśaṅketyadhikaraṇamidaṃ nojjihīteti cenna |
vyomātītaṃ nimittaṃ daharamidamupādānamityudgṛṇantaḥ
pūrvaṃ kṣiptāḥ prasaṅgāt punarapi gamitā śchinnamūlatvamatra || 46 ||

nanu bhoḥ, purā daharādhikriyāyām - prathamādhyāyatṛtīyapādīye daharādhikaraṇe, guṇādyaiḥ paramātmāsādhāraṇabhūtairguṇādibhiḥ jñāpakaiḥ, ākāśa : - ' daharo'sminnantara ākāśaḥ ityuktākāśapadāmidheyaḥ, para iti paramātmaiveti uktam – nirṇītamevā'sti, tasmāt - etadadhikaraṇakāryasya pūrvameva niṣpannatvāt, anyārthaśaṅkā neti - ākāśapadasya paramātmetarārthakatva- śaṅkā naivotpattamalamiti, idamadhikaraṇam – kāmādyadhikaraṇam, naiva ujjihīta naiva udaya- māpnuyāt (kṛtakaratvāpatteḥ ) iti cet na evaṃ śaṅkanamanucitam nimittam – jagannimitta-

kāraṇabhūtaṃ vastu, vyomātītam - dahā kā śāpekṣayā parabhūtaṃ tasvameva idaṃ daharam - daharākāśa- rūpamidaṃ vastu tu, upādānam - asvatantramupādānakāraṇameva iti udgṛṇantaḥ–evaṃ vādinaḥ (vyomātītavādinaḥ ) pūrva kṣiptāḥ - itaḥ pūrvatanaistaistairadhikaraṇairnirastaprāyāḥ | atrā'pi - etadadhikaraṇīyādyasūtre'pi prasaṅgāt – nirguṇatattvanirasanaprasaṅgamāśritya puna:- bhūyaḥ, chinna- mūlatvaṃ gamitāḥ - nirmūlasvatātparyakatā prāpitāste iti adhikaraṇodayāt || 46 ||

-0-

anuṣṭhānanaiyatyaṃ na śrutyabhimatam, arthavatvāt – karmaphalā pratibandharūpa pṛthakphalavattvāt tasya, anupāsiturapi – udgīthopāsanaṃ tyaktavato'pi karmitāhetoḥ anuṣṭhitakratutvasya ' tenobho kuruta:' iti śrutivākyato'vagatatvāddhetoḥ || 37 ||

udgīthe prāṇadṛau kratughaṭitaphalādanyaduktaṃ phalaṃ tat svīkṛtya prāgūvicāraḥ sthita iti vihataḥ pūrvapakṣo'tra maivam | atratye'naṅgabhāve sthiranihita ghiyastatra vidyaikyaśaṅkā

tyaktvā'ṅgānaṅgabhāvau pṛthagapṛthagiti syācca pūrvatra cintā || 48 ||

udgīthe prāṇadṛṣṭau udgīthā va yava bhūtapraṇavadharmika prāṇatvaprakāra kopāsane, ṛtughaṭita- phalādanyat — katvanvitaphalādbhinnam phalam - asura vidhvaṃsanarūpaṃ phalam, uktam- 'hantā'surānudgīthenā'tyayā me 'ti śrutau spaṣṭīkṛtam, tat svīkṛtya – tadabhyupagamya, prāk- anyathātvāghikaraṇe pūrvam, vicāraḥ sthita iti — vicāra: prastuta iti heto, atra pūrvapakṣo vihataḥ asminnadhikaraṇe pūrvapakṣo bhagna: (pūrvapakṣapravṛttireva naiva ghaṭate) iti śaṅkane, maivam- pūrvapakṣānudayā kṣepo nocitaḥ, atratye- etadadhikaraṇanirṇīte, anaṅgabhāve - upāsanasya tvaṅgatvā- bhāve, sthiranihitadhiyaḥ acālya dṛḍhāvasthamateḥ puruṣamyaiva, tatra vidyaikyaśaṅkā - tasminnadhikaraṇe vidyābhedābheda cintā kṛtā, (athavā ) aṅgānaṅgabhāvau tyaktvā tvaṅgatvānaṅgatvavicāraṃ parityajya, pṛthagapṛthagiti – vidyā minnā ? abhinnā vā iti, pūrvatra - anyathātvādhikaraṇe, cintā syācca–vicārapravṛttirbhavedapi || 48 ||

pradānādhikaraṇam || 3-3-18||
śārīrakanyāya kalāpasaṅgrahaḥ ||
sādhāraṇatvaheto: guṇī tu guṇacintaneṣu 'nā'vayaiḥ ' |
naivaṃ viśiṣṭabhedāt 'pratiguṇa' māvartanīya eva guṇī || 38 ||

sādhāraṇatvahetoḥ sarvān guṇān prati paramātmano guṇitvena sādhāraṇyāt, ekavāraṃ guṇicintanenaiva kāryasiddheḥ, guṇacintaneṣu – pṛthak pṛthakkāryatā vihiteṣu guṇacintanā vartaneṣu, guṇī - cintyamānaguṇānāṃ dharmibhūtaḥ paramātmā, na āvartyaḥna āvartanīyacintanaviṣayaḥ, (iti śaṅkane) naivam – evaṃrītikaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na sādhu, viśi-dāt – tattadguṇaviśiṣṭaguṇikharūpasya minnatvāddhetoḥ, pratiguṇam – cintyamānaguṇabhedānuguṇyena, guṇī - guṇānāṃ dharmibhūtaḥ paramātmā, āvartanīya eva āvartyamāna cintana viṣaya eva || 38 ||

hi yataḥ, rājatvādyaiḥ pṛthaktvāt — rājasva adhirājatva-svarāṭtvākhyaiḥ viśeṣaṇaiḥ kṛtaṃ pārthakyamavalambya, indre – indrākhye ekasminneva yāgoddeśyabhūte dharmiṇi viṣaye haviṣo dānam- vidhyuktaṃ havistyajanam āvṛttaṃ bhavati - trirāvṛttameva kāryamiti pūrvakāṇḍe nirṇītameva vartate ||49||

tatadbhoga' pratīte ' guṇaghaṭitaparopāsanā bhoga hetu-

muktiśca syāt kramādityasadaguṇavacasyānyaparyābhidhānāt | śāstre'sminnāsamāpteḥ kva phalamamihitaṃ nirguṇopāstisādhyaṃ
nocchāstra ca prakarūpyaṃ guṇaniyamanataḥ khyātimāṃścaṣa pādaḥ || 50 ||

atra - daharopāsane, daharaguṇagaṇe – upāsyasya daharākāśarūpiṇaḥ apahatapāpmatvādike guṇajāte, pratyekaṃ melanādvā saścintyamāne'pi - ' para ātmā apahatapāpmā, vijaraḥ ' ityevamādirītyā ekaikaśaḥ tattadguṇavattāyāṃ viśeṣaṇatayā vā, 'paramātmagatāḥ apahatapāpmatvādyāḥ aṣṭau guṇāḥ' ityekatra dharmiṇi nānādharmāṇāṃ militatayā viśeṣyatayā vā anusandhīyamāne'pi, - guṇyā .... labdheḥ guṇān pratyāśrayatayā guṇicintana paunaḥpunyasya svayameva lābhāt, iha - asmin pradānādhikaraṇe, tada...śaṅkā guṇicintanāvṛtteḥ abhāvākṣepaḥ, katham 9 – kena vā prakāreṇa yujyeta ? iti cenna—evam etadadhikaraṇapūrvapakṣānutthitiśaṅkā naiva yuktā, guṇānāṃ buddhayārohe- etadvidyā prakaraṇāmnātānāṃ brahmaguṇānāṃ upāsanopayoginyā buddhayā viṣayīkaraṇe lambhanīye, yadavadhi - yāvat paryantam, guṇino rūpam - guṇāśrayasya dharmiṇaḥ svarūpam, arthyam - avaśyamapekṣitam, tato'nyat - tādṛśa satyatvā disvarūpanirūpakadharmāpekṣayā bhinnaguṇaviśiṣṭaṃ dharmi - svarūpam, vidyaikāntam — prakṛtāyā daharavidyāyā evāsādhāraṇaṃ yat daharākāśatvākhyarūpa- viśiṣṭabrahmasvarūpamasti, tadāvṛtya... pravṛtteḥ tādṛśadharmisvarūpa cintanā vṛtyanvitatattatpratiguṇa- cintana sambandhipūrvottarapakṣayoḥ pravṛtyupapatteḥ (adhikaraṇotthānasyārthavattvāditi ) || 51 ||

liṅgabhūyastvādhikaraṇam || 3-3-19||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṃgrahaḥ ||
pūrvānuvāka' nītyā' 'daha ropāstisamupāsya nirṇayanam |

prakṛtatvāditi naivaṃ svavākyabalataḥ samastanirṇayanam || 39 ||

pūrvānuvākanītyā pūrvasmin anuvāke daharākhyasya upāsyasya prasaktatvāt, tameka nyāyamanurudhya, daharo ....yanam – tatpaścāttane sahasraśīrṣānuvā ke'pi daharopāsane upāsyaṃ yat tastvaṃ tasyaiva viśeṣato nirṇayaḥ kriyate, prakṛtatvāt tasyaiva prakrāntatvāt, ( taccheṣatayā tadapekṣitārthanirṇayasyaivocitatvāt ), iti, naivam – evaṃ śaṅkane, nedaṃ sādhu, svavākyabalataḥ - svagataparamātmacihnapara bahuvākya balena, samastanirṇayanam — sakalaparavidyopāsyasya tattvasyaiva nirṇayaḥ asmin anuvāke kriyate ityeva sādhu || 39 ||

puruṣam – sarvāsu vidyāsu upāsyatayā vihitaṃ paramapuruṣam, iha pratyabhijñāpya – asminnanuvāke sa evā'yamiti pratisaṃjñāpya, tasmin - sarvavidyopāsyatvena rūpeṇa pratyabhijñāpite paramapuruṣarūpe tantve, nārāyaṇatvaṃ vadat – nārāyaṇapadā bhidheyapara devatā viśeṣasvarūpatāmupadiśat, vākyam – 'sahasra-- śīrṣa' mityanuvākapraviṣṭaṃ vākyapramāṇatāpannaṃ vākyajātam, prakriyātaḥ - prakaraṇākhyapramāṇāpekṣayā, adhikabalaṃ hi—pramāṇāntarānupamadya svatātparya viṣayārthakatva rūpabalādhikyavat khalu || 52 ||
vākyaiḥ sarvārthatāyāṃ daharabhajanamapyatra bhāgīti sārtha-
stalliṅgopetabhāgo na ca bahubhiralaṃ yodhumekaṃ ' kṛtārtham ' | naikasyā'syopakuryāt prakaraṇamalasaṃ kiṃ ca sarvopajīvye

tatve tātparyamatra sphuṭamiti vitathā tatparityāgaklaptiḥ || 53 ||

vākyaiḥ –' sahasraśīrṣa' mityūrdhvānuvākasthaiḥ paramātmacihnabahulai : bahubhirvākyaiḥ (abādhita- pramāṇabhāvaiḥ) sarvārthatā yām - asyā'nuvākasya sarvaparavidyopāsyadevatā viśeṣanirdhāraṇārthatāyāṃ labdhāyām, daharabhajanamapi sarvapara vidyāntargataṃ daharopāsanamapi, atra bhāgi iti prakṛte nirṇeyopāsyakatvena lakṣya bhūtameveti hetoḥ, talli .... bhāgaḥ – daharavidyā prakāśanasamarthaśabda- viśeṣavān ' padmakośapratīkāśa' mityādiko bhāgaḥ, sārthaḥ labdhaprayojanaḥ, (atrāpi upāsya- taccalābhāt ) kṛtārtham – sampannaprayojanam, ekam – 'padmakośe tyādikaṃ daharaprakāśanasāmarthya- rūpaliṅgavadvākyam, bahubhiḥ - nānā vidyā tātparyakaiḥ labdhapramāṇabhāvairbahubhirvākyaiḥ saha yoddhuṃ nālam - bādhakabhāvena sthātuṃ naiva samartha bhavet, ekasya asya – tādṛśavākyastoma praviṣṭasya asahāyasya daharasambandhina: 'padmakośe 'ti vākyasya, alamam – svasambandhino vākyasya tadgata- liṅgata eva svaśeṣatvalā bhadarśanataḥ mantharapravṛttimat, prakaraṇam - daharavidyāsambandhiprāktanavākya- nicayarūpaṃ-prakaraṇam, na upakuryāt etadvākyaghaṭitasya samagrasyā'nuvākasya dahara vidyāśeṣatva- nirṇayajananaupayikaṃ sat sahakāri na bhavet kiṃ ca vākya-prakaraṇādigatavirodhacintā paraṃ śamitā | tatkathā astu nāma, sarvopajīvye - sarvāsāmapi paravidyānāṃ aviśeṣeṇa upāsana- dharmitvataḥ ekaikāśrayabhūte, tattve – paratasvabhūte śrīmati nārāyaṇe, tātparyam - prādhānyena tadeka- pratipādanavivakṣā, atra sphuṭamiti - asmin anuvāke anapahnavanīyatayā akathañcinneyatayā ca nyaceti hetoḥ, tatparityāgaklṛptiḥ – tādṛśātyantasphuṭaśrutitātparyāpalāpaprayatnaḥ, vitathāna sāphaśyavān bhavati || 53 ||

ātmaikyaṃ devataikyaṃ trikasamadhigatā tulyataikyaṃ trayāṇā- manyatraiśvaryamityādyanipuṇaphaṇitīrādriyante na santaḥ |
trayyantai rekakaṇṭhaistadanuguṇamanu-vyāsa mukhyoktibhiśca
śrīmān nārāyaṇo naḥ patirakhilatanurmuktido muktabhogyaḥ || 54 ||

ātmaikyam — ātmapadābhidheyaṃ tattvamekameva ; na nānā, iti, devataikyam – devatā- rūpo'rtha eka eva ; na bahuriti, trikasamadhigatā tulyatā — brahma-viṣṇu-maheśvarātmakaṃ devatātrayamāśritā nyūnādhikabhāvarāhityena samānataiveti trayāṇām aikyam - brahma-viṣṇu- maheśvarāṇāmabheda eva ; na svarūpabheda iti, anyatraiśvaryam – trimūryuttīrṇe tastvaviśeṣe eva sarveśitṛtvam iti, ityādya... ṇitī:- evamādyā: tīvratayā śāstrārthavicārākṣamāṇāṃ uktīḥ, - santaḥ - pramāṇaiḥ upāsya tattva nirṇayakṣamāḥ parāṅkuśanāthayāmunādyāḥ, na ādriyante — pramāṇānāṃ ādriyante—pramāṇānāṃ prāmāṇikānāṃ ca viśvāsyatayā na manyante, ekakaṇṭhaiḥ - mitho virodhagandharāhityena spaṣṭai kārthakaiḥ, trayyantaiḥ vedāntavākyaiḥ sarvaiḥ, tada ...ktimiva vedāntavākyānuyāyisvabhāvābhiḥ āptatvena sampratipannānāṃ manu-vyāsādīnāṃ sākṣātkṛtaparāvaratattvānāṃ tasvārthanirṇayedamparā bhirvāgbhiśca, (niścito'yamarthaḥ) naḥ patiḥ - anantajīvarūpāṇāṃ cetanānāmasmākaṃ śeṣibhūtaḥ svāmī, akhilatanu : – samastacetanācetanaśarīrī, śrīmān nārāyaṇaḥ - lakṣmīsamā liṅgitaḥ nirapāyi- śrīkaḥ, nārāyaṇākhyaḥ paradevatāviśeṣa eva ( sa eva ca ) muktidaḥ - mokṣākhyaparamapuruṣārtha- prāpakaḥ, muktabhogyaḥ— muktairā vibhunasvarūpaiścetanaiḥ bhogyatayā phalabhūtaśca | ( paramapuruṣārthopāya- bhūtaḥ upeyabhūtaḥ paramapuruṣārthaśca || 54 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||
prāk sarvadahavidyaikye prakriyābalasaṃmate | liṅgabhūyastvādhikṛtau vākyācchaṅkā vyapodyate || 147 ||
iti liṅgabhūyastvādhikaraṇam || 19 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||
aṅga pūrvaprasakte kacitasamupasthāpitasya kratoḥ syād buddhyātmā'gnirmanaścitprabhṛtirapi yathā mānasaṃ dvādaśāhe | tadvīryasyātideśāditi na saduditoyatra vidyāmayo'ṅgī

śrutyādyaireva tasminnupakṛtisamatā bodhanārtho'tideśaḥ || 55 ||

manaścitprabhṛtiḥ - 'manaścit ' ' vākcit' ityādinā agnirahasye pratipāditaḥ, agniḥ – nidhīyamānānyādhārabhūto'rthaḥ, buddhayātmā'pi kevalaṃ jñānaikā kāro'pi, dvādaśāhe- karmakāṇḍīye dvādaśāhākhye ṛtau, mānasaṃ yathā— kevalamanovyāpārasādhyaṃ mānasaṃ nāma graha- (pātram ) iva, pūrva sthāpitasya tataḥ prāgukkāntena iṣṭakacitākhyena kiyāmayenā'gninā (sthaṇḍila viśeṣeṇāgnyādhārabhūtena) buddhisthatāṃ prāpitasya, kratoḥ - daihika vyāpārasādhya ( kriyāmaya ) yāgaviśeṣasya, aṅgaṃ syāt — iṣṭakacitāgnisthāne vaikalpika prāptimavalambya upakārako bhavitu- maīti tadvīryasya iṣṭakacinānigatasya ṛtupakaraṇasāmarthyasya, atideśāt – ' teṣāmekaika eva tāvān yāvānasau pūrva:' iti vākyena manaścidādau tatsādṛśyataḥ pratipādanāt (iti śaṅkane) iti na sat — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na samīcīnam atra manaścidādyupasthāpake ammirahasyavākya eva, vidya mayaḥ aṅgī manovyāpāramātrasādhyajñānakā kāratāśrayaḥ kratuḥ manaścidādyupakāryaḥ, śrutyādyaireva - aṅgāṅgibhāvanirṇāyakapramāṇabhūtaiḥ 'vidyayā haivaita evaṃvidaścitā bhavanti ' ityeva mādigataśruti-liṅgādibhireva, uditaḥ - sphuṭapratipannaḥ, atideśaḥ – 'tāvān....yāvān' ityanena vākyena kriyamāṇa: manaścidādau iṣṭakacitavīryātideśaḥ, tasmin — manaścidādau manaḥ sādhye'mau, upakṛtisamatābodhanārthaḥ iṣṭakacitāmau svasādhyaṃ kriyāmayaṃ kratuṃ prati sampādyaḥ yādṛśa upakāraḥ tatsāmyasya jñānasādhyaṃ kratuṃ prati pratipādana siddhyarthaḥ iti jñeyam || 55 || kalpyo hyatra kriyātmā ṛturapi tadapi prāgupāttāṅgaśaktyā vākyasthaiścānubandhairiha samupanato bhāti vidyāmastu |

..

dūrasthākṛṣṭayogāt svavacanapaṭhitā kṛṣṭayogo 'balīyā - 1

naprāpte'rthe vidhitvaṃ hyanuvadanasame'pyāśritaṃ tadvadatra || 56 ||
atra — prakrānte'smin prakaraṇe, kriyātmā— daihika vyāpārāyattasvarūpalābhavān, ṛtu- rapi yāgaviśeṣo'pi, kalpyo hi ! – kalpanīyavidhyadhīna kartavyatājñānagocara eva khalu, tadapi -

ṛtuvidhikalpanamapi, prāgupāttāṅgaśaktyā – pūrva prastuteṣṭaka citāgnisambandhināmaṅgānāṃ svopa- kāryārthāvinābhāvarūpasāmartheyenaiva, vidyāmayastu – manovyāpāramātrasādhyatvakṛta vidyaka svarūpaḥ mana- ścidādiḥ kratustu, iha vākyasthaiśva – etatprakaraṇopalabhyamāna vākyagataśabdopasthāpyaireva, anubandhaiḥ kratumvarūpaniṣyādanopayogibhirarthaiḥ stotra-śastra graha dibhirmānasaiḥ samupanataḥ - saṃnihitaśabdopāttasvarūpaḥ san bhāti – buddhyārūḍho bhavati, dūrasthākṛṣṭayogāt – bhinnakāṇḍa- gatatvataḥ asaṃnihitaḥ iṣṭakacitākhya kriyāmayāṅgopasthityā ākarṣaṇaviṣayabhūto yaḥ kriyāmayaḥ kratuḥ tena saha aṅgāṅgibhāvenā'nvayāpekṣayā, svavacana .... yogaḥ - vidyāmayārthajātabodhaka- vākyopāttaḥ uttaratrākṛṣṭaśca yo vidyāmayaḥ kratuḥ tena sākamaṅgatayā yogaḥ, balīyān- śīghropasthitikatva kṛtabalādhikyavān aprāpte pramāṇataḥ ajñātasvarūpe, arthe—anuṣṭheyatvenā- vaśyajñeye viṣaye, anuvadanasame'pi - anuvādavākya sadṛśā kāravattayā jñāyamāne'pi vākyaviśeṣe, vidhitvam – vidhikarūpanaupayikatvarūpaṃ vidhitvam, āśritaṃ hi — karmamīmāṃsakairabhyupagataṃ khalu ! śeṣabhakṣaṇādhikaraṇe tṛtīyapañcame atra tadvat - manaścidādiviṣayake'pi anuvādasarūpe vākye tatreva vivikalpanata: vidyā mayakratunirvāha: cintanīyaḥ || 56 ||

śarīre bhāvādhikaraṇam || 3-3-21 ||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṃgrahaḥ ||
kartṛtvādiviśiṣṭo jīvo dhyeyaḥ śarīritāhetoḥ |

'naivaṃ' guṇāṣṭakayutaḥ prāpyatvāt syād yathākatunyāyāt || 41 ||

kartṛtvādiviśiṣṭaḥ– kartṛtvabhoktṛtvehā mutra sañcārakṣamatvādiguṇaviśiṣṭatayaiva jīvaḥ— paramātmaviśeṣaṇatayā (tadviśeṣyatayā vā ) cintanīyaḥ pratyagātmā, dhyeyaḥ – upāsanaviṣayatāmā pāda- nīya: ; ( na tu sarvopādhivinirmuktasvarūpavattayā), śarīritāhetoḥ – upāsanadaśāyāṃ tasya śarīrāvasthitasvāt, (tadā kartṛtvādīnāmavarjanāt ), ( iti śaṅkane) naivam — evaṃrītyā śaṅkanamasādhu, guṇāṣṭakayutaḥ syāt — sarvopādhivigamāvasthā kā līnāpahatapāpmatvādiguṇāṣṭaka - sampannatayaiva jīvaḥ upāsanaviṣayī kāryo bhavet, ( na tu saṃsāradaśā kālīnaguṇavattayā ), yathāṛtu- nyāyāt—— yathopāsanaṃ phalaprāpti: ' iti nyāyamanurudhya, prāpyatvāt – āvirbhūtaguṇāṣṭakayukta- svātmasvarūpasyaiva mokṣadaśāyāṃ svena prāptavyatvāt || 41 ||

tatkāle tasyākārasya asaṃnidhānena buddhāvanārohāt iti yathā niścīyate tadvat iti cet na- evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na yuktam, anyathā saṃnikarṣāt – prakārāntarataḥ saṃnikarṣasya śuddhātmagatākā re vidyamānatvāt, (yataḥ mumukṣorupāsakasya svaprāpyaḥ svagataḥ śuddhākāraḥ nirantaraṃ buddhistha eva) atra - cikīrṣite sādhanabhūte upāsane, sādhyaḥ - phalatayā vivakṣitaḥ, śuddhaḥ ātmā hi- vigatasamastopādhiḥ jīva eva khalu, (na tūpādhimān tasya upāsanātpūrvameva prāptatvena upāsanasyaivā'nupayoga patteḥ), phalamatiḥ – sādhanīyārthaviṣayiṇī dhīḥ param, avinābhāvinī - yāvatphalāvāpti sādhakasya apracyutā niyamena vartamānā jāgarti, boddhṛkṛtye – upāyopeya- viṣayaka bodhavatā puruṣeṇa sampādye buddhayāsanne – ata eva sadā buddheḥ saṃnihite phale, antaraṅge sati—nivartanīyasāṃsārikākārāpekṣayā sampādanīye muktākāre labdhāntaraṅgabhāve purovartivastuvat jāgrati sati, tatkratunyāyasiddhiḥ – yathopāsana phalāvāptinyāyalābhaḥ, vidhiniyatā - upāsana- vidhinaiva śuddhātmākāre avarjanīyānvayavān || 57 ||

karturbhoktṛtvamātraṃ gaṇayitumucitaṃ dṛṣṭabhogārthayatne
prasāra pra svargādyarthe'nyadehānugatirapi paraṃ svādhikārānuviddhā |
muktyarthe prāyavasthāpraṇidhikathanatastatkatunyāya'vācā'
'cintyasya prāpyatārtho gamita' iti kuto'tiprasaṅgādiśaṅkā || 58 ||

iṣṭa bhogārtha yatne – aihika phalopabhogasādhanabhūtalaukika karmārambhe kriyamāṇe, kartuḥ karmārambhapravṛttasya puruṣasya, bhoktṛtvamātraṃ gaṇayitumucitam - anuṣṭhāsyamāna karmajanyaphalānu- bhavitṛtvameva kevalam karmārambhakāle cintayitumarham, svargādyarthe – svargādyāmuṣmika phalasādhana- bhūtayāgādikarmārambhe kriyamāṇe, svādhikārānuviddhā – anuṣṭhātṛbhūtasvagatatraivarṇikatvādirūpa - yāgānuṣṭhānaupayikā ghikāracintanasaha kṛtā, anyadehānugatirapi – yāgādyanuṣṭhānasādhanabhūtaśarī- rātiriktaduḥkhāsambhinnasukhaviśeṣānubhavopayogiśarīrāvādhyaī tā'pi param - ādhikyena kartuḥ cintanāha bhavati, muktyarthe mokṣasādhanatvena vedāntavihitopāsane kriyamāṇe tu prāptyava.... nataḥ — upāsanaphalāvāptikāle labhyāyā: svakīyāvasthāyā api dhyeyatvasya śrutau spaṣṭamuktatvāt (tadanurodhena ) cintyasya prāpyatārthaḥ upāsitasya anubhāvyatārūpo'rthaḥ, tatkratunyāyavācā gamita iti - ' yathātura smilloke puruṣo bhavati tathetaḥ pretya bhavati' ityevaṃrūpayā yathopāsanaṃ phalāvāptiḥ' ityaṃśasya sārvatrikatvajñāpikayā śrutigirā atrāpi viṣaye buddhayārohaṃ prāpita iti

to:, atiprasaṅgādiśaṅkā kutaḥ 1 – asyārthasya laukike karmaṇi vaidike ca svargādyarthe karmaṇi prasaṅgaḥ ityādirūpā śaṅkā naivodeti | (asya nyāyasya vedāntavihitopāsanapratiniya --- tatvāditi ) || 58 ||
vidyābhedeṣu vedyākṛtiviṣamatayā yāvadukte'pi cintye
prāpyaṃ sarvopapannaṃ tata iha kathana prāpyacintāniṣedhaḥ |
tasmādbaddhasya cintāstviti na kaluṣito na hyahaṃ śabdamukhyaḥ
prājāpatyāttu vākyādakaluṣadaśayā bhāvanīyatvāsiddhiḥ || 59 ||

vidyābhedeṣu - mithovilakṣaṇeṣu vidyā viśeṣeṣu, vedyā... tayā – upāsanakāle cintanīyā- nām upāsyārthasambandhināmākārāṇāmapi vilakṣaṇatvena, yāvadukte- yāvadukte tatra tatra vihitamātra evākāre, cintyespi cintanīyatayā siddhe'pi, sarvopapannam - samasta viśeṣaṇa (ubhaya vibhūti) viśiṣṭameva brahma, prāpyam - phaladaśāyāṃ (muktau ) anubhāvyaṃ bhavati, tataḥ – samasta viśeṣaṇa koṭi- praviṣṭabaddhāvasthajīvarūpaviśeṣaṇayuktasyā'pi brahmaṇaḥ prāpyatvāvaśyaṃbhāvāt, (aprāpyasyāṃśasyā- bhāvāta ) aprāpya cintā niṣedhaḥ katham — asmin adhikaraṇe nirṇīyamānaḥ, upāsakena muktikāle aprāpyasya jīvīyarūpasya upāsanakāle cintāyāḥ pratiṣedhaḥ (abhāva) kena vā prakāreṇa yuktaḥ syāt ? tasmāt – baddhajīvīyākārasyā'pi sarvaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭabrahma viśeṣaṇatā panna-- jīva viśeṣaṇatayā muktāvapi prāpyatvāt baddhasya cintā astu -bandhakālīna sopādhikā kārava- to'pi jīvasya upāsanadaśāyāṃ cintanaṃ śrutisaṃmatamityevā'bhyupagamyatāṃ ; ko doṣa:, 1 iti na- evaṃ śaṅkanamanucitam, kaluṣitaḥ —bandhakālīnaupādhikākārarūpakāluṣyavān jīvaḥ, 'ahaṃ'- śabdamukhyo na hi ! – ' ahaṃ brahmā'smī' tyahaṅgahopāsana vidhivākyīyasya 'ahaṃ' śabdasya (puṣkalaṃ pravṛttinimittamādāya bodhya ( tāvān ), mukhyo'rtho na bhavitumarhati khalu (svābhāvikākārasyaiva śabdapravṛttinimittatvāt ) prājāpatyāt tu vākyāt – 'ya ātmā'pahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyu - rviśoko vijighatso'pipāsaḥ satyakāmaḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ so'nveṣṭavyaḥ sa vijijñāsitavyaḥ ' iti chāndogyāṣṭamaprapāṭhakāmnātāt prajāpatervākyādeva (jīvātmopāsanaparāt), akaluṣadaśayā bhāvanīyatvasiddhiḥ – āvirbhūtaguṇāṣṭaka va tārūpanivṛtta sarva kāluṣya | vasthayaiva jīvaviśeṣaṇatāpannayā jīvasya upāsanakāle cintanīyatvasya śrutitātparyaviṣayatālābho bhavatīti || 59 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ ||

śarīre bhāvādhikaraṇe—upāsanadaśāyāṃ jīvasya kartṛtvādyākāreṇā'nusandhānam—

ityāśaṅkaya,

apahṛtapāpmatvādyākāreṇaiva anusandhānaṃ kartavyam ; apahatapāpmatvādyākārasyaiva prāpyatvena,

prāpteśca yathopāsanabhāvyatvāt — ityuktam ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||

udgīthāderviśeṣe bhajanavidhirasau syāt svasānnidhyagīte maivaṃ sarvānibhūtakratumukhata ihāśeṣasānidhyasiddheḥ |
sāmānyaṃ vyaktyapekṣakriyamapi niyamādarśane vrīhitāva-

cchandaścodgīthamātraṃ vadati na tu bhidāṃ chāganītistu nātra || 60 ||

asau bhajanavidhiḥ ' omityetadakṣaramudgīthamupāsīta ' ityayam udgīthopāsanavidhiḥ, svasāṃnidhyagīte udgīthāderviśeṣe syāt – upāsana vidhyāmnāna kaṇṭhoktimacchā khā viśeṣīya- ṛtvaṅgodgītharūpe viśiṣṭe viṣaye eva upāsanavidhāyako bhavitumarhati ; na tu sarvaśākhīyodgītha- sāmānye, (iti prāpte) maivam-evamaṅgīkaraṇaṃ na sādhu, sarvāṅgi .... mukhataḥ – kṛtsnodgīthopa- kāryakratudvārā, iha – udgīthopāsanāmnāna kaṇṭhoktimatyāṃ śākhāyāmapi, aśeṣasāṃ nidhyasiddheḥ- sakalodgīthānāṃ (nānāśākhīyānāmapi ) saṃnihitatvalā bhāt, sāmānyam - sāmānyatvenocyamānaṃ sarvamapi, vyaktyapekṣa kriyamapi - ' nirviśeṣaṃ na sāmānyam' iti nyāyena viśeṣabhūtāṃ vyakti- mapekṣyaiva anuṣṭheyatāvat yadyapi, ( ' gāmānayetyuktau ānayanānuṣṭhānaṃ goviśeṣāśritameva khalu bhavati ) (tathāpi ), niyamādarśane - viśeṣamātra paryavasānaupayikapramāṇānupalambhe sati vrīhitāvat- carupādhanabhūtatrī hitvā śrayasya yasya kasyā'pi 'vrīhibhiryajete' tyanena grahaṇamiva sāmānye paryavasāna- vadbhavati, śabdaśca – 'udgīthamupāsīte' tyayaṃ vidhivākyarūpaḥ śabdaśva, udgīthamātraṃ vadati- udgīthatvāśritatvena rūpeṇa sāmānyameva bodhayati ; na tu midām na śākhā viśeṣīya mudgītham, chāganītistu – chāgapaśunyāyaḥ param atra na-asmin viṣaye nā'vakāśaṃ labhate ; (viśeṣaparyavasāyakā bhāvāt ) || 60 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||
pūrvādhikṛtisiddhāntādā satyatiśaye'pi ca |
aṅgāvabaddhanītyā syāt sarvodgīthasamanvayaḥ || 150 ||

iti aṅgāvabaddhādhikaraṇam || 22 ||

-0-

vaiśvānarātmā vaiśvānara vidyāyāṃ bhajanīyatayā pratipannaḥ vaiśvānarākhyaḥ paramātmā, vyastaḥ cintanīyaḥ trailokyātmake śarīre mūrdhatvādityacakṣuṣṭrādirūpeṇa pṛthakpṛthageva pratyeka - śa evopāsyaḥ pratiniyataphalodāhṛteḥ tattadavayavayutopāsanamātra vyavasthitasyaiva pratyeka phalasya 'atyannaṃ paśyati priyam ; bhavatyasya brahmavarcasaṃ kule' ityādinā śrutau pratyavayavopāsanaṃ vibhaktata- yaivoktatvāt iha — asminnevopāsanaprakaraṇe, kṛtsnopāstau phaloktiḥ sakalāvayavayutopāsana- kriyāyāṃ nirvartitāyāṃ sambhavata: 'sarveṣu... annamatti ' ityevaṃrūpasya phalasya vācoyuktistu, stutiḥ ekaikaśaḥ ekaikāvayavayutopāsanapūrtī satyāṃ sampadyamānasya phalajātasya militoktyā upāsanapūrteḥ praśaṃsārūpaiva na tu pṛthaṅgilitopāsanasya vidhānatātparyikā ) yadi vā — athavā kṛtsnarūpo'pi mūrdhatvādinikhilarūpavānapi vaiśvānarātmā, upāsyaḥ - ( vyastopāsanava deva samastopāsanenā'pi) bhajanīyaḥ na tu kevalaṃ samastopāsanena, (vaiśvānarātmanaḥ vyastaṃ samastaṃ copāsanaṃ kāryamityarthaḥ) maivam - evaṃrītyā abhyupagamo naiva sādhuḥ, vyasteṣu - pratyekaśaḥ kriyamāṇeṣu upāsaneṣu doṣaḥ - sambhavan anarthaḥ, pṛthak anukathitaḥ prativyastāṃśopāsanānupadameva varṇitaḥ 'mūrdhā te vyavatiṣyat' ityādinā, tatphaloktiḥ stutiḥ syāt – mūrdhādyekaikāṃśopāsana- phalavacanavyaktiḥ, samagropāsanaikadeśasyānupekṣaṇīyatvapratipādanābhiprāyavatī praśaṃsā bhavitumarhati, aṣṭākapālaprabhṛtiṣu ca - dvādaśakapālajāteṣṭayeka deśabhūteṣvaṣṭākapālādiṣu apṛthaganuṣṭheyatayā siddheṣu karmasvapi, tathā - aṣṭākapālādyeka deśaphalakīrtanaṃ viśiṣṭakadeśānuvādarūpam dṛṣṭaṃ hi ! - 'vaiśvānaraṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ nirvapet putre jāte' iti vaiśvānaradevatākāṃ jāteṣṭiṃ vidhāya, anupadameva 'yadaṣṭākapālo bhavati gāyatriyaivainaṃ brahmavarcasena punāti yannavakapāla:' ityādikaṃ pratyekameka deśa- phalotkīrtanarūpaṃ karmakāṇḍe śrutameva khalu !, tena – pṛthakphalakīrtanasya stutiparatvenā'nyathā- siddhatvaniścayena, ekaikopāsanasya ninditatvāvagamena ca (tathā) samastopāsanaphalasya pṛthagante kīrtanenā'pi ca samastaḥ cintyaḥ vaiśvānarātmā samagratrailokyaśarīratayā viśiṣṭa-

t

mahāvākyatātparyavṛtyā -- vivakṣitārthabodhanasādhanabhūtena prakaraṇaparyālocanāvagatena mahāvākyatātparyeṇa sāmastyasyaiva yoge - vaiśvānarātmasambandhinikhilāvayavastomasyaiva upāsana- sambandhe, draḍhimavati – acālyatayā niścite sati vyasteṣu — avayava stomaika deśabhūteṣu upāsanaviṣayeṣu mūrdhādiṣu, aṃśānanūdya phalokteḥ – ekaikaśaḥ anuvādapūrvakaṃ 'sutaṃ prasutamāsutaṃ kule dṛśyate' ityādinā pratyekaphalavarṇanasya, aṃśānanūdya stutiniyatanayāt — jāteṣṭirūpadva | daśa- kapālapuroḍāśasādhyayāgaikadeśabhūtāṣṭākapālanava kapālādyanuvādapūrvakaṃ praśaṃsātatpara vākyagataṃ nyāya- manurudhya, ānyaparyam – śabdavācyārthatātparya rāhityena upāsanaikadeśasyā'pi prāśastyabodhana- damparyameva grāhmam, (naśveka deśopāsana vidhānaparatvam ), bhūmavākye — bhūmavidyā sambandhini, nāmādīnāmupāstau nāma-vāṅ - manaādīnāmupāsane kṛte prāpyatayā śrūyamāṇam phalam - nāmagocarayāvadeśakā macārādirūpaṃ phalam avadhitayā apekṣitam – uttarottaropāsanajanya phalo- tkarṣanirūpakatvena utkarṣabodhaupayikam evam vaiśvānara vidyāyāmiva pratyavāyaḥ – ekaikaśo- pāsanasya phalabhūtaḥ anarthaḥ, na śruta iti — bhūmavidyaikadeśa bhūtānāmādyupāsane phalatayā śrutau na nirdiṣṭa iti hetoḥ, viṣamodāhṛtiḥ - dṛṣṭāntataupayikasāmyarahitasya dṛṣṭāntatvenopādānam, na arthasiddhayai—abhimatārthalā bhopayogi na bhavati || 62 ||

śabdādibhedādhikaraṇam || 3-3-24 ||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ ||
phalacodanādyabhedādekatvaṃ syāt sadādividyānām | naivaṃ saṃjñārūpaprabhedato bhinnataiva syāt || 44 ||

sadādividyānām - (brahmaprāptyeka phalānāṃ ) sadvidyā - bhūmavidyā prabhṛtikānāṃ paramātmopāsa- nātmikānāṃ vedāntoditānāṃ vidyānām, phala- codanādyabhedāt- brahmaprāptirūpaphalasya 'dhyāyet, vidyāt, upāsīta' ityādirūpāvicchinnasmṛti santativiṣayaka vidhyādeśca bhedābhāvāt ekatvaṃ syāt — abheda eva bhavitumarhati (iti pūrvapakṣe ) naivam – evaṃ śaṅkanamasādhu, ( yataḥ) saṃjñārūpa- prabhedataḥ sadvidyā- bhūmavidyā - dahara vidye 'tyā dikānāṃ vidyānāmadheyānāṃ anekatvāt, (tathā) upāsyākārādīnāṃ rūpāṇāṃ bhinnatvādapi hetoḥ, bhinnataiva syāt – ( vidyānāṃ ) nānātvameva siddhayet ; na tvekatvamiti || 44 ||

ākhyā- rūpabhedāt – 'sadvidyā, bhūmavidyā' ityāditattadvidyā viśeṣasaṃjñānām (tathā) vedyā - kārākhyasya vidyāsambandhino rūpasya ca bhinnatvāt, tadupahitavidhau – tattadguṇaviśeṣopadhānavato vedyasya vedane vidhīyamāne sati, tena - upadhīyamānena vedyaviśeṣaṇabhūtena tena tena guṇena, vaiśiṣṭyasiddheḥ—–vidhisvarūpe viśeṣasya vailakṣaṇyasya lābhāt iha - vedāntoditāsu vividhāsu vidyāsu, madhvādividyā phalaṃ mitraṃ pṛthak ca - madhuvidyādirūpāsu kāsucana vidyāsu vidyāsādhyaṃ phalaṃ phalāntarasambhinna anyāsu kāsucit tadasambhinnaṃ ceti yathāyathaṃ phalasaṃyoge'pi bhedo'varja- nīyaḥ | (ataḥ saṃyogarūpacodanā'khyāsu sarvāsvapi bheda uktaḥ) evam – mokṣaikaphalāsu brahmavidyāsviva, kāmyavidyāsu ca - mokṣetaraphalāsu dṛṣṭividyā prabhṛtikā svapi saṃyogādiviśeṣakṛto viśeṣaḥ ū iti || 63 ||
nānā śabdādibhedāditi kathamavadat sūtrakṛcchandasāmye
na hyete yāga-dānādaya iva midurā bhaktiviśrāntisiddheḥ |
satyaṃ śabdasya bhedastvayamupacarito rūpabheda' draḍhimne '
jñāna ye cā'vidheyaṃ karaṇamiti jagustannirāse'bhisandhiḥ || 64 ||

śabdasāmye upāsana vidhāyinā 'dhyāyīta, upāsīta, vidyāt' ityādiśabdānāṃ paryāyatvanibandhane abhede sphuṭe sati sūtrakṛt — śārīrakasūtrapraṇetā bādarāyaṇaḥ, nānā śabdādibhedāt' iti kathamavadat – uktena sūtreṇa śabdādīnāṃ bhedaṃ kathaṃ vā uktavān ? ete dhyānopāsana vedanādiśabdābhidheyā arthāḥ, yāga- dānādaya iva - 'devatoddeśena dravyatyāgo yāgaḥ, svasvatvāpākaraṇapūrvakaṃ parasvatvāpādanaṃ dānam, tyāgapūrvakadeśa viśeṣa viśiṣṭadravya prakṣepo homa:' ityevaṃ viviktasvarūpā yāgadānaprabhṛtayo'rthā iva, na hi bhidurā:- na khalu mitho vilakṣaṇasvarūpā bhavanti, bhaktiviśrāntisiddheḥ - dhyānopāsanādyarthānāṃ ' vedanaṃ dhyānaviśrāntaṃ dhyānaṃ śrāntaṃ dhruvasmṛtau | sā ca dṛṣṭitvamabhyeti dṛṣṭirbhaktitvamṛcchati' ityuktayā rītyā bhaktau paryavasānasyaiva siddhāntasiddhatvāt, ( iti śaṅkane) satyam – kiṃcidaṃśe'ṅgīkārya evā'yamākṣepaḥ, ayaṃ śabdasya bhedastu – sūtroktaḥ śabdabhedaḥ param rūpabhedadra ḍhimne vidyābhedāpādaka rūpa- bhedāvagatidārvyārtham, upacaritaḥ – amukhyayā vṛttyā prayuktaḥ, (śabda bhedapadena pūrvakāṇḍokta- śabdāntaraparyāyeṇa atra rūpabheda eva vivakṣita iti ) ye ca – ye punadvaitavādinaḥ, avidheyaṃ jñānam - vidhātumanaī vākyādhīna siddhimat tatvamasyādimahāvākyārthajñānameva, karaṇamiti

jaguḥ - mokṣasādhanamityudghoṣitavantaḥ, tannirāse-tasya sarvathā śāstratātparyāviṣayatākhyāpane, abhisandhiḥ – paramaṃ tātparyamiti | (vidheyabhūtajñānasyaiva vidyopāsanādināmnaḥ mokṣasādhana- tvāt ) || 64 ||

yadvā śabdādibhedāditi tu kathayatā sūtrakāreṇa samyaṅ-
nyāsopāse vibhakte yajana-havanavacchabdabhedādabhāktāt |
ākhyā rūpādibhedaḥ śruta itarasamaḥ kiṃ ca bhinno'dhikāraḥ

śīghraprātyādibhiḥ syājjaguriti ca madhūpāsanādau vyavasthām || 65 ||
yadvā—athavā, (vyākhyānaprakārāntarametat ) śabdādibhedāditi tu kathayatā- (padāntaraṃ vihāya ) ' śabdādibhedāt ' itīmameva viśiṣṭaṃ śabdaṃ prayuñjānena, sūtrakā reṇa- bādarāyaṇena, abhāktāt — śabda bhedapada mukhyārthabhūtāt, śabdabhedāt – paryāyatvarahitavilakṣaṇa- śabdātmakāt (pūrvakāṇḍe śabdāntarapadenā'bhihitāt ) śrutyādiṣu vidyamānāt nyāsopāsana- vidhirūpāt 'śaraṇaṃ vraja, bhajasva' ityādikāt (śabdabhedāt ) yajana - havanavat - yajati - juhotibhyāṃ bhinnaśabdābhyāṃ yāga- homāviva, nyāsopāse brahmaprātyekaphale prapattiḥ bhaktiścetī me ubhe api vidye, samyak vibhakte - pṛthaktayā spaṣṭaṃ pratipādite, ākhyārūpādibhedaḥ prapattiriti bhaktiriti ca dvayoḥ saṃjñābhedaḥ, mokṣārthopāyasthāna niviṣṭatvaṃ satyatva- jñānatvānanda- tvādikaṃ ceti dvayoḥ rūpabhedaḥ (evamevāvāntara bhedādyapi) śīghra - vilambitaprātyādi ceti phale bhedaḥ, śrutaḥ - vidhāyaka prakaraṇeṣu pratipanna: sarvo'pi, itarasamaḥ – karmakāṇḍīyayāgahomadānādiṣviva atrāpi samānaḥ, kiṃ ca— ito'dhikaṃ kiṃcidasti, adhikāra : mokṣārtha bhaktau pravartamānasya prapattau pravartamānasya ca cetanasya pravṛtyaītāpādako dharmaḥ, śīghraprātyādibhiḥ bhinnaḥ syāt - etaddehāvasāna eveti janmāntarāvāptikṛta vilambā sahiṣṇutva - tatsahiṣṇutvādikṛtaḥ, vilakṣaṇa eva bhavet iti ca - evaṃ rītyaiva ca madhūpāsanādau – madhuvidyā dṛṣṭavasvā diśarīrakaparamātmopāsana- tādṛśabhagavatprapaśyorapi, vyavasthām — sūtrakāravivakṣitāṃ vasvādipadaprāptipūrvaka paramātmaprāpti- sākṣādbhagavatprāptibhyāṃ pratiniyatasvarūpatām, jaguḥ– sūtrāśayavarṇanaparāḥ ācāryāḥ sphuṭīkṛta-

}-

vantaḥ || 65 |

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||
nānāśabdādhikaraṇe vaiśvānaranayakramāt |
sadakṣihārdādidhiyā maikyaśaṅkā vyapodyate || 152 ||

madhyo'pi nānā vidyāḥ, adhikānanda siddhayai ānandādhikyalābhamuddiśya, (svargaphala gatādhi- kyāyeva ) samuccityarhāḥ syuḥ ekaikasminnapi puruṣe samuccayena anuṣṭhānayogyā bhaveyuḥ, kaścit bhajanavidhiḥ yaḥ ko'pi upāsanātmaka brahmavidyāvidhiḥ, ekaṃ prati na ca syāt- yaṃ kaṃcidekaṃ puruṣamuddiśya naiva bhavitumarhati (śāstrasya lokahitāya pravṛttatvāt ) tā:- nānā- bhūtā api vidyā:, (karma) kālabhedāt- bhinna-bhinna kālaviśeṣamavalambya kartum – anuṣṭhitum, ( upāsitum ) kṣamam – sādhyaṃ bhavati iti na – evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na sādhu, mithaḥ- parasparam (vidyābhiḥ) vāsanā sthairya bāghāt – ekaikavidyā sambandhicintanīyārthaviśeṣaviṣayaka saṃskāradā sya pratighātāt, (upāsanaśaithilyaprasaṅgena akṛtasāmyāpatteḥ ) iha mokṣopāyavidhāyini vedānte, sampūrṇa brahmadhye paripūrṇabrahmānubhavarūpaṃ phalamuddiśya, prāyaṇānte samādhau vidhiḥ—yāva- dāyuṣam aharahanuṣṭheya nirantara parabrahmopāsanarūpasamā dhiviṣayako vidhiḥ, pṛthak ca pratyekameva, (ekaikasya puruṣasyaikaika vidyāviṣayaka eveti na samuccitānuṣṭhāne śāstratātparya viṣayateti vikalpitānuṣṭhānārhā eva vidyāḥ || 66 ||

rūpādīnāṃ viśeṣairnanu parabhajanaṃ naikarūpaṃ vibhakta
sāmagrībhedatastaphalamapi viṣamaṃ 'saṃmataṃ nyāyataḥ ' syāt | na syāt sarvāsu vidyāsvapi hi ! phalatayā vakṣyate bhogasāmya

prāpyaikyaṃ ca prasiddha bahusaraṇijuṣāṃ lokato vedataśca || 67 ||

janānām prāpyaikyaṃ ca – ekamātragamyasthānavatvam, lokato vedataśca prasiddham – loke nadī-- grāmārāmādigantṛṣu vede'pi vividheṣṭāpūrtādyavalambiṣu kāmyaphalaviśeṣaprepsuṣu ca ( viṣayabhūteṣu )- laukikāstikajanāvagatamiti ( na phalaikyavirodhaḥ) || 67 ||

phalitvācca yadeva vidyaye 'ti vākyāvagatavīryavattaratvarūpaphalasādhanabhūtā vidyā, kimāśritya vīryavattaratvaṃ janayatītyākāṅkṣāyām, 'udgīthamupāsīta ' iti upāsanaviṣayabhūtamudgīthamāśrityeti avagamena, asyaivā''śrayavidheḥ vīryavattaratvarūpaphala sambandhasatvāt uktārthe eva dāt || 46 ||

-0-

- iva atrā'pi, 'yadeva vidyayā karoti' iti (kuryāditi vidhirūpaṃ vihāya ) laṭlakāreṇa yat vartamānakālikatvaṃ vyapadiṣṭaṃ tat jñāpakatvenāsssśrityā'pi, parṇamayīnyāyānusaraṇaṃ nā'tra sambhavatītyarthaḥ || 68 ||
tādarthyaṃ nātra karma śrutiravagamayedāśrayā lambamātrād
vidyāhānau ca yukta pratividhivacanaṃ tatphalārthiprasaṅge |
tāre sopāsane'stu stavanamanugamāt tāvatā sā tu nāṅga
prāg vaktavyasya hitvā vaca idamupari sthāpanīyaprasaktayai ||69||

atra – udgīthopāsanavidhivākye 'udgīthamupāsīte' tyatra, karmaśrutiḥ – 'udgītham ' iti dvitīyā śrutiḥ, tādarthyam – taduddeśya kasya tadupakārakatvāparaparyāyam aṅgatvam, na aba- gamayet — naiva bodhayituṃ śaknuyāt āśrayālambamātrāt – upāsanasya udgīthākhyāśrayāva- lambanasyaiva kevalaṃ dvitīyayā avagamāt vidyāhānau ca - udgātṛkartṛkasya udgīthapraṇavaikya- vijñānarūpavedanasya abhāvarūpe durudgīthe aśaktyādinā samāpatite ca tatphalārthiprasaṅge– udgīthopāsana phalabhūta kratuvīryavattaratva rūpaphalakāminaḥ karmādhikāre prastute, prati vidhivacanam - durudgītha prayuktadoṣa prahāṇataḥ prakārāntareṇa kāmitaphala sampādanopāyakathanam (hotṛgatodgītha- praṇavaikyavijñānataḥ tatsamādhānapradarśanam ) yuktam – nyāyyameva khalu, anugamāt – ' teneyaṃ trayī vidyā vartate ' ityādinā vedatraye'pi omityasya āśravaṇa-śaṃsanodgāneṣvanuvṛttānvayoktitaḥ, sopāsane tāre—upāsanarūpavedanasambaddhe praṇave, stavanamastu - prāśastyaṃ pratipāditaṃ bhavatu nāma, tāvatā – praṇavasya sopāsanasya praśaṃsanamātreṇa, sā tu praṇavopāsanā param, na aṅgam- ṛtvaṅgatvena naiva siddhā bhavati, (puruṣārthabhūta tadvarṇanasya praśaṃsāphalitve virodhābhāvāt ) prāka- vaktavyasya tannirdhāraṇa niyamāghikaraṇe eva upapādyasya kratvaṅgatvābhāvāṃśasya, hitvā — tatsthāna- gatatvaṃ vihāya, upari-pādānte atra, idaṃ vacaḥ kratudgīthopāsanayoḥ aṅgāṅgibhāvābhāva- varṇanamidam, sthāpanīyapramatyai - turye pāde (aṅgapāde) vidyāṃ prati aṅgāṅgibhāvapratipādanasya - prasaṅgākhyasaṅgatisampattaye eva (ataḥ atra sthāne cintanaṃ sthāne eva) | 69 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||
udgīthopāsti niyamo vikalpaniyamo yathā | iti śaṅkā'podyate'tra yathāśrayanaye punaḥ || 154 ||
evaṃ tṛtīyādhyāye'smin tṛtīyacaraṇe'pi ca | ṣaḍviṃśatyadhikārāṇāṃ saṅgatiḥ samudāhṛtā || 155 ||

nasya puruṣopāsanasya mitho vibhinna svarūpatā, vedhādyadhyayanāṅgatā - 'śukraṃ pravidhye' tyādeḥ vedhādi- mantrasya vidyāvidhāyaka vedabhāgādhyayanopakārakatvameva ; na tūpāsanopakārakatvamiti nirṇayaḥ || 48 || hānādyanyonya sambandhaḥ 'kālastaccintanāvidheḥ ' |
sādhāraṇyaṃ saraṇyāśca tathā'sthūlādisāmyatā || 49||

hānādyanyonyasambandhaḥ — yatra sukṛta- duṣkṛtayoḥ hānamātraṃ śrutaṃ upāyanamātraṃ vā tatro bhayatrāpi hānopāyanayorubhayorapi mitho'vinābhāvarūpaḥ sambandhaḥ, taccintanāvidheḥ kālaḥ- viduṣaḥ sukṛtaduṣkṛta- hānopāyana cintana vidhānasya dehādutkramaṇakā lāvacchinnatadviṣayakatā, saraṇyāḥ sādhāraṇyaṃ ca - viduṣaḥ arcirādimārgaḥ ityasya vedāntoditasarva vidyā niṣṭhān pratyapi aviśiṣṭatā, tathā asthūlādisāmyatā – akṣaraparabrahmavidyāmnātānāṃ brahmaguṇānāmasthūlatvādīnāṃ vedāntodi- tāsu sarvavidyāsūpasaṃhāraḥ || 49 ||

prāṇāśanāyādyekatvaṃ tataśca dahakatā |

udgīthopāstya niyamo guṇyā vṛśyanucintanam || 50 ||
prāṇāśanāyādyekatvam — upasta-kaholapraśnaprativacanayoḥ

' yaḥ prāṇena prāṇiti

'yo'śanāyā-pipāse' iti dvividhayorapi pratipannasya upāsanasya abhedaḥ, tataśca tadanantarādhikaraṇe ca, daharaikatā – chāndogya vājasaneyoktayordahara vidyayoḥ abhedaḥ, udgīthopāstya niyamaḥ- karmāṅgāśritodgīthādyupāsanasya ṛtuṣu upādānānaiyatyam guṇyāvṛtyanucintanam - sarveṣvapi

brahmaguṇānāṃ cintaneṣu guṇibhūtasya brahmaṇo'pi cintanā vṛttiḥ || 50 ||
sarvopāsya vinirṇītividyātvaṃ ca manaścitām |
jīvasya guṇito 'pāstiḥ sarvatrodgīthacintanam || 51 ||
sarvopāsya vinirṇītiḥ taittirīyakagatasya 'sahasraśīrṣa 'mityādyanuvākasya vedāntodita- vinirṇītiḥ_taittirīyakagatasya sarvavidyopāsyatatva viśeṣa nirṇāyakatvam, manaścitāṃ vidyātvaṃ ca 'manaścito vākcita: prāṇacitaḥ' ityevamādinā agnirahasyabrāhmaṇoktānāmagnīnāṃ vidyāmayakatvanupraveśa eva; na tu kriyāmayakatvanupraveśa iti nirṇayaḥ, jīvasya guṇitopa stiḥ — jīvātmana upāsane kārye jīvasambandhināṃ nirupādhikānāṃ guṇānāmeva muktikālīnānāmupāsyatā; na tu bandhakālīnānām (yathopāsanaphalaprāpteḥ cintanīyatvāt ) iti nirṇayaḥ, sarvatrodgīthacintanam – udgītha| śrito- pāsanasya udgīthavatsarvakatvanvayitā || 51 ||

vaiśvānarasya sāmastyaṃ sadvidyādervibhinnatā |
vidyānāmasamāhāra udgīthā niyatiḥ punaḥ || 52 ||

vaiśvānarasya sāmastyam — vaiśvānaravidyāyāṃ trailokyaśarīrasyaiva vaiśvānarātmanaḥ samasto- pāsanasya kāryatā ; na vyastopāsanasyeti sthāpanam, sadvidyādeḥ vibhinnatā vedāntoditāsu sarvāsu vidyāsu upāsyasya brahmaṇa ekatve'pi tadupāsanabhūtānāṃ sadādikānāṃ vidyānāṃ mitho bheda eva; natvabhedaḥ, vidyānām asamāhāraḥ- mokṣopāyatvena anuṣṭhīyamānānāṃ vidyānāṃ na samuccaye- nānuṣṭhānaṃ kiṃ tu vikalpenaiveti nirṇayaḥ, punaḥ udgīthāniyatiḥ tannirdhāraṇā niyamāghikaraṇe nirṇītasyaivārthasya tvaṅgā śritodgīthādyupāsanasya kratuṣūpādeyatā niyamābhāvasya punarekavāraṃ

dṛḍhīkāra: || 52 ||

bhedābhedau ca vidyānāṃ guṇānāṃ ca grahāgrahau |
itthametena pādena sādhiṣṭhaṃ paricintitam || 53 ||

vidyānāṃ bhedābhedau–mokṣopāyatayā (dṛṣṭirūpatayā vā ) śrutipratipannānāṃ vidyānāṃ mitho vibhinnasvarūpatva- tadabhāvau, guṇānāṃ ca grahāgrahau – vidyāsu brahmaguṇānāṃ upasaṃhārānupasaṃhārau, ittham —āhatyaivaṃ prakāreṇa etena pādena tṛtīya tṛtīyena guṇopasaṃhārākhyena pādena, sādhiṣṭham – abhyupagantavyeṣvāvaśyakatamaṃ arthajātam, paricintitam - ṣaḍviṃśatisaṃkhyā kevadhi- karaṇeṣu vicārya samyanirṇītaṃ vartate || 53 ||

|| iti śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahe tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

sarvāsvā .... gatiḥ - ānandatvā malatvādikānāṃ brahmasvarūpanirūpakāṇāṃ guṇānāṃ upāsyasya- guṇāśrayasya brahmaṇa iva sarvāsu vidyāsu cintanīyatayā anuvṛttiḥ, prāṇavāsastvadṛṣṭiḥ- ācama- nīyānāmapāṃ mukhyaprāṇaṃ prati vāsarūpatvānusandhānavidhiḥ, śāṇḍilyaikyam - vedaśākhā dvayo- ditayoḥ śāṇḍilya vidyayorekatvam, aharahamoḥ vibhajya sthitiḥ — vyāhṛtiśarī ra katvena ādityamaṇḍale akṣaṇi ca upāsyasya brahmaṇa: 'ahaḥ aham' iti rahasyanāmnoḥ tattadupāsana- pratiniyatatayā pārthakyenāvasthānam, sambhṛteḥ sthānasīmā - vilakṣaṇa vīryasambharaṇa deḥ brahmaguṇa- jātasya viśiṣṭasthānopāsyabrahmopāsaneṣvevānvaya iti sthāna niyamaḥ puṃvidyāyāḥ vibhedaḥ - taittirīyāmnātāyāḥ chāndogyāmnātāpekṣayā pārthakyam, 'śaṃ na' ityādikānām - śaṃ no mitrādi- śāntikaramantrāṇām adhyayananiyatatā - vedabhāgādhyayanamātraśeṣatvena vidyāṅgatvābhāvavyava- sthāpanam (ityete'rthāḥ daśasvadhikaraṇeṣu sādhyāḥ) || 70 ||
hānādyanyonyayogastaducitasamayo devayānādisāmyaṃ
sarvatrā'sthūlatādi vyatiharaṇamathā'neka śiṣyaśrutānām | dahopāstyekabhāvo guṇaphala vidhirudgīthamāśritya dṛṣṭau
guṇyāvṛtti' guṇārthī' nikhila parataropāstivedyāvasāyaḥ || 71 ||

hānādyanyonyayogaḥ - viduṣaḥ mukteḥ pūrvaṃ sukṛta- duṣkṛtayoḥ prahāṇānyatra saṃkramayoḥ cintanaviṣaye sarvatra samuccitya sthitiḥ, taducitasamayaḥ - puṇyapāpaprahāṇa. saṃkramayoḥ yogya :- kālaḥ (dehādutkramaṇakālaḥ) devayānādisāmyam – sarvavidhavidyā niṣṭhānāmapi viduṣāṃ devayāna -- padavācyasya arcirādimārgaprayāṇasya aviśiṣṭatā, sarvatrā'sthūlatādi - vedāntoditasarva- vidyāsvapiṃ cintanīyaṃ brahmasvarūpanirūpakāṇām asthūlatvādiguṇānāṃ bṛndam atha aneka śiṣya- śrutānām vyatiharaṇam - anantaram dvābhyāṃ uṣasta- kaholābhyāṃ (yājñavalkyaṃ prati ) śāsanīyatvena pṛṣṭatayā śrutito'vagatānām, 'yaḥ prāṇena prāṇiti' iti, 'yo'śanāyā - pipāse' iti coktānā- mubhayatrāpyuttaratayā'nvitya cintanīyatvam, dahropāstyekabhāvaḥ — daharavidyayoḥ chāndogya- bṛhadāraṇyakāmnātayoḥ aminnasvarūpatā, udgīthamāśritya dṛṣṭau - karmāṅgabhūtamudgīthamavalambya pravṛtteḥ upāsane 'udgīthamupāsīte 'ti śrute udgīthopāsanarūpaguṇasya vīryavattaratvarūpaphala sambandhavidhāna- rūpatvam guṇārthī guṇyā vṛttiḥ – daharavidyota pratibrahmaguṇacintanārtham āśrayasya brahmaṇa --

ścintana vṛttiḥ nikhila....vasāya : – taittirīya gatasahasraśīrṣānuvākena sarvaparavidyopāsya paratatva- nirdhāraṇam, (ityete'rthāḥ ekādaśādhikaraṇataḥ ekonaviṃśādhikaraṇapūrti yāvat navasu adhikaraṇeṣu saṃsādhanīyāḥ pravṛttāḥ ) || 71 ||
vidyārūpā manaścitprabhṛtaya ucitajñānarūpakratusthāḥ

kṣetrī śuddho'nucintyaḥ kratuguṇa sakalodgīthapūrveṣu dṛṣṭi: |
sāmastyenaiva vaiśvānarabhajanamathā neka vidyopapatti-
mokṣārthānāṃ vikalpaḥ punaraniyatirudgīthadṛṣṭerihoce || 72 ||

vidyārūpāḥ manaści prabhṛtayaḥ — 'manaścito vākcitaḥ' ityādinā ammirahasyabrāhmaṇo- kta: cetavyā jñānarūpā ammayaḥ, jñānarūpakratusthāḥ - vidyāmayakatvanupraveśina eva iti ucitāḥ – abhyupagantavyatvaucityavantaḥ iti nirṇaya:, ('te haite vidyācita eva' ityukteḥ) kṣetrījīvaḥ, śuddhaḥ – āvirbhūtanirupādhika svarūpavāneva, anucintyaḥ - bhagavadupāsanā ṅgabhūta- svopāsanagocaraḥ ; ( na tu bandhakālīna kartṛtvādyopādhikadharmavattayā upāsyaḥ) iti vyavasthāpanam kratuguṇa sakalodgīthapūrveṣu — ṛtvaṅgabhūtodgīthaprabhṛtiṣu sarveṣu (sarvaśākhāmnā teṣvapi ) dṛṣṭiḥ— upāsanāsambandhaḥ, vaiśvānarabhajanam - vaiśvānarākhyaparamātmopāsanaṃ vaiśvānara vidyāmnātam, sāmastye- naiva – samastatayaiva ; na tu vyastatayā atha anekavidyopapattiḥ anantaraṃ mokṣārthānām upāsanānāṃ nānātvameva ; na tvekatvamityaṃśasamarthanam, mokṣārthānāṃ vikalpaḥ – mokṣopāyabhūtānāṃ vidyānāṃ sad-dahara-vaiśvānarādyākhyānām ekasmin puruṣe asamuccayataḥ pākṣikamekamātrānuṣṭhānam, punaḥ udgīthadṛṣṭeḥ aniyatiḥ tannirdhāraṇā niyamādhikaraṇe nirṇītasyaiva 'udgīthopāsanasya niyamenā'nuṣṭheyatvābhāvaḥ' ityasya punarekavāraṃ dāḍhardhamuddiśyopapādanam (ityeteṃ'śāḥ viṃśādhi - karaṇamārabhya ṣaḍviṃśatyadhikaraṇa mūrtiyāvat samarthitāḥ) || 72 ||

kartṛnirṇāyikāyāḥ - śrutivihitakarmasu kartṛbhūtasya cetanasya yathāsthita svarūpa -- nirṇayaupayikyaḥ, vidyāyāḥ – vedāntavihitāyā upāsanarūpāyā vidyāyāḥ, karmāṅgatvāt- kartṛdvārā karmopakārakatvāt tat karma – vedāntavihitavidyāṅgakaṃ karmakāṇḍoditaṃ karmaiva, phalāni datte - puruṣārtharūpāṇi phalāni prayacchati ; na tu aṅgabhūtā vidyā ; aṅgina eva phalapradānasāmarthyasya nyāyyatvāt – ( iti śaṅkane) naitat -vidyāyāḥ karmaprati aṅgatvaṃ, karmaṇaḥ phalapradatvaṃ cetyubhayamapi naiva yuktam, jīvā .... katvāt — jīvaśarīrakaḥ jīvaniyantā ca ya īśvaraḥ parabrahmabhūtaḥ tasyaiva nirṇāyakatvādvedāntodita vidyāyāḥ sarvasyā api, vidyā datte - vidhaiva puruṣārtharūpāṇi phalāni prayacchati ; na karma, karma ca karma tu, amuṣyāḥ aṅgaṃ tu — asyā vidyāyāḥ aṅgameva || 54 ||

apauruṣeyavacasām — svatantrapuruṣoccāritatvarūpapauruṣeyatvarahitānāṃ

artheṣu – svargāpavargādiphalavatsu yāgopāsanādirūpeṣu setikartavyatā keṣvartheṣu, naisargikam -
svabhāvāyattam, aidamparyam – bodhanatātparyam (karma), sākṣāt - mukhyayā vṛyā, akṣarasiddhi- paryavasitaḥ—varṇagrahaṇamātre
paryavasitaḥ varṇagrahaṇamātre viśrāntaḥ, svādhyāyavidhyāśayaḥ –' svādhyāyo'dhyetavyaḥ

-ityasyā'dhyayanavidherabhiprāyaḥ vyāhanti viruṇaddhi, iti ye vadanti ityevaṃ ye varṇayanti, āyuṣmadbhiḥ – bāliśabuddhi mistaiḥ prābhākaraiḥ, japavidhinyāyena – triḥ svādhyāyaṃ vedamadhīyīta 'vedameva japennityam ' iti vedajapavidhāyakavākyārthanirṇaya rītitaḥ (japa vidhervarṇāvṛttiparatāyāḥ svābhāvikatve'pi pada- tadarthasaṅgatijñasya svata eva bhavan arthabodho japavidhinā yathā na vāryate ; tathā adhyayanavidhāvapi vidherakṣaragrahaṇamātra viśrāme'pi svataḥ sambhavato'rthabodhasya na tena pratibandhaḥ iti vicintya) santuṣyatām — vedānāṃ nirarthakatā pattiriti khedaṃ visṛjya sthīyatām śabdaḥ - vidhirūpaḥ śabdaḥ ananyalabhya viṣaye pramāṇānta- rataḥ aprāpte evārthe, samujjṛmbhate — niraṅkuśavyāpṛtimān bhavati ( ityayaṃ nyāyavidabhyupa- gato'rthaḥ || 98 ||

yaḥ svārtheṣvanidaṃparāḥ śrutigiraḥ svādhyāyavidhyāśayā-
dācaṣṭe sa vikalpamarhati vidhiḥ sārthaḥ kimāsthīyatām |
yadvā'narthaka ityanena vacasā tulyatvamāsāṃ girā-
māghe syāditaratra tatpratibhaṭaḥ kiṃsyādakasmādvidhiḥ || 99 ||

yaḥ _ ' svādhyāyo'dhyetavyaḥ' ityasya arthajñānaparyavasāyitva varṇanāgrahī yaḥ prābhākaraḥ, svādhyāya vidhyāyāt – 'akṣaragrahaṇa vidhānamātreṇa viratavyāpāraḥ adhyayanavidhiḥ, ityasyāśaya- syānurodhataḥ, śrutigiraḥ - vedavācaḥ, svārtheṣu anidaṃparāḥ ācaṣṭe - svabodhyeṣvartheṣu bodhana- tātparya rahitā bhaveyuriti āpādayati, saḥ āpādako mīmāṃsakaikadeśī, vikalpamarhati - vakṣyamāṇarītyā vikalpya praśnaviṣayībhavitumarhati (kimiti ?) vidhiḥ – ' svādhyāyo'dhyetavyaḥ ityayaṃ vidhiḥ, kiṃ sārtha: āsthīyatām ? yadvā anarthaka iti āsthīyatām iti - - svārthatātparyaka iti aṅgīkriyatā vā ? svārthatātparyakatvarahita iti abhyupagamyatāṃ vā? iti, ādye—arthaparatvāṅgīkārapakṣe, āsāṃ girām – sarvāsāmapi vedavācām, anena tulyatvaṃ syāt - (svārthagrahaṇa vidhāyaka vidhyantarasyā'bhāve'pi yathā adhyayanavidhiḥ gṛhītārtha eva bhavati svābhāvyāt, tadvat sarvasya vedasya arthagrahaṇavidhāyaka vākyā bhāve'pi svabhāvādeva gṛhītārthatā siddhā bhavediti) adhyayayavidhinā sāmyaṃ sidhyati itaratra - adhyayanavidhiḥ anarthaka āsthīyate iti pakṣe, vidhiḥ—adhyayanavidhiḥ akasmāt – vinaiva niyāmakam, tatpratibhaṭaḥ kiṃ syāt :- svato vyutpattibalād bhavataḥ arthajñānasya virodhī bhavati kim ? naiva; (pakṣadvaye'pi arthajñāna- siddheḥ pratibandhābhāva iti) || 99 ||

yacchroturgṛhamedhino gurugṛhe kāṣṭhodakādyāhṛti- vyāsaktasya na sambhavatyavasara stattatsvakarmakrame | syāccadgurvanuvartanādyanuguṇaḥ kutrā'vakāśo bhave-
dityuktaṃ katibhiścidatra kuśalairitthaṃ samādhīyate || 100 ||

gṛhamedhinaḥ svīkṛtagṛhasthāśramasya śrotuḥ — vedārthavicāraśravaṇapravṛttasya, gurugṛhe kāṣṭhodakādyāhṛtivyāsaktasya - ata eva gurukule avasthāya guruśuśrūṣoddeśena edho - jalādīnāṃ gurvarthānāmāharaṇe tatparasya sataḥ, tattatsvakarmakrameteṣāṃ teṣāṃ svasvavarṇāśramavidditadharmāṇāṃ pūrvāparībhā banaiyatya sa hitānuṣṭhānanairantarye, avasaro na sambhavati — samayo naiva sidhyati, syāccet- svadharmānuṣṭhāne samayalābho yadi bhavati, gurvanuvartanādyanuguṇaḥ avakāśaḥ – guruśuśrūṣaupayikaḥ samayaḥ, kutra bhavet kīdṛśakālāśrito bhavitumarhati ? iti evam, katibhiścit— vidhyarthānuṣṭhānasāmañjasya cintanapravṛttaiḥ kaiścit mīmāṃsakaikadeśibhiḥ, uktam — adhyayanavidhi- vyāpārasya akṣaragrahaṇamātrajñāpanena viśrāntivādināmupari dūṣaṇamuktam, atra - asmin saṅkaṭaprāye viṣaye, kuśalaiḥ itthaṃ samādhīyate nipuṇairdārśanikaiḥ vakṣyamāṇayā rītyā samādhānaṃ

kriyate || 100 ||

yadvedākṣararāśisiddhyuparataḥ svādhyāya vidhyāśaya-

stattanmūlaka śiṣyakṛtya viraterlabdhāvakāśāḥ kriyāḥ |
śrotuḥ śrāvayituryathetyavasaraḥ śrotuśca siddhayettathā

snehena spṛhayā ca vitta-yaśasoḥ śiṣyaṃ guruḥ śrāvayet || 101 ||

yat- - yena hetunā, svādhyāya vidhyāśayaḥ - ' svādhyāyo'dhyetavyaḥ' itmasyā'dhyayanavidheḥ abhiprāyaḥ, vedā.... parataḥ – vedākhyavarṇasamudāya karmakādhyayanasampādyatvajñāpanamātreṇa viśrānta- vyāpāraḥ, tat — tena hetunā, śrotuḥ – vedārthavicāraśravaṇodyuktasyā'pi gṛhasthasya, tanmūla... virateḥ vedaprāptiphalakatayā śāstraprāptasya guruśuśrūṣārūpasya śiṣyānuṣṭheya kriyākalāpasya vedalābhā- nantarakāle nivṛtteḥ kriyāḥ – nitya naimittikābhidhāḥ karmakalāpāḥ, labdhāvakāśā iti- sampannasamayā iti hetoḥ, śrāvayituryathā-vedārthaśrāvakasyācāryasyeva, śrotuśca śravaṇa- pravṛttasya śiṣyasyā'pi avasaraḥ sidhyet — samaya labhyetaiva tathā — adhyayanakāle śiṣyakṛtayā paricaryayeva, (vicāraśravaṇamaye) snehena - śiṣyavātsalyena, vitta- yaśasoḥ spṛhayā ca- lābha-khyātyorviṣayiṇyā icchayā ca sahakāribhūtayā, guruḥ- vidvān ācāryaḥ śiṣyaṃ
