- karma prāk cintayitvā nanu paramamatha brahma jijñāsyamuktaṃ

pāde vidyāṅgatoktiḥ punariha vitathā'nekadhā tyājyatokteḥ | maivaṃ karmaiva tattadguṇayuta viniyuktayanyabhāvena bhinna
vidyāniṣpattihetuḥ kimapi ca sukṛtaṃ syānnivṛśyeka' saṃjñam || 1 ||

prāk — brahmavicārāt pūrvam, karma cintayitvā tatpaścādbhāvena, paramaṃ brahma – sarveśvarābhidhānaṃ paraṃ brahma jñānagocaratāmāpādanīyamiti, ukta nanu -' athāto brahmajijñāse 'ti śāstrārambhapratijñāsūtra eva pratipāditaṃ khalu, (heyopādeyayormadhye 'heyasya prathamaṃ cintopādeyasya tataḥ param' iti rītyā karmaṇaḥ prathamaṃ vicārya tyaktatvāt ) anekadhā - nānāprakāreṇa, tyājyatokteḥ karmaṇāmadhuvatvā- dṛḍhatvabandhakatvādidoṣān nimittīkṛtya parityājyatāyāḥ śāstreṣu varṇitatvāt punaḥ - pūrvameva prastutaṃ karma punarapi prastutya, iha pāde – tṛtīyādhyāyasya caturthe caraṇe, vidyāṅgavoktiḥ- karmaṇāṃ vidyāṅgatvapratipādanaprakriyā, vitathā nirupayogā ( tyājye viṣaye aṅgatvānaṅgatvayo- ścintāyāḥ prekṣāvadbhiḥ kartumanaītvāt ) , maivam — evaṃ śaṅkanamanucitam, karmaivakarmakāṇḍoktaṃ bandhakatvādidoṣatastyājyatayā niścitaṃ yāga-dāna- homādikaṃ karmaiva, tattadguṇa.... bhāvena - phalābhisandhirā hityā diguṇasahabhāvato viniyogakṛtapārthakyena hetunā, bhinnam —–—tyājyatvapratipattigocarāt karmaṇaḥ sakāśādatiriktameva sat, vidyāniṣpattihetuḥ— vidyāpratibandhaka dūrīkaraṇa- satvavivṛddhayā didvārā vidyāsiddheḥ prayojakaṃ bhavati, kimapi ca-

karmasvarūpaṃ vicārya, atha
jijñāsyam - abhīpsitavicārākhya-

karmakāṇḍoktebhyaḥ karmabhyaḥ atyanta vilakṣaṇamapi ca nivṛttyeka saṃjñam – nivṛttidharmamātratayā bhagavacchāstrādiṣu vyapadiśyamānam sukṛtam - duṣkṛta samparkaleśarahitaṃ sat sukṛtamātrarūpaṃ bhagavatsevātmakaṃ karma ca vartata iti ( tādṛśakarmaṇāṃ vidyāṅgatvanirṇayaḥ etatpādaprayojana- miti ) || 1 ||

i

tyāgaḥ kāmyakriyāṇāṃ kvacana paravidaḥ kā'pyanaī kriyāṇāṃ

svaikādhīnastrabuddheḥ kacidanupadhika svārtha buddheśca gītaḥ | atrā'hiṃsādikānāṃ tvagha' vihatikṛtāṃ sarvasādhāraṇānāṃ
te varṇāśramādipratiniyatimatāmapyupāstyaṅgabhāvam || 2 ||

kvacana - bhagavadgītāyāṃ pradeśaviśeṣe, paravidaḥ parabājñāninaḥ, kāmyakriyāṇāṃ tyāgaḥ – bhagavatprāptītaraphalā pekṣayā pravartyamānānāṃ śrutyādyuditakarmaṇāṃ svarūpato varjanam, kvā'pi — anyatra kvacana sthānaviśeṣe ca anarhakriyāṇām – niṣiddhakarmaṇāṃ svarūpatastyāgaḥ kvacit - aparatra sthānaviśeṣe ca svaikādhīnatvabuddheḥ karmasu jīvasya svātantryeṇa svakartṛtā- buddheḥ tyāgaḥ (tathā) anupadhikasvārtha buddheśca – karmaphaleṣu paramātmaśeṣatāṃ vihāya svātiśaya- prayojanakatvabuddhestyāgaśca gītaḥ — āvaśyakatvena bhagavatā kīrtitaḥ, atra tu – tṛtīyacaturtha- pāde tu adhavihatikṛtām - pāpakṣaya phalakānām, sarvasādhāraṇānām – sarveṣāṃ varṇānāmāśramāṇāṃ ca aviśeṣata ācaraṇīyānām, ahiṃsādikānām – hiṃsāvarjana- steyākaraṇa-satyavadanādīnāṃ dharmāṇām, (tathā) varṇāśra... matāmapi tattadvarṇa tattadāśramādiviśeṣamuddiśya vihitānāṃ karmaviśeṣāṇāmapi ca, upāstyaṅgabhāvam – upāsanarūpāṃ vidyāṃ prati upakārakatvarūpamaṅgatvam, brūte — vicārya nirṇayati bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ || 2 ||

kartā''tmā karmaṇāṃ yastadadhikamiha na brahma tasmānmakhādau
tabuddhayaivopayuktāḥ syurupaniṣada ' ityartha' mīmāṃsakoktau | jīvānyabrahmacintātmakabhajanavidhau karmaṇāmaṅgabhāvaṃ

prāha kṣiptānyaliṅgaḥ kaluṣaśamanataḥ sasvasaṃvardhakānām || 3 ||
karmaṇāṃ kartā - agnihotrādīnāṃ śrautānāṃ smārtānāṃ vā karmaṇāṃ kartṛtvena pratipannaḥ

yaḥ ātmā—yorthaḥ jīvākhyo vartate dehī, tadadhikam — tadapekṣayā atiriktamatiśayitaṃ vā, brahma brahmapadābhidheyaṃ vastu, iha – vedānteṣu vedyabhūtaṃ kiṃcit, na naivāsti, tasmāt -

jīvātiriktabrahmākhyacetana viśeṣābhāvādeva, makhādau – yajñādirūpe karmaṇi, tabuddhayaiva- kartṛbhūtajīvasvarūpavivecanajananatātparyeṇaiva, upaniṣadaḥ trayyantabhāgāḥ, upayuktāḥ syuḥ- prāptaprayojanā bhaveyuḥ, iti – evaṃ rītyā, ardhamīmāṃsakoktau – vicārya nirṇayasya tattvajātasya ardhe ekadeśe eva viśrāntānāṃ mīmāṃsakānāṃ vyāhāre pravṛtte sati, kṣiptānyaliṅgaḥ— svābhimataviruddhārthagamakatayā ardhamīmāṃsakoktāni liṅgāni pratikṣiptavān san kaluṣaśamanataḥ- vidyaupayike satvaguṇe kaluṣabhāvaṃ prāpnuvatāṃ atirekadaśāpannānāṃ rājasatāmasādyaṃśānāṃ śamanapūrvakam, sattvasaṃvardhakānām – satvātirekāpādakānām, karmaṇām — śrauta - smārtātmakānāṃ nitya naimittikā - dirūpāṇāṃ ca karmaṇām, jīvānya..... vidhau – (cetanebhyaḥ) pratyagātmabhyaḥ sakāśāt atiriktasya brahmapadābhidheyasya paramātmanaḥ nirantaradhyānarūpe upāsane (mānase karmaṇi), aṅgabhāvam - atiśayādhāyakatvarūpam aṅgatvam, prāha – nirūpayati bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ || 3 || pādairatraivamekādaśabhirapi paraṃ brahma vedyaṃ nirūḍhaṃ

bhūyaḥ kiṃ kṣudraliṅgairgaganalipi' nibhai: ' kṣobhyate brahmavidyā |
satyaṃ tattādṛgalpaśrutamatikalaha trāsitacchātra ḍimbha-
stomakṣemāya jaiminyahṛdayakathitaṃ pakṣamutkṣipya hanti || 4 ||
atra — brahmavicārārthameva pravṛtte śārīrakamīmāṃsāśāstre, evam ekādaśabhiḥ pādairapi - āditaḥ etadantaiḥ ekādaśasaṅkhyaiśvaraṇairapi paraṃ brahma – sarvātiśāyi brahmākhyaṃ vastveva, vedyaṃ nirūḍham – mumukṣubhiravaśyaṃ vicārya (śravaṇādidvārā) sākṣātkaraṇīyamiti ca suṣṭhu dṛḍhīkṛta-- māste, (evaṃ sthite) bhūyaḥ—punarasmin pāde, gagana lipinibhaiḥ– vyomalikhitavarṇarekhāsamaiḥ ata eva akiñcitkaraiḥ, kṣudraliṅgaiḥ alpairgamakaiḥ (pūrvapakṣodbhāvanahetubhiḥ) brahmavidyā- vedāntavicārātmikā (mumukṣūṇāṃ hṛdaye nirūḍhā vidyā hetutvā dvidyā) vidyā, kim — kasmai prayojanāya, kṣobhyate— cālyate, apanītyupakṣepeṇa sakampā kriyate !, (iti śaṅkane) satyam - brahmaṇo'ghikatvaṃ nirūḍhamastīti yaducyate tadasandigdhameva ; param, tattādṛgalpa... kṣemāya- vilakṣasvabhāvānāṃ kiṃcijjñānāṃ vipratipattimahimnā prāptasādhvasāḥ ye apariṇatā antevāsinaḥ tadvṛndasya hitakāmanayā ( pravṛtto) bādarāyaṇaḥ, jaiminya.... thitaṃ pakṣam – jaiminyṛṣiṇā pūrvamīmāṃsāyāṃ tātparyāntareṇoktaṃ jīvātiriktabrahmābhāvapakṣam, utkṣipya hanti - atra pāde udbhāvya nirākaroti || 4 ||

kurvanneveti vākyaṃ paravidi niyatācāra toktiḥ 'sahasves' - nvārambho dhīkriyābhyāmapi na niyamayedaṅgatāmaṅgitāṃ vā | vidyāpūrva kriyāṇāṃ karaṇamanuvadadvākyamanyārthayuktaṃ
na hyetad brahmavidyāmanuvadati na codgīthavidyā kriyāṅgam || 5 ||
kurvanneveti vākyam – ' kurvanneveha karmāṇi jijīviṣecchataṃ samāḥ' ityevaṃrūpam īśāvāsyagataṃ vākyam, paravidi — paratattvavettari (brahmajñe), niyatācāratoktiḥ - śrutyādiprati-

pannācārāvarjanarūpaniyamabodhakaṃ vacanam, dhī- kriyābhyām anvārambho'pi - vidyayā karmaṇā ca mitha: (ekasmin viduṣi uttaratra svasvaphalapradānāya ) saṃśleṣoktirapi sahatve – sāmānādhi - karaṇyamātre eva tātparyavatī satī, aṅgatāmaṅgitāṃ vā na niyamayet — vidyāyāḥ karma prati karmaprati aṅgabhāvaṃ vā aṅgibhāvaṃ vā niṣkṛṣya bodhayituṃ naiva śaknuyāt kriyāṇām – vihitāna karmaṇām, karaṇam – anuṣṭhānam vidyāpūrvaṃ vadat – jīva - parayāthātmyajñānānantarabhāvyeva samayaṃ bhavatīti pratipādayat vākyam - gārgibrāhmaṇagataṃ 'yo vā etadakṣaraṃ gārgi viditvā M ityādikaṃ vacanam, anyārtham uktam - vidyārahitasya karmaṇaḥ ('hatā cājñānināṃ kriyā iti rītyā ) upāsananiṣpattyau payikatā vaikalya pratipādanāyaiva pravṛttam ; na vidyāṃ prati aṅgitāyāḥ pratipādanāyeti, hi-yataḥ, etat – 'yo vā etadakṣaraṃ gārgi viditvā .... aviditvā' ityādikaṃ vākyam, brahmavidyām – mokṣārthaparamātmopāsanātmikāṃ vidyām na anuvadati – karmāṅgatva- jñāpanāya anuvāda na karoti ; udgīthavidyā ca - 'omityetadakṣaramudgīthamupāsīte 'ti vihitā udgīthopā stirapi na kriyāṅgam – na karma prati aṅgatvavatī ; vīryavattaratvarūpa phala- viśeṣasiddhinimittatvāditi || 5 ||
svādhyāyaprāptaye hyadhyayanamuditamādhānavannottarā

vidyāṅga'cā'rtha'bodho bhavatu yadadhikā sā svarūpaiḥ phalaiśca | nityātmajñānamātraṃ kratuṣu niyamato'bekṣitaṃ nā'nyavidyā

kāmyatyāgaḥ savidye kathamiha bhavitā sā'pi yadyaṅgameṣām || 6 ||

adhyayanam — adhyayanapadābhidheyaṃ karma, svādhyāyaprāptaye hi - svādhyāyārūyasya svīya kulakramāgatasya śākhāviśeṣasya svāyattīkaraṇāya khalu, uditam – 'svādhyāyo'dhyetavyaḥ'

ityanena śrutivākyena vihitam, na uttarāṅgam - tatpaścātkā labhā vikarmānuṣṭhānāṅgatayā na vihitam, ādhānavat – 'agnīnādadhīte 'ti vākyena yathā kevalā magnisiddhimuddiśyaiva agnyādhā- nākhyaṃ karma vihitam ; na tu tadanantara kālikā gnihotrādikarmānuṣṭhānārthatayā vihitam ; ( sannihita- syaiva sākṣātphalatvaucityāt ) tadvat, arthabodha: - vedantavākyārthaviṣayako bodhaḥ, vidyāṅga ca bhavatu – upāsanātmikāyāḥ brahmavidyāyā upakārakatvataḥ aṅgamapi āstāṃ nāma, yat - yasmāddhetoḥ sā – upāsanarūpā brahmavidyā, svarūpaiḥ- āditaḥ pratyahaṃ kriyamāṇena dhyānābhyāsena prāpsya- mā nātiśayatva viśadatama pratyakṣarūpatva- niratiśaya priyatva brahmaprāpakatvādibhi: svagataiḥ dharmaiḥ, phalaiśca – anādyavidyā niḥśeṣanivṛttipūrvaka brahmaprāpti - tadanantara - niravadhikabrahmānubhavaparamparā dirūpaḥ svaprayojanabhūtairarthaiśca, adhikā vākyārthaśravaṇajanyajñānāpekṣayā sarvātmanā atiśayitā, kratuṣu - śrutivihiteṣu aihikāmuṣmikārtheṣu karmasu niyamataḥ - avarjyatayā, apekṣitam — upayokṣya- māṇatvena sampādanīyam, nitya tmajñānamātram - dehavat dhvaṃsamaprāpnuvataḥ sthāyinaḥ cetanasya, karmaphalānāmanubhavituḥ astitvajñānameva na anya vidyā - jīvātmano'pyadhikaspa sarvaniyantuḥ paramātmanaḥ upāsanarūpaṃ jñānam karmānuṣṭhānopayogitayā naiva apekṣaṇīyaṃ bhavati, (ataḥ sarvathā na karmāṅgatvaṃ brahmavidyāyāḥ) sā'pi - jīvādhikasya brahmaṇo vidyā'pi eṣām – karmakāṇḍoditānā sarveṣāmeva karmaṇām, yadi aṅgam — upakārakamityetat śāstrasaṃmataṃ cet !, savidye – nitya- naimittika kāmyānāṃ sarvakarmaṇāmapyaṅgabhūtatvenāpādyayā brahmavidyayā sampanne puruṣe, iha - vidyāṅga- keṣu karmasu madhye kāmyatyāgaḥ - vidyāṅgakānāmeva satāṃ phalārthānāṃ karmaṇāṃ varjanarūpo'rtha:, kathaṃ bhavitā – kena vā prakāreṇa śāstrābhimato yukta; syāt ? (kasyā'pi kāmyānuṣṭhāturevābhāva- prasaṅgena tadvidhānasyaiva vaiyarthyāpātāt ) || 6 ||

nā'ṅgaṃ vidyā makhādernahi tadadhikṛteṣveva tāmāmanāmaḥ
syāttattatkarmaṇāṃ setyapi na bahuvidhādvaiparītyopadeśāt |
jābālairūrdhvaretovidhirapi paṭhito'nūdyate'nyaiśca tasmāt

prāptigrahyā'nyathā'pi svayamiha tu vidhistanniṣedhaḥ sarāge || 7 ||

vidyā - brahmopāsanātmikā vedāntoditā vidyā, makhāde: - yajñādeḥ karmamātrasya na aṅgam — aṅgatvena na śrutisaṃmatam, hi yataḥ, tām — brahmavidyām tadadhikṛteṣveva — yajñādikarmādhikāravatsu gṛhasthādipuruṣeṣveva, ( tādṛśapuruṣamātrā dhikṛtatvena ) na āmanāma: - śrutau nāghīmahe ; ( kintu karmānadhikṛtapārivrājyavatsvapi ) ato na karmāṅgatā vidyāyāḥ,

tattatkarmaṇāṃ syāt–tattadāśramavihitānāṃ karmaṇāmevāṅgaṃ bhavet, sābrahmavidyā, ityapi na ityevamabhyupagamo'pi nocitaḥ, bahuvidhāt — nānāprakārāt vaiparītyopadeśāt – vidyāyāḥ karma prati aṅgatvaviparītasya aṅgitvasyaiva śrutiṣūpadiṣṭatvāt, ūrdhvaretovidhirapi – sannyāsādi- kānāṃ pārivrājyāśramānāmapi vidhāyakaḥ śrautaḥ śabdaḥ, jābālaiḥ – jābālākhyaśākhā viśeṣā- dhyāyibhiḥ, paṭhitaḥ – ' yadahareva virajet tadahareva pravrajet' ityādirūpeṇāghīto dṛśyate anyaiśca—śākhāntarādhyetṛbhirapi ( chandogairapi ) anūdyate – 'trayo dharmaskandhāḥ' ityādinā kṛtānuvādo dṛśyate, tasmāt uktāt jñāpakāt prāptiḥ grāhyā – anuvādānyathānupapattiṃ nimittīkṛtya vidhāyakavākyamapi kiṃcit tacchaḥ khīyasaṃmatamabhyupetyam, anyathā'pi - vidhāyaka - vākyāntarākalpane'pi ca, iha tu – atraiva svayam – 'trayo dharmaskandhāḥ' ityādivākyameva, vidhiḥ- vidhāyakaṃ bhavitumarhati tanniṣedhaḥ – vīrahā vā eṣa devānām' ityādinā agnityāginindayā pārivrājyāśrama niṣedhaparyavasānam, sarāge - aviraktādhikāriṇi viśrāntaṃ- jñeyam || 7 ||

stutimātrādhikaraṇam ||
śārīrakanyā yakalāpasaṃgrahaḥ ||
karmāṅgodgīthādisambandhamātraṃ dṛṣṭaṃ yasmāt tatstutitvaṃ rasādeḥ |
nā'sānnidhyāt tadvidheyaprakarṣādaprāptatvāccā'tra kalpyo vidhi: syāt || 55 ||

yasmāt – yato hetoḥ karmāṅgo....mātram – kasvavayavabhūtasyodgīthāderupāsanasya sambandha eva kevalam dṛṣṭam – 'sa eṣa rasānāṃ rasatamaḥ paramaḥ parārthyo'ṣṭamo yadudgīthaḥ ' iti śrutivākye jñāto'sti tattasmaddhetoḥ ramādeḥ udgīthādau rasatamatvādiprati- pādakasyoktajātīyavākyasya, stutitvam – siddhānuvādena prāśastya mātrapratipādanaparatvameva yuktam, (iti śaṅkane) na – etadasādhu, asāṃnidhyāt– udgītha dividhiprakaraṇasya | saṃnihitatvāt, tadvidheyaprakarṣāt kratuvidhividheyabhūtakratugataṃ vīryavattaratvarūpaṃ utkarṣa phalabhūtaṃ nimittīkṛtya asya vidheyatāyā aucityāt aprāptatvācca – udgīthādau rasatamatvādeḥ pramāṇāntarataḥ asiddha- tvācca heto:, atra 'sa eṣa rasānāmiti vākye, vidhiḥ – udgīthādau rasatamatvādidṛṣṭividhiḥ, kalpyaḥ syāt — kalpanīya eva bhavet || 55 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||
juhvādistotranītyā bhajatu rasatamā yuktigṅgastutitvaṃ
maivaṃ tattadvidhānaprakaraṇarahitāpūrvanirdeśayukteḥ |
tattaddṛṣṭervidhānaṃ vividhamiha samālakṣi caitatsabhīpe
tenā'nanyārthaśiṣṭe phalavati ca vidhiryujyate kalpyamānaḥ || 8 ||

juhvāditotranītyā' iyameva juhūḥ svarge loka āhavanīyaḥ' iti vākyaṃ yathā juhvāṃ pṛthivītvasya, āhavanīye svargatvasya ca pratipādana mukhena juha havanīyayoḥ praśaṃsāparaṃ tadvat, rasatamādyukti: - ' sa eṣa rasānām' iti udgīthe rasatamatvoktirapi aṅgastutitvam- yajñāṅgabhūtodgīthaprāśastyaparavākyatām bhajatu — prāpnotu na tu vidhitvam, ( iti śaṅkane) maitram — evaṃrītyā na śaṅkanīyam, tattadvi....yukteḥ udgīthā dividhiprakaraṇātmakasāṃnidhyarāhitye sati pramāṇāntarānadhigatārthakathanarūpāt vidhittvajñāpakabhūtāddhetoḥ, (tattadvidhiprakaraṇasthatvaṃ, prāptārthānuvādaśva arthavādatājñāpako; vidhyasānnidhyaṃ aprāptārthakathanaṃ ca vidhitvajñāpakāviti bhāvaḥ) iha – prakaraṇe cā'smin etatsamīpe ca – udgīthe rasatamatvapratipādakavākyasaṃnidhāvapi,

vividham – nānāprakāram, tattaddṛṣṭervidhānam – udgīthe dṛṣṭiviśeṣasya phalaviśeṣārtha jñāpanamapi, samālakṣi— sphuṭaṃ dṛśyamānamevā'sti, (dṛṣṭividhisāṃnidhyamapi asya dṛṣṭividhitva niścāyakamiti bhāvaḥ) tena – uktenā'nena jñāpakajātena, ananyārthaśiṣṭe - itarānupakārakatvena śrutisiddhe, phalavati cakratuvīryavattaratvarūpaphalajanake ca udgīthopāsanākhye kriyāviśeṣe, vidhiḥ kalpyamānaḥ– vidhitva kalpanameva, yujyate - yuktisampanna bhavati ; na tu prāśastyaparatayā -arthavāditvakalpanam (ataḥ anyārthaśiṣṭaphalarahitajuhvādinyāyo nātra svīkārya iti || 8 ||
kiṃ ca prāpterabhāvānna tadanuvadanaṃ nā'dhiropyastutirvā

vidhyekavākye gatiriyamagateḥ sā'tra nā''saktihāneḥ |
utkarṣaḥ kalpyate cedagatikaviṣaye tat prasahya prasahyaṃ

yuktā

madhye vidyāvidhīnāṃ vacanamidamiti syāt tu 'vidyārtha' metat || 9 ||

kiṃ ca – api ca (yukticintāyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ ukte'rtha eva dāyamiti) prāpterabhāvāt - ' sa eṣa rasānā 'mityuktasyārthasya etadatiriktena vākyāntareṇānadhigateḥ, na tadanuvadanam- rasatamatvādikathanaṃ vihitārthānuvādarūpaṃ na bhavati, (ata eva ca ) na adhiropyastutirvā— atādṛśe'rthe āropataścintanīyasyāṃśasya prāśastyakathanapravṛttamapi na bhavatīdaṃ vākyam, iyam- adhiropyastutitvavarṇanarūpā, agateḥ gatiḥ– gatyantararahitasya cintanīyā nirvahaṇarītiḥ, vidhyekavākye yuktā - vidhāyakavākyena sahaikavākyatāpanne vākye eva sāmañjasyavatī na tu tadrahite, sā— tādṛśī rītiḥ, atra na - ' sa eṣa rasānā 'mityādike vākye na yuktā bhavati, āsa (kti) tihāneḥ - vidhivākya saṃnidherabhāvāt (vidhivākyaikavākyatāyā vaktuma- śakyatvāt ) utkarṣaḥ svābhāvikatayā yatra vartate vidhiḥ tatsaṃnihitatvāropeṇa tadaikavākyam, kalpyate cet — (vastuto'vidyamānamapi ) adhyasyate iti cintanamapi na sādhu bhavati, (yataḥ ) prasahya prasahyam - balātkṛtya abhyupagamyam, tat — utkarṣa kalpanam, agatikaviṣaye- nirvāhakopāyāntararahite sthale eva yuktaṃ bhavati na tu dṛṣṭividhiparatayā nirvahaṇayogye prakṛtasthale, vidyāvidhīnāṃ madhye – upāsana vidhāyakānāṃ bahūnāṃ vākyānāṃ madhye, idaṃ vacanamiti - ' sa eṣa rasānā 'miti vākyamastīti hetoḥ, etat prastutaṃ vākyam, vidyārthī syātta- vidyāvidhānaprayojanakaṃ bhavedeveti || 9 ||

'vidyārthatve'pi yuktā stutiriyamasataḥ kīrtanādityayuktaṃ dṛṣṭyuddeśe'ticārādatha ca viśayanaṃ syāditi tvarbhakoktiḥ | nitye'smin hmapramāṇaṃ prasajati nigamo yuktivāryatvapakṣe
svitthambhāve bubhutsye vaca iha phalavad dṛṣṭividhyarthameva || 10 ||

vidyārthatvepi —upāsanavidhitātparyakatve'pi iyam - " " sa eṣa rasānā 'mityevaṃrūpā rasatamatvoktiḥ, asataḥ kīrtanāt – udgītharūpe'rthe asambhavinaḥ rasatamatvasya kathanāddhetoḥ, stutiḥ—prāśastyakathanarūpā'pi bhavitumarhati (anyadīyaguṇānāmanyatra dharmiṇyasatā māropasyaiva stutitvāt ) iti ayuktam — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na yuktimat (kutaḥ ?) dṛṣṭayuddeśe - anyatra anyadṛṣṭividhipare (viṣahā rimantrādiprayogasthalīye 'ātmānaṃ garuḍaṃ dhyāyet' ityādivākye, aticārāt – ' yatrā vidyamānārthakathanarūpatvaṃ tatra stutirūpatvam' iti vyāpteḥ naiyatyabhaṅgāt, tasya stutitvā vyāpyatvāt, atha ca – vyāptiniścayābhāvepi viśayanaṃ syāt – vyāpakatva - saṃśayenā'hitaḥ stutirūpatvasaṃśayo'pi vā bhavitumarhaḥ, iti tu arbhakoktiḥ syāt - evaṃ kathanaṃ tu bāla-bhāṣitameva bhavet ; na tu vyavahāravyutpannavacanaṃ syāt, hi-yataḥ, asmin — saṃśaye, nitye - sārvakālikatayā'nuvartamāne sati, nigamaḥ vedaḥ, apramāṇaṃ prasajati – niścayājananāddhetoḥ (pramājanakatvaṃ hi pramāṇatvamiti ) pramāṇatva rahita āpatati, yuktivāryatvapakṣe tu - saṃśayasya sārvakālikatāṃ vihāya viśeṣadarśanādirūpayā yuktatyā parihārya ityabhyupagame tu, itthambhāve – pramāṇānugrāha ke nyāye, bubhutsye—bodhanīyatayā vivakṣā sati, iha — etatprakaraṇagatam, phalavat vacaḥ – vīryavattaratvarūpa phalotkīrtanayuktaṃ ' yadeva vidyayā iti vākyam, dṛṣṭividhyarthameva – rasatamatvavākyasya udgīthe rasatamatvadṛṣṭirūpopāsanavidhitva- niścāyakameva na tu stutitvaparyavasāna saham || 10 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||

brahmasaṃsthārthavādatvābhāve'pyāśramavākyayoḥ : 1 śaṅkodgīthārthavādatve stutinītyā pratīyate || 158 ||
iti stutimātrādhikaraṇam || 2 ||

vidyāsu tattadvidyāviśeṣaprakramavatīṣūpaniṣatsu dṛṣṭāḥ, ākhyānabhedāḥ pratardanaḥ śvetake- ketvādisambaddhā sarvepyete ākhyāyikā viśeṣāḥ, vidhimahimahatā : - ' ākhyānāni pāriplave śaṃsanti' ityevaṃvidhavidhigatena sāmarthyena kṛtaprāpaṇāḥ santaḥ, pāriplavārthāḥ santu - āśvamedhikapāriplavaśastre viniyogayogyā bhavantu ( iti śaṅkane) na syuḥ sarve'pyākhyāna - viśeṣāḥ pāriplave viniyoktumardā na bhaveyuḥ, prakaraṇapaṭhitaiḥ– ākhyāyikā prakramataḥ āmnātaiḥ, vidyāvidhānaiḥ – tattadvidyopadeśa paraiḥ śrutivākyaiḥ sākam, ekavākyatvadṛṣṭe:- tadarthānvitārthapratipādakatvarūpaikavākyatāyā ākhyāneṣu darśanāt tatra – pāriplavanāma ke śāstre vidhīyamāne ca kathitam – viśiṣya nirdiṣṭam, mantrādyākhyānamātram - manvādirūpāṇāmā- khyeyapuruṣāṇāṃ pratiniyatānām ākhyānameva, pāriplavārtha bhavati - pāriplavākhye śastre viniyojyaṃ bhavati, tena—viśiṣyoktena manvādyākhyānaviśeṣeṇaiva, ākāṅkṣānivṛttau – ākhyāna - śaṃsanavidhigatāyāḥ ākāṅkṣāyāḥ (svārthanirvahaṇavyāpṛteḥ) śāntau saṃbhavantyām, tadadhika.... klaptiḥ na – viśiṣyoktebhya ākhyānebhyotiriktānāṃ itaraviṣayakāṇāmākhyānānāṃ sarveṣāmapi pāriplavaśastrārthaviniyojyatā kalpanam, na saṅgataṃ bhavati || 11 ||

agnīndhanādyadhikaraṇam || 3-4-4 ||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṃgrahaḥ ||

na vidyeti—–brahmavidyā naiva sambhāviteti śaṅkane, nanadaṃ sādhu, bahuvidhasvāśramāhaṅga- tokteḥ– yajñakarmādīni vihāya nānāvidhānāṃ tattadāśramamāśritānuṣṭhānayogyānāṃ praṇavajapa saṃgavarjana- prabhṛtīnāmeva vidyopakārakatāyāḥ śrutismṛtyādiṣu uktatvāt caturṇām - caturvvāśrameṣu pravṛttā- nāmapi traivarṇikānām, vidyāyogaḥ - apavargo pāyabhūtabrahmavidyāsvanuṣṭhātṛtvenānvayaḥ, vidhirapi- vidyā- tadanuṣṭhānaprakārādyupadeśaśca samaḥ – agnyadhikāravadgṛhasthādyapekṣayā aviśeṣeṇa śrūyate smaryate ca hi śrutivākyato jñāyate smṛtibhyo'vagamyate ca khalu, ( evaṃ sthite) kvā'pi yatra kvacit smṛtipurāṇādau mokṣāśramatvam – saṃnyāsāśramasya mokṣāśramatvena vyapadeśaḥ, prāyeṇau- cityabhūmnā - vidyāsampādanau cityādhikyena munibhiḥ – ṛṣibhiḥ dharmatattvāvedanapravṛttaiḥ abhihitam — kathitamāste || 12 ||

sā tu — apavargopāyatayā śrutyavagatā brahmavidyā param, pravājivat - saṃnyāsāśramiṇāmiva,

gṛhamedhināṃ ca - gṛhasthānāmapi, tadaṅgahīnā - vidyāṅgabhūtakarmo kāryā sādhāraṇatvāt – (mokṣopāyavidyātvasya saṃnyāsi - gṛhasthākhyānuṣṭhātṛbhedamātreṇa aprāpteḥ) aviśiṣṭasvarūpatvāt, ('saṃnyāsiṣu anaṅgikāyā eva tasyāḥ labdhasvarūpatā ; gṛhastheṣu punaḥ sāṅgāyā eva svarūpasiddhi:' iti bhedakalpanāyogāt ) ( iti śaṅkane) naivaṃ tu- evaṃ naiva śaṅkāīm, yajñādikavākyabhāvāt – 'yajñena dānena tapasā'nāśakena' ityādividyāṅga- jñāpakavākyasya sattāṃ (āśayaṃ vā ) avalambya, sarvatra ca sarvasminnapyāśrame svāśramadharma- vattvāt — svasvāśramānuguṇadharmāṇāmeva vidyāṅgatāyāḥ siddheḥ (kutrāpyaṅgahīnāyāḥ svarūpāniṣpatteḥ) | ||

na bhavati,

bhinnarūpatāyā

yajñādinā vidyā niṣpattisādhanabhūtenaṃ vidyāṃ sveṣu niṣpādayitumicchanti iti, siddheḥ || 13 ||
nanvicchārthatvahāni jiṃgamiṣati padetyādike gatyabhāvācū-

śabdārtha-

chrutyukte'smiṃstathā netyasadiha ca yato gatyabhāvaḥ samānaḥ |
icchā syād dhīviśeṣāt tadanupajanane karmabhiḥ sā na sādhyā jijñāsāṃ prāptumicchorna ca na bhavati tajjñānamiṣṭaṃ puraiva || 14 ||

'padā jigamiṣati' ityādi ke - 'padā jigamiṣati, aśvena jigamiṣati asinā jighāṃsati' ityevaṃprabhṛti ke prayogaviśeṣe, icchārthatvahāni: – (padā, aśvena, asinā' iti tṛtīyāntapadabodhyasya pāda-aśva asiprabhṛterarthasya) gamanecchā-hananeccha| dyātma ke sannantārthe karaṇatvenānvayaḥ parityāgaḥ, gatyabhāvāt nanu – prayogānuguṇārthe ayogyatārūpaṃ virodhamālocya upāyāntaravighataḥ khalu kriyate śrutyukte - 'vividiṣanti yajñena' ityādyupaniṣadvākyāvagate, asmin yajñādikarmarūpe sādhane, tathā na - vedanecchārthatva parityāgaḥ, na kartuṃ śakyaḥ ; (arthasya śrī tatvena virodhā virodha cintāyāḥ asmā bhirakartavyatvāt ) ityasat - evaṃ śaṅkanamasādhu, yataḥ- yasmāddhetoḥ, iha ca - prakṛte śrutivākye'pi gatyabhāvaḥ —– yajñādeḥ karmaṇaḥ jñānecchāsampā. dakatvāyogarūpaḥ upāyāntaravirahaḥ samānaḥ - aviśiṣṭa eva, icchā – jñānaviṣayiṇī lipsā, dhīviśeṣāt syāt – iṣyamāṇārthagatasukhatamatvajñānāt ( saundaryajñānādvā ) utpattumaīti, (jānāti, icchati yatate iti nīteḥ) tadanupajanane - viṣaya saundarya jñānasyānutpattau satyām, sā—vedanaviṣayaṇī lipsā, karmabhiḥ– yajñādibhiḥ, na sādhyāna utpattumardhā, jijñāsāṃ prāptumiccho: - vividiṣāprepsoḥ puruṣasya tat jñānam - karmasādhyayā vividiṣayā viṣayī- karaṇīyaṃ vedanam, puraiva vividiṣārthayajñādyanuṣṭhānataḥ, prāgeva, iṣṭaṃ na bhavati na ca- īpsitaṃ nāsīditi naiva vaktuṃ śakyam ( avaśya miṣṭamevāsīditi tadartha karmācaraṇānupapa- tireva ) ||14||

nanvatrecchānuvṛttiṃ prati vihitamidaṃ karma yojyaṃ tataḥ kiṃ
necchārthaṃ ghī viśeṣaprajananamuditaṃ tāvatā vāritaṃ syāt | jñānārtha karmavidhyantaramapi vividhaṃ nā'palāpakṣamaṃ te
nidhyānanyāyato'tastvanuvadati vidheyeṣṭatāṃ samprayogaḥ || 15 ||
nanu – ayi bhoḥ ! atra - ' vividiṣanti yajñene 'tyā diśrutivākye, idaṃ karma_ yajñādyācaraṇarūpaṃ karma, icchānuvṛttiṃ prati brahmavedanecchā sāntatyaṃ prati hetutayā, vihitaṃ

yojyam - upadiṣṭamiti anuvartitavyaṃ tat (anvetavyam ) ( na tu kevalā sādhāraṇecchā prati yena pūrvasiddhatvānyonyāśrayādikaṃ āpādyeta ; iti cintane) tataḥ kim - tasmāt ko lābhaḥ !, ( na ko'pi lābhaḥ icchāsāntatyāya karmasāntatye, karmaṇo jñānāsaha bhāvitvaṃ siddhāntasiddhaṃ bhajyeta ) icchārthaṃ uditaṃ dhīviśeṣaprajananam - vividiṣārthaṃ prathamataḥ jñānaviśeṣasya utpatte- rāvaśyakatvaṃ yaduktaṃ tat, tāvatā – icchā santatyatātparyakatvābhyupagamamātreṇa, na vāritaṃ syāt - naiva prativihitaṃ bhavet, (jñānaṃ vinā icchāyāḥ sarvathā anutpādāt ) jñānārtha karma- vidhyantaramapi mokṣopāyabhūtaṃ jñānamuddiśya pravṛttaṃ karmavidhāyakaṃ vākyāntaramapi vividham- śrautasmārta ceti nānāprakāramupalabhyamānam, te bhavataḥ,

apalāpakṣamaṃ na nāstītyapahnavāī sanprayogaḥ - ' vividiṣantī' tyayaṃ -icchārtha sanpratyayaghaṭitaḥ śabdaḥ, nidhyānanyāyataḥ - ' nididhyāsitavyaḥ' iti sanghaṭita - śabdavat vidheyeṣṭatām – vidheyabhūtasya vedanasya icchāviṣayatām, anuvadati jñātāmeva punarapi vadati (spaṣṭatāyai dāya ca || 15 ||

vyāpārakarmādhikṛtatvahetoḥ nivṛttirūpatārahiteṣu bāhyāntarakaraṇavyāpārākhyeṣu karmasu prāptādhikāratāyā darśanāt teṣāṃ vidyā gṛhasthānāṃ mokṣopāyabhūtā brahmavidyā, śamādiyuktā na tu — śamādyantaraṅgopāyopakṛtā naiva bhavitumarhā, ( iti śaṅkane) naitat na yuktaṃ cedaṃ śaṅkanam śamādestu — śamāderapi tadaṅgabhāvāt pramāṇapratipannāyāḥ vidyopakārakatāyāḥ satvāt, tayoḥ dvayoḥ – bāhyakaraṇavyāpāratannivṛttirūpayoḥ karma- śamādyordvayorapi, bhinnanibandhana -- tvāt — vihitavyāpṛti-niṣiddha | vihitanivṛttirūpatayā mithovirodhābhāvāt || 59 ||

prajñātastrāparādhāḥ - ( loke khalu rājasevā parāḥ puruṣāḥ jñānādajñānato vā kṛtāparādhā: - santaḥ, rājakopāspadatāṃ prāptāḥ) svam āgaḥ ( rājño'nabhimataṃ svenā'patitaṃ ) kālaviśeṣe jānantaḥ, anupatane— paścāttāpe jāte sati, prabhum - svasevyabhūtaṃ rājānam, ātmāhaiḥ- svairanuṣṭhātuṃ yogyaiḥ, lokasiddhaiḥ loke evā'nyatrā'vagataiḥ upāyaiḥ kṣamāyācana-puruṣakāra- yojana- tadabhimatopāyanasamarpaṇaprabhṛtibhiḥ prabhuprasādanocitaiḥ vyāpāraiḥ, arcayantaḥ pūjayantaḥ, prasannāntaḥ karaṇaṃ kurvantaḥ kramaśamitaruṣaḥ kālakrameṇa nivārita (prabhu) kopāḥ, tasya sevām – (pūrva kriyamāṇāṃ satīṃ ) prabhoḥ cittaprasādanīṃ tatsaṃmatāmeva kiṅkarabhāvayutāṃ (tatsaṃnidhau) vyāpṛtim labhante – punarapi prāpnuvanti khalu ! ittham - anenaiva prakāreṇa, śāstra- siddhaiḥ śāstrato'vagataiḥ, idamanidamiti vibhaktaiḥ – 'idaṃ karma anuṣṭheyam idaṃ nā'nuṣṭheyam' – ityevaṃ pṛthak pṛthak (nirdiṣṭa) viviktasvarūpasvabhāvaiḥ, svādhikāraiḥ – svasvavarṇāśramakā ladeśā- dyanuguṇaiḥ (nirupādhikaniravadhika prabhuprasādanavyājabhūtaiḥ) vyāpāraiḥ, nirupadhisuhṛdaḥ- nirupadhisuhṛdaḥ - avyāja- -sakhasya, śrīdharasya lakṣmīpateḥ, niṣpratyūhaḥ- śaṅkātaṅka vidūraḥ, prasādaḥ - anugrahaḥ, naḥ - asmākam adhigamyaḥ – sampādyo bhavati || 17 ||

sarvānnānumatyadhikaraṇam || 3-4-7 ||
śārīrakanyāya kalāpasaṅgrahaḥ ||

'sarvānnabhuktistu' viśeṣahāneḥ syāt sarvadā prāṇavidāmitīdam | na śobhate śaktiviśeṣabhājaḥ prāṇātyaye brahmavido'pi dṛṣṭeḥ | 60 ||

prāṇavidām – prāṇavidyāniṣṭhānām sarvānnabhuktiḥ - ( niyamānanusaraṇena ) sarvānna bhakṣaṇābhyanujñānam, viśeṣahāne :- (brahmavidāṃ prāṇavidāṃ ca vidyāniṣṭhatvasāmyena) vailakṣaṇyasyā - bhāvāt (brahmavidāṃ tadanumatau prāṇavidāmapi tadanumati siddheḥ ) sarvadā tu syāt - - sārvakāli- kameva bhavitumarhati, itīdam – evaṃrūpaṃ etacchaṅkanam, na śobhatenaiva suṣṭhu bhavati, śakti- viśeṣabhājaḥ – prāṇavidapekṣayā mahimātiśayavataḥ, brahmavido'pi brahmavidyā niṣṭhasyāpi prāṇā- tyaye prāṇasaṃśayāpattivelā yāmeva dṛṣṭeḥ – uktasya sarvānnā – uktasya sarvānnānujñānasya śāstra siddhatvadarśanāt | 60|

|

aṅgatvena avidheḥ – prāṇopāsanaṃ prati sarvānnabhakṣaṇasya upakārakatayā vidhito'siddheḥ, anumati- vacanam – sarvānnabhakṣaṇānumatiparaṃ vākyam, prāṇa bhaṅgaprasaṅge syāt – (prāṇopāsakasya prāṇa- rakṣaṇotkaṭāsaktyai) prāṇātyayāpattāveva (anumatiparaṃ) bhavitumarhati etat - prāṇātyaye sarvānna - bhakṣaṇa / nujñānadarśanam, prāṇaniṣṭhāt dapyatiśayitamāhātmya vati, brahmaniṣṭhe parabrahmopāsake, upastau hi – uṣastināmni cākrāyaṇe eva paśya chāndogya-

śrutyavagataṃ samālocaya || 18 ||

kathaṃ syāt ? - (virodhaścaivaṃ nityaṃ phalaṃ bhavati vidyāṅgasve; mokṣarūpasya tatphalasya nityatāyāmavivā dāt | anityaṃ phalaṃ bhavati āśramāṅgatve ; kevalāśramiṇāṃ svargāvāpteḥ śāstra siddhatvāt | tathā ca nityānityaphalasamāveśo bhavitā | avilakṣaṇasya karmasvarūpasya īdṛśobhayavidhaphalahetutā na ghaṭate | sarveṣāṃ nityameva vā syāt phalam ; sarveṣamapyanityameva veti ) kathamidaṃ yuktaṃ syānnityamanityaṃ ca phalam ? svargāpavargobhayarūpam ? (naiva sarvathā ) _iti śaṅkane ; na etatprasaṅgaḥ ekavidhasyaiva karmaṇaḥ vidyāṅgatvāśramamātrāṅgatvābhyāṃ nitya nitya phalasamāveśa- prasaṅgo naiva bhavitā, viniyogabhedāt saṅkalpavailakṣaṇyādikṛtāṅgabhāva vailakṣaṇyādeḥ avaśyaṃ bhāvāt . (ekavidhasyaiva tantoḥ kārpāsajasya, paṭaṃ prati maṅgalasūtraṃ prati ca aṅgabhāve'pi, aṅgatā vailakṣaṇyāta kāryavailakṣaṇyamanapalapanīyaṃ bhavati khalu tadvadatrāpīti ) || 61||

nityāgrihotrānuṣṭhānamapi siddhayati ) para vinnityavarge'pi - paravidyā niṣṭhānāṃ nityārthānuṣṭhānajāte kriyamāṇe'pi, (tena vidyāṅgakarmaṇaḥ, āśramadharmānuṣṭhānasyetyubhayorapi kṛtatvam ) yojyam - nyāya siddhārthaparyālocanayā nirvahaṇīyam || 19 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||

cedvidyā''śramadharmāṅgā nā'dhikuryādanāśramī |
ityākṣepo'podyate'thā'ntarā cāpi na ye punaḥ || 165 ||
iti vidhurādhikaraṇam || 9||

pratinivartanaṃ prāpya, yaḥ patitaḥ yo bhraṃśa prāptavān, tatrā'pi - āśramapracyute tādṛśe'pi, taddaśārhaiḥ- āśramapracyutāvasthāyām anuṣṭhātumarhaiḥ, svadharmaiḥ - japopavāsa bhagavannāmasaṅkīrtanādirūpaiḥ kā yikavācikamānasikā dikarmabhiḥ, sahakṛtā- labdhopakārā, brahmavidyā brahmopāsanātmikā vidyā, bhavatu — śāstrābhimatānuṣṭhāneti svīkriyatā nāma ( iti śaṅkane) maivam — evamabhyugamo nocitaḥ, yadyapi vastutaḥ, idam - ārūḍhapatitatvam, upapatanamabhīcchanti – upapāta katvena saṃmanyante, sarvārham—– sarvavarṇasādhāraṇam, kīrtanādyam - bhagavannāmasaṃkīrtanādikam, śodhakaṃ ca-śuddhikārakamapi, anekamasti – nānāvidhaṃ śrutismṛtyādipratipannaṃ vidyate, tadapi – tathāpi, eṣaḥ - bhagavatkīrtanādiḥ na taducitaḥ — bhraṣṭasya brahmavidyādhikāra siddhyarhaḥ parihāro na bhavitumarhati tādṛṅ niṣedhāt – ' prāyaścittaṃ na paśyāmi' iti āśramabhraṃśadoṣa- nivārakaprāyaścittābhāvajñāpanāt || 21 ||

prāyaścittaṃ vadanti papatana - mahāpātayorbrahmayogaṃ
prāpte pāte pramādādvidadhati munayo yogināṃ yogameva | tasmādārūḍhapāte'pyadhikṛtiriti nā'codanīyaṃ hi śāstra
yāvajjīvaṃ tu tanniṣkṛtiriti niyamaḥ sūtrakārādyabhīṣṭaḥ || 22 ||

upapatana mahāpātayoḥ upapātakasya mahāpātakasya ca mokṣavirodhinaḥ, prāyaścittam — nāśakamupāyam, (nāśopāyatvena ), brahmayogam – nairantaryeṇa paramātmacintanameva (paramātmaniruddha- cittatām ) vadanti – śāstrāṇi kathayanti yoginām - mokṣārthayogānuṣṭhānaparāṇāṃ janānām, pramādāt prāpte - abuddhipūrvakapravṛttitaḥ samāpatite, pāte — pātakaviṣaye, munayaḥ - hitopadeśārthaṃ pravṛttā maharṣayaḥ, yogameva vidadhati – yogānuṣṭhānameva prāyaścittatayā upadiśanti, tasmāt - uktāt jñāpakadvayāt, ārūḍhapāte'pi naiṣṭhikatvādau ārūḍhasya āśramabhraṃśarūpapātakaviṣaye'pi, adhikṛtiḥ - prāyaścittarūpatayā anuṣṭhānādhikārasattvāyatta mokṣārthopāsane'pi adhikārāstitvamapi, iti na - ityevaṃ śaṅkanamanucitam hi yataḥ, śāstram - cetaneṣu vātsalyātiśayena tavahita -- puruṣārthāvedanaikapravaṇaṃ śāstram, acodanīyam - aparyanuyojyam (anākṣepyameva), yāvajjīvam - yāvadāyuṣamapi tanniṣkṛtiriti niyamastu tatprāyaścittaniyama eveti ( yāvacchvāsāvasthānaṃ bhagavaccintanarūpe prāyaścitta evādhikāraḥ : ; na tu mokṣopāyatayā śrutivihita vidyānuṣṭhāne kālasiddhiḥ adhikārasiddhivaiti ) sūtrakārādyabhīṣṭaḥ– śārīrakasūtrakarturdvaipāyanasya 'prāyaścittaṃ na paśyāmi' iti prāyaścittābhāvasmṛtikārāṇāṃ ca saṃmato'rthaḥ || 22 ||

yo bālaṃ hanti yaḥ strīṃ śaraṇamupagataṃ yaśca yo vā kṛtannaḥ
prāyaścittairviśuddhānapi jagadurimān sādhusaṃsṛṣṭyanan |
smṛtyācārānusārādiha ca gatiriyaṃ darśitā sūtrakārai:
śāstraṃ naḥ śāsanīyaṃ yadi bhavati tadā sampluto dharmasetuḥ || 23 ||

yo bāla hanti yaḥ puruṣaḥ śiśuṃ viyuktaprāṇaṃ karoti yaḥ strīṃ hanti yaḥ puruṣaḥ striyaṃ viyuktaprāṇāmāpādayati yaśca śaraṇamupagataṃ hanti - yo'pi vā puruṣaḥ ātmano rakṣāyācanapūrvakaṃ svaṃ pratyāgatamapi nirghṛṇatayā prāṇairviyojayati, yo vā–yaśca puruṣaḥ, kṛtaghnaḥ- pareṇa kṛtasyopakārasyā smaraṇena kṛtaghnapadavācyaḥ, imān — bālaghna - strīghna- śaraṇāgataghna-kṛtaghnā- khyān pātakinaḥ puruṣān, prāyaścittaiḥ tattatpāpanodakatvena śāstrasiddhaiḥ karmabhiḥ, viśuddhā- napi— nivāritapātakatvena vyavahartumarhānapi sataḥ, sādhusaṃsṛṣṭayana han – śāstrīya vidhi- niṣedhānurodhibhiḥ śiṣṭaḥ aihikāmuṣmika vyavahāreṣu saṃsraṣṭumayogyān,

jagadu: - dharmajñasamaya ve dino vadanti, iha ca naiṣṭhikyādito bhraṣṭaviṣaye'pi sūtrakārai: darśitā - dharmasūtra - śārīrakasūtrādi- praṇetṛbhiḥ nirūpitā, iyaṃ gatiḥ - brahmavidyādhikārāpāda kaniṣkṛtyabhāvasamarthana rītiḥ, smṛtyā- cārānusārāt — smṛtigranthān samayācārāṃśca anurudhya pravṛttā; śāstram - sarveṣāmapi hitānu- śāsane apratihatasvātantryavat śāstrameva ( śāsanakartṛtvavadeva), naḥ yadi śāsanīyaṃ bhavati- asmatkartṛkaśāsanaviṣayatayā pratihatakhātantryaṃ yadi bhavet, tadā tarhi, dharmasetuḥ — dharmā- dharma maryādā nirṇāyakatvena anādiparamparāprāpto'rthaḥ śāstrarūpaḥ, samplutaḥ - ( mahājalāśayagataḥ seturiva jale) nimagnaprāya eveti, na tena lokoddhāranirīkṣāyā avakāśa iti || ( ataḥ śāstram niyoga paryanuyogānarhasvātantryeṇa yuktaṃ sadvijṛmbhamāṇamasmadvidhaiḥ hitakā maissadbhiḥ, kevalamanurodhyameva ; na punaḥ śikṣaṇīyamiti ) || 23||
brahmāṃśasve samāne guṇaviṣamatayā śuddhayaśuddhisvabhāvai-
dehairyogādanujñā pariharaṇamapi prekṣaṇasparśanādeḥ |
ityevaṃ sūtritaṃ prāk punariha vividhālepakakṣobhaśāntyai
samyajjñātātmano'pi svatanusamucitā 'cārataḥ ' pratyabodhi || 24 ||
brahmāṃśatve - paraṃ brahma prati apṛtha siddhaviśeṣaṇatvarūpe aṃśatve, samāne – (sarveṣa

cetanānām taddehabhūtānāṃ sarveṣāmacetanānāṃ ca ) aviśiṣṭe sati prekṣaṇa - sparśanādeḥ- (brāhmaṇādidehasya ) darśana - sparśanādeḥ, anujñā – śāstrācārāditaḥ anumatiḥ, pariharaṇamapi - (śūdracaṇḍālādidehasya ) darśana - sparśanādivarjanājñā cetyubhayamapi, guṇaviṣamatayā śuddhayaśuddhi- svabhāva : – (svasvarūpe niṣpādye āpatitaṃ), yat satva rajas-tamasāṃ visadṛśaṃ vaiṣamyaṃ tena samprāptaiḥ śucitvāśucitvākhyaiḥ sva-svagatairguṇaireva ; nānyatheti, ityevam – īdṛśamarthatatvam, prāk – aṃśādhikaraṇīye anujñāparihāraḥ viti sūtre, sūtritam — sūtreṇaiva kaṇṭhoktayā pradarśitam, (tathāpi ) iha-asminnavikaraṇe, punaḥ- bhūyo'pi, vividhālepakakṣobhaśāntyai – nānāprakārāṇāṃ svasmin vidhi-niṣedhottīrṇatvakhyāpanataḥ pravṛttisvācchandyābhyupagamaparāṇā (alepakapadena svātmano- vyavahartṝṇāṃ ) kṣobhakarīṃ pravṛtti śamayitum, samyajjñātātmano'pi — ātmatattvayāthātmya vido'pi ( kṛtakṛtyasyāpi ) svatanusamucitācārataḥ karmaprāptasvasvaśarīrānuguṇaḥ niyataḥ samayācāraḥ (anullaṅghitamaryādatvam ) ( sārvavibhaktika: tasi pratyayaḥ), pratyabodhi - pratibodhitaḥ, ( na tena sūtrakṛtaḥ paunaruktayadoṣaḥ) || 24 ||

((( 119 ) svāmyadhikaraṇam || 3-4 -11 ||
śārīrakanyāya kalāpasaṃgrahaḥ ||

kratvaṅgasambandha 'mupāsanaṃ yat ' tat svāminastatphalabhoktṛbhāvāt | naivartvijāṃ karma tadaṅgavatsyāt karmāṅga sambandhata eva hetoḥ || 64 ||

kratvaṅgasambandham—kratvaṅgabhūtairarthaiḥ saha āśrayāśrayibhāvarūpasambandhavat upāsanaṃ yat—udgīthādyupāsanaṃ yadvihitamasti ( udgīthamupāsīteti) tat - tādṛśamupāsanam, svāminaḥ --- ṛtusvāminaḥ (yajamānakartṛkameva ; na ṛtvikkartṛkam ), tatphalabhoktṛbhāvāt – yāga-tadaṅgā śri- topāsanānāṃ yat phalaṃ tadbhoktṛtāyāḥ yajamāne eva satvāt, ( iti śaṅkane) naiva – tvaṅgā śri- topāsaneṣu yajamānakartṛkatvaṃ sarvathā nocitam aṅgavat - katvaṅgānīva, tat — upāsanamapi, ṛtvijāṃ karma syāt – ṛtvikartṛkaṃ karmaiva bhavitumarhati karmāṅgasambandhata eva hetoḥ– upāsane'pi kratvaṅgasambandhasatvā deva nimittāt | (pradhānaṃ prati aṅgabhūtaṃ svāṅgayutameva hi aṅgatāṃ pratipadyeteti, aṅgāṅgasyāpyaṅgatvākṣateḥ) || 64||

ṛtvikū, tatsambandhi karmarūpaṃ yadudgīthagānam tadāśrayakatvāt ( upāsanasya ) anye guṇaphala- vidhayo yathā - phaloddeśena vihitāḥ itare guṇabhūtakarmānuṣṭhitaya iva (paśūddeśena vihitapraṇaya- nārthagodohanapātrasvīkaraṇamiva adhvaryuṇā), ṛtvikasādhyā – udgātrākhyena ṛtvijaiva anuṣṭheyā ; na yajamānena, evam – udgāna sahacaritasādhana rūpatvakṛtodgātṛkartṛkatvena, eṣā – udgīthāśritā upāsanā, anūdyate ca – 'yadeva vidyayā karotītyādinā śrautena vākyena anūditā ca jñāyate, vidhivalaniyate—ṛtvigvaraṇavidhisāmarthyato vyavasthite, śakyatvaṃ na hi upādhiḥ- kṛtisādhyatvaṃ prayojakatayā naivābhyupagamārham, (atra – udgīthā śritopāsanaviṣaye), atra - yajamānasādhye'pi ṛtvijaivānuṣṭheye karmaṇi, svāmi bhṛtyakramaḥ – svāminā anuṣṭheyasya karmaṇaḥ, bhṛtitaḥ svāyattīkṛtena bhṛtyena anuṣṭhānaṃ rājāmātyādau yena prakāreṇa yuktaṃ bhavati sa kramaḥ, (yajamāna vijorviṣaye'pi ) cintanīyaḥ || 25 ||

mantavya ityeva vidhāna hetoḥ ' ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ śrotavyo mantavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ iti bṛhadāraṇyakavākye darśana - nididhyāsanayoriva tatsahakāribhūtasya mananasyā'pi ' mantavyaḥ iti vākyabhāgena vidhānāt jñāpakāt maunam - vihitamananātmaka- meva maunam, anūdyate - ' atha muniḥ ' ityanena sahakāritayā pūrva jñātameva puna: smārthate; na tu nūnatayā jñāpyate iti tu — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ tu, asat syāt - asamīcīnameva bhavet, hi — yena hetunā, maunam — maunapadam prakṛṣṭe – atiśaya viśeṣaśālini, manane—mananarūpe'rthe upāsyaviṣayakābhyāsātmake, prasiddhayate - prasiddhi (asandigdhaprayogatām ) prāptamasti, tena - tena hetunā, etat — īdṛśaṃ maunam vidheyam - dhyāna sahakāritvena nūlatayā jñāpyameva,

( śrutārthasya yuktayā cintanasya, upāsya viṣayaka punaḥ punaḥ saṃśīlanaspa ca mitho bhedāt | āśramadharmā iva śravaṇa-manane iva ca idaṃ maunaṃ bālya- pāṇḍitya sahabhāvena tṛtīyatāṃ sampannaṃ sat apūrvatayaiva vidyaupayikatayā jñāpyata iti | 65 ||

śravaṇa-mananato'nantaram - 'śrotavyo mantavyaḥ' iti vākyāvagatābhyāṃ śravaṇa-mananābhyāṃ paścādbhāvyeveti ; (' mantavya' ityuktaṃ, ' atha muniḥ ' ityuktaṃ ca mananam naiva abhinna syāt ) maunakhyātiḥ – maunatvena prasiddhi:, prakṛṣṭe manana iti prakarṣarūpavailakṣaṇyavati maunaviśeṣa eva viditacareti, eṣā – 'atha muniḥ' iti śrutā, vibhau dhāraṇādyuktiḥ– yogāṅgabhūtasya paramātmaviṣayaka dhāraṇādeḥ dhyānaupayikasya upadeśarūpaivoktiḥ (sā ca dhyānālambanasya śubhāśraya- bhagavadvigrahasya punaḥ punaḥ saṃśīlanābhyāsādirūpeti tādṛśaṃ maunamevātra bālya- pāṇḍityarūpa- prathamadvitīyāpekṣayā tṛtīyaṃ sat vidheyameveti ) || 26||

-0-

kāmācaraṇādi sarva ca svacchandācaraṇa - svacchanda bhakṣaṇa- svecchānurodhivacanādirūpam sarvameva ;
na viśeṣabhūtaṃ yatkicit, (iti śaṅkane) etat na - idaṃ na yuktam, nā'viratādivākyaiḥ- " | nā'virato duścaritāt nāḍaśānto nā'samāhitaḥ | nā'śāntamānaso vā'pi prajñānenaina- māpnuyāt ||' ityādimiraupaniṣadairvākyaiḥ viśeṣataḥ - vidyaupayikānāṃ guṇānāṃ duścaritānni-

vṛttiḥ śānti samāhitatvādīnāṃ viśeṣarūpeṇa kathanāt jñāpakāt kulādeḥ anāviṣkaraṇaṃ tu- svakulābhijanapāṇḍityādīnāṃ viśiṣṭadharmāṇāmaprakaṭīkāreṇa bālavadvartanameva, bālyam – (vidvāṃsaṃ prati ' bālyena tiṣṭhāse' diti vākyato vidhīyamānaṃ) bālasvabhāvāntargatam ; na tu sarva bālakarmabhūtam || 66 ||

asatkarmaṇaḥ sakāśāt, saṃnirundhe nivārayati, ataḥ asmāddhetoḥ, asau-mokṣārtha-- vidyānuṣṭhānapravṛttaḥ (siddhopāsano vā), svaṃ māhātmyaṃ nigūhet iti munivihite - svīyaṃ kulā mijana vidyāsampacyā dikaṃ mahattvāpādakaṃ dharmam aprakaṭayanneva jīvet ; na tu tena janasaṃmāna- nādikaṃ nirīkṣet ityevaṃ munibhiḥ, ('aheriva janādbhītaḥ saṃmānānmaraṇādiva') upadiṣṭe hitarūpe'rthe, bālyavidhyāśayaḥ syāt – ' bālyena tiṣṭhāset' iti bālyavidhāyakavākyasya abhiprāyaḥ sambhāvitaḥ ; (na tu svairavartanarūpe'rthe ) || 27 ||

sadyaḥ phalaṃ syāt – anuṣṭhāna sampattiprayojaka dehāvacchinnānubhava yogyaphalayogavadeva bhavitumaīti ; na dehāntarāvacchedenānubhava| phalavat, ( iti śaṅkane) na tat naiva tadaṅgīkāryam, yadeveti tu coditatvāt – 'yadeva vidyayā karoti śraddhayopaniṣadā tadeva vīryavattaram' ityevameva śrutau āmnātatvāt, baliṣṭhaiḥ pratibandhahānau – balavattarapūrva karmaprayojyasya pratibandhasya abhāve sati, tat syāt – yajñopāsanādyanuṣṭhānāvyavadhānena phalasiddhiḥ bhavet ; ( pratibandhasya udaye tu tannira- vaśeṣavilayānantarameveti dehāntare'pi tatsambhavāditi ) || 67 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṃgrahaḥ ||

aihikādhikaraṇe_aihikaphala vidyānāṃ

svasādhanabhūtakarmānuṣṭhānānantaram

utpatti:-

ityāśaṅkaya,

pratibandhābhāve anantaramutpattiḥ, tadbhāve tadvigama: - ityuktam ||

-0-

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||

astvanyatrā'ntarāyaḥ prabalatamamidaṃ sātvikatyāgayukta karma proktaṃ nivṛttyāhvayamiti sapadi brahmavidyāṃ vidadhyāt | maivaṃ tasmād balīyān yadi bhavati parabrahma bhaktāparādha-

stacchāntau tatprasūtiḥ sa na yadi jhaṭiti syāt paropāstilābhaḥ || 29||

anyatra — pravṛttidharmāntaḥpātini karmajāte, antarāyaḥ astu – phalaniṣpattipratibandhaḥ prāpnotu nāma, sāccikatyāgayuktam – 'sa tyāgaḥ sāttviko mataḥ' iti gītācāryoktena sātvikatyāgena juṣṭam, nivṛtyāhvayamidaṃ karma – nivṛttidharmapadābhidheyam upāsanāṅgabhūtaṃ karma, prabalatama proktamiti — sarvāpekṣayā valavattamatvena śrutismṛtyādiṣu parigaṇitamiti hetoḥ, (balavattamasya durbalaiḥ pratibandhāsambhavāt ) sapadi — upāsanottarakālameva, brahmavidyām - mokṣopāyabhūtāṃ āparokṣyeṇa brahmāvagatim vidadhyāt – niṣpādayet na tu pratihatasva kārya ; bhavet, (iti śaṅkane) maivam — evaṃ mā śaṅkayatām, ( kuta iti cet ) tasmād balīyān - nivṛttidharmākhyādupāsanāṅgabhūtāt karmaṇo'pi baliṣṭhaḥ, parabrahmabhaktāparādhaḥ – parabrahmavettṛviṣayakaḥ- apacāraḥ, yadi bhavati - prāpnoti prasajyeta vā yadi, (tadā) tacchāntau tādṛśāpacāra- niravaśeṣavilaye sati, tatprasūtiḥ - brahmavidyāyā udayaḥ, saḥ - brahmavidapacāraḥ, na yadi- naiva vidyeta cet, jhaṭiti – avilambenaiva, paropāstilābhaḥ – parabrahmavidyā niṣpattirūpā pekṣitārthalābhaḥ syāt—sambhavedeva || 29 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṃgrahaḥ ||

muktiphalā dhikaraṇe-muktiphalakavidyāṅgakarmaṇāṃ prabalatvena, tasyāḥ na pratibandhaḥ sambhavatīti, tāsāṃ vidyānāṃ svasādhanānuṣṭhānānantarameva utpattiḥ sambhavati — ityāśaṅkaya,
teṣāmapi brahmavidapacāraiḥ pratibandhasambhavāt pūrvavat aniyamaḥ - ityuktam ||

-01

śārīrakanyā yakalāpasaṃgrahaḥ ||

ṛtvijaivānuṣṭheyatvam, ' 12 atha muniḥ' iti, śravaṇānantaramanana vilakṣaṇatayā vidyāsaha - kāribhūtamau nitā vidhānam, 13 'bālyena tiṣṭhāset' iti vihitasya bālyasya vidyāniṣṭhagata. śakti-vidyā- abhijanādyātmaka māhātmyāprakaṭīkā rarūpasyaiva vidyaupayikatā, 14 svargākhyā bhyu - dayaupayikakarmaṇāṃ nirantara yatve eva tasmiñjanmani svargādipradatvaṃ ; sāntarāyatve janmāntara- sāpekṣatvam, 15 tathaiva mokṣakaphalānāṃ vidyāṅgabhūtānāṃ karmaṇāmapi brahmavidapacārādibhiḥ pratibandhe sati vilambitatayaiva vidyānniṣpādakatvam ; evaṃrītyā adhikaraṇa paṃcadaśakayuktenānena pādena vidyāṅgabhūtārthastomaviṣayiṇī cintā nirvyūḍhā || 73 ||

seneśvarāryaracitasannyāya kalāpasaṅgrahasyā'sya | śrīrāmabhadrakṛtinā tārtīyādhyāyāṃvavṛtirāyoji ||

aniyamaviṣayau phale karmānuṣṭhānānantaryanaiyatyābhāvabodhakau, adhikārau dve adhikaraṇe aihika - muktiphalākhye, śāstrakartrā — śārīrakaśāstra nirmātrā bādarāyaṇena, nyāsiṣātām - -sthāpitāvabhavatām || 30 ||

pratyūhasya prasaṅgaḥ kṣama iha paravitkarmaṇāṃ tadvidhūtyai
bhogārthopā stihetuṣvayamanu kathitaḥ kiṃ mudhā'treti cenna |
kāmye nirvighnabuddhyā patati matiratastatra vairāgyabhūmne

tatpratyūhaprasaktibhavati phalavatī syācca vidyārthakāmyam || 31 ||

1-vidyā karmāṅgikā syāt - mokṣopāyabhūtā brahmavidyaiva karmo kāryā; na tu karma vidyopakāryam 2- rasatamamukhadhīḥ – udgīthādiṣu rasatamatvādidṛṣṭiḥ prātyabhāvāt - vidhivākyāntareṇā'prāptatvāt, coditā-' sa eṣa rasānāṃ rasatamaḥ paramaḥ parārthyo mo yadudgīthaḥ' iti vākyāt [ vihitaiva na anūditā, 3 vidyā... juṣām – vidyāvidhāyaka- vākyasamīpavartinām, ākhyānānāṃ ca - vedāntagatākhyāyikā viśeṣāṇāmapi tadvidhānārtha- taiva-tattadvidyāvidhyarthatvameva ; na tu pāriplavaśaṃsanārthatvam 4 ūrdhvaretassvapi pāri- vājyādyāśramiṣvapi, sāṅgā vidyātattadā śramavihitairja popavāsādibhiḥ setikartantākā brahmavidyā, 5- sā brahmavidyā gṛhavati gārhasthyavati puruṣe, yajñadānādyapekṣā yāga dāna- homādi- sāpekṣā satyeva svarūpalābhavatī mokṣādhāyikā, 6- gṛhastho'pi — svīkṛta gārhasthya: brahmavidyā- pravṛtta: puruṣopi, śāntya dyarhaḥ - śamadamādisampatteraha eva san sādhita vidyaḥ syāt, 7- viduṣo'pi - niṣpannopāsanasyā'pi puruṣasya, avipadi - prāṇātyayarūpāyāṃ vipadi asatyām, annaśuddhacaiva śuddhiḥ – āhāraśucitvakṛtaiva vidyaupayikī karaṇakalevaraśuddhatā śāstra- samateti || 32 ||

ekaṃ vidyāśramārthaṃ vidhura' mapi yutaṃ' vidyayā''huścyutaṃ na
kratvaṅge dṛṣṭirṛtvikkṛtiratha munitā ' coditadhyāna siddhyai | bālyaṃ śaktyā diguptirbalavadabhihataiḥ karmabhirnānyavidyā

tadvanmuktyarthavidyā svajanakasukṛtairityuvācā'tra pāde || 33 ||

tiṣṭhā se 'diti vidvāṃsaṃ prati vihitavālya svarūpam 14- karmabhiḥ – vidyāṅgatayā anuṣṭhitairapi yajñādibhiḥ, balavadabhihataiḥ – balavatā karmāntareṇa pratiruddhaiḥ, na anyavidyā - mokṣetara phalaka- vidyāyā aniṣpattiḥ, 15-tadvat — mokṣetaraphalā vidyeva, muktyarthavidyā - mokṣaikaphalā vidyā'pi, balavadabhihataiḥ svāpekṣayā atyantaṃ prabalairbrahmavidapacārādibhiḥ pratirudvaiḥ svajanakasukṛtaiḥ-

sva (vidyā) niṣpādakatayā svāṅgabhūtaiḥ yajñadānādibhiḥ puṇyakarmabhiḥ na-naiva niṣpādyate, iti – evaṃrūpān pañcadaśārthān atra pāde – prakṛte tṛtīyaturīye caraṇe, uvāca – nyāyato

nirṇīyoktavān kṛṣṇadvaipāyanaḥ || 33 ||

yadduḥkhaṃ vartate tat kṣaṇamiduratayā na svayatnoparodhyaṃ
nā'tītārthaṃ ca yatno supariharaṃ bhāvi hetau samagre |
duḥkhātyantoparodhe karaṇa vighirato vyartha ityardharamyaṃ
prāyaścittyā kṛtānāṃ paramakaraṇato duḥkhasāmagryapohāt || 35 ||

yat dukhaṃ vartate - prakṛtakāle vartamānaṃ yat duḥkhākhyaṃ pratikūlavedanīyamasti tat- vartamānaṃ du:kham, kṣaṇamiduratayā – kṣaṇamātrabhaṅgaratvasvābhāvyāt, yatnoparodhyaṃ na- puruṣakṛtināśyaṃ na bhavati; (nāśanayatnaṃ vinā'pi svata eva naśyati) atītārthaṃ ca na yatnaḥ- gatasya duḥkhasya nirāsārthamapi prayatnaḥ kāryo na bhavati, pūrvameva naṣṭatvena, (nivartanīyasyaivābhāvāt ) bhāvi - āgāmi ca du:kham, hetau samagre - kāraṇakūṭe jāgrati sati, na ca supariharam- naiva sulabha sampādyanivṛtti bhavitumarham, (yāvatkāraṇasamūhe sati kāryāvaśyaṃbhāvena duḥkhasyā- nivāryatvāt | nivāryatve kāraṇa kūṭameva nāstītyāpadyeteti ) ataḥ - atītavartamānāgāmirūpāṇāṃ trividhānāmapi duḥkhānāmapā karaṇīyatvāyogāt duḥkhātyantoparodhe karaṇavidhiḥ – duḥkhānāṃ niravaśeṣakṣayamuddiśya śāstraṣūpāya vidhānam, vyarthaḥ na saprayojanam, iti - evaṃ śaṅkanam, ardharamyam – śabdaprayogaphalabhūtasya abādhitārthabodhaparyavasānasyābhāvena kevalaśabdajanya- jñānasya svacchanda pravṛttipoṣakatvajñānena ca alasānām avicāritamaṇīyamityarthaḥ, (uktārtha- viṣayake vicāre kriyamāṇe) kṛtānām – upāyānuṣṭhānataḥ pūrvamajñānataḥ kṛtaiḥ karmabhiḥ phala- bhogayogyatāṃ prāptānāṃ duḥkhānām prāyaścityā – upāsanarūpaniṣkṛtyanuṣṭhānena parihārāt, param — āgāmitvena bhāvyamānānāṃ ) upāyānuṣṭhānānantarakāle, akaraṇataḥ – duḥkhāpādakatva- yogyatāvatāṃ karmaṇāmakaraṇena, duḥkhasāmagrayapohāt - yāvadduskhakāraṇasamudāyasya anudayāpādanena nivartanāt, (duḥkhātyantoparodhasya susādhyatvasaṅghaṭanāt ) || 35 ||

rājadviṣṭādi - bhautikasya nṛpasya anabhimataṃ gṛhakṣetrādikaṃ svīyamanyadīyaṃ vā vastu, sarpādyapi (tathaiva) asmākamaniṣṭakāritvena jñātaṃ sarpa vṛścika corādikaṃ ca pariharasi āgāmiduḥkhanirākaraṇabuddhyā varjayasi khalu, chatrapūrvam — varṣatrātapatrādikam vibharṣi āgāmivarṣātapajanyā niṣṭadūrīkaraṇāya dhārayasi evam - rājadviṣṭasarpādiviṣaya prāyaścittāpravṛttau – duṣkṛtānāṃ prāyaścittasyānanuṣṭhāne, duritaṃ phalatīti- duṣkṛtaṃ duḥkharūpa- phalapradaṃ bhavatīti vibhāvya, iha – mokṣopāyabhūtopāsanānuṣṭhāne'pi, īhām iccha— pravṛti saṃmanyasva, utprekṣā... śamanataḥ – svīyasambhāvanāmātrādhyāsitapaścātkālā vaśyānubhāvyatvaśaṅkā- gocaraparājayapariharaṇataḥ, svoktisāphalyamicchan – aniṣṭa prāpteḥ pūrvameva svena prayuktāyā: hitavācaḥ patnīputrādiṣu sārthakyamākāṅkṣamāṇo vartase, ābālatiryak — śiśumārabhya paśuparyantam, bhayacakitagatim — āgāmiduḥkhaśaṅkayā vihvalāṃ gamanakriyām, paśyaṃśca – svacakṣuṣaiva bhavaloka- yannapi saṃsaramāṇastvam, ittham - vedāntodita sarvaśokāpanodakara paramātmopāsanaprakāram : juṣasva – śāstrānuṣṭhānaparamparādiviruddhaṃ yatkicit acintayan nibhṛtaṃ sevasva || 36||

iva,

joṣaṃ

sādhyaṃ vā sādhanaṃ vā 'svavihata' vipathairdurnirūpaṃ vadanta-
stadvāde kiṃ pravṛttāḥ kimiti ca vidadhatyanvahaṃ bhojanādīn | mukternityatvasiddhayā bhajanamaphalamityapyasat prāgasiddheḥ

sāṅkhyāstvatrā'nuyojyāḥ sakaraṇadaśayā nityamuktiṃ gṛṇantaḥ || 37 ||

sādhyaṃ vā sādhanaṃ vā kārya vā kāraṇamapi vā durnirūpam – nirūpaṇānarhamiti, svavihata vipathaiḥ svīyavyavahāravyāghātavadbhiḥ durnītimārgaiḥ sādhanīkṛtaiḥ, vadantaḥ pratipādana - miva kurvantaḥ laukā yatikāḥ, tadvāde-tasya vādabhūmyāṃ kathane, kimarthaṃ pravṛttāḥ – kenoddeśena prayatitavantaḥ ? (tena kasyā'pi kāryasyāsiddheḥ; kārya kāraṇabhāvasyaiva abhāvāt ) kimiti ca - kasmai coddiṣṭāya phalāya, anvaham - pratidinam, bhojanādīn – āhārapari- grahādikān vidadhati — kurvanti ? ( bhojanasya kāraṇatāyāḥ tṛpteḥ kāryatāyāśca durvacatvāt ) mukteḥ mokṣasya, nityatvasiddhayā - yatna niṣpādyatvāvagamena, (svayaṃ nityasiddhatvāt ) bhajanam - mokṣopāyatayā śrutyādyavagatam upāsanam, aphalam - nirarthakam ityapi - iti varṇanamapi, asat – asamīcīnam prāgasiddheḥ – ( upāyānuṣṭhānasādhyasya mokṣasya ) upāyānuṣṭhānataḥ pūrva aniṣpannatvāt, sakaraṇadaśayā - karaṇakalevarādiyogarūpasāṃsārika daśāsahitatayaiva, nityamuktim-

( saṃsāradaśāyāḥ nityāyā

nityāmeva mokṣadaśām, muktidaśāyāśca virodhāt )

gṛṇantaḥ vyāhataṃ

vyāharanta:,

atra — asmin sandarbhe

sāṅkhyāstu – sāṅkhyā eva,

anuyojyāḥ - praśnataḥ ākṣepyāḥ

|| 37 ||

anyatra vivādāspadāṃ muktiṃ vihāyā'nyatra ghaṭādau lokadṛṣṭe'rthe, nāśaḥ- nāśapadābhidheyaḥ, kapālaprabhṛti bahuvidhāvasthayā santatyātmā – uttarottarāvasthayā ghaṭāvayava- bhūtakapālādyātmikayā nānāvidhayā santanyamānasvarūpataiva ; ( nānyo'sti nāśo nāma vaiśeṣikādi- vat ) atra – ātmanaḥ saṃsāranāśarūpamuktiviṣaye tu āgamāt - āgamapramāṇamanurudhya, atathābhūtam - ghaṭādibhāvapratiyogikanāśavilakṣaṇaṃ sat, vikasanam - aparicchinnanirupādhi- katvaprāptyā sarvaviṣayagrā hitvameva, dhīsaṅkocapraṇāśaḥ syāt - ātmadharmabhūtajñānasya paricchinna- tvākhyāyāḥ saṅkucitāvasthāyāḥ nirasanameva nāśapadārtho bhavitumaīti, anye tu muktisvarūpa- pariśīlanapravṛttā itare tu, muktabuddheḥ – muktātmano dharmabhūtāyā ghiyaḥ, dhārāvahanamatinayāt- dhārāvāhikabuddheḥ avicchedrato'nuvṛttinyāyāt santatim - dhārārūpeṇa santatāmanuvṛttim, manyante nityatvavidhāṃ jānanti, sāmagrī ca — mukta dhiyaḥ dhārāsāntatyaprayojakaṃ yāvatkāraṇa- kūṭaṃ tu, īśvarecchāprabhṛtisamudayaḥ - bhagavatsaṅkalpā dighaṭita kāraṇastoma eva | evam — uktayā rītyā, anāvṛtti : - muktasyātmanaḥ punaḥ saṃsāridaśāyāḥ aprasaktiḥ, syāt- sambhavatyeva (' na ca punarāvartate ' ityādyuktā ) || 39 ||

adhikṛtisārāvalyā vedāntācāryaracitāyāḥ | āyojitā'rthavivṛttiḥ tṛtīya ke sādhane'dhyāye || 1 ||
kratunāthavaṃśajanuṣā śrīnivāsarāghavāyatanujena |
śrīrāmabhadranāmnā tvadhyetṝṇāṃ sukhena bodhāya || 2 ||

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ | śriyai namaḥ | śrīdharāya namaḥ ||
caturtho'dhyāyaḥ | prathamaḥ pādaḥ ||
āvṛttyadhikaraṇam ||4- 1-1 ||
śārīrakanyāya kalāpasaṅgrahaḥ ||

jñānasyopāyatvāt tasya ca sakṛdeva kartumarhatvāt |

sakṛdeva vedanaṃ syānnaitadupāstitvacodanājjñapteḥ || 1 ||
jñānasya – jñāna viśeṣarūpasya vedanasya, upāyatvāt - mokṣopāyatvena śrutyādyavagatatvāt tasya ca—jñānasya ca vedanapadābhidheyasya, sakṛdeva kartumarhatvāt – ekavārameva anuṣṭhātuṃ śakya- tvāt vedanam — mokṣopāyabhūtaṃ jñānam, (upāsanākhyam ) sakṛdeva syāt - ekavāra- manuṣṭhitameva vidhāyakaśāstratātparyaviṣayaḥ, (iti śaṅkane) naitat — ekavārānuṣṭhānaṃ na śāstrārthatāṃ jñapte :- mokṣopāyabhūtasya jñānasya, upāstitvacodanāt – upāsanarūpatāvidhānāt jñāpakāt, (tasya ca asakṛdanuṣṭheyatvāvaśyaṃbhāvāt ) || 1 ||

bhajate,

iti – pūrvavṛttena tṛtīyenā'dhyāyena, utpattikrameṇa – svarūpaniṣpattau vidyamānaṃ paurbā- paryarūpaṃ kramamanurudhya, ( ādau upāsanena nirantaradhyānarūpeṇa mokṣopāyabhūtāyāḥ vidyāyā: niṣpattiḥ ; tataḥ tatphalabhūtasya mokṣasya - apavargasya prāptiḥ iti ) prathamataḥ - ādau, aṅgaiḥ saha - agnihotrādikarma- śamadamādi- pāṇḍitya - bālya - maunādyātmakaiḥ vidyetikartavyatābhūtaiḥ sahakāri kalāpaiḥ, saha, muktayupāyaḥ – saṃsāranigaḍādvimocana rūpasya mokṣasya sādhanabhūtaḥ vidyārūpo'rthaḥ abhihitaḥ - nyāya niṣpādanapūrvakaṃ nirūpitaḥ, atha tataḥ param, tatsādhyam - vidyā niṣpādyaṃ mokṣākhyaṃ phalam viduṣaḥ – niṣpannopāsanasya puruṣasya, parvabhedaiḥ kramāyattasiddhimiḥ parvaviśeṣaiḥ sūtrakāraḥ vyanakti - śārīrakasūtrakṛt kṛṣṇadvaipāyanaḥ spaṣṭīkaroti, asya vidyā siddhi- mataścetanasya, sthūle dehe - indriyagrāhyasthūla deha va sthiti kāle, yat sidhyet- yat kārya, niṣpannaṃ bhavet (pūrvotara ghāśleṣa- dehādapakramaṇādikam ) tat tādṛśaṃ kārya mokṣaprāgbhāvi, iha – caturthe'dhyāye, pūrvaṃ pādayugmena ādimacaraṇadvayena, vadati-nirūpayati nyāyena, atha paścāttanena, pādayugmāntareṇa – avaśiṣṭanānyena pādadvayena, niṣkrāntasya yat syāt - sthūlāt dehādutkrāntasya yadbhavet, tat tādṛśamarthadvayam (arcirādipathagamana- parabrahmaprāyu- bhayam, parigaṇayati – cintayati || 1 ||

sūktā prāgeva vidyā kimiti punarimāṃ vakti ? yadyasti śeṣaṃ

brūtāmetacca pūrva na hi tadiha phale saṅghaṭeteti cenna |
mukteranyairasiddhiṃ prakṛtatadavinābhāvamāsanna siddhi

muktāvasthāsamaṃ ca sthirabhajanarasaṃ vyaṅkṅkumatrā'nubandhāt || 2 ||
vidyā mokṣopāyabhūtā upāsanātmikā vidyā prāgeva – pūrvasmin adhyāye eva, -

sūktā – suṣṭhu nirūpitā vartate, imām – nirūpitāmeva vidyām kimiti—kena hetunā, punaḥ vakti-muhuḥ pratipādayati ? (ekavāraṃ nirūpaṇataḥ āvaśyakasya prayojanasya siddhatvena prāptavyābhāvāt ) śeṣam – nirūpaṇīyaṃ nirūpitāvaśiṣṭam asti yadi vartate cet ! etacca – adyāvaśiṣṭatayā ucyamānamapyartha jātam, pūrva brūtām — vidyānirūpaṇāyaiva pravṛtte pūrvasminneva adhyāye nirūpayatu nāma, (tatra nirūpyamavaśeṣya atra anirūpyasya nirūpaṇaṃ kasmai phalāya ? ) hi yataḥ, tat — avaśiṣṭavidyāṃśanirūpaṇam phale iha – tatphalabhūtārtha- rūpaṇaikaphale, asmin phalādhyāye, na saṅghaṭeta naiva susambaddhatā prāpnuyāt iti cet-

evaṃ śaṅkanam na – naiva vicārapūrvakam, mukteḥ - mokṣarūpasya phalasya, anyaiḥ asiddhim-- vidyetaraiḥ upāyaiḥ sarvathā alābham prakṛtatadavinābhāvam — prastutasya vidyākhyopāyasya mokṣarūpaphalāyogavyavacchedam (mokṣaphalakatvanaiyatyam ), āsanna siddham – ( upāyabhūtāyā vidyāyāḥ) apavargarūpaphalena adūrasaṃnikarṣam, muktavasthāsamaṃ sthirabhajanarasaṃ ca - mokṣadaśātulyaṃ nirantarā - nuvartyamānopāsanajanyaprītiviśeṣaṃ ca vyaṅktum — vidyāsambaddhatayā prakaṭīkartum atra anu- -bandhāt — asmin phalādhyāyepi vidyāsambandhyarthāśayojanasya kṛtatvataḥ, (paunaruktayā - sāṅgatyādyanavakāśāt ) || 2 ||

viśeṣanirūpaṇe kriyamāṇe) nirūpitārthanirūpaṇa nibandhanaṃ paunaruktatyaṃ naiva prasajyata ityetat . subodham – sukhena avagantumarham || 3 ||

ET

jñānaṃ mukterupāyaḥ śrutibhirabhihitastasya saṅkhyā tu noktā
saukarya syāt sakṛtve tata iha tu bhaveccāritā vidhīnām |
samyaktvāt taddhruvatvaṃ na punaranugamāt proktamityapyasāraṃ sāmānyokterviśeṣe sati paśunayatastatra viśrāntisiddheḥ || 4 ||
jñānam - brahmavedanam, mukterupāyaḥ – apavargasādhana miti,

śrutibhirabhihitaḥ. brahmavidāpnoti param jñātvā devaṃ mucyate' ityādibhiḥ vedāntavākyaiḥ spaṣṭaṃ pratipāditam, tasya muktyupāyabhūtasya jñānasya saṅkhyā tu— anāvṛttatvena ekasaṅkhyatvaṃ vā āvṛttatvena nānāsaṅkhyatvaṃ vetyetatparam noktā-vedanāvagamakavākye na spaṣṭīkṛtam, sakṛtve - ekavāra - mātramevānuṣṭheyatve śrutisaṃmate sati saukarya syāt - ' sakṛtkṛta: śāstrārtha : ' iti mīmāṃsā- nyāyasyānugraha lābho bhavet, tataḥ - tāvanmātrādeva, iha - mokṣopāyopadeśaprakaraṇe, vidhīnām- brahmavedana vidhāyakavākyānām, cāritārthyaṃ bhavettu – sāphalyaṃ bhavedeva, taddhruvatvam- dhruvā smṛtiḥ' iti chāndogyaśrutyuktaṃ vedanagataṃ dhruvapadavācyatvam samyaktvāt proktam - jñānāntarakṛtabādhāgocaratvakṛtameva pradarśitam, na punaḥ anugamāt na tu nairantaryeṇa cirakālānu- vartamānatākṛtam, ityapi asāram — evaṃ rītyā varṇanamapi phalgu, sāmānyokte:- sāmānya - dharmāvacchinnārthapratipādikāyā vācaḥ, viśeṣe sati – viśeṣadharmāvacchinnāvagamakapramāṇopalambhe vidyamāne, paśunayataḥ - chāga - paśunyāyamanusṛtya, tatra - viśeṣadharmāvacchinnerthe eva, viśrāntisiddheḥ - paryavasānasya svabhāvato lābhāt || (ataḥ asakṛdāvṛttanirantarānuvṛttadarśana sadṛśatāpanna bhaktirūpajñānākhye eva vedane mokṣopāyatā- pratipādanasya sarvavedāntavidhitātparyam ) iti || 4 ||

mukterupāyaḥ – apavargasādhanamiti,

kiṃ ca – api ca, iha vidiḥ - mokṣopadeśaparavākyastho vidadhātuḥ āgneyyādi- nītyā – jyotiṣṭoma prakaraṇagatam āgneyyāmīmupatiṣṭhate' itīdaṃ vākyaṃ yathā prakṛtakatuprakaraṇa- śrutāyā eva agnidevatākāyā ṛcaḥ, tatkratusambandhyāmnīghrākhyamaṇḍapopasthānāṅgatvaṃ vidhatte ; na tu ṛgvedagatamaṇḍaladaśakāntaḥpātinyāḥ yasyāḥ kasyāścidamidaivatyāyā ṛcaḥ, tathaiva dhyāna- śabdārthameva vadati – 'omityevaṃ dhyāyathātmānam' ityevamāditatprakaraṇasyadhyānapadabodhyamarthameva pratipādayati ; na tu yatkiñcidvadanam (prākaraṇikāprākaraṇikayormadhye prākāraṇikagrahaṇasyaiva - nyāyyatvāt ) dhyāne ca upāsanoktiḥ tasminneva ca dhyāne viṣaye, uttaratra ' upāsate puruṣaṃ ye kāmāḥ -ityevamādau upāsana padavyavahāraḥ, parabhajanatayā svāpekṣayotkṛṣṭa viṣaya kabhajanarūpatābodhanadvārā, sevātmakatvaṃ vakti–sevāparaparyāyabhaktirūpārthaparyavasānaṃ bodhayati, (ataḥ vedana-dhyāna- upāsana- sevā-bhakti śabdāḥ ekārthaparyavasitāḥ) evameva ca vidyupāsyoḥ – vidadhātoḥ upapūrvakās- dhātoścetyubhayoḥ, vyatikaritatayā - mithaḥ saṃkrameṇa, aikārthya prayogaśca śrūyate—– upāsīte- tyārabhya vedeti, vedetyārabhya upāsīteti caivakrameṇa samānārthaparyavasānapratipattikaraḥ śrītaḥ prayogaśca upalabhyate, bhakteḥ kārye bhaktisādhye kīrtanācanādau bhaktarhetau bhakteḥ sādhane śravaṇa- smaraṇa - pādasevanādau ca viṣaye, kvacit bhaktibhedatvavādaḥ - smṛtipurāṇādau bhakti- viśeṣarūpatā vyavahāraḥ, upacaritaḥ - kāryakāraṇayorabhedānusandhānādirūpopacāraprayuktaḥ (upā- -sane eva tu mukhyaḥ) || 5 ||

yogodyukteṣu yaḥ syāt svavaraṇaviṣayastena labhyaḥ parātmā
grāhyatvaṃ ca priyatvāt tadapi hi guṇato bhaktireva śrutaivam | sā ca prītyātmikā dhīḥ kvacidatiśayite smṛtyacādho'pyataḥ syāt proktā caiṣā dhruvānusmṛtiranuvahanāt tailadhārākrameṇa || 6 ||

yogodyukteṣu – 'ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ śrotavyo mantavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ' ityādi- śrutyukteopāyānuṣṭhānapravṛtteṣu madhye yaḥ svavaraṇaviṣayaḥ syāt – yādṛśaḥ adhikārī paramātma- kartṛkasvīkārasya gocaratāṃ prāpto bhavet tena tādṛśenādhikāriviśeṣeṇaiva, paramātmā labhyaḥ syāt – upāyānuṣṭhānena prāpyatayā śrutisiddhaḥ paratantvabhūto'rthaḥ sampattumarho bhavet, grāhyatvaṃ ca- paramātmakartṛkavaraṇaviṣayatvamapi jīvasya priyatvāt — tasya bhagavanniṣṭhaprītiviṣayatvata eva, tadapi bhagavadgatapratyāspadatvamapi jīve, guṇato hi - niratiśaya priya paramātmakatvarūpāt jīvagatāt dharmādeva khalu siddhayati, evam - etādṛśārthapratipādanapravṛttāyāḥ 'yamevaiṣa vṛṇute tena

labhyaḥ' iti śrutestātparyaprakāra cintane, bhaktireva śrutā jīvagatā bhagavadviṣayiṇī bhaktireva bhagavatkartṛkajīva karmakapriyatva hetukavaraṇe prayojakatvena śrutisiddhā bhavati sā ca - bhaktiśca, atiśayite kvacit – svāpekṣayā atyantamutkṛṣṭe kasmiścana viṣaye prītyātmikā dhīḥ- prītipadavācyāvasthāviśeṣāpannajñāna viśeṣa eva ataḥ - śrutyarthasya evaṃ paryavasānāt smṛtya - bādho'pi syāt - bhaktermokṣopāyatābodhikayā 'bhaktayā tvananyayā śakyaḥ ityādismṛteḥ śrutiviruddhārthakatvakṛto bādhopi parihṛto bhavati, eṣā - bhaktirūpāpannā dhīreva, tailadhārākrameṇa anuvahanāt– tailadhārāvat santatyena anuvartanāddhetoḥ, dhruvānusmṛtiḥ proktā' savaśuddhau dhruvā smṛtiḥ' ityatra dhruvāsmṛti' śabdenā'pi vyavahṛtā || 6 ||
rāgādādau pravṛttiḥ ' śravaṇa-mananayordhyānamekaṃ vidheyaṃ
tatra draṣṭavyaśabdo viśadatamatayā vakti vaiśiṣṭyamātram | śabdotthaṃ darśanaṃ ye viṣayamakathayan vedanokteramīṣāṃ
vyāghātādiprapañcaḥ svayamiha nipuṇaiḥ sūkṣmamanveṣaṇīyaḥ || 7 ||

(sāṅgasaśiraskavedādhyayanajanyavedāntārthaviṣayakā pātapratītimataḥ puruṣasya, ātmā vā

are' iti śruteṣu madhye) śravaṇa - mananayoḥ – gurumukhāt vedāntatastvārthaśravaṇe, śrutārthapratiṣṭhāyai upapattitaḥ (nyāyataḥ) cintane (manane) ca, pravṛttiḥ — prayatnaḥ, ādau – vidhivyāpārāt pūrvameva, rāgāt - icchāta eva sampadyate, dhyānamekam – nididhyāsanamekameva, vidheyam- anyato'prāptaṃ vidhivyāpāreṇaiva jñāpyam ('nididhyāsitavyaḥ' ityanena ) draṣṭavyaśabdaḥ -

H

dhyāne vaiśadyamātrādupacaraṇavatī dṛṣṭiśabdapravṛttiḥ
tulyaṃ tat tvanmate'pi hyabhilaṣati viyaṃ cākṣuṣīṃ dṛṣṭiśabdaḥ | ghātā spaṣṭeti śabdaḥ karaṇavirahite mukhyavṛtto na tu syāt
tadgrāhyastvasmadīyo bahunayaguṇavāṃstyājya eva tvadīyaḥ || 8 ||

dhyāne dṛṣṭiśabdapravṛttiḥ smṛtyantaḥ pātini dhyānarūpe'rthe abhidheye, darśana rūpa- cākṣupajñānabodhata svābhāvyavato dṛśdhātughaṭitaśabdasya prayogaḥ ( śrutyādidṛṣṭaḥ ), upacaraṇavatī- mukhyapravṛttinimittarahite mukhyārtha sāmīpyakṛtaḥ, vaiśadyamātrāt - cākṣuṣajñānagataṃ atyanta spaṣṭatvamātraṃ nimittīkṛtya (āgataḥ), (idaṃ viśiṣṭādvaitimate mukhyamartha vihāya upacatārthasvīkaraṇam ) tat- - aupacārikā rthasvīkaraṇam tvanmate'pi - asmanmate doṣaṃ cintayatāmadvaitināṃ mate'pi, tulyam – samānameva, hi yataḥ, dṛṣṭiśabdaḥ - dṛśdhātughaṭitaśabdaprayogaḥ, cākṣuṣīṃ dhiyam – cakṣuḥ karaṇakamativiśeṣam, abhilaṣati – abhipreti, karaṇavirahite - indriyaśūnye (indriyā karaṇajñānadharma ke ) brahmaṇi prayujyamānaḥ, ghātā spraṣṭeti śabdaḥ - ghaṇe ndriya | ghīnajñāna- vadarthako prātṛśabdaḥ tvagindriyādhīnajñānavadarthakaḥ spraṣṭṛśabdo vā, mukhyavṛttoṃ na tu syāt- puṣkalapravṛttinimittayogena prayukto naiva bhavitumarhati tat — tasmāddhetoḥ, bahunayaguṇavān- sāmānyaviśeṣanyāyā dirūpabahunyāya sahakāravān śrutismṛtyādyanurodharūpaṃ bahuguṇavāṃśdha, asmadīyaḥ- viśiṣṭādvaityanusṛtaḥ pakṣa eva grāhyaḥ – vivecakaiḥ upādeyaḥ tvadīyaḥ advaitimirāśritaḥ pakṣaḥ param tyājya eva anupādeya eva || 8 ||

hi—yataḥ,

nadvāra - dvāriklaptiḥ kathamapi ghaṭate viśramaścadviśeṣe
sāmānyātmā viśeṣairlaghu na ca gurubhiḥ kartu raikye vikalpyam | arthaikye darśanoktyā smṛtirupacaritā yujyate spaṣṭyasiddhayai
smṛtyuktayā darśanasyopacaraṇamasadityanyadetat phalānnaḥ || 9 ||
viśeṣe viśramaḥ ghaṭate cet — ( vedanā de: śabdasya jñānasāmānyavācinaḥ ) dhyāne pāsana- bhaktayātmaka jñānaviśeṣe paryavasānagavṛtyā anvayaḥ sambhavati yadi, (tadā) dvāra-dvāriklaptiḥ— ( vedanaṃ dhyānaṃ janayati, dhyānaṃ dhruvāṃ smṛrti, dhruvā smṛtiḥ darśana, darśanaṃ bhaktiṃ janayatītyevaṃ ) vedanasya dhyānādidvārā bhaktijanakatvaṃ nimittīkṛtya tādṛśaśabdaprayogarūpā dvāra-dvāribhāvakalpanā, kathamapi na ghaṭate - kenāpi prakāreṇa na saṅgatā bhavati ( kalpanāyā gurutvāt ), sāmānyātmā - vedanarūpajñānasāmānyātmako'rthaḥ, viśeṣaiḥ – dhyāna dhruvasmṛtyādirūpaiḥ jñānatvāvāntaradharmavadbhiḥ saha

na vikalyaḥ – vikalpitopāyatayā vidhātuṃ naiva śakyaḥ, (samānayoreva vikalpāt ) karture kyai - – upāyānuṣṭhātuḥ puruṣasya abhede siddhe, laghu- alpaśramādiniṣpādyaṃ sādhanam, gurubhiḥ — bahnāyāsādiniṣpādyaiḥ sādhanaiḥ saha na ca vikalpyam - vikalpato vidhānāī naiva bhavitumarham (samānayoreva vikalpāītvā), arthaikye – mokṣopāyabhūtajñānābhede sati, smṛtiḥ -- mokṣopāyabhūtā śubhāśraya bhagavadrūpasmṛtiḥ, spāṣṭathasiddhayai – viśadatamatvapratipattilābhāya, darśanoktyā upacaritā pratyakṣavācinā śabdena upacārataḥ prayuktā ityetat yujyate- saṅgataṃ bhavati, smṛtyuktyā – smaraṇavācinā śabdena, darśanasya – sākṣātkārarūpasya jñānasya, upacaraṇam – upacārataḥ kathanam asat na sādhu ( utkṛṣṭopacāra: nikṛṣṭa yuktaḥ ; na tu utkṛṣṭe nikṛṣṭopacāraḥ ) iti ato hetoḥ smṛtereva pratyakṣatāpannāyāḥ santatadhyānarūpāyā eva mokṣopāyateti siddham, etat idaṃ vedanam, phalāt — phalarūpāddarśanāt, anyat - atiriktamevopāsya bhūtam ' draṣṭavya iti vihitaṃ darśanamiti ( phalasya vidheyatvāsambhavāt, draṣṭavyaḥ' iti ca vidhipratyayasya spaṣṭaṃ śruteḥ iti) || 9 ||

kṛcchra dau śaktihīno dṛḍhaparitapanaḥ saṃyamāheśca kuryāt

sarvārthaṃ keśavānusmṛtimiti ghaṭate tadvikalpo yathārham | sāmānyāt sarvadoṣeṣviyamupakurute sarvanaimittikānāṃ
mukterapyatra mūlaṃ muniranumanute tādṛśadhyānarūpam || 10||

kṛcchrādau –kṛcchra- cāndrāyaṇa sāntapana - parākā dau duṣkṛtaviśeṣaniṣkṛtirūpatayā śāstra- siddhe karmaṇi, śaktihīna ḥ– vidhivadanuṣṭhānānuguṇadaihika-mānasikā diyogyatā vidūra: ( puruṣa : ), dṛḍhaparitapanaḥ paścāttāpadāyavān, saṃyamāśca – sampannadhyānayogatayā manaādīndriya- niyamanapūrvakaṃ bhagavatsmaraṇākhye mānase vyāpāre kṛtsnatāpādanasāmarthya vāṃśca janaḥ, sarvārthām- sarvavidhaduṣkṛtaprāyaścittasthānāpaśyarhām, keśavānusmṛtim – ' prāyaścittānyaśeṣāṇi, kṛṣṇa smaraṇaṃ param ' iti pratipāditām, śrīkṛṣṇadhyānarūpāṃ nirantarānuvṛttāṃ smṛtim kuryāditi- anutiṣṭhet iti hetoḥ, yathārham - duṣkṛnaprāyaścittānuguṇyena ( prāyaścittasthāne anuṣṭhānarūpaḥ), vikalpaḥ —–pākṣikānuṣṭhānavyāpāraḥ ghaṭate yukto bhavati, sāmānyāt sarvadoṣeṣu- pra-teṣu satsu teṣu sarveṣvapi sarvanaimittikānām- - sarva ttannimittakānāṃ prāyaśvittānāmapyanuṣṭhinānām iyam - ante kriyamāṇā kṛṣṇānummṛtiḥ, upakurute - ( prāyaścitta) pūrtimāpādayantī atiśayamādhatte, (kiṃca tādṛśadhyānarūpāṃ keśavānu- smṛtim - niratiśayaprītirūpatva- viśadatamapratyakṣatāpannatvā khya viśiṣṭaveṣayuktāṃ bhagavatsmṛti santatim,

muniḥ – bādarāyaṇaḥ, atra - śārīrakaśāstre, mukterapi mūlamanumanute - mokṣasyāpyupāyatvena (anādisañcitā vidyāyā api prāyaścittībhūya saṃsārabandhanivartikāṃ saṃmanyate (asmin ākṛtya- dhikaraṇe liṅgāccetyanena pārāśarīṃ smṛtiṃ pramāṇayan ) || 10 ||

nanvevaṃ ye mukundaṃ śaraṇamupagatā varjitā dhyānayogaḥ
te cātikramya mṛtyuṃ yamina iva paraṃ dhāma yāntītyuśanti |
satyaṃ te'pīti śabdānniyata viṣayayogara ve lāghave'pi
prāptārthaṃ dhyānaśāstraṃ praṇidhisahadaśāyogibhiryogavidbhiḥ || 11 ||
nanu bhoḥ, evam – kṛṣṇānusmṛtinairantaryarūpasya dhyānayogasyaiva sarvaprāyaścittatā mokṣo, pāyatā ceti sarvasāmarthya niścīyate yadi !, ye - yādṛśāścetanāḥ, mukundaṃ śaraṇamupagatā:- muktidāyakaṃ bhagavantaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ rakṣakatvena svīkṛtya nyastasamastātmabharāḥ, dhyānayogaḥ varjitāḥ- mokṣopāyatayā vedāntapratipannaiḥ vividha vidyānuṣṭhāna kāṣṭhābhūtaiḥ dhyānairvirahitāḥ santi, te ca- tādṛśā api cetanāḥ, mṛtyumatikramya – saṃsāramatilaya, yamina iva - prāptasaṃnyāsayogā iva paraṃ dhāma yāntīti - viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ prāpnuvantīti, uśanti - śāstrāṇi prati- pādayanti ('śaraṇaṃ tvāṃ prapannā ye dhyānayogavivarjitāḥ | te'pi mṛtyumatikramya yānti tadvaiṣṇavaṃ padam ||' iti ), evaṃ sthite pūrvamuktaṃ kathaṃ vā yujyeteti śaṅkāyām, satyam - uktaṃ sarva- maṅgīkāryameva śaraṇavaraṇasyā'pi muktayupāyatve avivādāta (param ) te'pīti śabdāt- 'te'pi mṛtyumatikramye' tyatra dhyānayogitaulyena śaraṇāgatānāmapyullekhāt, gaurave lāghave'pi niyataviṣayayoḥ praṇidhānasamarthe gurubhūtayogānuṣṭhānavidhiḥ, tadasamarthe ākiñcanyānanyagatika - tvādimati laghubhūtaprapatyanuṣṭhāna vidhiriti rītyā labdhaviṣayavyavasthayoḥ ubhayorapyupāyayormadhye, dhyānaśāstram - mokṣopāyatayā dhyānavidhāyi śāstram, praṇidhisahadaśāyogibhiḥ — aṅga- kalāpasampanna ( aṣṭāṅgayoga ) cittapraṇidhānayogyakaraṇa- kalebarasampattirūpāvasthāviśeṣavadbhiḥ yogavidbhiḥ–yogānuṣṭhānaprakārathāthātmyavedanayuktaiḥ (jñānānuṣṭhānapūrṇaiḥ) mahadbhiḥ prāptārtham - sāvakāśatvena cāritāvadbhavati ||

dharmo varṇādiyogyaḥ kaluṣaśamanataḥ satvavṛddhayopakārī
bhaktestadvat prapattistvagatikasamayeṣvantarāyā pahantrī | sānukrośe hi śakte śaraṇavaraṇataḥ sarvasādhyaṃ susādhaṃ

mokṣākāṅkṣī prapadye śaraṇamahamiti kāpi mantre śrutaṃ ca

varṇādiyogyaḥ dharmaḥ tattadvarṇādiyogyaḥ vedānuvacana-yajña - dāna - tapaādirūpo vidhi - vihito dharmaḥ, kaluṣaśamanataḥ -- antarāyabhūtapāpanirharaṇadvārā, sacvavṛddhayā - āvaśyaka pramāṇena saśvaguṇasya vṛddhyāpādanapūrvakam bhakteḥ upakārī mokṣasādhanabhūtāyā upāsanākhyāyāḥ bhakteḥ svarūpaniṣpanyaupayikaḥ san aṅgaṃ bhavati, tadvat — varṇāśramādidharmā iva, prapattistu – svābhīṣṭe ananyasādhye sati tatsādhanāya bhagavadekopāyatāyācñā'pi, agatikasamayeṣu — ananyagatika- svavatsu avasareṣu antarāyāpahantrī bhaktavighnanivāriṇī bhavati | (bhaktayaṅgakarmasthāne sthitvā bhaktipratibandhakān dūrīkṛtya bhaktiniṣpattau sahakāriṇī bhavati) śakte—phala. pradānasamarthe, sānukrośe hi — pagduḥ khaprahāṇecchāvati khalu puruṣe ( kriyamāṇāt ) śaraṇa- varaṇataḥ –rakṣakatvaprārthanāt, sarvasādhyam – sarvavidhaistaistairupāyaiḥ sampādyatvena lokaśāstra- kāspi- siddhaṃ sarvavidhamapi prayojanam, susādham - aśrameṇa sampādayituṃ śakyaṃ bhavati mantre śvetāśvatarīye ' yo brahmāṇaṃ vidadhāti pūrvam' iti mantre, mokṣākāṃkṣī aham - mumukṣāyuktaḥ aham śaraṇaṃ prapadye iti-rakṣakatāvaraṇapūrvakaṃ prapanno bhavāmīti śrutaṃ ca- 'mumukṣurve śaraṇamahaṃ prapadye' iti mantrāntimabhāge āmnātamapi dṛśyata iti || 12 ||

atha turyodhyāyapādapradhānārthā matā ime |
adhā śleṣotkāntimārgaparabrahmopasampadaḥ || 173 ||

nītiṣaṭ kā'parā nītipañcakā phalabodhinī || 174 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||

jīvādatyantabhinnaḥ sa vibhurabhidadhe lakṣaṇaiḥ sādhanāntai- muktayarthopāsane'smin muṣitatamasi na brahmadṛṣṭyādiyuktiḥ | tatvajñe vītarāge tadiha na ghaṭate so'hamasmītyupāsti-
stanna svātmāntarātmanyahamiti vacaso'pyatra mukhyapravṛtteḥ || 13 ||

sādhanāntaiḥ lakṣaṇaiḥ—sādhanādhyāyaparyantaistribhiradhyāyaiḥ, sa vibhuḥ – jagatkāraṇatva--- kṛtopāsyatvavān paramātmā, jīvāt atyantabhinnaḥ abhidadhe – jīvātmanaḥ sakāśāt sarvathā vilakṣaṇatvena nirūpitaḥ, mupitatamasi tamaḥ kṛtabhrāntyādisamparkarahite, asmin muktyartho - pāsanesādhanādhyāyena pradhānato nirūpite mokṣakaphalake asmin upāsane, na brahmadṛṣṭyādi- yukti: brahmaṇi abrahmadṛṣṭirūpatāyāḥ abrahmaṇi brahmadṛṣṭirūpatāyā vā sāṅgatyaṃ na sambhavati, ( dṛṣṭirūpāyā vidyāyāḥ atasmiṃstadbuddhirūpatayā bhrāntitvāvarjanāt mukti prati ca paramārtha - viṣayakasya yathārthajñānasyaivopāyatāsambhavena, ayathārthajñānātmikāyāḥ anyasminnanyadṛṣṭeḥ upāya- tvāyogādityāśayaḥ ) | tat — tasmāddhetoḥ, tatvajñe – vāstavārthajñānavati, (ayathārthajñānarahite)- vītarāge – kāmyaphalānāsaṅgini, iha – mumukṣayā pravṛtte upāsitari, 'so'hamasmī' syupāstiḥ- upāsyabhūtaḥ paramātmaivā'hamasmi ' ahaṃ brahmāsmi' ityevamākāropāsanasambhavaḥ, na ghaṭate- naiva abādhitā bhavati āropatāpatteḥ (iti śaṅkane), tanna — tadetacchaṅkanaṃ naiva yuktam, svātmāntarātmani — jīvaṃ pratyapi ātmatvavattayā antarātmabhūte, atra – upāsye paramātmani, ahamiti vacasopi—ahantvollekhinyāḥ svābhedaparavācopi, mukhyapravṛtteḥ – anaupacārikatayā pradhānatvenaiva pravṛtteḥ svābhāvikatvāt, (upāsanasyā'pi āropatvāderanavakāśāditi || 13 || ( 445 ) vasvindrāderupāstau prakṛtiśabalitasvānvitekṣā puroktā

śuddhaḥ svātmā ca cintyaḥ kvacidiha tu vibhustādṛśā svena yuktaḥ | vyaktirjīveśabhede vyadhikaraṇapadairbhāvane syāt tathāpi brahmāyattasvarūpapramitisudṛḍhatā siddhaye'haṅgrahoktiḥ || 14 || vasvindrāderupastau—vasurudrādityānāmupāsanarūpāyāṃ madhuvidyāyām, indropāsanātmikā-

yām indraprāṇavidyāyāṃ ca prakṛtiśaba litasvānvitekṣā vasvādipadavācya deha viśeṣaśarīri- bhūtajīvaviśiṣṭaparamātmacintanā, indrapadavācya saṃsthāna viśeṣa vadde ha śarīribhūtajī va viśiṣṭaparamātma- cintanā ca, purā — ādyādyapāde madhvadhikaraṇe indraprāṇādhikaraṇe ca śārīrakīye

uktā nyāyatto nirūpitā, śuddhaḥ - acitsaṃsargāyattakāluṣya ra hitaḥ, svātmā ca - upāsakī- bhūtaścetana eva, kvacit – jīvopāsanātmakapañcāgnividyā dyupāsanaviśeṣe, cintyaḥ — upāsya- tayā śrutisaṃmataḥ, iha tu - mokṣopāyabhūta brahmopāsanātmikāyāṃ sarvasyāmapi paravidyāyām, tādṛśā svena yukto vibhuḥ – prakṛtisaṃsargarahitatvarūpaśuddhimatā jīvena viśiṣṭaḥ paramātmaiva, (cintya- -vena vedāntatātparyaviṣayaḥ) jīveśabhede vyaktiḥ - īdṛśī ca jīva - paramātmabhedaghaṭitā upāsanā, vyadhikaraṇapadaiḥ bhāvane– 'tvamevā'ham ahameva tvam' iti upāsyopāsaka bhūtapara jīva- vācakaśabdayoḥ samānavibhaktikatāṃ vihāya tvaṃ mama svāmī, ahaṃ tava svabhūtaḥ' ityevaṃ virūpa- vibhaktikaiḥ padaiḥ cintanārambhe kriyamāṇe satyeva, syāt — niṣpannā bhavet, na tu abheda- jñāpakatvasvabhāvaiḥ samānādhikaraṇaiḥ padaiḥ pūrvoktākāraiḥ ) ata: 'ahaṃ brahmāsmi, brahmaivāhamasmi, tvaṃ vā ahamasmi ityādayaḥ śabdāḥ upāsanākārānanukūlāḥ iti śaṅkane ), uktārthasya kiṃcidaśe yuktatve'pi, brahmāyatta ... siddhaye - upāsitṛ jīva svarūpe sarvathā paramātmā- cīnatvayāthātmyajñānātyanta! vicālanīyatvalābhāya, ahaṅgrahoktiḥ vaiyadhikaraṇyaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyena brahmāhamarthayoḥ abhedaparyavasāyitvāvagāhi bhāvanājana kā haṃśabdagrahaṇapūrvakopāsana- pradarśana śrutau yuktameveti || 14 ||

tathā'pi -

vihāya

aikyopāstāvahaṃ tvaṃ tvamaddamiti matirnirviśeṣe kathaṃ syād ! bhedābhedābhilāpaḥ karakamaṇikatadvayomanītyā na mukhyaḥ |
mattulyastvaṃ tvayā'haṃ sama iti vadatāṃ nopacāro'pi yukta-

stasmāt sarvāntarātmanyayamahamiti dhīrākṛtinyāyasiddhā || 15 ||

nirviśeṣe - (brahmaṇi upāsyabhūte ) sarvaviśeṣarahitatvenābhimate sati, aikyopāstau- 'tvaṃ vā ahamasmī' tyādyahaṃ grahopāsanarūpe aikyopāsane, antargatā, 'ahaṃ tvam tvam aham ' - iti matiḥ - ahaṃśabdoccārayitṛtvopalakṣitātmatvavati sambodhyatvopalakṣitātmavadabhedāvagāhinī, (tathā) sambodhyatvopalakṣitātmatvavati ahaṃśabdoccāra yitṛtvopalakṣitātmatvavadabhedāvagāhinī ca pratītiḥ kathaṃ vā syāt – kena vā prakāreṇa yuktā bhavet ? (uccārayitṛtva sambodhyatvādeḥ viśeṣātmakasya dharmasya cintane apāramārthyāpatteḥ, acintane upāsanasyā'niṣpatteḥ ) karaka- maṇikatadvayomanītyā—(karaka-maṇiko jaladhāramṛtpātraviśeṣau ) karaka-maṇikayoḥ mṛtpātrayoḥ svarūpakṛtaḥ bhedaḥ, tadgatākāśasvarūpe tu abheda itivat bhedābheda bhilāpaḥ pāramārthikaḥ abhedaḥ kalpitaśca bheda iti kalpitaṃ bhedamupajīvya ! tvamaham ahaṃ tva' miti upāsanapravṛttiḥ

iti bhāskarapakṣo'pi, anucita eva yataḥ na mukhyaḥ - viruddha yorbheda - tadabhāvayorekatra cintanasya anyatarāmukhyatvaṃ vinā anirvāhyatvāpattiḥ | (tathā) ' tvam mattulyaḥ aham tvayā samaḥ ' iti vadatām — aikyopāstivākyasyāyamarthaḥ ; 'he upāsyatatvabhūta ! tvaṃ matsadṛśaḥ, ahaṃ ca tvatsadṛśaḥ iti tvamahaṃ śabdayoḥ tvatsadṛśa - matsadṛśaparatāvādināṃ (vyavahāragataḥ ),

upa-

cāro'pi - amukhyārthatātparyamapi na yuktaḥ - nyāyyatā nāvalambate, | tasmāt — itareṣā kalpanā viśeṣasya sarvasyā'pi doṣagrasta- tvāt sarvāntarātmani sarveṣāmapi jīvānāmantaḥ praviśya niyamanaśīle, ata eva sarva-

prakāra prakāribhūte paramātmani (sarvavyavahāraṃ pratyapi parama viśeṣye),
ayam aham ' iti dhīḥ - ' yo'sau so'ham, yo'ham so'sau ' iti, 'tvamaham, ahaṃ tvam' iti ca paramātmani svābhedasya svasmin paramātmābhedasya ca prakāryaikyarūpasya avagāhanātmikā śrutyu- llikhitā matiḥ, ākṛtinyāyasiddhā - pūrvamīmāṃsāgatena ākṛtyadhikaraṇasiddhena nyāyena anupacaritatvena niścitamukhyabhāvaiva || 15 ||

advaitaṃ dvaitahānau na bhavati suvacaṃ tatpratidvandvikatvād
dvaitaṃ cā'dvaitagarbha dvitayamiha hi ! tat svasvarūpādabhinnam |
dvaitādvaitaṃ ca tādṛk tadubhayaniyamānujjhanādeva sidhyet
sarvaṃ sthāne sthitaṃ syāt pramitiparavatāṃ netareṣāṃ tu kiṃcit || 16 ||
dvaitahānau — dvaitapadābhilapyasyā'rthasya anaṅgīkā ratastyāge kriyamāṇe, advaitam —

dvaitasya viruddhamadvaitapadābhilapyamartha svarūpamapi, na suvacam - na suṣṭhu pratipādanārham, tatpratidvandvikatvāt — dvaitapratiyogikatvāt, (abhāvasiddhau pratiyogisiddherhetutvāt ) dvaitaṃ ca- dvaitapadavācyamapi, advaitagarbham advaita padavācyārthaghaṭita svarūpavat, hi-yata:, iha — dvaitapadārthe ( antaḥ praviṣṭam ), svakharūpādabhinnam – pratyekaṃ svīyasvarūpapratiyogika bhedānadhikaraṇam, dvitayam – ekaikātmakāvayavadvayameva, tat - dvaitavyavahāragocaro bhavitumarhati, (bheda - dvaitaśabdayoḥ paryāyatayā, dvayātmaka kūṭapraviṣṭasyaikaikasya, svasmin svabhedābhāvena advaitasyāvarjanīyatayā, advaita- vatpadārthadvayavati samudāye eva dvaitavyavahārapravṛttisambhavāt ityarthaḥ) | tādṛk — dvaitatulyam tadabhāvarūpādvaitatulyaṃ ca, dvaitādvaitaṃ ca- dvaitādvaitapadavācyamarthatattvamapi tadubhayaniyamānujjhanā- deva - dvaitapadārthasiddhau yo niyamaḥ advaitaghaṭitatvāvarjanarūpaḥ, advaitapadārthasiddhau yo niyamaḥ svapratiyogibhūtadvaitāvarjanarūpaḥ taṃ niyamama vihāyaiva sidhyet — labdhasvarūpaṃ bhavet, ( ataḥ idaṃ niyamamavidyāyaiva,

trayamapi tattanmatābhiniveśavatāṃ nā'nukūlam ; pratyuta pratikūlameva ) pramitiparavatāṃ tu pramāṇa- paratantrāṇāṃ param, sarvam – uktaṃ sarvamapi, sthāne yogye viṣaye, sthitaṃ syāt - avasthāna- svābhāvyayuktaṃ bhavet, itareṣām — pramāṇānanurodhena svacchandānurodhināṃ mataikaśaraṇānām, na tu kiṃcit naiva sidhyet yatkiñcidapyarthatattvam || 16 ||
nanvadvaite niṣedhyo gaganakusumavad bhrāntisiddho'stu bhedaḥ

maivaṃ satyādabhinnaḥ sa yadi | na khalu tadbhrāntisiddhatvasiddhiḥ | bhinnatvaṃ cāsya tasmād yadi bhavati mṛṣā ! viddhi dattottaraṃ tat
satyaṃ cet ! satyabhedopagatiriti mudhā dūrato ghāvanaṃ vaḥ || 17 ||

nanu bhoḥ vivecanapravṛttāḥ ! advaite niṣedhyaḥ– advaitaśabdārthaghaṭaka najabodhya- niṣedhapratiyogibhūtaḥ, bhedaḥ - dvaitapadābhilapyo bhedaḥ, gaganakusumavat gaganakusumaṃ nāstīti niṣedhe pratiyogibhūtaṃ gaganakusumamiva bhrāṃntisiddho'stu — bhramamātra gocarasvarūpo bhavatu, na tu pramāgocarasvarūpa : ; ( yena tasyā'pi vāstavikatā avarjyā syāt | gaganakusumaṃ hi niṣidhya- mānaṃ, niṣedhapratiyogitvāya na vāstavatāṃ pratipadyate | tadvat 'advaita' mityatra na dvaitamiti niṣidhyamānaṃ dvaitamapi, aparamārthadṛṣṭīnāmajñānināmavidyāvṛtamatīnāṃ grāhyatayā siddhameveti, niṣedha- pratiyogitvamātreṇa na dvaitapāramārthyamiti) śaṅkane, maivam evaṃ śaṅkitu nāīm saḥ - 'advaitam' ityatra niṣedhyo dvaitarūpo bhedaḥ, satyāt abhinno yadi - paramārthādbrahmaṇaḥ sakāśāda- natirikto yadi ! tadā na khalu tadbhāntisiddhatvasiddhi : – (paramārthabrahmā natirekiṇo bhedasyāpi, brahmaṇa iva pāramārthyāvaśyaṃbhāvena bhramavidūratāyā eva ca pāramārthyarūpatvena ) bhedasyoktāyā: bhrāntyā siddheḥ naiva yuktayā siddhiḥ sambhāvyā, asya niṣedhyabhūtasya bhedasya, tasmādbhinnatvaṃ ca brahmaṇaḥ sakāśādatiriktatā ca, yadi mṛṣā bhavati – mithyābhūtaiva cet syāt, tat dattottaraṃ viddhi – tadetat pūrvameva pratyuttaritaṃ jānīhi (paramārthābhinnabrahmā bhinnatāyā eva bhedepyaṅgīkṛtatvāpattyā tadabhinnābhinnanyāyena tasyā'pi pāramārthyāvaśyambhāvāt ), satyaṃ cet — bhedasya brahmaṇaḥ sakāśādbhinnatvaṃ, pāramārthikameva cedabhyupagamyate tadā, satyabhedopagatiriti- brahmabhedākhyasya brahmātiriktasyānyasya satyasyābhyupagamāpacyā brahmakameva satyamityabhyupagamasya nirviṣayatvāpātaḥ — iti hetoḥ, vaḥ - yuṣmākam, dūratodhāvanam - advaita samayasiddhārthasamartha - nāya vahuprayāsāṅgīkaraṇam, mudhā-vyarthameva ; na abhilaṣitasādhakaṃ bhavatīti ||17||

adhikaraṇārtha saṃgraha:

ātmatyopāsanādhikaraṇe___jīva brahmaṇorbhinnatvāt, jīvādanyatvena brahma upāsyam -

ityāśaṅkaya,

' tvaṃ vā ahamasmi ityādiṣu pūrvopāsakai: 'brahmā'ham' ityanusandhānadarśanāt, arthāntarabhūtasyā'pi tasya ātmano'ntaryāmitvena ' aha' mityanusandhānasya aviruddhatvācca ātmatvenopāsanam - ityuktam ||
