adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
nāmādibrahmadṛṣṭāvapi tadahamiti pratyayaḥ pūrvavat syād brahmadhyānatvasāmyāditi yadi | na na khalvantarātmapratīkaḥ |
na brahmaṇyanyadṛṣṭiḥ kathamapi ghaṭate tasya sarvādhikatvā-

danyasmin brahmadṛṣṭau paramiha niyatailakṣaṇaistaho'dhyaḥ || 18 ||
nāmādidṛṣṭāvapi nāma brahmetyupāsīta, mano brahmetyupāsīta '
itya diśrutivihita- nāma-manaādiviśeṣya kabrahmatvaprakāraka buddhirūpapratīkopāsane'pi tadahamiti pratyayaḥ – 'upāsya nāmādimadabhinnam ' ityevaṃrūpaḥ ātmatvopāsanapadābhidheyaḥ dhīviśeṣaḥ, pūrvavat – para vidyārūpa-

bhavedeva,

viviktāṃ sthitim na śithilayati naivāpohituṃ prabhavet, ( phalaviśeṣāya kriyamāṇā 'garuḍoham' ityākārā hi ! āropātmikā svasmin garuḍābheda cintā, na hi cintayituḥ garuḍasya ca vidyamānaṃ svābhāvikaṃ bhedaṃ nāśayitumīṣṭe ; tadvat śrutyāśayānurodhena kriyamāṇā ahaṃ brahmāsmīti cintā'pi upāsyopāsakayorvāstavikaṃ bhedamapagamayituṃ naiva śaknuyādityarthaḥ) etāvanmātramohāt – bhinnayorabhedadṛṣṭeḥ phalādhāyakatvadarśanamātrakṛtāt ajñānāt, idamaham iti tu sthāpayan 'dhyeyaṃ jagatkāraṇatvādimat brahma ahameva ' ityevaṃ manasi dṛḍhīkartuṃ prayatamāno indratvāropadarpoddhatanahuṣamahābhogisaṃsthāmavasthām – svasmin tātkālikatayā prāptena indratvādhyavasānena samāpatitaṃ garvamavalambya naṣṭaviveko yo nahuṣanāmā mānuṣo rājā, (svānanuguṇe indrāṇī saṃśleṣarūpe mahābhoge pravṛttasya ) tasya ajagaraśarīrāvāptiparyavasāyinīṃ sthitim, kṣipram ṛcchet — śīghrameva prāpnuyāt ; nā'tra sandehaḥ || 19||

naraḥ

ādityādimatyadhikaraṇam || 4-1-4||
śārīrakanyāya kalāpasaṃgrahaḥ ||
udgīthadṛṣṭistapane tu nīce śreṣṭha hi karmaiva phalapradatvāt |
tannaiva tasyā'pi phalapradatvaṃ tatpūjanatvāditi nā'nyathā syāt || 4 ||

udgītha eṣṭiḥ–' ya evā'sau tapati tamudgīthamupāsīta iti vihitaṃ dṛṣṭayupāsanam, nīce - yāgīya devatātvā khyarūpatyakṛtā prādhānyavati, tapane tu — āditye eva yuktā, hi- yataḥ, phalapradatvāt – puruṣāpekṣita phalapradānasamarthatvāt karmaiva — yāgādikameva śreṣṭham- utkarṣavadbhavitumarham, (iti śaṅkane) tannaiva tadetat śaṅkanaṃ naiva sādhu bhavati, tasyā'pi — karmaṇo'pi tatpūjanatvāt – devatāprītyā pādana hetvākāratvā deva, phalapradatvam - vidhyavagata- phalasampādakatvam, anyathā - devatāprītyanāpādakatve tu na syāt iti – naiva phalādhāyakatvaṃ syāditi (ārthotkarṣasya devatāgatatvāt ārtha- śābdaprādhānyayormadhye ārthaprādhānyasyaiva - jyāyastvāt ) ādityādidevatādṛṣṭireva udgīthādau karmāne kāryeti || 4 ||

devataiva khalu, phalaṃ pradiśatīti karmasādhyatayā vidhisiddhaṃ svargādirūpaṃ kāmyamartha pradadātīti

hetoḥ sā arthataḥ pradhānam — devataiva arthasvabhāvādhīnotkarṣavatī bhavati ; na karma ( śābda-- vāstavika prādhānyayormadhye vāstavikasyaiva kāryakarasya grāhyatvāt || 20 ||
anyasmin anyadṛṣṭerviciriha ghaṭatāmanyathākhyā tipakṣe

yāthārthyaṃ sarvabodhānugatamiti mate yujyate nā'nyadṛṣṭiḥ |
tannā'trādye hi na syād vimatirabhimatā bhrāntinītiḥ parasmi-

stādṛgbhedāgrahopaplutamatiyugalasthāpanāyāṃ niyogāt || 21 ||

iha — 'mano brahmetyupāsīta ' ' nāma brahmetyupāsīta' ityādau, anyasmin anyadṛṣṭe - vidhiḥ – mano nāmaprabhṛtike brahmātirikte'rthe mano-nāmādītarabrahmā bhedacintāyāḥ śrutito vidhāna- mityetat . anyathākhyātipakṣe – bhramasya anyathākhyātirūpatābādinā mate, (yatkiñciddharmi- viśeṣyaka- viśeṣyāvṛttidharmaprakāraka khyāti (jñāna) viśeṣasyaiva bhramarūpatāvādināṃ tārkikāṇāṃ mate) , ghaṭatām – yujyatāṃ nāma yāthārthyam - viśeṣyāvṛttidharmāprakārakatvameva sarvabodhānu- gatam — pramā'pramātmakajñānasāmānyasādhāraṇam, iti mate ityabhyupagantṝṇāṃ prābhākarāṇī viśiṣṭaikyavādināṃ ca mate, anyadṛṣṭiḥ śrutyavagatā manaādau brahmatādātmyacintā, yujyate- -naiva saṅgatā bhavati, (iti śaṅkane) tanna — tadetat śaṅkanaṃ naiva yuktam, atra — dvayoranayoḥ pakṣayormadhye (anyatrānyadṛṣṭiviṣaye iti vā ) ādye – anyathākhyātipakṣe, vimatiḥ- vipratipattiḥ, na hi syāt – na khalu bhavet, (avipratipattyā nirvāha: sidhyati ) parasmin- paścāttane yathārthakhyātipakṣe, bhrāntinītiḥ - śuktyādau rajatabhramasthale iva nirvāharīti : ', abhimatā - yathārthakhyātivādyabhimatā, (mano brahmaṇoḥ bhedāgrahasahakṛta mano-brahmo bhayajñānadvayameva dṛṣṭayupāsanamiti ) tādṛgbhedāgrahopalputamatiyugalasthāpanāyāṃ niyogāt - śuktirajatabhrānti- sthalīya bhedāgrahasadṛśamano- brahmobhayabhedagrahaṇābhāvasaha kṛtā gṛhīta bhedakamanojñāna- brahmajñānarūpajñānadvaya- sthirīkaraṇe eva dṛṣṭividhestātparyāt || (ubhayathā'pi śrautasya dṛṣṭividheḥ na virodhaḥ ) || 21 || draṣṭavyā havena bhramadanuvihitā dṛṣṭiradvaitamoha :

na

syādutkṛṣṭāparādhastata iha vihitā tādṛśatvasya dṛṣṭiḥ | tādṛgdṛṣṭau na doṣastata iti katicit tārkṣyadṛṣṭyā dinītyā
dvedhā'nyatrā'nyadṛṣṭirna kimanaghigatā vyaktito jātitaśca || 22 ||

dṛṣṭiḥ – ' mano brahma, nāma brahme tyā dau dṛṣṭividhivākye kartavyatvena coditā dṛṣṭirnāma, bhramadanu vihitā bhrāntaṃ puruṣamanusṛtya ( tadīyabhramānuguṇyena ) sampādanīyatayā śrutyuktā, draṣṭavyāpasavena dṛṣṭi: dṛṣṭiviṣayī kāryasya manaāderarthasya apalāpena (sattāyāḥ buddhāva- viṣayīkāreṇa) kevalaṃ brahmaṇa evaikasya cintārūpeti vivaraṇam, advaitamohaḥ – ( 'cora; sthāṇuḥ iti vyavahāre yathā core sthāṇutvadṛṣṭi: corāpahnavena sthāṇumātrapāriśeṣyeṇa bhadvaitasiddhiḥ ; tathā dṛṣṭividhau mana ādyapalāpena brahmamātra pāriśeṣyarūpādvaitasiddhirbhavatīti) advaitā mimāna- mūlam, tataḥ uktāddhetoḥ, iha vihitā – dṛṣṭividhivākye ( manaādau vā ) coditā, tādṛśatvasya dṛṣṭiḥ – brahmatādātmyasya ( brahmasādṛśyasya vā) cintā, utkṛṣṭā - apakṛṣṭe atiśayitadṛṣṭirūpatvena utkṛṣṭā satyapi, aparādhaḥ syāt — asatyevārthe brahmatādātmyā nusandhāna- rūpatvāpacyā, na satkalāya kalpeta ; kintu aparādhatvāpatyā doṣāyaiva kalpeta, (yadvā) utkṛṣṭā- parādhaḥ syāt - aparādhaparākāṣṭhāṃ prāpto bhavet tādṛgdṛṣṭau - brahmasādṛśyamūlakabrahmā bhedādhyavasāye vidhīyamāne sati tārkṣyadṛṣṭyādinītyā- ' svātmānaṃ garuḍaṃ jānīyāt' iti vihite svasya garuḍābhedacintane iva ( viṣabādhā nivāraṇopayogini ), tataḥ – manaādau brahmā bhedacintana- to'pi na doṣa iti – aparāgho na bhaviteti, katicit — kecana abhiprayanti, anyatrā'nya- dṛṣṭiḥ- dharmyantare dharmyantaratādātmyā ropātmikā cintā, vyaktitaḥ jātitaśca- 'garuḍa evāha' miti garuḍa vyaktyabhedena ' garuḍa jātīyo'ham' iti tajjātīyābhedena ca avagāhyamānena, dvedhā- prakāradvayena, kim anadhigatā -- loke (anyatra vā ) na kim viditacarā, (jñātapūrvaiveti śrautasya dṛṣṭividhānasya na kimapi vaidhavyamiti) || 22||

mukhyaṃ brahmakamevetyavahitamanasāṃ brahmadṛṣṭiḥ kathaṃ syā-
dekatveneti kecinna hi balini vidhau śaṅkanīyo virodhaḥ |
svecchā pūrvadvicandrabhramavadiha bhidākalpanā māhuranye

bambhramyante yathā ca śrutiṣu bahuvidhaṃ brahma bhātīti bālāḥ || 23 ||
mukhyam – sarvavyavahāraprakāribhūtam brahma - brahmākhyaṃ tattvam, ekameveti nānābhūtaṃ na bhavati ityevam avahitamanasām — askhalitacetasām (niścitamatīnām ), brahmadṛṣṭi: - brahmasmin manaādau brahmatādātmyādhyavasitiḥ, kathaṃ syāt - kena vā prakāreṇa ghaṭeta ? ekatvena - dharmiṇa evaikatvena (dharmyantare dharmyantarāropākhyasya dṛṣṭiviśeṣāśrayaṇasya dharmyantarā- bhāvaniścayasamānakālikatvāyogakṛtāt virodhāt manovyavahāre'pi brahmaiva khalu dharmi | na

hi manobrahmaṇī dharmyantarabhūte iti ) iti kecit evaṃ viśiṣṭabrahmakyavādināmupari dṛṣṭividhya- sāṅgatyamukhena kecana pratyavasthātāro bhavanti cet ! (tatredaṃ samādhānam ) – vidhau – manaādau brahmadṛṣṭiniyogapare śrutivākye, balini-yauktika sarvavirodha nivārakatvarūpabalavattayā jāgrati sati, virodhaḥ - matiprabhavayuktivyāghātarūpo virodhaḥ, na hi śaṅkanīyaḥ śaṅkayā upasthāpyo naiva bhavitumaī:, (vidhiprāmāṇye dṛḍhādhyavasāyavatāṃ matikṛtaguṇa-doṣaiḥ kampanāsambhavāt ) iha dṛṣṭividhiviṣaye, ( āropye manaādau āropaṇīye brahmaṇi ca vastuto'bhinne'pi ) svecchā pūrvadvicandrabhramavat – svīyāpekṣā pūrvaka vyāpārarūpeṇa netragolakamadhye karāṅgulera vaṣṭambhena āpādite ekamātracandradharmikadvitvākhyaprakārāvagāhi me iva, midākalpanām – dvaidhībhāvāropa- sampādanām, anye āhu: - apare kecana ta vaikyasamarthanapravṛttāḥ ( prakāryaikyakṛtaṃ brahmatatva- vādamanavalabmyaiva mukhyata eva ekatatvavādasamarthana pravṛttāḥ advaitinaḥ ) āhuḥ — varṇayanti ( tasyodāharaṇametat - ) bālāḥ – anupacaritaśrutisvārasyāvirodhivedārthacintane akuśalāḥ brahma paraśvabhūtaṃ advitīyameva sat paraṃ brahma, śrutiṣu vedavākṣu bahuvidhaṃ brahma bhāti iti- jīva brahmātmakadvaividhyavattayā cidacidīśvarātmaka traividhyavattayā evamevamanyābhīrītibhiḥ nānātvena pratipādyamānatayā bhāsate iti yathā ca bambhramyante atiśayena bhrāntā bhavanti yathā tadvat – iti, (sarvamidaṃ varṇanaṃ śrutyarthacintane anaipuṇyakṛtam ; yat śrīte'rthe svakalpitārthena sākaṃ yauktikaṃ virodhamāśaṅkaya anyathīkaraṇapravartanam | vastutastu brahmapratīke brahmadṛṣṭividhānaṃ brahmasādṛśyādikṛtamabhedādhyavasānarūpaṃ loka- vedadṛṣṭabhramavyavahārānurodhyāropasadṛśaṃ phalāyopadiṣṭaṃ sunirvahamiti ) || 23 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṃgrahaḥ ||

ādityādimatyadhikaraṇe—udgīthādīnāṃ phalasādhanatayā utkṛṣṭatvāt, ādityādiṣu udgīthādidṛṣṭiḥ kāryā — ityāśaṅkaya,

āsīnādhikaraṇam || 4-1-5 ||
śārīrakanyāya kalāpasaṃgrahaḥ ||
āsīna ityeva viśeṣahāne : sarvapravṛttiṣvapi sā vidheyā |

syānnaivamaikāgryaviśeṣabhāvād dhyānaśruteścopaniṣeduṣaḥ sā || 5 ||

sā— mokṣopāyabhūtā bhagavaccintā, sarva pravṛttiṣvapi – āsana-sthiti-gamana - śayanādi- rūpā khilāvasthāsvapi, vidheyā — kartavyatvena śrutyādyabhimatā, āsīna ityeva viśeṣahāne:- upaviṣṭa eva kuryāditi āsīnatvāvasthā mātra samarpaka pramāṇānupalambhāt, (iti śaṅkane) naivaṃ syāt - evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ naivocitaṃ bhavet, aikāmyaviśeṣabhāvāt - cittaikāgratā rūpasya viśeṣasya tātparya : viṣayatāyāḥ avaśyaṃbhāvāt, (yataḥ ) dhyānaśruteḥ - 'nididhyāsitavyaḥ' iti brahmacintāyā, dhyānarūpatājñāpikāyāḥ śruteḥ satvam, tasmāt sā - mokṣopāyatvena vihitā brahmacintā - upaniṣeduṣaḥ – upaviṣṭasyaiva sataḥ kartavyatayopadiṣṭā ; ( na tu sthiti śayanādimataḥ ; aikāgryāsiddheḥ) || 5 ||

prāptaḥ san pākṣikāsīnatāyām – upaviśya karaṇapakṣe anuṣṭheyatayā prāptāyāṃ celā jinādyadhi- karaṇakāsīnatāyāyeva paryavasito bhavati ; ( na tu dhyānasāmānyasya upaviṣṭamātreṇaiva kartavyatve ) tasmāt uktāddhetoḥ, etat - mokṣādyarthatayā kriyamāṇaṃ dhyānam aprakampyendriyasya- cāpalya vidūrakaraṇavataḥ puruṣasya, sthiti - gati - śayaneṣu utthitāvasthāna-gamana - śayanarūpā su deha gatāsvava sthāsvapi, ghaṭeta – yujyetaivānuṣṭhātuma, ( evaṃ śaṅkane) ityaprāptam - idaṃ pramāṇato labdhaṃ na bhavati, avicchinnadhī santatiḥ vicchedāsaha kṛta cintanānumyūtiḥ, yatna nidrānvayini- sthityādau tadanuguṇayatnena śayane nidrayā ca vya te puruṣe, kathaṃ syāt – kayā vā rītyā sambhavet ? (naiva sambhāviteti dhyānamupaviṣṭasyaiva yuktam ) || 24||
cittaikāgryopapaśyai vidadhati niyati deśakālāsanā de :

sacchidraṃ tvanyadā'pi kṣamamiti satataṃ cintanaṃ saṃsmaranti | pratyaksaṃskārabhūmnā paranihita ghiyastā dadhīnyā dibuddhayā
juṣṭaṃ vyāsaktanītyā bhavati hi samaye yoginaḥ karma sarvam || 25 ||

cittaikāgrayopapaśyai— dhyeyaikapravaṇamanaskatāyāḥ saṃsiddhaye, deśa - kālāsssanādeḥ niyatim - viviktaṃ deśam, kālasandhyādirūpaṃ kālam, celājinādyāsanam - iti (uktānāmeṣāma- vaśyāśrayaṇīyatām ) niyamam vidadhati - śrutayaḥ tatsmartāro munayaśca dhyānārthamupadiśanti, anyadā – dhyānārthetaradaihikavyavahāravelāyām, sacchidramapi - vijātīyapratyayāntarasaṃbalitamapi, satataṃ cintanaṃ saṃsmaranti - sārvakālikaṃ śubhāśrayabhūtadhyāna' lambana saṃsmaraṇam, munayaḥ svīyāsu smṛtisu dhyānakāle prāvaṇyaupayikatayā kartavyaṃ jñāpayanti, pratyavasaṃskārabhūmnā evaṃ kramaśaḥ sampannena antarmukhapravṛttyā hitasaṃskārātiśayabalena, paranihita dhiyo yoginaḥ— pare brahmaṇi niveśitamateḥ sādhakasya, sarva karma – upāsanāṅgabhūtaṃ nitya naimittikādi karma (tathā) daihika jīvanopayogi kauṭumbikādi karma cetyakhilaṃ kriyā jātam, tādadhinyādibuddhayā juṣṭam- paramātmāhitapravṛttiprayojyatva tatprītiparyavasāyitvādiniścayena pūritaṃ vyāpṛtaṃ sat, samaye- vihite yobhye ca kāle, vyāsaktanītyā - kāryaviśeṣodyatasya tatraiva viśeṣeṇa saṃbaddha cetaso'pi laukikasya janasya yathā avarjanīyeṣu dehadhāraṇārtheṣu karmasu pravṛttiḥ putramitrakalatrādiṣu vyavahārapravṛttiśca bhavati tadvat bhavati hi ghaṭata eveti, (na dhyānaparāṇāṃ karmāsiddhi- prasaṅgaḥ) || 25 ||

karmopāstyaṅgabhūtaṃ yadiha nigaditaṃ tatpare pūrvapāde yogena brahmadṛṣṭiryadapi ca paramo dharma ityuktamātaiḥ |
tena prādhānya siddhā vitaradanuguṇaṃ tasya neyaṃ tathā ca
prāyaḥ prakrāntayoge paṭimalaghutayā kalpitaḥ kālayogaḥ || 26 ||
karma — yajña dāna- tapaḥ prabhṛtikaṃ kriyājātam,

upāstyaṅgabhūtam - upāsanapravṛttena puruṣeṇa anuṣṭhīyamānopāsanāṅgatayā kartavyamiti, iha - śārīrakaśāstra, tatpare – upāsanāṅga nirṇaya -

pradhāne, pūrvapāde – tṛtīyaturīye aṅgapāde, yat nigaditam - vicārya yannirṇītamasti, āptaiḥ yathāvasthitārthadarśana-yathārthavāditvobhayavadbhiḥ, yājñavalkyādibhiḥ, yogena brahmadṛṣṭiḥ- dhyānayoga- māśritya parabrahmasākṣātkaraṇam, paramo dharma:- paramo anuṣṭheyatayā śāstrasiddheṣu dharmeṣu madhye utkṛṣṭatamo dharma:, iti ca yadayuktam -' ijyācāradamā hiṃsādāna svādhyāyakarmaṇām | ayaṃ tu paramo dharmo yad yogenā''tmadarśanam ||' ityādinā yacca sphuṭaṃ pratipāditam tena nyāya pramāṇobhayena, prādhānyasiddhau - brahmopāsanasya avaśyānuṣṭheyatvarūpasya aṅgitārūpasya ca prādhānyasya niśvaye sati, itarat – nitya naimittikādirūpaṃ karmajātam, tasya anuguṇam – pradhānabhūtasyopāsanasyānukūlyaṃ virodhābhāvaśca yathā bhavet tathā, neyam – yathāīmanuṣṭheyaṃ bhavati, tathā ca karmaṇāmupāsanopa- kāraikaphalatve dṛḍhe sati, prakrāntayoge – yuñjānākhye yogārambhatvaritamanasi puruṣe, paṭimalaghu- tayā_pāṭavasyā'lpīyastvena hetunā ( paṭutvātiśayalābhāya ). kālayogaḥ– yogye kāle dhyānasyānuṣṭheyatā niyamaḥ, kalpitaḥ - śāstreṣu vihito vartate || 26 || vidyāṅga pūrvamuktaṃ kimiha punarasāvāsanādyaṅga cintā

dṛṣṭārthāṃśa vibhajya prathayitumapi na prāk śamādyaṅgavādāt |
satyaṃ dhyānākhyadhārā vahanamatidaśā tvanvahaṃ sādhanīyā

cittaikāgryeṇa sarvaprayatanavirahe syāditīdaṃ prakāśyam || 27 ||

vidyāṅgam — pradhānabhūtāyāḥ vidyāyā upakārakaṃ sarvam pūrvamuktam - tṛtīyacaturthe nirūpitameveti, iha — turīyādyapāde atra, asau āsanādyaṅgacintā – kriyamāṇā āsanādi - yogāṅgānāṃ vicāraṇā, punaḥ kim - kimartha punaḥ kriyate ? kṛtakaratvāpatteḥ, (iti praśna) dṛṣṭāthāṃśaṃ vibhajya pradhānaṃ prati dṛṣṭādṛṣṭopakārakeṣu dṛṣṭopakāram aṅgaṃ pṛthakkṛtya, prathayitumapi pradarśayitum (atra āsanāṅganirūpaṇam ) ityapi, na-na vivektuṃ śakyam, prāk - pūrvameva (tṛtīyaturīye eva), śamādyaṅgavādāt - yogaṃ prati dṛṣṭopakārakāṇāṃ śama- damādīnāṃ nirūpitatvena tadanaucityāpātāt (yataḥ tadatraiva nirūpyaṃ bhavet ) satyam - pṛṣṭametadyuktameva,

anvahaṃ cittaikāyeṇa sādhanīyā – āprayāṇāt yāvadāyuṣam dhyeyaikapravaṇamanaskatayā aharahara- bhyāsenādheyātiśayā dhyānākhyadhārā | mativahanadaśā tu— dhyānapadavācyadhīsantānasya | vicchinna- dhārātvāvasthitiḥ param, sarva prayatana virahe - dhyānetara viṣayaka nikhilaprayatnadūrīkaraṇe eva syāt- sidhyet, itīdam — ityetadevātyāvaśyakam prakāśyam – sādhakān prati sphuṭīkāryamabhimataṃ bhagavataḥ sūtrakārasyeti || 27 ||

ekasminneva dine anuṣṭheyatvam, na saṃsiddhayati - śāstrataḥ siddhaṃ naiva bhavati yāvadāyurityeva śabdāt –'sa khalvevaṃ vartayan yāvadāyuṣam ' ityevamākārādeva yāvadāyupapadaghaṭitāt asandigdhā- rthakāt śrutivākyāt, tasya – āmaraṇaṃ pratyahakartavyatvasya vidhitaśca – spaṣṭameva vidhānāt, ( ekava saramātrakaraṇe na śāstra | nirvāha siddhiḥ ; kintu pratidinam āmaraṇaṃ karaṇe eveti ) | 6 |

0-

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||
ekasminneva ghane yadi bhavati paradhyānarūpā tu bhakti-

stadviśrānto vidhi: syādupari tu viphalā brahmacintetyayuktam |
chāndogye yāvadāyuḥ sucaritamuditaṃ brahmalokāptiheto-
staddhi dhyānasya vṛddhyai tadapi ca vihitaṃ kutracit prāyaṇāntam || 28 ||

ekasmin eva ghastre – ekasminnahanyeva, paradhyānarūpā tu bhaktiḥ - paramātmano dhyāna- padābhidheyatvā va mokṣopāyabhūtā bhaktiḥ, yadi bhavati - labdhasvarūpā bhavedyadi vidhiḥ- mokṣasādhanatayā brahmavedanavidhi: ( śrutyādigataḥ), tadviśrāntaḥ syāt – eka dinamātra kartavyatā- jñāpanaparyavasita eva bhavet upari tu-śāstraparyavasāna siddhārthānuṣṭhānānantaraṃ tu (āyuśśeṣe), brahmacintā—brahmopāsanā. vikalā-prayojanaśūnyaiva ityayuktam — evaṃ śaṅkanamanucitam, chāndogye – chāndogyaśrutau brahmalokāptihetoḥ – brahmaprāptirūpaṃ phalaṃ prati hetutayā, sucaritam- sukṛtapadā midheya satkarmānuṣṭhānam yāvadāyuḥ uditam - āmaraṇameva vihitam ( uktam ), ( ' evaṃ vartayan yāvadāyuṣaṃ brahmalokamabhisampadyate ' iti ) tat — yāvadāyuṣaṃ pratyahaṃ satkarmānuṣṭhānam, dhyānasya vṛddhayai hi— dhyāne atyantavaiśadyalābhāya khalu, tadapi - dhyānameva ca prāyaṇāntam - dehādutkramaṇaparyantam, kutracit - śrutiviśeṣe, vihitam - ' prāyaṇāntamoṅkāramabhidhyāyīta ' iti spaṣṭameva anuṣṭheyatayā coditam || 28 ||

-0-

nayasaṃgatimālikā ||

āvṛttā'pyupasaṃhāryā svecchayetyeṣa saṃśayaḥ | āprayāṇādhikaraṇe'podyate śrutidarśanāt || 180 ||
iti āprayāṇādhikaraṇam || 6 ||

purāṇavacanena spaṣṭīkṛtam, tataḥ tasmāt jñāpakāt pāpāśleṣapraṇāśaśrutirapi — brahmavidaḥ puruṣasya uttara-pūrvākhyānā maghānāṃ lepābhāva-praṇāśabodhinī ' evaṃvidi pāpaṃ karma na śliṣyate evaṃ hāsya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante ' ityādirūpā vedavāṇyapi, paravidvaibhavoktiḥ syāt- brahmavidvatamāhātmyātiśayamātrabodhana tātparyapravṛttā bhavitumarhati ; na tu yathāśrutārthaparā bhavet, ( iti śaṅkane) maivam — anayā rītyā śaṅkanaṃ naiva sādhu, nābhuktamityādyapi ' nā'bhuktaṃ kṣīyate ' iti smṛtivacanamapi, karmaṇaḥ sukṛtaduṣkṛnākhyasya karmaṇaḥ, phalajanane dām- apandehena strīya phalotpādanadhrauvyam, āha— spaṣṭīkaroti, iha - śrutibacasi, parabhajanam - paramātmopāsanam, karmaśāntyai - apavargavirodhināṃ sarveṣāṃ ca karmaṇāṃ phalānutpādakatvāpādanāya, prāyaścittakrameṇa – anyānyairvividhaiḥ śāstraiḥ vividhānāṃ pāpānāṃ viṣaye prāyaścittāni yathā tathaiva, coditam — vihitamasti (iti mantavyam ) || 29 | niṣkṛtyātmatvamasyoditaduritasamunmūlanasyopapannaṃ

nā'tra svargādinītiḥ pratihativigame dhīvikāsaḥ svato hi | pāpa/lepaḥ pramādoditasahanamiti sthāpyamājñānuvṛtyai
no cenna syādgatirnā'virata iti giro yogināṃ ca pravṛtteḥ || 30 ||

asya – paramātmopāsanasya, uditaduritasamunmūlanasya – sañjātasarvapāpanirmūlana kāriṇaḥ, upapannam – (tenaiva hetunā) yujyamānaṃ bhavati, niṣkṛtyātmatvam – prāyaścittarūpatvam, hi- yataḥ, pratihativigame—pratibandhakanirāse sati, svato dhīvikāsaḥ cetanasvabhāvāyatta eva labhyate asaṅkucitadharmabhūtajñānatvāvasthā (brahmānubhūtyanuguṇā ) ; tataḥ, atra apavargārthaparamātmo- pāsanasthaḻe, svargādinītiḥ na kāmyabhūtasya jyotiṣṭoma | deḥ svargākhyasukhaviśeṣa hetutvamiva apavargahetutvam itīyaṃ rītiḥ naiva vartate, pāpālepaḥ – upāsana niṣpattipaścādbhāvināṃ pāpānāṃ bhajanaśīle lepābhāvo yaḥ śrutigaditaḥ saḥ, ājñānuvṛtyai— upāsana siddhyanantaramapi upāsakasya yāvadāyuṣam ājñānujñā kaiṅkaryanairantarya jñāpana siddhyartham, pramādoditasahana miti sthāpyam- pramādataḥ - avaśāt prāptānāṃ pāpānāmeva paramātmanā kriyamāṇaṃ marṣaṇameveti (na svacchandacārasyā- vakāśa: iti) samarthanīyama, no cet — pramādataḥ āpatitānītyetadvihāya asaṅkocena sarvapāpā- ślaṣaparatvaṃ śrutisaṃmataṃ manyeta cet !, 'nā'virata' iti giraḥ - ' nā'virato duścaritānnā'śānto nā'samāhitaḥ | nāśāntamānaso vā'pi prajñānenainamāpnuyāt ||' ityākārāyāḥ śrutivācaḥ, yogināṃ pravṛtteśca mahāyogināṃ janakakekayādīnāṃ vihitānuṣṭhānaniṣiddhavarjanapravaṇāyāḥ pravṛtteśca śrutipurāṇādisiddhāyāḥ, gatiḥ na syāt iti – aucityena nirvāho na bhaviteti |

(siddhopāsanānāṃ vidhi - niṣedhollaṅghanameva śrutimataṃ yadi, tadā janaka ke kayādayaḥ kuto vā śāstra maryādānusāriṇaḥ śāstre śrutā bhaveyuḥ ? iti ) || 30||
bhaśleṣaḥ karmaśakteranudaya udaye tannivṛttirvināśaḥ
śaktiḥ sā ca praṇetustaducitaphalakṛnnigrahānugrahātmā |
prāyaścittaṃ nimitte kathamanudita ityatra naivā'nuyojyaṃ
nā'dharmastasya tat syāditi khalu hṛdayaṃ grāhyamaśleṣavācaḥ || 31 ||
aśleṣaḥ - ' evaṃvidi pāpaṃ karma na śliṣyate' ityuktaḥ viduṣi pāpmanāmaśleṣaḥ, karmaśakteḥ anudayaḥ kṛteṣvapi pāparūpeṣu karmasu jñānavirodhinyāḥ śakteḥ tatrā'nutpattireva, vināśaḥ – evaṃ hāsya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante ' ityuktaḥ viduṣaḥ sarvapāpavināśo nāma, udaye tannivṛttiḥ karmasu jñānavirodhinyāḥ śakteḥ utpattau satyāṃ, tasyāḥ śakteḥ nivāraṇam, sā ca śaktiḥ - asaṅkucitajñānatvāvasthāvirodhinī ca śakti: ( karmagatā ), taducitaphalakṛt- tattādṛśakarmaṇāṃ kartustasya tasya cetanasya yathāyogyaṃ phalajanikā, praṇetuḥ nigrahānugrahātmā - sarvaniyantuḥ parameśvarasya tattajjīvaviṣayaka nigrahānugrahasaṅkalpātmakaiva nānyā apūrvādiśabda- vācyā, prāyaścittam – 'prāyaścitta' padena śāstroditaṃ karma nimitte anudite prāyaścitta- prayojake nimitte anutpanne eva sati katham — kena vā prakāreṇa svarūpaṃ labhate ? ; (paścāttāpa- tatprayuktakarmaviśeṣādyācaraṇasya pūrvamaprasakteḥ) iti atra naivā'nuyojyam - ityevam uttarādhā śleṣa --

akṛtatvāt taṃ prati upāsanasya prāyaścittatvāyoga

viṣaye naivā''kṣepyam ' ( uttarasya aghasya iti), aśleṣavācaḥ – uttarāgha| śleṣokteḥ

hṛdayam — tātparyam, tat tasya nā'dharma iti

khalu grāhyam - prāmādikaṃ pāpānuṣṭhānam, upāsakasya pāparūpaṃ na bhavatītyevameva khalu grahītavyam

( śrutyarthacintakeneti ) || 31 ||
dhīpūrva tūttarādhaṃ na sṛjati paravit sarvadā sāvadhāno
jātaṃ naimittikaiśca kṣipati samucitairāśu bhuñjīta vā tat |
vṛtrādau dṛṣṭametanniyativibhavataścedigajā disiddhi-
yuktā vā'nyadā vā muniriha manute brahmaniṣṭhasya muktim || 32 ||
paravit— paramātmopāsananairantaryavān puruṣaḥ, dhīpūrvam – buddhipūrvakatayā, uttarādham - upāsanapaścādbhāvinaṃ pāpmānam, na tu sṛjati niṣiddhānuṣṭhānādibhirnaiva sampādayitumīhate, ( yataḥ ) sarvadā sāvadhānaḥ—upāsanapravṛttimārabhya yāvadāyuṣam kṣaṇamapi avihāya apramatta eva

jīvati, (yastu avadhānarahitaḥ saḥ) jātam — pramādāt utpannamuttarādham samucitaiḥ- tattatpāpasvabhāvasvarūpānuguṇaiḥ, naimittikaiśca vihitaiḥ prāyaścittaireva pariṣadājñaptaiḥ kṣamāyācana- śaraṇavaraṇādibhirvā, āśu kṣipati - śarīrapātāvadhike kāla eva śīghraṃ vināśayati, tat - tādṛśaṃ pāpam, āśu bhuñjīta vā ihaiva janmāntarānanuvṛttyanuguṇaṃ bhogena kṣapayedeva vā, etat — paramātmacintanaikarasasya cetanasya sambhavināṃ pāpānāmuktarītyā nirharaṇam, vṛtrādau dṛṣṭam – vṛtrātmanā labdhajanmani brahmajñe'pi cetanaviśeṣe devara jena vadhyatvaprāptipūrvakam anupada- meva muktisampanne purāṇādikathayā jñātam, niyativibhavataḥ - vidhimahimavaśataḥ, cedirājādi- siddhiḥ – śiśupālādirūpamāpannasya bhagavadbhaktasya cetana viśeṣasya madhye vairapūrvakaṃ bhagavadavā ptiḥ yuktā nyāyyaiva śrūyate muniḥ sūtrakṛt bādarāyaṇaḥ, iha — śārīrake, brahmaniṣṭhasya - mumukṣoḥ brahmacintakasya, adya vā anyadā vā etajjanmani vā janmāntare vā, muktim- avarjanīyamapavargam manute - śāstrasaṃmatatayā svābhipretamāviṣkaroti || 32 ||

itarādhikaraṇam || 4-1-8||
śārīrakanyāya kalāpasaṅgrahaḥ ||
aśleṣa - nāśau hitakarmaṇāṃ tu naiva prasaktau sukhalābha hetoḥ |
tannaiva mokṣapratibandhakatvasāmyānmumukṣoḥ sphuṭataḥ prasaktau || 8 ||
hitakarmaṇāṃ tu sukṛtarūpāṇāṃ hitātmakānāṃ karmaṇāṃ tu (puṇyakarmaṇāṃ punaḥ ), aśleṣa - nāśau - pāpānāmiva lepābhāva kṣayau, naiva prasaktau – sarvathā na sambhāvitau, sukhalābhahetoḥ - puṇyātmakaiḥ karmabhiḥ iṣṭarūpasukhasya lābhāt nimittāt ( upāsakasya mumukṣorapi cetanasya iṣṭaphala - jananārthamapekṣaṇīyatvāt ), iti śaṅkane, tannaiva śaṅkita yat tat naivocitam mokṣaprati- bandhakatvasāmyāt muktivighaṭakatāyāḥ pāpeṣviva puṇyeṣvapi karmasu samānatvāt mumukṣoḥ svātmānaṃ saṃsāranigaḍānmocayitumiccho: puruṣasya, sphuṭataḥ - asandehenaiva prasaktau – puṇya- karma gāmapyaśleṣanāśau avaśyamapekṣaṇīyau sambhāvitāveveti || 8 ||

tallakṣmasiddheḥ – pāpalakṣaṇasya iṣṭatamabhagavatprāptītara svargādikṣudrasukharūpāniṣṭalambhakatvasya yogāt |

asya - puṇyasyā'pi pāpmatā - pāparūpatā, na sukṛtamiti - 'na sukṛtaṃ na duṣkṛtaṃ sarve pāpmāno'to nivartante' iti śrūyate ca śrutāveva sphuṭamullikhyate'pi || 33 ||

puṇyaṃ vidyānukūlaṃ yadiha saphalatā tasya vidyāpradatvāt tasyāṃ yannopayuktaṃ tadapi duritavadbandhakatvena vāryam | kārāgāroparuddhe nigalayugalataḥ sārvabhaumasya bhṛtye

haimaṃ kārṣṇāyasaṃ ca prasadanasamaye bhañjanīyaṃ sahaiva || 34 ||

iha – puṇyapadābhidheyeṣu karmasu madhye yat vidyānukūlaṃ puṇyam - apavargasādhanabhūta- vidyāsiddhayaupayikaṃ yat nitya naimittikākhyaṃ puṇyaṃ karmā'sti tasya tādṛśasya vidhicoditasya puṇyasya karmaṇaḥ, vidyāpradatvāt - nirantarabhagavadupāsanātmaka vidyāyāḥ sacvaśuddhayāpādanadvārā sampādakatvāt iha saphalatā - vidyānuṣṭha| nopayogiśarīrāvacchedenaiva saprayojanatā ( kāryakaratvaṃ ) siddhaiva, yat – yādṛśaṃ puṇyam tasyām - bhagavadupāsanarūpāyāṃ vidyāyām, upakārakatvarūpopayogarāhityenā'vasthitam, tadapi - tathāvidhaṃ puṇyaṃ karmā'pi saṃsāravivṛddhihetutvena nimittena, duritavat - pāpasāmyena (duritaṃ yathā tathaiva), vāryam - tyājyam (dūrīkaraṇīyameva) sārvabhaumasya bhṛtye – samrājaḥ sevake kasmiṃścana puruṣe, nigala- yugalata: — sauvarṇakāṣrṇāyasarūpeṇa śṛṅkhalādvayena, kārāgāroparuddhe corādyaparādhibandhana gehe bandhite sati, (sāparādhatvāt kāra gṛhe niyantrite sati ) prasadanasamaye - śikṣākālāvadhi - pūrtyā tasmin rājño vimocanārthānugrahapravṛttivelāyām, haimaṃ kārṣṇāyasaṃ ca – sauvarṇa lauhātmaka- mubhayavidhamapi bandhakavastu, sahaiva bhañjanīyam - yugapadeva mocanā hai khalu ; na tu sauvarṇa- nigalasya sauvarṇatvakṛtaṃ svīkāryatvamiti (evameva mokṣakāle, puṇya-pāpayorubhayorapi bhavabandhahera vo:- sahaiva vidhūnanamavarjyam ) || 34 ||

nopayuktam -
bandhakatvena -

kāmyaṃ necched virakto yadi kila kurute rāgayogāt phalet ta-
nā'dhīpūrva ca kāmyaṃ kimapi na ca diśed bandhamajñātadharmaḥ |
vidyārthailipyate'taḥ kimitaraditi cennā'dhikaiḥ sāntarāyai-
ranyāthairmoha jaiḥ kvāpyanutapanavato bandhujaiśvā'stvalepaḥ || 35 ||

viraktaḥ– mumukṣuḥ san sāṃsārikeṣu phaleṣvanicchuḥ puruṣaḥ, kāmyam – kāmyapadāmidheyam aihikaphalahetubhūtaṃ sukṛtam, na icchet — kartuṃ naiva īheta, (naiva īheta yadi sutarāṃ na kuryāt ;

icchāyā evānutpattau kimu vaktavyaṃ tatkaraṇaviṣaye), rāgayogāt – vāsanādhīnāt ākasmikā- dicchā saṃbandhāt, (acintitopanatā dicchā viśeṣāt ), yadi kila kurute - kadācit kāmyamapi puṇyaṃ karma anutiṣṭhedeva cet ! tat phalet tādṛśaṃ sukṛtaṃ jīvitakāle eva uddeśyabhūtaṃ phalaṃ datvaiva virataṃ bhavet ; na āprayāṇamavaśiṣyeta ; yasya (mokṣāpratibandhāya ) alepaḥ vidheyaḥ syāt, adhīpūrvaṃ ca – abuddhipūrvakamapi, kāmyam – kāmyapadābhidheyaṃ sukṛtam, na kimapi - naitra yatkicit sambhavi, ( kāmyatvasya jñānapūrvakecchā viṣayatvātmakatayā, tatra adhīpūrvakatāyāḥ asaṅgatatvāt ), ajñātadharmaḥ – mumukṣorupāsakasya avidita eva san tatratyaḥ sukṛtaviśeṣaḥ, bandhaṃ na ca diśet — mokṣapratidvandvinaṃ saṃsāraṃ naiva asmai prayacchet, (mokṣavirodhi sāṃsārika phalaṃ mumukṣave asmai dattvā na enaṃ mokṣāyā'ghaṭamānaṃ kuryāt ) vidyārthaiḥ – vidyāṅgatayā'nuṣṭhitaiḥ sukṛtaiḥ punaḥ, lipyate — lepaṃ bhajate eva; ( no cet tānyapi naiva vidyāpradāyīni bhaveyuḥ ; hanta kimatopyadhikaṃ hānikaram ? ) ataḥ kimitarat ? iti cet — ( uktavidhānāṃ sarveṣāmapi sukṛtānām alepavacanasyānupayuktatve siddhe) etebhyo'nyat kiṃjātīyaṃ vā sukṛtam (asya mokṣama- pratibandhum ) asaṃśleṣabhāg vaktavyamavaśiṣyate ? na kimapīti; aśleṣavacanamanupayogyeveti śaṅkane na - anucitaṃ caitat śaṅkanam, adhikaiḥ – vidyā niṣpattaye yāvadapekṣitaṃ tato'tiriktaiḥ sāntarāyaiḥ – vidyārthamevānuṣṭhitairapi vighnapratihatatayā akṛtvā vidyopakāraṃ tathaivāvasthitaiḥ anyārtheḥ mohajaiḥ vastuto vidyetara phalajanakatva svabhāvairapi vidyārthīnīmānīti mohena (bhrāntyā ) saṃjātaiḥ, (tathā) bandhujaiśva – muktasya svabandhoḥ dehāpakramaṇakāle vidhūtasukṛtatayā tatpriyaṃ jñātibhūtaṃ svaṃ pratyāgatairvā sukṛtaiḥ, kvāpyanutapanavataḥ yādṛgvidhe'pi mokṣapratibandhake sukṛte prāptānutāpasya mumukṣoḥ, alepaḥ astu sambhāvitasya lepasyā'bhāvaḥ sūtrakṛtā nirṇīto bhavatu || ( iti lipyamānasukṛtābhāvakṛtaṃ alepavacanānaucityaṃ parihṛtaṃ bhavatītyāśayaḥ) || 35 ||

||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||
agretarādhikaraṇe puṇyānantye'pi pūrvavat |
nāśā śleṣā vimau prāmādika (duṣkarma) satkarmagocarau || 182 ||

iti itarādhikaraṇam || 8||

anārabdhakāryādhikaraṇam || 4-1-9|| śārīrakanyāyakalā pasaṃgrahaḥ ||
sarvasya vaiśeṣikaśabdahānera lepa- nāśau bhavato hi ! tasya |
nāssrabdhakāryasya hi ! naiva yasmād 'yāvanna mokṣye 'ti viśeṣaśabdaḥ || 9 ||

tasya sukṛtaduṣkṛtapadābhidheyasyā'rthasya mumukṣūpāsaka sambandhinaḥ sarvasya – ārabdhā- nārabdhakāryobhayavidhasyāpi, aśleṣa-nāśau bhavatau hi uttarasyā'lepaḥ, pūrvasya kṣayaḥ ityeta- dhvam, vaiśeṣikaśabdahāne :- anārabdhakāryasukṛta- duṣkṛtarūpa viśiṣṭārthapratyāyakaśabdābhāvāt, ( iti śaṅkane) na-na yuktametacchaṅkanam yasmāt - yato hetoḥ, 'yāvanna mokṣye' ti viśeṣaśabda: – 'yāvanna vimokṣye atha sampatsye' iti dehavimokṣāvadhikatvarūpaviśeṣa pratyāyakaḥ -śabdaḥ śrutau sphuṭaṃ śrūyata eva ataḥ, ārabdhakāryasya naiva – phalapradānapravṛttasya sukṛta- duṣkṛtākhyasya karmaṇaḥ naiva nāśaḥ ; anyathā avaśiṣṭe kāle dehadhāraṇaprayojakābhāvaprasaṅgāt ) || 9 ||

karmākhyaviśeṣagamakaḥ śabdaḥ, na adhītaḥ na śrutau paṭhitaḥ, 'sarve pāpmāna' ityapyabhi- hitam - pratyuta ārabdhānārabdhobhaya karmanivṛttibodhakaṃ 'sarve pāpmānaḥ' iti sarvaśabdaghaṭitameva vākyam śrutau paṭhitam iti cet na evaṃ śaṅkanamasādhu, upalambhādibādhāt - śakti - syā'rthasya pratyakṣādibhirvirodhāt, (niṣpannopāsano'pi hi puruṣaḥ, anantaraṃ yāvadāyu dehadhāraṇaṃ karoti, īpsita - jihā sitayoḥ pravṛtti nivṛttī labhate | iti pratyakṣeṇā'numānena ca virodhāt, sarvakarmakṣayo naiva nirṇayaḥ; karmārabdhaṃ hi śarīraṃ sarvakarmāpagame kathaṃ vā dhārya syāt iti ) sadvidyā - ( chāndogyaṣaṣṭhaprapāṭhakātmaka) sadvidyāprakaraṇam tasya tāvacciramiti vadati ca - tasya tāvadeva ciraṃ yāvanna vimokṣye' iti śrutikhaṇḍena śarīrapāta paryantakālavilambaṃ spaṣṭameva kaṇṭhato vaktayeva ca, āgamajñāḥ utsanna - paṭhita śākhāviśeṣadarśinaḥ smartāro munayaḥ, asmaraṃśca- yāvaccharīrapātaṃ śubhāśubhakarmānuvṛtti svīyasmṛtyādiṣu sphuṭīcakruḥ, jīvanmuktyādiśabdo'pi - śarīraṃ dhārayata eva mokṣaprātyādipratipattikaraḥ 'amṛta iha bhavati, atra brahma samaśnute' ityādikaḥ śrutyādigataḥ śabdo'pi upacaraṇaparaḥ- bhāvi sarvakarmakṣaya siddhavatkārāvalambī viśadatamabrahmā- parokṣyāvalambī vā san aupacārikaḥ ; ( na tu karmārabdhadehavatyapi puruṣe mokṣaśabdārthasya paripūrṇa - tayā siddhiparaḥ jīvatva - muktatvayoḥ karmasambandha-niravaśeṣakarmakṣayarūpayoḥ yaugapadyavyāghātāt ) tena – upacaritāmṛtatvādeḥ ihaiva siddhavatkāravyapadeśena śrutyādigatena mandāḥ – acaturāḥ ( mugdhā iti vā ), mohitāḥ sañja (tabhramāḥ santaḥ jīvanmuktivādamaya kārṣuḥ || 36 || (468) rogādyārambhakāṇāṃ praśamanavidhayaḥ santi dānārcanādyāḥ

!

pratyetuḥ svāvatārādyapi vibhurapunarjanmatāmadhyagāyat |
tasmādārandhakārye phalaniyativaco durghaṭaṃ bhāti maivaṃ

prāyaścittojjhitānāṃ nanu phalaniyatiḥ sūtrakārādyabhīṣṭā || 37 ||
rogādyārambhakāṇām —-yādhi- dāridryā diduḥkhabhogādhāya kāna

prāktanapāpānām, praśamana vidhayaḥ smṛtyādicoditā: nivāraṇopāyāḥ santi vṛddhavyavahārādiparicitā vidyanta eva, (ke te ?) dānārcanādyāḥ – gomūhiraṇyadhānyavastrādirūpāṇāmarthānāṃ śrotriyebhyo dānam, (tathā) (rogādinivārakatayā ) bhagavato viśiṣṭamarcanādikam auṣadhasevanam, samidājyacarcādibhiḥ homaviśeṣa:, (ityādīni viśiṣṭāni karmāṇi prārabdhasyā'pi karmaṇo nāśakānyabhimatāni), vibhuḥ - bhagavān gītācāryaḥ, svāvatārādi pratyeturapi - bhagavataḥ puruṣottamasya svasya avatārādi- divyacāritracintanaparasyā'pi puruṣasya, apunarjanmatāmapi agāyat - punarjanma hityamapi gītabān;

janma karma ca me divyaṃ .... punarjanma naiti' ityanena vacanena tasmāt – uktā detasmāddhetoḥ, ārabdhakārye phalaniyativacaḥ kāryārambhodyukte karmaṇi niyamenānubhāvya phalatvacanam (sūtroktam ), durghaṭaṃ bhāti—asaṅgatamityeva jñāyate, (iti śaṅkane) maivam — evaṃ mā śaṅkayatām, prāyaścitto- jjhitānām - akṛtaprāyaścittānāṃ (prāyaścittairanā śitānām ) prārabdhakarmaṇām, phalaniyatiḥ- bhaugaika pariharaṇīyatā, sūtrakārādyabhīṣṭā nanu – śārīrakasūtrakārādibhiḥ abhyupagatā khalu ||37||

na– evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ tu naiva sādhu, etattu kārya khalu – etat agnihotrādikarma param anuṣṭheyameva hi, yajñaśabdāt - ' vividiṣanti yajñena ' iti yajñādeḥ vedanasādhanatayā śabdato vihitatvāt, vidyāsamutpādanaśaktitaśca – agnihotrādiṣvāśramakarmasu antarāyādinibarhaṇadvārā vidyā svarūpa- niṣpattyā vāyakaśaktisattvācca || 10 ||

-0-

sarvāpakṣeti – 'sarvāpekṣā ca yajñādiśruteraśvavat' ityadhikaraṇe, parabhajane - paramātmopāsanaṃ prati, karmaṇām aṅgabhāvam - agnihotrādirūpāṇāṃ nitya naimittika karmaṇāmupakārakatvākhyaṃ śeṣatvam, avocat nanu - sūtrakāraḥ śārīrake uktavān khalu, ( evaṃ sthite ) bhūyaḥ- punarapi, agnihotretyapi - 'agnihotrādi tu tatkāryāyaiva taddarśanāt' ityasmin prakṛtādhi- karaṇe'pi tatkarmaṇāṃ parabhajanaṃ pratyaṅgabhāvameva, vakti iti sūtrakāro bravīti ityato hato:, atra ekam - sarvāpekṣā'gnihotrādyadhikaraṇayoranyatarat, mudhā - vyartham ; (ekenaiva iṣṭārtha- sādhanāt ) iti cet na - evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na sādhu, prāk pūrvasmin sarvāpekṣādhikaraṇe, śaṅkitā- śaṅkāspadībhūtā vidyāyā nairapekṣyāt tadaparikaratā - upāsanarūpāyāḥ vidyāyā: itara sāpekṣatvaṃ vinā svasādhya mokṣasādhakatvasvābhāvyāt karmāṅgakatvābhāvāpattiḥ, apāstā- nirākṛtā, (upāsanasya karmasāpekṣatvasvā bhāvyasamarthanataḥ), iha punaḥ - prastute agnihotrā- dyadhikaraṇe tu śleṣābhāvaprasaktā tu — ārabdhopāsane viduṣi karmaṇāṃ puṇyātmakānāṃ kriya- māṇānāmapyalevasvābhāvyāyattā aparikarataiva nirākriyate iti — śaṅkāvaiṣamyena hetunā, tannītivaiṣamya siddhaḥ - vidyāyāḥ karmāṅgakatvābhāvanirākaraṇaparādhikaraṇa niṣpādyanyāyayoḥ mitho bailakṣaṇyasiddheḥ (na paunaruktatyakṛtā vaiyarthyaśaṅketi ) || 39 ||

0-

itarakṣapaṇādhikaraṇam || 11 ||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ ||

tātparyāntarayuktatvāt (asmāccharīrādityasya prākṛtāt aihalaukikā dehā dityevā'bhiprāyāt ) bhūyodehasmṛteśva – yogānuṣṭhānapravṛttānāmapi nānādeha sambandhamya prārabdhaphalabhogaupayikatāyāḥ itihāsapurāṇa dismṛtisiddhatvāt antimavapuṣi - prārabdhakarmaṇāṃ paripūrṇasamāptera baccheda kī- bhūte caramaśarīre, dṛḍhodyatsamādhestu — acālanīya prādurbhāvāpannabrahmānubhavarūpapūrṇa samādhimata eka viduṣaḥ, mokṣaḥsaṃsāra nigalā dvimokṣaḥ siddho bhavatīti || 40 ||

samānā:- avaiṣamyavantaḥ, vividham- mithovilakṣaṇanānāprakārama, anādikarma ca-ani- damādyasvarūpaṃ puṇya-pāpākhyaṃ jīvasambandhi karmā'pi, iha - jīvajāte, tulyam - samānatayā vartanaśīlam, bhagavati-muktivitaraṇaśīle mukunde'pi paramapuruṣe, vaiṣamyādiḥ - jīveṣu pakṣa- pātapravṛttiḥ nairdhṛṇyam evamādikaḥ, doṣaśca - doṣo'pi na bhavati - naiva vidyate, anyathā- bhagavati doṣābhyupagame, śāstrabhaṅgaḥ - bhagavato nirasta nikhila doṣatvābhidhāyināṃ śāstrāṇāṃ bādhi- tārthakatā syāt, ataḥ — asmāddhetoḥ, muktau - jīvānāṃ mokṣadānaviṣaye vilambaprabhṛti- kasmaicana kālavilambamāśritya kasmaicana śīghraṃ ca (mokṣadānam ) iti vaiṣamyānusaraṇam, na ghaṭatena saṅgataṃ bhavati, iti anuyogaḥ - īdṛśa ākṣepakaraḥ praśnaḥ, alpasāraḥ - atyantaṃ durbala, hi yataḥ, karmapravāhe - anāditaḥ santanyamāne karmajāle, citre - atyāścaryā - dhāya ke vibhinna vilakṣaṇasvarūpasvabhāve ca niśvite sati, phalasamayabhidā-tattatkarmaparipākāya- taphalajanakakālabhedasya avarjanīyatā, sarvatantraiḥ - nikhiladarśa nikairapi, āśritā- aṅgīkṛtaiveti || 42 |

-0-

śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṃgrahaḥ ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||

ā ( vartyā) vṛttā brahmavidyā tvasakṛdahamiti syācca na syāt pratīke karmā'rkādidṛṣṭiḥ praṇidhirapi bhavet sāsanaḥ pratyahaṃ ca | pāpe puṇye ca nāśādikamatha tu tayorbhogyatā''rabdha kārye
kāryatvaṃ svāīvṛtteriti kathitamihārabdhaśāntau ca mokṣaḥ || 43 ||

brahmavidyā - brahmopāsanātmikā aupaniṣadī vidyā, asakṛt āvṛttā tu — anekavāra- manuṣṭhitaiva satī mokṣopāyatvena śrutividheyatāṃ labhate ; na tu sakṛdanuṣṭhitā, ahamiti syācca- brahmopāsanarūpā vidyā upāsye brahmaṇi viṣaye ahantvagrahavatyeva ( ahaṃ brahmāsmītyeva ) bhavet ; na tu upāsakādanyatvena (tasya ' na sa veda akṛtsno hyeṣa : ' iti anupāsanarūpatvokteḥ) pratīke na syāt phalaviśeṣāptaye upāsyatvenā'vagate mano-nāmādau pratīkabhūte viṣaye, ' ahaṃ manaḥ ahaṃ nāma' iti ahantvenopāsanaṃ naiva yuktaṃ bhavet, karmāṅge arkādidṛṣṭiḥ karmāṅgabhūte udgīthādau nikṛṣṭe'rtha eva utkṛṣṭādityādidṛṣṭiḥ kāryā bhavati ; ( na tu ādityādau udgīthādi- dṛṣṭiḥ ; tasya anyāyyatvāt ), praṇidhirapi — dhyānarūpo mokṣopāyo'pi, sāsanaḥ bhavet — yogāṅgabhūtapadmādyāsanavāneva bhavitumarhati ; ( na tu gamana- dhāvana - śayanādyavasthāvān ), pratyahaṃ ca bhavet praṇidhiḥ—yāvadāyuṣaṃ pratidinamapyanuṣṭheyo bhavet dhyānākhya upāyaḥ ; na tvekasminneva dine, pāpe puṇye ca duṣkṛte sukṛte ca jīvasambandhini, nāśādikam - praṇāśa: aśleṣaḥ ityetadu- bhayam, atha tu - anantarādhikaraṇe param tayoḥ - puṇya pāpayorubhayorapi, ārabdhakārye - phalapradānodyuktakarmavati puruṣe, bhogyatā - bhogenaiva kṣapaṇīyatā; (anārabdhakārye tu puruṣe aśleṣa nāśa veva), svārhavṛtteḥ kāryatvam – upāsakena puruṣeṇa svīyavarṇāśramānuguṇāgnihotrādyā- caraṇasya niyamenānuṣṭheyatā, ārabdhaśāntau prārabdhānāṃ sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāmavasāne ca sati, mokṣaḥ —sarvabandhavinirmokapūrvaka paripūrṇabrahmānubhavarūpo mokṣaḥ sidhyati iti iha kathitam - caturthādya caraṇe nirūpitamabhavat || 43 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||
itthaṃ turīyādhyāyasya pāde prāthamike punaḥ |
ekādaśānāṃ nyāyānāṃ kramāt saṅgatisaṅgrahaḥ || 186||
iti caturthasyā'dhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ || 1 ||

|| eft: ||

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ | śriyai namaḥ | śrīdharāya namaḥ ||
dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ || 2 ||
adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ || nityatva- jñatvapūrvairnipuṇamabhihitaṃ jīvatantvaṃ dvitīye
jāgaryādau ca khedo jagadaṭavimahājāṅghikasyā'tha gītaḥ | vidyārambhāvasānāvaghi yadapi paraṃ dhyātugyaṃ taduktaṃ

prārabdhasyāvasāne vapuriha jahato gatyupakrāntimāha || 1 ||

dvitīye – avirodhanāma ke dvitīye'dhyāye, nityatva- jñatvapūrvaiḥ utpatti - vināśarahitatva- jñānadharmakatvanaiyatyaprabhṛtibhiḥ jīvāsādhāraṇaiḥ dharmaiḥ jīvatadhvam — cidacidīśvarākhyatastvatrayāntargataṃ cittatvam, nipuṇam – samyak (upāsanopayogi yāvadbhavet tāvat ), abhihitam - nirūpitam, atha - tṛtīye'dhyāye, jagaṭavimahājā cikasya – saṃsārākhye mahāraṇye mahatyāṃ pādayātrāyāṃ pravṛttasya (aviratabhavāraṇyasaṃcaraṇodyatasya asya jīvātmanaḥ ), jāgaryādau khedaśva- jāgrat-svapna suṣupti-mūrcchākhyāsvavasthāsu bhramato'sya jāyamāno duḥkharāśirapi ( āyāso vā ) gīta: - nirūpitaḥ ( upāyānuṣṭhānapravṛttidārvyāya), param - tataḥ paraṃ prakrānte asmin turīye'dhyāye ādyacaraṇe, dhyātuḥ— mokṣopāyabhūte paramātmadhyāne pravartamānasya puruṣasya, vidyārambhā- vasāvadhi - vidyārambhaṃ pūrvāvadhīkṛtya vidyā niṣpattirūpamavasānam uttarāvadhīkṛtya ca madhyabhāvi- tayā sambhavi, ayaṃ yadapi tat - apekṣaṇīyaṃ vidyā niṣpattaye yadasti tat sarvamapi uktam- nirūpitamabhavat, iha – caturthadvitīye utkrāntināmake pāde, prārabdhasyā'vasāne-phalapradāna- pravṛtta karmaṇāṃ niravaśeṣakṣaye sampanne, vapuḥ jahataḥ - antimaṃ śarīraṃ parityajataḥ cetanasya, gatyupakrāntim āha—– dehāt utkramya paramapadaprayāṇasyopakramaṃ varṇayati || 1 ||

varṇyo vairāgyapāde tanukaraṇagaṇakṣobha īdṛk tathāpi
brahmajñasyā'pi kāle bhavati mṛtiriti jñaptaye'trā'nubandhaḥ | tasyotkrāntau viśeṣaṃ kathayitumucitā cā'tra sādhāraṇoktiḥ
madhye vidyāphalānāṃ maraṇamapi vadan tatpriyaṃ cintyamāha || 2 ||

īdṛk tanu- karaṇagaṇakṣobhaḥ – maraṇapatrābhidheyaḥ śarīre indriyagrāme ca sampadyamānaḥ kṣobha:, (asvasthatā viśeṣaḥ) vairāgyapāde - tṛtīyādhyāyādyapāde eva, ( cairābhyajananaupayike), varṇyaḥ yadyapi nirūpayitumucitaḥ ; ( na tu paramapuruṣārthaprāptipare phalādhyāye ) tathā'pi - tathā tatra nirūpaṇasya uktadṛṣṭyā āvaśyakatve'pi, atra anubandhaḥ phalādhyāye tannirūpaṇasya prathanam, brahmajñasyā'pi — brahmavido'pi puruṣasya, mṛtiḥ - dehaviyogarūpaṃ maraṇam, kāle- samayaviśeṣe, bhavati — avarjanīyaṃ prāpnotyeva ; ( na tu brahmajñasya kutrāpi kāpi amaradehatā), iti jñaptaye evaṃ jñānajananāya tasya brahmavidaḥ puruṣasya, utkrāntau dehādutkramaṇe, viśeṣam – dehisāmānyāpekṣayā vailakṣaṇyam (mūrdhanyayā nāḍyā niṣkramaṇarūpam ), kathayitum - nirūpayitum atra — asmin prakaraṇe, sādhāraṇoktiḥ brahmajñā brahmajñayoḥ sāmānyarūpeṇa sambhavataḥ maraṇākhyasya avasthāviśeṣasya kathanam vidyāphalānāṃ madhye vidyā māhātmyasādhya- bhūtānāṃ sukṛtaduṣkṛtā śleṣa vināśaprabhṛtīnāṃ brahmajñāsādhāraṇānāṃ madhye, maraṇamapi vadan- maraṇākhyavasthā viśeṣamapi nirūpayan sūtrakāraḥ tatpriyaṃ cintyamāha - ( paramapadaprāptaye nirupādhikasvarūpāvāśyaupayikatayā ) maraṇamapi priyatayā cintanīyaṃ brahmajñenetyāha || 2 ||

layarūpā vā saṃyogamātrarūpā vā ? ityādeḥ arthasya śārīraka phalādhyāye cintanam kvāpi - vidyā svarūpasiddhiprabhṛtau yasmin kasmiṃścana kārye, upakurute - sādhakaṃ sat aṅgabhāvaṃ bhajate iti — imaṃ hetumāśritya tat - śrutyukta kramayutametatsarvam, yathārham – yathāyogyam, sthāpyate- āvaśyakena nyāyāvalambanena samarthyate || 3 |

*

jīvaṃ ye nityamuktaṃ nijagaduratha vā jīvato muktimāhu- stattajjalpopajāpapracalita nigamamā masaṅkṣobhaśāntyai |
uktvā prārabdhakā viduṣi ca phalavat prāyaṇāntaṃ ca tulyaṃ nāḍī bhedapraveśaprabhṛti samadhikaṃ vakṣyati brahmagatyai || 4 ||

ye – advaitinaḥ sāṅkhyā vā jīvam – jīvātmānam, nityamuktaṃ nijagaduḥ- svābhāvika sārvakālikamokṣavantaṃ varṇayanti, atha tathaiva jīvato mukti vā āha:- śarīre avasthitasyaiva prakṛtibandhavinirmokarūpaṃ mokṣaṃ tattvajñānanibandhanaṃ vā badanti, tattajjalpo- ...śāntyai - teṣām advaiti-kapilākhyānāṃ vārtālāparūpāḥ ye tatvādhvasāyapracyāvakā vyāpārā:, taiḥ apratiṣṭitārthakatāmāpādito yaḥ śrautaḥ śabdarāśi:, tadgatasya virodhābhāsarūpasya kṣobhasya nivāraṇāya viduṣi ca tavajñe'pi jīve, prārabdhakāryam- - – prārabdhākhya sukṛta- duṣkṛtarūpaṃ phaladānapravṛttaṃ karma phalavat – saprayojanameva bhavati, (sukha-duḥkhādibhogaprada mena bhavati ; na niṣphalam ) prāyaṇāntaṃ ca- dehādapakramaṇaparyantamapi sarvam, tulyam - brahmajña taditarayorubhayorapi samānam iti uktvā nirūpya nāḍībheda praveśaprabhṛti -.

mūrdhanyākhyāyāṃ brahmanāmā praveśamārabhya ca

brahmagatyai— brahmaprāyupayogi, samadhikam —

brahmavidaḥ itarāpekṣayā vilakṣaṇam adhikaṃ ca, vakṣyati - nirūpayiṣyati asminnadhyāye || 4 ||
āyusīmā maniṣṭairiha bahubhirasāvātmanaḥ prekṣya yogī
yat kuryādyacca putraprabhṛtiṣu na hi tat tarkyamatretyanuktiḥ
gītādiṣvantyakāle yadagaṇi tadapi prācyayogaprakāre
tattatprāpyānurūpe niyatamiti yathāpūrvaghīratra bhāvyā || 5 ||
asau yogī brahmopāsanarūpayogaparaḥ puruṣaḥ, ātmanaḥ - svastha, āyussīmām - āyuṣaḥ antyāvadhirūpaṃ kālam, ( maraṇam ) bahubhiḥ – nānāprakāraiḥ, aniṣṭaḥ — aniṣṭa-

sūcakaiḥ śākuna- jyautiṣādibhiḥ gamakaiḥ, prekṣya - niṣkṛṣṭamavabudhya, yacca kuryāt – āyuravasāne ātmanaḥ yat kartavyamāpatet, ( śāstrataḥ sampradāyato vā), putraprabhṛtiṣu ca - putra - kalatra- mitrādiṣu vā yacca vaktavya. kārayitavyādi aihikapāratrikādisambandhi ca yat saṃbhāvyeta tat— tadapi vā atra – śarīra ke, na tarkyamiti na cintanīyamiti hetoḥ, anuktiḥ tasya tasyārthaviśeṣasya atrā nirūpaṇam, gītādipu - bhagavadgītāprabhṛtiṣu

antyakāle yadagaṇi— āyuravasāne kāryatayā yacca kathitam, tadapi - tathāvidhamapyarthajātam, tattatprā- cyānurūpe yogārambhapravṛttena prāpyatayā yat niścitya yoga ārabdhaḥ tatadanuguṇe, prācyayoga- prakāre prācīna paramparāgataḥ prāpte yogānuṣṭhānopadeśapare granthe, sāmpradāyike vā vyavahāre, niyatamiti idamitthamityavadhārya upadiṣṭamiti hetoḥ, atra yathāpūrvadhīḥ– vicārapūrvaka - nirṇeyārtha vicāraṇameva asmin prakaraṇe'pi śārīrake kāryamiti nirṇītiḥ, bhāvyā-ālocanataḥ

sampādyā || 5 ||

iti pādārambhopodghātaḥ ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||

karmajñānākṣavargo manasi na vilayaṃ yātyatatsambhavatvāt tadvattestādadhīnyāt taduparatimiha prāha sampattiśabdaḥ | ityetannopapannaṃ tadubhayavilaye codyanistārasāmyāt

sampattiḥ śleṣamātraṃ karaṇaviṣayavākchabda mukhyatvasiddhayai || 6 ||

karmajñānākṣavargaḥ - karmendriyāṇāṃ jñānendriyāṇāṃ ca stomaḥ, manasi na vilayaṃ yāti - manorūpe indriye vilayarūpāṃ sampatti naiva prāptumarhati ; atatsambhavatvāt - ( karma - jñānendriya- vargasya) mana upādānakatvābhāvāt, (vilayarūpā sampatiḥ upādānakāraṇe hi ! ghaṭate) tadvṛtteḥ vāgādīndriyavyāpārarūpāyāḥ indriyavṛtteḥ tādadhīnyāt — manodhīnatvāt, tadu- paratim - vāgādivyāpārasyoparamam (nivṛttim ), iha – ' asya puruṣasya prayato vāṅmana si sampadyate' ini utkrāntikā likakaraṇagrāmasampattiparāyāṃ śrutau sampattiśabdaḥ – sampannatā bodhanaparaḥ 'sampadyate' ityayaṃ śabdaḥ, prāha - bodhayati, ( na tu vāgādisvarūpasya manasi sampattim ) ityetat — evamākāraṃ śaṅkanam na upapannam – na saṅgataṃ bhavati, tadubhayavilaye - vāgādeḥ tadvṛttervā layārthakatvavarṇane'pi codyasya – (manaḥ prakṛtikatvāyogāt layāyogaḥ iti), ākṣepasya, manodhīnatvāt tadyogaḥ iti) nistārasya parihārasya ca samānayogakṣematvāt, (ākṣepavidhānasya parihāravidhānasya ca ubhayatra samatvāt ) karaṇaviṣaya.... siddhayai – vāgrūpendriya- vācakasya śrutistha ' vāk śabdasya lakṣaṇāṃ vinā mukhyārthabodhakatvalābhāya, ( ' vākū ' śabdasya vāgvṛttibodhakatvaṃ hi na śaktayā sambhavati | ato lakṣaṇāsvīkāre mukhyatvahāni: sampattiḥ–' sampadyate ' ityatra dhātvarthabhūtā sampattiḥ, śleṣamātram - saṃyogarūpasambandhamātram ; na tu layaḥ iti || 5 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||
atra saptacatussaṅkhyairnyāyaiḥ syāt peṭikādvayam | krameṇa sāmānyagati-viśeṣagatigocaram || 187 || prārabdhānte'pyaniyatotkāntau phalavilambanam |
ityākṣepaṃ pratikṣeptuṃ vāgādinyāyacintanā || 188 ||
iti vāgadhikaraṇam || 1 ||

annasya vikāro mana iti uktam- 'annamayaṃ hi somya manaḥ' iti vākye annaśabdāt vikārārthaka mayapratyayayute manasaḥ annavikāratvaṃ kathitam manasaḥ prāṇasampattivākye— 'manaḥ prāṇe' ityasmin mana indriyasya ( jīvotkrāntivelāyāṃ ) prāṇe sampadyamānatvapratipāda ke śrutivākye, (pratipāditā sampatti ), prāṇasya ambhomayatvāt prakṛti vikṛtitāsambhavāt- 'āpomayaḥ prāṇaḥ' iti vacanataḥ prāṇasya ambikāratva kṛtopādānopādeyabhāvasya abādhitatvāt, ('pṛthivyapsu pralīyate' iti kramasyaivānusṛtatvalābhāt ) tallayosstu – svaprakṛtibhūte (pṛthivīmaya- - svopādāne) prāṇe (ammaye) vilayātmikaiva bhavatu ; ( na tu pūrvavat saṃyogamātrātmikā ) iti śaṅkane, maivam – evaṃ prakāreṇa śaṅkanaṃ nocitam tattanmayatvaśrutiḥ - manasaḥ anna- mayatvaśrutiḥ, prāṇasya ammayatvaśrutiḥ, vācaḥ tejomayatvaśrutiśca taiḥ pṛthivī - jala-tejobhiḥ tattadāpyāyanam – manaḥ prāṇa vācāmāpyāyanam ( svasvakāryakaraṇakṣamatva rūpasāmarthyasya vyañjanam), abhimanute - abhipreti, tātparya to bodhayati ; ( na tu prakṛti-vikārabhāvam ), tataḥ- tasmāddhetoḥ, iha–prāṇe, prāgvat - vācaḥ manasīva saṃśleṣamātraṃ hi – saṃyuktatā mātra rūpaṃ saṃśleṣameva khalu, manaḥ prāṇa iti – 'manaḥ prāṇe' ityasmin vākye, — ' manaḥ prāṇe' ityasmin vākye, āmananti - śrutimataṃ varṇayanti nyāyacintakāḥ || 7 ||

adhyakṣādhikaraṇam || 4-2-3 ||
śārīrakanyā yakalāpa saṅgrahaḥ ||
prāṇo bhavet tejasi yogabhāgī tathaiva śabdaśravaṇāditīdam |
na syācu jīvopagamādiśabdāt prāṇasya tejaḥ śravaṇaṃ tu paścāt || 3 ||
prāṇaḥ - prāṇākhyo vāyuḥ, tejasi yogabhāgī bhavet – tejasva saṃśliṣṭo bhavitu- maīti ; na anyatra, tathaiva śabdaśravaṇāt – 'prāṇastejasi ' ityeva śrataśabdaśravaṇasya spaṣṭa - svāt, itīdaṃ na syāt tu — evaṃ śaṅkana naiva sambhavitumarhati prāṇasya jīvopagamādi- śabdāt - prāṇasya jīvopagamaṃ pratipādayataḥ 'imamātmānamantakāle sarve prāṇā abhisamāyanti, iti śrautaśabdasya satvāt, tejaḥ śravaṇaṃ tu - tejasi prāṇasampattiparaṃ 'prāṇastejasi' iti vākyaṃ tu paścāt – prāṇasya jīvopagamānantarakālikatejaḥ saṃśleṣapratipādanaparam || 3 ||

syāt — sambhavinī, gaṅgānipatitayamunā sāgara prāptivat - gaṅgānadyāṃ nipatya, tathā saṃśliṣṭāyāḥ yamunānadyāḥ, gaṅgāyāḥ samudrasaṅgame sati tayā sākaṃ samudre saṅgamena 'samudrasaṅgatā yamunā' iti pāramparikaprāptiriva, pāramparikaprādhyāyattā; na sākṣātprāśyāyattā || 8 ||

bhajate, trivṛtkṛtatva śravaṇāt - 'tāsāṃ trivṛtatrivṛtamekaikāṃ karavāṇi' iti śruteḥ, ('pṛthagbhūtāstatas saṃhiti vinā | nā'śaknuvan prajāḥ sraṣṭum ' iti smṛteśca ), aśakteḥ - asaṃhatānāṃ bhūtā- nāmekaikaśaḥ kāryākṣamatvena (sarvabhūtamayatayā saṃcarato jīvasya saprāṇasya, kevale tejasi layāyogāt ) [ atra ' tatteja iti sthāne ' syātte jasīti' sthite yuktamiti bhāti |'

adhikaraṇārthasaṃgrahaḥ ||

bhūtādhikaraṇe - ' prāṇastejasī 'ti śravaṇāt jīvena saṃyuktasya prāṇasya tejomātreṇa

sampattiḥ ityāśaṅkaya,

' pṛthivīmayaḥ āpomayaḥ' ityādinā sañcarato jīvasya sarvabhūtamayatvaśruteḥ, bhūtāntara- saṃsṛṣṭe tejasi sampattiḥ - ityuktam ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||

ślo. 10-14

atraiva brahma vindatyamṛta iha bhavatyāgamād brahmaniṣṭho

notkāntistasya yuktatyasadupaniṣado hyasya gatyādyaśanti | nā'nyaprātyarthametat saguṇasamadhike kutracinmānhāne -

stasmādatrā'mṛtatvaprabhṛtivacanatastādṛśī taddaśoktā || 10 ||

brahmaniṣṭhaḥ sākṣātkṛtaparatatva: vidvān, atraiva asminneva śarīre brahma vindati— brahma prāpnoti iha amṛto bhavati — asmindeha eva prāptamokṣo bhavati, āgamāt- 'atra brahma samaśnute' 'vidvānamṛta iha bhavati' iti śruteḥ, tasya tādṛśasya viduṣaḥ, utkrānti:- dehādutkramaṇam ityayamarthaḥ, na yuktā na prāmāṇikī, ityasat - evamāśaṅkanam asādhu, hi yataḥ asya gatyādi - brahmavidopyasya gati - tadarthokāntyādikaṃ virajāgamanādikaṃ ca upaniṣada: – ' tayordhvamāyan amṛtatvameti', 'sa āgacchati virajāṃ nadīm' ityādayo vedāntāḥ, uśanti- āmananti etat anyaprāpyartha na – idamaupaniṣadaṃ gatyukāntyādivacanam, abrahmaniṣṭhasya mukhyetara saguṇabrahma prāptitātparyakameva ; na nirguṇa brahmāvāptitātparyakam - iti tu na sādhu, saguṇasamadhike kutracit - saguṇādatirikte tasmādatiśayite ca prāpye'rthe kasmiścana, mānahāneḥ prāmāṇasyaivā'nupalambhāt, (pramāṇaṃ vināpi yatkiñcidarthābhyupagamasya ucchṛṅkhalatvā- pādakatayā unmattapralāpasāmyāt ) tasmāt – uktāddhetoḥ, atrā'mṛtatva prabhṛtivacanataḥ – 'amṛta iha bhavati, atra brahma samaśnute' ityādibhirvākyaiḥ, tādṛśī - muktadaśā sadṛśī, tadaśā- upāsananiṣpattyanantarakā likī brahmavido daśā, uktā - sphuṭīkṛtā || 10 ||

niśśeṣaṃ bhoga hetau galati paravidaḥ karmaṇi prāyaṇo'tho
gatyartha sūkṣmadehānugatiraṇutayā niścitasyā'phalā syāt |
mārge saṃvādavādastaducitavapuṣā tvastvitīvānuyoge
kṛtsnā vidyānivṛttiḥ parapadagamanāpekṣiṇītyādi vācyam || 11 ||
paravidaḥ brahmavedinaḥ prāyaṇe - dehānte

pāpākhye karmaṇi, niśśeṣaṃ galati - niravaśeṣatayā vinaṣṭe sati atho- dehapātānantaram, gatyartham - arcirādigatisiddhaye, aṇutayā niścinasya – sūkṣmatvena aṇuparimāṇavattayā siddhasya sūkṣma dehānugati :-- sūkṣmasya śarīrasyānuvṛttiḥ, aphalā syāt — vyarthā bhavet, (deśāddeśāntara prāptirūpāyāḥ gateḥ aṇurūpe vastunyabādhāt vibhau khalu tabādhaḥ - iti) saṃvāda bāda:-

gamanavelāyāṃ madhye candramasā saha sambhāṣaṇavyavahārastu śrutidṛṣṭaḥ, mārge taducitavapuṣā tu- arcirādimārge eva utpannena saṃvādānuguṇena tātkālikena dehenaiva astu — saṅgato bhavatu, itīvā'nuyoge - evaṃrūpe praśne pravṛtte (taduttaratayā ) kṛtsnā vidyānivṛttiḥ - sakalāyā apyavidyāyāḥ niravaśeṣanivṛttiḥ, parapadagamanāpekṣiṇī — brahmapadaprāptisāpekṣā - ( bhagavatprāpti- paryantakālakāṅkakṣiṇī, ityādi vācyam - ityevamādikaṃ ( prāptasya praśnasya samādhānatayā ) vaktavyaṃ bhavati || 11 ||
kalpādau bhūtasūkṣmaprabhṛtibhiruditaṃ varṣma kalpāntanāśyaṃ
pratyekaṃ prāṇibhede niyatamaniyatasthūla dehānuyāyi |
liṅgākhyaṃ bhastrikāntaḥ paruvakavadavasthāyi sāṅkhyaiḥ pragītaṃ

sūkṣmāṃśaḥ pūrvamūrteruparitanatanorbī jamatreṣyate tat || 12 ||

kalpādau – sargādyakāle bhūtasūkṣmaprabhṛtibhiḥ uditam – pañcabhūtānāṃ sūkṣmāṃśaiḥ karma- jñānendriyaiḥ prāṇa-mano buddhimicotpannam, kalpāntanāśyam — sargāvasāne vinaśyat prāṇi- bhede pratyekaṃ niyatam — jīvajātaviśeṣe prativyakti vyavasthitaviviktasvarūpam, aniyata - sthūla dehānuyāyi — janmabhedena avyavasthita vividhākāratayā prāpnuvatsu anekeṣvapi sthūleṣu deheṣu ekatayā anuvartamānam, bhastrikāntaḥ paruvakavat avasthāyi – cīlakagarbhapraviṣṭaputtalikāsā dṛśyena nigūḍhāvasthānaśīlam, liṅgākhyaṃ varṣma – liṅgapadābhidheyaṃ sūkṣmaṃ śarīramiti, sāṅkhyaiḥ pragītam - kāpilaiḥ sāṅkhyaiḥ ālocya varṇitam, atra - vedāntināṃ mate tu tat - sūkṣmaṃ śarīram, pūrva- mūrteḥ sūkṣmāṃśaḥ atītasya prāktanadehasya sāratamaḥ svalpo bhāgaḥ, uparitanatanoḥ – āgāmino dehasya, bījam - nidānabhūtamiti, iṣyate - pramāṇānurodhenā'bhyupagamyate || 12 ||

tatvajñānena bandhaḥ kila galati purā notkrameṇetya sāraṃ
mātā vandhyetivaddhi svavacana vihatirjīvato muktivāde |
muktaścet tasvabodhāt tanubhṛdiha tadā tatparaṃ naiṣa duḥkhye-

nmithyā duḥkhaṃ tadā cet kathaya tava kadā tasya satyatvamiṣṭam || 13 || tatvajñānena – anadhyastabrahmātmaikatvavijñānena bandhaḥ – saṃsāraḥ, purā kila — tatva- jñānotpatyanupadameva khalu, galati - nivṛtto bhavati, na utkrameṇa – dehādapakramaṇena bandhanivṛttirna bhavati, iti asāram - ityetanmataṃ na vicārakṣamam, hi-yataḥ, jīvato muktivāde - ' dhṛta- śarīrasyaiva mokṣaḥ' ityasmin vyavahāre, bandhyā mātā itivat – 'jananī apatyaprajanana- yogyatārahitā ' iti vyavaha|re iva, svavacanavihatiḥ - svoktivirodhaḥ, tanubhṛt — dehī cetanaḥ,

taccayabodhāt tatvajñānāt hetubhūtāt, iha muktaścet — jīvitakāle eva bandhādvigalito yadi, tadā tādṛśapakṣe samīcīne sati, eṣaḥ – jñānamātreṇa muktaścetanaḥ, tatparam — tattvajñāna- paścādbhāvini jīvitakāle, na duḥkhyet - duḥkha naiva prāpnuyāt; (kṣut- pipāsādiprabhavaṃ duḥkha- manubhūyamānaṃ ca na apalapitumarham ) tadā - jñānānantarajīvanakāle, duḥkhaṃ mithyā cet — duḥkham anubhūyamānamapyavāstavikaṃ yadi, tasya satyatvam - duḥkhasya vāstavikatvam, taba kadā iṣṭam ? kathaya - bhavataḥ kasmin samaye saṃmatam ? ityetat spaṣṭaṃ brūhi ! ( brahmātiriktamithyātvavādinastava duḥkhasya sarvadaiva mithyātvāt jīvanmuktau ko viśeṣa: ? – iti || 13 ||

yaṃ yaṃ bhāvaṃ smaranto jahati vapuridaṃ dehino yānti taṃ taṃ

tasmādutkrāntisāmyaṃ na ghaṭata iti cet ! tanna tanmātrasāmyāt |
vidyābhedādinītyā bhavati viṣamatā hyantimapratyayā dau

kiñcitsādharmyavāde na ca niyatimatī sarvathā sāmyasiddhiḥ || 14 |

dehinaḥ tanubhṛtaścetanāḥ, yaṃ yaṃ bhāvaṃ smarantaḥ - yādṛśa- yādṛśa viṣaya bhāvanābhāvitāntaḥ- karaṇāḥ santaḥ, idaṃ vapuḥ jahati – parigṛhītaṃ śarīraṃ parityajanti ( mriyante), taṃ taṃ yānti- uttaratra tattatsadṛśameva deha viśeṣaṃ prāpnuvanti, (iti, ' yaṃ yaṃ vā'pi smaran bhāvaṃ tyajatyante kalevaram | taṃ tamevaiti kaunteya sadā tadbhāvabhāvitaḥ ||' iti bhagavadgītābacanā (siddham |) tasmāt uktāddhetoḥ, utkrānti sāmyaṃ na ghaṭate - vidvadaviduṣoḥ dehādutkramaṇa viṣaye availakṣaṇyaṃ yaduktaṃ tat, na saṅgataṃ bhavati, iti cet tanna – ityevamāśaṅkane, tat na yuktam, tanmātrasāmyāt – 'dehādutkrānti:' ityetāvatyaṃśe eva samānatāyāḥ sthāpitatvāt (utkrānti- gatāvāntaravailakṣaṇyasya aniṣiddhatvāt ), antimapratyayādau dehāvasānaca ramakṣaṇabhāviviṣaya- viśeṣabhāvanādau viṣaye, vidyābheda dinītyā – sadvidyā va dahara vidyātvādirūpāvāntaraviśeṣāna- palāpe'pi brahmavidyāśvena rūpeṇa sāmyamiva (vaiṣamyamiva ca), viṣamatā bhavati hi —mithovailakṣaṇyaṃ vartata eva (utkrāntitvarūpasāmānya sahakṛtam ) khalu kiñcitsādharmyavāde – yatkiṃcidaṃśe samāna- dharmatāyā varṇanasthale, sarvathā sāmyasiddhiḥ sarvāśe samānatāyā (uktito) lābhaḥ, na ca niyatimatī naiva vyavasthitaḥ, ( nāvyabhicariteti ) || 14 ||

āsṛtyupakramāghikaraṇe—'atha martyo'mṛto bhavati' itya divākye, 'atra brahma samaśnute' iti ihaivā'mṛtatvavacanāt, 'viduṣaḥ utkrāntirnāsti' – ityāśaṅkaya,

tayordhvamāyannamṛtatvameti' iti viduṣo'pi utkrānterdarśanāt, 'atha martyaḥ' ityādi- śravaṇasya adagdhadeha sambandhasya jīvasya uttarapūrvādhayoraśleṣa- vināśarūpāmṛtatvapratipādanaparatvācca, viduṣopyutkrāntirastīti sā ca ānāḍīpraveśāt samānā — ityuktam ||

nayasaṃgatimālikā ||
atra brahmā'śnute vidvāniti spaṣṭamanuśravāt | utkrāntyāśkṣepasaṃśāntyai āsṛtinyāyacintanā || 192 ||
iti āmṛtyu kramādhikaraṇam || 5 ||

0-

parasampattyadhikaraṇam ||4-2-6||
śārīrakanyāya kalā pasaṃgrahaḥ ||
bhogādyabhāvena nirarthakatvādyathāyathaṃ syurna tu devatāyām |

saṃyanti tānīti na śaṅkayametat tejaḥ parasyāmiti coditatvāt || 6 ||

tāni jīva saṃsṛṣṭāni bhūtasūkṣmāṇi bhogādyabhāvena - sukhādyanubhava - pravṛttinivṛttyādi- prayojanaviśeṣasya dehasādhyasya uttaratrāsambhavena nirarthakatvāt — jīvasahabhāvena vṛtteḥ prayojanā-

bhāvāt, yathāyatham — yathāyogyam, syuḥ – yatra kutrāpi gatāni bhaveyuḥ, na tu devatāyāṃ saṃyanti—parasyāṃ devatāyāṃ paramātmani naiva sampannāni bhaveyuḥ, iti na śaṅkayametat — uktamidaṃ naivaivaṃ śaṅkanīyam, tejaḥ – teja : padopalakṣitāni sarvāṇi bhūtasūkṣmāṇi parasyām — parasyā devatāyām parātparadevatābhūte parasmin brahmaṇi sampadyante iti coditatvāt iti 'teja: parasyāṃ devatāyām ' ityevamākārayā śrutyā upadiṣṭatvāt (suṣupti - pralayayoriva viśramārthatvayogena parasampatti pārthakyāt || ) || 6||

-0-

jīvotkrāntyukti kāle viduṣi tu ghaṭate tatpratikṣepabhaṅgo
bhūtotkrāntestu paścāt tadupanipatane saṅgatiḥ syāt kathaṃcit |
tasmāt sādhāraṇo'pi yamupari tataḥ sthāpyate hārdayoga-

stulye'pyasminna tulyāḥ saraṇimukhatayā prāpyabhedena nāḍyaḥ || 15 ||

jīvotkrāntyuktikā le— jīvasāmānyasya śarīrādutkrānticintanavelāyāmeva viduṣi tu tatpratikṣepabhaṅgaḥ– jīva viśeṣabhūte brahmavidi paraṃ śarīrādutkrāntyabhāvavādasya nirāsa:, ghaṭate — saṅgato bhavati, bhūtotkrānteḥ paścāt bhūtasūkṣmāṇāṃ dehādutkramaṇa vicārānantaram tadupanipatane– vidvajjīvotkāntipratikṣepasya nirasane kriyamāṇe tu saṅgatiḥ kathaścit syāt–vicāropakṣepasāṅgatyaṃ kliṣṭakalpanāī bhavati, tasmāt – uktāddhetoḥ, ayaṃ hārdayogaḥ- pratipādayiṣyamāṇaḥ hārdenā''tmanā yoga ityaṃśaḥ, sādhāraṇo'pi - viduṣaḥ aviduṣaśca aviśiṣṭo'pi tata upari sthāpyate - viduṣopi jīvasya dehādutkrāntisamarthanataḥ paścāt upakṣipya samarthyate, asmin — hārdena paramātmanā yoge, saraṇimukhatayā - vividhānāṃ mārgāṇā dvārasthānāpannatayā, tulye'pi — sādhāraṇe satyapi prāpyabhedena – gantavyārthaviśeṣānuguṇyena, nāḍya: - nāḍīpadavācyāḥ dhamanyaḥ, atulyāḥ – viduṣāmaviduṣāṃ ca mitho vilakṣaṇarūpā eva ( gantavyanānādhvadvāraikyamātreṇa adhvanāmaikyasya anāpādanīyatvāt ) || 15 ||

prāptuṃ bhogāpavargoṃ prayati tanubhṛti prāptasūkṣma svadehe

sampatyā kiṃ parasyāṃ śritahṛdayasuṣau labhyate devatāyām |
ātastejaḥ parasyāmiti ciraghaṭite'pyastu siddhānuvādo
maivaṃ mānānusārāt phalamiti parame saṅkramaḥ śrāntiśāntyai || 16 ||

prayati tanubhṛti – dehāniṣkramya prayāṇodyukte (sthūla dehavati ) jīve, bhogāpavargoṃ prāptum—svargaprabhṛtikaṃ bhogaṃ vā parabrahmāvāptirūpamapavarga vā labdham prāptasūkṣmavadehe- dehāntaranirmāṇahetubhūtapūrva dehasārāṃśarūpaṃ svīyasūkṣmadehamavāptavati sati śritahṛdayasupau hṛdayākhyātyantasūkṣmasthānamāśrityāvatiṣṭhamānāyām parasyāṃ devatāyām - paramātmā para paryāyāyāṃ paradevatāyām, sampatyā - sampattipadābhidheyena saṃśleṣeṇa, kiṃ labhyate - kiṃ vā pūrvamalabdhaṃ nūtanaṃ phalaṃ prāptaṃ syāt ? na kimapi ityataḥ sampattireva nāṅgīkriyatām ), āta: – ataḥ, uktāddhetoḥ, ciraghaṭite'pi sadā saṅgate'sminneva paramātmani siddhānuvādaḥ astu - pūrvasiddhasyaiva samāgamasya anuvādarūpo bhavatu, tejaḥ parasyāmiti – 'tejaḥ parasyāṃ devatāyām ' ityayaṃ śrutikhaṇḍa: ; ( na tu 'prayāṇakāle sampattiviśeṣo bhaviṣyatī' tyarthakaḥ - iti śaṅkane ) maivam – īdṛśamāśaṅkanaṃ naiva sādhu bhavet, mānānusārāt - tādṛśārthāvagamakaṃ pramāṇamanusṛtya phalamiti — prayojanaṃ kiñcidbhavedeveti cintanīyaṃ pramāṇe śraddhāvatā ; ( na tu svasyā'nupalambha- mātreṇa tadabhāva eva nirṇeyaḥ ; yena antato nāstikyapatha evāśrito bhavet ), iti, para me

saṃkramaḥ paramātmani sampattirūpaḥ tatkāla prāptaḥ samāgamaviśeṣaḥ, śrāntiśāntyai - dehotkrama- na jātasya āyāsaviśeṣasya niharaṇarūpaṃ phalaṃ prāptum || 16 ||

-0-

sampattirdevatāyāṃ bhavatu laya iyaṃ sā hi sarvasya yoni- bhūyaḥ srapuṃ kṣamā cetyasadanupajato vācyavairūpyadoṣāt | viśleṣo bhūtasūkṣmairiha na ca ghaṭate sṛṣṭiklaptistu gurvīṃ
proktā dhūmādimārge gatirapi bhavinatpastariṣvañjanena || 17 ||

iyaṃ devatāyāṃ sampattiḥ - ante uktā ' tejaḥ parasyāṃ devatāyām ' itīyaṃ sajīva bhūtasūkṣmāṇāṃ paradevatāyāṃ sampatti, layo bhavatu - kāraṇāpattirūpaḥ pralaya iti abhyupagamyatām, hi yataḥ, sā parā devatā, sarvasya – kāryātmakasyākhilajagataḥ, yoniḥ - upādānabhūtā satī bhūtayonipadavācyā, bhūyaḥ punaḥ sraṣṭuṃ kṣamā ca - uttaratra gatyarthaṃ śarīramutpādayitumapi samarthā vartate, (sarvasya svakāraṇe layo hi svābhāviko nirbādha : ) ityasat - evaṃ śaṅkanamasādhu, anuṣajata: - pūrvataḥ prāptānuṣaṅgasya ' sampadyate ' ityasya, vācyavairūpyadoṣāt – 'ekatra saṃśleṣo vācyo'rthaḥ ; aparatra pralayo vācyo'rthaḥ ' ityevaṃ vācyārtha vairūpyābhidhāne prakramabhaṅgarūpa doṣā- pātāt bhūtasūkṣmaiḥ viśleṣaḥ - teja ādi bhūtasūkṣmaiḥ sākaṃ dūrībhāvarūpo vibhāgaḥ paran iha na ghaṭate - atra prakaraṇe na saṅgato bhavati ; gamanādivyāpārasya bhaviṣyato bādhāpatteḥ, sṛṣṭiklaptistu gurvī- sarvaśaktayā paradevatayā gamanādyarthaṃ punardehasṛṣṭirvarṇyamānā kalpanagauravadūṣitā syāt, bhavinaḥ– anatikrāntasaṃsaraṇapathasya jīvasya dhūmādimārge gatirapi – 'dhūmo ratristathā kṛṣṇaḥ' ityādyuktapitṛyāṇe saṃcaraṇavidhā'pi, tatpariṣñjanena proktā — bhūtasūkṣmapariṣvaṅgeṇa sahaiveti — tadantarapratipactyadhikaraṇe sthāpiteti (tadvat devayāne'pi gatiḥ tatpariṣvaṅgeṇaiveti sarva sustham ) || 17 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||
parasampattisatve sā laya eva bhaviṣyati |
iti śaṅkāpavādāyā'vibhāganayanirṇayaḥ || 194 ||
iti avibhāgādhikaraṇam || 7 ||

atisūkṣme – atyantaṃ durjñeye, nāḍījāle - gantavyanāḍīnāṃ stomamadhye, muktināḍī- muktyarthanirgamanamārgabhūtā mūrdhanyanāḍī, vivektum - vivicya jñātum, na suśakā naiva susādhyā

syāt, tasmāt - tato hetoḥ, mucyamānamya puṃsaḥ - mokṣamāpnuvataḥ puruṣasya mūrdhanyanāḍī- gatiḥ mūrdhasthānagatryāṃ suṣumnākhyabrahmanāḍya praveśaḥ, niyataḥ, vākyam – 'śataṃ caikā ce 'tyādivākyaṃ tu, tayā ūrdhvagantuḥ – ākasmikatayā tannāḍīpathena ūrdhvayānaṃ sampannasya sambhavāt amṛtatāṃ pravadat astu – sambhāvita muktatvam anuvādavidhayā jñāpayat syānnāma, ( iti śaṅkane) maivam – naivamucitam vidyāsamprītahārda- prasadanamahasā — brahmopāsanarūpayā vidyayā upāsake prīta hṛdayasya paramātmano hārdābhidhānasya prasādāyattena hṛdayāprasthānaprakāśena svāī nāḍīpraveśāt – paramapadaprāptimabhīpsataḥ svastha yogyāyā madhya nāḍyāṃ praveśasya sukhena lābhāt || 18 ||

aniyamataḥ avyavasthita eva,

na

svādhīno hārdasaṃjñaḥ svayamavikalayā sampadā sākamekaḥ

sthitvā hṛtpadmamadhye sthagita nijatanuḥ saptalokī gṛhasthaḥ |
nāḍīcakre suṣumnāṃ nikhiladhṛtikarīṃ nābhimūrdhāntarūpāṃ
mitvā tanmadhyarandhraprahitamiṣumivotkṣipya netā mumukṣum || 19 ||

saptalo kī gṛhasthaḥ - bhūrādisatyāntānāṃ saptānāṃ lokānāṃ svāmī rakṣitṛtvenāvasthitaḥ paramapuruṣaḥ, svādhīnaḥ – akarmavaśya eva san ekaḥ - ( ātmaśarīrendriyasaṅghāte asmin ) pradhānatamaḥ kṣetrajñaḥ, svayam – svasaṅkalpamātrapreritaḥ, avikalayā sampadā sākam - anyūnayā- (asaṅkucitayā nirupādhikyā vā ) nityaniravadyayā śriyā saha sthagita nijatanuḥ– carmacakṣuṣāma- vedyasvīyadivyavigrahaḥ, hṛtpadmamadhye dehināṃ hṛdayakamalamadhyakarṇikāyām hārdasaṃjñaḥ sthitvā - hārda ' iti anvarthanāmnā rakṣitṛtva - niyantṛtvādidharmairavasthāya, ('samo'haṃ sarvabhūte 'ṣviti niṣpakṣapātena madhyasthatayā vā avasthāya) mumukṣu – saṃsāranigaḍādvimoka micchantam svābhāvika niratiśaya nirupadhika svasā yujyānandamabhilaṣannam iti vā ) cetanam iṣumitra utkṣipya netā- bāṇamitra ūrdhvamasitvā svapadaṃ neṣyan (netukāmaḥ), nāḍīcakre – asaṅkhye nāḍīstome pradhāna- tamām nikhiladhṛtikarīm sarvaviśvadhārakatvena dehamadhyāvasthitām nābhimūrdhāntarūpām- dehagatanābhisthānamārabhya mūrdhāntaṃ vyāpya sarvāsāṃ nāḍīnāmupajīvyatayā itaranāḍīle parahitatayā ca avasthitām suṣumnām – suṣumnākhyāṃ brahmanāḍīm, bhittvā - anupāsakānām (ayatīnām ) apraveśāya pihitadvārāmapi svīyena bhaktānugrahasaṅkalpena udghāṭitadvārāṃ kṛtvā tanmadhyarandhra- prahitam – suṣumnāmadhyasuṣirāntaḥ praviṣṭaṃ kṛtvā, netā — arcirādipathe gamayitā bhavati || 19 ||

adhikaraṇārtha saṃgrahaḥ ||

tadoko'dhikaraṇenāḍīnāṃ durvivecatyāt ' tayordhvamā yannamṛtatvameti' iti mūrdhanya-

nācyā udgamanam aniyatam — ityāśaṅkaya,

paramātmaprasādena prakāśitadvāratvāta, mūrdhanyanāḍyā ugamanaṃ niyatam ityuktam ||

-0-

nayasaṅgatimālikā || gatisāmānye'pi nāḍī durvivekā yadā bhavet | anaiyatyoditā''śaṅkā tadokonītyapoditā || 195|| iti tadoko'dhikaraṇam ||| 8 ||

'etaireve' ti vākye 'atha yatraitasmāccharīrādutkrāmati; athaitaireva raśmibhirūrdhvamākramate ' ityetasmin śrutivacane, dinakara kiraṇālambanena - sūrya ra imīnāmāśrayaṇena yoginaḥ - niṣpanno- pāsanasya puruṣasya, ūrdhvayānam - gantavya mūrdhvagamanam yat proktam — yat spaṣṭīkṛtamasti, tat -sūryaraśmīnāmāśrayaṇata ūrdhvagamanamityetat niśi ayuktayā - rātrau (sūryābhāvataḥ) raśmī nāmapyasambhavena, dinamṛtiniyatam ahnimaraṇe eva niyatasambhavam na maraṇasāmānye, iti cet na—evaṃ śaṅkā na yujyate, hi —– yataḥ, ahni - divasa eva, chāyāsu - anāpa- pradeśeṣu, (tathā) ra triṣvapi - kṣapāsvapi ca ( sākṣātsūrya darśana rahita kā ladeśa diṣu ca ), liṅgaiḥ svedoṣmādyupalambhādibhirjñāpakaiḥ laghutaraḥ raśmayaḥ santi ātape yāvatpramāṇā: tadapekṣayā'lpapramāṇāḥ sūryāṃśavaḥ vartamānatvenānumeyā bhavantyeveti niścīyate, varṣādirātrau- prāvṛṣi niśāsamaye, yadi na tāpaḥ – ūṣmaṇaḥ svedādeśca anupalambho yadi tadā, etat - ūṣmopalambharā hityam, himadinanyā yataḥ hemantata ātape'pi ūṣmānupalambhavat kiraṇau- bhibhāvaka jalāṃśabāhulyakṛtaṃ vicintya samādheyam || ( ato yogino rātrimṛtāvapi sūryaramyanusāro'styeveti ) || 20 || ||

niśādhikaraṇam ||4-2-10||
śārīrakanyāya kalāpasaṃgrahaḥ ||

bhavati (naṣṭaṃ bhavati), asmin — viduṣo brahmasampadane, vilambaḥ - śīghra kālatvābhāvaḥ, ' tāvadeve 'tyuktaḥ ' tāvadeva ciraṃ yāvanna vimokṣye' iti śrutau kathitaḥ, ataḥ — uktāddhetoḥ, saḥ– vidvān cetanaḥ, vidyayā - paramātmaprītyā pādikayā brahmopāsanarūpayā vidyayā, ahani niśi ca – divā ca rātrau ca param aśnute - paraṃ brahma upasanno bhavati na niśāmaraṇa-- mātreṇa punarjanmavān bhavati ||22||
yatraikasyā'pavargaḥ pratiniyatatayā gaṇyate jātakajñai-
stat syādantyaṃ śarīraṃ nanu bhavati tato'pyanyayogavyudāsaḥ | nahayantrā'pyantakāle dina. rajanibhidā deśabhedādi caivaṃ

śavyaṃ kurvanti cā'smin yaduca yaducanetyādi caivaṃ vibhāvyam || 22 || jātakajñaiḥ—dehino janmakālamavalambya taduttaraphalacintakaiḥ jyotirvidbhiḥ ekasya - yasya kasyacana puruṣasya, yatra - yasmin śarīre avasite, apavargaḥ - mokṣaḥ, pratiniyatatayā - avarjanīyatayā, gaṇyate gaṇanato niścīyate, tat śarīram - tādṛśaniśvayaviṣayībhūta- jātakavat śarīram antyaṃ nanu tadbhogāyakeṣu śarīreṣu caramaṃ bhavatītyetat dhruvam, tato'pi - jātakagaṇavato niścayādapi, anyayogavyudāsaḥ bhavati tasya śarīrāntarasambandhā- bhāvaniścayaḥ dṛḍho bhavati, atrā'pi - īdṛśadehicetanaviṣaye'pi na hi dina- rajanibhidā-- divārātrimaraṇayoḥ praśastatva- tadabhāvakṛtaṃ vaiṣamyaṃ naiva khalu sambhavitam evam uktarītyā . deśabhedādi ca - uttamakṣetre maraṇam adhamakṣetre maraṇam iti deśabhedakṛnamaraṇaprāśastyādi ca naiva sambhavi evam – yuktiriva śrutivacanamapi 'śatryaṃ kurvanti cā'smin yadu ca yadu ca na' ityādi - 'yadu caivā'smin śavyaṃ kurvanti yadu ca na' iti bṛhadāraṇyakīyaṃ vacanamapi śava- sambandhyuttarakriyāṇāmabhāve'pi asaṃdigdhamokṣatā pratipādanaparaṃ) vibhāvyam - prārabdhakarmaṇāṃ nirava- śeṣakṣaye yoginaḥ kāla- deśādivaiṣamyamavigaṇayyaiva parabrahmasampattijñāpakaṃ viśeṣataścintanīyam | 22 |

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||
satsu saureṣu tejassu kāladoṣanibandhanam |
pratihantumihā''kṣepaṃ niśāghikaraṇaṃ matam || 197||
iti niśādhikaraṇam || 10 ||

-0-

dehaṃ yadi tyajati – śarīraṃ jahāti cet, indoḥ sāyujyaṃ vindet candraṃ prāptaḥ san tatsamāna-- bhogatvarūpaṃ tatsāyujyaṃ prāpnuyādeva, iha punarbhavati martye loke punarāvṛttimān saṃsārī ca

jāyate tacchruteḥ tatsmṛteśva – tadarthakaśruteḥ 'atha yo dakṣiṇe pramīyate pitṛṇāmeva mahimānaṃ gatvā candramasaḥ sāyujyaṃ gacchati' ityākārāyāḥ, tadarthakasmṛteḥ, 'tatra cāndramasaṃ jyotiryogī prāpya nivartate' ityākārāyāśca satvāt ( viśiṣya tatra pramāṇe upalabhyamāne, tadarthānabhyupagame hetvabhāvāt ), maivam - evamapārthābhyumagamo māstu, hi yataḥ, pūrvoktanītiḥ -- niśi viduṣo maraṇe brahmānavāptiśaṅkāyāḥ parihāro yena nyāyena kṛtaḥ sa nyāyaḥ, (sarva prārabdhakṣaye niśāmaraṇasya brahmaprāptivighaṭakatvaṃ na sambhavatītyayam ), iha - viduṣo dakṣiṇāyanamaraṇaviṣaye'pi, vihitāna dūrībhūtaḥ ; kintu vartata eva atha – evaṃ sthite, asya – dakṣiṇāyane tyakta dehasya viduṣaḥ indau sāyujyam - śrutipratipannaḥ candrasayugbhāvaḥ, mṛtasyāpi viduṣaḥ apunarāvṛttyā brahma prāpnuvato'pi ādityasayugbhāva iva nudagayane pramīyate devānāmeva mahimānaṃ gatvādityasya sāyujya gacchati' viśrāntyai— madhyemārga dehotkramaṇādikṛtāyā saparihārapūrvakaṃ prasannatāyai ghaṭate, bhīṣmakāla- pratīkṣā – kurukula pitāmahena bhīṣmācāryeṇa kṛtā dehatyāgāyottarāyaṇa kālapratīkṣā tu, jagadupa- kṛtaye - sādhāraṇānāṃ janānāṃ uttarāyaṇamaraṇaprāśastya paraśāstraviśvā sadāyapādana rūpopakārāya yudhiṣṭhirājena sarvadharmarahasyopadeśataḥ lokopakārādhānāya vā svacchandamaraṇatāyāḥ śantanu- varasiddhatvāt ) yujyate ; ( na tāvatā dakṣiṇāyanamaraṇe doṣaḥ ) || 23 ||
kiṃ ca prārabdhakarma pratiniyatamidaṃ jāhrabīsambhavasya

na

sūrya nītyā — udagayane

(ya evaṃ vidvā-
iti śrutyukta iva )

svecchā yenaivamāsīt sa ca vasurabhavannaiṣa sākṣādvimuktaḥ |
tasmād vindeta mukti nara-pitṛ diviṣadra trikāle'pi yogī

syātāṃ prāśastya - ninde taditaraviṣaye gītayoktau tu mārgoṃ || 24 ||

kiṃ ca - vivicya jñātavyamanyadapi kiṃcidasti ( kimiti cet ) idaṃ prārabdhakarma -... māsaṣṭrakadehadhāraṇapūrvakaṃ kālapratīkṣaṇaupayikaṃ prārabdhaṃ karma, jāhnavīsambhavasya – gaṅgā sūnorbhīṣmasya, pratiniyatam — tadvyaktimātre sīmitam, (anyena kenāpyevaṃ kartumaśakyatvāt ), yena – tanmātra- niyatena yādṛśena prārabdhakarmaṇā, evaṃ svecchā āsīt - īdṛśāvasthityanuguṇā evamākāra rā asādhāraṇī svatantrecchā tasya samabhavat sa ca - bhīṣmaśca, vasurabhavat - pūrvam aṣṭasu vasu ṣu yādṛśo vasurāsīt punastādṛśa evājāyata, eṣaḥ - bhīṣmaḥ, sākṣāt - bhīṣmanāmakā dehā dvi-

nirgamādeva kevalam ( advārakatayā ), na vimuktaḥ – sarvabandhavinirmokapūrvakaṃ paramapadaṃ prāptavān na; kintu vasupadaprāptidvāraiva tasmāt uktāddhetukalāpāt yogī- vidvān brahmajñaḥ nara- pitṛ-diviṣadrātrikāle'pi narāṇāṃ rātribhūtāyāṃ dainandinyāṃ niśāyām, pitṝṇa rātribhūte kṛṣṇapakṣe devānāṃ rātribhūte dakṣiṇāyane'pi mṛta (aviśeṣeṇa) muktiṃ vindeta arcirādiyānena gatvā parabrahmasampattiṃ labhetaiva ; nātra saṃśayaḥ, prāśastya - ninde uttarāyaṇa maraṇastutitaditaragaīṇaṃ ca taditaraviṣaye vidvaditaraviṣaye (mokṣetara kāmanayā iṣṭāpūrta-

dhanuṣṭhānaśīlasya svamārgāvāptau anukūla-pratikūla kālapratipādanaida paryapravṛtte), gītayā uktau tu — 'yatrakāle svanāvṛttimāvṛti caiva yoginaḥ' ityatra kālaśabdena nirdiṣṭau tu mārgoṃ - arci- rādi-dhūmādi pathāveva ; na tu kālātmakau, (iti sarvaṃ niravadyameva ) || 24 ||

śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṅgrahaḥ ||
vāk tu cetasi tat prāṇe tat punarjīvatejasoḥ | tejasaśca taraśleṣastāvanmātrasya tulyatā || 12 ||
teṣāṃ parasmin gamanaṃ tasya ca śleṣamātratā |
mūrdhanyanāḍīniyamo raśmineti vyavasthitiḥ || 13 ||
niśāyāṃ cā'pi sanmokṣo mokṣaṇaṃ dakṣiṇāyane |
ityetat sarvametena pādenā'tra vicintitam || 14 ||

vāk tu cetasi – itarairindriyairyutaṃ vāgindriyaṃ (ceta ākhye) manasi utkrānti samaye sampadyamānaṃ bhavati, tat prāṇe - tacca manaḥ vācāyutaṃ prāṇavāyau sampannaṃ bhavati, tat punaḥ jīvatejasoḥ - vāgādiprāṇāntaṃ karaṇajātaṃ jīve sampadyamānaṃ bhavati, jīvasampatya- nantaraṃ jīvena sahitaṃ tejasi sampannaṃ bhavati, tejamaśca itaraśleṣaḥ – saprāṇena jīvena sampadyamānasya tejasaḥ na tejomātratā ; kintu itarabhūtasaṃśliṣṭate jorūpatā (sarvabhūtasūkṣmātmakatā) 5) tāvanmātrasya tulyatā - etāvatparyantasya utkramaṇasya viduṣi aviduṣi ca jīve aviśiṣṭatā, teṣāṃ parasmin gamanam - indriyādīnāṃ sajīvabhūtasūkṣmāntānāṃ paramātmani sampannatā, tasya saśleṣarūpatā - sampattipadoktasya cārthasya kevalaṃ saṃśleṣapadābhidhānāī sambandhamātra- rūpatā ; na tu kāraṇāpatyātmakala parūpatā, mūrdhanya nāḍīniyamaḥ – viduṣaḥ mūrdhāntavyāpinyā ata eva mūrdhanyapadavācyayā suṣumnānāḍyaiva nirgama iti vyavasthā, raśmineti vyavasthitiḥ – nirgamanaṃ sūryaraśmīnanusṛtyaiva ; mā'nyatheti niṣkarṣaḥ, niśāyāṃ cā'pi sanmokṣaḥ – niśānāṃ maraṇe'pi satāṃ - viduṣāṃ mokṣāvaśyambhāva eva mokṣaṇaṃda kṣiṇāyane- viduṣāṃ cetanānāṃ dakṣiṇāyanamaraṇe'pi mokṣa vinābhāvaḥ (mokṣasya prārabdhasarvakarmakṣayamātrādhīnatvāt tasya niśā-dakṣiṇāyanayoḥ taulyāt ityetat sarvam – uktamidam arthaikādaśakam etena pādenāstra vicintitam – śārīrakagatena turīyadvitīyapādena ekādaśa saṃkhyairavikaraṇaiḥ vicārya nirṇītamasti || 12, 13, 14 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||
sampadyetā'nyadakṣaṃ manasi tadapi tatsaṃyutaṃ prāṇavāya

so'dhyakṣe taiḥ sametaḥ sa ca tadakhilavān bhūtavarge tu sūkṣme |

utkrāntiḥ syāt samānā yutiratha ca pare sā ca saṃśleṣamātraṃ
nirgacchedbrahmanāḍyā ghṛṇibhiratha niśā - devarājyośca mokṣī || 25 ||

anyat akṣam - manaso'tiriktaṃ karma- jñānendriyadaśakam, manasi sampadyeta - ( dehā- dutkrāntikāle ) manorūpe antarindriye sampattimadbhavati, tadapi manorūpamapīndriyam, tatsaṃyutam — svasmin sampannaiḥ karma- jñānendriyaiḥ sahitaṃ sat, prāṇavāyau prāṇākhye vāyau ( prāṇanādhārabhūte ) sampadyate, saḥ taiḥ sametaḥ prāṇākhyo vāyuḥ indriya manobhiḥ sahitaḥ san, adhyakṣe—karaṇādhipe jīve sampadyate, sa ca tadakhilavān - jīvo'pi indriya- manaḥ prāṇaiyuktaḥ san sūkṣme bhūtavarge - sūkṣmadaśāvasthite ākāśādibhūtapañcakastome, sampadyate, utkrāntiḥ samānā syāt - vāgādīnāṃ mana ādāvāramya etadantamuktā utkrāntiḥ viduṣāmaviduṣāṃ ca jīvānāṃ tulyarūpaiva, atha ca – paścācca, pare yutiḥ– sajīvasya bhūta- pañcakastomasya pare brahmaṇi sampatti: sā ca saṃśleṣamātram — uktā ca sampattiḥ saṃśleṣa padavācyaṃ sambandhamātrameva ; na tu kāraṇa patti rūpo layaḥ, 8 (mokṣī - muktibhāgujīvaḥ), brahma nāḍyā nirgacchet — brahmanāḍīpadābhidheyayā suṣumnayā dvārabhūtayā niṣkrānto bhavet 9 athaḥ ghṛṇibhiḥ tasya gamanam sūryaraśmibhiḥ saha ūrdhvānam, atha niśā - devarājyo śva – niśāyāṃ dainandinyāṃ devānāṃ rātribhūtāyāṃ vā mṛtopi, mokṣī mokṣārho bhavati jīvo vidvān | ityevam ekādaśa miradhikaraṇaiḥ ekādaśā'rthāḥ śrutimatāḥ sthāpitāḥ) || 25 ||

.
