|| śrīḥ ||

śrīmate

rāmānujāya namaḥ | śriyai namaḥ | śrīdharāya namaḥ ||
caturthasyāsdhyāyasya

tṛtīyaḥ (gati) pādaḥ || 4-3 || arcirādyadhikaraṇam || 4-3 - 1 || śārīrakanyā yakalāpasaṃgrahaḥ ||
śākhāsu bahuvitvādgatiśruterarcira diniyamo na |
tannaiva niyamanaṃ syāt sarvatrārkādicihnasambhavataḥ || 1 ||

nādinā satkurvadbhiḥ madhyemārgamavasthitairdevagaṇaiḥ arpitopahāraḥ ayaṃ yuktau mukta ātmā, yena prayāti yena mārgeṇa gacchati, asau - tādṛśaḥ, naḥ mohākūpārapāraṃ paraṃ puram abhigamayan- asmān paraprāptikāmān ajñānamayasya prakṛtimaṇḍalākhyasya dustarasya sāgarasadṛśasya parastāt tīre vidyamānaṃ ( tamasaḥ parastāt ) 'yā'yodhyetyaparājiteti viditā nākaṃ pareṇa sthitā' iti gīyamānam ayodhyā midhaṃ brahmapuraṃ prāpayan maukundaḥ muktighaṇṭāpathaḥ – mukunda sambandhī mokṣārtho rājamārgaḥ, iha — turīyatṛtīye caraṇe, pañcabhiḥ nyāyabhedaiḥ pañcasaṃkhyaiḥ adhikaraṇaviśeṣaiḥ

sthāpyate vicārapūrvakaṃ dṛḍhī kriyate || 1 ||

śākhā bhedeṣu bhinnāṃ gatimupaniṣado'vīyate tanmumukṣo-

vidyāvaiṣamyanītyā gati ( suti) rapi viṣamā na vyavasthā'cirādeḥ | naitat sarvatra taistairiha tadidamiti pratyabhijñānasiddhau
bhāvyaṃ nyūnādhikatvaprabhṛti nikhilamapyatra siddhā viruddham || 2 ||

śākhābhedeṣu upaniṣadaḥ vibhinna vedaśākhā viśeṣeṣvantaḥ pātinaḥ chāndogya vājasaneyaka- kauṣītakyādyāḥ śrutiśirobhāgāḥ mumukṣoḥ mokṣārthayā icchayā sampāditaparabrahmavedana sya puruṣasya, bhinnāṃ gatim - mithovilakṣaṇām anyānyāṃ paramapadaprayāṇasaraṇim, adhīyate- āmananti tat — tasmāddhetoḥ, mumukṣoḥ gatirapi – mumukṣucetanasya paramapadaprāptisaraṇirapi, vidyāvaiṣamyanītyā mokṣārthānuṣṭheyāsu vidyāsu vailakṣaṇyanyāyamanurudhya, viṣamā - vilakṣaṇatā - pannaiva, iti hetoḥ, arcirādeḥ vyavasthā na sarveṣāmapyaciḥ prabhṛtikā paddhatireveti nirbandho naivāsti (iti pūrvapakṣe ), naitat īdṛśaṃ śaṅkanaṃ naiva sādhu bhavitumarham, sarvatra – sarvasminnapi - śākhāviśeṣe upalabhyamānaiḥ tai taiḥ agni vāyuprabhṛtibhiḥ mārgāsādhāraṇa cihnabhūtaiḥ, iha- mokṣārtha gantavya mārgaviṣaye, tadidamiti pratyabhijñānasiddhau – ' tadevedaṃ vartma ' ityevaṃ pratyabhijñā- rūpajñānasyaikatvapratyāyakasya upalabdhau satyām (' sarvatra śruto'pyeka eva panthāḥ ' iti niścaye ca sampanne), atra nyūnādhikatvaprabhṛti nikhilamapi - asmin viṣaye tatra tatropalabhyamānaṃ nyūnatvamā dhikyaṃ cetyādi sarvamapi siddhāviruddhaṃ bhāvyam -- pratyabhijñānasiddhasyārthasya anuguṇaṃ yathā syāt tathā vicārya nirṇeyam || 2 ||

caidyādīnāmayodhyā sthiracara januṣāṃ puṇḍarīkādikānāṃ

bhīṣmādīnāmupāstikramabhava vibhavavyūha loka sthitīnām |
dhātṛṇāṃ tatsutānāmadhikṛtivigame brahma samprāpsyatāma-
vyanyeṣāṃ prasthitiḥ sā śitapṛthumatibhiścintanīyā yathārham || 3 ||

caidyādīnām – śiśupālaprabhṛtīnām, ( bhagavatā prāptamaraṇānāṃ tatraiva bhagavati līnatvena itihāsaprasiddhānām ), ayodhyā sthiracarajanuṣām - sāketanagaravṛttīnāṃ (śrīrāmacandraparamapadā- rohaṇasamaye tena sahaiva gatvā sāntānikalokagāminī) sthāvara - jaṅgamātmaka dehavatāṃ cetanānām, puṇḍarīkādikānām - ( bhagavataiva svayaṃ nītatvena purāṇāvagatānā) puṇḍarīkamukhānāṃ bhaktaviśeṣā- ṇām bhīṣmādīnām – (kurupitāmahatvena bhārataprasiddha brahmavidagragaṇya) bhīṣmaprabhṛtīnāṃ vasvaṃśa- bhūtatayā vasupadādyavātidvārā prāptabhagavatsthānānām, upāstikramabhava vibhavavyūha lokasthitī- nām — upāsanānuguṇyena kramikasambhavāspadaśrīrāma- śrīkṛṣṇā dibhagavadvibhavalokāvasthāna- vāsudeva saṅkarṣaṇa| dibhagavadvayūha lokā vasthānabhājām, dhātṛṇām - vividhabrahmāṇḍanirvahaṇādhikṛtānāṃ anantāna parameṣṭhinām, ( svalokata evānte paramapadagāminām ), tatsutānām - brahmaputratayā purāṇāva- gatānāṃ marīci - kāśyapādiprajāpatīnām tathā sanaka- nāradādīnāṃ ca, adhikṛti- vigame brahma samprāpsyatām – prāptasvādhikārāvasāne paraṃ brahma prāpnuvānānām, anyeṣāmapi uktetareṣā mumukṣucetanānāmapi sā prasthitiḥ – tatra tatra purāṇetihāsādibhiravagamyamānā mokṣa- prayāṇarītiḥ, - śita- pṛthumatimiḥ – kuśāgravat sūkṣmārthāvagāhanaśīladhīmi:, vividhavilakṣaṇa -- tātvikārthavistārāvagāhibahujñānavadbhiśca prājñaiḥ yathārham – yathāyogyam, ( viṣayagatavāstavā - nurodhi yathā syāt tathā), cintanīyā – niṣkṛṣya ālocayitumardā ; na tu svācchandyena nirṇeyeti || 3 ||

vāyvadhikaraṇam ||4-3-2 ||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ ||
vāyuśca devalokaḥ śabdavibhedāt pṛthag bhavataḥ |
na syāt yo'yaṃ pavate sa devaloka iti tadviśeṣaṇataḥ || 2 ||
vāyuḥ devalokaśca vāyuśabdabodhyaḥ devalokaśabdabodhyaścetīmāvarthau, pṛthak bhavataḥ - mitho vibhinnāveva bhavitumarho śabdavibhedāt-arthabodhakayoḥ tayordvayoḥ śabdayoḥ vibhinnarūpa- svāt (śabdabhedasya arthabhedaniyatatvāt - iti śaṅkane) na syāt — arthayorbhedo naiva bhavitumarhati, 'yo'yaṃ pavate sa devaloka' iti tadviśeṣaṇataḥ – 'yo'rthaḥ vāti tādṛśo devaloka : ' ityevaṃ vāyugatā sādhāraṇadharma viśeṣaṇīkṛtya devalokasvarūpokteḥ sadāgatiśīlatvavānarthaḥ vāyureveti devaloka śabdārthasya niścayāt || 2 ||

varṇitam, tataḥ tasmāt uktāt arthaucityāt idam-atra pradarśitam, nirvikalpam- vikalpadoṣāvakāśarahitam, tadaikyam - vāyudevalokapadārthayoḥ abhinnatvameva, ucitam -

nyāyyam || 4 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||
kauṣītakyāgamoktā varuṇa - śatamakhau so'pi nāthaḥ prajānāṃ
kvā'pi sthāpayā nipīḍya śrutimapi viphalā dhanyathā tacchrutiḥ syāt | tasmāt te vāyulokāt paramanupaṭhanāt tatra santvityayuktaṃ
sambandhādvidyuto'nvaśvaruṇa iti parau pāṭhatastatparau staḥ || 5||
kauṣītakyāgamoktaḥ 'sa varuṇalokaṃ, sa indralokaṃ, sa prajāpatilokam' ityevaṃ kauṣītakiśrutipratipannāḥ, varuṇa - śatamakhau prajānāṃ nāthaḥ so'pi varuṇaḥ indraḥ prajāpatiryaḥ śrutaḥ so'pi śrutiṃ nipīḍyā'pi - anyatrāvagamyamānaṃ śrutisiddhaṃ kramaṃ bādhitvā'pi, kāspi sthāpyāḥ-yatra kutracit sthāne avaśyaṃ niveśyā eva, anyathā - teṣāṃ kvacidapyaniveśe, tacchrutiḥ- teṣāmarcirādāvavasthānabodhikā śrutiḥ, viphalā hi syāt - arthahīnā khalu bhaveta, tasmāt - uktāddhetoḥ, te varuṇendraprajāpatayaḥ, vāyulokāt paramanupaṭhanāt – bāyulokato'nantarameva kramikapāṭhasya satvāt tatra santu - vāyoranantarameva niveśitā bhavantu, ityayuktam - evaṃ śaṅkanam asādhu, sambandhāt– vidyuto meghanaikaṭyarūpāt sambandhāt vidyuto'nva - vi vidyuto'nva-vidyu- to'nantarameva, varuṇaḥ iti – varuṇo niveśya iti hetoḥ, parau - anantaraśrutau indraprajāpatī, pāṭhataḥ - pāṭhakramamanurudhya, tatparau staḥ- varuṇapaścādbhāvināveva bhavataḥ || 5 ||
yattvasyā mānavasya śrutimiha parasamprāpakatvaṃ bhavettat sadvāratve'pyabādhaṃ tadapi sahakṛtau tacchrutaucityabhūmā |
yaśvokto mānasākhyastaṭita upari tu brahmalokā ptihetu-
stasmānnetā sa nā'nyo vidurativahanaṃ vaidyutenaiva tasmāt || 6 ||
amānavasya—vaidyutākhyasya amānavatvaviśiṣṭasya ātivāhikasya parasamprāpakatvam- parabrahmagamayitṛtvam, iha yat śrutam - atra muktapuruṣātivahana prakaraṇe 'tatpuruṣo'mānavaḥ sa enān brahma gamayati' iti śrutivākyato yadavagatam, tat- amānavapuruṣasya brahmagamayitṛtvam, sadvāratve'pi varuṇendra prajāpatidvārā brahmagamayitṛtvepi, abādham - yadyapi naiva bāghitaṃ bhavati ; (amānavapuruṣaprādhānya siddhāvapi gamayitṛtvasya alopāt ) tadapi - tathā'pi, saha- kṛtau — amānavakartṛke brahmagamayitṛtve varuṇādīnāṃ sahakāritvasya siddhau satyām,

tacchrutau- cityabhūmā - prakaraṇapratipannārthasya atiśayitaṃ sāṅgatyamasti; (amānavakartṛkatāyāḥ svārasyāt ) taṭita upari tuvaidyuta puruṣā danantarameva brahmalokāptihetuḥ mānasākhyaḥ ya uktaḥ-

brahmaprāpaṇahetutayā mānasanāmā puruṣaviśeṣaḥ yaḥ 'vaidyutāt puruṣo mānasa etya brahmalokān gamayati' iti śrutyā pratipanna:, tasmāt — tādṛśāt mānasākhyapuruṣāt sa netā – gamayitṛ- svena jñātaḥ amānavaḥ puruṣaḥ, na anyaḥ - atirikto na bhavati (ananya eva; amānava eva mānasaḥ) tasmāt – uktāddhetoḥ, ativahanam – pūrvapūrva sīmāmatikramya uttarottaraṃ muktapuruṣa - prāṇarūpaṃ karma, vaidyutenaiva āhuḥ – vaidyuta puruṣeṇaiveti dṛḍhaṃ varṇayanti śrutyarthacintakāḥ || 6 ||

paṭhitāḥ 'arciḥ, ahaḥ, āpūryamāṇapakṣa : ' ityevamādyā arthāḥ, saraṇe: cihnāni tu — paramapada- prāptyarthaṃ gantavyasyā'dhvanaḥ lakṣmabhūtā artha eva, upadeśasya tathātvāt – upadeśakaiḥ puruṣai: tattanmārgacihnaḥ sardaivopadeśasya kriyamāṇatāyā lokadṛṣṭatvāt, ( ityevaṃ śaṅkane) naitat - naitadyuktam, 'gamayita' śabdāt - 'sa enān brahma gamayati' iti vaidyutaṃ puruṣamuddiśya viduṣā brahmaprāpakatāyāḥ spaṣṭapratipādakaśabdaśravaṇāt upari vaidyuta puruṣānantaraṃ śrūyamāṇāḥ, parespi- vaidyuta puruṣādanye'pi arcirādayaḥ sarve, ātivāhikāḥ hi syuḥ paramapuruṣeṇa muktātmanāmati vaha ne (prapyabrahmapadaprāpaṇe) niyuktāḥ puruṣā eva bhavitumarhanti || 4 ||

-0-

lokokticchāyayaiva sthitam -' imāṃ nadīmatikramya imaṃ ca parva pārśvabhūbhāgamāśritya idaṃ kṣetra labhyaṃ yuṣmābhiḥ' ityevaṃ laukika vyavahāra siddhamārgaviśeṣopadeśapara vākya sājātyenaiva sthitam - śrutāvapi dṛṣṭam iti na evaṃ śaṅkanam na yuktimat kālaśabde - kālābhidhānayobhye ahaḥ pūrvapakṣādiśabde asambhavāt mārgacihnavā citāyāḥ bhomabhūmivācakatāyā vā bādhopahatatvāt, kvacit — vaidyutākhye mānasanāmni amānave, netṛtva-puṃstve – brahmagamayitṛtvaṃ puruṣatvaṃ cetyubhayamapi, spaṣṭe – 'saḥ enān brahma gamayati' iti vākyataḥ asandigdhapratipannam ; tadvat – vaidyuta puruṣeṇeva, anyaiśca – aharādibhirapi ativahanam – (paramātma niyogamanusṛtya ) tatsamīpaprāpaṇam, kāryam - kartavyameva vartate, sandigdhe - aspaṣṭoktatayā saṃśayāspade viṣaye, vākyaśeṣāt - pradhānavākyopa - kārakasvabhāvāt saṃnihitāttadekakā kyatāpanna dvākyāntarāt, gati :- nirvāha: kārya:, iti jaguḥ - ityevaṃ nītivido mīmāṃsakāḥ ūcuḥ 'sandigdhe tu vākyaśeṣāt ' ityatra, agnyādivācaḥ śrutyādipaṭhitāḥ agniśabdaprabhṛtayo hi śabdāḥ, abhimantūn - tattadabhi - mānibhūtān devatāviśeṣāṃścaiva jaguḥ - abhidadhyuḥ || 7 ||

yāṃ śrutvā dharmamūnuyapatadabhipatet tāmasau tāmasaugho vargastraivargikāṇāṃ pitṛsaraṇidhaṭhīyantracakre vighūrṇat | varta yeṣā'rcirādeḥ śrutebhirapunarāvartināṃ saṃvibhaktā

tatra brūte'tiboḍhuṃstaditaravadaha : pakṣamāsādiśabdaḥ || 8 ||

dharmapūnuḥ dharmanandano yudhiṣṭhiraḥ, yām – yādṛśīṃ kevala pāpakāribhirgamyāṃ nāraka vartanīm śrutvā nyapatat — śrotrataḥ śravaṇaṃ prāpyaiva (śravaṇamātreṇaiva dukhe + prāptamūrccha: san ) bhūmau nipatitavān, tām tādṛśīma cintanīyaghoratamā nārakasaraṇim, asau tāmasaudhaḥ - andha- tāmasayogyaḥ mahātāmasasvabhāvo janastomaḥ, abhipatet - ābhimukhyena anudhāvet (svakarma- vātyā nunnaḥ), traivarṇikāṇāṃ varga : aihika - pāratrikā khya vividhabhogatadanuguṇaiśvarya tadyogyamthā- nādyābhilāṣukatayā dharmācaraṇapravṛttānāṃ (kevalaṃ kāmyasaktānāṃ ) cetanānāṃ samūhaḥ paraṃ, pitṛsaraṇi- ghaṭīyantracakre – jaloddhārakaghaṭīyantravat cakrākāreṇa punaḥ punarāvartana pratyāvartanābhyāṃ avirata- akāriṇi pitṛvānākhye, vighūrṇet - svānadhīnapravṛttimattayā sadā bhramyet eṣā arciradiḥ vartanī - 'te'cimabhisambhavanti, arciṣo'ha:' ityādirūpā acirādyabhidhānena prathitā saraṇiḥ punaḥ, śrutibhiḥ — trayyantavāgbhiḥ, apunarāvartināṃ saṃvibhaktā janmādicakre āvartana pratyāvartana- vidūrīkaraṇasamarthabhakti prapattirūpopāyasiddhānāmeva cenanānāṃ saṃvibhajya varṇitā, tatra - tāha śarta -

nyantaḥ praviṣṭārthabodhanaparaḥ, ahaḥ pakṣamāsādiśabdaḥ - ( ahaḥ ) dina- pūrvapakṣa - udagayanaṣaṇmāsā- dika: śabda:, taditaravat - vaidyutāmānavādiśabdavat, ativoḍhan – parabrahmapadaprāpakān tatta- -dabhimānidevatāviśeṣāneva, brūte svavṛttyā abhidhatte || 8 ||

pūrva dhūmādimārge sukṛtiṣu kathitā candramaḥ prāptiranyā

sāyujyaṃ nyāsavidyāprakaraṇapaṭhitaṃ cāndramanyā dṛguktam | anyā'trā'rcimukhānāmativahanakṛtā maṣṭamasyā''ptirindo-
ritthaṃ susthā vyavasthā praṇihitahṛdayai revamanyacca sūham || 9 ||

pūrvam – iṣṭādikāriṇāṃ gativarṇanapare prakaraṇe, sukṛtiṣu – iṣṭāpūrta- dattādipuṇyakarmācaraṇa- paravacetanaviṣaye, dhūmādimārge kathitā - 'te dhūmamabhisambhavanti' ityārabhya 'ākāśā- candramasam' ityevamādinā varṇitā, candramaḥ prāptiḥ– candreṇa samāgatiḥ, anyā - vilakṣaṇā, (tasyā devabhogya somarāja bhāvātmakatvāt, nyāsa vidyāprakaraṇapaṭhitam – 'tasmānnyā sameṣāṃ tapasā matiriktamāhuḥ' ityevaṃ prastute prakaraṇe adhītam cāndraṃ sāyujyam - candrasayugbhāva- prāpaṇam, anyādṛg – pūrvoktacandramaḥ prātyapekṣayā vilakṣaṇam uktam– 'atha yo dakṣiṇe pramīyate pitṛṇāmeva mahimānaṃ gatvā candramasaḥ sāyujyaṃ gacchati' iti pratipāditaṃ vartate ( tacca viduṣaḥ brahmamārge kiñcitkālaṃ viśramya yānaupayikam ) atra - chandogyīye 'te'rciṣamami- sambhavanti' ityādivākyamadhye, indoḥ prāptiḥ - ' ādityāccandramasam' ityuktā candropalabdhi:, arcirmukhānām ativahanakṛtām aṣṭamasya indoḥ prāptiḥ - arciḥ prabhṛtyātivāhikagoṣṭhī- praviṣṭasya teṣvaṣṭamasya ātivāhikabhūtasya candrasya prāptirūpā satī, anyā - visadṛśī vartate, ittham - evaṃ -rītyā, susthā vyavasthā - dṛḍhā viviktā'vasthitiḥ (candraprāptisambandhinī), evam- - anayaiva rītyā, anyacca – tatra tatra asaṅkīrṇatayā śrutyā pratipāditamapi saṅkīrṇatvaśaṅkāvahaṃ viṣayajātam, praṇihitahṛdayaiḥ suham – sāvadhānacetobhiḥ suṣṭhu ūhato niśceyam || 9 ||

.8

bhūlokeśābhipūrvān katicana bhavinaḥ ke'pi manyanta etā- chabdaikye hyatra gurvī bhavati tadabhimantrantarāṇāṃ praklaptiḥ |
anye ca mānavasya svapadagataparabrahma netṛtvadṛṣṭyā

nityatvaṃ tadvadetānnijagadura khilān nityavaikuṇṭhabhṛtyān || 10 ||

kespi - śrutyartha paryālocanapravṛttāḥ kecana vibudhāḥ, etān - arcirādīnātivāhikān, bhūlokeśāgnipūrvān pṛthivyantarikṣa vyākhyalokatrayādhipatibhūtānivāyvādityāneva katicana bhavinaḥ– kāṃścana saṃsāramaṇḍalāntargatāneva, manyante śrutyuktānabhiprayanti, ( etanmate) śabdekye hi bodhakaśabdasya samānānupūrvīkatvakṛte abhede sphuṭe sati hi atra - agnyādi - viṣaye, tadabhimantrantarāṇāṃ praklaptiḥ sāṃsārikāṇāṃ taditareṣāṃ ceti abhimāni devatā bhedānāṃ kalpanā param, gurvī bhavati — gauravagrastā bhavati ( ityetadavivādam ), anye ca - apare ca śrutitattvārtha cintanapravaṇāḥ, amānavasya – amānavākhyasyātivahika puruṣasya, svapadagata parabrahma- netṛtvadṛṣṭayā - paramavyomagataparamātmaprāpakatvasya śrutau darśanena nityatvam - asaṃsāritvasahakṛtaṃ nityasūritvaṃ tāvadavarjanīyam, tadvat - amānavapuruṣavadeva, etānakhilān– arcirādīn sarvānapi, nityavaikuṇṭhabhṛtyān - nirvikāraṃ yadvaikuṇṭhākhyaṃ nākaṃ pareṇa virājamānaṃ sthānam tatrasthasya vaikuṇṭhākhyasya paramapuruṣasya nityabhṛtyān asaṃsārisvabhāvāneva nijagaduḥ – niṣkṛṣya khacitaṃ varṇayāmāsuḥ || 10 ||

kāryādhikaraṇam ||4-3-5||
śārīrakanyā ya kalāpasaṃgrahaḥ ||
caturmukhamupāsiturgatiriyaṃ sahi prāpyate
parasya paripūrṇataḥ satatameva samprāptiḥ |
na tatparamupāsituḥ śrutita eva taccā'pyasat
paraṃ tadanujīvamapyubhayataḥ śruterdarśanāt || 5 ||

iyaṃ gatiḥ—arcirādikā gatiḥ, caturmukhamupāsituḥ kāryabrahmabhūtaṃ hiraṇyagarbhamu pāsīnasyaiva śrutisaṃmatā, hi yataḥ, saḥ prāpyate hiraṇyagarbharūpo brahmaiva khalu pūrvamaprāptaḥ san prāptimarhati (parimitadeśavarttitvāt ) parasya – parabrahmarūpasya nityasya brahmaṇaḥ, paripūrṇata:- sarvatra paripūrṇatvena hetunā, satatameva sarvadā sarvatraiva ca samprāptitaḥ - prāpteḥ svayameva siddhatvena nūnnatayā gatipūrvakaṃ prāpyatvāsambhavāt na-nedaṃ sādhu tat — arcirādigatyā gamanamityetat paramupāsituḥ—paraṃ brahmopāsīnasyaiveti yat, taccāpi – ubhayamapi asat - asādhu, śrutita eva - śrutibalādeva, param – ( kintu ) ' paraṃ brahmopāsīnam', tadanujīvamapi - 'paramātmātmakaṃ prakṛtiviyuktaṃ svātmānamupāsīnamapi ca arcirādikayā gatyā ātivāhikagaṇo nayatī 'tyetatsādhu, ubhayataḥ śruterdarśanāt - ' tadya itthaṃ viduḥ ' iti, 'ye ceme'raṇye śraddhā tapa ityupāsate ' iti cadvidhāpyullikhya, 'arciṣamabhisambhavanti' iti arcira digatiśrutidarśanāt || 5 ||

.

upāsyabhūtaṃ brahmavastu, atra – svasyādhārabhūte sthāne, na khalu na labhyam - aprāpyaṃ naiva bhavati khalu, (sarvagatasya vastunaḥ sarvairnityaprāptatvāt ), sarasijavasatiḥ – kamalāsanaścaturmukhaḥ, sāmīpyācca brahmaṇaḥ prathamajatvena hetunā tadatyantanai kaṭayaṃ nimittīkṛtya, brahma uktaḥ- ' sa enān brahma gamayatī 'tyatra svakāraṇabhūtabrahmatvena nirdiṣṭaḥ muktiḥ - ( kāryabrahmopāsakānāṃ ) sarvāvidyā nivṛttyā apunarāvṛttirūpā mukti:, tena sārdhaṃ ca svopāsitena svaprāptena ca hiraṇyagarbheṇa sahaiva yuktā - ' brahmaṇā saha te sarve' ityādi mṛtyavagatatvāt prāmāṇyayuktā, ityetat na – itīdṛśaṃ bādarermatam na sādhutāṃ bhajate, nānāśruti... dyabādhāt – bahu- śrutyāmnātasya paramapadākhyasya brahmasthānasya tatsthānagamanasya ca pramāṇasaṃ pratipannasya bādhā- yogāt || 11 ||

mārgo'sāvarcirādirgamayati paramaṃ dhāma gatyādi yogād
brahmoktermukhyabhāvāditi tu niraṇayajjaiminiyuktametat |

vaktavyaṃ tvatra kiṃcinnayati paravidaḥ svānvitabrahmaniṣṭhān taddehasvātmaniṣṭhānapi mukhabhidayā'mī ca sampūrṇa niṣṭhāḥ || 12 || arcirādiḥ mārgaḥ– arciḥ prabhṛtyātivāha kayukto brahmamārgaḥ, gatyādiyogāt – brahmaprātyarthagamana brahmaprāyāderviṣayasya abādhitatvāt, (tathā) brahmokteḥ mukhyabhāvāt — brahmaśabdavyapadeśasya (brahma gamayatī 'ti ) anaupacārikatvalābhāt paramaṃ dhāma- parabrahmasthānameva, gamayati – prāpayati, iti tu ityeva jaiminiḥ niraṇayat — jaiminināmā ācāryo nirdhāritavān yuktametat – jaimininā nirṇītaṃ nyāyyameva atra vaktavyaṃ tu. kiṃcit-asmin prastāve kathanīyaṃ kiñcidasti, (kimiti cet ) para vidaḥ - parabrahmopāsakān, svānvitatrahmaniṣṭhān – svātmaśarīrakaparamātmopāsakān, taddehasvātmaniṣṭhānapi — paramātmaśarīra- bhūna svo ( pratyagātmo) pāsakānapi (iti dvividhānapi ), nayati – paramaṃ dhāma prāpayatyātivāhiko gaṇaḥ, amī ca – uktā dvividhā apyupāsakāḥ mukhabhidayā - paramātma viśeṣyakatva-svātma- viśeṣya katvarūpamukhabhedataḥ, sampūrṇa niṣṭhāḥ kṛtsnopāsakā eva (ātmopāsakatvākṣateḥ ) na tu ' akṛtsno hyeṣa : ' ityuktā'kṛtsnopāsakā iti || 12 ||

asau prastutaḥ,

dhyāyeyurye ca jīvān prakṛtiśabalitān kevalān vā yatheṣṭaṃ
dveṣā'pi brahmadṛṣṭyā jaḍanivahamapi svena yadvā'nyadṛṣṭyā |
te sarve hi pratīpraṇihitamanaso nā'cirādyadhvayogyā

brahmopāsteśca liṅgaṃ gatiriyamanaghā sūtritā pūrvameva || 13 ||

ye ca ye punarupā sakāḥ, prakṛtiśaba litān - acinmiśran vā, kevalān vā- śuddha'n vā, jīvān — cidrūpānarthān, yatheṣṭam – svasyābhilāṣānuguṇam, dhyāyeyuḥ – upāsīran dvedhā'pi - prakṛtiścalitān kebalāneva vā tāneva jīvān brahmadṛṣṭayā - adhyasta brahmabhāvena, ye ca dhyāyeyuḥ – ye vā upāsīran, (tathaiva) jaḍanivahamapi_acetanavargamapi vā, svena— svarūpeṇa vā yadvā anyadṛṣṭayā dharmyantarādhyāsato vā ye upāsīran, te sarve – uktavidhāḥ

yataḥ, pratīkapraṇihitamanasaḥ – śarīrabhūtārthopāsanā saktacetasaḥ, arcirādyā tivāhika yuktabrahmapathayānāha na bhavanti, anaghā agha

ṣaṭprakārā upāsakā api hi
ataḥ acirādyadhvayogyāḥ na

(pāpa) sambandhavidūrā, iyaṃ gatiḥ - arcirādipathe yātrā, brahmopāsteśva – nirantarānuṣṭhitāyāḥ brahmopāsanakriyāyā eva liṅgam – avyabhicaritaṃ jñāpakamiti, śrutopaniṣatkagatyabhidhānācce 'ti sūtrataḥ spaṣṭīkṛtā || 13 ||

pūrvameva sūtritā - prāgeva

pratyāpattiprakāraḥ pratiniyatimatī karmayogādiniṣṭhā vidyābhedādhikāraḥ parabhajana balānmukti vighnopaśāntiḥ | antyāvastha vyavasthā pṛthagayanagatau viśramāptirvibhaktā

sākṣānmuktiḥ kramādvetyakhilamagati kara praṇodyaṃ paraiśca || 14 ||

paraiśca–siddhāntāntarālambibhi: (advaita mataniṣṭhaira pi), svabhāvasiddhe avidyāvinirmukte'vasthāviśeṣe icchayā) mokṣopāye pravṛttiḥ, pratiniyatimatī- varṇāśramādivyavasthānurodhinī, karmayogādiniṣṭha karmayoge jñānayoge vā tattaditi kartavyatā- kalāpayuktā acālyā anuṣṭhānapravṛttiḥ, vidyābheda viduṣo maraṇaviṣaye aho rātri dakṣiṇāyanottarāyaṇādivyavasthā- viśeṣasyā'bhāvaḥ, pṛthagayanagatau viśramāptiḥ – udagayane taditarasmin dakṣiṇe'yane vā gatau ( mṛtau ) jātāyāṃ āditye candramasi ca āntarālika viśrāntilābhaḥ, sākṣāt kramāt iti vibhaktā muktirvā - ( advārakatayā parabrahmopāsakānāṃ sadvidyādividyā viśeṣaniṣṭhānāṃ ) sākṣāt mukti:, (madhuvidyārūpa viśeṣaniṣṭhānāṃ vasvādipadaprāptipūrvikā ) kramikī muktirvā, ityakhilam- ityetat sarvamapi viṣayajātam, agatikaiḥ – nirvāhakāntararahitaiḥ sadbhiḥ, apraṇodyam —

pratyāpattiprakāraḥ - ( jīva sya

vedāvagatatvaṃ nimittīkṛtya anirasanīyatayā kathañcit avaśyābhyupagantavyamāste | (paraṃ tatraiva śāstrapratipanne'rthe vyāvahārikatva - pāramārthikatvādikaṃ svaireva kalpitaṃ sat teṣāṃ vailakṣaṇyakaramiva bhāsate || 14 ||
panthānaṃ devayānaṃ tamimamadhigataḥ paścime dehapāte

tavastomāṃsta montānatipatati taratyāpagāṃ tatra divyām | divyaṃ dehādi labdhvā janilayarahitaṃ yāti viṣṇoḥ padaṃ tat
paryaṅkārohaṇāntāṃ bhajati bahumatiṃ brahmasaṃvādadhanyaḥ || 15||

paścime dehāte -carame dehāvasāne, tamimam – etāvaddūraṃ prastutya varṇitam, devaya naṃ panthānam — devayānābhidhānena śrutisiddhaṃ brahmamārgam, adhigataḥ prāptavāṃścetanaḥ, tamontān tavastomān –' pṛthivyapsu pralīyate' ityārabhya ' akṣaraṃ tamasi līyate, tamaḥ pare deva ekībhavati' ityuktavidhayā pṛthivyāditamaḥ paryantān vyaṣṭi samaṣṭitattvasaṅghātān svabandhakatayā anādipravṛttān sarvānapi, atipatati — atilaṅghayordhvaṃ gacchati, tatra — paramapadasīnni, divyām āpagām – daivīṃ saritaṃ virajākhyām, tarati - (tatra snānataḥ nirmuktasamastaprakṛtisaṅgaḥ san ) tāmatyeti, divyaṃ dehādi labdhvā – paramapadāsādhāraṇaṃ tanukaraṇādikaṃ prāpya, janilayarahitam - utpattivināśāsaṃspṛṣṭam, tat – avāṅmanasagocarajñānādiṣāḍguṇyamayam vācaḥ paramam, viṣṇoḥ padam – jagadādipūruṣasya mahāviṣṇordhāma, yāti prāpnoti, paryaṅkārohaṇāntām - bhagavatparyaṅkā- rohaṇādhyāvasānām, bahumatim - brahmasaṃmānasampattim bhajati – labhate brahmasaṃvādadhanyaḥ- parabrahmabhūtena bhagavatā sahāmṛtamayena vārtālāpena etadantamalabdhvālabdhena dhanyapadābhidheyaśca bhavati || 15 ||

saṅghāte nityatā na kvacidapi na pṛthivyādibhāvastathātve

paryaṅka dhairna sādhyaṃ kimapi bhagavataḥ pūrṇaniḥ sīmaśakteḥ |
vyāptaprāptyarthamūrdhvaṃ gatirapi viphaletyādikān haitukānāṃ

kṣodiṣṭhakṣība jalpān śrutirupaśamayedadbhutārthasvatantrā || 16 ||

saṅghāte – (nitya) vaikuṇṭha bhyupagantṛbhiḥ avaśyamaṅgīkārye divye bhūtasaṅghāte (avayavānāṃ stome), kvacidapi kvacidapi yatra kutrā'pi nityatā na- avināśimvarūpatā naivā'bhyupetumarhā, ( avayavasaṃyogasya kriyāsādhyasya vināśāvaśyaṃbhāvāt nāśatvāvacchinnaṃ prati janyabhāvatvasya prayojakatvāt ) tathātve – yadi saṅghāte nityatvamabhyupagamyeta tarhi, pṛthivyādibhāvaḥ na--

utpattivināśāvinābhāvipṛthivītvādikaṃ naiva yujyeta, (tathaiva ) pūrṇaniḥsīmaśakteḥ sarvatra pūrṇasya niravadhikaśaktisampannasya ca, bhagavataḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ paryaṅkādyaiḥ – (apūrṇānāmalpaśaktīnāṃ ca bhogādhānasvābhāvyavadbhiḥ) paryaṅkakhaṭvātalpaprabhṛtibhiḥ sāmāgrīviśeṣaiḥ (viśiṣṭādvaiti saṃmataiḥ), sādhyaṃ kimapi na–sampādyaṃ yatkicidapi phalaṃ naiva sambhavati, vyāptaprā tyartham - sarvavyāptasya tasya prāptaye, ūrdhvaṃ gatirapi — arcirādikayā saraṇyā uccairgamanamapi, viphalā – niṣprayojanameva, ityādikān — evamādirūpeṇa svacchandaṃ pravartamānān, haitukānāṃ kṣodiṣṭhakṣīvajalpān- kautaskutapravṛttīnāṃ kṣudratamān mattapralāpānukāriṇo vārtālāpān, adbhutārthasvatantrā - āścarya- tamāpūrvārthapratipādane parānapekṣasvayamprāmāṇyavatī, śrutiḥ - vedavākū, upaśamayet — niravaśeṣī- kuryāt, (vedaprāmāṇyasya sarvasvatantrasya cintane kriyamāṇe, kevalayuktyā bhāsaviruddhatvena nimittatāmāpāditena tadarthākṣepaṇaṃ naiva jāyeta keṣāmapīti ) || 16 || bhāvo'bhāvaśca yatrā'nupadhi samuditau tatra bhāvīvirodhaḥ prajñātavyāptirodhe'pyadhika nijabalānmānataḥ svārthasiddhiḥ |
dṛṣṭaṃ sarveṣṭametanna yadi na hi bhavenmānamadhyakṣato'nyad

bādho'dhyakṣe mitho vā vaṭadalaśayanādyadbhutaṃ caivamuhyam || 17 ||

anapahvanīyaiveti nityavibhūtyādayo naiva nirāsārthā bhavanti ||) dṛṣṭam - loke'pi viṣayaviśeṣe anubhūtam, sarveṣṭam — sarvairapi tatra tatra abhyugataṃ ca etat — sāmānyapramāṇabhūtasya pramāṇaviśeṣeṇa viṣaya viśeṣāvagāhinā tadviṣaye abhāsīkaraṇamityetat na yadi - nitya vibhūtyā diviṣayake śrutirūpe prāṇenābhyupagamyeta yadi tadā, adhyakṣato'nyat — pratyakṣetarat yatkimapi āpta- vākyādikam, na hi mānaṃ bhavet — pramāṇaṃ naiva bhavet, adhyakṣe bādhaḥ – pratyakṣetarasyāpta- vākyāderaprāmāṇye tanmūlabhūte pratyakṣe'pi bādhitatvamavarjanīyaṃ syāta, mitho vā bādhaḥ- vyāptimūlakānumānāptavākyayorubhayorapi vā parasparaṃ bādhaḥ syāt, vaṭadalaśayanādi - anantā- nanta brahmāṇḍāni svodare nigīrya saṃsthāpya vaṭapatre śayanādirūpam, adbhutaṃ ca-bhagavataḥ āścaryacaritamapi itihāsa-purāṇādiprasiddham, evamūhyam – anayaiva rītyā śāstra kavedye viṣaye - pratyakṣānumānādīnāṃ pratirodhakatvasambhavaṃ vicārya sādhu nirṇeyam || 17 ||

caturthādhyāyīyatṛtīyapādagatādhikaraṇānām arthasaṅkṣepaḥ
śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ ||
arcigadestu niyamo vāyoḥ svargasya caikatā | taṭito varuṇaprāptiśativāhikatā tataḥ || 6 || phalaṃ jīva paropāstyoracirādigatistviyam |
iti sarvamanenaiva pādena paricintitam || 7 ||

1) acirādestu niyamaḥ - brahmaprāptiyogyānāṃ viduṣāṃ caramaśarīrāvasāne dehotka- maṇānantaraṃ arciḥ prabhṛtyā tivāhikagaṇayutasyaiva vartmano brahmamārgatvam ; nānyasyeti niyamaḥ, 2) vāyoḥ svargasya ca ekatā śrutau kvacit vāyuśabdena, kvacit devaloka (svarga) śabdena coktasyā'rthasya abhedaḥ, 3) taṭito varuṇaprāptiḥ - vidyato'nantarameva varuṇasya niveśaḥ, 4) ātivāhikatā tataḥ – madhyemārga nirdiṣṭānām arcirdinādīnāṃ viduṣo brahmapadaprāpakatva- rūpam ativoddhṛtvameva ; na tu mārgacihnatvam, na vā bhogabhūmitvam, 5 ) iyam arcirādi- gatiḥ jīva- paropāstyoḥ phalam - ukta arcirādisaraṇiḥ paramātmaśarīratayā jīvopāsanasya śuddhasya paramātmana upāsanasyaiva phalatayā labhyam ; na tu kārya - pratīkādyapāsanasya, iti sarvam- evaṃrūpamarthapañcakam anena pādenaiva vivṛtena turīyādhyāyīyatṛtīya caraṇena, cintitam — vicārya sapramāṇaṃ nirdhāritam || 6, 7 ||

bāyau syāt devalokaśrutiḥ - arcirādisaraṇau paripaṭhite vāyurūpe'rthe eva vācakatayoktaḥ śrutisthaḥ devalokaśabdo bhavet, vidyuto'dhi- tanmārgasthāt vidyudrapādarthāduparyeva, varuṇādyanvayaḥ- varuṇendraprajāpatīnāṃ niveśyatvenānvayaḥ, amānavāntāḥ acirādyāḥ amānava puruṣaparyantāḥ sarve ( madhyemārga śrutāḥ ), parapadagamane netāraḥ - parasya brahmaṇaḥ sthānaṃ prati prayāṇe kārye viduṣāṃ gamayitāraḥ — atiboḍhāraḥ, so'yam adhvā - evaṃ prakrānto'rcirādipathaḥ, tadvidām- paramātmopāsakānāṃ gantavyo brahmapatha: ; na pratīka dṛṣṭyādyupāsakānām

śratyuktāḥ

ittham - anena

prakāreṇa, turyādhyāyasya tṛtīye pāde – caturthādhyāyagate tārtīyī ke caraṇe, pañcasaṅkhyairadhikaraṇaiḥ, sṛtiḥ uktā - viduṣāṃ brahmagatiḥ cintitā vartate || 18

adhikaraṇaiḥ -

||

caturthaḥ phala (mukti) pādaḥ || 4-4 || sampadyāvirbhāvādhikaraṇam || 4-4-1 || śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṃgrahaḥ ||
nityopalabdhera sukhatvataśca kenā'pi rūpeṇa sukhaikabhājā |

muktasya sambandha iti tvasat syāt ' svene 'ti śabdādatirohitatvāt || 1 ||

nityopalabdheḥ svaṃ prati svātmanaḥ sadaiva labdhatvāt asukhatvataśca – svasya svānubhava - kāle ānandarūpatāyā aviṣayatvāt, , muktasya-saṃsāranigaḍāt vimocanaṃ prāptavato jīvasya, sukhaikabhājā kenāpi ākāreṇa – ānandekāspadena yena kenacit svīyabhinnena ākāraviśeṣeṇa sākam sambandhaḥ muktidaśākālīnaḥ anvaya eva ; na tu svarūpalābhātmakaḥ saḥ, iti tu — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ tu, asat syāt — asādhveva bhavet, atirohitatvāt — bandhāvasthā kā līnatirohita- tvavirodhyatirohitatvāvasthāṃ puraskṛtya sveneti śabdāt — guṇāṣṭake dharmabhūtajñānasaṅkoce ca ātma- svarūpe 'svene’ti viśeṣaṇaśabdaprayogāt svīyātiriktākārānvayasya śrutyanabhimatatvāt 'svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate' ityevaṃrūpāt chāṃdogyaśrutigatāt spaṣṭavacanāt jñāpakāt atirohitatvāt- saṃsārakāle (muktaḥ pūrva) tirohitasya ānandaikarūpasvarūpasya muktau tirodhānanivṛttimātrasyaiva śrutyarthatvāt || 1 ||

nirdhūtopādhirāśeḥ – nivṛttakarmādinikhilopādhijātasya cetanasya, nirupadhika mahānandam- sarvopādhisambandhavidhuram aparimitamānandam (muktātmano bhāvinam ) antyena – caturthenānena phala 'mukti) pādena, vakti-sūtrakṛt nirūpayati, atra tribhiradhikaraṇaiḥ asminnapi pāde sampadyāvirbhāva avibhāgena dṛṣṭatva-brāhmāghikaraṇarūpaiḥ tribhirnyāyaiḥ, svāvirbhāvaḥ svena rūpeṇā- bhiniṣpadyate ityuktaḥ, svasvarūpasya muktau tirodhānavimokena prakāśaḥ, cintyaḥ – vicārya nirṇeyaḥ tribhiḥ tatpaścāttanaiḥ saṅkalpa abhāva- jagadvyāpāravarjākhyaiḥ tribhiradhikaraṇaiḥ, chanda- vṛttiśca - 'sa svarāḍ bhavati, sarveṣu lokeṣu kāmacāro bhavati' ityuktamokṣakālikasvacchandavartana (bhagavadicchādhīna svecchānuguṇavartanam ), cintyaṃ vartate, ityevaṃ dve peṭike staḥ uktādhikaraṇa- trayadvikarūpeṇa peṭike dvidhā vibhakte vartete, svataḥ - ātmasvarūpānubandhi, idamubhayam - svarūpā- virbhāva sva saṅkalpānuguṇāpratihatasañcaraṇarūpamidaṃ dvayamapi na upadheḥ upādhinibandhanaṃ na bhavati, nāthanighnam - śaraṇyabhūtasya paramātmanaḥ satyasaṅkalpāyattameva || 1 ||

paravākye, aphalo bhavet —– vyartho bhavet, rūpavācā – 'svena' śabdarahitenā'pi kevalenā pi rūpaśabdena, arthasiddheḥ – mūrtiviśeṣarūpasyārthasya lābhāt || 2 ||

nityasvātmasvarūpasthitiriyamabhiniṣpattirityacivāṃsa-
stanmātraṃ syānna vidyāphalamiti na vidurnityasiddhaṃ tvasādhyam | siddhaṃ sādhyānuvedhādapi bhavati phalaṃ sātvavidyādibhaṅgo
yadvā siddhasya vittistadiha na vihataṃ mukta ityādisūtram || 3 ||
itham abhiniṣpattiḥ—— paraṃjyotirupasampadya svena rūpeṇā'bhiniṣpadyate ' ityuktā mokṣakālikī svarūpābhiniṣpattiḥ, nitya... sthitiriti - anādisiddhaṃ yat jīvātmanaḥ svasvarūpaṃ tena rūpeṇa avasthānamiti, ūcivāṃsaḥ vandataḥ advaitinaḥ, tanmātram — anādi- siddhasvarūpataḥ avasthānamātram, vidyāphalaṃ na syāditi - vedāntavihitamokṣopāyānuṣṭhānasya phalaṃ na bhavitumaītīti, na viduḥ naiva jānīyuḥ, nityasiddhaṃ tu - anādisiddhaṃ param, asādhyam—sādhyabhūtenā'rthena sākaṃ tādātmyaṃ prātumarham, (siddha-sādhyayorabhedā yogāt mithovyāhateḥ) siddhamapi –pūrva siddhamapi vastu, sādhyānuvedhāt — sādhyāṃśasya tatrānupraveśāt (viśiṣṭaveṣasya bhedāt ) phalaṃ bhavatyapi - prayojanatāpannaṃ (sādhyatāpannaṃ) bhavatyeva, sā tu - sādhyarūpatāṃ prāptā abhiniṣpattistu, avidyādibhaṅgaḥ - avidyāyā karmavāsanāyāḥ prakṛti-- sambandhasya (sthūla-sūkṣmātmanā dvividhāyā prakṛterapi sambandhasya) praṇāśaḥ eva nityātmarūpa- viśeṣaṇabhūtaḥ, yadvā- athavā, siddhasya vittiḥ - anādisiddhasyāpyātmasvarūpasya apahṛta- pāpmatvādimataḥ jñaptireva abhiniṣpattiḥ, tat — tasmāddhetoḥ, iha–asmin prakaraṇe, mukta ityādisūtram -'muktaḥ pratijñānā 'dityādikaṃ sūtram, na vihatam - na viphalatā prāptam || (muktayaṃśasya jīvaviśeṣaṇa bhūtasya apūrvatayā sādhyatvānvayāt muktiviśiṣṭaveṣeṇa anāderapyātmanaḥ sādhyatvasyā virodhāditi) || 3 ||

svākārānnityabhātādadhikasukhatayā bhāti jīvastadānīṃ
siddhāvirbhāvamātre'pyanuvidurabhiniṣpattivācaḥ prayogam | saṅkocātyantahānirdhiya iyamabhiniṣpattirāganturuktā
niṣpratyūhasvarūpopadhikamapi tadā 'svena 'śabdaḥ pravakti || 4 ||
jīvaḥ — pratyagātmabhūtaścetanaḥ, tadānīm - mokṣakāle, nityabhātāt svākārāt-

sadā bhāsamānāt pratyaktvānukūlyādeḥ svasvarūpasiddhāt ākārāt, (ākārāpekṣayā) adhika-

sukhatayā - atiśayitānukūlatvena (ānandamātrarūpatayā ), bhāti svasmai prakāśate, siddhā- virbhāvamātre'pi pūrvasiddhasyā'pi anāvirbhūtasyārthasya āvirbhāve (tirodhāna vigame ) 'pi viṣaye, abhiniṣpattivācaḥ–abhiniṣpattiśabdasya prayogam — vyavahāram, anuviduḥ – anujānanti prayoktāraḥ ('anayā yuktyā ayamartho'bhiniṣpadyate ' ityādau ) iyam abhiniṣpattiḥ – 'svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyate' iti śrutivākyapratipannā abhiniṣpattiḥ, dhiyaḥ saṅkocātyantahāniḥ - jīvātmano dharmabhūtasya jñānasya saṃsārakāle prāptaḥ yaḥ saṅkocaḥ tasya niravaśeṣāpagamarūpā satī (asaṅkucitajñānatvāvasthā), āganturuktā - saṃsaraṇakāle avidyamānā mokṣakāle eva apūrva - tayā prāptā, (atastasyāḥ sādhyarūpatvam) tadā - mokṣakāle, niṣpratyūhamvarūpopadhikamapi - avidyādyantarāyarahitaśuddha jīvātmasvarūpāyattamapi jñānāvirbhāvam (anyūnatayā jñānānandādyākāra- vaiśiṣṭyena svasvarūpasya svīyadharmabhūta jñānena grahaṇamapi, svenaśabdaḥ - 'svena rūpeṇā'bhiniṣpadyate ' iti vākyasthaḥ svenetyayaṃ śabdaḥ pravakti - spaṣṭaṃ brate || 4 ||

prāyaḥ svasmin svadehinyanaghaguṇanidhau tadvibhūtidvaye vā

dṛṣṭaṃ yat syāt samādhau bhavati pṛṣatavattaddhi muktānubhūteḥ | kṛtsnaṃ tacchaka raughatryatikaritasudhā sindhuvat svādubhūtaṃ
tasmāt svātmaprakāśe na phalamiti vadan kūpakūrmo ghṛṇāīḥ || 5 ||

svasmin —sarvopādhivinirmukte śuddhe svasvarūpe, anaghaguṇanidhau – nitya nirdoṣagandha- nikhila kalyāṇaguṇā kare, svadehini svaṃ prati nirupādhikātmabhūte paramātmani tadvibhūtidvaye vā – nitya vibhūti līlā vibhūtyākhye paramātmano ubhayavidhāyāṃ vibhūtāvapi vā viṣaye, prāyaḥ - bāhulyena samādhau – bahukāla nirantaradhyānasampādya samādhyākhyāvasthāviśeṣe yaddṛṣṭaṃ syāt - yadyadanubhūtaṃ bhavet, tat — samādhyanubhūtaṃ sarvam, muktānubhūteḥ – muktātmakartṛkā nubhavasāgaramadhye, pṛṣatavat bhavati — ekajalabindusadṛśaṃ vartate | tat kṛtsnam - muktidaśānubhava viṣayabhūtaṃ sarva- mapi militam, (jīvasvarūpa paramātmasvarūpa tadguṇastoma - vibhūtidvayātmakaṃ sarvam ) śarkaraugha- vyatikaritasudhā sindhuvat – śarkarā stoma saṃmiśritāmṛtasāgara iva svādubhūtam - atyantasukhā- svādyarūpaṃ vijṛmbhate, tasmāt - uktāddhetu jātāt, svātmaprakāśe na phalamiti vadan- muktikāle svasvarūpasyā''virbhāve sati na kimapi prayojanamasti iti ajñānataḥ pralapan janaḥ, kūpakūrma:- atyantasaṅkucitajalādhāragataḥ kamaṭha iva vidyamāna iti hetoḥ, ghṛṇāī: kevalamanukampāyā eba -pātrabhūta iti, na vicārāderahaḥ saḥ || 5 ||

mallī śuddhaṃ prasūnaṃ janayati samaye rañjakopādhimukte tadvannāthānukampā phalamupaśamitāvagrahaṃ sūritulyam | tasmādāgantuke'pi pratiitivirahāt tādṛśe dhīvikā se

pratyāpannasvadāyakrama iha kathitaḥ prāptasiddheḥ pratīcaḥ || 6 ||

mallī – mallīkākhyā · latā, rañjakopādhimukte samaye – lauhityādhāyaka kiṃśukādirūpo- pādhitaḥ vimokavati kāle, śuddha prasūnam – śuklavarṇameva kusumam janayati - utpādayati,

(śuklavarṇa kusumotpādanaṃ mallīlatāyāḥ svabhāvata eva siddhamapi upādhivaśāt madhye prāptaviccheda- mapi, upādherapagame sati svābhāvikameva punaḥ pravartate) tadvat — tathaiva, nāthānukampā – sarvaloka- śaraṇyasya bhagavataḥ karuṇā, upaśamitāvagraham — nirmūlitapratyūham, (nirvighnatā māpāditam ) sūritulyaṃ phalam - anantagaruḍādinitya sūribhogyaphalam rañjakopādhimukte samaye – jīvasya sāṃsārikaphalāsaktijanakā vidyākāmakarmādyupādhivigamavati kāle, janayati- āvirbhāvayati, tasmāt - uktāddhetoḥ, dhīvikāse - asaṅkocena dharmabhūtajñāna vikāse muktikā like, āgantuke'pi - pūrvamavidyamānatvena paścāt kālaviśeṣe labdhasvarūpe satyapi pratihativirahāt - nāśakasyā- prasaktaḥ, tādṛśe - svābhāvike nityānapāye ca sati, pratyāpannasvadāyakramaḥ – madhye vicchinnaḥ svabhāvato nyāyalabdhaḥ yaḥ putrasya paitṛkadhanādidāyalābhaḥ tannyāyaḥ, iha — mokṣata: svābhāvika- svarūpāvirbhāvaviṣaye, prāptasiddheḥ pratīcaḥ - niṣpannamokṣopāyasya pratyagātmano viṣaye kathitaḥ vicārya nirṇayenoktaḥ || 6 ||

arvāñca muktibhedaṃ katicidagaṇayan svātmamātrānubhūrti

tatra brahmānubhūterviddatirupadhitaḥ syāt svato veti cintyam | santi dvedhā'pi doṣāstata upacaraṇānmuktiśabdo'tra yuktaḥ
sālokyādiprabhedeṣvapi saraṇiriyaṃ sāvadhānairvibhāvyā || 7 ||

arvāścaḥ katicit — anantaraṃ vedāntārthacintanapravṛttāḥ kecana, svātmamātrānubhūtim - kevalapratyagātmabhūtasvātmānubhavam, muktibhedam - mokṣaviśeṣatvena, agaṇayan – nirṇītavantaḥ, tatra - tasmin viṣaye, cintyam - vicāraṇīyaṃ kiñcidasti, tatra — kevalasvānubhavarūpe mokṣe (iti vā arthaḥ) brahmānubhūtervihatiḥ brahmānubhavasyābhāvaḥ, upadhitaḥ syāt svato vā iti cintyam - upādhiviśeṣakṛto bhavet ? uta svābhāvika eva bhavet ? iti vicāraṇīyaṃ bhavati, dvedhā'pi - brahmānubhavābhāvasya aupādhikatvapakṣe svābhāvikatvapakṣe'pi ca, doṣāḥ santi — karmāde- rupādherabhāvādaupādhikatvayoga, ātma viśeṣasya kevalaṃ brahmānubhavābhāvasya svābhāvikatvāyogaḥ:

iti ubhayatra doṣāḥ bhavanti, tataḥ tasmāddhetoḥ, atra — kevalasvātmānubhave, muktiśabda:- muktipadaprayogaḥ upacaraṇāt yuktaḥ - gauṇārthanibandhano yuktaḥ, sālokyādiprabhedeṣvapi - sālokya sāmīpya-sārūpyākhyeṣu daśāviśeṣeṣvapi, iyaṃ saraṇiḥ – muktiśabdasya aupacārika- tvavidhā, sāvadhānaiḥ śāstrārtha cintā kuśalaiḥ, vibhāvyā - avadhāryā bhavati ( sarva pratibandhaka - vimokena paripūrṇabrahmānubhavasyaiva mokṣapadārthatvena tatsāmīpyavatyarthe tacchabdaprayogasya aupacārika- svāvaśyaṃbhāvāt iti || 7 ||

parānubhūti : - mokṣadaśāyāṃ parabrahmānubhavaḥ, vibhedatastu syāt svasmāt pṛthagbhāvenaiva bhavitumaīti ; na tu brahmaṇaḥ svātmanaśca avibhāgena, atisāmyaśabdāt – 'nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti' iti muktasyātmanaḥ paramātmanā saha bhedaghaṭitaparama sāmyasyaiva śrutisiddhatvāt, sādharmyaśabdāt – 'mama sādharmyamāgatāḥ' iti bhagavadgītāyāṃ muktikāle dharmasāmyāpattereva spaṣṭamuktaḥ, (iti śaṅkane) idam – svasmādvibhaktatayā brahmaṇo'nubhavaḥ ityetat na syāt – naiva brahmātmakatvāt (śarīrātmabhāvasattvena)

T

bhavitumaīm, tadātmakatvāt — muktasya svātmanaḥ sāmyaśrutiḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaitītīyaṃ muṇḍakaśrutiḥ, tadguṇasāmyalabdhā - brāhmaguṇaiḥ sākaṃ svagata- guṇānāṃ samānatvato hetoḥ pravṛttā || (ataḥ muktidaśāyāṃ jīvena brahmaṇo'nubhavaḥ ' ahaṃ brahmā'smīti svāpṛthagbhāvenaiva ; na tu pṛthagbhāveneti || 8 ||

pṛthagbhāvaviṣayakaḥ bhānāṃśaḥ saṃmataḥ, dehātmabhāve - jīva- parayoḥ śarīrātmabhāve, siddhe tu - niścite param, iha – dharmabhūtajñānāsaṅkocayute kāle, tatsvaniṣṭhatvadṛṣṭiḥ- paramātmā pṛthaksaddhe svatantra vastutā buddhiḥ, na hi ghaṭatenaiva saṅgatā bhavati iti || 8 || mantroktaṃ bhoktabhāve yadi sahabhavanaṃ brahma na syāt pradhānaṃ tasmāt bhogyabhāve bhavatu taducitaṃ brāhmaṇavyākṛteśca | dvedhā'pi brahmatatvāt pṛthagitara iha syāditīdaṃ ca vārta
siddhe bhede svaniṣṭhasthiti pariharaṇaṃ hyatra sūtropapādyam || 9 ||
mantroktam- 'so'śnute sarvān kāmān saha brahmaṇā vipaścitā' iti mantravarṇakhaṇḍa- pratipāditam, moktabhāve sahabhavanaṃ yadi - jīvasya brahmaṇā saha bhoktṛtvamiti sāhityaṃ yadi saṃmataṃ syāt ; tarhi, brahma pradhānaṃ na syāt – ' putreṇa saha gataḥ pite'tyādau putra iva brahmāpyatra na pradhānaṃ bhavet, (sarvaniyantuḥ sarvasvāminaḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ aprādhānyaṃ na hi kaścana vaidikaḥ anujānīyāt), tasmāt – uktāddhetoḥ, tat - sāhityam, bhogyabhāve bhavatu - bhoktāraṃ muktātmānaṃ prati brahmaguṇānāṃ brahmaṇaśca bhogyateti brahmaṇo bhogyatve sāhityamāstām ; na bhoktṛtve, tat — uktametat bhogyabhāve sahabhavanam ucitam – yuktameva bhavati, brāhmaṇa- vyākṛteśva' brahmavidāpnoti param ' iti brāhmaṇavākyavyākhyānarūpatvaucityādapi, (brahmapadārtha- vyākhyānaṃ ' satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahme 'ti ; 'vit āpnoti param' ityaṃśasya vyākhyānaṃ 'yo veda.... so'śnute sarvān kāmān saha brahmaṇā vipaścitā' iti, ityevaṃ sandarbhasāṅgatyasya bhogyasāhitya - pakṣe eva sampatteriti dvedhā'pi - jīvena saha bhoktṛtvamiti, svaguṇaiḥ sākaṃ bhogyatvamiti ubhaya- pakṣe'pi, iha – 'sośnute' ityatra, itara:- jīvaḥ, brahma taccāt – brahmākhyāt paratattvāt, pṛthak syāt – pṛthagbhūta eva bhavet ; na apṛthagbhūtaḥ, itīdaṃ ca - ityevaṃ cintanaṃ param, vārtam — vārtāmātraviśrāntaṃ ; na vastutastve, siddhe bhede - pramāṇa- tarkādibhiḥ niścite jīva- brahmaṇoḥ pṛthagbhāve, svaniṣṭhasthitipariharaṇaṃ hi - jīvasya brahmaṇaḥ sakāśāt pṛthasthitiprati- panyaītādūrīkaraṇameva khalu, atra — asminnacikaraṇe, sūtropapādyam —' avibhāgena dṛṣṭatvāditi sūtreṇa saṅgatatayā pratipādyam || 9 ||

pāramyaṃ yacca sāmye śrutamiha na hi tat sarvathetyapyadhītaṃ
grāvṇo hemnaśca yadvat samadharaṇaghṛtau syāttaduktistathā'tra |
evaṃ taddehabhāve sthitavati vividhāvāghitāmnāya bhūmnā
brahmavetyādivākyaṃ prathayati samatāṃ tādṛgityapyadhīteḥ || 10 ||

iha mokṣadaśāpannapratyagātmaviṣaye, sāmye parabrahmaṇā sākaṃ samānatve, pāramyam — "parāṃ kāṣṭhāṃ prāptatvam, yacca śrutam - ' nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti ' iti yadapi śrutau paṭhita- māste, tat — jīvasya brahmaṇā sāmyam, sarvathetyapi brahmagata sarvavidhaprakāreṇā'pītyetadapi, na hi adhītam — śrutau nāmnātaṃ khalu, grāvṇo hemnaśca – śilākhaṇḍasya suvarṇakhaṇḍasya ca, samadharaṇadhṛtau – ekasyāmeva tulāyāṃ tulāpātrayoḥ (ghaṭayoḥ) sthāpitayoḥ dhṛtiviṣaye, taduktiḥ--- ime grāva- hemnī same' iti sāmyoktiḥ, yadvat syāt — yathā abādhitayāvatprakāramātrāva- gāhinī bhavet (parimāṇamātra kṛtasāmyamātraviṣayiṇyeva bhavet ; na tu prāṇi grāvatvaprahāṇena suvarṇatvāśrayaṇena vā, suvarṇe tattāvimo kena prāvatvāśrayaṇena vā samatvaprāpteruktirūpā naiva syāt, tathā - tayaiva rītyā, atra syāt — jīvasya brahmaṇā saha paramasāmye'pi abādhitayāvatprakāramātra- sāmyaprāptitātparyakaiva syāt ; ( na tu jīve jīvatvaprahāṇena brahmatvaprāptitātparyikā bhavitumarhā syāt ) evam — śruti-smṛtyādipramāṇaiḥ pratipāditayā rītyā, taddehabhāve - jīvasya paramātmānaṃ prati śarīratve, vividhābādhitāmnāya bhūmnā – nānā pradeśagatanānākāra pramāṇāntarabādha vidūra- śruti- vākya balena, sthitavati — aprakampyatayā dṛḍhe sati, brahmavetyādivākyam – 'brahmaiva bhavatī' tyeva - mādikaṃ brahmavidi brahmatvāptipratipādakamiva bhāsamānaṃ śrautaṃ vākyam, samatāṃ prathayati- sāmyameva pratipādayati ; na abhedam, ' tādṛk ' ityapi adhīteḥ – 'yathodakaṃ śuddhe śuddha- māsiktaṃ tādṛgeva bhavati | evaṃ munervijānata ātmā bhavati' ityevaṃ sādṛśyapratipādaka tādṛk' śabdasyā'pi śrutipāṭhāt || 10 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā ||

jñānatve - muktātmano jñānamātrasvarūpatāpariśeṣe, kiṃ phalaṃ syāditi – muktidaśāyāṃ puruṣārthabhūtaṃ phalaṃ kiṃ vā sampannaṃ syāt ? naiva kimapīti hetoḥ, tādṛśam – ' tādṛgeva bhavatī 'ti śrutyuktam, brahmasāmyam – jīvasya brahmasāmyam guṇaiḥ miṣatu - apahatapāpmatvādibhiraṣṭabhi guṇaiḥ militaṃ sat, jīvasvarūpanirūpakaṃ bhavatu, (iti jaiminermatam ) asvarūpe:- - jīvasvarūpe apraviṣṭaiḥ, kim – kiṃ phalaṃ sampannaṃ syāditi, tat – tasmāddhetoḥ, cinmātrarūpaprakāśaḥ - jñānaikasvarūpatārūpaprakāśātmako bhavatu muktau jīvaḥ, (ityauḍulomimatam ) ittham – evaṃrītyā, chātroktapakṣadvayam – svaśiṣyābhyāṃ jaiminyau ḍulomibhyāṃ svīkṛtya vivṛtamabhiprāyadvayamapi, (ekaikaśaḥ) ubhayavidhaśrutyupāttāvirodhāt - prajāpativākyarūpayā chāndogyaśrutyā apahatapāpmatvādeḥ, bṛhadā- raṇyakaśrutyā vijñānaghanatvasya cetyanayoḥ ātmani avirodhamādāya, nirdhūya - nirākṛtya, atha— paścāt, siddhataśvaprakāśāt — pramāṇasiddhaṃ apahatapāpmatvādiyogaṃ jñāna - tadāśrayatva rūpatvaṃ cābhyupagamya, dvidhā āvirbhavanam - rūpadvayasahitatayā muktā vā virbhāvam, aghaṭayat – muktātmani aviruddhaṃ yojayāmāsa sūtrakāraḥ || 11 ||
paryāyau nāmapāṭheṣvanu paripaṭhitau muktikaivalyaśabdau
kaivalyaṃ cā'nyayogatyajanamiti kathaṃ dharmi- dharmānvayo'sya | maivaṃ brahmasvarūpādaparamiha na khalvasti kiṃcit svaniṣṭhaṃ

na dravyaṃ cā'guṇaṃ syādadhikaraṇayugaṃ tannirātaṅkametat || 12 || nāmapāṭheṣu — nāmaliṅgānuśāsanagateṣu paṭhyamāneṣu nāmasu, mukti - kaivalyaśabdau- mokṣavācī muktiśabdaḥ kaivalyaśabdaśca paryāyau ekārthaviśeṣyaka bodhajanakaśabdadvayatayā, anu paripaṭhitau- kramaśaḥ prayuktau 'muktiḥ kaivalya nirvāṇaśreyoniḥ śreyasāmṛtam' iti, kaivalyaṃ ca — muktiparyāyabhūtaṃ kaivalyaṃ nāma, anyayogatyajanamiti - svetareṇa dharmiṇā vā dharmeṇa sambandhasya vimoka eveti hetoḥ, asya – mokṣadaśa pannasya jīvasya dharmi-dharmānvayaḥ katham ? - dharmitayā brahmaṇaḥ dharmatayā jñānāderguṇasya ca saṃsargaḥ kathaṃ yujyate ? (iti śaṅkane) maivam - evaṃ na śaṅkanīyam, brahmasvarūpāt aparam - paramātmanaḥ svarūpādbhinnam, iha – paramātmavibhūtidvaye, svaniṣṭhaṃ kiṃcit — brahmaṇaḥ sakāśāt pṛthakke sthiti pratipattiyogyaṃ yatkiṃcidapi vastu, nāsti kiṃcit–brahmaṇaḥ khalu–naiva vidyate khalu, (iti pūrvasmin adhikaraṇe) dravyaṃ ca - dravyapadābhidheyaṃ kiṃcidapi, na aguṇaṃ syāt – guṇarahitaṃ naiva bhavet, (iti asminnadhikaraṇe) tat — tasmāddhetoḥ, etat adhikaraṇayugam - prastutaṃ avibhāgena dṛṣṭatva - brāhmākhyam adhikaraṇadvikam nirātaṅkam - bādhitārthanirṇāyakatvadoṣaśaṅkāyā aviṣayabhūtaṃ sat jāgarti || 12 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṃgrahaḥ

brāhmāghikaraṇe –'svena rūpeṇā'bhiniṣpadyate' ityatra, abhiniṣpādyatayoktaṃ, kim apahatapāpmatvādikameva ? uta jñānamātrameva ? utobhayam ? – iti viśaye ; muktamadhikṛtya apahatapāpmatvādyupanyāsāt tenaiva rūpeṇāvirbhavati iti jaiminimatamanūdya; 'vijñānaghana evetyādiṣu jñānamātramevā'sya svarūpamityavagamāt, apahatāmādiśabdānāṃ śokādyabhāvaparatvācca, jñānamātrasvarūpeṇāvirbhavati — ityauḍulomimatamupanyasya pakṣadvaye'pi anyataraśrutibādhāt, 'vijñāna- ghana evetyavadhāraṇasya ekadeśajaḍatvaśaṅkā vyudāsaparatvena guṇāntarapratikṣepakatvābhāvāt ubhaya- rūpeṇāvirbhavatīti svamatena matadvayaṃ pratikṣiptam ||

ityatra,

satyasaṅkalpatvaviyogataḥ syāt – satyasaṅkalpatvābhāvāt sambhavati na tu satyasaṅkalpavatsu

muktātmasu tat || 4 ||

viśvasyaitasya janma-sthiti-layaracanā viśvakarturyathā syā-
dicchā santānabhedānniyatiriha tathā nityamukta kriyāṇām | tasyā'nucchedavṛtyā pratikalabhidurāstāśca tādṛkpravāhā-
stadbuddherā parokṣyaṃ tvaniśamabhiduraṃ vartate brahmadhīvat || 14 ||

etasya viśvasya asya jagataḥ, janma-sthiti-layaracanā-sṛṣṭi · sattā· tadbhaṅgānāṃ nirmitiḥ, viśvakartuḥ yathā syāt — jagattraṣṭuḥ paramātmano yathārītyā abādhitā saṅgacchate, tathā - tathaiva rītyā, nityamukta kriyāṇām anantādīnāṃ nityasurīṇāṃ, upāyānuṣṭhānato bandhā- nmuktātmanāṃ ca yāḥ kriyāḥ śrutipratipannāḥ tāsām, icchā santāna bhedāt - ucchedarāhityenānu- vartamānāt icchāviśeṣāt niyatiḥ - abhagnā vyavasthā, iha - ' jakṣat krīḍan ramamāṇaḥ ' sa yadi pitṛlokakāma:' itya dimuktāvasthā prakaraṇoktārthaviṣaye cintanīyā bhavati, tasya – icchā santānasya, anuccheda vṛcyā- vicchedarā hityenā'nuvartamānatayaiva, pratikalabhidurā:- pratikṣaṇaṃ bhinnabhinnasvarūpāḥ, tāśva — nityānāṃ muktānāṃ ca kriyā api tādṛkpravāhāḥ – anucche- dena santanyamānasvarūpā eva satyaḥ ityadhigantavyāḥ, tadbuddheḥ – nityānāṃ muktānāṃ ca dharma- bhūtadhiyo'pi, āparokṣyam – atirohitārthaprāhitvarūpaṃ pratyakṣatvaṃ ; ( na tu indriyāyattadhītvam, ) aniśam – aviratam, abhiduraṃ tu - aviluptameva sat, brahmadhīvat – paramātmajñānavat, vidyata eva || 14 ||

pratyūhāhaḥ prajeśaprabhṛtisukṛtināṃ satyasaṅkalpabhāvo

martyārambhaspṛhādau taditaravividhaprāṇisṛṣṭyādidṛṣṭeḥ | tenecchāsantatīnāṃ saphala-viphalate puṇya-pāpairamīṣāṃ

muktasyoktā cikīrṣātvavihataviṣayā tāratamyaṃ na doṣaḥ || 15 ||

prajeśa....tinām – prajāpatipramukhānāṃ puṇyaviśeṣaprāptasvādhikārāṇāṃ cetanaviśeṣāṇām, satyasaṅkalpabhāvaḥ–apratihatasaṅkalpatvarūpo dharmaḥ, pratyūhāhī – pratibandhārho bhavati, martyā - rambhaspṛhādau manujasisṛkṣādau satyapi taditara...dṛṣṭeḥ manujavyatiriktanānāprakārajantu- sṛṣṭiprasaṅgasya upaniṣadādau śrutaśvāt, tena-uktena hetunā, amīṣām icchāsantatīnām- prajāpatyādyādhikārika puruṣasambandhinīnāṃ kāryānuguṇābhilāṣapravādānuvṛttīnām, viphalate-phalānvaya- tadabhā vau, amīṣāṃ puṇya-pāpaiḥ - īdṛśapuruṣāṇāṃ sukṛta- duṣkṛtākhyaiḥ karmabhireva bhavataḥ, muktasyoktā muktātmānamadhikṛtya śrutiṣu upadiṣṭā, cikīrṣā tu-

saṅkalpākhyā icchā param, ahita viṣayā svaviṣaya nirvahaṇāṃśe vighātaśūnyaiva (satyaiva) jāgarti, tāratamyam – kriyā- tanniṣpādyārtheṣu mitho vailakṣaṇyam, na doṣaḥ – naiva icchā vaiphalyajñāpakaḥ (vividhavilakṣaṇabhagavatsaṅkalpaprayojyatvāt tasya ) || 15 ||
sūtre mukto'pyananyādhipatirabhihitastena kastasya śeṣī

śrutyaivoktaḥ svarāḍityapi vihatirataḥ prāk pratiṣṭhāpitānām |
maivaṃ nāthetarān pratyadhavihita iha kṣipyate śeṣabhāvo
viśvasyātmeśvaro yaḥ patiriti paṭhitastatpatitvaṃ tvabādhyam || 16 ||
sūtre – 'ata eva cā'nanyādhipatiḥ ' ityasmin śārīrakasūtre, mukto'pi - labdhasaṃsāra- mokṣaḥ cetano'pi, ananyādhipatiḥ abhihitaḥ – svaniyāmaka puruṣāntararahita iti pratipāditaḥ, tena – sūtra siddhena ananyādhipatitvena jñāpakena tasya kaḥ śeṣī 1 – muktātmanaḥ ko vā prabhurasti; na ko'pi vartate, svara| ḍityapi — svayaṃ prabhurityapi śrutyaivoktaḥ - 'sa svarāḍ bhavati' iti upaniṣadā bodhitaḥ, ataḥ-uktāddhetoḥ prākpratiṣṭhāpitānām – pūrva śrutyādipramāṇaiḥ samarthitānāṃ bhagavaccheṣatva-tatpāratantryādīnāṃ cetanadharmāṇām, vihatiḥ muktikā le vimoka eva ; nā'nuvṛttiḥ, iti śaṅkane) maivam — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na sādhu, iha – 'ananyādhipatiḥ' iti sūtre, 'sa svarāṭ' iti śrutau ca, adhavihitaḥ - duṣkarmanibandhanaḥ, nāthetarān prati śeṣabhāvaḥ sarvaśaraṇyavyatiriktān jīvādīn prati pārārthayabhāva:, kṣipyate-nivāryate muktikā le, yaḥ yadvidhaḥ punaḥ, ātmeśvaraḥ viśvasya patiḥ iti paṭhitaḥ - ' parti viśvatyātmeśvaram' iti śrutyā sarvaśeṣitvena sarvātmaniyantṛ- tvena cāmnātaḥ paramapuruṣaḥ, tatpatitvaṃ tu taccheṣitva nirūpakatvaṃ punaḥ, abādhyam - muktāvapi nivārya naiva bhavati (svarūpāyattasya svabhāvasya kadācidapi apracyuteḥ || 16||
