abhāvādhikaraṇam || 4-4-5 ||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ ||
mukto'śarīrastvaśarīraśabdādicchāśarīrī bahugheti śabdāt |

naiṣā vyavasthā bahudhā tu śabdādvahu (bhoga) śruteśca tvayamaicchikaḥ syāt || 5 || muktaḥ - saṃsārādvimokaṃ prāptaścetanaḥ, aśarīraḥ śarīrarahita eva, aśarīraśabdāt- ' aśarīraṃ vāva santaṃ na priyāpriye spṛśataḥ ' iti muktasya aśarīratvabodhakaśrutisaśvāt, ( ityekaḥ pakṣaḥ) icchāśarīrī - svasaṅkalpāyattaśarīraparigrahavāneva muktātmā ; na karmāyattaśarīravān nāpyaśarīraḥ, bahudheti śabdāt - śarīra bahutvabodhakāt 'sa ekadhā bhavati tridhā bhavati ityādiśrautaśabdāt jñāpakāta, (ityaparaḥ pakṣaḥ) eṣā vyavasthā na – aśarīratvameva, akarmā- yattaśarīravatvameva vetyubhayamapi na ekaikaśo vyavasthitam, bahudhā śabdāt - bahuśarīrasambandha- bodhakāt ' tridhā bhavatītyevaṃrūpāt bhogaśruteśca – ' manasaitān kāmān paśyan ramate ' iti śarīraṃ vinā'pi manasaiva bhogaprāptiśruterapi ayam – deha parigrahaḥ, aicchikastu syāt - sāṅkalpika eva bhavet ; na niyataḥ || 5 ||

sa ekadhā bhavatīti' bahudhāsambhavaḥ - nānāśarī ra viśiṣṭatayā bhavanam adhītaḥ āmnātam, gāna krīḍādi ca - sāmagānaṃ vihārādikamapi ca, uktam- 'sāma gāyan jakṣat krīḍan ityādiśrutiṣu bodhitam ārṣaṃ tadanuguṇamatadvandvam - tādṛśaśrutidvayānurodhibādari-jaiminyākhya- ṛṣisaṃmataṃ āśayadvayam nirasyan – niyamadūrīkaraṇena pariharan, svacchandasya – akarmavaśyasya muktātmanaḥ, tadubhayam — aśarīratvaṃ saśarīratvaṃ cetyubhayamapi, kṣamamiti – sambhāvitameveti, svaṃ matam — śārīrakasūtrakṛdvādarāyaṇaḥ, – svīyamāśayam, sūtrakāraḥ - śārīrakasūtra kṛdvādarāyaṇaḥ, vadati - 'dvādaśāhavadu-

bhayavidham' iti sūtreṇa vyaktaṃ karoti, tat nikhilamapi - muktātmānamadhikṛtyoktaṃ sarvamapi,

– – parabrahmavat parasya brahmaṇa iva, tatsamasya parabrahmatulyasya muktasyāpyāmanaḥ, akarmodbhavaṃ syāt - karmādhīnameva bhavet || 17 ||
mokṣe puṇyādyabhāvāt tadupadhikavapurvarjitatvaṃ na dūṣyaṃ

tasmin duḥkhāī dehatyaji ca śubhava pussatvapakṣo'pyabādhyaḥ | itthaṃ satyatra muktasthitiriha muninā kīdṛśī sūtritā syā- nmaivaṃ svacchanda dehagraha vada niyamasthāpane'trā'bhisandheḥ || 18 || mokṣe-mokṣāvasthāyām, puṇyādyabhāvāt - puṇya pāpābhivayoḥ karmaṇorasatvāt, tadupadhikavapurvarjitatvam - muktasya karmo ghinibandhanadeha sambandharā hityam ( bāda rimatam ) na dūṣyam – nirasanāī na bhavati, tasmin — muktātmani duḥkhāīdehatya ji ca – duḥkhānubhava- prayojakadeha sambandhaparityāgavati ca sati, śubhavapussadhvapakṣo'pi maṅgalamayaśarīravattāpakṣo'pi (jaiminisaṃmataḥ) abādhyaḥ - bādhanārho na bhavati, atra – muktātmano deha tadabhāvaviṣaye, itthaṃ sati – pakṣadvayasyā'pi adūṣyatve sati iha — asminnadhikaraṇe, muninā - bādarāyaṇena, muktasthitiḥ — muktātmano'vasthā, kīdṛśī sūtritā syāt kimākārā vā sūtreṇa nirṇītā bhavet ? ( atra kīdṛśasya 'pi nirṇayasyāvakāśo naiva bhavet ? ataḥ etadadhikaraṇārambho niṣprayojana eva ) – iti śaṅkāyām, maitram — evaṃ śaṅkanamasādhu, svacchanda ....sthāpane — svasaṅkalpamā trāyatta- śarīra svīkārāṃśayuktasya saśarīratvāśarīratvānyatarapakṣatrauvyābhāvaviśrāmasya samarthane eva atra sūtra kārābhisaṃdhisatvāt, (adhikaraṇasārthakyāt ) || 18||

nānā dehā yadi syuryugapadavigatabrahmasāmyasya puṃsa-

steṣāṃ vyāptasvarūpānvaya ucita iti prāktanāṇutvahānam | naitaddhīvyāptisiddherbhavati ca jagadāveśvāk tannidānā
saubharyādau praklaptā gatimapavadituṃ na kṣamaṃ brahmasāmyam || 19 ||

adhigatatrahmasāmyasya puṃsaḥ - brahmaṇā sākaṃ paramaṃ sāmyaṃ prāptavato muktātmanaḥ, yadi yugapat nānādehāḥ syuḥ – ekasminneva kāle anekāni śarīrāṇi yadi nāma bhaveyuḥ tarhi, teṣām — tādṛśamuktātmasambandhināmanekeṣāṃ śarīrāṇām, vyāptasvarūpānvayaḥ ucita iti- sarvairdehaiḥ saha yugapadeva vyāptyarheṇa jīvasvarūpeṇaiva sambandhaḥ śārīravyavahāra nirvāhakaḥ san yukto bhavatīti hetoḥ, prāktanāṇutvahānam - bandhakāle jīvīya parimāṇatvena samarthitasya aṇuparimāṇasya mokṣakāle nāśo'bhyupagantavya āpatatīti, mokṣe jīvasya vibhutvaparigraha eva nyāyyaḥ (iti śaṅkane), etat na – jīvasya mokṣe svābhāvikaparimāṇatyā gābhyupagamanaṃ naiva nyāyyam, dhīvyāptisiddheḥ – dharmabhūtajñānadvārā jīvātmanaḥ sarvavyāpteḥ 'sa cā''nantyāya kalpate' iti śrutitaḥ siddhatvāt jagadā veśavāka - 'sarvamāpnoti sarvaśaḥ' ityukta sarva jagatpraveśa vācoyuktiḥ, tannidānāvyāptinibandhanā satī bhavati ca - sābhiprāyā bhavati, brahmasāmyam — paramaṃ sāmyamupaitīti śrutyavagataṃ muktasyā''tmanaḥ parabrahmaṇā saha samatvam, saubharyādau praklṛptāṃ gatim – saubhariprabhṛtau ātmaviśeṣe nirṇīta rūpāṃ anekaśarīreṣvekadā vyāptinirvāharītim, apavadituṃ – bādhitum, na kṣamam – naiva samartha bhavet || 19 ||

pūrvakāle ( saṃsāradaśāyām), prayojikayā yogaśaktyā hetubhūtayā, bahutanubhajane – saubharyādibhirnānāśarīrasvīkāre kārye, karmabandho'pi - tadanuguṇapuṇyādikarmasambandho'pi, apekṣyaḥ - nānāśarīraparigrahaprayojakatvena apekṣa- ṇīyo bhavati, (na hi sarveṣāṃ yogināṃ ṛṣīṇāmapi vā evaṃ nānāśarīraparigrahaḥ purāṇetihāsādau prasiddhaḥ) api tu saubharyādīnāṃ keṣāṃcideva vyaktiviśeṣāṇāmiti) || 20 ||

tādṛgyogaśaktyā - sarvaśaktabhagavaddattayā

śaktyāśrayasaṅkalpa-

śakti: kṣetrajñasaṃjñā munibhirabhidadhe veṣṭitā karmabhiḥ svaiḥ
puṃso'naṃśā'pi buddhirbahuvidhavikṛtiḥ svīkṛtaivaṃ sa cā'stu | bālāyetyādivākyāt tadayamaṇurapi syādananto'pi mukto
na syājjainādibhaṅge parihṛtavikṛte raikarūpyānapāyāt || 21 ||
kṣetrajñasaṃjñā śakti:- jīvātmarūpā śaktiḥ, svaiḥ karmabhiḥ svīyaiḥ (tattadīyaiḥ) avidyā- padavācyaiḥ puṇya-pāpākhyaiḥ karmabhiḥ sādhanabhūtaiḥ, veṣṭitā - āvṛteti, munibhiḥ abhidadhe- parāśarādibhī ṛṣibhiḥ pratipāditaṃ vartate (' yayā kṣetrajñaśaktiḥ sā veṣṭitā' iti ) puṃso

: - puruṣasya (jīvasya) dharmabhūtajñānābhidhā dhīḥ, anaṃśā'pi — niravayavā'pi satī, bahuvidha- vikṛtiḥ svīkṛtā nānākāravikāravattayā saṅkoca vikāsāśrayatayā bhavadbhirabhyupagatā bandhakāle karmāyattasaṃkocavatī; mokṣakāle svābhāvika vikāsavatī iti), sa ca tādṛśyā ghiyaḥ āśrayabhūto jīva eva, evamastu – svayaṃ niravayavo'pi saṅkoca - vikāsādyātmakanānā vikārāśrayo'bhyupagamyatāṃ nāma, ( ko doṣa: ?) aṇurapi – aṇuparimāṇasvābhāvyavānapi prakṛto jīvaḥ, mukta:- muktā- vasthāmadhigataḥ, vālāgretyādivākyāt - ' vālāgraśatabhāgasya śatadhā kalpitasya ca | bhāgo jīvaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ sa cānantyāya kalpate ||' iti śvetāśvataraśruteḥ pramāṇāt, ananto'pi syāt — vibhuparimāṇo'pyupeyo bhavatu, (iti śaṅkane) na syāt - aṇurjīvaḥ naiva vibhurbhavet, jainādibhaṅge - ātmasvarūpa vikāravādināṃ jainādyavaidikānāṃ nirāsapare ekasminnasambhavādhikaraṇe, parihṛtavikṛteḥ–nirvikāratvenaiva vyavasthitasya jīvasya aikarūpyānapāyāt – nirvikāratvaika- rūpatāyā apāyasya (kadācit savikāratāyāḥ) anabhyupagantavyatvāt || 21||
jīvasyaikaikaśo hi tyajanata udito vṛkṣaśākhāsu śoṣa-
stasmādadvārakaṃ syādapagatavaraṇe mukhyamānantyamasmin |
maivaṃ śākhāsu bhogāśrayaniyatikaropā ghināśaḥ prahāṇaṃ
kṣetrādinyāyato'sāvabhimativirahāt syādadhīto jaṅghātiḥ || 22 ||

buddhi:-

jīvasya – vṛkṣādhiṣṭhātṛbhāvenā'vasthitasyā''tmanaḥ, ekaikaśaḥ tyajanato hi - vṛkṣāṃśa- bhūtaikaikabhāgaparityāgata eva khalu, vṛkṣaśākhāsu – vṛkṣasambandhinīṣvekaikāsu aṃśātmikālu śākhāsu, śoṣaḥ - śuṣkatāyāḥ (nīrasatāyāḥ) sambhavaḥ, uditaḥ - ' yadekāṃ śākhāṃ jahāti ; atha sā śuṣyati' ityādinā śrutivākyenoktaḥ, tasmāt – svīyaśarīrāṃśatyāgāt aṃśāntare'va- sthānācca jñāpakāt, apagatavaraṇe — nivṛtta karmakṛtatirodhāne āvirbhūtasvarūpe jīve, ānantyam - ' sa cāssnantyāya kalpate' iti śrutyuktam aparicchinnaparimāṇatvam advārakam – dharmabhūta- jñānarūpaṃ dvāramanāśritya vartamānam mukhyaṃ syāt – ātmasvarūpagatameva sat agauṇameva bhavitu- marham ; (iti śaṅkane) maivam - ātmasvarūpaparimāṇataḥ ātmani savikāratvaśaṅkanaṃ naivocitam, śākhāsu - vṛkṣāṃśeṣu prahāṇam – jīvasambandhaparityāgo hi nāma, bhogāśraya niyatikaropādhi- nāśaḥ - ātmanaḥ strīyasukha duḥkhānubhavā vaccheda kavyavasthāsampādakasya puṇya-pāpākhyopādhisambandhasya vimoka eva ; (na tu jīvasya pūrṇe vṛkṣe svarūpavyāptiḥ tadekadeśe vyāptivarjanataḥ ekadeśāntare avasthitiḥ ityādi) asau jahātiḥ - ' śākhāṃ jahāti ' iti śruto hādhātu:, kṣetrādinyāyataḥ - devadattādeḥ puruṣasya ārāma-kedārādikṣetrāṇāṃ tyāgavyavahāra iva, abhimativirahāt-tadviṣayakamvīyatvābhimānavarjanāt (svayatnakṛtāt īśvarecchākṛtādvā) prayojaka - bhūtāt, adhītaḥ syāt — śrutipaṭhitaḥ syāt, na tu svarūpavyāpti- tadvarjanābhyām iti || 22 ||
eko naikaḥ parasmāt pṛthagapṛthagapi svasvarūpeṇa muktaḥ
svābhīṣṭāśeṣabhoktā svayamiti pṛthukakṣīvavad ye bhramanti | te'nvīkṣya svoktibādhaṃ śrutiśatavihati tattaduktayānyaparyaṃ
jaināvaṣṭambhaḍapyanmati kalahamucaḥ satpathaṃ saṃśrayantu || 23||

muktaḥ - saṃsārabandhādvimokaṃ prāptavān cetanaḥ, (muktāvastheo jīvaḥ), svasvarūpeṇa- svayaṃ dharmabhūtaṃ jñānamadvārīkṛtya svasvarūpata eva, eko naikaḥ nānāprabhedarāhityena abhinnarūpaḥ, tadvattvena bhinnarūpaśca parasmāt pṛthak apṛthagapi — paramātmanaḥ sakāśāt pārthakyavān tadrahitaśca svayam — paramātmasaṅkalpānadhīnasva saṅkalpavaśādeva, svābhīṣṭa śepabhoktā svābhi lapitanikhila viṣaya karma bhoktṛtvavān iti uktarītyā muktikālikajīva svarūpa nirṇayataḥ, pṛthukakṣīvavat - bāla-mattasādṛśyena, ye bhramanti—ye vā kecana dārśanikaṃmanyā: apratipattyanyathā- pratipatimanto vartante, te tādṛśā dārśanikāḥ, svoktibādham – svavacanagatameva mitho virodha-

kṛtamupamardam, śrutiśatavihatim - jīveṣu nānātvaṃ, jīva-brahmaṇoḥ īśeśitavyatvādikṛtaṃ bhedaṃ, jagadbrahmaṇośca sṛjya sraṣṭṛbhāvādikṛtamanaikyaṃ ca ghoṣayantībhyo bahvībhyaḥ śrutibhyaḥ sampadyamānaṃ svamatasya spaṣṭaṃ bhaṅgam, tattaduktayānyaparyam - jīveṣvabhedaparaśrutīnāṃ jñānakā kāratvādikṛtasvarūpā- vailakṣaṇye tātparya, jīvabrahmā bhedaparaśrutīnāṃ jñānānandādyavailakṣaṇyakṛta sājātya- śarīrātmabhāvādi- kṛtābhedavyavahāra|rddatva-brahmā pṛthaniṣṭhatveṣu tātparya, jagadbahmābhedaparaśrutīnāṃ niyataviśeṣaṇaviśeṣya- bhāvakṛtavaiśiṣṭyatātparyaṃ ceti svāsaṃmatameva tātparyāntaraṃ āgrahagṛhītikāṃ vihāya samyagvivekata ālocya, jaināvaṣṭambhaḍapyanmati kalahamucaḥ – mitho viruddha dharmābhyupagame prathamodāharaṇabhūtānāṃ jainānāmādarśāśrayaṇena tattvānavagā himatimatāṃ tattaddāśanikānāṃ mitho gharṣaṇaṃ dūrīkṛtavantaḥ, satpathaṃ saṃśrayantu – bhagavadrāmānujapravardhitāṃ sadvastulā bhaupayikī saraṇimevā''śritāḥ śāntā bhavantu || 23||

sarvaṃ saṅkalpamātrāllabhata iti samāmnāyate sūtrita ca

svecchā to dehayogādyaniyatikathanaṃ syādataḥ piṣṭapeṣaḥ |
tannā'nyonyoparuddhaśrutigati niyatiḥ kāmato'nekadeha-
svīkāraprakriyetyādyaniyatikathane sūtrakārābhisandheḥ || 24 ||

sarvam — pitṛlokādikamabhīpsitaṃ sarvam, saṅkalpamātrāt - prayatnādyanapekṣāt kevalādeva saṅkalpāt, labhata iti – prāpnotīti, samāmnāyate - ' sa yadi .... saṅkalpā devāsya pitaraḥ ityādikayā śrutyā paripaṭhyate, sūtritaṃ ca tadetat ' saṅkalpādeva tacchrateḥ' iti sūtrāt vicārya sahetukaṃ nirṇītamapyāste, ataḥ - etadadhikaraṇasampādyārthasya pūrvameva nirṇītatvāt, svecchāto dehayogādyaniyatikathanam – svasaṅkalpāt muktātmano dehasambandha tadabhāvayoranyatara- niyamabhāvasya asmin abhāvādhikaraṇe sthāpanam piṣṭapeṣa: pūrva piṣṭasyaiva punaḥ peṣaṇasadṛśaḥ -pūrva kṛtakaraṇavyāpāra eva ; na nūtano'gatārthaḥ, (iti śaṅkane) tanna - śaṅkārūpeṇa yaduktaṃ tat na sādhu, anyonyo.... niyatiḥ– mithoviruddhavadbhāsamānānāṃ śrutīnāṃ (saśarīratvāśarīratvādau viṣaye ) tātparyavyavasthā, kāmataḥ svecchayā bhagavata icchayā vā, anekadeha kriyā- nānāśarīra- parigrahaprakāraḥ, ityādyaniyatikathane - evamādyaṃśeṣu ekataranaiyatyā bhāvavarṇane, sūtrakārāmi- sandheḥ śārīrakasūtrakṛto dvaipāyanasya paramatātparyāt || 24 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ ||

abhāvādhikaraṇe—śarīrasya duḥkhahetutvāt, 'muktasya śarīraṃ ne 'ti bādarimatamanūdya, sa ekadhā bhavatī 'tyādiṣu muktasya anekadhābhāvaśravaṇāt, acchedyasya niravayavasya śarīreṇa vinā anekībhāvānupapatteḥ karmakṛtaśarīrasyaiva duḥkhahetutvācca, 'saśarīra evāyamiti jaimini- matena pratikṣipya, satyasaṅkalpatvatyā icchānurodhena kadācitsaśarīraḥ, aśarīraśca kadācit- iti svamatamupanyastam ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||
yadyapyukto vimuktaḥ paratanurapṛthasiddha ityatra pūrva
vyāpārāṃśe tathā'pi śranamiha paramaṃ sāmyamakṣobhaṇīyam |
saṅkalpādeva sarvotthatirapi hi tataḥ syāditi pratyavasthāṃ
kṛntatyanye'dhikāre kṛtimaditarayoḥ sthāpayan bhogasāmyam || 25 ||

yadyapi vastutaḥ, vimuktaḥ – labdhasaṃsāra vimokaścetanaḥ, paratanuḥ apṛthaksiddha iti- "paramātmaśarīrabhūtaḥ, śarīratvakṛtatada pṛthaksiddhimāṃśceti, atra pūrvam – etatpādīye prāktane avi- bhāgena dṛṣṭatvādhikaraṇe, uktaḥ– vicārya sthāpitaḥ, tathā'pi tasyāṃśasya siddhatve'pi, iha śrutam — upaniṣadi muktaprakaraṇe ('nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti ' iti ) paramasāmyam - jīvasya muktasya paramātmanā saha sarvātmanā sāmyam, vyāpārāṃśe'pi — jagatsṛṣṭi - tanniyamanādau bhagavadvyāpārarūpeṃśe'pi anvīyamānam, akṣobhaṇīyam – avarjanīyameva bhavati (na hi kiṃci- daṃśa parityāge paramasāmyaṃ sidhyati ; anyathā paramatvaviśeṣaṇa nairarthakyāt), tataḥ uktāddhetoḥ (jagadvyāpārasyā'pi muktātmani saccāt ), sarvotthitirapi – manorathagocarapitrā disarvārthapūrti- rapi, saṅkalpādeva hi syāt – bhagavata iva asyāpi icchāmātrādeva hi sidhyet iti pratyava- sthām – evamākārāṃ pūrvapakṣyāśaṅkām, antye adhikāre – śārīrakaśāstrasya antimabhūte asmin jagadvyāpāra va rjādhikaraṇe, kṛtimaditarayoḥ – jagadvyāpāravataḥ īśvarasya tadabhāvavato'pi jīvasya ca, bhogasāmyaṃ sthāpayan – bhogāṃśe - paramātmasvarūpa tadguṇānāṃ nirantarānubhavāṃśe availakṣaṇyakṛtaṃ śrutyuktaṃ paramasāmyaṃ abādhitaṃ samīkurvan, kṛntati - nirmūlayati || 25 ||

sāyujyaṃ bhogasāmyaṃ samagaṇi nipuṇaiḥ śabdaśaktyādyabādhāt
tacca vyāpārasāmye tvasati na ghaṭate svakriyāsvādahāneḥ | tasmānmuktasya sṛṣṭiprabhṛtirapi jagadvyāpṛtirbrahmatulyā

maivaṃ tallakṣaṇaṃ sā kathamanugamatastasya cā'nyasya ca syāt || 26 ||

sāyujyam – sāyujyaśabdārthaḥ, śabdaśaktyādyabādhāt - tacchandāvayavavyutpatteḥ abādha- nena siddhiṃ nimittokṛtya, (yujyata iti yuk – bhogāya puruṣeṇa sambadhyamānaṃ bhogyabhūtaṃ vastu | samānaṃ - avelakṣaṇaṃ, yuk-bhogyaṃ yeṣāṃ te sayujaḥ | teṣāṃ bhāvaḥ sāyujyam — iti śabdalabhyo - yaugiko'rthaḥ ) nipuṇaiḥ śabdārthavyutpatti kuśalaiḥ, bhogasāmyaṃ samagaṇi bhogāśe availakṣaṇya- rūpameveti niścitam ; na tu svarūpābhedarūpamiti, tacca – bhogasāmyāmidhaṃ sāyujyaṃ ca, vyāpāra-

sāmye asati tu – jīva- paramātmanoḥ vyāpārāṃśe samastre avidyamāne punaḥ, na ghaṭate- naiva saṅgataṃ bhavati, svakriyāsvādahāneḥ – svayāpāraṃ vicintya ānandānubhavasyā'pi bhogāntargata- tayā svīyavyāpārasyaiva muktātmanyabhāve taccintanapūrvakā nandasyāpyabhāvena bhogasāmyasya asiddheḥ, tasmāt - bhogasāmyasya nirvahaṇītvāt, muktasya brahmasāyujyaṃ prāptavataścetanasya, sṛṣṭiprabhṛtiḥ - sṛṣṭi niyamanādirūpā, jagadvayāpṛtirapi jagatsambandhivyāpārasiddhirapi, brahmatulyā - paramātma- sāmyāvahā avaśyābhyupagamyā – (iti śaṅkane), maivam – jagadvyāpṛtyanvayenā'pi brahmatulyatva - nirvahaṇaṃ naiva sādhu, tallakṣaṇaṃ sā - paramātmalakṣaṇabhūtā jagadvyāpṛtiḥ, tasya anyasya ca- tallakṣaṇalakṣyabhūtasya paramātmanaḥ, lakṣyetarasya muktātmano jīvasya ca, anugamataḥ – anugatenaiva rūpeṇa, kathaṃ ca syāt – kathaṃ vā bhavitumarhati ? ('yato vā imānī' tyādilakṣaṇasya jīvabrahmo- bhayasādhāraṇatve brahmalakṣaṇatvacyutiḥ ; asādhāraṇasyaiva lakṣaṇatvāt | brahmalakṣaṇatve'pi jīvānvaye ativyāptiriti vaighaṭhyāt) || 26||

kathyante sṛṣṭivākye kvacidapi na jagatkāraṇatvena muktāḥ
prādhītaḥ kāmacāro bhavatu na jagadārambhakatvaṃ tataḥ syāt | sarvākāropabhogyeśvaraviṣayadhiyaḥ sāmyato bhogasāmyaṃ

yujyetā''nandavallyā samagaṇi vibhunā''nandamātre samatvam ||27||

kvacidapi sṛṣṭivākye - śrutyantaḥpātini jagatsṛṣṭikāraṇābhidhāyini kasminnapi vākye, muktāḥ - nivṛta saṃsārāścetanāḥ, jagatkāraṇatvena na kathyante jagatsṛṣṭinirvāhakatayā naiva prati- pādyante, kāmacāraḥ – muktātmanām akarmavaśaḥ svacchandaḥ lokeṣu viśiṣṭaḥ sañcāraḥ, prādhīto bhavatu – ' imān lokān kāmānnī kāmarūpyanusañcaran' ityevaṃ śrutau sphuṭamāmnāto'stu nāma, tataḥ kāmacārasya śrutāvāmnānamātreṇa jagadārambhakatvaṃ na syāt — muktānāṃ jagatkāraṇatvaṃ naiva sidhyet ; ( na hi jagati svacchandaḥ sañcāro jagatsarge hetutāmāpādayati sañcarataḥ ; tādṛśānvaya--vyatirekayorabhāvāt ) sarvākāro... dhiyaḥ – svasvarūpeṇa svagatairakhilairguṇairapi ca anubhāvyānandasvarūpasya paramātmano viṣayiṇyāḥ buddheḥ, sāmyataḥ paramātmani muktātmani ca avilakṣaṇatvataḥ, bhogasāmyam – jīva parayorbhogāvailakṣaṇyam, yujyet — nirvyūḍhaṃ bhavati, ānandavallyām – ānandavallīnāmake taittirīyopaniṣadbhāge, vibhunā – paramātmanā saha, ānandamātre samatvaṃ samagaṇi' brahmaṇa ānandaḥ, śrotriyasya ca' ityevam ānandāṃśe eva availakṣaṇyaṃ nirūpitaṃ vartate ; na anyāṃśe'pi || 27 ||

niṣkāmaśrotriyasyā'pyatidiśati sukhaṃ mānuṣānandatulyaṃ
sā''nandān vaktukāmā śataguṇamadhikānā viriñca krameṇa tattadbhogāt tvadiṣṭaṃ kathamidamavaraṃ kaṣṭamiṣṭaṃ (hi) ca mukte

nityānande mukunda priyajana sadṛśe hyekadeśānuvādaḥ || 28 ||

āviriñcam – manuṣyamārabhya prajāpatiparyantam, krameṇa – uttarottaram, madhikān — ekaikasya śataguṇane yāvanto bhavanti tāvantaḥ atiśayitān ānandān - sukha- viśeṣān, vaktukāmā - pratipādayituṃ pravṛttā sā taittirīyānandavallī niṣkāmaśrotriya- syāpi akāmahataśrotriyasyā'pi ( aupādhikaprākṛtasarvasukhāśā vidūrasya anaupādhikamahānanda- manasya muktātmano'pi ) mānuṣānandatulyaṃ sukham - manuṣyānanda sadṛśamānandam, atidiśati- tatsadṛśavacanādbodhayati ; na tu sākṣātatsvarūpaṃ vadati, tattadbhogāt — śataguṇitamanuṣyādyānandā- pekṣayā, avaram iṣṭam – nyūnatvena saṃmanyamānam idam – manuṣyānandādipadābhidheyaṃ sukhajātam, kaṣṭam – nyūnatvā deva duḥkharūpam, mukte - muktātmani anaupādhikaparamānandamā gini, kathaṃ tvadiṣṭam — muktāvānandatāratamyavādinastava saṃmataṃ kathaṃ vā yuktaṃ syāt !, (mukte avarānandasyā- yogāt | vaiṣamya tāratamyādikaṃ sarvam upādhyanuṣaṅgavata eva khalu yuktam | vaiṣamyaprayojaka- sarvopādhividhurasya, ata eva muktasya vaiṣamyāderaprasakteḥ | prasaktau vā muktatvāyogāditi | ataḥ atideśārthaḥ evaṃ varṇanīyā) mukunda priyajanasadṛśe - kāladeśāparicchedena bhagavato nitya- priyāṇāṃ nityasūrīṇāṃ samāne, nityānande - avināśyānanda bhāgini muktātmani, ekadeśānu- vādaḥ śate pañcāśannyāyena sāgare jalabinduvat samudāye eka deśa bhūtasyānandasyā va yutyaḥ pṛthakkṛtya anuvādarūpā satī śrutyuktā saṅgacchate - iti || 28 ||

śataguṇa-

ānandānantyamāha śrutiriha hi yato vāca ityādikā'syāḥ viśrāntiḥ śrāntimātrāditi ca nigaditaṃ yāmunācāryavaryaiḥ |
nānando mitaḥ syādvidhiśatavacanepyasya yaṣṭyā nabhovat
taccoktaṃ bhūbhṛdṇvorudadhipatitayormajjane ko viśeṣaḥ || 29 ||

hi— yataḥ, iha — brahmānandaparigaṇanārthe prakaraṇe. yato vāca ityādikā śrutiḥ - yato vāco nivartante, aprāpya manasā saha, ānandaṃ brahmaṇo vidvān' ityevamādirūpā taittirīyaśrutiḥ, ānandānantyamāha - bhāgavataḥ ānandasya kālato deśato vā vāṅmanasābhyāṃ vā kenāpyupādhinā aparicchinnatāṃ vadati, asyāḥ viśrāntiḥ – bhagavadguṇavarṇanapravṛttāyā vedavāco viratiḥ, śrāntimātrāt — varṇanavyāpārāyatāt āyastatvādeva kevalaṃ; na tu varṇana pūrteḥ

iti—ityevam, yāmunācāryavaryaiḥ—bhagavadrāmānujaparamācāryaiḥ ' āḻavandār ' aparanāmadheyaiḥ, nigaditaṃ ca - niṣkṛṣyoktaṃ ca vartate vidhiśata- vacane'pi — śatasaṃkhyān prajāpatīn nidarśya tacchataguṇitānandavattāyā: brahmaṇi kathane kṛte'pi, asya ānandaḥ– prastutasya brahmaṇa ānandātiśayaḥ, na hi mitaḥ syāt – naiva khalu parimāṇataḥ iyattayā pradarśito bhavet, yaṣṭayā nabhovat – kenacana daṇḍena ākāśaṃ pramātuṃ yatnaḥ kṛtaścet ! tādṛśaiḥ koṭikoṭisaṃkhyākaiḥ daṇḍapramāṇairatidiṣṭairapi yathā vā ākāśasya iyattā naivoktā bhavet tadvadeva paramātmano'pīti, tacca uktam - tasminviṣaye nidarśanamapi sphuṭīkṛtam, udadhipati- tayoḥ – mahājalaughe sāgare prāptapatanayoḥ, bhūbhṛdaṇvoḥ parvata- paramāṇvoḥ, majjane - antarni - majjanataḥ svarūpatirodhānaviṣaye, ko viśeṣaḥ - taratamabhāvaḥ kīdṛśo vā'sti, na kopyasti, ko majjatoraṇukulācalayorviśeṣaḥ ' stotraratne lo. 8) ||29|| viśva muktasya deho na khalu tadapṛthaksiddhyabhāvopalambhā- nopādānaṃ tato'sau kathayitumucitaḥ sarvaśaktitvahāneḥ | nā''tmā'śakyaṃ cikīrṣettaditaraviṣaye nirvighātā tadicchā

vyāpāre bhidyamāne'pyaviṣamarasatā dṛśyate vā'pi loke || 30 ||

viśvam — jagat, muktasya – prāptamokṣasya cetanasya, na khalu dehaḥ – naiva ādheyatva- vidheyatvaśeṣatvaiḥ śarīrabhāvaṃ prāptaḥ, tadapṛtha lambhāt — taccharīratvaniścāyikāyāḥ tada- pṛthaksiddheḥ (pṛthasthitipratipattyanaītve sati tatsahabhāvenaiva sthitipratipattimattāyāḥ) rāhityasyaiva jagati darśanāt, viśva hi cetanācetanātmakaṃ paramātmā pṛthaksiddhinaiyatyavat | ato hi paramātmo- pādānakaṃ ca) tataḥ svāpṛthaksiddha viśvakatvābhāvānmuktasya, asau - muktātmā, na upādānaṃ kathayitumacitaḥ—naiva jagata upādānakāraṇatvena vaktumarhaḥ, sarvaśaktitvahāneḥ mukte, upādānatva niyatasya sarvaśaktatvasya abhāvāt, (evaṃ sthite ) ātmā - muktaścetanaḥ, aśakyam- svenāsādhyam, na cikīrṣet naiva kartumabhilaṣet, taditaraviṣaye – aśakyetarasmin arthe (arthe'rthe) tadicchā - muktasamavetā icchā, nirvidhātā - paramātmanā anyenā'pi ca na prāpya pratibandhā, vyāpāre bhidyamāne'pi – gṛhakṛtyeṣu pākavyāpāre, tadanyasmin kṣetrarakṣaṇādau vā vyāpāre gṛhavartināṃ janānāṃ anyānyasmin vidyamāne'pi, aviṣamarasatā – pakvānna- bhojanādau viṣaye aikarasyam — rasa tāratamyābhāvaḥ, loke kvāpi dṛśyate – laukikeṣu

tattat sevā viśeṣa sthirapariṇamitaḥ sārvabhaumaprasādaḥ
sūte bhṛtyasya sāmyaṃ khayamanabhimatasvā micidvaikavarjam | evaṃ devaṃ dayāluṃ śaraṇamupagatairlabhyate'nanyala bhyā

nirdhūtāvṛttiśaṅkā nirupadhikarasā savyavasthāsvavasthā || 31 ||

bhṛtyasya – sevā pravṛttasya tattatsenā.... mitaḥ - mahārājaniyogataḥ prāptasya tasya tasya sevārūpasya kāryaviśeṣasya niraparādhānuṣṭhānena labdharūpaḥ, sārvabhaumaprasādaḥ — atiśayitaḥ rājānugrahaviśeṣaḥ, anami...varjam – rājñaḥ tadbhṛtyabhūtasya svasya ca asaṃmataṃ yat chatracāmararādi- sārbabhaumalāñchanaṃ tadvihāya, sāmyam - vihārāsanabhojanādau viṣaye rājñā samānabhogatvam, svayaṃ bhūte viśiṣṭajanapreraṇādikaṃ vinā'pi svata eva sampādayati, evam – bhṛtyaḥ sārvabhaumamiva, dayāluṃ devam – karuṇaikarasaṃ śriyaḥ patiṃ paramātmānam śaraṇamupagataiḥ– rakṣakatayā gatitvena ca prapannaiścetanaiḥ, ananyalabhyā - itarairduṣprāpā, nirdhūtāvṛttiśaṅkā – (bhavamarukāntāraṃ prati ) punarāvṛttisambhāvanāgandhavidūrā, nirupādhikarasā - sarvopādhivinirmuktānandaikasvabhāvā, savya- basthā — anatikrāntamaryādā, avasthā - viśiṣṭā svasvabhāvāyattā daśā, labhyate svayameva saṃpadyate || 31 ||

atrāhuḥ sūtramantyaṃ pṛthagadhikaraṇaṃ kecidāñjasyalābhā-

dastyāvarte niṣedhye tvimamiti hi padaṃ tena śaṅkotthiteti | bhāṣyādau tanna dṛṣṭaṃ bhavati ca sugamaṃ pūrvaśeṣatvamasya
syādāvṛttiprasaṅgādapi nikhilajagannirmitau sāmyaśaṅkā || 32 ||

kecit — śārīrakārthacintāpravṛttāḥ kecanā'cāryāḥ, atra antyaṃ sūtram - asminnadhi- karaṇe'ntimaṃ anāvṛttisūtram, āñjasyalābhāt – sūtra pravṛttisāmañjasya lābhaṃ vicintya pṛthagadhikaraṇam – saptamamadhikaraṇam, āhuḥ- varṇayanti, hi-yataḥ, niṣedhye āvarte - muktātmanaḥ āvṛtyaviṣayatayā bodhye āvartarūpe'rthe imamiti padam asti - 'imaṃ mānavamāvarta nā''vartante' iti yatkicidāvartaviśeṣaparaṃ imāmitipadaṃ śrutau upalabhyate, tena śaṅkā utthi- teti - tena hetunā manvantararūpe āvarte punarāvṛttiḥ sambhāviteti śaṅkā udbhūteti adhikaraṇā- ntarābaśyakatāyāṃ hetumapi vadanti, tat — adhikaraṇāntaratvam, bhāṣyādau na dṛṣṭam - śrībhāṣye tadvayākhyādau vā naiva prastutamasti asya – anāvṛttisūtrārthanirṇayau pāyikatvarūpaṃ taccheṣatvam, sugamaṃ ca bhavati - suṣṭhu jñeyaṃ ca vartate, āvṛttiprasaṅgādapi - muktasya punarāvṛttiprasaṅgāt prāptādapi '

nikhilajagannirmitau sāmyaśaṅkā syāt – prakṛtimaṇḍalaṃ prāptasya muktasya satyasaṅkalpena parama- puruṣeṇa sākaṃ nikhila jagannirmāṇāśe samatvāpattiśaṅkā pravarteteti (tannivāraṇāya punarā- vṛttireva nirmūlyate asmin sūtre pūrvasūtrārthau vyāyeti ) || 32 ||
sargādyāvartalīlārasasahacarite daṇḍarā se niyuktaṃ

jīvaṃ devo vimucya kramata iti sayugbhāvadhanyaṃ bhunakti | karmātyantopaśānteriha na ca punarāvartayatyena mitya-
pyāmnāteḥ karmamūlākṣayaphalakathanādyutthaśaṅkopaśāntiḥ || 33 ||
sargādyatra rite - punaḥ punarāvartyamānasṛṣṭisthitisaṃhārātmakānanya preritasvaprītyartha- vyāpārajanitasukha viśeṣa sahabhā vini,

daṇḍa se daṇḍakrīḍākarmasadṛśe karmaṇi saṃsārāvarte jīvam - jīvātmānam . deva: – jagallīlādhurandharaḥ īśvaraḥ,

niyuktam - yojitam,

kramataḥ yathāyogyaṃ niyatayā rītyā vimucya āvartabhramaṇādvimuktaṃ kṛtvā sayugbhāva- dhanyam – svānugṛhītena sāyujyena kṛtārthamāpādya ca bhunakti iti taṃ svayameva pālayatīti, enam - prāpitasāyujyaṃ muktātmānam karmātyantopaśānteḥ sarveṣāmapi karmaṇāṃ saṃsāra praveśa hetūnāṃ niravaśeṣonmūlitatvāddhetoḥ, iha prakṛtimaṇḍale, punaḥ —

punaḥ nāvartayati bhūya: parivṛttyāśrayaṃ na karoti bhagavān ityapi āmnāteḥ - ityevamapi śrutau pāṭhāt jñāpakāt,

karmamūlā ..... śāntiḥ – 'akṣayyaṃ ha vai cāturmāsyayājinaḥ sukṛtam - ityādirūpeṇa cātu- rmāsyādikriyājanya phalānāṃ akṣayyatāpratipādanapareṇa śrutivākyādinā upacaritārthena cira- kālāvasthāyitvamātratātparyakeṇa utpannā ' na ca punarāvartate ' ityādāvapi dīrghakālā- ntarabhāvyāvṛttitātparya katvaśaṅkāyāḥ anupacaritamukhyarūpavāstavikā nāvṛttipratipādanataḥ parihāraḥ anena sūtreṇa kriyata iti, na adhikaraṇāntaratvaprasaktiriti || 33 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṃgrahaḥ |

jagadvyāpāravadhikaraṇe - muktasya bhagavatā paramasāmyāpattiśravaṇāt jaganniyama- nādikamapi muktasyaiśvaryam – ityāśaṅkaya

jaganniyamanapratipādanapareṣu paramātmanaḥ prakṛtatvāt muktasya asannihitatvācca, bhogamātra sāmyaliṅgācca, jagadvyāpāravarja mityuktam ||
anāvṛttyadhikaraṇe—bhagavatsvātantryāt muktasya punarāvṛttiḥ sambhavati - ityāśaṅkya,

'imaṃ mānavamāvarta nāssvartante ' iti tadanāvṛttiśravaṇāt, āśritavyā mugdhasya bhagavataḥ tannivartanasaṅkalpāsambhavācca na muktasya punarāvṛttiḥ - ityupasaṃhṛtam ||

svecchayā sarvasambhoga : mokṣasya ca phalodayaḥ | ityevamarthajātaṃ syāt pādenānena cintitam || 8 ||

muktau cetanasya, pūrvam anādikarmaṇā tirohitasya guṇāṣṭaka viśiṣṭasvasvarūpasyaiva āvirbhāva eva 'svena rūpeṇa | 'bhiniṣpadyate ' ityuktābhiniṣpattiśabdārtha : ; na tu svasminna- vidyamānarūpānnarasya parigrahaḥ, muktātmanā paramātmano'nubhavaḥ, svātmano'pyātmatayā svasmādavibhāgenaiva ; na pṛthagbhāvena, muktasyātmanaḥ svarūpam, apahatapāpmatvādibhirguṇaiḥ jñānena ca svābhāvikena viśiṣṭameva ; na ekaikātmakam muktasyātmanaḥ śrutyukte jakṣaṇa-kīḍanādi ke pitrādisampannatve ca prayatnāntaranirapekṣā bhagavatprasādāyattā satya- saṅkarātaiva prayojikā, muktātmanaḥ śrutyuktaḥ sarvavidho'pi bhogaḥ, bhagavatsaṅkalpānuguṇasva- saṅkavrvāyattaśarīrasampattikkṛtaḥ, (tathaiva aśarīratayā brahmānubhavaścobhayamapi) muktasya sarva- bandhavinirmokasya phalabhūtaḥ yaḥ bhagavadasādhāraṇajagadvyāpārādīta reṣāṃ muktaiśvaryāṇāṃ lābhaḥ tadyoga:, apunarāvṛtipahitaḥ — itīdam arthaṣaṭkam turyaturyeṇa phalapādena samyagvicārya nirṇītamiti || 7, 8||

iti śrīmatseneśvarācāryaviracitaḥ śārīrakanyā ya kalāpasaṅgrahaḥ
rāmabhadrāya grathita vivaraṇasahitaḥ samāptaḥ || seneśvarāyairacitaśārīrakasannyāyasaṅgrahasyā'sya | śrīrāmabhadravibudharacitā vivṛtiriyaṃ samāptimagāt ||
vidyarthijanatoṣāya racitā vivṛttiḥ śubhā | śrīhareḥ prītaye bhūyāt sarveṣāṃ ca sadā śubham ||

śeṣatvam - itaradāsyam, duḥkham īkṣitaṃ nanu pratikūlatāṃ pratipadyamānameva sarvatra loke dṛṣṭam, ataḥ śeṣabhāvasya pratikūlatvenaiva dhruvamanubhūtatvāt, mukteṣu sarvaduḥkha- vinirmuktatvarūpamuktattvavatsu cetanepu, tat na yuktam- bhagavaccheṣatvamapi nā'bhyupagamā īm (iti śaṅkane) maivam - muktidaśāyāṃ bhagavaccheṣatvasya duḥkharūpatvaṃ tataḥ muktātmani taccheṣa- tvānabhyupagamaścetyubhayaṃ na sādhu, aupaniṣadam - upaniṣadavagatam, mādhavadāsyam – śriyaḥ patyau śeṣatvam bhūyaḥ priya jāgarti - atyantaṃ prītyāspadaṃ sat vijṛmbhate, ataḥ - asmāddhetoḥ, tadduḥ khatāsādhanam — bhagavaddāsye pratikūlatā kalpanam (ānumānikam ) ), dharmigrāhakamāna dhikkṛtam - niduḥkhatvena mokṣātmakārthagraha janakena 'na rogaṃ nota duḥkhatām iti śrutipramāṇena prāptabādham, yadvā -- yadi ca tadvyatiriktadāsyam – bhagavadanyaṃ prati śeṣatvam asukhaṃ sādhyeta – duḥkharūpamiti anumānataḥ kalpyeta tadā tat - bhagavaditara- śeṣattrasya duḥkharūpatvam, siddhaṃ hi loke avagatameva khalu ! (lokāvagata viṣaye anumānasyā - pravṛttiḥ ; na hi jalaśaityavadyauṣṇyādikamanumānena sādhyam, siddhasādhanatāpātāt )

* sad- brahmā''tmādiśabdairabhihitamajaḍaṃ chāgapaśvādinītyā tastvaṃ nārāyaṇākhyaṃ niradhikamasamaṃ sevyamekaṃ mumukṣoḥ | ārabhyaiko ha vā ityupaniṣadi mahatyāṃ yato jāyate ce-

tyantānnārāyaṇasyopaniṣadi vidhipūrvātmasṛṣṭatvabodhaḥ || 104 ||
saddbrahmātmādiśabdaiḥ – 'sadeva somyedamagra āsīt ' ityādau sacchabdena, 'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma' ityādau brahmaśabdena 'ātmā vā idameka evāgra āsīt' ityādau

ātmaśabdena, ( evam akṣaraparatattva-paramātmādiśabdairapi ) chāgapaśvādinītyā
abhihitam – 'agnīṣomīyaṃ paśumālabhete 'ti sāmānyākāraṃ paśutvamāśritya paśuśabdena pratipanno yo'rthaḥ sa eva, 'chāgasya banāyā medasaḥ' iti mantravarṇe chāgaśabdenokta iti hetunā chāgākhyaḥ paśureva agnīṣomīyālambhanaviṣaya iti yathā mīmāṃsakairniścitaḥ ; tadvat sāmānya kāreṇoktvā viśeṣe prāptaparyavasānam niradhikam asamam – svoktṛṣṭasvasadṛśa- vastvantararahitam, nārāyaṇākhyam ajaḍam ekaṃ tattvam - nārāyaṇapadavācyaṃ svayaṃjyotirūpam,

*

pradhānamekameva paraṃ tatvam, mumukṣoḥ sevyam – saṃsāra niṃgaḍāt svātmānaṃ mocayitu- kāmasya cetanasya svābhilaṣitamokṣasādhana
mahatyāmupaniṣadi – mahopaniṣadi 'eko ha vā

( bhakti - prapattirūpa ) sevā viṣayabhūtam, eko havā' iti ārabhya - ' eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt ' iti prakṛtya, jāyate cetyantāt – ' puruṣo jāyate ' ityetadantāt bhāgāt, (tathā) nārāyaṇasyopaniṣadi nārāyaṇopaniṣadākhye ' ambhasyapāre ' ityādipraśne ca yataḥ - yasmāddhetoḥ vidhipūrvātmasṛṣṭatvabodhaḥ – brahmādisamastacenanānām nārāyaṇādevotpannatva-- nirṇayo bhavati || (sarvakāraṇasyaiva ca vastunaḥ ' kāraṇaṃ tu dhyeyaḥ' iti mokṣārthepāsanaviṣayatva-- siddhiriti ) || 104 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ ||

svāvirbhāvo'pavarge nirupadhiniyata svaprakāryantadṛṣṭi- ścidrūpasyaiva tadvacchratimakuṭamitaiḥ svaprakāraiḥ sahekṣā |
saṅkalpādeva siddhistanuṣu ca niyamonmuktatā brahmasāmya-
prāptau tallakṣaṇāṃśojjhanamiti caramādhyāyaturyāṅghrisāraḥ || 34 ||

apavarge—muktidaśāyām, svāvirbhāvaḥ svasvarūpasyaiva tirohitasya tirodhānāpagamena prakāśaḥ na anyarūpasya, nirupadhi dṛṣṭiḥ– jīvasya nirupādhikatayā niyatatayā ca yaḥ svaṃ prati prakāribhūtaḥ paramapuruṣaḥ tatparyantasya svātmasvarūpasya muktikāle anubhavaḥ paramātmavibhakta- svasvarūpadarśanam, tadvat – svaṃ prati niyataprakāriṇaḥ paramātmano darśanamiva cidrūpasyaiva- jñānarūpasyaiva sataḥ svasya, śrutimakuṭamitaiḥ svaprakāraiḥ saha – vedāntaprameyabhūtaiḥ svaprakārabhūtaiḥ apahatapāpmatvādibhiḥ jñānena ca svaguṇaiḥ sahitatayā darśanam, saṅkalpādeva siddhi:- śrutyuktānāṃ jakṣaṇa krīḍanādīnāṃ pitrādisamutthānādīnāṃ ca bhogānāṃ sahakāryantaranirapekṣāt saṅkalpādeva sampattiḥ, tanuṣu ca niyamonmuktatā - muktasya vividheṣu deheṣu parigṛhyamāṇeṣvapi tanniyamābhāvaḥ (saśarīratvabhaśarīratvaṃ ca yathāyogyam ), brahmasāmyaprāptau talakṣaṇāṃśojjhanam - muktasya paramātmanā sākaṃ paramasāmyalābhe, parabrahmā sādhāraṇa cihnāni vihāya taditarāṃśe eva (parabrahma-tadguṇānāṃ nirupādhikānubhave eva ) paripūrṇa samatvam iti caramā sāraḥ - ityevaṃ śārīra kāntimādhyāyāntimapādā ( muktipādā) rthasārasaṅgrahaḥ arthaṣakarūpaḥ cintanīyaḥ || 34 ||

*

brahmanāḍyā niṣkrāntim- anubhūyamānādasmāt (svātma-paramātmaprakāśāvaraṇatvataḥ) śarīrātma -- kāt kārā(bandhana)gṛhāt sakāśāt suṣumnayā mokṣārthanirgamanam, supathā gatimapi - arcirādi- rūpeṇa sanmārgeṇa brahmasthānārthe gamanaṃ ca svaprakāśa ca sādhyam - tirohitasyaiva svātmanaḥ svarūpasya muktābāvirbhāvam, icchātaḥ svaṣṭasṛṣṭim – svasaṅkalpamātreṇa muktasya svābhilaṣitārtha- sampattim, parabrahmaṇā bhogamātreṇa paramaṃ sāmyamapi - muktaprāpyeṇa paramātmanā sākaṃ muktasya brahma tadguṇānubhavāṃśa evaṃ paramasāmyam – ityevaṃrūpam, antimādhyāya sādhyam - śārīrakīya- phalādhyāyapratipādyamarthajātam, māyāmatasthaḥ – advai samaya śraddhāluḥ kathamiva ghaṭayet — kena vā prakāreṇa saṅgataṃ cintayet ? na kathamapi || 35 ||

mānāmānaprabhedaprabhṛtivibhajanādādime karmabhāge

nānā vā devatetyādyanupariṣṭhanānmadhyame devatāṃśe | bhāge'smin bhedadharmaprabhṛtikathanataḥ saugatādeśca bhaṅgā-

nmīmāṃsāyāṃ trikāṇḍyāṃ kvacidapi na mṛṣāvādagandhāvakāśaḥ || 36 ||

ādime - karma devatā brahmakāṇḍātmakasya ādyabhūte, karmabhāge karmamīmāṃsābhāge, mānāmāna.... vibhajanāt – pramāṇataditarādiviṣaya vibhāga karaṇāt madhyame - kāṇḍadvayamadhya- pātini, devatāṃ devatākāṇḍe, nānā vā paṭhanāt – 'nānā vā devatā pṛthaktvāt ityādinā devatānāṃ bhedasyaiva spaṣṭaṃ nirūpaṇāt, asmin bhāge – śārīrakacaturadhyāyīrūpe brahmabhāge ca bhedadharmaprabhṛtikathanataḥ — jīvaparamātmabhedasya paramātmā sādhāraṇadharmasyetyādeḥ artha- viśeṣasya nirūpaṇataḥ, saugatādeśva bhaṅgāt – saṃvṛtivādināṃ bauddhānāṃ spaṣṭaṃ nirākaraṇādapi hetoḥ, trikāṇḍyāṃ mīmāṃsāyām - kāṇḍatrayavatyāṃ karmamīmāṃsāyām, devatāmīmāṃsāyāṃ brahma- mīmāṃsāyāmapi ca kvacidapi kasminnapi bhāge, mṛṣāvādagandhāvakāśo na - mithyāvāda - vāsanāyā api na prasaṅgaḥ || 36 ||

trayyantodanvadantasmṛtiriyamuditā deśikaiḥ karṇadhārai- muktyarthajñānapāraṃ gamayati viśadaṃ tvanyadīkṣyaṃ vimuktau |
kartavye kalpakāraiḥ kacidabhidadhire kṛtyasandehabhedā-
staśve'pyevaṃ kvacit syāt tadapi na vitathaḥ svopayuktāṃśabodhaḥ || 37 ||

karṇadhāraiḥ–nāvikatulyaiḥ, deśikaiḥ - ācāryapravarai: mārgadarśanapravṛttaiḥ, iyam - śārīraka - mīmāṃsāpratipāditārtharūpā, trayyanto.... sṛtiḥ - vedāntamahodadheḥ antaḥ prayāṇaupayikī saraṇiḥ, uditā – evaṃ prakaṭīkṛtā ( vartate sā ca), muktyarthajñānapāram – mokṣopayogitattvajñānasya paramāvadhi, gamayati-mumukṣūn prāpayati, anyat — uktāvaśiṣṭaṃ prameyaṃ yadasti tat, vimuktau tu — mokṣadaśāyāṃ parame vyomni eva, viśadamīkṣyam – suspaṣṭaṃ darśanīyaṃ bhavati, (sarvāṃśasyā'pyatraiva niravaśeṣajñānaṃ duḥsampādam ; niravaśeṣataḥ sarvāṃśajñānābhāvamātreṇa na ko'pi mokṣopāyānuṣṭhānabhagavatprasādalābhādeḥ vighātaḥ) kalpakāraiḥ śrautārthānuṣṭhānaprakriyā niṣkarṣaṇa- pravṛttaiḥ kalpasūtrakṛdbhiḥ bodhāyanāpastambādibhirapi, kvacit kartavye – anuṣṭheyārthaviṣaye kasmiścana, kṛtya sandehabhedāḥ – anuṣṭhānānirṇayena evaṃ vā evaṃ veti saṃśayaviśeṣā eva, abhidadhire – uktāḥ sandṛśyante kalpasūtreṣu, tatve'pi - vedāntavedyatattvārthaviṣaye'pi, kvacit — sthalaviśeṣe, evaṃ syāt – sandehaviśeṣo bhavennāma, tadapi tathā'pi, svopayuktāṃśavodhaḥ – upāyaparijñāna- tadanuṣṭhānādyupayoginoṃ'śasya vipratipattyādirahitaḥ niṣkṛṣṭo bodhaḥ, na vitathaḥ – naiva viphalo bhavitumarhati (yāvallābhaṃ santoṣasya vivekijanasampādyatvāt ) || 37 ||

śrutyantaikāntatarkakramagarimadhṛtau tūlavacchelavarga-

statsiddhabrahmabodhadyumaṇiruci tamasstoma kalpo'nyajarūpaḥ ( kalpaḥ) | mokṣopāyaikarājye taditaravidhayaḥ kiṅkaratvaṃ bhajante
muktānandāmṛtaiko dadhipṛṣata kaṇaspardhino'nye pumarthāḥ || 38 ||

śrutyantaikā .... dhṛtau — upaniṣadarthanirṇayai kaphalāḥ ye tarkāḥ śārīraka niṣpannāḥ, tadgatasya rītiviśeṣasya dhāraṇe cetasi kriyamāṇe, śailavargaḥ – śilāstomavat kevalapāruṣyapradhānaḥ parama- puruṣārthasādhanā paryavasitaḥ dārśanikāntarasambandhī tarkavargaḥ niḥsāratvāt kārpāsatūlasadṛśa eva, tatsiddhana.... ruci — tādṛśaśārīraka niṣpanna tarkabalā hita (aupaniṣada) brahmajñānarūpasya bhāskarasya kāntau bāhyāntarakaraṇeṣu vyāpṛtāyāṃ satyām ('ruk' śabdāt saptamī 'ruci' iti ) tatsannidhau, anyajalpaḥ – śārīrakārthaprāvaṇyarahitānāṃ itareṣāṃ vākprasaraḥ (vividhaḥ pakṣā ḍambaro vā ), tamaḥ stomakalpaḥ – anghatamasasadṛśa eva; (svābhāvikāntarbāhyakaraṇapravṛtterapi taiḥ pakṣaiḥ teṣāṃ vāgbhiśva mālinyāpādanāt ) mokṣopāyaikarājye paramapuruṣārthabhūtāpavargopāyānuṣṭhānasāmrājye pravartamāne sati , taditaravidhayaḥ - mokṣopāya vidhibhinnāḥ yajñadānādividhayaḥ sarve, kiṅkaratvaṃ bhajante

mokṣopāyabhūtabhaktyā disādhanopakārakāḥ santaḥ mokṣopāyavidhiśeṣatāṃ āśrayanti ; na svātantrye - ṇā'vasthitiṃ labhante, anye pumarthā:- apavargādi re sarve'pi puruṣārthāḥ, muktānanda.... spardhinaḥ muktacetanānubhāvya viśiṣṭānanda rūpā mṛtamayasāgara sambandhibindvekadeśabhūtaśīkara tulyā eva; yena kenā'pi prakāreṇa na mokṣānandasamāḥ || 38 ||

pārāvaryaṃ vivicya prathamamavitathairāgamaistattvavarge
saṃsāre tīvrabhītiḥ parasamadhigame'tīva (tīvra) niṣpannarāgaḥ | kañcidvidyāviśeṣaṃ saparikaramadhiṣṭhāya śāntāntarāyaḥ
sampadya brahma bhukte nirupadhikamanāvṛttiritthaṃ śrutārthaḥ || 39 ||
śrutārthaḥ – gurusakāśe saniyamaśravaṇataḥ

15 I 'atha ya ime grāme iṣṭāpūrte dattamityupāsate te dhūmamabhisambhavanti'

atha ya ime grāme iṣṭāpūrte dattamityupāsate ' pūrvoktopāsaka vilakṣaṇāḥ ye puruṣāḥ śrautasmārtāḥ kriyānuṣṭhānaparā api santaḥ, svargādiphalakaṃ yāgādikarma, lokopakāraphalakaṃ vāpī- kūpa-taṭākādinirmāṇa, dainandinajanajīvanopayogidhānyavastragṛhakṣetrā- rāmādidānaṃ ca kurvāṇāḥ, grāma-nadgatagṛhādyavalambina eva santo jīvanti; te punaḥ svaprāpyambargādisthānaprāpakā tivā hikatayā ādau dhūmābhimāni- devatāṃ pratipadyante | teṣāṃ ca ' dhūmo rātriḥ' itya yuktaḥ panthā evaṃ ; na tvarcirādiḥ ||

ārṣeyaṃ vṛṇīte, hotāraṃ vṛṇīte' ityasyāṃśasya prāptau satyāṃ cāturmāsyaprakaraṇa- gatāt 'nārṣeyaṃ vṛṇīte, na hotāraṃ vṛṇīte' iti viśeṣavākyāt prakṛtiprāptāṃśasya pratiṣedhacintanam ; tathaiva 'yajatiṣu ye yajāmahaṃ karoti' iti yāgasāmānye prāptasya ye yajāmahakaraṇasya, 'nāḍanūyā jeṣu ye yajāmahaṃ karoti' iti anūyājākhye yāgaviśeṣe pratiṣedhacintanaṃ ca na bhavati | tathā sati, 'ārṣeyaṃ vṛṇīte- nārṣeyaṃ vṛṇīte' ye yajāmahaṃ karoti na karoti' iti ca vidhi pratiṣedhayoḥ ubhayorapyupasthititaḥ prasaktasya) vikalpasya varaṇa- tadatyāntābhāvayoranyatarasya, ye yajāmahakaraṇa- tadatyantābhāvayoranyatarasya ca aniyatapākṣikagrahaṇarūpavikalpasya, anyā - yyatvāt-nyāyyetaratvāt, aṣṭadoṣaduṣṭatayā varjanīyatvāt | api tu— kintu, vākyaśeṣaḥ syāt — pratiṣedhaparatayā bhāsamānaḥ 'nārṣeyaṃ vṛṇīte, ye yajāmahaṃ na karoti' ityubhayavidho'pi bhāgaḥ ' prakṛtivadvikṛtiḥ kārye'tyatideśavākyārthānvitasvārthakaḥ san vākyaśeṣo bhavet | tathā ca sati, idaṃ niṣedhavadavabhāsamānaṃ vākyadvayamapi, 'ārṣeyavaraṇetara prākṛti- kāmajātavatī vikṛtiḥ kāryā, anū yājetareṣu ye yajāmahaṃ kuryāt ' ityevaṃ paryudāsārthakameva bhavet | tathā ca, vidhīnāmekadeśaḥ syāt —

( yadā khalu cetanaḥ mokṣopāyabhūtenopāsanena nirantara (nuṣṭhitena, suvarṇavarṇadhye bhūtaṃ paramātmānaṃ aparokṣīkaroti ;) tadā niṣpannopāsana- tayā vidvatpadā milapyaḥ ayaṃ, svasmin saṃlagne mokṣavighaṭanasvarūpa yogye puṇya pāpākhye karmaṇī svasmānnirdhūya, karmaleparahitaḥ san, svasya svabhāvaprāptenā'pi adya ''virbhūtena svātma-paramātmanoḥ sādhāraṇena apahṛta- pāpmatvādinā guṇajātena sampannaḥ tādṛśaguṇa pūrṇatvakṛtaṃ brahmaparamasāmyaṃ prāpnoti |
' tadeva prītaye bhūtvā punarduḥkhāya jāyate | tadeva kopāya yataḥ prasādāya ca jāyate || tasmādṛḥ khātmakaṃ nāsti na ca kiṃcit sukhātmakam ||'
yataḥ, ekameva vastu kāla- deśa - vyaktatyā divailakṣaṇyamanurudhya prīti - duḥkhayorubhayorapi sādhanaṃ bhavati ; tathā aviśeṣeṇa kopa- prasādayoścā- dhāyakaṃ bhavati ; tasmāddhetoḥ 'idaṃ sukhātmaka' miti vā idaṃ duḥkhātma- kamiti vā avyabhicaritasvarūpasvabhāvatayā kimapi vastu naiva nirūpaṇāī

vyomni vidyotamāne sūryamaṇḍale, ' ya eṣo'ntarāditye hiraṇmayaḥ puruṣa:' iti, puruṣasya dakṣiṇe netramaṇḍale, ' ya eṣo'ntarakṣiṇi puruṣo dṛśyate ' iti ca śrutyavagataḥ āditya khyaḥ puruṣaḥ, akṣistha puruṣaśceti yadasti, tat nirvikāratvāt satyapadābhidheyam ||
218 'tadyathā puṣkarapalāśa āpo na lipyante evamevaṃvidi pāpaṃ karma na śliṣyate'

yathā padmapatre patitāḥ salilabindavaḥ naiva liptā bhavanti, tathaiva bhagavadupāsake puruṣe sukṛtaduṣkṛtākhyaṃ mokṣavirodhitvāt pāpapadābhidheyaṃ sarvamapi karma naiva liptaṃ bhavati | ātmalābhāntarāyatāpannaṃ naiva bhavati || ' tadyatraitat suṣuptaḥ'

pṛ.saṃ.

' tadyathā mahāpatha ātata ubhau grāmau gacchatīmaṃ cāmuṃ ca; evamevaita ādityasya raśmaya ubhau lokau gacchantīmaṃ cāmuṃ ca | amuṣmādādityāt pratāyante ta āsu nāḍīṣu sṛptāḥ | ābhyo nāḍībhyaḥ pratāyante ; temuṣminnāditye sṛptāḥ ' 352 tasmāt, yathā dīrghaḥ (mahādhvā ) panthāḥ, sambaddhaṃ sannikṛṣṭaṃ grāmaṃ viprakṛṣṭaṃ grāmāntaraṃ ceti grāmadvayaṃ saṅgato bhavati ; ata eva sthānadvayaṃ vyāpya bartate; tathaiva prastutāḥ sūryasya marīcayo'pi, asmadāśritaṃ imaṃ martya- lokaṃ aprāptaṃ prepsitaṃ ādityākhyaṃ paraṃ lokaṃ ca sambadhya vartamānāḥ loka. dvayavyāptā bhavanti | viprakṛṣṭādādityalokāt sarvatra prāptaprasarā bhavanti | te ca sūryamarīcayaḥ hṛdayasambandhinīṣu nāḍīṣu sambaddhāḥ | tathā tābhyo nāḍībhyo niṣkramya prasṛtāḥ viprakṛṣṭe āditye'pi sambaddhasvarūpāḥ || ' tadyatheṣīkatūlamagnau protaṃ pradūyetaivaṃ hāsya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante
tasmāt vahnispṛṣṭam iṣīkāyāḥ tūlaṃ sparśamātreṇaiva dagdha nāma-. mātrāvaśeṣaṃ bhavet, na svarūpāvasthānam ; tathaiva brahma viduṣaḥ jñāninaḥ sarvāṇyapi pāpāni vidyārūpeṇāgninā niravaśeṣaṃ dagdhā bhavanti || tadyo'haṃ so'sau yo'sau so'ham '
upāsako yo'haṃ pratyagātmā'smi, so'ham madupāsya paramātmanaḥ sakāśāt abhinna eva | tathā madupāsyaḥ paramātmā yo'sti, saḥ upāsakāt pratyagātmano mattaḥ sakāśāt abhinna eva; upāsyo- pāsaka bhūtayorjīvaparayoḥ svarūpabhede'pi śarīrātmabhāvakṛtasyā bhedasya anapāyāt yathā 'ahaṃ devadattaḥ' iti devadattapadābhilapyasya dehasya ahamarthasyātmanaśca abhedo vyavahriyate ||

tacca dhyānaṃ yogopakārakaiḥ yamaniyamāsanaprāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇākhyaiḥ ṣaḍūbhiḥ labdhasvarūpaṃ bhavati | etairaṅgaiḥ niṣpannam aṅgi dhyānam | tadeva saptamam | aṣṭamaḥ samādhistu dhyānaparyavasānabhūmiḥ ||
' tannirdhāraṇā niyamasadṛṣṭeḥ pṛthagdhya pratibandhaḥ phalam' tadvidvāṃsaḥ śrotriyāḥ aśiṣyanta ācāmantyaśitvā cācāmanti | etameva tadanamananaṃ kurvanto manyante | tasmādevaṃvida śiṣyannā- cāmedaśitvā cācāmet | etameva tadanamanagnaṃ kurute '
uktayā rītyā apāṃ prāṇaṃ prati vāsassthānīyatayā ācchādakatva- guṇayogacintanāt, vedārthacintana- tadanuṣṭhānaparāyaṇā: śiṣṭāḥ, bhojanāt prākpaścādapi ca adbhirācamanaṃ kurvanti | tena ca prāṇasyācchādanaṃ bhāvayantaḥ, taṃ prāṇamanagnaṃ kurvāṇāḥ smo vayamiti jānanti | ataḥ loke yaḥ ko'pi etattattvavettā puruṣaḥ, prāṇasyānagnatāyai bhojanaṃ kariṣyan kṛtvā ca ācamanamavaśyaṃ kuryāt ||
' tapasā brahmacaryeṇa śraddhayā vidyayā''tmānamanviṣya '
(asya vākyasya 'athottareṇa' iti ādimo bhāgaḥ | 'āditya- mabhijayante' iti antimo bhāga: |) ye punaḥ uktavilakṣaṇāḥ bhoga- vaimukhyalakṣaṇena tapasā, grāmyasukhapravṛttivai mukhyarūpeṇa brahmacaryeṇa, śraute mokṣopāye aprakampya viśvāsākhyayā śraddhayā ca sahitāḥ paramātma- śarīra bhūtasvātmaśuddhasvarūpaviṣaya kopāsanarūpayā vidyayā, sarvātmabhūtaṃ paramātmānamupāsya, tata eva ca hetoḥ uttarāyaṇākhyena arcirādivartmanā gatāḥ, brahmaprāptidvārabhūtam ādityākhyaṃ sthānaṃ prāpnuvanti ||
' taṃ pṛthivyanravīt '
('prajāpatiragnimacikīṣata taṃ pṛthivyatravīt ' iti tatratyaṃ vākyam ) prajāpatyākhyo devatāviśeṣaḥ pṛthivyāmādhāre agniṃ cetumaicchat | tadā tamuddiśya pṛthivyabhimāninī devatā kathitavatītyarthaḥ ||

yadā mriyamāṇasyā'sya jīvasya vāgrūpaṃ karmendriyaṃ svābhimāninyamau apyayamavāpnoti ; na tu svavyāpāre preritaṃ bhavati ; tathā prāṇākhyaḥ śarīradhārako vāyuḥ sāmānye mahāvāyau, cakṣurākhyaṃ ca jñānendriyam svāmimāninyāditye ca saṃśliṣṭe bhavitumarhataḥ ; naiva svīye tattadvyāpāre yojitau bhavataḥ, tadā ayaṃ cetanaḥ kaṃ vā āśrayamavalambyāvatiṣṭhate ? iti yājñavalkyamuddiśyārtabhāgaḥ papraccha ||
438 ' yatraitatpuruṣaḥ svapiti nāma satā somya tadā sampanno bhavati '

iti gamyate ||

dehādya rambhā hi ! ' tāsāṃ trivṛnaṃ trivṛtamekai kāmakarot ' iti trivṛtkaraṇam ||

ukrāmati jīve, prāṇānā tadanugatiḥ śrūyate | na ca nirāśrayāṇāṃ gatirupapadyate iti, tadāśrayabhūtānāṃ bhūtasūkṣmāṇāmapi gatiḥ abhyupagantavyā ||

jīvamaraṇakāle cakṣurādyapyayaśrutiḥ, adhiṣṭhātṛdevatāpakramaṇaparā ||
bhūtāntara saṃsṛṣṭābhiradbhiḥ sampariṣvakto jīvo rahatī' tyupapannam ||
śraddhāvastha deha viśiṭo (jīva ) somarūpa deha viśiṣṭo bhavatī' tyuktamiti gamyate | dehasya jīvaviśeṣaṇatai svarūpamya vācakaḥ śabdaḥ, viśeṣye jīva eva paryavasyati ||
iṣṭa dikāriṇaḥ anātmavittvāt, saḥ devānāṃ bhogopakaraṇatvena ihāmutra ca vartate | ārādhanaprītairdevaiḥ dattam amuṃ lokaṃ prāpya,
iha iṣṭādinā tadārādhanaṃ kurvan urakaroti |
tatra tatsamānabhogaḥ tadupakaraṇaṃ (tṛptisādhanaṃ ) bhavati ||
amuṃ lokaṃ gatāḥ, (kevaleṣṭā pūrnakāriṇaḥ ) mānuśayā eva ārohaṇaprakāreṇa dhūmarājya- parapakṣadakṣiṇāyanapanāsa pitṛlokā kāśacandra krameṇa ) prakārāntareṇa ca (candramasaḥ sthānāt ākāśavāyudhūpābhrameghakrameṇa) punarnivartante || 3-1–9)
sugva- duḥkhe hi ! puṇyapāparūpakarmaphale || ( ataḥ ) ' ramaṇīyacaraṇāḥ kapyacagṇāḥ iti caraṇaśrutiḥ karmopalakṣaṇārtheti, 'caraṇa 'śabdena puṇya-pāparūpaṃ karmā'bhidhīyate; kevalādā- cāgat sukha-duḥkhaprātyasambhavāt iti kārṣṇājinigacāryo manyate ||

ācāravata eva puṇyakarmasvadhikāra : ;

sandhyāhī no'śucirnitya manaha: sarvakarmasu |

ācārahīnaṃ na punanti vedā: ' ityādivacanebhyaḥ ||

sukṛtaduṣkṛte eva caraṇa 'śabdābhidheye, 'puṇyaṃ karmācarati ' iti karmaṇi carate: prayogāt | ācārānuminaśrutivihita sandhyāvandanādeḥ karmāntarādhikārāpādanaṃ phalam - iti bādarirācāryo manyate || 3-1-17)
aniṣṭādikāriṇāṃ yathā vidyāvidhuratvāt devayānena pathā gamanaṃ na sambhavati ; tadeva iṣṭāpūrtadattavidhuratvāt pitṛyāṇena candragamanamapi na sambhavati || dyalīkā rohāvaroddābhāvena dyalokā-
tṛtīyasthānasya, (kevala pāpakarmaṇā cetanānāṃ, saṃpūrtivacanāt, asya tṛtīyasthānasya śarīrārambhāya na pañcamāhutyapekṣā ||
puṇyakarmaṇāmapi keṣāṃcit pañcamā hutyanapekṣayā dehārambho loke smaryate draupadī- dhṛ0 dhunaprabhṛtīnām ||

( jīvasya ) suṣupti: nāḍīṣu purītati ātmani ca bhavati | tatra nāḍī-purītatoṃ prāsādakhaṭvā - sthānīyau | brahma tu paryaṅkasthānīyam | ato brahmava sākṣāt suṣuptisthānam ||
suṣuptaḥ, saṃsaranneva āyasta sarvakaraṇaḥ jñānabhogādyaśaktaḥ, viśramasthānaṃ paramātmānamupa- sampadya āśvastaḥ punarbhogāyottiṣṭhati | ( yataḥ ) suṣuptena pūrvakṛtaṃ puṇyapāparūpaṃ karma tenaiva bhoktavyam ; ya evā'haṃ suptaḥ sa eva prabuddho'mmīti anusmṛtirapi ( vartate), śabdo'pi ca (śrataḥ) suptaprabuddhaṃ sa eveti darśayati (ataḥ) suṣupta eva prabodhasamaye uttiṣṭhati ; ( nā'nyaḥ) ||
pūya śoṇitādimajjanaṃ hi | svecchākāritamapi apuruṣārtha eva ||
yataḥ śruti-smṛtiṣu paraṃ brahma, nirastanikhiladoṣatva- kalyāṇaguṇā karatvalakṣaṇopetaṃ ubhaya- -lakṣaṇamabhidhīyate ; (ataḥ ) pṛthivyātmādisthānato'pi parasya brahmaṇaḥ apuruṣārthagandho na sambhavati || sa ta ātmā'ntaryāmyamṛta: ' iti antaryāmiṇaḥ amṛtatvavacanena, svecchayā niyamanaṃ kurvataḥ (tasya) tattatsambandhaprayuktā puruṣārthapratiṣedhāt || 3-2-12, 13)

karmavaśyānāṃ karmasvabhāvānuguṇyena paramapuruṣasaṅkalpāt ekameva vastu kālabhedena puruṣa bhedena ca sukhāya duḥkhāya ca bhavati | vastusvarūpaprayukte tu tādrūpye, sarva sarvadā sarvasya, sukhāyaiva duḥkhāyaiva vā syāt | na caivaṃ dṛśyate | ataḥ, jīvasya karmavaśyatvam tattatkarmānu- dha apuruṣārthaḥ syāt | parasya tu brahmaṇaḥ svādhīnasya sa eva sambandhaḥ, tattadvicitraniyamanarūpa līlārasāyaiva syāt ||

eke śākhino'pi, ' tayoranya; pippalaṃ svādvatti anaśnannanyo abhicākaśīti' iti ekasminneva dehasaṃyoge jīvasyāpuruṣārtham parasya tu tadabhāvaṃ niyamanarūpaiśvaryāyattadīptiyogaṃ ca

3..

darśayati ca vedāntagaṇaḥ, kalyāṇaguṇākaratvaṃ nirasta nikhiladoṣatvaṃ ca |
ataḥ sarvatrā'vasthitasyā'pi brahmaṇaḥ ubhayaliṅgatvāta, sthānaprayuktā doṣāḥ na paraṃ brahma spṛśanti ||
yataḥ, nānāvidheṣu sthāneṣu sthitasyā'pi parasya brahmaṇaḥ na tatprayukta doṣabhāktvam, ana eva ' jala darpaṇādipratibimbitasūryādivat paramātmā tatra tatrāvasthito'pi nirdoṣa: ' iti śāstraṣūpamā kriyate ||

yathā ghaṭa - karakādiṣu vṛddhihāsabhākṣu pṛthak pṛthak saṃyujyamānamapyākāśaṃ, vṛddhihāsādi- doṣairna spṛśyate ; yathā ca jalādhāreṣu viṣameṣu dṛśyamāno'śumān tadgatavṛddhivāsādibhirna spṛśyate; tathā bhayaṃ paramātmā pṛthivyā daṣu nānākā reṣvacetaneṣu ca sthitaḥ, tadgata vṛddhi- hrāsādidoṣairasaṃspṛṣṭaḥ sarvatra vartamāno'pyeka eva aspṛṣṭadoṣagandhaḥ

kalyāṇaguṇākara

eva ||

yat brahmaṇaḥ prakṛtaviśeṣavatvaṃ 'neti netī 'ti pratiṣidhyate iti ; naitadupapadyate ; tathā sati bhrāntajalpitāyamānatvāt | na hi ! brahmaṇo viśeṣaṇatayā pramāṇāntarāprajñātaṃ sarva, tadviśeṣaṇatvenopadiśya, punastadeva anunmattaḥ pratiṣedhati | yataśca niṣedhānantaraṃ brahmaṇo bhūgro guṇajātaṃ bravīti ataśca prakṛtaviśeṣaṇayogitvamātraṃ brahmaṇaḥ pratiṣedhati || 3-2-21

i

jīvāstāvat satyam ; viyadādivat svarūpānyathābhāvarūpapariṇāmābhāvāt | tebhyo jīvebhyopyeṣa paramapuruṣaḥ satyam | jīvānāṃ karmānuguṇyeta jñānasaṅkocavikāsau vidyete | paramapuruṣasya tu apahatapāpmanaḥ tau na vidyete | ataḥ tebhyo'pyeva satyam ||
bhaktirūpāpannamevopāsanaṃ ( tasya parabrahmaṇaḥ ) samyakprīṇanam | ( tādṛśe ) nididhyāsane evā'sya sākṣātkāraḥ ; nānyatreti śrutismṛtibhyāmavagamyate |
yataḥ sākṣātkṛtaparabrahmasvarūpāṇāṃ vāmadevādīnāṃ darśane, jñānānandādisvarūpavat mūrtā - mūrtādiprapañcaviśiṣṭatāyā api brahmaguṇatvāvaiśeṣyaṃ pratīyate ; itaśca (neti netīti) prakṛtai- tāvatvameva pratiṣedhati, na mūrtāmūrnādiviśiṣṭatvam ||
acidvastunaḥ jīvavat pṛthaksiddhayana ī viśeṣaṇatvena, brahmāṃśatvam | viśiṣṭavastvekadeśa- tvena abhedavyavahāraḥ, viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyayoḥ svarūpa- svabhāvabhedena bhedavyavahāraśca mukhyaḥ |

tadevaṃ maṇi prati prakāśasya, vyakti prati jāteḥ, guṇinaṃ prati guṇānāṃ, ātmānaṃ prati śarīrasya ca yathā apṛthaksiddhilakṣaṇaviśeṣaṇatayā aṃśatvam ; tathā iha jīvasya acidvastunaśca brahma prati aṃśatvam ||
aṃśāṃśibhāvaḥ viśeṣaṇa viśeṣyatvenaiva || brahma | sthūlacidacidvastuviśiṣṭaṃ kāryabhūtaṃ brahma iti bhūta brahmavijñānena kāryasya jñātatā - ityādi sarvamupapannam

ataḥ sūkṣmacidacidvastuviśiṣṭaṃ kāraṇabhūtaṃ kāraṇāt kāryasyānanyatvam | kāraṇa- brahmaṇo nirdoṣatvaṃ ca rakṣitam ||

ataḥ ebhi: (saṃyogarūpacodana khyābhiḥ) pratyabhijñānāt śākhāntarepi vidyaikyam ||

yatra hi ekasmin pratipatari punaḥ śrutiḥ prakaraṇāntaraṃ ca vidyate tatrānyathānupapatyā vidheyabhedāt vidyābhedaḥ | pratipattabhede tu tatpratipatyarthatayā punaḥ śrutyādyupapatteḥ tatra na vidheyāntarasambhavaḥ ||
evaṃ sarvavedānteṣu upāsane samāne sati vedāntāntarāgnātānāṃ guṇānāṃ vedāntare upasaṃhāraḥ kartavyaḥ | yathā ekasmin vedānte śruto vaiśvānara - daharā dividyāviviśeṣaḥ guṇaḥ tadvidyāsambandhāt tadupakārarūpaprayojana siddhyarthamanuṣṭhīyate ; tathā vedāntāntarodito'pi tadvidyā- sambandhitvena tadupakārāviśeṣādupasaṃhartavyaḥ ||

pradhānasya guṇino brahmaṇaḥ sarveṣūpāsaneṣvabhedāt guṇānāṃ guṇyapṛthagbhāvāt sarvatra ānandādayastadguṇāḥ upasaṃhartavyā: |

arthasvarūpanirūpaṇadharmatvena arthapratītyanubandhinaḥ, te arthasvarūpavat sarvatrānu- vartante, te ca satyajñānānandāmalatvānantatvāni, upāsya svarūpāvagamāya sarvatrānuvartante | ye tu nirūpita svarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ kāruṇyādayo guṇāḥ pratipanna:, teṣāṃ guṇyapṛthasthitatve'pi pratītyanu- bandhitvābhāvāt ye yatra śratāḥ te tatropasaṃhāryāḥ ||
daharākāśa eka eva apahatapāpmatvā diguṇānāṃ guṇī | apahatapāpmatvādiguṇānāṃ
sa ca prathamaṃ cintitaḥ svarūpa- mātrāt guṇaviśiṣṭākārasya bhinnatvāt guṇaviśiṣṭatayā ca upāsyatvena vihitatvāt, svarūpe'numaṃhitasya apahatapāpmatvā diviśiṣṭatayā anusandhānārtham āvṛttiḥ kartavyā ||

ye

asya (sahasraśīrṣamityanuvākasya ) nikhilapara vidyopāsya nirdhāraṇaparatve bhūyāṃsi liṅgāni hayante | paravidyamu akṣara- śiva śambhu parabrahma-parajyotiḥ - paratatva paramātmādiśabdanirdiṣṭam upāsyaṃ vastu, iha taireva śabdairanūdya, tasya nārāyaṇatvaṃ vidhīyate | bhūyasīṣu vidyāsu śrutānanūdya nārāyaṇatvavidhānabhūyastvaṃ, nārāyaṇa evaṃ sarvavidyāsūpāsyam asthūlatvādi viśeṣitānandādiguṇakaṃ paraṃ brahma iti nirṇaye bahutaraṃ liṅgaṃ bhavati ||
iṣṭakaciteṣvabhiṣu yat kriyāmayaṃ yajñīyaṃ karma kriyate, tat manonirvartheṣu manavidādyagniṣu manomayamevākriyata iti vacanāt kraturapi vidyāmayo'tra pratīyate || evaṃvide vidyāmayakratumate sarvadā sarvāṇi bhūtāni cinvanti ||

pratyagātmano'pi upāsya bhūtaparabrahmaśarīratayā upāsya ko ṭenikṣiptatvāta

asya mokṣa-

daśāyāṃ yādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ tādṛgrūpa eva upāsanavelāyāmātmā anusandheyaḥ | 'yathākratuḥ asmin loke puruṣo bhavati tathetaḥ pretya bhavati' iti yathopāsanameva hi prāptiḥ śrūyate | ata prajā- pativākyādināpahatapāpmatvādiguṇaka pratyagātmaśarīrakaparamātmopāsanasya tathārūpameva prāpyam
iti prāpyākāra eva pratyagātmā anusandheyaḥ ||

.

tattatprakaraṇodita jagade kakāraṇatyā pahatapāpmatvādiviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭabrahmaviṣaya (ka) pratyayā- vṛtyavabodhinaḥ 'veda, upāsīta' ityādayaḥ śabdāḥ pratyayāvṛttirūpāḥ vidyāḥ bhindanti | brahmaprāptirūpaphala sambandhyupāsanaviśeṣā bhidhāyīni ca nirākāṅkṣāṇi vākyāni pratiprakaraṇaṃ vilakṣaṇavidyābhidhāyīni - iti niścīyate ||
brahma hi svamya parasya svayamanubhūyamānam anavadhikā tiśayamānandaṃ bhavati, sa ca tādṛśo brahmānubhavaḥ, ekayā vidyayā avāpyate cet ! kimanyayeti, na samuccayasambhavaḥ | svargāderhi deśataḥ, kālataḥ, svarūpataśca parimitatvena tatra deśa dyapekṣayā bhūyastvasambhavāt, tadarthinaḥ samuccayaḥ sambhavani || sarvāśca vidyāḥ brahmānubhavavirodhyanādikarmāvidyā nirasana mukhena brahmaprāptiphalā iti, aviśiṣṭaphalatvāt sarvāsāṃ vikalpa eva |

karmasu kartṛrjīvāt heyapratyanī kānaḥ dhikātiśayāsaṅkhyeyakalyāṇaguṇā karatvena adhi- kasya arthāntarabhūtasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ vedyatayā upadeśāt vidyātaḥ phalamityeva bādarāyaṇasya matam ||

yajñādinā vividiṣanti yajñādibhiḥ vedanaṃ prāptumicchanti iti yajñādīnāṃ jñāna- sādhanatvamavagamyate, 'asinā jighāṃsati' iti vyapadeśavat | jñānaṃ ca vākyārthajñānāda- rthāntarabhūtaṃ dhyānopāsanādiśabdavācyaṃ viśadatamapratyakṣatāpannasmṛtirūpaṃ niratiśayapriyam, aha- raharabhyāsādheyātiśayam āprayāṇādanuvartamānaṃ mokṣasādhanam || evaṃrūpaṃ dhyānam aharaharanuṣṭhīya- mānaiḥ nityanaimittikakarmabhi. paramapuruṣā rāghanarūpaiḥ paramapuruṣārādhanarūpaiḥ paramapuruṣaprasādadvāreṇa jāyata jāyata iti

'yajñādinā vividiṣantī' ti śāstreṇa pratipādyate | ataḥ karmavatsu gṛhastheṣu yajñādinitya- naimittika sarvakarmāpekṣā vidyā ||
vidyotpatteḥ cittasamādhānarūpatvena dṛṣṭaparikaratvāt śamādīnāṃ vidyānirvṛttaye śamādīnāmapyavaśyānuṣṭheyatvācca tānyapyanuṣṭheyāni | na ca karaṇavyāpāra- tadviparyayarūpatvena karmaṇāṃ śamadamādīnāṃ ca parasparavirodhaḥ ; vihiteṣu karaṇavyāpāraḥ, avihiteṣu prayojanaśūnyeṣu ca tadupaśama iti bhinnaviṣayatvāt || ataḥ gṛhasthasya śamādayo'pyanuṣṭheyāḥ ||
ataḥ sarvāśrameṣu sthitasya viduṣaḥ yajñādisvāśramadharmavat maunatṛtīyaṃ vidyāyāḥ sahakāryantaraṃ vidhīyate; 'atha muniḥ' iti ||
vidvān svabhāvānāviṣkārarūpaṃ yat bālasya karma tadupādadāno varteta || bālyasya vayovasthāviśeṣasya anupādeyatvāt itareṣā ( kāmacārādikānāṃ ) vidyāvirodhitva śravaṇāt ||

muktiphalasyāpyupāsanasya svasādhanabhataiḥ atiśayita karmabhiḥ utpattau evameva kālā- niyamaḥ ; tasyāpi pūrvavat pratibandhābhāva pratibandhasamāptirūpāvasthāvagateḥ | tatrāpi pūrvakṛtānāṃ pravalānāṃ brahmavidapacārāṇāṃ sambhavāt pratibandhasambhava iti ||

upāsya brahma upāsiturātmatvenopāsyam | upāsitā pratyagātmā svaśarīrasya svayaṃ yathā ātmā; tathā paraṃ brahma svātmano'pyātmeti, upāsyaṃ brahma ātmetyevopāsīta sarvasya cida- cidvastunaḥ, 'sarva khalvidaṃ brahma tajjalāniti, tajjatvāt tallatvāt tadanatvāt tanniyāmyatvāt taccharīratvācca, sarvasya ayaṃ ( paramātmā) ātmā | yathā svaśarīraṃ prati pratyamātmana ātmatvāt 'devoshaṃ, manuṣyo'ham' ityanusandhānam ; tathā paramātmanaḥ pratyagātmano'pyātmatvāt, tasyāpi 'aham' ityeva anusandhāna yuktamiti ||
evaṃ śāstrairupapāditaṃ sarvabuddhīnāṃ brahmakaniṣṭhatvena sarvaśabdānāṃ brahmakaniṣṭhatvam abhyupa. gacchantaḥ, 'tvaṃ vā ahamasmi bhagavo devate ! ahaṃ vai tvamasi bhagavo devate ! ' iti vyati kareṇoktavantaḥ ||
evaṃ capā ātmetyevopāsīta' iti | 'atmatvā nanusandhā niṣeva, 'pṛthagātmānaṃ preritāraṃ ca matvā' iti pṛthatvānusandhānavidhānaṃ ca aviruddham | 'aham ' iti svātmatayā anusandhānāt, 'atha yaḥ anyāṃ devatāmupāste....na sa veda' iti anyatvānusandhāna- niṣedho rakṣitaḥ | straśarīrā svātmano'dhikatvānusandhānavat, svātmano'pi ( sakāśāt ) paramātmanaḥ adhikatvānusandhānāt pṛthaktvānusandhānavidhānaṃ ca rakṣitam ||
adhikasya brahmaṇaḥ pratyagātmana ātmatvāt tasya ca brahmaśarīratvāt niṣedhakavākye 'akṛtsno hyeṣa : ' ityuktam | ataḥ upāsiturātmatvena brahma upāsyam - iti sthitam ||
'mano brahmetyupāsīta' 'nāma brahmetyupaste ' ityādipratikopāsaneṣu, pratīke na ātma- tvānusandhānaṃ kāryam | na hi upāsiturātmā pratīkaḥ | pratīkopāsaneṣu, pratīka evopāsya: ; na brahma | brahma tu tatra dṛṣṭiviśeṣaṇamātram | pratīkopāsanaṃ hi nāma - abrahmaṇi brahmadṛṣṭyā'nu- sandhānam | tatra upāsyasya pratīkasya upāsiturātmatvābhāvāt, na tathā'nusandheyam ||

ādityādīnāmeva ( udvīthādyapekṣayā) utkṛṣṭatvopapatteḥ, ṛtvaṅge udgīthādau ādityā- didṛṣṭaya eva kāryā: ; ( na tu udgīthādidṛṣṭirādityādau ) | ādispādidevatārādhanadvāreṇa hi karmaṇāmapi phalasādhanatvam ||
mokṣasādhanatayā vedāntaśāstrairvihitaṃ dhyānopāsanādiśabdavācyaṃ jñānam asakṛdāvṛtta- smṛtisantatirūpam āsīnaḥ anutiṣṭhet | āsīnasyaiva hi ekāgracittatāsambhava: ; sthiti- gatyoḥ prayatnasāpekṣatvāt, śayāne ca nidrāsambhavāt | paścārdhadhāraṇaprayatnanivṛttaye sāpāśraye āsīnaḥ kuryāt ||
upāsanasya dhyānarūpatvāt ekāgracittatā avaśyaṃbhāvinī | dhyānaṃ hi vijātīya- pratyayāntarāvyahitam eka cintanam | ekāgracittatayā niścalatvam (pṛthivī - parvatādivat ) āsī- nasyaiva upāsakasya sambhavet || smaranti ca āsītasyaiva dhyānam -'śucau deśe .... ekāgraṃ manaḥ kṛtvā .... upaviśyāsane yujjyādyogamātmaviśuddhaye' iti || ekāgratātirikta deśakālaviśeṣā- śravaṇāt, ekāgratānukūlo yo deśaḥ kālaśca sa eva upāsanasya (yogyaḥ ) deśaḥ kālaśca | 'mano'nukūle' iti vākyaśeṣāt ||

śaktirapi paramapuruṣāprītireva | tadevaṃ vidyā, vedituḥ vedyātyaryapriyatvena svayamapi nirati- śayapriyā satī, vedyabhūtaparamapuruṣārādhanasvarūpā, pūrvakṛtāghasaṃcayajanitaparamapuruṣāprītiṃ vināśayati | saiva vidyā svotpatyuttarakālabhāvyagha nimittaparamapuruṣāprītyutpatti ca pratibadhnāti ||

uktena nyāyena puṇyasyāpi vidyayā aśleṣa - vināśau syātām; vidyāphalavirodhitva- sāmānyāt, 'sarve pāpmāno'to nivartante' iti, sukṛtasyā'pi pāpmaśabdena vyapadeśāt || mumukṣoraniṣṭaphalatvāt, sukṛtasya 'pi pāpmaśabdena vyapadeśa: || śarīrapātādūrdhvaṃ tu vidyānu- guṇadṛṣṭaphalāni sukṛtāni naśyantīti ||
vidyotpatteḥ pūrve sukṛta- duṣkṛte, apravṛttaphale eva vidyayā naśyataḥ, aviśeṣeṇa | 'tasya tāvadeva ciraṃ yāvanna vimokṣye, atha sampatsye iti śarīrapātavilambāvaghitvaśruteḥ | na ca puṇyāpuṇyakarmajanyabhagavatprīttivyatirekeṇa śarīra sthitihetubhūtasaṃskārasadbhāve pramāṇamasti ||
agnihotrādyāśramadharmā phalāśleṣāsambhavāt anuṣṭheyā eva | vidyā khyakāryāyaiva viduṣaḥ agnihotrādyanuṣṭhānamiti teṣāṃ tatkāryārthatvāt tada ( phalā) śleṣāsambhavaḥ | dṛśyate hi agnihotrādīnāṃ vidyāsādhanatvam, tametaṃ vedānuvacanena' ityādinā || āprayāṇāt abhyāsādheyātiśayāyāḥ vidyāyāśca aharaharutpādyatvāt, tadutpattyartham āśramakarmā'pi aharaharanuṣṭheyameva | anyathā āśramakarmalope, dūṣitāntaḥkaraṇasya vidyotpattireva na syāt ||

tadevaṃ brahmavidyāyāḥ prāganuṣṭhitam abhuktaphalam anārabdhaphalaṃ puṇya-pāparūpaṃ karma anādikālasañcitam anantam, vidyāmāhātmyādvinaśyati | vidyārambhottara kālamanuṣṭhitaṃ ca, na śliṣyati | tatra puṇyarūpaṃ sarva, viduṣaḥ suhṛdo gṛhṇanti | pāpaṃ ca dviṣantaḥ iti niravadyam ||

puruṣasya prayataḥ vāṅmanasi sampadyate ' iti vāco manasi sampattiśrutiḥ, vāk- svarūpasya manasi sampattāveva upapadyate ; na vṛttimātrasampattau | manasā vāk saṃyujyate; na tu tatra līyate | manasaḥ vākprakṛtikatvābhāvāt ||

( tathaiḥ ) sarvendriyasaṃyuktaṃ manaḥ prāṇe saṃyujyate; na manovṛttimātram | 'manaḥ prāṇe' iti vākyāt ||
sa ca prāṇaḥ karaṇādhipe jītre sampadyate |
jīvasaṃyuktasya prāṇasya tejasi sampatti:, sarveṣu bhūteṣu; jīvasya saṃcarataḥ sarvabhūta- mayatvabhūteḥ ||
iyam utkrāntiḥ nāḍīpraveśanāt prāk vidvadaviduṣoḥ samānā | tatpraveśadaśāyāṃ ca viśeṣaḥ śrūyate | mūrdhnā niṣkramaṇaṃ vidvadviṣayam : 'atra brahma samaśnute' iti ca upāsanavelāyāṃ yo brahmānubhavaḥ, tadviṣayam ||
vidvān, śatādhikayā mūrdhanyayā nāḍyaiva utkrāmati | vidvān hi paramapuruṣārādhana- bhūtātyarthatriyavidyāsāmarthyāt vidyāśeṣabhūtiyā ātmanotyartha priyagatya nummaraṇayogācca prasannena hārdena paramapuruṣeṇa anugṛhīto bhavati | tataśca tasya jīvasya sthānaṃ hṛdayam agrajvalanaṃ bhavati | paramapuruṣaprasādāt prakaśi dvāro vidvān, tāṃ nāḍīṃ vijānātīti tathā viduṣo gatirupapadyate ||
lakṣyate hi niśyapi nidāghasamaye (sūrya) raśnisadbhāva: ) tasmāt niśi mṛtānāmapi viduṣāṃ gramyanureṇaiva brahmaprāptiḥ astyeva |1
anārabdha kāryāṇam adhogatihetubhūtānāṃ karmaṇāṃ vidyāsambandhenaiva vināśāt, uttareṣāṃ ca aśleṣāt, prārabdhakāryasya carama dehāvadhitvāt, viduṣo niśāmṛtasyāpi brahmapra tiḥ siddhaiva ||
niśi mṛnamyāpi viduṣaḥ brahmaprāptau yo heturuktaḥ tata eva hetoḥ dakṣiṇe'pyayane mṛtasya brahmaprāptiḥ siddhā ||
ābhiḥ (sarvābhiḥ) śrutibhiḥ arcirādiḥ eka eva mārgaḥ sarvatra nyūnādhikabhāvena pratipādyata iti acira| dinaiva gacchati vidvān ||
ye paraṃ brahmopāsate, ye ca ātmānaṃ prakṛtiviyuktaṃ brahmātmakamupāsate, tānubhayavidhān arcirādiḥ ativāhiko gaṇo nayatīti bhagavān bādarāyaṇo manyate ||

apahatapatrādisvarūpa evāyamātmā saṃsāradaśāyāṃ karmākhyayā avidyayā tirohita- svarūpa, paraṃjyotirupasampadya avibhūtasvarūpo bhavati | ataḥ apahatapāpmatvādayaḥ pratyaga
parabrahmo sampacyā nivṛttā vidyātirodhānasya ( cetanasya) yāthātathyena svātmano dṛṣṭatvāt, muktaḥ paramātmātmakaṃ taccharīratayā tatprakārabhūtaṃ svātmānaṃ parasmādbrahmaṇo'vibhāgena 'ahaṃ brahmāsmi' ityeva nubhavati ||
nirasta nikhilatirodhānasya muktasya nikhilacetanācetanasvarūpa sthitipravṛttibheda niyamanam — jagadvyāpāraṃ varjayitvā niryāja brahmānubhavarūpamaiśraryam ||
yathā 'nikhila heya pratyanīka kalyāṇakatānaḥ jagajjanmādikāraṇaṃ samastavastuvilakṣaṇaḥ sarvajñaḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ āśrita vātsalyai kajaladhiḥ paramakāruṇikaḥ nirastasamābhyadhika sambhāvanaḥ āśritavātsalyai parabrahmābhidhānaḥ paramapuruṣaḥ asti' iti śabdādavagamyate; evam 'aharahagnuṣṭhīyamānavarṇāśrama- dharmānugṛhītatadupāsanarūpataḥ samārādhanaprīta, upāsīnān anādikālapravṛttānantadu tara karma sañcaya - rūpā vidyāṃ vinivartya, svayāthātmyānubhavarūpānavadhi kātiśayānandaṃ prāpayya punarnāvartayati' ityapi śabdādevāvagamyate ||

siddhāntavācaḥ śrīmādhye saṅgṛhītā yathāyatham | viduṣā rāmabhadreṇa tena prīṇātu mādhavaḥ ||
